




|| namau tassa bhagavatau arahatau sammāsambuddhassa||

vinayapiṭakai


parivārapāḷi

1. bhikkhuvibhaṅgau


sauḷasamahāvārau

1. katthapañ‍ñattivārau

1. pārājikakaṇḍaṃ

1. yaṃ  taina bhagavatā jānatā passatā arahatā sammāsambuddhaina paṭhamaṃ pārājikaṃ kattha pañ‍ñattaṃ, kaṃ ārabbha, kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ? atthi tattha pañ‍ñatti, anupañ‍ñatti, anuppan‍napañ‍ñatti? sabbatthapañ‍ñatti, padaisapañ‍ñatti? sādhāraṇapañ‍ñatti, asādhāraṇapañ‍ñatti? aikataupañ‍ñatti, ubhataupañ‍ñatti? pañ‍can‍naṃ pātimaukkhuddaisānaṃ katthaugadhaṃ kattha pariyāpan‍naṃ? katamaina uddaisaina uddaisaṃ āgacchati? catun‍naṃ vipattīnaṃ katamā vipatti? sattan‍naṃ āpattikkhandhānaṃ  katamau āpattikkhandhau? chan‍naṃ āpattisamuṭṭhānānaṃ katihi samuṭṭhānaihi samuṭṭhāti? catun‍naṃ adhikaraṇānaṃ katamaṃ adhikaraṇaṃ? sattan‍naṃ samathānaṃ katihi samathaihi sammati? kau tattha vinayau, kau tattha abhivinayau? kiṃ tattha pātimaukkhaṃ, kiṃ tattha adhipātimaukkhaṃ? kā vipatti? kā sampatti? kā paṭipatti? kati atthavasai paṭic‍ca bhagavatā paṭhamaṃ pārājikaṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ? kai sikkhanti? kai sikkhitasikkhā? kattha ṭhitaṃ? kai dhārainti? kassa vacanaṃ? kainābhatanti?

2. yaṃ taina bhagavatā jānatā passatā arahatā sammāsambuddhaina paṭhamaṃ  pārājikaṃ kattha pañ‍ñattanti? vaisāliyaṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ| kaṃ ārabbhāti? sudin‍naṃ kalandaputtaṃ ārabbha| kismiṃ vatthusminti? sudin‍nau kalandaputtau purāṇadutiyikāya maithunaṃ  dhammaṃ paṭisaivi, tasmiṃ vatthusmiṃ| atthi tattha pañ‍ñatti, anupañ‍ñatti, anuppan‍napañ‍ñattīti? aikā pañ‍ñatti, dvai anupañ‍ñattiyau| anuppan‍napañ‍ñatti  tasmiṃ natthi| sabbatthapañ‍ñatti, padaisapañ‍ñattīti? sabbatthapañ‍ñatti| sādhāraṇapañ‍ñatti, asādhāraṇapañ‍ñattīti? sādhāraṇapañ‍ñatti| aikataupañ‍ñatti, ubhataupañ‍ñattīti? ubhataupañ‍ñatti| pañ‍can‍naṃ pātimaukkhuddaisānaṃ katthaugadhaṃ kattha pariyāpan‍nanti? nidānaugadhaṃ, nidānapariyāpan‍naṃ| katamaina uddaisaina uddaisaṃ āgacchatīti? dutiyaina uddaisaina uddaisaṃ āgacchati| catun‍naṃ vipattīnaṃ katamā vipattīti? sīlavipatti| sattan‍naṃ āpattikkhandhānaṃ katamau āpattikkhandhauti? pārājikāpattikkhandhau| chan‍naṃ āpattisamuṭṭhānānaṃ katihi samuṭṭhānaihi samuṭṭhātīti? aikaina samuṭṭhānaina samuṭṭhāti – kāyatau ca cittatau ca samuṭṭhāti, na vācatau| catun‍naṃ adhikaraṇānaṃ katamaṃ adhikaraṇanti? āpattādhikaraṇaṃ| sattan‍naṃ samathānaṃ katihi samathaihi sammatīti? dvīhi samathaihi sammati – sammukhāvinayaina ca paṭiñ‍ñātakaraṇaina ca| kau tattha vinayau, kau tattha abhivinayauti? pañ‍ñatti vinayau, vibhatti abhivinayau| kiṃ tattha pātimaukkhaṃ, kiṃ tattha adhipātimaukkhanti? pañ‍ñatti pātimaukkhaṃ, vibhatti adhipātimaukkhaṃ| kā vipattīti? asaṃvarau vipatti| kā  sampattīti? saṃvarau sampatti| kā paṭipattīti? na aivarūpaṃ karissāmīti yāvajīvaṃ āpāṇakauṭikaṃ samādāya sikkhati sikkhāpadaisu| a॰ ni॰ 10.31 kati atthavasai paṭic‍ca bhagavatā paṭhamaṃ pārājikaṃ pañ‍ñattanti? dasa atthavasai paṭic‍ca bhagavatā paṭhamaṃ pārājikaṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ – saṅghasuṭṭhutāya, saṅghaphāsutāya, dummaṅkūnaṃ puggalānaṃ niggahāya, paisalānaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ phāsuvihārāya, diṭṭhadhammikānaṃ āsavānaṃ saṃvarāya, samparāyikānaṃ āsavānaṃ paṭighātāya, appasan‍nānaṃ pasādāya, pasan‍nānaṃ bhiyyaubhāvāya, saddhammaṭṭhitiyā, vinayānuggahāya| kai sikkhantīti? saikkhā ca puthuj‍janakalyāṇakā ca sikkhanti| kai sikkhitasikkhāti? arahantau sikkhitasikkhā| kattha ṭhitanti? sikkhākāmaisu ṭhitaṃ| kai dhāraintīti? yaisaṃ vattati tai dhārainti| kassa vacananti? bhagavatau vacanaṃ arahatau sammāsambuddhassa| kainābhatanti? paramparābhataṃ –

3.

 upāli dāsakau caiva, sauṇakau siggavau tathā|

mauggaliputtaina mauggalīputtaina (syā॰) pañ‍camā, aitai jambusirivhayai jambusarivhayai (sārattha)||

tatau  mahindau iṭṭiyau iṭṭhiyau (sī॰), uttiyau sambalau uṭṭiyau sampalau (itipi) tathā|

bhaddanāmau ca paṇḍitau||

aitai  nāgā mahāpañ‍ñā, jambudīpā idhāgatā|

vinayaṃ tai vācayiṃsu, piṭakaṃ tambapaṇṇiyā||

nikāyai  pañ‍ca vācaisuṃ, satta caiva pakaraṇai|

tatau ariṭṭhau maidhāvī, tissadattau ca paṇḍitau||

visāradau kāḷasumanau, thairau ca dīghanāmakau|

dīghasumanau ca paṇḍitau||

punadaiva punaraiva (syā॰) kāḷasumanau, nāgatthairau ca buddharakkhitau|

tissatthairau ca maidhāvī, daivatthairau raivatthairau (itipi) ca paṇḍitau||

punadaiva  sumanau maidhāvī, vinayai ca visāradau|

bahussutau cūḷanāgau, gajauva duppadhaṃsiyau||

dhammapālitanāmau ca, rauhaṇai rauhaṇau (sī॰) sādhupūjitau|

tassa sissau mahāpañ‍ñau khaimanāmau tipaiṭakau||

dīpai tārakarājāva pañ‍ñāya atiraucatha|

upatissau ca maidhāvī, phussadaivau mahākathī||

punadaiva sumanau maidhāvī, pupphanāmau bahussutau|

mahākathī mahāsivau, piṭakai sabbattha kauvidau||

punadaiva upāli maidhāvī, vinayai ca visāradau|

mahānāgau mahāpañ‍ñau, saddhammavaṃsakauvidau||

punadaiva abhayau maidhāvī, piṭakai sabbattha kauvidau|

tissatthairau ca maidhāvī, vinayai ca visāradau||

tassa sissau mahāpañ‍ñau, pupphanāmau bahussutau|

sāsanaṃ  anurakkhantau, jambudīpai patiṭṭhitau||

cūḷābhayau ca maidhāvī, vinayai ca visāradau|

tissatthairau ca maidhāvī, saddhammavaṃsakauvidau||

cūḷadaivau phussadaivau (sī॰) ca maidhāvī, vinayai ca visāradau|

sivatthairau ca maidhāvī, vinayai sabbattha kauvidau||

aitai  nāgā mahāpañ‍ñā, vinayañ‍ñū maggakauvidā|

vinayaṃ dīpai pakāsaisuṃ, piṭakaṃ tambapaṇṇiyāti||

4. yaṃ taina bhagavatā jānatā passatā arahatā sammāsambuddhaina dutiyaṃ pārājikaṃ kattha pañ‍ñattanti? rājagahai pañ‍ñattaṃ| kaṃ ārabbhāti? dhaniyaṃ kumbhakāraputtaṃ ārabbha| kismiṃ vatthusminti? dhaniyau kumbhakāraputtau rañ‍ñau dārūni adin‍naṃ ādiyi, tasmiṃ vatthusmiṃ| aikā pañ‍ñatti, aikā anupañ‍ñatti| chan‍naṃ āpattisamuṭṭhānānaṃ katihi samuṭṭhānaihi samuṭṭhātīti? tīhi samuṭṭhānaihi samuṭṭhāti – siyā kāyatau ca cittatau ca samuṭṭhāti, na vācatau; siyā vācatau ca cittatau ca samuṭṭhāti, na kāyatau; siyā kāyatau ca vācatau ca cittatau ca samuṭṭhāti…pai॰…|

5. tatiyaṃ pārājikaṃ kattha pañ‍ñattanti? vaisāliyaṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ| kaṃ ārabbhāti? sambahulai  bhikkhū ārabbha| kismiṃ vatthusminti? sambahulā bhikkhū añ‍ñamañ‍ñaṃ jīvitā vauraupaisuṃ, tasmiṃ vatthusmiṃ| aikā pañ‍ñatti, aikā anupañ‍ñatti| chan‍naṃ  āpattisamuṭṭhānānaṃ katihi samuṭṭhānaihi samuṭṭhātīti? tīhi samuṭṭhānaihi samuṭṭhāti – siyā kāyatau ca cittatau ca samuṭṭhāti, na vācatau; siyā vācatau ca cittatau ca samuṭṭhāti, na kāyatau; siyā kāyatau ca vācatau ca cittatau ca samuṭṭhāti…pai॰…|

6. catutthaṃ pārājikaṃ kattha pañ‍ñattanti? vaisāliyaṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ| kaṃ ārabbhāti? vaggumudātīriyai bhikkhū ārabbha| kismiṃ vatthusminti? vaggumudātīriyā bhikkhū gihīnaṃ añ‍ñamañ‍ñassa uttarimanussadhammassa vaṇṇaṃ bhāsiṃsu, tasmiṃ vatthusmiṃ| aikā pañ‍ñatti, aikā anupañ‍ñatti| chan‍naṃ āpattisamuṭṭhānānaṃ katihi samuṭṭhānaihi samuṭṭhātīti? tīhi samuṭṭhānaihi samuṭṭhāti – siyā kāyatau ca cittatau ca samuṭṭhāti, na vācatau; siyā vācatau ca cittatau ca samuṭṭhāti, na kāyatau; siyā kāyatau ca vācatau ca cittatau ca samuṭṭhāti…pai॰…|

cattārau pārājikā niṭṭhitā|

tassuddānaṃ –

maithunādin‍nādānañ‍ca, manussaviggahuttari|

pārājikāni cattāri, chaij‍javatthū asaṃsayāti||

2. saṅghādisaisakaṇḍaṃ

7. yaṃ taina bhagavatā jānatā passatā arahatā sammāsambuddhaina upak‍kamitvā asuciṃ maucaintassa saṅghādisaisau kattha pañ‍ñattau? kaṃ ārabbha? kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ? atthi tattha pañ‍ñatti, anupañ‍ñatti, anuppan‍napañ‍ñatti? sabbatthapañ‍ñatti, padaisapañ‍ñatti? sādhāraṇapañ‍ñatti, asādhāraṇapañ‍ñatti? aikataupañ‍ñatti, ubhataupañ‍ñatti? pañ‍can‍naṃ pātimaukkhuddaisānaṃ katthaugadhaṃ kattha pariyāpan‍naṃ? katamaina uddaisaina uddaisaṃ āgacchati? catun‍naṃ vipattīnaṃ katamā vipatti? sattan‍naṃ āpattikkhandhānaṃ katamau āpattikkhandhau? chan‍naṃ āpattisamuṭṭhānānaṃ katihi samuṭṭhānaihi samuṭṭhāti? catun‍naṃ adhikaraṇānaṃ katamaṃ adhikaraṇaṃ? sattan‍naṃ samathānaṃ katihi samathaihi sammati? kau tattha vinayau, kau tattha abhivinayau? kiṃ tattha pātimaukkhaṃ, kiṃ tattha adhipātimaukkhaṃ? kā vipatti, kā sampatti, kā paṭipatti? kati atthavasai paṭic‍ca bhagavatā upak‍kamitvā asuciṃ maucaintassa saṅghādisaisau pañ‍ñattau? kai sikkhanti, kai sikkhitasikkhā? kattha ṭhitaṃ? kai dhārainti? kassa vacanaṃ? kainābhatanti?

8. yaṃ taina bhagavatā jānatā passatā arahatā sammāsambuddhaina upak‍kamitvā asuciṃ maucaintassa saṅghādisaisau kattha pañ‍ñattauti? sāvatthiyaṃ pañ‍ñattau| kaṃ ārabbhāti? āyasmantaṃ saiyyasakaṃ ārabbha| kismiṃ vatthusminti? āyasmā saiyyasakau hatthaina upak‍kamitvā asuciṃ maucaisi, tasmiṃ vatthusmiṃ| atthi tattha pañ‍ñatti, anupañ‍ñatti, anuppan‍napañ‍ñattīti? aikā pañ‍ñatti, aikā anupañ‍ñatti| anuppan‍napañ‍ñatti tasmiṃ natthi| sabbattha pañ‍ñatti, padaisapañ‍ñattīti? sabbatthapañ‍ñatti| sādhāraṇapañ‍ñatti, asādhāraṇapañ‍ñattīti? asādhāraṇapañ‍ñatti| aikataupañ‍ñatti, ubhataupañ‍ñattīti? aikataupañ‍ñatti| pañ‍can‍naṃ pātimaukkhuddaisānaṃ katthaugadhaṃ kattha pariyāpan‍nanti? nidānaugadhaṃ nidānapariyāpan‍naṃ| katamaina uddaisaina uddaisaṃ āgacchatīti? tatiyai  uddaisaina uddaisaṃ āgacchati| catun‍naṃ vipattīnaṃ katamā vipattīti? sīlavipatti| sattan‍naṃ āpattikkhandhānaṃ katamau āpattikkhandhauti? saṅghādisaisau āpattikkhandhau| chan‍naṃ āpattisamuṭṭhānānaṃ katihi samuṭṭhānaihi samuṭṭhātīti? aikaina samuṭṭhānaina samuṭṭhāti – kāyatau ca cittatau ca samuṭṭhāti, na vācatau| catun‍naṃ adhikaraṇānaṃ katamaṃ adhikaraṇanti? āpattādhikaraṇaṃ| sattan‍naṃ samathānaṃ katihi samathaihi sammatīti? dvīhi samathaihi sammati – sammukhāvinayaina ca paṭiñ‍ñātakaraṇaina ca| kau tattha vinayau, kau tattha abhivinayauti? pañ‍ñatti vinayau, vibhatti abhivinayau| kiṃ tattha pātimaukkhaṃ, kiṃ tattha adhipātimaukkhanti? pañ‍ñatti pātimaukkhaṃ, vibhatti adhipātimaukkhaṃ| kā vipattīti? asaṃvarau vipatti| kā sampattīti? saṃvarau sampatti| kā paṭipattīti? na aivarūpaṃ karissāmīti yāvajīvaṃ āpāṇakauṭikaṃ samādāya sikkhati sikkhāpadaisu| a॰ ni॰ 10.31 kati atthavasai paṭic‍ca bhagavatā upak‍kamitvā asuciṃ maucaintassa saṅghādisaisau pañ‍ñattauti? dasa atthavasai paṭic‍ca bhagavatā upak‍kamitvā asuciṃ maucaintassa saṅghādisaisau pañ‍ñattau – saṅghasuṭṭhutāya, saṅghaphāsutāya, dummaṅkūnaṃ puggalānaṃ niggahāya, paisalānaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ phāsuvihārāya, diṭṭhadhammikānaṃ āsavānaṃ saṃvarāya, samparāyikānaṃ āsavānaṃ  paṭighātāya, appasan‍nānaṃ pasādāya, pasan‍nānaṃ bhiyyaubhāvāya, saddhammaṭṭhitiyā, vinayānuggahāya| kai sikkhantīti? saikkhā ca puthuj‍janakalyāṇakā ca sikkhanti| kai sikkhitasikkhāti? arahantau sikkhitasikkhā| kattha ṭhitanti? sikkhākāmaisu ṭhitaṃ| kai dhāraintīti? yaisaṃ vattati, tai dhārainti| kassa vacananti? bhagavatau vacanaṃ arahatau sammāsambuddhassa| kainābhatanti? paramparābhataṃ –

upāli dāsakau caiva, sauṇakau siggavau tathā|

mauggaliputtaina pañ‍camā, aitai jambusirivhayai||

tatau mahindau iṭṭiyau, uttiyau sambalau tathā|

bhaddanāmau ca paṇḍitau||

aitai nāgā mahāpañ‍ñā, jambudīpā idhāgatā|

vinayaṃ tai vācayiṃsu, piṭakaṃ tambapaṇṇiyā||

nikāyai pañ‍ca vācaisuṃ, satta caiva pakaraṇai|

tatau ariṭṭhau maidhāvī, tissadattau ca paṇḍitau||

visāradau  kāḷasumanau, thairau ca dīghanāmakau|

dīghasumanau ca paṇḍitau||

punadaiva kāḷasumanau, nāgatthairau ca buddharakkhitau|

tissatthairau ca maidhāvī, daivatthairau ca paṇḍitau||

punadaiva sumanau maidhāvī, vinayai ca visāradau|

bahussutau cūḷanāgau, gajauva duppadhaṃsiyau||

dhammapālitanāmau ca, rauhaṇai sādhupūjitau|

tassa sissau mahāpañ‍ñau, khaimanāmau tipaiṭakau||

dīpai tārakarājāva, pañ‍ñāya atiraucatha|

upatissau ca maidhāvī, phussadaivau mahākathī||

punadaiva  sumanau maidhāvī, pupphanāmau bahussutau|

mahākathī mahāsivau, piṭakai sabbattha kauvidau||

punadaiva upāli maidhāvī, vinayai ca visāradau|

mahānāgau mahāpañ‍ñau, saddhammavaṃsakauvidau||

punadaiva abhayau maidhāvī, piṭakai sabbattha kauvidau|

tissatthairau ca maidhāvī, vinayai ca visāradau||

tassa sissau mahāpañ‍ñau, pupphanāmau bahussutau|

sāsanaṃ anurakkhantau, jambudīpai patiṭṭhitau||

cūḷābhayau ca maidhāvī, vinayai ca visāradau|

tissatthairau ca maidhāvī, saddhammavaṃsakauvidau||

cūḷadaivau ca maidhāvī, vinayai ca visāradau|

sivatthairau ca maidhāvī, vinayai sabbattha kauvidau||

aitai nāgā mahāpañ‍ñā, vinayañ‍ñū maggakauvidau|

vinayaṃ dīpai pakāsaisuṃ, piṭakaṃ tambapaṇṇiyāti||

9. yaṃ taina bhagavatā jānatā passatā arahatā sammāsambuddhaina mātugāmaina saddhiṃ kāyasaṃsaggaṃ samāpaj‍jantassa saṅghādisaisau kattha pañ‍ñattauti? sāvatthiyaṃ pañ‍ñattau| kaṃ ārabbhāti? āyasmantaṃ udāyiṃ ārabbha| kismiṃ vatthusminti? āyasmā udāyī mātugāmaina  saddhiṃ kāyasaṃsaggaṃ samāpaj‍ji, tasmiṃ vatthusmiṃ| aikā pañ‍ñatti| chan‍naṃ āpattisamuṭṭhānānaṃ aikaina samuṭṭhānaina samuṭṭhāti – kāyatau ca cittatau ca samuṭṭhāti, na vācatau…pai॰…|

10. mātugāmaṃ duṭṭhul‍lāhi vācāhi aubhāsantassa saṅghādisaisau kattha pañ‍ñattauti? sāvatthiyaṃ pañ‍ñattau| kaṃ ārabbhāti? āyasmantaṃ udāyiṃ ārabbha| kismiṃ vatthusminti? āyasmā  udāyī mātugāmaṃ duṭṭhul‍lāhi vācāhi aubhāsi, tasmiṃ vatthusmiṃ| aikā pañ‍ñatti| chan‍naṃ āpattisamuṭṭhānānaṃ tīhi samuṭṭhānaihi samuṭṭhāti – siyā kāyatau ca cittatau ca samuṭṭhāti, na vācatau; siyā vācatau ca cittatau ca samuṭṭhāti, na kāyatau; siyā kāyatau ca vācatau ca cittatau ca samuṭṭhāti…pai॰…|

11. mātugāmassa santikai attakāmapāricariyāya vaṇṇaṃ bhāsantassa saṅghādisaisau kattha pañ‍ñattauti? sāvatthiyaṃ pañ‍ñattau| kaṃ ārabbhāti? āyasmantaṃ udāyiṃ ārabbha| kismiṃ vatthusminti? āyasmā  udāyī mātugāmassa santikai attakāmapāricariyāya vaṇṇaṃ abhāsi, tasmiṃ vatthusmiṃ| aikā pañ‍ñatti| chan‍naṃ āpattisamuṭṭhānānaṃ tīhi samuṭṭhānaihi samuṭṭhāti – siyā kāyatau ca cittatau ca samuṭṭhāti, na vācatau; siyā vācatau ca cittatau ca samuṭṭhāti, na kāyatau; siyā kāyatau ca vācatau ca cittatau ca samuṭṭhāti…pai॰…|

12. sañ‍carittaṃ samāpaj‍jantassa saṅghādisaisau kattha pañ‍ñattauti? sāvatthiyaṃ pañ‍ñattau| kaṃ ārabbhāti? āyasmantaṃ udāyiṃ ārabbha| kismiṃ vatthusminti? āyasmā udāyī sañ‍carittaṃ samāpaj‍ji, tasmiṃ vatthusmiṃ| aikā pañ‍ñatti, aikā anupañ‍ñatti| chan‍naṃ āpattisamuṭṭhānānaṃ chahi samuṭṭhānaihi samuṭṭhāti – siyā kāyatau samuṭṭhāti, na vācatau na cittatau; siyā vācatau samuṭṭhāti, na kāyatau na cittatau; siyā kāyatau ca vācatau ca samuṭṭhāti, na cittatau; siyā kāyatau ca cittatau ca samuṭṭhāti, na vācatau; siyā vācatau ca cittatau ca samuṭṭhāti, na kāyatau; siyā kāyatau ca vācatau ca cittatau ca samuṭṭhāti…pai॰…|

13. sañ‍ñācikā  kuṭiṃ kārāpaintassa saṅghādisaisau kattha pañ‍ñattauti? āḷaviyaṃ pañ‍ñattau| kaṃ ārabbhāti? āḷavakai bhikkhū ārabbha| kismiṃ vatthusminti? āḷavakā bhikkhū sañ‍ñācikāya kuṭiyau kārāpaisuṃ, tasmiṃ vatthusmiṃ| aikā pañ‍ñatti| chan‍naṃ āpattisamuṭṭhānānaṃ chahi samuṭṭhānaihi samuṭṭhāti…pai॰…|

14. mahal‍lakaṃ vihāraṃ kārāpaintassa saṅghādisaisau kattha pañ‍ñattauti? kausambiyaṃ pañ‍ñattau| kaṃ ārabbhāti? āyasmantaṃ chan‍naṃ ārabbha| kismiṃ vatthusminti? āyasmā chan‍nau vihāravatthuṃ saudhaintau añ‍ñataraṃ caitiyarukkhaṃ chaidāpaisi, tasmiṃ vatthusmiṃ| aikā pañ‍ñatti| chan‍naṃ āpattisamuṭṭhānānaṃ chahi samuṭṭhānaihi samuṭṭhāti…pai॰…|

15. bhikkhuṃ  amūlakaina pārājikaina dhammaina anuddhaṃsaintassa saṅghādisaisau  kattha pañ‍ñattauti? rājagahai pañ‍ñattau| kaṃ ārabbhāti? maittiyabhūmajakai bhikkhū ārabbha| kismiṃ vatthusminti? maittiyabhūmajakā bhikkhū āyasmantaṃ dabbaṃ mal‍laputtaṃ amūlakaina pārājikaina dhammaina anuddhaṃsaisuṃ, tasmiṃ vatthusmiṃ| aikā pañ‍ñatti| chan‍naṃ āpattisamuṭṭhānānaṃ tīhi samuṭṭhānaihi samuṭṭhāti…pai॰…|

16. bhikkhuṃ añ‍ñabhāgiyassa adhikaraṇassa kiñ‍ci daisaṃ laisamattaṃ upādāya pārājikaina dhammaina anuddhaṃsaintassa saṅghādisaisau kattha pañ‍ñattauti? rājagahai pañ‍ñattau| kaṃ ārabbhāti? maittiyabhūmajakai bhikkhū ārabbha| kismiṃ vatthusminti? maittiyabhūmajakā bhikkhū āyasmantaṃ dabbaṃ mal‍laputtaṃ añ‍ñabhāgiyassa adhikaraṇassa kiñ‍ci daisaṃ laisamattaṃ upādāya pārājikaina dhammaina anuddhaṃsaisuṃ, tasmiṃ vatthusmiṃ| aikā pañ‍ñatti| chan‍naṃ āpattisamuṭṭhānānaṃ tīhi samuṭṭhānaihi samuṭṭhāti…pai॰…|

17. saṅghabhaidakassa bhikkhunau yāvatatiyaṃ samanubhāsanāya na paṭinissaj‍jantassa saṅghādisaisau kattha pañ‍ñattauti? rājagahai pañ‍ñattau| kaṃ ārabbhāti? daivadattaṃ ārabbha| kismiṃ vatthusminti? daivadattau samaggassa saṅghassa bhaidāya parak‍kami, tasmiṃ vatthusmiṃ| aikā pañ‍ñatti| chan‍naṃ āpattisamuṭṭhānānaṃ aikaina samuṭṭhānaina samuṭṭhāti – kāyatau ca vācatau ca cittatau ca samuṭṭhāti…pai॰…|

18. bhaidānuvattakānaṃ  bhikkhūnaṃ yāvatatiyaṃ samanubhāsanāya na paṭinissaj‍jantānaṃ saṅghādisaisau kattha pañ‍ñattauti? rājagahai pañ‍ñattau| kaṃ ārabbhāti? sambahulai bhikkhū ārabbha| kismiṃ vatthusminti? sambahulā bhikkhū daivadattassa saṅghabhaidāya parak‍kamantassa  anuvattakā ahaisuṃ vaggavādakā, tasmiṃ vatthusmiṃ| aikā pañ‍ñatti| chan‍naṃ āpattisamuṭṭhānānaṃ aikaina samuṭṭhānaina samuṭṭhāti – kāyatau ca vācatau ca cittatau ca samuṭṭhāti…pai॰…|

19. dubbacassa bhikkhunau yāvatatiyaṃ samanubhāsanā  na paṭinissaj‍jantassa saṅghādisaisau kattha pañ‍ñattauti? kausambiyaṃ pañ‍ñattau| kaṃ ārabbhāti? āyasmantaṃ chan‍naṃ ārabbha| kismiṃ vatthusminti? āyasmā chan‍nau bhikkhūhi sahadhammikaṃ vuc‍camānau attānaṃ avacanīyaṃ akāsi, tasmiṃ vatthusmiṃ| aikā pañ‍ñatti| chan‍naṃ āpattisamuṭṭhānānaṃ aikaina samuṭṭhānaina samuṭṭhāti – kāyatau ca vācatau ca cittatau ca samuṭṭhāti…pai॰…|

20. kuladūsakassa  bhikkhunau yāvatatiyaṃ samanubhāsanāya na paṭinissaj‍jantassa saṅghādisaisau kattha pañ‍ñattauti? sāvatthiyaṃ pañ‍ñattau| kaṃ ārabbhāti? assajipunabbasukai bhikkhū ārabbha| kismiṃ vatthusminti? assajipunabbasukā bhikkhū saṅghaina pabbājanīyakammakatā bhikkhū chandagāmitā dausagāmitā mauhagāmitā bhayagāmitā pāpaisuṃ, tasmiṃ vatthusmiṃ| aikā pañ‍ñatti| chan‍naṃ āpattisamuṭṭhānānaṃ aikaina samuṭṭhānaina samuṭṭhāti – kāyatau ca vācatau ca cittatau ca samuṭṭhāti…pai॰…|

tairasa saṅghādisaisā niṭṭhitā|

tassuddānaṃ –

vissaṭṭhi  kāyasaṃsaggaṃ, duṭṭhul‍laṃ attakāmañ‍ca|

sañ‍carittaṃ kuṭī caiva, vihārau ca amūlakaṃ||

kiñ‍cidaisañ‍ca bhaidau ca, tassaiva tathaiva (ka॰) anuvattakā|

dubbacaṃ kuladūsañ‍ca, saṅghādisaisā tairasāti||

3. aniyatakaṇḍaṃ

21. yaṃ  taina bhagavatā jānatā passatā arahatā sammāsambuddhaina paṭhamau aniṃyatau kattha pañ‍ñattau? kaṃ ārabbha? kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ? atthi tattha pañ‍ñatti, anupañ‍ñatti, anuppan‍napañ‍ñatti, sabbatthapañ‍ñatti padaisapañ‍ñatti, sādhāraṇapañ‍ñatti asādhāraṇapañ‍ñatti, aikataupañ‍ñatti ubhataupañ‍ñatti, pañ‍can‍naṃ pātimaukkhuddaisānaṃ katthaugadhaṃ kattha pariyāpan‍naṃ, katamaina uddaisaina uddaisaṃ āgacchati, catun‍naṃ vipattīnaṃ katamā vipatti, sattan‍naṃ āpattikkhandhānaṃ katamau āpattikkhandhau, chan‍naṃ āpattisamuṭṭhānānaṃ katihi samuṭṭhānaihi samuṭṭhāti, catun‍naṃ adhikaraṇānaṃ katamaṃ adhikaraṇaṃ, sattan‍naṃ samathānaṃ katihi samathaihi sammati, kau tattha vinayau, kau tattha abhivinayau, kiṃ tattha pātimaukkhaṃ, kiṃ tattha adhipātimaukkhaṃ, kā vipatti, kā sampatti, kā paṭipatti, kati atthavasai paṭic‍ca bhagavatā paṭhamau aniyatau pañ‍ñattau, kai  sikkhanti, kai sikkhitasikkhā, kattha ṭhitaṃ, kai dhārainti, kassa vacanaṃ kainābhatanti|

22. yaṃ taina bhagavatā jānatā passatā arahatā sammāsambuddhaina paṭhamau aniyatau kattha  pañ‍ñattauti? sāvatthiyaṃ pañ‍ñattau| kaṃ ārabbhāti? āyasmantaṃ udāyiṃ ārabbha| kismiṃ vatthusminti? āyasmā udāyī mātugāmaina saddhiṃ aikau aikāya rahau paṭicchan‍nai āsanai alaṅkammaniyai nisaj‍jaṃ kappaisi, tasmiṃ vatthusmiṃ| atthi tattha pañ‍ñatti, anupañ‍ñatti, anuppan‍napañ‍ñattīti? aikā pañ‍ñatti| anupañ‍ñatti anuppan‍napañ‍ñatti tasmiṃ natthi| sabbatthapañ‍ñatti, padaisapañ‍ñattīti? sabbatthapañ‍ñatti| sādhāraṇapañ‍ñatti, asādhāraṇapañ‍ñattīti? asādhāraṇapañ‍ñatti| aikataupañ‍ñatti, ubhataupañ‍ñattīti? aikataupañ‍ñatti| pañ‍can‍naṃ pātimaukkhuddaisānaṃ katthaugadhaṃ kattha pariyāpan‍nanti? nidānaugadhaṃ nidānapariyāpan‍naṃ| katamaina uddaisaina uddaisaṃ āgacchatīti? catutthaina uddaisaina uddaisaṃ āgacchati| catun‍naṃ vipattīnaṃ katamā vipattīti? siyā sīlavipatti siyā ācāravipatti| sattan‍naṃ āpattikkhandhānaṃ katamau āpattikkhandhauti? siyā pārājikāpattikkhandhau siyā saṅghādisaisāpattikkhandhau siyā pācittiyāpattikkhandhau| chan‍naṃ āpattisamuṭṭhānānaṃ katihi samuṭṭhānaihi samuṭṭhātīti? aikai  samuṭṭhānaina samuṭṭhāti – kāyatau ca  cittatau ca samuṭṭhāti, na vācatau| catun‍naṃ adhikaraṇānaṃ katamaṃ adhikaraṇanti? āpattādhikaraṇaṃ| sattan‍naṃ samathānaṃ katihi samathaihi sammatīti? tīhi samathaihi sammati – siyā sammukhāvinayaina ca paṭiñ‍ñātakaraṇaina ca, siyā sammukhāvinayaina ca tiṇavatthārakaina ca| kau tattha vinayau, kau tattha abhivinayauti? pañ‍ñatti vinayau, vibhatti abhivinayau| kiṃ tattha pātimaukkhaṃ, kiṃ tattha adhipātimaukkhanti? pañ‍ñatti pātimaukkhaṃ, vibhatti adhipātimaukkhaṃ| kā vipattīti? asaṃvarau vipatti| kā sampattīti? saṃvarau sampatti| kā paṭipattīti? na aivarūpaṃ karissāmīti yāvajīvaṃ āpāṇakauṭikaṃ samādāya sikkhati sikkhāpadaisu| a॰ ni॰ 10.31 kati atthavasai paṭic‍ca bhagavatā paṭhamau aniyatau pañ‍ñattauti? dasa atthavasai paṭic‍ca bhagavatā paṭhamau aniyatau pañ‍ñattau – saṅghasuṭṭhutāya, saṅghaphāsutāya, dummaṅkūnaṃ puggalānaṃ niggahāya, paisalānaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ phāsuvihārāya, diṭṭhadhammikānaṃ āsavānaṃ saṃvarāya, samparāyikānaṃ āsavānaṃ paṭighātāya, appasan‍nānaṃ pasādāya, pasan‍nānaṃ bhiyyaubhāvāya, saddhammaṭṭhitiyā, vinayānuggahāya| kai sikkhantīti? saikkhā ca puthuj‍janakalyāṇakā ca sikkhanti| kai sikkhitasikkhāti? arahantau sikkhitasikkhā| kattha ṭhitanti? sikkhākāmaisu ṭhitaṃ| kai dhāraintīti? yaisaṃ vattati tai dhārainti| kassa vacananti? bhagavatau vacanaṃ arahatau sammāsambuddhassa| kainābhatanti? paramparābhataṃ –

upāli  dāsakau caiva, sauṇakau siggavau tathā|

mauggaliputtaina pañ‍camā, aitai jambusirivhayai||

tatau  mahindau iṭṭiyau, uttiyau sambalau tathā|

bhaddanāmau ca paṇḍitau||

aitai nāgā mahāpañ‍ñā, jambudīpā idhāgatā|

vinayaṃ tai vācayiṃsu, piṭakaṃ tambapaṇṇiyā||

nikāyai pañ‍ca vācaisuṃ, satta caiva pakaraṇai|

tatau ariṭṭhau maidhāvī, tissadattau ca paṇḍitau||

visāradau kāḷasumanau, thairau ca dīghanāmakau|

dīghasumanau ca paṇḍitau||

punadaiva  kāḷasumanau, nāgatthairau ca buddharakkhitau|

tissatthairau ca maidhāvī, daivatthairau ca paṇḍitau||

punadaiva sumanau maidhāvī, vinayai ca visāradau|

bahussutau cūḷanāgau, gajauva duppadhaṃsiyau||

dhammapālitanāmau ca, rauhaṇai sādhupūjitau|

tassa sissau mahāpañ‍ñau, khaimanāmau tipaiṭakau||

dīpai tārakarājāva pañ‍ñāya atiraucatha|

upatissau ca maidhāvī, phussadaivau mahākathī||

punadaiva sumanau maidhāvī, pupphanāmau bahussutau|

mahākathī mahāsivau, piṭakai sabbattha kauvidau||

punadaiva upāli maidhāvī, vinayai ca visāradau|

mahānāgau mahāpañ‍ñau, saddhammavaṃsakauvidau||

punadaiva  abhayau maidhāvī, piṭakai sabbattha kauvidau|

tissatthairau ca maidhāvī, vinayai ca visāradau||

tassa sissau mahāpañ‍ñau, pupphanāmau bahussutau|

sāsanaṃ anurakkhantau, jambudīpai patiṭṭhitau||

cūḷābhayau ca maidhāvī, vinayai ca visāradau|

tissatthairau ca maidhāvī, saddhammavaṃsakauvidau||

cūḷadaivau ca maidhāvī, vinayai ca visāradau|

sivatthairau ca maidhāvī, vinayai sabbattha kauvidau||

aitai nāgā mahāpañ‍ñā, vinayañ‍ñū maggakauvidā|

vinayaṃ dīpai pakāsaisuṃ, piṭakaṃ tambapaṇṇiyāti||

23. yaṃ taina bhagavatā jānatā passatā arahatā sammāsambuddhaina dutiyau aniyatau kattha pañ‍ñattauti? sāvatthiyaṃ pañ‍ñattau| kaṃ ārabbhāti? āyasmantaṃ udāyiṃ ārabbha| kismiṃ vatthusminti? āyasmā udāyī mātugāmaina saddhiṃ aikau aikāya rahau nisaj‍jaṃ kappaisi, tasmiṃ vatthusmiṃ| atthi tattha pañ‍ñatti, anupañ‍ñatti anuppan‍napañ‍ñattīti? aikā pañ‍ñatti| anupañ‍ñatti  anuppan‍napañ‍ñatti tasmiṃ natthi| sabbatthapañ‍ñatti, padaisapañ‍ñattīti? sabbatthapañ‍ñatti| sādhāraṇapañ‍ñatti asādhāraṇapañ‍ñattīti? asādhāraṇapañ‍ñatti| aikataupañ‍ñatti ubhataupañ‍ñattīti? aikataupañ‍ñatti| pañ‍can‍naṃ pātimaukkhuddaisānaṃ katthaugadhaṃ kattha pariyāpan‍nanti? nidānaugadhaṃ nidānapariyāpan‍naṃ| katamaina uddaisaina uddaisaṃ āgacchatīti? catutthaina uddaisaina uddaisaṃ āgacchati| catun‍naṃ vipattīnaṃ katamā vipattīti? siyā sīlavipatti, siyā ācāravipatti| sattan‍naṃ āpattikkhandhānaṃ katamau āpattikkhandhauti? siyā  saṅghādisaisāpattikkhandhau, siyā pācittiyāpattikkhandhau| chan‍naṃ āpattisamuṭṭhānānaṃ katihi samuṭṭhānaihi samuṭṭhātīti? tīhi samuṭṭhānaihi samuṭṭhāti – siyā kāyatau ca cittatau ca samuṭṭhāti, na vācatau; siyā vācatau ca cittatau ca samuṭṭhāti, na kāyatau; siyā kāyatau ca vācatau ca cittatau ca samuṭṭhāti| catun‍naṃ adhikaraṇānaṃ katamaṃ adhikaraṇanti? āpattādhikaraṇaṃ| sattan‍naṃ samathānaṃ katihi samathaihi sammatīti? tīhi samathaihi sammati – siyā  sammukhāvinayaina ca paṭiñ‍ñātakaraṇaina ca, siyā sammukhāvinayaina ca tiṇavatthārakaina ca…pai॰…|

dvai aniyatā niṭṭhitā|

tassuddānaṃ –

alaṅkammaniyañ‍caiva, tathaiva ca na haiva khau|

aniyatā supañ‍ñattā, buddhasaiṭṭhaina tādināti||

4. nissaggiyakaṇḍaṃ

1. kathinavaggau

24. yaṃ taina bhagavatā jānatā passatā arahatā sammāsambuddhaina atiraikacīvaraṃ dasāhaṃ atik‍kāmaintassa nissaggiyaṃ pācittiyaṃ kattha pañ‍ñattanti? vaisāliyaṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ| kaṃ ārabbhāti? chabbaggiyai bhikkhū ārabbha| kismiṃ vatthusminti? chabbaggiyā bhikkhū atiraikacīvaraṃ dhāraisuṃ, tasmiṃ vatthusmiṃ| aikā pañ‍ñatti, aikā anupañ‍ñatti| chan‍naṃ āpattisamuṭṭhānānaṃ dvīhi samuṭṭhānaihi  samuṭṭhāti  – siyā kāyatau ca vācatau ca samuṭṭhāti, na cittatau; siyā kāyatau ca vācatau ca cittatau ca samuṭṭhāti…pai॰…|

25. aikarattaṃ ticīvaraina vippavasantassa nissaggiyaṃ pācittiyaṃ kattha pañ‍ñattanti? sāvatthiyaṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ| kaṃ ārabbhāti? sambahulai bhikkhū ārabbha| kismiṃ vatthusminti? sambahulā bhikkhū bhikkhūnaṃ hatthai cīvaraṃ nikkhipitvā santaruttaraina janapadacārikaṃ pak‍kamiṃsu, tasmiṃ vatthusmiṃ| aikā pañ‍ñatti, aikā anupañ‍ñatti| chan‍naṃ āpattisamuṭṭhānānaṃ dvīhi samuṭṭhānaihi samuṭṭhāti – siyā kāyatau ca vācatau ca samuṭṭhāti, na cittatau; siyā kāyatau ca vācatau ca cittatau ca samuṭṭhāti…pai॰…|

26. akālacīvaraṃ paṭiggahaitvā māsaṃ atik‍kāmaintassa nissaggiyaṃ pācittiyaṃ kattha pañ‍ñattanti? sāvatthiyaṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ| kaṃ ārabbhāti? sambahulai bhikkhū ārabbha| kismiṃ vatthusminti? sambahulā bhikkhū akālacīvaraṃ paṭiggahaitvā māsaṃ atik‍kāmaisuṃ, tasmiṃ vatthusmiṃ| aikā pañ‍ñatti, aikā anupañ‍ñatti| chan‍naṃ āpattisamuṭṭhānānaṃ dvīhi samuṭṭhānaihi samuṭṭhāti – siyā kāyatau ca vācatau ca samuṭṭhāti, na cittatau; siyā kāyatau ca vācatau ca cittatau ca samuṭṭhāti…pai॰…|

27. añ‍ñātikāya bhikkhuniyā purāṇacīvaraṃ dhauvāpaintassa  nissaggiyaṃ pācittiyaṃ kattha pañ‍ñattanti? sāvatthiyaṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ| kaṃ ārabbhāti? āyasmantaṃ udāyiṃ ārabbha| kismiṃ vatthusminti? āyasmā  udāyī añ‍ñātikāya bhikkhuniyā purāṇacīvaraṃ dhauvāpaisi, tasmiṃ vatthusmiṃ| aikā pañ‍ñatti| chan‍naṃ āpattisamuṭṭhānānaṃ chahi samuṭṭhānaihi samuṭṭhāti…pai॰…|

28. añ‍ñātikāya bhikkhuniyā hatthatau cīvaraṃ paṭiggaṇhantassa nissaggiyaṃ pācittiyaṃ kattha pañ‍ñattanti? rājagahai pañ‍ñattaṃ| kaṃ ārabbhāti? āyasmantaṃ udāyiṃ ārabbha| kismiṃ vatthusminti? āyasmā udāyī añ‍ñātikāya bhikkhuniyā hatthatau cīvaraṃ paṭiggahaisi, tasmiṃ vatthusmiṃ| aikā pañ‍ñatti aikā anupañ‍ñatti| chan‍naṃ āpattisamuṭṭhānānaṃ chahi samuṭṭhānaihi samuṭṭhāti…pai॰…|

29. añ‍ñātakaṃ  gahapatiṃ vā gahapatāniṃ vā cīvaraṃ viñ‍ñāpaintassa nissaggiyaṃ pācittiyaṃ kattha pañ‍ñattanti? sāvatthiyaṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ| kaṃ ārabbhāti? āyasmantaṃ upanandaṃ sakyaputtaṃ ārabbha| kismiṃ vatthusminti? āyasmā upanandau sakyaputtau añ‍ñātakaṃ saiṭṭhiputtaṃ cīvaraṃ viñ‍ñāpaisi, tasmiṃ vatthusmiṃ| aikā pañ‍ñatti aikā anupañ‍ñatti| chan‍naṃ āpattisamuṭṭhānānaṃ chahi samuṭṭhānaihi samuṭṭhāti…pai॰…|

30. añ‍ñātakaṃ gahapatiṃ vā gahapatāniṃ vā tatuttari cīvaraṃ viñ‍ñāpaintassa nissaggiyaṃ pācittiyaṃ kattha pañ‍ñattanti? sāvatthiyaṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ| kaṃ ārabbhāti? chabbaggiyai bhikkhū ārabbha| kismiṃ vatthusminti? chabbaggiyā bhikkhū na mattaṃ jānitvā bahuṃ cīvaraṃ  viñ‍ñāpaisuṃ, tasmiṃ vatthusmiṃ| aikā pañ‍ñatti| chan‍naṃ āpattisamuṭṭhānānaṃ chahi samuṭṭhānaihi samuṭṭhāti…pai॰…|

31. pubbai appavāritassa añ‍ñātakaṃ gahapatikaṃ upasaṅkamitvā cīvarai vikappaṃ āpaj‍jantassa nissaggiyaṃ pācittiyaṃ kattha pañ‍ñattanti? sāvatthiyaṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ| kaṃ ārabbhāti? āyasmantaṃ upanandaṃ sakyaputtaṃ ārabbha| kismiṃ vatthusminti? āyasmā upanandau  sakyaputtau pubbai appavāritau añ‍ñātakaṃ gahapatikaṃ upasaṅkamitvā cīvarai vikappaṃ āpaj‍ji, tasmiṃ vatthusmiṃ| aikā pañ‍ñatti| chan‍naṃ āpattisamuṭṭhānānaṃ chahi samuṭṭhānaihi samuṭṭhāti…pai॰…|

32. pubbai appavāritassa añ‍ñātakai gahapatikai upasaṅkamitvā cīvarai vikappaṃ āpaj‍jantassa nissaggiyaṃ pācittiyaṃ kattha pañ‍ñattanti? sāvatthiyaṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ| kaṃ ārabbhāti? āyasmantaṃ upanandaṃ sakyaputtaṃ ārabbha| kismiṃ vatthusminti? āyasmā upanandau sakyaputtau pubbai appavāritau añ‍ñātakai gahapatikai upasaṅkamitvā cīvarai vikappaṃ āpaj‍ji, tasmiṃ vatthusmiṃ| aikā pañ‍ñatti| chan‍naṃ āpattisamuṭṭhānānaṃ chahi samuṭṭhānaihi samuṭṭhāti…pai॰…|

33. atiraikatikkhattuṃ caudanāya atiraikachakkhattuṃ ṭhānaina cīvaraṃ abhinipphādaintassa nissaggiyaṃ pācittiyaṃ kattha pañ‍ñattanti? sāvatthiyaṃ  pañ‍ñattaṃ| kaṃ ārabbhāti? āyasmantaṃ upanandaṃ sakyaputtaṃ ārabbha| kismiṃ vatthusminti? āyasmā upanandau sakyaputtau upāsakaina – ‘‘aj‍jaṇhau, bhantai, āgamaihī’’ti  vuc‍camānau nāgamaisi, tasmiṃ vatthusmiṃ| aikā pañ‍ñatti| chan‍naṃ āpattisamuṭṭhānānaṃ chahi samuṭṭhānaihi samuṭṭhāti…pai॰…|

kathinavaggau paṭhamau|

2. kausiyavaggau

34. kausiyamissakaṃ santhataṃ kārāpaintassa nissaggiyaṃ pācittiyaṃ kattha pañ‍ñattanti? āḷaviyaṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ| kaṃ ārabbhāti? chabbaggiyai bhikkhū ārabbha| kismiṃ vatthusminti? chabbaggiyā bhikkhū kausiyakārakai upasaṅkamitvā aivamāhaṃsu ‘‘bahū, āvusau, kausakārakai pacatha| amhākampi dassatha| mayampi icchāma kausiyamissakaṃ santhataṃ kātu’’nti, tasmiṃ vatthusmiṃ| aikā pañ‍ñatti| chan‍naṃ āpattisamuṭṭhānānaṃ chahi samuṭṭhānaihi samuṭṭhāti…pai॰…|

35. suddhakāḷakānaṃ aiḷakalaumānaṃ santhataṃ kārāpaintassa nissaggiyaṃ pācittiyaṃ kattha pañ‍ñattanti? vaisāliyaṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ| kaṃ ārabbhāti? chabbaggiyai bhikkhū ārabbha| kismiṃ vatthusminti? chabbaggiyā bhikkhū suddhakāḷakānaṃ aiḷakalaumānaṃ santhataṃ kārāpaisuṃ, tasmiṃ vatthusmiṃ| aikā pañ‍ñatti| chan‍naṃ āpattisamuṭṭhānānaṃ chahi samuṭṭhānaihi samuṭṭhāti…pai॰…|

36. anādiyitvā  tulaṃ audātānaṃ tulaṃ gaucariyānaṃ navaṃ santhataṃ kārāpaintassa nissaggiyaṃ pācittiyaṃ kattha pañ‍ñattanti? sāvatthiyaṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ| kaṃ ārabbhāti? chabbaggiyai bhikkhū ārabbha| kismiṃ vatthusminti? chabbaggiyā bhikkhū thaukañ‍ñaiva audātaṃ antai audātānaṃ antai antai (sī॰), audātānaṃ antai (syā॰) ādiyitvā tathaiva suddhakāḷakānaṃ aiḷakalaumānaṃ santhataṃ kārāpaisuṃ, tasmiṃ vatthusmiṃ| aikā pañ‍ñatti| chan‍naṃ āpattisamuṭṭhānānaṃ chahi samuṭṭhānaihi samuṭṭhāti…pai॰…|

37. anuvassaṃ santhataṃ kārāpaintassa nissaggiyaṃ pācittiyaṃ kattha pañ‍ñattanti? sāvatthiyaṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ| kaṃ ārabbhāti? sambahulai bhikkhū ārabbha| kismiṃ vatthusminti? sambahulā bhikkhū anuvassaṃ santhataṃ kārāpaisuṃ, tasmiṃ vatthusmiṃ| aikā pañ‍ñatti, aikā anupañ‍ñatti| chan‍naṃ āpattisamuṭṭhānānaṃ chahi samuṭṭhānaihi samuṭṭhāti…pai॰…|

38. anādiyitvā purāṇasanthatassa sāmantā sugatavidatthiṃ navaṃ nisīdanasanthataṃ kārāpaintassa nissaggiyaṃ pācittiyaṃ kattha pañ‍ñattanti? sāvatthiyaṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ| kaṃ ārabbhāti? sambahulai bhikkhū ārabbha| kismiṃ vatthusminti? sambahulā bhikkhū santhatāni ujjhitvā ārañ‍ñikaṅgaṃ piṇḍapātikaṅgaṃ paṃsukūlikaṅgaṃ samādiyiṃsu, tasmiṃ vatthusmiṃ| aikā pañ‍ñatti| chan‍naṃ āpattisamuṭṭhānānaṃ chahi samuṭṭhānaihi samuṭṭhāti…pai॰…|

39. aiḷakalaumāni paṭiggahaitvā tiyaujanaṃ atik‍kāmaintassa nissaggiyaṃ pācittiyaṃ kattha pañ‍ñattanti? sāvatthiyaṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ| kaṃ ārabbhāti? añ‍ñataraṃ bhikkhuṃ ārabbha| kismiṃ vatthusminti? añ‍ñatarau bhikkhu aiḷakalaumāni paṭiggahaitvā tiyaujanaṃ atik‍kāmaisi, tasmiṃ vatthusmiṃ| aikā pañ‍ñatti| chan‍naṃ āpattisamuṭṭhānānaṃ dvīhi  samuṭṭhānaihi samuṭṭhāti – siyā kāyatau samuṭṭhāti, na vācatau na cittatau; siyā kāyatau ca cittatau ca samuṭṭhāti, na vācatau…pai॰…|

40. añ‍ñātikāya bhikkhuniyā aiḷakalaumāni dhauvāpaintassa nissaggiyaṃ pācittiyaṃ kattha pañ‍ñattanti? sak‍kaisu pañ‍ñattaṃ| kaṃ ārabbhāti? chabbaggiyai bhikkhū ārabbha| kismiṃ vatthusminti? chabbaggiyā bhikkhū añ‍ñātikāhi bhikkhunīhi aiḷakalaumāni dhauvāpaisuṃ, tasmiṃ vatthusmiṃ| aikā pañ‍ñatti| chan‍naṃ āpattisamuṭṭhānānaṃ chahi samuṭṭhānaihi samuṭṭhāti…pai॰…|

41. rūpiyaṃ paṭiggaṇhantassa nissaggiyaṃ pācittiyaṃ kattha pañ‍ñattanti? rājagahai pañ‍ñattaṃ| kaṃ ārabbhāti? āyasmantaṃ upanandaṃ  sakyaputtaṃ ārabbha| kismiṃ vatthusminti? āyasmā upanandau sakyaputtau rūpiyaṃ paṭiggahaisi, tasmiṃ vatthusmiṃ| aikā pañ‍ñatti| chan‍naṃ āpattisamuṭṭhānānaṃ chahi samuṭṭhānaihi samuṭṭhāti…pai॰…|

42. nānappakārakaṃ rūpiyasaṃvauhāraṃ samāpaj‍jantassa nissaggiyaṃ pācittiyaṃ kattha pañ‍ñattanti? sāvatthiyaṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ| kaṃ ārabbhāti? chabbaggiyai bhikkhū  ārabbha| kismiṃ vatthusminti? chabbaggiyā bhikkhū nānappakārakaṃ rūpiyasaṃvauhāraṃ samāpaj‍jiṃsu, tasmiṃ vatthusmiṃ| aikā pañ‍ñatti| chan‍naṃ āpattisamuṭṭhānānaṃ chahi samuṭṭhānaihi samuṭṭhāti…pai॰…|

43. nānappakārakaṃ kayavik‍kayaṃ samāpaj‍jantassa nissaggiyaṃ pācittiyaṃ kattha pañ‍ñattanti? sāvatthiyaṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ| kaṃ ārabbhāti? āyasmantaṃ upanandaṃ sakyaputtaṃ ārabbha| kismiṃ vatthusminti? āyasmā upanandau sakyaputtau paribbājakaina saddhiṃ kayavik‍kayaṃ samāpaj‍ji, tasmiṃ vatthusmiṃ| aikā pañ‍ñatti| chan‍naṃ āpattisamuṭṭhānānaṃ chahi samuṭṭhānaihi samuṭṭhāti…pai॰…|

kausiyavaggau dutiyau|

3. pattavaggau

44. atiraikapattaṃ  dasāhaṃ atik‍kāmaintassa nissaggiyaṃ pācittiyaṃ kattha pañ‍ñattanti? sāvatthiyaṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ| kaṃ ārabbhāti? chabbaggiyai bhikkhū ārabbha| kismiṃ vatthusminti? chabbaggiyā bhikkhū atiraikapattaṃ dhāraisuṃ, tasmiṃ vatthusmiṃ| aikā pañ‍ñatti, aikā anupañ‍ñatti| chan‍naṃ āpattisamuṭṭhānānaṃ dvīhi samuṭṭhānaihi samuṭṭhāti – siyā kāyatau ca vācatau ca samuṭṭhāti, na cittatau; siyā kāyatau ca vācatau ca cittatau ca samuṭṭhāti…pai॰…|

45. ūnapañ‍cabandhanaina pattaina añ‍ñaṃ navaṃ pattaṃ caitāpaintassa nissaggiyaṃ pācittiyaṃ kattha pañ‍ñattanti? sak‍kaisu pañ‍ñattaṃ| kaṃ ārabbhāti? chabbaggiyai bhikkhū ārabbha| kismiṃ vatthusminti? chabbaggiyā bhikkhū appamattakainapi bhin‍naina appamattakainapi khaṇḍaina vilikhitamattainapi bahū pattai viñ‍ñāpaisuṃ, tasmiṃ vatthusmiṃ| aikā pañ‍ñatti| chan‍naṃ āpattisamuṭṭhānānaṃ chahi samuṭṭhānaihi samuṭṭhāti…pai॰…|

46. bhaisaj‍jāni  paṭiggahaitvā sattāhaṃ atik‍kāmaintassa nissaggiyaṃ pācittiyaṃ kattha pañ‍ñattanti? sāvatthiyaṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ| kaṃ ārabbhāti? sambahulai bhikkhū  ārabbha| kismiṃ vatthusminti? sambahulā bhikkhū bhaisaj‍jāni paṭiggahaitvā sattāhaṃ atik‍kāmaisuṃ, tasmiṃ vatthusmiṃ| aikā pañ‍ñatti| chan‍naṃ āpattisamuṭṭhānānaṃ dvīhi samuṭṭhānaihi samuṭṭhāti kathinakai…pai॰…|

47. atiraikamāsai  saisai gimhānai vassikasāṭikacīvaraṃ pariyaisantassa nissaggiyaṃ pācittiyaṃ kattha pañ‍ñattanti? sāvatthiyaṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ| kaṃ ārabbhāti? chabbaggiyai bhikkhū ārabbha| kismiṃ vatthusminti? chabbaggiyā bhikkhū atiraikamāsai saisai gimhānai vassikasāṭikacīvaraṃ pariyaisiṃsu, tasmiṃ vatthusmiṃ| aikā pañ‍ñatti| chan‍naṃ āpattisamuṭṭhānānaṃ chahi samuṭṭhānaihi samuṭṭhāti…pai॰…|

48. bhikkhussa sāmaṃ cīvaraṃ datvā kupitaina anattamanaina acchindantassa nissaggiyaṃ pācittiyaṃ kattha pañ‍ñattanti? sāvatthiyaṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ| kaṃ ārabbhāti? āyasmantaṃ upanandaṃ sakyaputtaṃ ārabbha| kismiṃ vatthusminti? āyasmā upanandau sakyaputtau bhikkhussa sāmaṃ cīvaraṃ datvā kupitau anattamanau acchindi, tasmiṃ vatthusmiṃ| aikā pañ‍ñatti| chan‍naṃ āpattisamuṭṭhānānaṃ tīhi samuṭṭhānaihi samuṭṭhāti…pai॰…|

49. sāmaṃ suttaṃ viñ‍ñāpaitvā tantavāyaihi cīvaraṃ vāyāpaintassa nissaggiyaṃ pācittiyaṃ kattha pañ‍ñattanti? rājagahai pañ‍ñattaṃ| kaṃ ārabbhāti? chabbaggiyai bhikkhū ārabbha| kismiṃ vatthusminti? chabbaggiyā bhikkhū sāmaṃ suttaṃ viñ‍ñāpaitvā tantavāyaihi cīvaraṃ vāyāpaisuṃ, tasmiṃ vatthusmiṃ| aikā pañ‍ñatti| chan‍naṃ āpattisamuṭṭhānānaṃ chahi samuṭṭhānaihi samuṭṭhāti…pai॰…|

50. pubbai appavāritassa añ‍ñātakassa gahapatikassa tantavāyai upasaṅkamitvā  cīvarai vikappaṃ āpaj‍jantassa nissaggiyaṃ pācittiyaṃ kattha pañ‍ñattanti? sāvatthiyaṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ| kaṃ ārabbhāti? āyasmantaṃ upanandaṃ sakyaputtaṃ ārabbha| kismiṃ vatthusminti? āyasmā upanandau sakyaputtau pubbai appavāritau añ‍ñātakassa gahapatikassa tantavāyai upasaṅkamitvā cīvarai vikappaṃ āpaj‍ji, tasmiṃ vatthusmiṃ| aikā pañ‍ñatti| chan‍naṃ āpattisamuṭṭhānānaṃ chahi samuṭṭhānaihi samuṭṭhāti…pai॰…|

51. ac‍caikacīvaraṃ  paṭiggahaitvā cīvarakālasamayaṃ atik‍kāmaintassa nissaggiyaṃ pācittiyaṃ kattha pañ‍ñattanti? sāvatthiyaṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ| kaṃ ārabbhāti? sambahulai bhikkhū ārabbha| kismiṃ vatthusminti? sambahulā bhikkhū ac‍caikacīvaraṃ paṭiggahaitvā cīvarakālasamayaṃ atik‍kāmaisuṃ, tasmiṃ vatthusmiṃ| aikā pañ‍ñatti| chan‍naṃ āpattisamuṭṭhānānaṃ dvīhi samuṭṭhānaihi samuṭṭhāti kathinakai…pai॰…|

52. tiṇṇaṃ  cīvarānaṃ añ‍ñataraṃ cīvaraṃ antaragharai nikkhipitvā atiraikachārattaṃ vippavasantassa nissaggiyaṃ pācittiyaṃ kattha pañ‍ñattanti? sāvatthiyaṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ| kaṃ ārabbhāti? sambahulai bhikkhū ārabbha| kismiṃ vatthusminti? sambahulā bhikkhū tiṇṇaṃ cīvarānaṃ añ‍ñataraṃ cīvaraṃ antaragharai nikkhipitvā atiraikachārattaṃ vippavasiṃsu, tasmiṃ vatthusmiṃ| aikā  pañ‍ñatti| chan‍naṃ āpattisamuṭṭhānānaṃ dvīhi samuṭṭhānaihi samuṭṭhāti kathinakai…pai॰…|

53. jānaṃ  saṅghikaṃ lābhaṃ pariṇataṃ attanau pariṇāmaintassa nissaggiyaṃ pācittiyaṃ kattha pañ‍ñattanti? sāvatthiyaṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ| kaṃ ārabbhāti? chabbaggiyai bhikkhū ārabbha| kismiṃ vatthusminti? chabbaggiyā bhikkhū jānaṃ saṅghikaṃ lābhaṃ pariṇataṃ attanau pariṇāmaisuṃ, tasmiṃ vatthusmiṃ| aikā pañ‍ñatti| chan‍naṃ āpattisamuṭṭhānānaṃ tīhi samuṭṭhānaihi samuṭṭhāti…pai॰…|

pattavaggau tatiyau|

tiṃsa nissaggiyā pācittiyā niṭṭhitā|

tassuddānaṃ –

dasaikarattimāsau ca, dhauvanañ‍ca paṭiggahau|

añ‍ñātaṃ tañ‍ca añ‍ñātakañ‍ca (ka॰) uddissa, ubhin‍naṃ dūtakaina ca||

kausiyā suddhadvaibhāgā, chabbassāni nisīdanaṃ|

dvai ca laumāni uggaṇhai, ubhau nānappakārakā||

dvai ca pattāni bhaisaj‍jaṃ, vassikā dānapañ‍camaṃ|

sāmaṃ vāyāpanac‍caikau, sāsaṅkaṃ saṅghikaina cāti||

5. pācittiyakaṇḍaṃ

1. musāvādavaggau

54. yaṃ  taina bhagavatā jānatā passatā arahatā sammāsambuddhaina sampajānamusāvādai pācittiyaṃ kattha pañ‍ñattanti? sāvatthiyaṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ| kaṃ ārabbhāti? hatthakaṃ sakyaputtaṃ ārabbha| kismiṃ vatthusminti? āyasmā hatthakau sakyaputtau titthiyaihi saddhiṃ sal‍lapantau avajānitvā paṭijāni, paṭijānitvā avajāni, tasmiṃ vatthusmiṃ| aikā pañ‍ñatti| chan‍naṃ āpattisamuṭṭhānānaṃ tīhi samuṭṭhānaihi samuṭṭhāti – siyā kāyatau ca cittatau ca samuṭṭhāti, na vācatau; siyā vācatau ca cittatau ca samuṭṭhāti, na kāyatau; siyā kāyatau ca vācatau ca cittatau ca samuṭṭhāti…pai॰…|

55. aumasavādai pācittiyaṃ kattha pañ‍ñattanti? sāvatthiyaṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ| kaṃ ārabbhāti? chabbaggiyai bhikkhū ārabbha| kismiṃ vatthusminti? chabbaggiyā bhikkhū paisalaihi bhikkhūhi saddhiṃ bhaṇḍantā bhaṇḍaintā (ka॰) paisalai bhikkhū aumasiṃsu, tasmiṃ vatthusmiṃ| aikā pañ‍ñatti| chan‍naṃ āpattisamuṭṭhānānaṃ tīhi samuṭṭhānaihi samuṭṭhāti…pai॰…|

56. bhikkhupaisuñ‍ñai pācittiyaṃ kattha pañ‍ñattanti? sāvatthiyaṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ| kaṃ ārabbhāti? chabbaggiyai bhikkhū ārabbha| kismiṃ vatthusminti? chabbaggiyā bhikkhū bhikkhūnaṃ bhaṇḍanajātānaṃ kalahajātānaṃ vivādāpan‍nānaṃ paisuñ‍ñaṃ upasaṃhariṃsu, tasmiṃ vatthusmiṃ| aikā pañ‍ñatti| chan‍naṃ āpattisamuṭṭhānānaṃ tīhi samuṭṭhānaihi samuṭṭhāti…pai॰…|

57. anupasampan‍naṃ padasau dhammaṃ vācaintassa pācittiyaṃ kattha pañ‍ñattanti? sāvatthiyaṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ| kaṃ ārabbhāti? chabbaggiyai bhikkhū  ārabbha| kismiṃ vatthusminti? chabbaggiyā bhikkhū upāsakai padasau dhammaṃ vācaisuṃ, tasmiṃ vatthusmiṃ| aikā pañ‍ñatti| chan‍naṃ āpattisamuṭṭhānānaṃ dvīhi samuṭṭhānaihi samuṭṭhāti – siyā vācatau samuṭṭhāti, na kāyatau na cittatau; siyā vācatau ca cittatau ca samuṭṭhāti, na kāyatau…pai॰…|

58. anupasampan‍naina uttaridirattatirattaṃ sahasaiyyaṃ kappaintassa pācittiyaṃ kattha pañ‍ñattanti? āḷaviyaṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ| kaṃ ārabbhāti? sambahulai bhikkhū ārabbha| kismiṃ vatthusminti? sambahulā bhikkhū anupasampan‍naina sahasaiyyaṃ kappaisuṃ, tasmiṃ vatthusmiṃ| aikā pañ‍ñatti, aikā anupañ‍ñatti| chan‍naṃ  āpattisamuṭṭhānānaṃ dvīhi samuṭṭhānaihi samuṭṭhāti – siyā kāyatau samuṭṭhāti, na vācatau na cittatau; siyā kāyatau ca cittatau ca samuṭṭhāti, na vācatau…pai॰…|

59. mātugāmaina sahasaiyyaṃ kappaintassa pācittiyaṃ kattha pañ‍ñattanti? sāvatthiyaṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ| kaṃ ārabbhāti? āyasmantaṃ anuruddhaṃ ārabbha| kismiṃ vatthusminti? āyasmā anuruddhau mātugāmaina sahasaiyyaṃ kappaisi, tasmiṃ vatthusmiṃ| aikā pañ‍ñatti| chan‍naṃ āpattisamuṭṭhānānaṃ dvīhi samuṭṭhānaihi samuṭṭhāti aiḷakalaumakai…pai॰…|

60. mātugāmassa uttarichappañ‍cavācāhi dhammaṃ daisaintassa pācittiyaṃ  kattha pañ‍ñattanti? sāvatthiyaṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ| kaṃ ārabbhāti? āyasmantaṃ udāyiṃ ārabbha| kismiṃ vatthusminti? āyasmā udāyī mātugāmassa dhammaṃ daisaisi, tasmiṃ vatthusmiṃ| aikā pañ‍ñatti, dvai anupañ‍ñattiyau| chan‍naṃ āpattisamuṭṭhānānaṃ dvīhi samuṭṭhānaihi samuṭṭhāti padasaudhammai…pai॰…|

61. anupasampan‍nassa uttarimanussadhammaṃ bhūtaṃ āraucaintassa pācittiyaṃ kattha pañ‍ñattanti? vaisāliyaṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ| kaṃ ārabbhāti? vaggumudātīriyai bhikkhū ārabbha| kismiṃ vatthusminti? vaggumudātīriyā bhikkhū gihīnaṃ añ‍ñamañ‍ñassa uttarimanussadhammassa vaṇṇaṃ bhāsiṃsu, tasmiṃ vatthusmiṃ| aikā pañ‍ñatti| chan‍naṃ āpattisamuṭṭhānānaṃ tīhi samuṭṭhānaihi samuṭṭhāti – siyā kāyatau samuṭṭhāti, na vācatau na cittatau; siyā vācatau samuṭṭhāti, na kāyatau na cittatau; siyā kāyatau ca vācatau ca samuṭṭhāti, na cittatau…pai॰…|

62. bhikkhussa duṭṭhul‍lāpattiṃ anupasampan‍nassa āraucaintassa pācittiyaṃ kattha pañ‍ñattanti? sāvatthiyaṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ| kaṃ ārabbhāti? chabbaggiyai bhikkhū ārabbha| kismiṃ vatthusminti? chabbaggiyā bhikkhū bhikkhussa duṭṭhul‍lāpattiṃ anupasampan‍nassa āraucaisuṃ, tasmiṃ vatthusmiṃ| aikā pañ‍ñatti| chan‍naṃ āpattisamuṭṭhānānaṃ tīhi samuṭṭhānaihi samuṭṭhāti…pai॰…|

63. pathaviṃ  khaṇantassa pācittiyaṃ kattha pañ‍ñattanti? āḷaviyaṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ| kaṃ ārabbhāti? āḷavakai bhikkhū ārabbha| kismiṃ vatthusminti? āḷavakā  bhikkhū pathaviṃ khaṇiṃsu, tasmiṃ vatthusmiṃ| aikā pañ‍ñatti| chan‍naṃ āpattisamuṭṭhānānaṃ tīhi samuṭṭhānaihi samuṭṭhāti…pai॰…|

musāvādavaggau paṭhamau|

2. bhūtagāmavaggau

64. bhūtagāmapātabyatā  pācittiyaṃ kattha pañ‍ñattanti? āḷaviyaṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ| kaṃ ārabbhāti? āḷavakai bhikkhū ārabbha| kismiṃ vatthusminti? āḷavakā bhikkhū rukkhaṃ chindiṃsu, tasmiṃ vatthusmiṃ| aikā pañ‍ñatti| chan‍naṃ āpattisamuṭṭhānānaṃ tīhi samuṭṭhānaihi samuṭṭhāti…pai॰…|

65. añ‍ñavādakai vihaisakai pācittiyaṃ kattha pañ‍ñattanti? kausambiyaṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ| kaṃ ārabbhāti? āyasmantaṃ chan‍naṃ ārabbha| kismiṃ vatthusminti? āyasmā chan‍nau saṅghamajjhai āpattiyā anuyuñ‍jiyamānau añ‍ñainañ‍ñaṃ paṭicari, tasmiṃ vatthusmiṃ| aikā pañ‍ñatti, aikā anupañ‍ñatti| chan‍naṃ āpattisamuṭṭhānānaṃ  tīhi samuṭṭhānaihi samuṭṭhāti…pai॰…|

66. ujjhāpanakai khiyyanakai pācittiyaṃ kattha pañ‍ñattanti? rājagahai pañ‍ñattaṃ| kaṃ ārabbhāti? maittiyabhūmajakai bhikkhū ārabbha| kismiṃ vatthusminti? maittiyabhūmajakā bhikkhū āyasmantaṃ dabbaṃ mal‍laputtaṃ bhikkhū ujjhāpaisuṃ, tasmiṃ vatthusmiṃ| aikā pañ‍ñatti, aikā anupañ‍ñatti| chan‍naṃ āpattisamuṭṭhānānaṃ tīhi samuṭṭhānaihi samuṭṭhāti…pai॰…|

67. saṅghikaṃ mañ‍caṃ vā pīṭhaṃ vā bhisiṃ vā kaucchaṃ vā ajjhaukāsai santharitvā anuddharitvā anāpucchā pak‍kamantassa pācittiyaṃ kattha pañ‍ñattanti? sāvatthiyaṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ| kaṃ ārabbhāti? sambahulai bhikkhū ārabbha| kismiṃ vatthusminti? sambahulā bhikkhū saṅghikaṃ saināsanaṃ ajjhaukāsai santharitvā anuddharitvā anāpucchā pak‍kamiṃsu, tasmiṃ vatthusmiṃ| aikā pañ‍ñatti, aikā anupañ‍ñatti| chan‍naṃ āpattisamuṭṭhānānaṃ dvīhi samuṭṭhānaihi samuṭṭhāti kathinakai…pai॰…|

68. saṅghikai  vihārai saiyyaṃ santharitvā anuddharitvā anāpucchā pak‍kamantassa pācittiyaṃ kattha pañ‍ñattanti? sāvatthiyaṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ| kaṃ ārabbhāti? sattarasavaggiyai bhikkhū ārabbha| kismiṃ vatthusminti? sattarasavaggiyā bhikkhū saṅghikai vihārai saiyyaṃ santharitvā anuddharitvā  anāpucchā pak‍kamiṃsu, tasmiṃ vatthusmiṃ| aikā pañ‍ñatti| chan‍naṃ āpattisamuṭṭhānānaṃ dvīhi samuṭṭhānaihi samuṭṭhāti kathinakai…pai॰…|

69. saṅghikai vihārai jānaṃ pubbupagataṃ bhikkhuṃ anupakhaj‍ja saiyyaṃ kappaintassa pācittiyaṃ kattha pañ‍ñattanti? sāvatthiyaṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ| kaṃ ārabbhāti? chabbaggiyai bhikkhū ārabbha| kismiṃ vatthusminti? chabbaggiyā bhikkhū thairai bhikkhū anupakhaj‍ja saiyyaṃ kappaisuṃ, tasmiṃ vatthusmiṃ| aikā pañ‍ñatti| chan‍naṃ āpattisamuṭṭhānānaṃ aikaina samuṭṭhānaina samuṭṭhāti – kāyatau ca cittatau ca samuṭṭhāti, na vācatau…pai॰…|

70. bhikkhuṃ kupitaina anattamanaina saṅghikā vihārā nik‍kaḍḍhantassa pācittiyaṃ kattha pañ‍ñattanti? sāvatthiyaṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ| kaṃ ārabbhāti? chabbaggiyai bhikkhū ārabbha| kismiṃ vatthusminti? chabbaggiyā bhikkhū kupitā anattamanā bhikkhū saṅghikā vihārā nik‍kaḍḍhiṃsu, tasmiṃ vatthusmiṃ| aikā pañ‍ñatti| chan‍naṃ āpattisamuṭṭhānānaṃ tīhi samuṭṭhānaihi samuṭṭhāti…pai॰…|

71. saṅghikai vihārai uparivaihāsakuṭiyā āhac‍capādakaṃ mañ‍caṃ vā pīṭhaṃ vā abhinisīdantassa pācittiyaṃ kattha pañ‍ñattanti? sāvatthiyaṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ| kaṃ ārabbhāti? añ‍ñataraṃ bhikkhuṃ ārabbha| kismiṃ vatthusminti? añ‍ñatarau bhikkhu saṅghikai vihārai uparivaihāsakuṭiyā āhac‍capādakaṃ mañ‍caṃ sahasā abhinisīdi, tasmiṃ vatthusmiṃ| aikā  pañ‍ñatti| chan‍naṃ āpattisamuṭṭhānānaṃ dvīhi samuṭṭhānaihi samuṭṭhāti – siyā kāyatau samuṭṭhāti, na vācatau na cittatau; siyā kāyatau ca cittatau ca samuṭṭhāti, na vācatau…pai॰…|

72. dvattipariyāyai adhiṭṭhahitvā tatuttari adhiṭṭhahantassa pācittiyaṃ kattha pañ‍ñattanti? kausambiyaṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ| kaṃ ārabbhāti? āyasmantaṃ chan‍naṃ ārabbha| kismiṃ vatthusminti? āyasmā chan‍nau katapariyausitaṃ vihāraṃ punappunaṃ chādāpaisi, punappunaṃ limpāpaisi, atibhārikau vihārau paripati, tasmiṃ vatthusmiṃ| aikā pañ‍ñatti| chan‍naṃ āpattisamuṭṭhānānaṃ chahi samuṭṭhānaihi samuṭṭhāti…pai॰…|

73. jānaṃ sappāṇakaṃ udakaṃ tiṇaṃ vā mattikaṃ vā siñ‍cantassa pācittiyaṃ kattha pañ‍ñattanti? āḷaviyaṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ| kaṃ ārabbhāti? āḷavakai bhikkhū ārabbha| kismiṃ vatthusminti? āḷavakā bhikkhū jānaṃ sappāṇakaṃ udakaṃ tiṇampi mattikampi siñ‍ciṃsu, tasmiṃ vatthusmiṃ| aikā pañ‍ñatti| chan‍naṃ āpattisamuṭṭhānānaṃ tīhi samuṭṭhānaihi samuṭṭhāti…pai॰…|

bhūtagāmavaggau dutiyau|

3. auvādavaggau

74. asammataina  bhikkhuniyau auvadantassa pācittiyaṃ kattha pañ‍ñattanti? sāvatthiyaṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ| kaṃ ārabbhāti? chabbaggiyai bhikkhū ārabbha| kismiṃ vatthusminti? chabbaggiyā bhikkhū asammatā bhikkhuniyau auvadiṃsu, tasmiṃ vatthusmiṃ| atthi tattha pañ‍ñatti, anupañ‍ñatti, anuppan‍napañ‍ñattīti? aikā pañ‍ñatti, aikā anupañ‍ñatti| anuppan‍napañ‍ñatti tasmiṃ natthi| chan‍naṃ āpattisamuṭṭhānānaṃ dvīhi samuṭṭhānaihi samuṭṭhāti – siyā vācatau samuṭṭhāti, na kāyatau na cittatau; siyā vācatau ca cittatau ca samuṭṭhāti, na kāyatau…pai॰…|

75. atthaṅgatai sūriyai bhikkhuniyau auvadantassa pācittiyaṃ kattha pañ‍ñattanti? sāvatthiyaṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ| kaṃ ārabbhāti? āyasmantaṃ cūḷapanthakaṃ ārabbha| kismiṃ vatthusminti? āyasmā cūḷapanthakau atthaṅgatai sūriyai bhikkhuniyau auvadi, tasmiṃ vatthusmiṃ| aikā pañ‍ñatti| chan‍naṃ āpattisamuṭṭhānānaṃ dvīhi samuṭṭhānaihi samuṭṭhāti padasaudhammai…pai॰…|

76. bhikkhunupassayaṃ upasaṅkamitvā bhikkhuniyau auvadantassa pācittiyaṃ kattha pañ‍ñattanti? sak‍kaisu pañ‍ñattaṃ| kaṃ ārabbhāti? chabbaggiyai bhikkhū  ārabbha| kismiṃ vatthusminti? chabbaggiyā bhikkhū bhikkhunupassayaṃ  upasaṅkamitvā bhikkhuniyau auvadiṃsu, tasmiṃ vatthusmiṃ| aikā pañ‍ñatti, aikā anupañ‍ñatti| chan‍naṃ āpattisamuṭṭhānānaṃ dvīhi samuṭṭhānaihi samuṭṭhāti kathinakai…pai॰…|

77. ‘‘āmisahaitu  bhikkhū bhikkhuniyau auvadantī’’ti bhaṇantassa pācittiyaṃ kattha pañ‍ñattanti? sāvatthiyaṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ| kaṃ ārabbhāti? chabbaggiyai bhikkhū ārabbha| kismiṃ vatthusminti? chabbaggiyā bhikkhū ‘‘āmisahaitu bhikkhū bhikkhuniyau auvadantī’’ti bhaṇiṃsu, tasmiṃ vatthusmiṃ| aikā pañ‍ñatti| chan‍naṃ āpattisamuṭṭhānānaṃ tīhi samuṭṭhānaihi samuṭṭhāti…pai॰…|

78. añ‍ñātikāya  bhikkhuniyā cīvaraṃ daintassa pācittiyaṃ kattha pañ‍ñattanti? sāvatthiyaṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ| kaṃ ārabbhāti? añ‍ñataraṃ bhikkhuṃ ārabbha| kismiṃ vatthusminti? añ‍ñatarau bhikkhu añ‍ñātikāya bhikkhuniyā cīvaraṃ adāsi, tasmiṃ vatthusmiṃ| aikā pañ‍ñatti, aikā anupañ‍ñatti| chan‍naṃ āpattisamuṭṭhānānaṃ chahi samuṭṭhānaihi samuṭṭhāti…pai॰…|

79. añ‍ñātikāya bhikkhuniyā cīvaraṃ sibbaintassa pācittiyaṃ kattha pañ‍ñattanti? sāvatthiyaṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ| kaṃ ārabbhāti? āyasmantaṃ udāyiṃ ārabbha| kismiṃ vatthusminti? āyasmā udāyī añ‍ñātikāya bhikkhuniyā cīvaraṃ sibbaisi, tasmiṃ vatthusmiṃ| aikā pañ‍ñatti| chan‍naṃ āpattisamuṭṭhānānaṃ chahi samuṭṭhānaihi samuṭṭhāti  …pai॰…|

80. bhikkhuniyā saddhiṃ saṃvidhāya aikaddhānamaggaṃ paṭipaj‍jantassa pācittiyaṃ kattha pañ‍ñattanti? sāvatthiyaṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ| kaṃ ārabbhāti? chabbaggiyai bhikkhū ārabbha| kismiṃ vatthusminti? chabbaggiyā bhikkhū bhikkhunīhi saddhiṃ saṃvidhāya aikaddhānamaggaṃ paṭipaj‍jiṃsu, tasmiṃ vatthusmiṃ| aikā pañ‍ñatti, aikā anupañ‍ñatti| chan‍naṃ āpattisamuṭṭhānānaṃ catūhi samuṭṭhānaihi samuṭṭhāti – siyā kāyatau samuṭṭhāti, na vācatau na cittatau; siyā kāyatau ca vācatau ca samuṭṭhāti, na cittatau; siyā kāyatau ca cittatau ca samuṭṭhāti, na vācatau; siyā kāyatau ca vācatau ca cittatau ca samuṭṭhāti…pai॰…|

81. bhikkhuniyā saddhiṃ saṃvidhāya aikaṃ nāvaṃ abhiruhantassa pācittiyaṃ kattha pañ‍ñattanti? sāvatthiyaṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ| kaṃ ārabbhāti? chabbaggiyai bhikkhū ārabbha| kismiṃ vatthusminti? chabbaggiyā bhikkhū bhikkhunīhi saddhiṃ saṃvidhāya aikaṃ nāvaṃ abhiruhiṃsu, tasmiṃ vatthusmiṃ| aikā pañ‍ñatti, aikā anupañ‍ñatti| chan‍naṃ āpattisamuṭṭhānānaṃ catūhi samuṭṭhānaihi samuṭṭhāti…pai॰…|

82. jānaṃ  bhikkhuniparipācitaṃ piṇḍapātaṃ bhuñ‍jantassa pācittiyaṃ kattha pañ‍ñattanti? rājagahai pañ‍ñattaṃ| kaṃ ārabbhāti? daivadattaṃ ārabbha| kismiṃ vatthusminti? daivadattau jānaṃ bhikkhuniparipācitaṃ  piṇḍapātaṃ bhuñ‍ji, tasmiṃ vatthusmiṃ| aikā pañ‍ñatti, aikā anupañ‍ñatti| chan‍naṃ āpattisamuṭṭhānānaṃ aikaina samuṭṭhānaina samuṭṭhāti – kāyatau ca cittatau ca samuṭṭhāti, na vācatau…pai॰…|

83. bhikkhuniyā  saddhiṃ aikau aikāya rahau nisaj‍jaṃ kappaintassa pācittiyaṃ kattha pañ‍ñattanti? sāvatthiyaṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ| kaṃ ārabbhāti? āyasmantaṃ udāyiṃ ārabbha| kismiṃ vatthusminti? āyasmā udāyī bhikkhuniyā saddhiṃ aikau aikāya rahau nisaj‍jaṃ kappaisi, tasmiṃ vatthusmiṃ| aikā pañ‍ñatti| chan‍naṃ āpattisamuṭṭhānānaṃ aikaina samuṭṭhānaina samuṭṭhāti – kāyatau ca cittatau ca samuṭṭhāti, na vācatau…pai॰…|

auvādavaggau tatiyau|

4. bhaujanavaggau

84. tatuttari āvasathapiṇḍaṃ bhuñ‍jantassa pācittiyaṃ kattha pañ‍ñattanti? sāvatthiyaṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ| kaṃ ārabbhāti? chabbaggiyai bhikkhū  ārabbha| kismiṃ vatthusminti? chabbaggiyā bhikkhū anuvasitvā anuvasitvā āvasathapiṇḍaṃ bhuñ‍jiṃsu, tasmiṃ vatthusmiṃ| aikā pañ‍ñatti, aikā anupañ‍ñatti| chan‍naṃ āpattisamuṭṭhānānaṃ dvīhi samuṭṭhānaihi samuṭṭhāti aiḷakalaumakai…pai॰…|

85. gaṇabhaujanai pācittiyaṃ kattha pañ‍ñattanti? rājagahai pañ‍ñattaṃ| kaṃ ārabbhāti? daivadattaṃ ārabbha| kismiṃ vatthusminti? daivadattau saparisau kulaisu viñ‍ñāpaitvā viñ‍ñāpaitvā bhuñ‍ji, tasmiṃ vatthusmiṃ| aikā pañ‍ñatti, satta anupañ‍ñattiyau| chan‍naṃ āpattisamuṭṭhānānaṃ dvīhi samuṭṭhānaihi samuṭṭhāti aiḷakalaumakai…pai॰…|

86. paramparabhaujanai pācittiyaṃ kattha pañ‍ñattanti? vaisāliyaṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ| kaṃ ārabbhāti? sambahulai bhikkhū ārabbha| kismiṃ vatthusminti? sambahulā bhikkhū  añ‍ñatra nimantitā añ‍ñatra bhuñ‍jiṃsu, tasmiṃ vatthusmiṃ| aikā pañ‍ñatti, catassau anupañ‍ñattiyau| chan‍naṃ āpattisamuṭṭhānānaṃ dvīhi samuṭṭhānaihi samuṭṭhāti kathinakai…pai॰…|

87. dvattipattapūrai pūvai paṭiggahaitvā tatuttari paṭiggaṇhantassa pācittiyaṃ kattha pañ‍ñattanti? sāvatthiyaṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ| kaṃ ārabbhāti? sambahulai bhikkhū ārabbha| kismiṃ vatthusminti? sambahulā bhikkhū na mattaṃ jānitvā paṭiggahaisuṃ, tasmiṃ vatthusmiṃ| aikā  pañ‍ñatti| chan‍naṃ āpattisamuṭṭhānānaṃ chahi samuṭṭhānaihi samuṭṭhāti…pai॰…|

88. bhuttāvinā  pavāritaina anatirittaṃ khādanīyaṃ vā bhaujanīyaṃ vā bhuñ‍jantassa pācittiyaṃ kattha pañ‍ñattanti? sāvatthiyaṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ| kaṃ ārabbhāti? sambahulai bhikkhū ārabbha| kismiṃ vatthusminti? sambahulā bhikkhū bhuttāvī pavāritā añ‍ñatra bhuñ‍jiṃsu, tasmiṃ vatthusmiṃ| aikā pañ‍ñatti, aikā anupañ‍ñatti| chan‍naṃ āpattisamuṭṭhānānaṃ dvīhi samuṭṭhānaihi samuṭṭhāti kathinakai…pai॰…|

89. bhikkhuṃ bhuttāviṃ pavāritaṃ anatirittaina khādanīyaina vā bhaujanīyaina vā abhihaṭṭhuṃ pavāraintassa pācittiyaṃ kattha pañ‍ñattanti? sāvatthiyaṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ| kaṃ ārabbhāti? añ‍ñataraṃ bhikkhuṃ ārabbha| kismiṃ vatthusminti? añ‍ñatarau bhikkhu bhikkhuṃ bhuttāviṃ pavāritaṃ anatirittaina bhaujanīyaina abhihaṭṭhuṃ pavāraisi, tasmiṃ vatthusmiṃ| aikā pañ‍ñatti| chan‍naṃ āpattisamuṭṭhānānaṃ tīhi samuṭṭhānaihi samuṭṭhāti…pai॰…|

90. vikālai  khādanīyaṃ vā bhaujanīyaṃ vā bhuñ‍jantassa pācittiyaṃ kattha pañ‍ñattanti? rājagahai pañ‍ñattaṃ| kaṃ ārabbhāti? sattarasavaggiyai bhikkhū ārabbha| kismiṃ vatthusminti? sattarasavaggiyā bhikkhū vikālai bhaujanaṃ bhuñ‍jiṃsu, tasmiṃ vatthusmiṃ| aikā pañ‍ñatti| chan‍naṃ āpattisamuṭṭhānānaṃ dvīhi samuṭṭhānaihi samuṭṭhāti aiḷakalaumakai…pai॰…|

91. san‍nidhikārakaṃ khādanīyaṃ vā bhaujanīyaṃ vā bhuñ‍jantassa pācittiyaṃ kattha pañ‍ñattanti? sāvatthiyaṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ| kaṃ ārabbhāti? āyasmantaṃ bailaṭṭhasīsaṃ ārabbha| kismiṃ vatthusminti? āyasmā bailaṭṭhasīsau san‍nidhikārakaṃ bhaujanaṃ  bhuñ‍ji, tasmiṃ vatthusmiṃ| aikā pañ‍ñatti| chan‍naṃ āpattisamuṭṭhānānaṃ dvīhi samuṭṭhānaihi samuṭṭhāti aiḷakalaumakai…pai॰…|

92. paṇītabhaujanāni attanau atthāya viñ‍ñāpaitvā bhuñ‍jantassa pācittiyaṃ kattha pañ‍ñattanti? sāvatthiyaṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ| kaṃ ārabbhāti? chabbaggiyai bhikkhū ārabbha| kismiṃ vatthusminti? chabbaggiyā bhikkhū paṇītabhaujanāni attanau atthāya viñ‍ñāpaitvā bhuñ‍jiṃsu, tasmiṃ vatthusmiṃ| aikā pañ‍ñatti, aikā anupañ‍ñatti| chan‍naṃ āpattisamuṭṭhānānaṃ catūhi samuṭṭhānaihi samuṭṭhāti…pai॰…|

93. adin‍naṃ mukhadvāraṃ āhāraṃ āharantassa pācittiyaṃ kattha pañ‍ñattanti? vaisāliyaṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ| kaṃ ārabbhāti? añ‍ñataraṃ bhikkhuṃ ārabbha| kismiṃ vatthusminti? añ‍ñatarau bhikkhu  adin‍naṃ mukhadvāraṃ  āhāraṃ āhari, tasmiṃ vatthusmiṃ| aikā pañ‍ñatti, aikā anupañ‍ñatti| chan‍naṃ āpattisamuṭṭhānānaṃ dvīhi samuṭṭhānaihi samuṭṭhāti aiḷakalaumakai…pai॰…|

bhaujanavaggau catutthau|

5. acailakavaggau

94. acailakassa vā paribbājakassa vā paribbājikāya vā sahatthā khādanīyaṃ vā bhaujanīyaṃ vā daintassa pācittiyaṃ kattha pañ‍ñattanti? vaisāliyaṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ| kaṃ ārabbhāti? āyasmantaṃ ānandaṃ ārabbha| kismiṃ vatthusminti? āyasmā ānandau añ‍ñatarissā paribbājikāya aikaṃ mañ‍ñamānau dvai pūvai adāsi, tasmiṃ vatthusmiṃ| aikā pañ‍ñatti| chan‍naṃ āpattisamuṭṭhānānaṃ dvīhi samuṭṭhānaihi samuṭṭhāti aiḷakalaumakai…pai॰…|

95. bhikkhuṃ ‘‘aihāvusau, gāmaṃ vā nigamaṃ vā piṇḍāya pavisissāmā’’ti tassa dāpaitvā vā adāpaitvā vā uyyaujaintassa pācittiyaṃ kattha pañ‍ñattanti? sāvatthiyaṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ| kaṃ ārabbhāti? āyasmantaṃ upanandaṃ sakyaputtaṃ ārabbha| kismiṃ vatthusminti? āyasmā upanandau sakyaputtau bhikkhuṃ ‘‘aihāvusau, gāmaṃ piṇḍāya pavisissāmā’’ti, tassa adāpaitvā uyyaujaisi, tasmiṃ vatthusmiṃ| aikā pañ‍ñatti| chan‍naṃ āpattisamuṭṭhānānaṃ tīhi samuṭṭhānaihi samuṭṭhāti…pai॰…|

96. sabhaujanai  kulai anupakhaj‍ja nisaj‍jaṃ kappaintassa pācittiyaṃ kattha pañ‍ñattanti? sāvatthiyaṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ| kaṃ ārabbhāti? āyasmantaṃ upanandaṃ sakyaputtaṃ ārabbha| kismiṃ vatthusminti? āyasmā upanandau sakyaputtau sabhaujanai kulai anupakhaj‍ja nisaj‍jaṃ kappaisi, tasmiṃ vatthusmiṃ| aikā pañ‍ñatti| chan‍naṃ āpattisamuṭṭhānānaṃ aikaina samuṭṭhānaina samuṭṭhāti – kāyatau ca cittatau ca samuṭṭhāti, na vācatau…pai॰…|

97. mātugāmaina saddhiṃ rahau paṭicchan‍nai āsanai nisaj‍jaṃ kappaintassa pācittiyaṃ kattha pañ‍ñattanti? sāvatthiyaṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ| kaṃ ārabbhāti? āyasmantaṃ upanandaṃ sakyaputtaṃ ārabbha| kismiṃ vatthusminti? āyasmā upanandau sakyaputtau mātugāmaina saddhiṃ rahau paṭicchan‍nai āsanai nisaj‍jaṃ kappaisi, tasmiṃ vatthusmiṃ| aikā pañ‍ñatti| chan‍naṃ āpattisamuṭṭhānānaṃ aikaina samuṭṭhānaina samuṭṭhāti – kāyatau ca cittatau ca samuṭṭhāti, na vācatau…pai॰…|

98. mātugāmaina  saddhiṃ aikau aikāya rahau nisaj‍jaṃ kappaintassa pācittiyaṃ kattha pañ‍ñattanti? sāvatthiyaṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ| kaṃ ārabbhāti? āyasmantaṃ upanandaṃ sakyaputtaṃ ārabbha| kismiṃ vatthusminti? āyasmā upanandau sakyaputtau mātugāmaina saddhiṃ aikau aikāya rahau nisaj‍jaṃ kappaisi, tasmiṃ vatthusmiṃ| aikā pañ‍ñatti| chan‍naṃ āpattisamuṭṭhānānaṃ aikaina samuṭṭhānaina samuṭṭhāti – kāyatau ca cittatau ca samuṭṭhāti, na vācatau…pai॰…|

99. nimantitaina sabhattaina santaṃ bhikkhuṃ anāpucchā puraibhattaṃ pacchābhattaṃ kulaisu cārittaṃ āpaj‍jantassa pācittiyaṃ kattha pañ‍ñattanti? rājagahai pañ‍ñattaṃ| kaṃ ārabbhāti? āyasmantaṃ upanandaṃ sakyaputtaṃ ārabbha| kismiṃ vatthusminti? āyasmā upanandau sakyaputtau nimantitau sabhattau samānau puraibhattaṃ pacchābhattaṃ kulaisu cārittaṃ āpaj‍ji, tasmiṃ vatthusmiṃ| aikā pañ‍ñatti, catassau anupañ‍ñattiyau| chan‍naṃ āpattisamuṭṭhānānaṃ dvīhi samuṭṭhānaihi samuṭṭhāti kathinakai…pai॰…|

100. tatuttari bhaisaj‍jaṃ viñ‍ñāpaintassa pācittiyaṃ kattha pañ‍ñattanti? sak‍kaisu pañ‍ñattaṃ| kaṃ ārabbhāti? chabbaggiyai bhikkhū ārabbha| kismiṃ vatthusminti? chabbaggiyā bhikkhū mahānāmaina sak‍kaina ‘‘aj‍jaṇhau, bhantai, āgamaithā’’ti  vuc‍camānā nāgamaisuṃ, tasmiṃ vatthusmiṃ| aikā pañ‍ñatti| chan‍naṃ āpattisamuṭṭhānānaṃ chahi samuṭṭhānaihi samuṭṭhāti…pai॰…|

101. uyyuttaṃ sainaṃ dassanāya gacchantassa pācittiyaṃ kattha pañ‍ñattanti? sāvatthiyaṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ| kaṃ ārabbhāti? chabbaggiyai bhikkhū ārabbha| kismiṃ vatthusminti? chabbaggiyā bhikkhū uyyuttaṃ sainaṃ dassanāya agamaṃsu, tasmiṃ vatthusmiṃ| aikā pañ‍ñatti, aikā anupañ‍ñatti| chan‍naṃ āpattisamuṭṭhānānaṃ dvīhi samuṭṭhānaihi samuṭṭhāti aiḷakalaumakai…pai॰…|

102. atiraikatirattaṃ saināya vasantassa pācittiyaṃ kattha pañ‍ñattanti? sāvatthiyaṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ| kaṃ ārabbhāti? chabbaggiyai bhikkhū ārabbha| kismiṃ vatthusminti? chabbaggiyā bhikkhū atiraikatirattaṃ saināya vasiṃsu, tasmiṃ vatthusmiṃ| aikā pañ‍ñatti| chan‍naṃ āpattisamuṭṭhānānaṃ dvīhi samuṭṭhānaihi samuṭṭhāti aiḷakalaumakai…pai॰…|

103. uyyaudhikaṃ gacchantassa pācittiyaṃ kattha pañ‍ñattanti? sāvatthiyaṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ| kaṃ ārabbhāti? chabbaggiyai bhikkhū ārabbha| kismiṃ vatthusminti? chabbaggiyā bhikkhū uyyaudhikaṃ  agamaṃsu, tasmiṃ vatthusmiṃ| aikā pañ‍ñatti| chan‍naṃ āpattisamuṭṭhānānaṃ dvīhi samuṭṭhānaihi samuṭṭhāti aiḷakalaumakai…pai॰…|

acailakavaggau pañ‍camau|

6. surāpānavaggau

104. surāmairayapānai  pācittiyaṃ kattha pañ‍ñattanti? kausambiyaṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ| kaṃ ārabbhāti? āyasmantaṃ sāgataṃ ārabbha| kismiṃ vatthusminti? āyasmā sāgatau maj‍jaṃ pivi, tasmiṃ vatthusmiṃ| aikā pañ‍ñatti| chan‍naṃ āpattisamuṭṭhānānaṃ dvīhi samuṭṭhānaihi samuṭṭhāti – siyā kāyatau samuṭṭhāti, na vācatau na cittatau; siyā kāyatau ca cittatau ca samuṭṭhāti, na vācatau…pai॰…|

105. aṅgulipataudakai pācittiyaṃ kattha pañ‍ñattanti? sāvatthiyaṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ| kaṃ ārabbhāti? chabbaggiyai bhikkhū ārabbha| kismiṃ vatthusminti? chabbaggiyā  bhikkhū bhikkhuṃ aṅgulipataudakaina hāsaisuṃ, tasmiṃ vatthusmiṃ| aikā pañ‍ñatti| chan‍naṃ āpattisamuṭṭhānānaṃ aikaina samuṭṭhānaina samuṭṭhāti – kāyatau ca cittatau ca samuṭṭhāti, na vācatau…pai॰…|

106. udakai hasadhammai hassadhammai (sī॰ syā॰) pācittiyaṃ kattha pañ‍ñattanti? sāvatthiyaṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ| kaṃ ārabbhāti? sattarasavaggiyai bhikkhū ārabbha| kismiṃ vatthusminti? sattarasavaggiyā bhikkhū aciravatiyā nadiyā udakai kīḷiṃsu, tasmiṃ vatthusmiṃ| aikā pañ‍ñatti| chan‍naṃ āpattisamuṭṭhānānaṃ aikaina samuṭṭhānaina samuṭṭhāti – kāyatau ca cittatau  ca samuṭṭhāti, na vācatau…pai॰…|

107. anādariyai pācittiyaṃ kattha pañ‍ñattanti? kausambiyaṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ| kaṃ ārabbhāti? āyasmantaṃ chan‍naṃ ārabbha| kismiṃ vatthusminti? āyasmā chan‍nau anādariyaṃ akāsi, tasmiṃ vatthusmiṃ| aikā pañ‍ñatti| chan‍naṃ āpattisamuṭṭhānānaṃ tīhi samuṭṭhānaihi samuṭṭhāti…pai॰…|

108. bhikkhuṃ bhiṃsāpaintassa pācittiyaṃ kattha pañ‍ñattanti? sāvatthiyaṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ| kaṃ ārabbhāti? chabbaggiyai bhikkhū ārabbha| kismiṃ vatthusminti? chabbaggiyā bhikkhū bhikkhuṃ bhiṃsāpaisuṃ, tasmiṃ vatthusmiṃ| aikā pañ‍ñatti| chan‍naṃ āpattisamuṭṭhānānaṃ tīhi samuṭṭhānaihi samuṭṭhāti…pai॰…|

109. jautiṃ samādahitvā visibbaintassa pācittiyaṃ kattha pañ‍ñattanti? bhaggaisu pañ‍ñattaṃ| kaṃ ārabbhāti? sambahulai bhikkhū ārabbha| kismiṃ vatthusminti? sambahulā bhikkhū jautiṃ samādahitvā visibbaisuṃ, tasmiṃ vatthusmiṃ| aikā pañ‍ñatti, dvai anupañ‍ñattiyau| chan‍naṃ āpattisamuṭṭhānānaṃ chahi samuṭṭhānaihi samuṭṭhāti…pai॰…|

110. aurainaddhamāsaṃ nahāyantassa pācittiyaṃ kattha pañ‍ñattanti? rājagahai pañ‍ñattaṃ| kaṃ ārabbhāti? sambahulai bhikkhū ārabbha| kismiṃ vatthusminti? sambahulā bhikkhū rājānampi passitvā na  mattaṃ jānitvā nahāyiṃsu, tasmiṃ vatthusmiṃ| aikā pañ‍ñatti, cha anupañ‍ñattiyau| sabbatthapañ‍ñatti, padaisapañ‍ñattīti? padaisapañ‍ñatti| chan‍naṃ āpattisamuṭṭhānānaṃ dvīhi samuṭṭhānaihi samuṭṭhāti aiḷakalaumakai…pai॰…|

111. anādiyitvā  tiṇṇaṃ dubbaṇṇakaraṇānaṃ añ‍ñataraṃ dubbaṇṇakaraṇaṃ navaṃ cīvaraṃ paribhuñ‍jantassa pācittiyaṃ kattha pañ‍ñattanti? sāvatthiyaṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ| kaṃ ārabbhāti? sambahulai bhikkhū ārabbha| kismiṃ vatthusminti? sambahulā bhikkhū attanau cīvaraṃ na sañ‍jāniṃsu, tasmiṃ vatthusmiṃ| aikā pañ‍ñatti| chan‍naṃ āpattisamuṭṭhānānaṃ dvīhi samuṭṭhānaihi samuṭṭhāti aiḷakalaumakai…pai॰…|

112. bhikkhussa vā bhikkhuniyā vā sikkhamānāya vā sāmaṇairassa vā sāmaṇairiyā vā sāmaṃ cīvaraṃ vikappaitvā appac‍cuddhāraṇaṃ paribhuñ‍jantassa pācittiyaṃ kattha pañ‍ñattanti? sāvatthiyaṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ| kaṃ ārabbhāti? āyasmantaṃ upanandaṃ sakyaputtaṃ ārabbha| kismiṃ vatthusminti? āyasmā upanandau sakyaputtau bhikkhussa sāmaṃ cīvaraṃ vikappaitvā appac‍cuddhāraṇaṃ paribhuñ‍ji, tasmiṃ vatthusmiṃ| aikā pañ‍ñatti| chan‍naṃ āpattisamuṭṭhānānaṃ dvīhi samuṭṭhānaihi samuṭṭhāti kathinakai…pai॰…|

113. bhikkhussa pattaṃ vā cīvaraṃ vā nisīdanaṃ vā sūcigharaṃ vā kāyabandhanaṃ vā apanidhaintassa pācittiyaṃ kattha pañ‍ñattanti? sāvatthiyaṃ  pañ‍ñattaṃ| kaṃ ārabbhāti? chabbaggiyai bhikkhū ārabbha| kismiṃ vatthusminti? chabbaggiyā bhikkhū bhikkhūnaṃ pattampi cīvarampi apanidhaisuṃ, tasmiṃ vatthusmiṃ| aikā pañ‍ñatti| chan‍naṃ āpattisamuṭṭhānānaṃ tīhi samuṭṭhānaihi samuṭṭhāti…pai॰…|

surāmairayavaggau chaṭṭhau|

7. sappāṇakavaggau

114. sañ‍cic‍ca pāṇaṃ jīvitā vauraupaintassa pācittiyaṃ kattha pañ‍ñattanti? sāvatthiyaṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ| kaṃ ārabbhāti? āyasmantaṃ udāyiṃ ārabbha| kismiṃ vatthusminti? āyasmā udāyī sañ‍cic‍ca pāṇaṃ jīvitā vauraupaisi, tasmiṃ vatthusmiṃ| aikā pañ‍ñatti| chan‍naṃ āpattisamuṭṭhānānaṃ tīhi samuṭṭhānaihi samuṭṭhāti…pai॰…|

115. jānaṃ sappāṇakaṃ udakaṃ paribhuñ‍jantassa pācittiyaṃ kattha pañ‍ñattanti? sāvatthiyaṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ| kaṃ ārabbhāti? chabbaggiyai bhikkhū ārabbha| kismiṃ vatthusminti? chabbaggiyā bhikkhū jānaṃ sappāṇakaṃ  udakaṃ paribhuñ‍jiṃsu, tasmiṃ vatthusmiṃ| aikā pañ‍ñatti| chan‍naṃ āpattisamuṭṭhānānaṃ tīhi samuṭṭhānaihi samuṭṭhāti…pai॰…|

116. jānaṃ yathādhammaṃ nihatādhikaraṇaṃ puna kammāya uk‍kauṭaintassa pācittiyaṃ kattha pañ‍ñattanti? sāvatthiyaṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ| kaṃ ārabbhāti? chabbaggiyai bhikkhū ārabbha| kismiṃ vatthusminti? chabbaggiyā bhikkhū jānaṃ yathādhammaṃ nihatādhikaraṇaṃ puna kammāya uk‍kauṭaisuṃ, tasmiṃ vatthusmiṃ| aikā pañ‍ñatti| chan‍naṃ āpattisamuṭṭhānānaṃ tīhi samuṭṭhānaihi samuṭṭhāti…pai॰…|

117. bhikkhussa jānaṃ duṭṭhul‍laṃ āpattiṃ paṭicchādaintassa pācittiyaṃ kattha pañ‍ñattanti? sāvatthiyaṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ| kaṃ ārabbhāti? añ‍ñataraṃ bhikkhuṃ  ārabbha| kismiṃ vatthusminti? añ‍ñatarau bhikkhu bhikkhussa jānaṃ duṭṭhul‍laṃ āpattiṃ paṭicchādaisi, tasmiṃ vatthusmiṃ| aikā pañ‍ñatti| chan‍naṃ āpattisamuṭṭhānānaṃ aikaina samuṭṭhānaina samuṭṭhāti – kāyatau ca vācatau ca cittatau ca samuṭṭhāti…pai॰…|

118. jānaṃ ūnavīsativassaṃ puggalaṃ upasampādaintassa pācittiyaṃ kattha pañ‍ñattanti? rājagahai pañ‍ñattaṃ| kaṃ ārabbhāti? sambahulai  bhikkhū ārabbha| kismiṃ vatthusminti? sambahulā bhikkhū jānaṃ ūnavīsativassaṃ puggalaṃ upasampādaisuṃ, tasmiṃ vatthusmiṃ| aikā pañ‍ñatti| chan‍naṃ āpattisamuṭṭhānānaṃ tīhi samuṭṭhānaihi samuṭṭhāti…pai॰…|

119. jānaṃ  thaiyyasatthaina saddhiṃ saṃvidhāya aikaddhānamaggaṃ paṭipaj‍jantassa pācittiyaṃ kattha pañ‍ñattanti? sāvatthiyaṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ| kaṃ ārabbhāti? añ‍ñataraṃ bhikkhuṃ ārabbha| kismiṃ vatthusminti? añ‍ñatarau bhikkhu jānaṃ thaiyyasatthaina saddhiṃ saṃvidhāya aikaddhānamaggaṃ paṭipaj‍ji, tasmiṃ vatthusmiṃ| aikā pañ‍ñatti| chan‍naṃ āpattisamuṭṭhānānaṃ dvīhi samuṭṭhānaihi samuṭṭhāti – siyā kāyatau ca cittatau ca samuṭṭhāti, na vācatau; siyā kāyatau ca vācatau ca cittatau ca samuṭṭhāti…pai॰…|

120. mātugāmaina saddhiṃ saṃvidhāya aikaddhānamaggaṃ paṭipaj‍jantassa pācittiyaṃ kattha pañ‍ñattanti? sāvatthiyaṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ| kaṃ ārabbhāti? añ‍ñataraṃ bhikkhuṃ ārabbha| kismiṃ  vatthusminti? añ‍ñatarau bhikkhu mātugāmaina saddhiṃ saṃvidhāya aikaddhānamaggaṃ paṭipaj‍ji, tasmiṃ vatthusmiṃ| aikā pañ‍ñatti| chan‍naṃ āpattisamuṭṭhānānaṃ catūhi samuṭṭhānaihi samuṭṭhāti…pai॰…|

121. pāpikāya diṭṭhiyā yāvatatiyaṃ samanubhāsanāya na paṭinissaj‍jantassa pācittiyaṃ kattha pañ‍ñattanti? sāvatthiyaṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ| kaṃ ārabbhāti? ariṭṭhaṃ bhikkhuṃ gaddhabādhipubbaṃ ārabbha| kismiṃ vatthusminti? ariṭṭhau bhikkhu gaddhabādhipubbau pāpikāya diṭṭhiyā yāvatatiyaṃ samanubhāsanāya na paṭinissaj‍ji, tasmiṃ vatthusmiṃ| aikā pañ‍ñatti| chan‍naṃ āpattisamuṭṭhānānaṃ aikaina samuṭṭhānaina samuṭṭhāti| kāyatau ca vācatau  ca cittatau ca samuṭṭhāti…pai॰…|

122. jānaṃ tathāvādinā bhikkhunā ariṭṭhaina bhikkhunā (ka॰) akaṭānudhammaina taṃ diṭṭhiṃ appaṭinissaṭṭhaina saddhiṃ sambhuñ‍jantassa pācittiyaṃ kattha pañ‍ñattanti? sāvatthiyaṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ| kaṃ ārabbhāti? chabbaggiyai bhikkhū ārabbha| kismiṃ vatthusminti? chabbaggiyā bhikkhū jānaṃ tathāvādinā ariṭṭhaina bhikkhunā akaṭānudhammaina taṃ diṭṭhiṃ appaṭinissaṭṭhaina saddhiṃ sambhuñ‍jiṃsu, tasmiṃ vatthusmiṃ| aikā pañ‍ñatti| chan‍naṃ āpattisamuṭṭhānānaṃ tīhi samuṭṭhānaihi samuṭṭhāti…pai॰…|

123. jānaṃ tathānāsitaṃ samaṇuddaisaṃ upalāpaintassa pācittiyaṃ kattha pañ‍ñattanti? sāvatthiyaṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ| kaṃ ārabbhāti? chabbaggiyai bhikkhū ārabbha| kismiṃ vatthusminti? chabbaggiyā  bhikkhū jānaṃ tathānāsitaṃ kaṇṭakaṃ  samaṇuddaisaṃ upalāpaisuṃ, tasmiṃ vatthusmiṃ| aikā pañ‍ñatti| chan‍naṃ āpattisamuṭṭhānānaṃ tīhi samuṭṭhānaihi samuṭṭhāti…pai॰…|

sappāṇakavaggau sattamau|

8. sahadhammikavaggau

124. bhikkhūhi  sahadhammikaṃ vuc‍camānaina ‘‘na tāvāhaṃ, āvusau, aitasmiṃ sikkhāpadai sikkhissāmi yāva na añ‍ñaṃ bhikkhuṃ byattaṃ vinayadharaṃ paripucchissāmī’’ti  bhaṇantassa pācittiyaṃ kattha pañ‍ñattanti? kausambiyaṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ| kaṃ ārabbhāti? āyasmantaṃ chan‍naṃ ārabbha| kismiṃ vatthusminti? āyasmā chan‍nau bhikkhūhi sahadhammikaṃ vuc‍camānau ‘‘na tāvāhaṃ, āvusau, aitasmiṃ sikkhāpadai sikkhissāmi yāva na añ‍ñaṃ bhikkhuṃ byattaṃ vinayadharaṃ paripucchissāmī’’ti bhaṇi, tasmiṃ vatthusmiṃ| aikā pañ‍ñatti| chan‍naṃ āpattisamuṭṭhānānaṃ tīhi samuṭṭhānaihi samuṭṭhāti…pai॰…|

125. vinayaṃ vivaṇṇaintassa pācittiyaṃ kattha pañ‍ñattanti? sāvatthiyaṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ| kaṃ ārabbhāti? chabbaggiyai bhikkhū ārabbha| kismiṃ vatthusminti? chabbaggiyā bhikkhū vinayaṃ vivaṇṇaisuṃ, tasmiṃ vatthusmiṃ| aikā pañ‍ñatti| chan‍naṃ āpattisamuṭṭhānānaṃ tīhi samuṭṭhānaihi samuṭṭhāti…pai॰…|

126. mauhanakai pācittiyaṃ kattha pañ‍ñattanti? sāvatthiyaṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ| kaṃ ārabbhāti? chabbaggiyai bhikkhū ārabbha| kismiṃ vatthusminti? chabbaggiyā bhikkhū mauhaisuṃ, tasmiṃ vatthusmiṃ| aikā pañ‍ñatti| chan‍naṃ āpattisamuṭṭhānānaṃ tīhi samuṭṭhānaihi samuṭṭhāti…pai॰…|

127. bhikkhussa kupitaina anattamanaina pahāraṃ daintassa pācittiyaṃ kattha  pañ‍ñattanti? sāvatthiyaṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ| kaṃ ārabbhāti? chabbaggiyai bhikkhū ārabbha| kismiṃ vatthusminti? chabbaggiyā bhikkhū kupitā anattamanā bhikkhūnaṃ pahāraṃ adaṃsu, tasmiṃ vatthusmiṃ| aikā pañ‍ñatti| chan‍naṃ āpattisamuṭṭhānānaṃ aikaina samuṭṭhānaina samuṭṭhāti – kāyatau ca cittatau ca samuṭṭhāti, na vācatau…pai॰…|

128. bhikkhussa kupitaina anattamanaina talasattikaṃ uggirantassa pācittiyaṃ kattha pañ‍ñattanti? sāvatthiyaṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ| kaṃ ārabbhāti? chabbaggiyai bhikkhū ārabbha| kismiṃ vatthusminti? chabbaggiyā bhikkhū kupitā anattamanā bhikkhūnaṃ talasattikaṃ uggiriṃsu, tasmiṃ vatthusmiṃ| aikā pañ‍ñatti| chan‍naṃ āpattisamuṭṭhānānaṃ aikaina samuṭṭhānaina samuṭṭhāti – kāyatau ca cittatau ca samuṭṭhāti, na vācatau…pai॰…|

129. bhikkhuṃ amūlakaina saṅghādisaisaina anuddhaṃsaintassa pācittiyaṃ kattha pañ‍ñattanti? sāvatthiyaṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ| kaṃ ārabbhāti? chabbaggiyai bhikkhū ārabbha| kismiṃ vatthusminti? chabbaggiyā bhikkhū bhikkhuṃ amūlakaina saṅghādisaisaina anuddhaṃsaisuṃ, tasmiṃ vatthusmiṃ| aikā pañ‍ñatti| chan‍naṃ āpattisamuṭṭhānānaṃ tīhi samuṭṭhānaihi samuṭṭhāti…pai॰…|

130. bhikkhussa sañ‍cic‍ca kuk‍kuc‍caṃ upadahantassa uppādaintassa (syā॰) pācittiyaṃ kattha pañ‍ñattanti? sāvatthiyaṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ| kaṃ ārabbhāti? chabbaggiyai  bhikkhū ārabbha| kismiṃ vatthusminti? chabbaggiyā bhikkhū bhikkhūnaṃ sañ‍cic‍ca kuk‍kuc‍caṃ upadahiṃsu, tasmiṃ vatthusmiṃ| aikā pañ‍ñatti| chan‍naṃ āpattisamuṭṭhānānaṃ tīhi samuṭṭhānaihi samuṭṭhāti…pai॰…|

131. bhikkhūnaṃ bhaṇḍanajātānaṃ kalahajātānaṃ vivādāpan‍nānaṃ  upassutiṃ upassuti (?) tiṭṭhantassa pācittiyaṃ kattha pañ‍ñattanti? sāvatthiyaṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ| kaṃ ārabbhāti? chabbaggiyai bhikkhū ārabbha| kismiṃ vatthusminti? chabbaggiyā bhikkhū bhikkhūnaṃ bhaṇḍanajātānaṃ kalahajātānaṃ vivādāpan‍nānaṃ upassutiṃ tiṭṭhahiṃsu, tasmiṃ vatthusmiṃ| aikā pañ‍ñatti| chan‍naṃ āpattisamuṭṭhānānaṃ dvīhi samuṭṭhānaihi samuṭṭhāti – siyā kāyatau ca cittatau ca samuṭṭhāti, na vācatau; siyā kāyatau ca vācatau ca cittatau ca samuṭṭhāti…pai॰…|

132. dhammikānaṃ kammānaṃ chandaṃ datvā pacchā khīyanadhammaṃ khiyyadhammaṃ (ka॰) āpaj‍jantassa pācittiyaṃ kattha pañ‍ñattanti? sāvatthiyaṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ| kaṃ ārabbhāti? chabbaggiyai bhikkhū ārabbha| kismiṃ vatthusminti? chabbaggiyā bhikkhū dhammikānaṃ kammānaṃ chandaṃ datvā pacchā khīyanadhammaṃ āpaj‍jiṃsu, tasmiṃ vatthusmiṃ| aikā pañ‍ñatti| chan‍naṃ āpattisamuṭṭhānānaṃ tīhi samuṭṭhānaihi samuṭṭhāti…pai॰…|

133. saṅghai vinicchayakathāya vattamānāya chandaṃ adatvā uṭṭhāyāsanā pak‍kamantassa pācittiyaṃ kattha pañ‍ñattanti? sāvatthiyaṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ| kaṃ ārabbhāti? añ‍ñataraṃ bhikkhuṃ ārabbha| kismiṃ vatthusminti? añ‍ñatarau bhikkhu saṅghai vinicchayakathāya vattamānāya chandaṃ adatvā uṭṭhāyāsanā pak‍kāmi, tasmiṃ vatthusmiṃ| aikā pañ‍ñatti| chan‍naṃ āpattisamuṭṭhānānaṃ aikaina samuṭṭhānaina samuṭṭhāti – kāyatau ca vācatau ca cittatau ca samuṭṭhāti…pai॰…|

134. samaggai  saṅghaina cīvaraṃ datvā pacchā khīyanadhammaṃ āpaj‍jantassa pācittiyaṃ kattha pañ‍ñattanti? rājagahai pañ‍ñattaṃ| kaṃ ārabbhāti? chabbaggiyai bhikkhū ārabbha| kismiṃ vatthusminti? chabbaggiyā bhikkhū samaggaina saṅghaina cīvaraṃ datvā pacchā khīyanadhammaṃ āpaj‍jiṃsu, tasmiṃ vatthusmiṃ| aikā pañ‍ñatti| chan‍naṃ āpattisamuṭṭhānānaṃ tīhi samuṭṭhānaihi samuṭṭhāti…pai॰…|

135. jānaṃ saṅghikaṃ lābhaṃ pariṇataṃ puggalassa pariṇāmaintassa pācittiyaṃ kattha pañ‍ñattanti? sāvatthiyaṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ| kaṃ ārabbhāti? chabbaggiyai bhikkhū ārabbha| kismiṃ vatthusminti? chabbaggiyā bhikkhū jānaṃ saṅghikaṃ lābhaṃ pariṇataṃ puggalassa pariṇāmaisuṃ, tasmiṃ vatthusmiṃ| aikā pañ‍ñatti| chan‍naṃ āpattisamuṭṭhānānaṃ tīhi samuṭṭhānaihi samuṭṭhāti…pai॰…|

sahadhammikavaggau aṭṭhamau|

9. rājavaggau

136. pubbai  appaṭisaṃviditaina rañ‍ñau antaipuraṃ pavisantassa pācittiyaṃ kattha pañ‍ñattanti? sāvatthiyaṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ| kaṃ ārabbhāti? āyasmantaṃ ānandaṃ ārabbha| kismiṃ vatthusminti? āyasmā ānandau pubbai appaṭisaṃviditau rañ‍ñau antaipuraṃ pāvisi, tasmiṃ vatthusmiṃ| aikā pañ‍ñatti| chan‍naṃ āpattisamuṭṭhānānaṃ dvīhi samuṭṭhānaihi samuṭṭhāti kathinakai…pai॰…|

137. ratanaṃ uggaṇhantassa pācittiyaṃ kattha pañ‍ñattanti? sāvatthiyaṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ| kaṃ ārabbhāti? añ‍ñataraṃ bhikkhuṃ ārabbha| kismiṃ vatthusminti? añ‍ñatarau bhikkhu ratanaṃ uggahaisi, tasmiṃ vatthusmiṃ| aikā pañ‍ñatti, dvai anupañ‍ñattiyau| chan‍naṃ āpattisamuṭṭhānānaṃ chahi samuṭṭhānaihi samuṭṭhāti…pai॰…|

138. santaṃ bhikkhuṃ anāpucchā vikālai gāmaṃ pavisantassa pācittiyaṃ kattha pañ‍ñattanti? sāvatthiyaṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ| kaṃ ārabbhāti? chabbaggiyai bhikkhū ārabbha| kismiṃ vatthusminti? chabbaggiyā bhikkhū vikālai gāmaṃ pavisiṃsu, tasmiṃ  vatthusmiṃ| aikā pañ‍ñatti, tissau anupañ‍ñattiyau| chan‍naṃ āpattisamuṭṭhānānaṃ dvīhi samuṭṭhānaihi samuṭṭhāti kathinakai…pai॰…|

139. aṭṭhimayaṃ  vā dantamayaṃ vā visāṇamayaṃ vā sūcigharaṃ kārāpaintassa pācittiyaṃ kattha pañ‍ñattanti? sak‍kaisu pañ‍ñattaṃ| kaṃ ārabbhāti? sambahulai bhikkhū ārabbha| kismiṃ vatthusminti? sambahulā bhikkhū na mattaṃ jānitvā bahū sūcigharai viñ‍ñāpaisuṃ, tasmiṃ vatthusmiṃ| aikā pañ‍ñatti| chan‍naṃ āpattisamuṭṭhānānaṃ chahi samuṭṭhānaihi samuṭṭhāti…pai॰…|

140. pamāṇātik‍kantaṃ mañ‍caṃ vā pīṭhaṃ vā kārāpaintassa pācittiyaṃ kattha pañ‍ñattanti? sāvatthiyaṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ| kaṃ ārabbhāti? āyasmantaṃ upanandaṃ sakyaputtaṃ ārabbha| kismiṃ vatthusminti? āyasmā upanandau sakyaputtau uc‍cai mañ‍cai sayi, tasmiṃ vatthusmiṃ| aikā pañ‍ñatti| chan‍naṃ āpattisamuṭṭhānānaṃ chahi samuṭṭhānaihi samuṭṭhāti…pai॰…|

141. mañ‍caṃ vā pīṭhaṃ vā tūlaunaddhaṃ kārāpaintassa pācittiyaṃ kattha pañ‍ñattanti? sāvatthiyaṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ| kaṃ ārabbhāti? chabbaggiyai bhikkhū ārabbha| kismiṃ vatthusminti? chabbaggiyā bhikkhū mañ‍caṃ vā pīṭhaṃ vā tūlaunaddhaṃ kārāpaisuṃ, tasmiṃ vatthusmiṃ| aikā pañ‍ñatti| chan‍naṃ āpattisamuṭṭhānānaṃ chahi samuṭṭhānaihi samuṭṭhāti…pai॰…|

142. pamāṇātik‍kantaṃ  nisīdanaṃ kārāpaintassa pācittiyaṃ kattha pañ‍ñattanti? sāvatthiyaṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ| kaṃ ārabbhāti? chabbaggiyai bhikkhū ārabbha| kismiṃ vatthusminti? chabbaggiyā bhikkhū appamāṇikāni nisīdanāni dhāraisuṃ, tasmiṃ vatthusmiṃ| aikā pañ‍ñatti, aikā anupañ‍ñatti| chan‍naṃ āpattisamuṭṭhānānaṃ chahi samuṭṭhānaihi samuṭṭhāti…pai॰…|

143. pamāṇātik‍kantaṃ kaṇḍuppaṭicchādiṃ kārāpaintassa pācittiyaṃ kattha pañ‍ñattanti? sāvatthiyaṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ| kaṃ ārabbhāti? chabbaggiyai bhikkhū ārabbha| kismiṃ vatthusminti? chabbaggiyā bhikkhū appamāṇikāyau kaṇḍuppaṭicchādiyau dhāraisuṃ, tasmiṃ vatthusmiṃ| aikā pañ‍ñatti| chan‍naṃ āpattisamuṭṭhānānaṃ chahi samuṭṭhānaihi samuṭṭhāti…pai॰…|

144. pamāṇātik‍kantaṃ  vassikasāṭikaṃ kārāpaintassa pācittiyaṃ kattha pañ‍ñattanti? sāvatthiyaṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ| kaṃ ārabbhāti? chabbaggiyai bhikkhū ārabbha| kismiṃ vatthusminti? chabbaggiyā bhikkhū appamāṇikāyau vassikasāṭikāyau dhāraisuṃ, tasmiṃ vatthusmiṃ| aikā pañ‍ñatti| chan‍naṃ āpattisamuṭṭhānānaṃ chahi samuṭṭhānaihi samuṭṭhāti…pai॰…|

145. sugatacīvarappamāṇaṃ  cīvaraṃ kārāpaintassa pācittiyaṃ kattha pañ‍ñattanti? sāvatthiyaṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ| kaṃ ārabbhāti? āyasmantaṃ nandaṃ  ārabbha| kismiṃ vatthusminti? āyasmā nandau sugatacīvarappamāṇaṃ cīvaraṃ dhāraisi, tasmiṃ vatthusmiṃ| aikā pañ‍ñatti| chan‍naṃ āpattisamuṭṭhānānaṃ chahi samuṭṭhānaihi samuṭṭhāti…pai॰…|

rājavaggau navamau|

dvainavuti pācittiyā niṭṭhitā|

khuddakaṃ samattaṃ|

tassuddānaṃ –

musā aumasapaisuñ‍ñaṃ, padasaiyyā ca itthiyā|

añ‍ñatra viñ‍ñunā bhūtā daisanāraucanā caiva (sī॰ syā॰), duṭṭhul‍lāpatti khaṇanā||

bhūtaṃ añ‍ñāya ujjhāyi bhūtañ‍ñavādaujjhāyi (sī॰), mañ‍cau saiyyau ca vuc‍cati|

pubbai nik‍kaḍḍhanāhac‍ca, dvāraṃ sappāṇakaina ca||

asammatā atthaṅgatai, upassayāmisaina ca|

dadai sibbai vidhānaina, nāvā bhuñ‍jaiyya aikatau||

piṇḍaṃ gaṇaṃ paraṃ pūvaṃ, pavāritau pavāritaṃ|

vikālaṃ san‍nidhi khīraṃ, dantapaunaina tai dasa||

acailakaṃ uyyaukhaj‍ja acailakānupakhaj‍ja (ka॰), paṭicchan‍naṃ rahaina ca|

nimantitau pac‍cayaihi, saināvasanuyyaudhikaṃ||

surā  aṅguli hāsau ca, anādariyañ‍ca bhiṃsanaṃ|

jauti nahāna dubbaṇṇaṃ, sāmaṃ apanidhaina ca||

sañ‍cic‍cudakakammā  ca, duṭṭhul‍laṃ ūnavīsati|

thaiyyaitthiavadaisaṃ ariṭṭhakaṃ (vibhaṅgai), saṃvāsai nāsitaina ca||

sahadhammikavilaikhā, mauhau pahārainuggirai|

amūlakañ‍ca sañ‍cic‍ca, saussāmi khiyyapak‍kamai||

saṅghaina cīvaraṃ datvā, pariṇāmaiyya puggalai|

rañ‍ñañ‍ca ratanaṃ santaṃ, sūci mañ‍cau ca tūlikā|

nisīdanaṃ kaṇḍucchādi, vassikā sugataina cāti||

taisaṃ vaggānaṃ uddānaṃ –

musā bhūtā ca auvādau, bhaujanācailakaina ca|

surā sappāṇakā dhammau, rājavaggaina tai navāti||

6. pāṭidaisanīyakaṇḍaṃ

146. yaṃ taina bhagavatā jānatā passatā arahatā sammāsambuddhaina añ‍ñātikāya bhikkhuniyā antaragharaṃ paviṭṭhāya hatthatau khādanīyaṃ vā bhaujanīyaṃ vā sahatthā paṭiggahaitvā bhuñ‍jantassa pāṭidaisanīyaṃ kattha pañ‍ñattanti? sāvatthiyaṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ| kaṃ ārabbhāti? añ‍ñataraṃ  bhikkhuṃ ārabbha| kismiṃ vatthusminti? añ‍ñatarau bhikkhu añ‍ñātikāya bhikkhuniyā antaragharaṃ paviṭṭhāya hatthatau āmisaṃ paṭiggahaisi, tasmiṃ vatthusmiṃ| aikā pañ‍ñatti| chan‍naṃ āpattisamuṭṭhānānaṃ dvīhi samuṭṭhānaihi  samuṭṭhāti – siyā kāyatau samuṭṭhāti, na vācatau na cittatau; siyā kāyatau ca cittatau ca samuṭṭhāti, na vācatau…pai॰…|

147. bhikkhuniyā vausāsantiyā na nivāraitvā bhuñ‍jantassa pāṭidaisanīyaṃ kattha pañ‍ñattanti? rājagahai pañ‍ñattaṃ| kaṃ ārabbhāti? chabbaggiyai bhikkhū ārabbha| kismiṃ vatthusminti? chabbaggiyā bhikkhū bhikkhuniyau vausāsantiyau na nivāraisuṃ, tasmiṃ vatthusmiṃ| aikā  pañ‍ñatti| chan‍naṃ āpattisamuṭṭhānānaṃ dvīhi samuṭṭhānaihi samuṭṭhāti – siyā kāyatau ca vācatau ca samuṭṭhāti, na cittatau; siyā kāyatau ca vācatau ca cittatau ca samuṭṭhāti…pai॰…|

148. saikkhasammataisu  kulaisu khādanīyaṃ vā bhaujanīyaṃ vā sahatthā paṭiggahaitvā bhuñ‍jantassa pāṭidaisanīyaṃ kattha pañ‍ñattanti? sāvatthiyaṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ| kaṃ ārabbhāti? sambahulai bhikkhū ārabbha| kismiṃ vatthusminti? sambahulā bhikkhū na mattaṃ jānitvā paṭiggahaisuṃ, tasmiṃ vatthusmiṃ| aikā pañ‍ñatti, dvai anupañ‍ñattiyau| chan‍naṃ āpattisamuṭṭhānānaṃ dvīhi samuṭṭhānaihi samuṭṭhāti – siyā kāyatau samuṭṭhāti, na vācatau na cittatau; siyā kāyatau ca cittatau  ca samuṭṭhāti, na vācatau…pai॰…|

149. ārañ‍ñakaisu saināsanaisu pubbai appaṭisaṃviditaṃ khādanīyaṃ vā bhaujanīyaṃ vā ajjhārāmai sahatthā paṭiggahaitvā bhuñ‍jantassa pāṭidaisanīyaṃ kattha pañ‍ñattanti? sak‍kaisu pañ‍ñattaṃ| kaṃ ārabbhāti? sambahulai bhikkhū ārabbha| kismiṃ vatthusminti| sambahulā bhikkhū ārāmai caurai paṭivasantai nāraucaisuṃ, tasmiṃ vatthusmiṃ| aikā pañ‍ñatti, aikā anupañ‍ñatti| chan‍naṃ āpattisamuṭṭhānānaṃ dvīhi samuṭṭhānaihi samuṭṭhāti – siyā kāyatau ca vācatau ca samuṭṭhāti, na cittatau; siyā kāyatau ca vācatau ca cittatau ca samuṭṭhāti…pai॰…|

cattārau pāṭidaisanīyā niṭṭhitā|

tassuddānaṃ –

añ‍ñātikāya vausāsaṃ, saikkhaārañ‍ñakaina ca|

pāṭidaisanīyā cattārau, sambuddhaina pakāsitāti||

7. saikhiyakaṇḍaṃ

1. parimaṇḍalavaggau

150. yaṃ  taina bhagavatā jānatā passatā arahatā sammāsambuddhaina anādariyaṃ paṭic‍ca puratau vā pacchatau vā aulambaintaina nivāsaintassa duk‍kaṭaṃ kattha pañ‍ñattanti? sāvatthiyaṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ| kaṃ ārabbhāti? chabbaggiyai bhikkhū ārabbha| kismiṃ vatthusminti? chabbaggiyā bhikkhū purataupi pacchataupi aulambaintā nivāsaisuṃ, tasmiṃ  vatthusmiṃ| aikā pañ‍ñatti| chan‍naṃ āpattisamuṭṭhānānaṃ  aikaina samuṭṭhānaina samuṭṭhāti – kāyatau ca cittatau ca samuṭṭhāti, na vācatau…pai॰…|

anādariyaṃ paṭic‍ca puratau vā pacchatau vā aulambaintaina pārupantassa duk‍kaṭaṃ kattha pañ‍ñattanti? sāvatthiyaṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ| kaṃ ārabbhāti? chabbaggiyai bhikkhū ārabbha| kismiṃ vatthusminti? chabbaggiyā bhikkhū purataupi pacchataupi aulambaintā pārupiṃsu, tasmiṃ vatthusmiṃ| aikā pañ‍ñatti| chan‍naṃ āpattisamuṭṭhānānaṃ aikaina samuṭṭhānaina samuṭṭhāti – kāyatau ca cittatau ca samuṭṭhāti, na vācatau…pai॰…|

anādariyaṃ paṭic‍ca kāyaṃ vivaritvā antaragharai gacchantassa duk‍kaṭaṃ…pai॰… aikā pañ‍ñatti| aikaina samuṭṭhānaina samuṭṭhāti – kāyatau ca cittatau ca samuṭṭhāti, na vācatau…pai॰…|

anādariyaṃ  paṭic‍ca kāyaṃ vivaritvā antaragharai nisīdantassa duk‍kaṭaṃ…pai॰… aikā pañ‍ñatti| aikaina samuṭṭhānaina samuṭṭhāti – kāyatau ca cittatau ca samuṭṭhāti, na vācatau…pai॰…|

anādariyaṃ paṭic‍ca hatthaṃ vā pādaṃ vā kīḷāpaintaina antaragharai gacchantassa duk‍kaṭaṃ…pai॰…  aikā pañ‍ñatti| aikaina samuṭṭhānaina samuṭṭhāti – kāyatau ca cittatau ca samuṭṭhāti, na vācatau…pai॰…|

anādariyaṃ paṭic‍ca hatthaṃ vā pādaṃ vā kīḷāpaintaina antaragharai nisīdantassa duk‍kaṭaṃ  …pai॰… aikā pañ‍ñatti| aikaina samuṭṭhānaina samuṭṭhāti – kāyatau ca cittatau ca samuṭṭhāti, na vācatau…pai॰…|

anādariyaṃ paṭic‍ca tahaṃ tahaṃ aulaukaintaina antaragharai gacchantassa duk‍kaṭaṃ…pai॰… aikā pañ‍ñatti| aikaina samuṭṭhānaina samuṭṭhāti – kāyatau ca cittatau ca samuṭṭhāti, na vācatau…pai॰…|

anādariyaṃ paṭic‍ca tahaṃ tahaṃ aulaukaintaina antaragharai nisīdantassa duk‍kaṭaṃ…pai॰… aikā pañ‍ñatti| aikaina samuṭṭhānaina samuṭṭhāti – kāyatau ca cittatau ca samuṭṭhāti, na vācatau…pai॰…|

anādariyaṃ paṭic‍ca ukkhittakāya antaragharai gacchantassa duk‍kaṭaṃ…pai॰… aikā pañ‍ñatti| aikaina samuṭṭhānaina samuṭṭhāti – kāyatau ca cittatau ca samuṭṭhāti, na vācatau…pai॰…|

anādariyaṃ  paṭic‍ca ukkhittakāya antaragharai nisīdantassa duk‍kaṭaṃ…pai॰… aikā pañ‍ñatti| aikaina  samuṭṭhānaina samuṭṭhāti – kāyatau ca cittatau ca samuṭṭhāti, na vācatau…pai॰…|

parimaṇḍalavaggau paṭhamau|

2. uj‍jagghikavaggau

151. anādariyaṃ paṭic‍ca uj‍jagghikāya antaragharai gacchantassa duk‍kaṭaṃ kattha pañ‍ñattanti? sāvatthiyaṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ| kaṃ ārabbhāti? chabbaggiyai bhikkhū ārabbha| kismiṃ vatthusminti? chabbaggiyā bhikkhū mahāhasitaṃ hasantā antaragharai gacchiṃsu, tasmiṃ vatthusmiṃ| aikā pañ‍ñatti| chan‍naṃ āpattisamuṭṭhānānaṃ aikaina samuṭṭhānaina samuṭṭhāti – kāyatau ca vācatau ca cittatau ca samuṭṭhāti…pai॰…|
