anādariyaṃ paṭic‍ca uj‍jagghikāya antaragharai nisīdantassa duk‍kaṭaṃ kattha pañ‍ñattanti? sāvatthiyaṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ| kaṃ ārabbhāti? chabbaggiyai bhikkhū ārabbha| kismiṃ vatthusminti? chabbaggiyā  bhikkhū mahāhasitaṃ hasantā antaragharai nisīdiṃsu, tasmiṃ vatthusmiṃ| aikā pañ‍ñatti| chan‍naṃ āpattisamuṭṭhānānaṃ aikaina samuṭṭhānaina samuṭṭhāti – kāyatau ca vācatau ca cittatau ca samuṭṭhāti…pai॰…|

anādariyaṃ paṭic‍ca uc‍cāsaddaṃ mahāsaddaṃ karauntaina antaragharai gacchantassa duk‍kaṭaṃ kattha pañ‍ñattanti? sāvatthiyaṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ| kaṃ ārabbhāti? chabbaggiyai bhikkhū ārabbha| kismiṃ vatthusminti? chabbaggiyā bhikkhū uc‍cāsaddaṃ mahāsaddaṃ karauntā antaragharai gacchiṃsu, tasmiṃ vatthusmiṃ| aikā pañ‍ñatti| chan‍naṃ āpattisamuṭṭhānānaṃ aikaina samuṭṭhānaina samuṭṭhāti – kāyatau ca vācatau ca cittatau ca samuṭṭhāti…pai॰…|

anādariyaṃ paṭic‍ca uc‍cāsaddaṃ mahāsaddaṃ karauntaina antaragharai  nisīdantassa duk‍kaṭaṃ kattha pañ‍ñattanti? sāvatthiyaṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ| kaṃ ārabbhāti? chabbaggiyai bhikkhū ārabbha| kismiṃ vatthusminti? chabbaggiyā bhikkhū uc‍cāsaddaṃ mahāsaddaṃ karauntā antaragharai nisīdiṃsu, tasmiṃ vatthusmiṃ| aikā pañ‍ñatti| chan‍naṃ āpattisamuṭṭhānānaṃ aikaina samuṭṭhānaina samuṭṭhāti – kāyatau ca vācatau ca cittatau ca samuṭṭhāti…pai॰…|

anādariyaṃ paṭic‍ca kāyappacālakaṃ antaragharai gacchantassa duk‍kaṭaṃ…pai॰… aikā pañ‍ñatti| aikaina samuṭṭhānaina samuṭṭhāti – kāyatau ca cittatau ca samuṭṭhāti, na vācatau…pai॰…|

anādariyaṃ paṭic‍ca kāyappacālakaṃ antaragharai nisīdantassa duk‍kaṭaṃ…pai॰… aikā pañ‍ñatti| aikaina samuṭṭhānaina samuṭṭhāti – kāyatau ca cittatau ca samuṭṭhāti, na vācatau…pai॰…|

anādariyaṃ paṭic‍ca bāhuppacālakaṃ antaragharai gacchantassa duk‍kaṭaṃ…pai॰… aikā pañ‍ñatti| aikaina samuṭṭhānaina samuṭṭhāti – kāyatau ca cittatau ca samuṭṭhāti, na vācatau…pai॰…|

anādariyaṃ paṭic‍ca bāhuppacālakaṃ antaragharai nisīdantassa duk‍kaṭaṃ…pai॰… aikā pañ‍ñatti| aikaina samuṭṭhānaina samuṭṭhāti – kāyatau ca cittatau ca samuṭṭhāti, na vācatau…pai॰…|

anādariyaṃ paṭic‍ca sīsappacālakaṃ antaragharai gacchantassa duk‍kaṭaṃ…pai॰… aikā pañ‍ñatti| aikaina samuṭṭhānaina samuṭṭhāti – kāyatau ca cittatau ca samuṭṭhāti, na vācatau…pai॰…|

anādariyaṃ paṭic‍ca sīsappacālakaṃ antaragharai nisīdantassa duk‍kaṭaṃ…pai॰… aikā pañ‍ñatti| aikaina samuṭṭhānaina samuṭṭhāti  – kāyatau ca cittatau ca samuṭṭhāti, na vācatau…pai॰…|

uj‍jagghikavaggau dutiyau|

3. khambhakatavaggau

152. anādariyaṃ paṭic‍ca khambhakataina antaragharai gacchantassa duk‍kaṭaṃ…pai॰… aikā pañ‍ñatti| aikaina samuṭṭhānaina samuṭṭhāti| kāyatau ca cittatau ca samuṭṭhāti, na vācatau…pai॰…|

anādariyaṃ  paṭic‍ca khambhakataina antaragharai nisīdantassa duk‍kaṭaṃ…pai॰… aikā pañ‍ñatti| aikaina samuṭṭhānaina samuṭṭhāti – kāyatau ca cittatau ca samuṭṭhāti, na vācatau…pai॰…|

anādariyaṃ paṭic‍ca auguṇṭhitaina antaragharai gacchantassa duk‍kaṭaṃ kattha pañ‍ñattanti? sāvatthiyaṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ| kaṃ ārabbhāti? chabbaggiyai bhikkhū ārabbha| kismiṃ vatthusminti? chabbaggiyā  bhikkhū sasīsaṃ pārupitvā antaragharai gacchiṃsu, tasmiṃ vatthusmiṃ| aikā pañ‍ñatti| chan‍naṃ āpattisamuṭṭhānānaṃ aikaina samuṭṭhānaina samuṭṭhāti – kāyatau ca cittatau ca samuṭṭhāti, na vācatau…pai॰…|

anādariyaṃ paṭic‍ca auguṇṭhitaina antaragharai nisīdantassa duk‍kaṭaṃ kattha pañ‍ñattanti? sāvatthiyaṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ| kaṃ ārabbhāti? chabbaggiyai bhikkhū ārabbha| kismiṃ vatthusminti? chabbaggiyā bhikkhū sasīsaṃ pārupitvā antaragharai nisīdiṃsu, tasmiṃ vatthusmiṃ| aikā pañ‍ñatti| chan‍naṃ āpattisamuṭṭhānānaṃ aikaina samuṭṭhānaina samuṭṭhāti – kāyatau ca cittatau ca samuṭṭhāti, na vācatau…pai॰…|

anādariyaṃ paṭic‍ca uk‍kuṭikāya antaragharai gacchantassa duk‍kaṭaṃ…pai॰… aikā pañ‍ñatti| aikaina samuṭṭhānaina samuṭṭhāti – kāyatau ca cittatau ca samuṭṭhāti, na vācatau…pai॰…|

anādariyaṃ paṭic‍ca pal‍latthikāya pal‍lattikāya (ka॰) antaragharai nisīdantassa duk‍kaṭaṃ…pai॰… aikā pañ‍ñatti| aikaina samuṭṭhānaina samuṭṭhāti  – kāyatau ca cittatau ca samuṭṭhāti, na vācatau…pai॰…|

anādariyaṃ  paṭic‍ca asak‍kac‍caṃ piṇḍapātaṃ paṭiggaṇhantassa duk‍kaṭaṃ…pai॰… aikā pañ‍ñatti| aikaina samuṭṭhānaina samuṭṭhāti – kāyatau ca cittatau ca samuṭṭhāti, na vācatau…pai॰…|

anādariyaṃ paṭic‍ca tahaṃ tahaṃ aulaukaintaina piṇḍapātaṃ paṭiggaṇhantassa duk‍kaṭaṃ…pai॰… aikā pañ‍ñatti| aikaina samuṭṭhānaina samuṭṭhāti – kāyatau ca cittatau ca samuṭṭhāti, na vācatau…pai॰…|

anādariyaṃ paṭic‍ca sūpañ‍ñaiva bahuṃ paṭiggaṇhantassa duk‍kaṭaṃ…pai॰… aikā pañ‍ñatti| aikaina samuṭṭhānaina samuṭṭhāti – kāyatau ca cittatau ca samuṭṭhāti, na vācatau…pai॰…|

anādariyaṃ  paṭic‍ca thūpīkataṃ piṇḍapātaṃ paṭiggaṇhantassa duk‍kaṭaṃ…pai॰… aikā pañ‍ñatti| aikaina samuṭṭhānaina samuṭṭhāti – kāyatau ca cittatau ca samuṭṭhāti, na vācatau…pai॰…|

khambhakatavaggau tatiyau|

4. piṇḍapātavaggau

153. anādariyaṃ  paṭic‍ca asak‍kac‍caṃ piṇḍapātaṃ bhuñ‍jantassa duk‍kaṭaṃ…pai॰… aikā pañ‍ñatti| aikaina samuṭṭhānaina samuṭṭhāti – kāyatau ca cittatau ca samuṭṭhāti, na vācatau…pai॰…|

anādariyaṃ paṭic‍ca tahaṃ tahaṃ aulaukaintaina piṇḍapātaṃ bhuñ‍jantassa duk‍kaṭaṃ…pai॰… aikā pañ‍ñatti| aikaina samuṭṭhānaina samuṭṭhāti – kāyatau ca cittatau ca samuṭṭhāti, na vācatau…pai॰…|

anādariyaṃ  paṭic‍ca tahaṃ tahaṃ aumasitvā piṇḍapātaṃ bhuñ‍jantassa duk‍kaṭaṃ…pai॰… aikā pañ‍ñatti| aikaina samuṭṭhānaina samuṭṭhāti – kāyatau ca cittatau ca samuṭṭhāti, na vācatau…pai॰…|

anādariyaṃ paṭic‍ca sūpañ‍ñaiva bahuṃ bhuñ‍jantassa duk‍kaṭaṃ…pai॰… aikā pañ‍ñatti| aikaina samuṭṭhānaina samuṭṭhāti – kāyatau ca cittatau ca samuṭṭhāti, na vācatau…pai॰…|

anādariyaṃ paṭic‍ca thūpakatau aumadditvā piṇḍapātaṃ bhuñ‍jantassa duk‍kaṭaṃ…pai॰… aikā pañ‍ñatti| aikaina samuṭṭhānaina samuṭṭhāti – kāyatau ca cittatau ca samuṭṭhāti, na vācatau…pai॰…|

anādariyaṃ paṭic‍ca sūpaṃ vā byañ‍janaṃ vā audanaina paṭicchādaintassa duk‍kaṭaṃ…pai॰… aikā pañ‍ñatti| aikaina samuṭṭhānaina samuṭṭhāti – kāyatau ca cittatau ca samuṭṭhāti, na vācatau…pai॰…|

anādariyaṃ paṭic‍ca sūpaṃ vā audanaṃ vā agilānau attanau atthāya viñ‍ñāpaitvā bhuñ‍jantassa duk‍kaṭaṃ kattha pañ‍ñattanti? sāvatthiyaṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ| kaṃ ārabbhāti? chabbaggiyai bhikkhū ārabbha| kismiṃ vatthusminti? chabbaggiyā bhikkhū sūpampi audanampi attanau atthāya viñ‍ñāpaitvā bhuñ‍jiṃsu, tasmiṃ vatthusmiṃ| aikā  pañ‍ñatti, aikā anupañ‍ñatti| chan‍naṃ āpattisamuṭṭhānānaṃ dvīhi samuṭṭhānaihi samuṭṭhāti – siyā kāyatau ca cittatau ca samuṭṭhāti, na vācatau; siyā kāyatau ca vācatau ca cittatau ca samuṭṭhāti…pai॰…|

anādariyaṃ  paṭic‍ca ujjhānasañ‍ñinā paraisaṃ pattaṃ aulaukaintassa duk‍kaṭaṃ…pai॰… aikā pañ‍ñatti| aikaina samuṭṭhānaina samuṭṭhāti – kāyatau ca cittatau ca samuṭṭhāti, na vācatau…pai॰…|

anādariyaṃ paṭic‍ca mahantaṃ kabaḷaṃ karauntassa duk‍kaṭaṃ…pai॰… aikā pañ‍ñatti| aikaina samuṭṭhānaina samuṭṭhāti – kāyatau ca cittatau ca samuṭṭhāti, na vācatau…pai॰…|

anādariyaṃ paṭic‍ca dīghaṃ ālaupaṃ karauntassa duk‍kaṭaṃ…pai॰… aikā pañ‍ñatti| aikaina samuṭṭhānaina samuṭṭhāti – kāyatau ca cittatau ca samuṭṭhāti, na vācatau…pai॰…|

piṇḍapātavaggau catutthau|

5. kabaḷavaggau

154. anādariyaṃ paṭic‍ca anāhaṭai kabaḷai mukhadvāraṃ vivarantassa duk‍kaṭaṃ…pai॰… aikā pañ‍ñatti| aikaina samuṭṭhānaina samuṭṭhāti – kāyatau ca cittatau ca samuṭṭhāti, na vācatau…pai॰…|

anādariyaṃ paṭic‍ca bhuñ‍jamānaina sabbaṃ hatthaṃ mukhai pakkhipantassa duk‍kaṭaṃ…pai॰… aikā pañ‍ñatti| aikaina samuṭṭhānaina samuṭṭhāti – kāyatau ca cittatau ca samuṭṭhāti, na vācatau…pai॰…|

anādariyaṃ paṭic‍ca sakabaḷaina mukhaina byāharantassa duk‍kaṭaṃ kattha pañ‍ñattanti? sāvatthiyaṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ| kaṃ ārabbhāti? chabbaggiyai bhikkhū ārabbha| kismiṃ vatthusminti? chabbaggiyā bhikkhū sakabaḷaina mukhaina byāhariṃsu, tasmiṃ vatthusmiṃ| aikā pañ‍ñatti| chan‍naṃ āpattisamuṭṭhānānaṃ aikaina samuṭṭhānaina samuṭṭhāti – kāyatau ca vācatau ca cittatau ca samuṭṭhāti…pai॰…|

anādariyaṃ  paṭic‍ca piṇḍukkhaipakaṃ bhuñ‍jantassa duk‍kaṭaṃ…pai॰… aikā  pañ‍ñatti| aikaina samuṭṭhānaina samuṭṭhāti – kāyatau ca cittatau ca samuṭṭhāti, na vācatau…pai॰…|

anādariyaṃ  paṭic‍ca kabaḷāvacchaidakaṃ bhuñ‍jantassa duk‍kaṭaṃ…pai॰… aikā pañ‍ñatti| aikaina samuṭṭhānaina samuṭṭhāti – kāyatau ca cittatau ca samuṭṭhāti, na vācatau…pai॰…|

anādariyaṃ paṭic‍ca avagaṇḍakārakaṃ bhuñ‍jantassa duk‍kaṭaṃ…pai॰… aikā pañ‍ñatti| aikaina samuṭṭhānaina samuṭṭhāti – kāyatau ca cittatau ca samuṭṭhāti, na vācatau…pai॰…|

anādariyaṃ paṭic‍ca hatthaniddhunakaṃ bhuñ‍jantassa duk‍kaṭaṃ…pai॰… aikā pañ‍ñatti| aikaina samuṭṭhānaina samuṭṭhāti – kāyatau ca cittatau ca samuṭṭhāti, na vācatau…pai॰…|

anādariyaṃ paṭic‍ca sitthāvakārakaṃ bhuñ‍jantassa duk‍kaṭaṃ…pai॰… aikā pañ‍ñatti| aikaina samuṭṭhānaina samuṭṭhāti – kāyatau ca cittatau ca samuṭṭhāti, na vācatau…pai॰…|

anādariyaṃ paṭic‍ca jivhānicchārakaṃ bhuñ‍jantassa duk‍kaṭaṃ…pai॰… aikā pañ‍ñatti| aikaina samuṭṭhānaina samuṭṭhāti – kāyatau ca cittatau ca samuṭṭhāti, na vācatau…pai॰…|

anādariyaṃ paṭic‍ca capucapukārakaṃ bhuñ‍jantassa duk‍kaṭaṃ…pai॰… aikā pañ‍ñatti| aikaina samuṭṭhānaina samuṭṭhāti – kāyatau ca cittatau ca samuṭṭhāti, na vācatau…pai॰…|

kabaḷavaggau pañ‍camau|

6. surusuruvaggau

155. anādariyaṃ  paṭic‍ca surusurukārakaṃ bhuñ‍jantassa duk‍kaṭaṃ kattha pañ‍ñattanti? kausambiyaṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ| kaṃ ārabbhāti? sambahulai bhikkhū ārabbha| kismiṃ vatthusminti? sambahulā bhikkhū surusurukārakaṃ khīraṃ piviṃsu, tasmiṃ vatthusmiṃ| aikā pañ‍ñatti| chan‍naṃ āpattisamuṭṭhānānaṃ aikaina samuṭṭhānaina samuṭṭhāti – kāyatau ca cittatau ca samuṭṭhāti, na vācatau…pai॰…|

anādariyaṃ paṭic‍ca hatthanil‍laihakaṃ bhuñ‍jantassa duk‍kaṭaṃ…pai॰… aikā pañ‍ñatti| aikaina samuṭṭhānaina samuṭṭhāti – kāyatau ca cittatau ca samuṭṭhāti, na vācatau…pai॰…|

anādariyaṃ  paṭic‍ca pattanil‍laihakaṃ bhuñ‍jantassa duk‍kaṭaṃ…pai॰… aikā pañ‍ñatti| aikaina samuṭṭhānaina samuṭṭhāti – kāyatau ca cittatau ca samuṭṭhāti, na vācatau…pai॰…|

anādariyaṃ paṭic‍ca auṭṭhanil‍laihakaṃ bhuñ‍jantassa duk‍kaṭaṃ…pai॰… aikā pañ‍ñatti| aikaina samuṭṭhānaina samuṭṭhāti – kāyatau ca cittatau ca samuṭṭhāti, na vācatau…pai॰…|

anādariyaṃ paṭic‍ca sāmisaina hatthaina pānīyathālakaṃ paṭiggaṇhantassa duk‍kaṭaṃ kattha pañ‍ñattanti? bhaggaisu pañ‍ñattaṃ| kaṃ ārabbhāti? sambahulai bhikkhū ārabbha| kismiṃ vatthusminti? sambahulā bhikkhū sāmisaina hatthaina pānīyathālakaṃ paṭiggahaisuṃ, tasmiṃ vatthusmiṃ| aikā pañ‍ñatti| chan‍naṃ āpattisamuṭṭhānānaṃ aikaina samuṭṭhānaina  samuṭṭhāti – kāyatau ca cittatau ca samuṭṭhāti, na vācatau…pai॰…|

anādariyaṃ paṭic‍ca sasitthakaṃ pattadhauvanaṃ antaragharai chaḍḍaintassa duk‍kaṭaṃ kattha pañ‍ñattanti? bhaggaisu pañ‍ñattaṃ| kaṃ ārabbhāti? sambahulai bhikkhū ārabbha| kismiṃ vatthusminti? sambahulā bhikkhū sasitthakaṃ pattadhauvanaṃ antaragharai chaḍḍaisuṃ, tasmiṃ vatthusmiṃ| aikā pañ‍ñatti| chan‍naṃ āpattisamuṭṭhānānaṃ aikaina samuṭṭhānaina samuṭṭhāti – kāyatau ca cittatau ca samuṭṭhāti, na vācatau…pai॰…|

anādariyaṃ paṭic‍ca chattapāṇissa dhammaṃ daisaintassa duk‍kaṭaṃ kattha pañ‍ñattanti? sāvatthiyaṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ| kaṃ ārabbhāti? chabbaggiyai bhikkhū ārabbha| kismiṃ vatthusminti? chabbaggiyā bhikkhū chattapāṇissa dhammaṃ daisaisuṃ, tasmiṃ vatthusmiṃ| aikā pañ‍ñatti, aikā anupañ‍ñatti| chan‍naṃ āpattisamuṭṭhānānaṃ aikaina samuṭṭhānaina samuṭṭhāti – vācatau ca cittatau ca samuṭṭhāti, na kāyatau…pai॰…|

anādariyaṃ  paṭic‍ca daṇḍapāṇissa dhammaṃ daisaintassa duk‍kaṭaṃ…pai॰… aikā pañ‍ñatti, aikā anupañ‍ñatti| chan‍naṃ āpattisamuṭṭhānānaṃ aikaina samuṭṭhānaina samuṭṭhāti – vācatau ca cittatau ca samuṭṭhāti, na kāyatau…pai॰…|

anādariyaṃ  paṭic‍ca satthapāṇissa dhammaṃ daisaintassa duk‍kaṭaṃ…pai॰… aikā pañ‍ñatti, aikā  anupañ‍ñatti| chan‍naṃ āpattisamuṭṭhānānaṃ aikaina samuṭṭhānaina samuṭṭhāti – vācatau ca cittatau ca samuṭṭhāti, na kāyatau…pai॰…|

anādariyaṃ paṭic‍ca āvudhapāṇissa dhammaṃ daisaintassa duk‍kaṭaṃ…pai॰… aikā pañ‍ñatti, aikā anupañ‍ñatti| chan‍naṃ āpattisamuṭṭhānānaṃ aikaina samuṭṭhānaina samuṭṭhāti – vācatau ca cittatau ca samuṭṭhāti, na kāyatau…pai॰…|

surusuruvaggau chaṭṭhau|

7. pādukavaggau

156. anādariyaṃ paṭic‍ca pādukāruḷhassa dhammaṃ daisaintassa duk‍kaṭaṃ…pai॰… aikā pañ‍ñatti, aikā anupañ‍ñatti| chan‍naṃ āpattisamuṭṭhānānaṃ aikaina samuṭṭhānaina samuṭṭhāti – vācatau ca cittatau ca samuṭṭhāti, na kāyatau…pai॰…|

anādariyaṃ paṭic‍ca upāhanāruḷhassa dhammaṃ daisaintassa duk‍kaṭaṃ…pai॰… aikā pañ‍ñatti, aikā anupañ‍ñatti| chan‍naṃ āpattisamuṭṭhānānaṃ aikaina samuṭṭhānaina samuṭṭhāti – vācatau ca cittatau ca samuṭṭhāti, na kāyatau…pai॰…|

anādariyaṃ paṭic‍ca yānagatassa dhammaṃ daisaintassa duk‍kaṭaṃ…pai॰… aikā pañ‍ñatti, aikā anupañ‍ñatti| chan‍naṃ āpattisamuṭṭhānānaṃ aikaina samuṭṭhānaina samuṭṭhāti – vācatau ca cittatau ca samuṭṭhāti, na kāyatau…pai॰…|

anādariyaṃ paṭic‍ca sayanagatassa dhammaṃ daisaintassa duk‍kaṭaṃ…pai॰… aikā pañ‍ñatti, aikā anupañ‍ñatti| chan‍naṃ āpattisamuṭṭhānānaṃ aikaina samuṭṭhānaina samuṭṭhāti – vācatau ca cittatau ca samuṭṭhāti, na kāyatau…pai॰…|

anādariyaṃ paṭic‍ca pal‍latthikāya nisin‍nassa dhammaṃ daisaintassa duk‍kaṭaṃ…pai॰… aikā pañ‍ñatti, aikā anupañ‍ñatti| chan‍naṃ āpattisamuṭṭhānānaṃ aikaina samuṭṭhānai  samuṭṭhāti – vācatau ca cittatau ca samuṭṭhāti, na kāyatau…pai॰…|

anādariyaṃ  paṭic‍ca vaiṭhitasīsassa  dhammaṃ daisaintassa duk‍kaṭaṃ…pai॰… aikā pañ‍ñatti, aikā anupañ‍ñatti| chan‍naṃ āpattisamuṭṭhānānaṃ aikaina samuṭṭhānaina samuṭṭhāti – vācatau ca cittatau ca samuṭṭhāti, na kāyatau…pai॰…|

anādariyaṃ paṭic‍ca auguṇṭhitasīsassa dhammaṃ daisaintassa duk‍kaṭaṃ…pai॰… aikā pañ‍ñatti, aikā anupañ‍ñatti| chan‍naṃ āpattisamuṭṭhānānaṃ aikaina samuṭṭhānaina samuṭṭhāti – vācatau ca cittatau ca samuṭṭhāti, na kāyatau…pai॰…|

anādariyaṃ paṭic‍ca chamāyaṃ nisīditvā āsanai nisin‍nassa dhammaṃ daisaintassa duk‍kaṭaṃ…pai॰… aikā pañ‍ñatti, aikā anupañ‍ñatti| chan‍naṃ āpattisamuṭṭhānānaṃ aikaina samuṭṭhānaina samuṭṭhāti  – kāyatau ca vācatau ca cittatau ca samuṭṭhāti…pai॰…|

anādariyaṃ paṭic‍ca nīcai āsanai nisīditvā uc‍cai āsanai nisin‍nassa dhammaṃ daisaintassa duk‍kaṭaṃ…pai॰… aikā pañ‍ñatti, aikā anupañ‍ñatti| chan‍naṃ āpattisamuṭṭhānānaṃ aikaina samuṭṭhānaina samuṭṭhāti – kāyatau ca vācatau ca cittatau ca samuṭṭhāti…pai॰…|

anādariyaṃ paṭic‍ca ṭhitaina nisin‍nassa dhammaṃ daisaintassa duk‍kaṭaṃ…pai॰… aikā pañ‍ñatti, aikā anupañ‍ñatti| chan‍naṃ āpattisamuṭṭhānānaṃ aikaina samuṭṭhānaina samuṭṭhāti – kāyatau ca vācatau ca cittatau ca samuṭṭhāti…pai॰…|

anādariyaṃ paṭic‍ca pacchatau gacchantaina puratau gacchantassa dhammaṃ daisaintassa duk‍kaṭaṃ…pai॰… aikā pañ‍ñatti, aikā anupañ‍ñatti| chan‍naṃ āpattisamuṭṭhānānaṃ aikaina samuṭṭhānaina samuṭṭhāti – kāyatau ca vācatau ca cittatau ca samuṭṭhāti…pai॰…|

anādariyaṃ paṭic‍ca uppathaina gacchantaina pathaina gacchantassa dhammaṃ daisaintassa duk‍kaṭaṃ…pai॰… aikā pañ‍ñatti, aikā anupañ‍ñatti| chan‍naṃ āpattisamuṭṭhānānaṃ aikaina samuṭṭhānaina samuṭṭhāti – kāyatau ca vācatau ca cittatau ca samuṭṭhāti…pai॰…|

anādariyaṃ paṭic‍ca ṭhitaina uc‍cāraṃ vā passāvaṃ vā karauntassa duk‍kaṭaṃ…pai॰… aikā pañ‍ñatti, aikā anupañ‍ñatti| chan‍naṃ āpattisamuṭṭhānānaṃ aikaina samuṭṭhānai  samuṭṭhāti – kāyatau  ca cittatau ca samuṭṭhāti, na vācatau…pai॰…|

anādariyaṃ paṭic‍ca haritai uc‍cāraṃ vā passāvaṃ vā khaiḷaṃ vā karauntassa duk‍kaṭaṃ…pai॰… aikā pañ‍ñatti, aikā anupañ‍ñatti| chan‍naṃ āpattisamuṭṭhānānaṃ aikaina samuṭṭhānaina samuṭṭhāti – kāyatau ca cittatau ca samuṭṭhāti, na vācatau…pai॰…|

anādariyaṃ paṭic‍ca udakai uc‍cāraṃ vā passāvaṃ vā khaiḷaṃ vā karauntassa duk‍kaṭaṃ…pai॰… kattha pañ‍ñattanti? sāvatthiyaṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ| kaṃ ārabbhāti? chabbaggiyai bhikkhū ārabbha| kismiṃ vatthusminti? chabbaggiyā bhikkhū udakai uc‍cārampi passāvampi khaiḷampi akaṃsu, tasmiṃ vatthusmiṃ| aikā pañ‍ñatti, aikā anupañ‍ñatti| chan‍naṃ āpattisamuṭṭhānānaṃ aikaina samuṭṭhānaina samuṭṭhāti – kāyatau ca cittatau ca samuṭṭhāti, na vācatau…pai॰…|

pādukavaggau sattamau|

pañ‍casattati saikhiyā niṭṭhitā|

tassuddānaṃ –

parimaṇḍalaṃ paṭicchan‍naṃ, susaṃvutaukkhittacakkhu|

ukkhittauj‍jagghikā saddau, tayau caiva pacālanā||

khambhaṃ auguṇṭhitau caivuk‍kuṭipal‍latthikāya ca|

sak‍kac‍caṃ  pattasañ‍ñī ca, samasūpaṃ samatittikaṃ samatitthikaṃ (ka॰)||

sak‍kac‍caṃ pattasañ‍ñī ca, sapadānaṃ samasūpakaṃ|

thūpakatau paṭicchan‍naṃ, viñ‍ñattujjhānasañ‍ñinā||

na mahantaṃ maṇḍalaṃ dvāraṃ, sabbaṃ hatthaṃ na byāharai|

ukkhaipau chaidanā gaṇḍau, dhunaṃ sitthāvakārakaṃ||

jivhānicchārakañ‍caiva, capucapu surusuru|

hatthau  pattau ca auṭṭhau ca, sāmisaṃ sitthakaina ca||

chattapāṇissa  saddhammaṃ, na daisainti tathāgatā|

aivamaiva daṇḍapāṇissa, satthaāvudhapāṇinaṃ||

pādukā upāhanā caiva, yānasaiyyāgatassa ca|

pal‍latthikā nisin‍nassa, vaiṭhitauguṇṭhitassa ca||

chamā nīcāsanai ṭhānai, pacchatau uppathaina ca|

ṭhitakaina na kātabbaṃ, haritai udakamhi cāti||

taisaṃ vaggānamuddānaṃ –

parimaṇḍalauj‍jagghi, khambhaṃ piṇḍaṃ tathaiva ca|

kabaḷā surusuru ca, pādukaina ca sattamāti||

mahāvibhaṅgai katthapañ‍ñattivārau niṭṭhitau|

2. katāpattivārau

1. pārājikakaṇḍaṃ

157. maithunaṃ  dhammaṃ paṭisaivantau kati āpattiyau āpaj‍jati? maithunaṃ dhammaṃ paṭisaivantau tissau āpattiyau āpaj‍jati| akkhāyitai sarīrai maithunaṃ dhammaṃ paṭisaivati, āpatti pārājikassa; yaibhuyyaina khāyitai  sarīrai maithunaṃ dhammaṃ paṭisaivati, āpatti thul‍lac‍cayassa; vaṭṭakatai vivaṭakatai (syā॰) mukhai acchupantaṃ aṅgajātaṃ pavaisaiti, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa – maithunaṃ dhammaṃ paṭisaivantau imā tissau āpattiyau āpaj‍jati|

158. adin‍naṃ ādiyantau kati āpattiyau āpaj‍jati? adin‍naṃ ādiyantau tissau āpattiyau āpaj‍jati| pañ‍camāsakaṃ vā atiraikapañ‍camāsakaṃ vā agghanakaṃ adin‍naṃ thaiyyasaṅkhātaṃ ādiyati, āpatti pārājikassa; atiraikamāsakaṃ vā ūnapañ‍camāsakaṃ vā agghanakaṃ adin‍naṃ thaiyyasaṅkhātaṃ ādiyati, āpatti thul‍lac‍cayassa; māsakaṃ vā ūnamāsakaṃ vā agghanakaṃ adin‍naṃ thaiyyasaṅkhātaṃ ādiyati, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa – adin‍naṃ ādiyantau imā tissau āpattiyau āpaj‍jati|

159. sañ‍cic‍ca  manussaviggahaṃ jīvitā vauraupaintau kati āpattiyau āpaj‍jati? sañ‍cic‍ca manussaviggahaṃ jīvitā vauraupaintau tissau āpattiyau āpaj‍jati| manussaṃ audissa aupātaṃ khaṇati ‘‘papatitvā marissatī’’ti, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa; papatitai dukkhā vaidanā uppaj‍jati, āpatti thul‍lac‍cayassa; marati, āpatti pārājikassa – sañ‍cic‍ca manussaviggahaṃ jīvitā vauraupaintau imā tissau āpattiyau āpaj‍jati|

160. asantaṃ abhūtaṃ uttarimanussadhammaṃ ul‍lapantau kati āpattiyau āpaj‍jati? asantaṃ  abhūtaṃ uttarimanussadhammaṃ ul‍lapantau tissau āpattiyau āpaj‍jati| pāpicchau icchāpakatau asantaṃ abhūtaṃ uttarimanussadhammaṃ ul‍lapati, āpatti pārājikassa; ‘‘yau tai vihārai  vasati, sau bhikkhu arahā’’ti bhaṇati, paṭivijānantassa āpatti thul‍lac‍cayassa; na paṭivijānantassa āpatti duk‍kaṭassa – asantaṃ abhūtaṃ uttarimanussadhammaṃ ul‍lapantau imā tissau āpattiyau āpaj‍jati|

cattārau pārājikā niṭṭhitā|

2. saṅghādisaisakaṇḍaṃ

161. upak‍kamitvā asuciṃ maucaintau tissau āpattiyau āpaj‍jati| caitaiti upak‍kamati  muc‍cati, āpatti saṅghādisaisassa; caitaiti upak‍kamati na muc‍cati, āpatti thul‍lac‍cayassa; payaugai duk‍kaṭaṃ|

mātugāmaina saddhiṃ kāyasaṃsaggaṃ samāpaj‍jantau tissau āpattiyau āpaj‍jati| kāyaina kāyaṃ āmasati, āpatti saṅghādisaisassa; kāyaina kāyapaṭibaddhaṃ āmasati, āpatti thul‍lac‍cayassa; kāyapaṭibaddhaina kāyapaṭibaddhaṃ āmasati, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa|

mātugāmaṃ duṭṭhul‍lāhi vācāhi aubhāsaintau tissau āpattiyau āpaj‍jati| vac‍camaggaṃ passāvamaggaṃ ādissa vaṇṇampi bhaṇati, avaṇṇampi bhaṇati, āpatti saṅghādisaisassa; vac‍camaggaṃ passāvamaggaṃ ṭhapaitvā adhakkhakaṃ ubbhajāṇumaṇḍalaṃ ādissa vaṇṇampi bhaṇati avaṇṇampi bhaṇati, āpatti thul‍lac‍cayassa; kāyapaṭibaddhaṃ ādissa vaṇṇampi bhaṇati avaṇṇampi bhaṇati, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa|

attakāmapāricariyā  vaṇṇaṃ bhāsantau tissau āpattiyau āpaj‍jati| mātugāmassa santikai attakāmapāricariyāya vaṇṇaṃ bhāsati, āpatti saṅghādisaisassa; paṇḍakassa santikai attakāmapāricariyāya vaṇṇaṃ bhāsati, āpatti thul‍lac‍cayassa; tiracchānagatassa santikai attakāmapāricariyāya vaṇṇaṃ bhāsati, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa|

sañ‍carittaṃ samāpaj‍jantau tissau āpattiyau āpaj‍jati| paṭiggaṇhāti vīmaṃsati pac‍cāharati, āpatti saṅghādisaisassa; paṭiggaṇhāti vīmaṃsati na pac‍cāharati, āpatti thul‍lac‍cayassa; paṭiggaṇhāti na vīmaṃsati na pac‍cāharati, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa|

sañ‍ñācikāya kuṭiṃ kārāpaintau tissau āpattiyau āpaj‍jati| kārāpaiti, payaugai duk‍kaṭaṃ; aikaṃ piṇḍaṃ anāgatai, āpatti thul‍lac‍cayassa; tasmiṃ piṇḍai āgatai, āpatti saṅghādisaisassa|

mahal‍lakaṃ vihāraṃ kārāpaintau tissau āpattiyau āpaj‍jati| kārāpaiti, payaugai duk‍kaṭaṃ; aikaṃ piṇḍaṃ anāgatai, āpatti thul‍lac‍cayassa; tasmiṃ piṇḍai āgatai, āpatti saṅghādisaisassa|

bhikkhuṃ amūlakaina pārājikaina dhammaina anuddhaṃsaintau tissau āpattiyau āpaj‍jati| anaukāsaṃ kārāpaitvā cāvanādhippāyau vadaiti, āpatti saṅghādisaisaina duk‍kaṭassa; aukāsaṃ kārāpaitvā ak‍kausādhippāyau vadaiti, āpatti aumasavādassa|

bhikkhuṃ añ‍ñabhāgiyassa adhikaraṇassa kiñ‍ci daisaṃ laisamattaṃ upādāya pārājikaina dhammaina anuddhaṃsaintau tissau āpattiyau āpaj‍jati| anaukāsaṃ kārāpaitvā cāvanādhippāyau vadaiti, āpatti saṅghādisaisaina duk‍kaṭassa; aukāsaṃ kārāpaitvā ak‍kausādhippāyau vadaiti, āpatti aumasavādassa|

saṅghabhaidakau bhikkhu yāvatatiyaṃ samanubhāsanāya na paṭinissaj‍jantau tissau āpattiyau āpaj‍jati| ñattiyā duk‍kaṭaṃ; dvīhi kammavācāhi thul‍lac‍cayā; kammavācāpariyausānai āpatti saṅghādisaisassa|

bhaidakānuvattakā  bhikkhū yāvatatiyaṃ samanubhāsanāya samanubhāsiyamānā (syā॰) na paṭinissaj‍jantā tissau āpattiyau āpaj‍janti| ñattiyā duk‍kaṭaṃ; dvīhi kammavācāhi  thul‍lac‍cayā; kammavācāpariyausānai āpatti saṅghādisaisassa|

dubbacau bhikkhu yāvatatiyaṃ samanubhāsanāya samanubhāsiyamānau (syā॰) na paṭinissaj‍jantau tissau āpattiyau āpaj‍jati| ñattiyā duk‍kaṭaṃ; dvīhi kammavācāhi thul‍lac‍cayā; kammavācāpariyausānai āpatti saṅghādisaisassa|

kuladūsakau bhikkhu yāvatatiyaṃ samanubhāsanāya na paṭinissaj‍jantau tissau āpattiyau āpaj‍jati| ñattiyā duk‍kaṭaṃ; dvīhi kammavācāhi thul‍lac‍cayā; kammavācāpariyausānai āpatti saṅghādisaisassa|

tairasa saṅghādisaisā niṭṭhitā|

3. nissaggiyakaṇḍaṃ

1. kathinavaggau

162. atiraikacīvaraṃ  dasāhaṃ atik‍kāmaintau aikaṃ āpattiṃ āpaj‍jati| nissaggiyaṃ pācittiyaṃ|

aikarattaṃ ticīvaraina vippavasantau aikaṃ āpattiṃ āpaj‍jati| nissaggiyaṃ pācittiyaṃ|

akālacīvaraṃ paṭiggahaitvā māsaṃ atik‍kāmaintau aikaṃ āpattiṃ āpaj‍jati| nissaggiyaṃ pācittiyaṃ|

añ‍ñātikāya bhikkhuniyā purāṇacīvaraṃ dhauvāpaintau dvai āpattiyau āpaj‍jati| dhauvāpaiti, payaugai duk‍kaṭaṃ; dhauvāpitai nissaggiyaṃ pācittiyaṃ|

añ‍ñātikāya bhikkhuniyā hatthatau cīvaraṃ paṭiggaṇhantau dvai āpattiyau āpaj‍jati| gaṇhāti, payaugai duk‍kaṭaṃ; gahitai nissaggiyaṃ pācittiyaṃ|

añ‍ñātakaṃ  gahapatiṃ vā gahapatāniṃ vā cīvaraṃ viñ‍ñāpaintau dvai āpattiyau āpaj‍jati| viñ‍ñāpaiti, payaugai duk‍kaṭaṃ; viñ‍ñāpitai nissaggiyaṃ pācittiyaṃ|

añ‍ñātakaṃ  gahapatiṃ vā gahapatāniṃ vā tatuttari cīvaraṃ viñ‍ñāpaintau dvai āpattiyau āpaj‍jati| viñ‍ñāpaiti, payaugai duk‍kaṭaṃ; viñ‍ñāpitai nissaggiyaṃ pācittiyaṃ|

pubbai appavāritau añ‍ñātakaṃ gahapatikaṃ upasaṅkamitvā cīvarai vikappaṃ āpaj‍jantau dvai āpattiyau āpaj‍jati| vikappaṃ āpaj‍jati, payaugai duk‍kaṭaṃ; vikappaṃ āpan‍nai nissaggiyaṃ pācittiyaṃ|

pubbai appavāritau añ‍ñātakai gahapatikai upasaṅkamitvā cīvarai vikappaṃ āpaj‍jantau dvai āpattiyau āpaj‍jati| vikappaṃ āpaj‍jati, payaugai duk‍kaṭaṃ; vikappaṃ āpan‍nai nissaggiyaṃ pācittiyaṃ|

atiraikatikkhattuṃ caudanāya atiraikachakkhattuṃ ṭhānaina cīvaraṃ abhinipphādaintau dvai āpattiyau āpaj‍jati| abhinipphādaiti, payaugai duk‍kaṭaṃ; abhinipphāditai nissaggiyaṃ pācittiyaṃ|

kathinavaggau paṭhamau|

2. kausiyavaggau

163. kausiyamissakaṃ santhataṃ kārāpaintau dvai āpattiyau āpaj‍jati| kārāpaiti, payaugai duk‍kaṭaṃ; kārāpitai nissaggiyaṃ pācittiyaṃ|

suddhakāḷakānaṃ aiḷakalaumānaṃ santhataṃ kārāpaintau dvai āpattiyau āpaj‍jati| kārāpaiti, payaugai duk‍kaṭaṃ; kārāpitai nissaggiyaṃ pācittiyaṃ|

anādiyitvā  tulaṃ audātānaṃ tulaṃ gaucariyānaṃ navaṃ santhataṃ kārāpaintau dvai āpattiyau āpaj‍jati| kārāpaiti, payaugai duk‍kaṭaṃ; kārāpitai nissaggiyaṃ pācittiyaṃ|

anuvassaṃ santhataṃ kārāpaintau dvai āpattiyau āpaj‍jati| kārāpaiti, payaugai duk‍kaṭaṃ; kārāpitai nissaggiyaṃ pācittiyaṃ|

anādiyitvā  purāṇasanthatassa sāmantā sugatavidatthiṃ navaṃ nisīdanasanthataṃ kārāpaintau dvai āpattiyau āpaj‍jati| kārāpaiti, payaugai duk‍kaṭaṃ; kārāpitai nissaggiyaṃ pācittiyaṃ|

aiḷakalaumāni  paṭiggahaitvā tiyaujanaṃ atik‍kāmaintau dvai āpattiyau āpaj‍jati| paṭhamaṃ pādaṃ tiyaujanaṃ atik‍kāmaiti, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa; dutiyaṃ pādaṃ atik‍kāmaiti, nissaggiyaṃ pācittiyaṃ|

añ‍ñātikāya bhikkhuniyā aiḷakalaumāni dhauvāpaintau dvai āpattiyau āpaj‍jati| dhauvāpaiti, payaugai duk‍kaṭaṃ; dhauvāpitai nissaggiyaṃ pācittiyaṃ|

rūpiyaṃ paṭiggaṇhantau dvai āpattiyau āpaj‍jati| gaṇhāti, payaugai duk‍kaṭaṃ; gahitai nissaggiyaṃ pācittiyaṃ|

nānappakārakaṃ rūpiyasaṃvauhāraṃ samāpaj‍jantau dvai āpattiyau āpaj‍jati| samāpaj‍jati, payaugai duk‍kaṭaṃ; samāpan‍nai nissaggiyaṃ pācittiyaṃ|

nānappakārakaṃ kayavik‍kayaṃ samāpaj‍jantau dvai āpattiyau āpaj‍jati| samāpaj‍jati, payaugai duk‍kaṭaṃ; samāpan‍nai nissaggiyaṃ pācittiyaṃ|

kausiyavaggau dutiyau|

3. pattavaggau

164. atiraikapattaṃ dasāhaṃ atik‍kāmaintau aikaṃ āpattiṃ āpaj‍jati| nissaggiyaṃ pācittiyaṃ|

ūnapañ‍cabandhanaina  pattaina añ‍ñaṃ navaṃ pattaṃ caitāpaintau dvai āpattiyau āpaj‍jati| caitāpaiti, payaugai duk‍kaṭaṃ; caitāpitai nissaggiyaṃ pācittiyaṃ|

bhaisaj‍jāni paṭiggahaitvā sattāhaṃ atik‍kāmaintau aikaṃ āpattiṃ āpaj‍jati| nissaggiyaṃ pācittiyaṃ|

atiraikamāsai  saisai gimhānai vassikasāṭikacīvaraṃ pariyaisantau dvai āpattiyau āpaj‍jati| pariyaisati, payaugai duk‍kaṭaṃ; pariyiṭṭhai nissaggiyaṃ pācittiyaṃ|

bhikkhussa sāmaṃ cīvaraṃ datvā kupitau anattamanau acchindantau dvai āpattiyau āpaj‍jati| acchindati, payaugai duk‍kaṭaṃ; acchin‍nai nissaggiyaṃ pācittiyaṃ|

sāmaṃ  suttaṃ viñ‍ñāpaitvā tantavāyaihi cīvaraṃ vāyāpaintau dvai āpattiyau āpaj‍jati| vāyāpaiti, payaugai duk‍kaṭaṃ; vāyāpitai nissaggiyaṃ pācittiyaṃ|

pubbai appavāritau añ‍ñātakassa gahapatikassa tantavāyai upasaṅkamitvā cīvarai vikappaṃ āpaj‍jantau dvai āpattiyau āpaj‍jati| vikappaṃ āpaj‍jati, payaugai duk‍kaṭaṃ; vikappaṃ āpan‍nai nissaggiyaṃ pācittiyaṃ|

ac‍caikacīvaraṃ  paṭiggahaitvā cīvarakālasamayaṃ atik‍kāmaintau aikaṃ āpattiṃ āpaj‍jati| nissaggiyaṃ pācittiyaṃ|

tiṇṇaṃ  cīvarānaṃ añ‍ñataraṃ cīvaraṃ antaragharai nikkhipitvā atiraikachārattaṃ vippavasantau aikaṃ āpattiṃ āpaj‍jati| nissaggiyaṃ pācittiyaṃ|

jānaṃ saṅghikaṃ lābhaṃ pariṇataṃ attanau pariṇāmaintau dvai āpattiyau āpaj‍jati| pariṇāmaiti, payaugai duk‍kaṭaṃ; pariṇāmitai nissaggiyaṃ pācittiyaṃ|

pattavaggau tatiyau|

tiṃsa nissaggiyā pācittiyā niṭṭhitā|

4. pācittiyakaṇḍaṃ

1. musāvādavaggau

165. sampajānamusāvādaṃ bhāsantau kati āpattiyau āpaj‍jati? sampajānamusāvādaṃ bhāsantau pañ‍ca āpattiyau āpaj‍jati| pāpicchau icchāpakatau asantaṃ abhūtaṃ uttarimanussadhammaṃ  ul‍lapati, āpatti pārājikassa; bhikkhuṃ amūlakaina pārājikaina dhammaina anuddhaṃsaiti, āpatti saṅghādisaisassa; ‘‘yau tai vihārai vasati, sau bhikkhu arahā’’ti bhaṇati, paṭivijānantassa āpatti thul‍lac‍cayassa; na paṭivijānantassa āpatti duk‍kaṭassa; sampajānamusāvādai pācittiyaṃ – sampajānamusāvādaṃ bhāsantau imā pañ‍ca āpattiyau āpaj‍jati|

aumasantau  dvai āpattiyau āpaj‍jati| upasampan‍naṃ aumasati, āpatti pācittiyassa; anupasampan‍naṃ aumasati, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa|

paisuñ‍ñaṃ upasaṃharantau dvai āpattiyau āpaj‍jati| upasampan‍nassa  paisuñ‍ñaṃ upasaṃharati, āpatti pācittiyassa; anupasampan‍nassa paisuñ‍ñaṃ upasaṃharati, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa|

anupasampan‍naṃ padasau dhammaṃ vācaintau dvai āpattiyau āpaj‍jati| vācaiti, payaugai duk‍kaṭaṃ; padai padai āpatti pācittiyassa|

anupasampan‍naina uttaridirattatirattaṃ sahasaiyyaṃ kappaintau dvai āpattiyau āpaj‍jati| nipaj‍jati, payaugai duk‍kaṭaṃ; nipan‍nai āpatti pācittiyassa|

mātugāmaina sahasaiyyaṃ kappaintau dvai āpattiyau āpaj‍jati| nipaj‍jati, payaugai duk‍kaṭaṃ; nipan‍nai āpatti pācittiyassa|

mātugāmassa uttarichappañ‍cavācāhi dhammaṃ daisaintau dvai āpattiyau āpaj‍jati| daisaiti, payaugai duk‍kaṭaṃ; padai padai āpatti pācittiyassa|

anupasampan‍nassa uttarimanussadhammaṃ bhūtaṃ āraucaintau dvai āpattiyau āpaj‍jati| āraucaiti, payaugai duk‍kaṭaṃ; āraucitai āpatti pācittiyassa|

bhikkhussa duṭṭhul‍laṃ āpattiṃ anupasampan‍nassa āraucaintau dvai āpattiyau āpaj‍jati| āraucaiti, payaugai duk‍kaṭaṃ; āraucitai āpatti pācittiyassa|

pathaviṃ  khaṇantau dvai āpattiyau āpaj‍jati| khaṇati, payaugai duk‍kaṭaṃ; pahārai pahārai āpatti pācittiyassa|

musāvādavaggau paṭhamau|

2. bhūtagāmavaggau

166. bhūtagāmaṃ  pātaintau dvai āpattiyau āpaj‍jati| pātaiti, payaugai duk‍kaṭaṃ; pahārai pahārai āpatti pācittiyassa|

añ‍ñainañ‍ñaṃ  paṭicarantau dvai āpattiyau āpaj‍jati| anāraupitai añ‍ñavādakai añ‍ñainañ‍ñaṃ paṭicarati, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa; āraupitai añ‍ñavādakai añ‍ñainañ‍ñaṃ paṭicarati, āpatti pācittiyassa|

bhikkhuṃ ujjhāpaintau dvai āpattiyau āpaj‍jati| ujjhāpaiti, payaugai duk‍kaṭaṃ; ujjhāpitai āpatti pācittiyassa|

saṅghikaṃ mañ‍caṃ vā pīṭhaṃ vā bhisiṃ vā kaucchaṃ vā ajjhaukāsai santharitvā anuddharitvā anāpucchā pak‍kamantau dvai āpattiyau āpaj‍jati| paṭhamaṃ pādaṃ laiḍḍupātaṃ atik‍kāmaiti, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa; dutiyaṃ pādaṃ atik‍kāmaiti, āpatti pācittiyassa|

saṅghikai vihārai saiyyaṃ santharitvā anuddharitvā anāpucchā pak‍kamantau dvai āpattiyau āpaj‍jati| paṭhamaṃ pādaṃ parikkhaipaṃ atik‍kāmaiti, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa; dutiyaṃ pādaṃ atik‍kāmaiti, āpatti pācittiyassa|

saṅghikai vihārai jānaṃ pubbupagataṃ bhikkhuṃ anupakhaj‍ja saiyyaṃ  kappaintau dvai āpattiyau āpaj‍jati| nipaj‍jati, payaugai duk‍kaṭaṃ; nipan‍nai āpatti pācittiyassa|

bhikkhuṃ kupitau anattamanau saṅghikā vihārā nik‍kaḍḍhaintau dvai āpattiyau āpaj‍jati| nik‍kaḍḍhati, payaugai duk‍kaṭaṃ; nik‍kaḍḍhitai āpatti pācittiyassa|

saṅghikai  vihārai uparivaihāsakuṭiyā āhac‍capādakaṃ mañ‍caṃ vā pīṭhaṃ vā abhinisīdantau dvai āpattiyau āpaj‍jati| abhinisīdati, payaugai duk‍kaṭaṃ; abhinisin‍nai āpatti pācittiyassa|

dvattipariyāyai adhiṭṭhahitvā tatuttari adhiṭṭhahantau dvai āpattiyau āpaj‍jati| adhiṭṭhaiti, payaugai duk‍kaṭaṃ; adhiṭṭhitai āpatti pācittiyassa|

jānaṃ sappāṇakaṃ udakaṃ tiṇaṃ vā mattikaṃ vā siñ‍cantau dvai āpattiyau āpaj‍jati| siñ‍cati, payaugai duk‍kaṭaṃ; siñ‍citai āpatti pācittiyassa|

bhūtagāmavaggau dutiyau|

3. auvādavaggau

167. asammatau  bhikkhuniyau auvadantau dvai āpattiyau āpaj‍jati| auvadati, payaugai duk‍kaṭaṃ; auvaditai āpatti pācittiyassa|

atthaṅgatai sūriyai bhikkhuniyau auvadantau dvai āpattiyau āpaj‍jati| auvadati, payaugai duk‍kaṭaṃ; auvaditai āpatti pācittiyassa|

bhikkhunupassayaṃ  upasaṅkamitvā bhikkhuniyau auvadantau dvai āpattiyau āpaj‍jati| auvadati, payaugai duk‍kaṭaṃ; auvaditai āpatti pācittiyassa|

‘‘āmisahaitu bhikkhū bhikkhuniyau auvadantī’’ti bhaṇantau dvai āpattiyau āpaj‍jati| bhaṇati, payaugai duk‍kaṭaṃ; bhaṇitai āpatti pācittiyassa|

añ‍ñātikāya bhikkhuniyā cīvaraṃ daintau dvai āpattiyau āpaj‍jati| daiti, payaugai duk‍kaṭaṃ; din‍nai āpatti pācittiyassa|

añ‍ñātikā  bhikkhuniyā cīvaraṃ sibbaintau dvai āpattiyau āpaj‍jati| sibbaiti, payaugai duk‍kaṭaṃ; ārāpathai ārāpathai āpatti pācittiyassa|

bhikkhuniyā  saddhiṃ saṃvidhāya aikaddhānamaggaṃ paṭipaj‍jantau dvai āpattiyau āpaj‍jati| paṭipaj‍jati, payaugai duk‍kaṭaṃ; paṭipan‍nai āpatti pācittiyassa|

bhikkhuniyā saddhiṃ saṃvidhāya aikaṃ nāvaṃ abhiruhantau dvai āpattiyau āpaj‍jati| abhiruhati, payaugai duk‍kaṭaṃ; abhiruḷhai āpatti pācittiyassa|

jānaṃ bhikkhuniparipācitaṃ piṇḍapātaṃ bhuñ‍jantau dvai āpattiyau āpaj‍jati| ‘‘bhuñ‍jissāmī’’ti paṭiggaṇhāti, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa; ajjhauhārai ajjhauhārai āpatti pācittiyassa|

bhikkhuniyā  saddhiṃ aikau aikāya rahau nisaj‍jaṃ kappaintau dvai āpattiyau āpaj‍jati| nisīdati, payaugai duk‍kaṭaṃ; nisin‍nai āpatti pācittiyassa|

auvādavaggau tatiyau|

4. bhaujanavaggau

168. tatuttari  āvasathapiṇḍaṃ bhuñ‍jantau dvai āpattiyau āpaj‍jati| bhuñ‍jissāmīti paṭiggaṇhāti, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa; ajjhauhārai ajjhauhārai āpatti pācittiyassa|

gaṇabhaujanaṃ bhuñ‍jantau dvai āpattiyau āpaj‍jati| bhuñ‍jissāmīti paṭiggaṇhāti, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa; ajjhauhārai ajjhauhārai āpatti pācittiyassa|

paramparabhaujanaṃ bhuñ‍jantau dvai āpattiyau āpaj‍jati| bhuñ‍jissāmīti paṭiggaṇhāti, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa; ajjhauhārai ajjhauhārai āpatti pācittiyassa|

dvattipattapūrai pūvai paṭiggahaitvā tatuttari paṭiggaṇhantau dvai āpattiyau āpaj‍jati| gaṇhāti, payaugai duk‍kaṭaṃ; gahitai āpatti pācittiyassa|

bhuttāvī pavāritau anatirittaṃ khādanīyaṃ vā bhaujanīyaṃ vā bhuñ‍jantau dvai āpattiyau āpaj‍jati| bhuñ‍jissāmīti paṭiggaṇhāti, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa; ajjhauhārai ajjhauhārai āpatti pācittiyassa|

bhikkhuṃ bhuttāviṃ pavāritaṃ anatirittaina khādanīyaina vā bhaujanīyaina  vā abhihaṭṭhuṃ pavāraintau dvai āpattiyau āpaj‍jati| tassa vacanaina khādissāmi bhuñ‍jissāmīti paṭiggaṇhāti, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa; bhaujanapariyausānai āpatti pācittiyassa|

vikālai khādanīyaṃ vā bhaujanīyaṃ vā bhuñ‍jantau dvai āpattiyau āpaj‍jati| khādissāmi bhuñ‍jissāmīti paṭiggaṇhāti, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa; ajjhauhārai ajjhauhārai āpatti pācittiyassa|

san‍nidhikārakaṃ khādanīyaṃ vā bhaujanīyaṃ vā bhuñ‍jantau dvai āpattiyau āpaj‍jati| khādissāmi bhuñ‍jissāmīti paṭiggaṇhāti, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa; ajjhauhārai ajjhauhārai āpatti pācittiyassa|

paṇītabhaujanāni attanau atthāya viñ‍ñāpaitvā bhuñ‍jantau dvai āpattiyau āpaj‍jati| bhuñ‍jissāmīti paṭiggaṇhāti, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa; ajjhauhārai ajjhauhārai āpatti pācittiyassa|

adin‍naṃ  mukhadvāraṃ āhāraṃ āharantau dvai āpattiyau āpaj‍jati| bhuñ‍jissāmīti paṭiggaṇhāti, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa; ajjhauhārai ajjhauhārai āpatti pācittiyassa|

bhaujanavaggau catutthau|

5. acailakavaggau

169. acailakassa, vā paribbājakassa vā paribbājikāya vā sahatthā khādanīyaṃ  vā bhaujanīyaṃ vā daintau dvai āpattiyau āpaj‍jati| daiti, payaugai duk‍kaṭaṃ; din‍nai āpatti pācittiyassa|

bhikkhuṃ – ‘‘aihāvusau, gāmaṃ vā nigamaṃ vā piṇḍāya pavisissāmā’’ti  tassa dāpaitvā vā  adāpaitvā vā uyyaujaintau dvai āpattiyau āpaj‍jati| uyyaujaiti, payaugai duk‍kaṭaṃ; uyyaujitai āpatti pācittiyassa|

sabhaujanai kulai anupakhaj‍ja nisaj‍jaṃ kappaintau dvai āpattiyau āpaj‍jati| nisīdati, payaugai duk‍kaṭaṃ; nisin‍nai āpatti pācittiyassa|

mātugāmaina saddhiṃ rahau paṭicchan‍nai āsanai nisaj‍jaṃ kappaintau dvai āpattiyau āpaj‍jati| nisīdati, payaugai duk‍kaṭaṃ; nisin‍nai āpatti pācittiyassa|

mātugāmaina saddhiṃ aikau aikāya rahau nisaj‍jaṃ kappaintau dvai āpattiyau āpaj‍jati| nisīdati, payaugai duk‍kaṭaṃ; nisin‍nai āpatti pācittiyassa|

nimantitau sabhattau samānau puraibhattaṃ pacchābhattaṃ kulaisu cārittaṃ āpaj‍jantau dvai āpattiyau āpaj‍jati| paṭhamaṃ pādaṃ ummāraṃ atik‍kāmaiti, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa; dutiyaṃ pādaṃ atik‍kāmaiti, āpatti pācittiyassa|

tatuttari bhaisaj‍jaṃ viñ‍ñāpaintau dvai āpattiyau āpaj‍jati| viñ‍ñāpaiti, payaugai duk‍kaṭaṃ; viñ‍ñāpitai āpatti pācittiyassa|

uyyuttaṃ  sainaṃ dassanāya gacchantau dvai āpattiyau āpaj‍jati| gacchati, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa; yattha ṭhitau passati, āpatti pācittiyassa|

atiraikatirattaṃ saināya vasantau dvai āpattiyau āpaj‍jati| vasati, payaugai duk‍kaṭaṃ; vasitai āpatti pācittiyassa|

uyyaudhikaṃ gacchantau dvai āpattiyau āpaj‍jati| gacchati, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa; yattha ṭhitau passati, āpatti pācittiyassa|

acailakavaggau pañ‍camau|

6. surāmairayavaggau

170. maj‍jaṃ  pivantau dvai āpattiyau āpaj‍jati| ‘‘pivissāmī’’ti paṭiggaṇhāti, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa; ajjhauhārai ajjhauhārai āpatti pācittiyassa|

bhikkhuṃ aṅgulipataudakaina hāsaintau dvai āpattiyau āpaj‍jati| hāsaiti, payaugai duk‍kaṭaṃ; hasitai āpatti pācittiyassa|

udakai kīḷantau dvai āpattiyau āpaj‍jati| haiṭṭhāgaupphakai udakai kīḷati, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa; uparigaupphakai kīḷati, āpatti pācittiyassa|

anādariyaṃ karauntau dvai āpattiyau āpaj‍jati| karauti, payaugai duk‍kaṭaṃ; katai āpatti pācittiyassa|

bhikkhuṃ bhiṃsāpaintau dvai āpattiyau āpaj‍jati| bhiṃsāpaiti, payaugai duk‍kaṭaṃ; bhiṃsāpitai āpatti pācittiyassa|

jautiṃ samādahitvā visibbaintau dvai āpattiyau āpaj‍jati| samādahati, payaugai duk‍kaṭaṃ; samādahitai āpatti pācittiyassa|

aurainaddhamāsaṃ nahāyantau dvai āpattiyau āpaj‍jati| nahāyati, payaugai duk‍kaṭaṃ; nahānapariyausānai āpatti pācittiyassa|

anādiyitvā  tiṇṇaṃ dubbaṇṇakaraṇānaṃ añ‍ñataraṃ dubbaṇṇakaraṇaṃ navaṃ cīvaraṃ paribhuñ‍jantau dvai āpattiyau āpaj‍jati| paribhuñ‍jati, payaugai duk‍kaṭaṃ; paribhuttai āpatti pācittiyassa|

bhikkhussa  vā bhikkhuniyā vā sikkhamānāya vā sāmaṇairassa vā sāmaṇairiyā vā sāmaṃ cīvaraṃ vikappaitvā appac‍cuddhāraṇaṃ paribhuñ‍jantau dvai āpattiyau āpaj‍jati| paribhuñ‍jati, payaugai duk‍kaṭaṃ; paribhuttai āpatti pācittiyassa|

bhikkhussa  pattaṃ vā cīvaraṃ vā nisīdanaṃ vā sūcigharaṃ vā kāyabandhanaṃ vā apanidhaintau dvai āpattiyau āpaj‍jati| apanidhaiti, payaugai duk‍kaṭaṃ; apanidhitai āpatti pācittiyassa|

surāmairayavaggau chaṭṭhau|

7. sappāṇakavaggau

171. sañ‍cic‍ca pāṇaṃ jīvitā vauraupaintau kati āpattiyau āpaj‍jati? sañ‍cic‍ca pāṇaṃ jīvitā vauraupaintau catassau āpattiyau āpaj‍jati| anaudissa aupātaṃ khaṇati – ‘‘yau kauci papatitvā marissatī’’ti, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa; manussau tasmiṃ papatitvā marati, āpatti pārājikassa; yakkhau vā paitau vā tiracchānagatamanussaviggahau vā tasmiṃ  papatitvā marati, āpatti thul‍lac‍cayassa; tiracchānagatau tasmiṃ papatitvā marati, āpatti pācittiyassa – sañ‍cic‍ca pāṇaṃ jīvitā vauraupaintau imā catassau āpattiyau āpaj‍jati|

jānaṃ sappāṇakaṃ udakaṃ paribhuñ‍jantau dvai āpattiyau āpaj‍jati| paribhuñ‍jati, payaugai duk‍kaṭaṃ; paribhuttai āpatti pācittiyassa|

jānaṃ yathādhammaṃ nihatādhikaraṇaṃ punakammāya uk‍kauṭaintau dvai āpattiyau āpaj‍jati| uk‍kauṭaiti, payaugai duk‍kaṭaṃ; uk‍kauṭitai āpatti pācittiyassa|

bhikkhussa jānaṃ duṭṭhul‍laṃ āpattiṃ paṭicchādaintau aikaṃ āpattiṃ āpaj‍jati| pācittiyaṃ|

jānaṃ ūnavīsativassaṃ puggalaṃ upasampādaintau dvai āpattiyau āpaj‍jati| upasampādaiti, payaugai duk‍kaṭaṃ; upasampāditai āpatti pācittiyassa|

jānaṃ  thaiyyasatthaina saddhiṃ saṃvidhāya aikaddhānamaggaṃ paṭipaj‍jantau dvai āpattiyau āpaj‍jati| paṭipaj‍jati, payaugai duk‍kaṭaṃ; paṭipan‍nai āpatti pācittiyassa|

mātugāmaina saddhiṃ saṃvidhāya aikaddhānamaggaṃ paṭipaj‍jantau dvai āpattiyau āpaj‍jati| paṭipaj‍jati, payaugai duk‍kaṭaṃ; paṭipan‍nai āpatti pācittiyassa|

pāpikāya  diṭṭhiyā yāvatatiyaṃ samanubhāsanāya na paṭinissaj‍jantau  dvai āpattiyau āpaj‍jati| ñattiyā duk‍kaṭaṃ; kammavācāpariyausānai āpatti pācittiyassa|

jānaṃ tathāvādinā bhikkhunā akaṭānudhammaina taṃ diṭṭhiṃ appaṭinissaṭṭhaina saddhiṃ sambhuñ‍jantau dvai āpattiyau āpaj‍jati| sambhuñ‍jati, payaugai duk‍kaṭaṃ; sambhuttai āpatti pācittiyassa|

jānaṃ  tathānāsitaṃ samaṇuddaisaṃ upalāpaintau dvai āpattiyau āpaj‍jati| upalāpaiti, payaugai duk‍kaṭaṃ; upalāpitai āpatti pācittiyassa|

sappāṇakavaggau sattamau|

8. sahadhammikavaggau

172. bhikkhūhi sahadhammikaṃ vuc‍camānau – ‘‘na tāvāhaṃ, āvusau, aitasmiṃ sikkhāpadai sikkhissāmi yāva na añ‍ñaṃ bhikkhuṃ byattaṃ vinayadharaṃ paripucchissāmī’’ti bhaṇantau dvai āpattiyau āpaj‍jati| bhaṇati, payaugai duk‍kaṭaṃ; bhaṇitai āpatti pācittiyassa|

vinayaṃ vivaṇṇaintau dvai āpattiyau āpaj‍jati| vivaṇṇaiti, payaugai duk‍kaṭaṃ; vivaṇṇitai āpatti pācittiyassa|

mauhaintau dvai āpattiyau āpaj‍jati| anāraupitai mauhai mauhaiti, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa; āraupitai mauhai mauhaiti, āpatti pācittiyassa|

bhikkhussa  kupitau anattamanau pahāraṃ daintau dvai āpattiyau āpaj‍jati| paharati, payaugai duk‍kaṭaṃ; pahatai āpatti pācittiyassa|

bhikkhussa kupitau anattamanau talasattikaṃ uggirantau dvai āpattiyau āpaj‍jati| uggirati, payaugai duk‍kaṭaṃ; uggiritai āpatti pācittiyassa|

bhikkhuṃ  amūlakaina saṅghādisaisaina anuddhaṃsaintau dvai āpattiyau āpaj‍jati| anuddhaṃsaiti, payaugai duk‍kaṭaṃ; anuddhaṃsitai āpatti pācittiyassa|

bhikkhussa sañ‍cic‍ca kuk‍kuc‍caṃ upadahantau dvai āpattiyau āpaj‍jati| upadahati, payaugai duk‍kaṭaṃ; upadahitai āpatti pācittiyassa|

bhikkhūnaṃ bhaṇḍanajātānaṃ kalahajātānaṃ vivādāpan‍nānaṃ upassutiṃ tiṭṭhantau dvai āpattiyau āpaj‍jati| ‘‘saussāmī’’ti gacchati, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa; yattha ṭhitau suṇāti, āpatti pācittiyassa|

dhammikānaṃ kammānaṃ chandaṃ datvā pacchā khīyanadhammaṃ āpaj‍jantau dvai āpattiyau āpaj‍jati| khiyyati, payaugai duk‍kaṭaṃ; khiyyitai āpatti pācittiyassa|

saṅghai vinicchayakathāya vattamānāya chandaṃ adatvā uṭṭhāyāsanā pak‍kamantau dvai āpattiyau āpaj‍jati| parisāya hatthapāsaṃ vijahantassa āpatti duk‍kaṭassa; vijahitai āpatti pācittiyassa|

samaggaina  saṅghaina cīvaraṃ datvā pacchā khīyanadhammaṃ āpaj‍jantau dvai āpattiyau āpaj‍jati| khiyyati, payaugai duk‍kaṭaṃ; khiyyitai āpatti pācittiyassa|

jānaṃ saṅghikaṃ lābhaṃ pariṇataṃ puggalassa pariṇāmaintau dvai āpattiyau āpaj‍jati| pariṇāmaiti, payaugai duk‍kaṭaṃ; pariṇāmitai āpatti pācittiyassa|

sahadhammikavaggau aṭṭhamau|

9. rājavaggau

173. pubbai  appaṭisaṃviditau rañ‍ñau antaipuraṃ pavisantau dvai āpattiyau āpaj‍jati| paṭhamaṃ pādaṃ ummāraṃ atik‍kāmaiti, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa; dutiyaṃ pādaṃ atik‍kāmaiti, āpatti pācittiyassa|

ratanaṃ  uggaṇhantau dvai āpattiyau āpaj‍jati| gaṇhāti, payaugai duk‍kaṭaṃ; gahitai āpatti pācittiyassa|

santaṃ bhikkhuṃ anāpucchā vikālai gāmaṃ pavisantau dvai āpattiyau āpaj‍jati| paṭhamaṃ pādaṃ parikkhaipaṃ atik‍kāmaiti, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa; dutiyaṃ pādaṃ atik‍kāmaiti, āpatti pācittiyassa|

aṭṭhimayaṃ vā dantamayaṃ vā visāṇamayaṃ vā sūcigharaṃ kārāpaintau dvai āpattiyau āpaj‍jati| kārāpaiti, payaugai duk‍kaṭaṃ; kārāpitai āpatti pācittiyassa|

pamāṇātik‍kantaṃ mañ‍caṃ vā pīṭhaṃ vā kārāpaintau dvai āpattiyau āpaj‍jati| kārāpaiti, payaugai duk‍kaṭaṃ; kārāpitai  āpatti pācittiyassa|

mañ‍caṃ vā pīṭhaṃ vā tūlaunaddhaṃ kārāpaintau dvai āpattiyau āpaj‍jati| kārāpaiti, payaugai duk‍kaṭaṃ; kārāpitai āpatti pācittiyassa|

pamāṇātik‍kantaṃ nisīdanaṃ kārāpaintau dvai āpattiyau āpaj‍jati| kārāpaiti, payaugai duk‍kaṭaṃ; kārāpitai āpatti pācittiyassa|

pamāṇātik‍kantaṃ kaṇḍuppaṭicchādiṃ kārāpaintau dvai āpattiyau āpaj‍jati| kārāpaiti, payaugai duk‍kaṭaṃ; kārāpitai āpatti pācittiyassa|

pamāṇātik‍kantaṃ vassikasāṭikaṃ kārāpaintau dvai āpattiyau āpaj‍jati| kārāpaiti, payaugai duk‍kaṭaṃ; kārāpitai āpatti pācittiyassa|

cīvaraṃ kārāpaintau kati āpattiyau āpaj‍jati? sugatacīvarappamāṇaṃ cīvaraṃ kārāpaintau dvai āpattiyau āpaj‍jati| kārāpaiti, payaugai duk‍kaṭaṃ; kārāpitai āpatti pācittiyassa – sugatacīvarappamāṇaṃ cīvaraṃ kārāpaintau imā dvai āpattiyau āpaj‍jati|

rājavaggau navamau| khuddakā niṭṭhitā|

5. pāṭidaisanīyakaṇḍaṃ

174. añ‍ñātikāya  bhikkhuniyā antaragharaṃ paviṭṭhāya hatthatau khādanīyaṃ vā bhaujanīyaṃ vā sahatthā paṭiggahaitvā bhuñ‍jantau kati āpattiyau āpaj‍jati? añ‍ñātikāya bhikkhuniyā antaragharaṃ paviṭṭhāya hatthatau khādanīyaṃ vā bhaujanīyaṃ vā sahatthā paṭiggahaitvā bhuñ‍jantau dvai āpattiyau āpaj‍jati| bhuñ‍jissāmīti paṭiggaṇhāti, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa; ajjhauhārai ajjhauhārai āpatti pāṭidaisanīyassa – añ‍ñātikāya bhikkhuniyā antaragharaṃ paviṭṭhāya hatthatau khādanīyaṃ vā bhaujanīyaṃ vā sahatthā paṭiggahaitvā bhuñ‍jantau imā dvai āpattiyau āpaj‍jati|

bhikkhuniyā vausāsantiyā na nivāraitvā bhuñ‍jantau dvai āpattiyau āpaj‍jati| bhuñ‍jissāmīti paṭiggaṇhāti, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa; ajjhauhārai ajjhauhārai āpatti pāṭidaisanīyassa|

saikkhasammataisu kulaisu khādanīyaṃ vā bhaujanīyaṃ vā sahatthā paṭiggahaitvā bhuñ‍jantau dvai āpattiyau āpaj‍jati| bhuñ‍jissāmīti paṭiggaṇhāti, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa; ajjhauhārai ajjhauhārai āpatti pāṭidaisanīyassa|

ārañ‍ñakaisu saināsanaisu pubbai appaṭisaṃviditaṃ khādanīyaṃ vā bhaujanīyaṃ vā ajjhārāmai sahatthā paṭiggahaitvā bhuñ‍jantau kati āpattiyau āpaj‍jati? ārañ‍ñakaisu saināsanaisu pubbai appaṭisaṃviditaṃ khādanīyaṃ vā bhaujanīyaṃ vā ajjhārāmai sahatthā paṭiggahaitvā  bhuñ‍jantau dvai āpattiyau āpaj‍jati| bhuñ‍jissāmīti paṭiggaṇhāti, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa; ajjhauhārai  ajjhauhārai āpatti pāṭidaisanīyassa – ārañ‍ñakaisu saināsanaisu pubbai appaṭisaṃviditaṃ khādanīyaṃ vā bhaujanīyaṃ vā ajjhārāmai sahatthā paṭiggahaitvā bhuñ‍jantau imā dvai āpattiyau āpaj‍jati|

cattārau pāṭidaisanīyā niṭṭhitā|

6. saikhiyakaṇḍaṃ

1. parimaṇḍalavaggau

175. anādariyaṃ  paṭic‍ca puratau vā pacchatau vā aulambaintau nivāsaintau kati āpattiyau āpaj‍jati? anādariyaṃ paṭic‍ca puratau vā pacchatau vā aulambaintau nivāsaintau aikaṃ āpattiṃ āpaj‍jati| duk‍kaṭaṃ – anādariyaṃ paṭic‍ca puratau vā pacchatau vā aulambaintau nivāsaintau imaṃ aikaṃ āpattiṃ āpaj‍jati|

…anādariyaṃ paṭic‍ca puratau vā pacchatau vā aulambaintau pārupantau aikaṃ āpattiṃ āpaj‍jati| duk‍kaṭaṃ…|

…anādariyaṃ paṭic‍ca kāyaṃ vivaritvā antaragharai gacchantau aikaṃ āpattiṃ āpaj‍jati| duk‍kaṭaṃ…|

…anādariyaṃ paṭic‍ca kāyaṃ vivaritvā antaragharai nisīdantau aikaṃ āpattiṃ āpaj‍jati| duk‍kaṭaṃ…|

…anādariyaṃ paṭic‍ca hatthaṃ vā pādaṃ vā kīḷāpaintau antaragharai gacchantau aikaṃ āpattiṃ āpaj‍jati| duk‍kaṭaṃ…|

…anādariyaṃ  paṭic‍ca hatthaṃ vā pādaṃ vā kīḷāpaintau antaragharai nisīdantau aikaṃ āpattiṃ āpaj‍jati| duk‍kaṭaṃ…|

…anādariyaṃ paṭic‍ca tahaṃ tahaṃ aulaukaintau antaragharai gacchantau aikaṃ āpattiṃ āpaj‍jati| duk‍kaṭaṃ…|

…anādariyaṃ paṭic‍ca tahaṃ tahaṃ aulaukaintau antaragharai nisīdantau aikaṃ āpattiṃ āpaj‍jati| duk‍kaṭaṃ…|

…anādariyaṃ  paṭic‍ca ukkhittakāya antaragharai gacchantau aikaṃ āpattiṃ āpaj‍jati| duk‍kaṭaṃ…|

…anādariyaṃ paṭic‍ca ukkhittakāya antaragharai nisīdantau aikaṃ āpattiṃ āpaj‍jati| duk‍kaṭaṃ…|

parimaṇḍalavaggau paṭhamau|

2. uj‍jagghikavaggau

176. …anādariyaṃ paṭic‍ca uj‍jagghikāya antaragharai gacchantau aikaṃ āpattiṃ āpaj‍jati| duk‍kaṭaṃ…|

…anādariyaṃ paṭic‍ca uj‍jagghikāya antaragharai nisīdantau aikaṃ āpattiṃ āpaj‍jati| duk‍kaṭaṃ…|

…anādariyaṃ paṭic‍ca uc‍cāsaddaṃ mahāsaddaṃ karauntau antaragharai gacchantau aikaṃ āpattiṃ āpaj‍jati| duk‍kaṭaṃ…|

…anādariyaṃ paṭic‍ca uc‍cāsaddaṃ mahāsaddaṃ karauntau antaragharai nisīdantau aikaṃ āpattiṃ āpaj‍jati| duk‍kaṭaṃ…|

…anādariyaṃ paṭic‍ca kāyappacālakaṃ antaragharai gacchantau aikaṃ āpattiṃ āpaj‍jati| duk‍kaṭaṃ…|

…anādariyaṃ paṭic‍ca kāyappacālakaṃ antaragharai nisīdantau aikaṃ  āpattiṃ āpaj‍jati| duk‍kaṭaṃ…|

…anādariyaṃ paṭic‍ca bāhuppacālakaṃ antaragharai gacchantau aikaṃ āpattiṃ āpaj‍jati| duk‍kaṭaṃ…|

…anādariyaṃ  paṭic‍ca bāhuppacālakaṃ antaragharai nisīdantau aikaṃ āpattiṃ āpaj‍jati| duk‍kaṭaṃ…|

…anādariyaṃ paṭic‍ca sīsappacālakaṃ antaragharai gacchantau aikaṃ āpattiṃ āpaj‍jati| duk‍kaṭaṃ…|

…anādariyaṃ paṭic‍ca sīsappacālakaṃ antaragharai nisīdantau aikaṃ āpattiṃ āpaj‍jati| duk‍kaṭaṃ…|

uj‍jagghikavaggau dutiyau|

3. khambhakatavaggau

177. …anādariyaṃ  paṭic‍ca khambhakatau antaragharai gacchantau aikaṃ āpattiṃ āpaj‍jati| duk‍kaṭaṃ…|

…anādariyaṃ paṭic‍ca khambhakatau antaragharai nisīdantau aikaṃ āpattiṃ āpaj‍jati| duk‍kaṭaṃ…|

…anādariyaṃ paṭic‍ca auguṇṭhitau antaragharai gacchantau aikaṃ āpattiṃ āpaj‍jati| duk‍kaṭaṃ…|

…anādariyaṃ paṭic‍ca auguṇṭhitau antaragharai nisīdantau aikaṃ āpattiṃ āpaj‍jati| duk‍kaṭaṃ…|

…anādariyaṃ  paṭic‍ca uk‍kuṭikāya antaragharai gacchantau aikaṃ āpattiṃ āpaj‍jati| duk‍kaṭaṃ…|

…anādariyaṃ paṭic‍ca pal‍latthikāya antaragharai nisīdantau aikaṃ āpattiṃ āpaj‍jati| duk‍kaṭaṃ…|

…anādariyaṃ  paṭic‍ca asak‍kac‍caṃ piṇḍapātaṃ paṭiggaṇhantau aikaṃ āpattiṃ āpaj‍jati| duk‍kaṭaṃ…|

…anādariyaṃ  paṭic‍ca tahaṃ tahaṃ aulaukaintau piṇḍapātaṃ paṭiggaṇhantau aikaṃ āpattiṃ āpaj‍jati| duk‍kaṭaṃ…|

…anādariyaṃ paṭic‍ca sūpañ‍ñaiva bahuṃ paṭiggaṇhantau aikaṃ āpattiṃ āpaj‍jati| duk‍kaṭaṃ…|

…anādariyaṃ paṭic‍ca thūpīkataṃ piṇḍapātaṃ paṭiggaṇhantau aikaṃ āpattiṃ āpaj‍jati| duk‍kaṭaṃ…|

khambhakatavaggau tatiyau|

4. piṇḍapātavaggau

178. …anādariyaṃ paṭic‍ca asak‍kac‍caṃ piṇḍapātaṃ bhuñ‍jantau aikaṃ āpattiṃ āpaj‍jati| duk‍kaṭaṃ…|

…anādariyaṃ  paṭic‍ca tahaṃ tahaṃ aulaukaintau piṇḍapātaṃ bhuñ‍jantau aikaṃ āpattiṃ āpaj‍jati| duk‍kaṭaṃ…|

…anādariyaṃ paṭic‍ca tahaṃ tahaṃ aumasitvā piṇḍapātaṃ bhuñ‍jantau aikaṃ āpattiṃ āpaj‍jati| duk‍kaṭaṃ…|

…anādariyaṃ paṭic‍ca sūpañ‍ñaiva bahuṃ bhuñ‍jantau aikaṃ āpattiṃ  āpaj‍jati| duk‍kaṭaṃ…|

…anādariyaṃ paṭic‍ca thūpakatau aumadditvā piṇḍapātaṃ bhuñ‍jantau aikaṃ āpattiṃ āpaj‍jati| duk‍kaṭaṃ…|

…anādariyaṃ paṭic‍ca sūpaṃ vā byañ‍janaṃ vā audanaina paṭicchādaintau aikaṃ āpattiṃ āpaj‍jati| duk‍kaṭaṃ…|

…anādariyaṃ  paṭic‍ca sūpaṃ vā audanaṃ vā agilānau attanau atthāya viñ‍ñāpaitvā bhuñ‍jantau aikaṃ āpattiṃ āpaj‍jati| duk‍kaṭaṃ…|

…anādariyaṃ paṭic‍ca ujjhānasañ‍ñī paraisaṃ pattaṃ aulaukaintau aikaṃ āpattiṃ āpaj‍jati| duk‍kaṭaṃ…|

…anādariyaṃ paṭic‍ca mahantaṃ kabaḷaṃ karauntau aikaṃ āpattiṃ āpaj‍jati| duk‍kaṭaṃ…|

…anādariyaṃ paṭic‍ca dīghaṃ ālaupaṃ karauntau aikaṃ āpattiṃ āpaj‍jati| duk‍kaṭaṃ…|

piṇḍapātavaggau catutthau|

5. kabaḷavaggau

179. …anādariyaṃ paṭic‍ca anāhaṭai kabaḷai mukhadvāraṃ vivarantau aikaṃ āpattiṃ āpaj‍jati| duk‍kaṭaṃ…|

…anādariyaṃ paṭic‍ca bhuñ‍jamānau sabbaṃ hatthaṃ mukhai pakkhipantau aikaṃ āpattiṃ āpaj‍jati| duk‍kaṭaṃ…|

…anādariyaṃ paṭic‍ca sakabaḷaina mukhaina byāharantau aikaṃ āpattiṃ  āpaj‍jati| duk‍kaṭaṃ…|

…anādariyaṃ  paṭic‍ca piṇḍukkhaipakaṃ bhuñ‍jantau aikaṃ āpattiṃ āpaj‍jati| duk‍kaṭaṃ…|

…anādariyaṃ paṭic‍ca kabaḷāvacchaidakaṃ bhuñ‍jantau aikaṃ āpattiṃ āpaj‍jati| duk‍kaṭaṃ…|

…anādariyaṃ paṭic‍ca avagaṇḍakārakaṃ bhuñ‍jantau aikaṃ āpattiṃ āpaj‍jati| duk‍kaṭaṃ…|

…anādariyaṃ paṭic‍ca hatthaniddhunakaṃ bhuñ‍jantau aikaṃ āpattiṃ āpaj‍jati| duk‍kaṭaṃ…|

…anādariyaṃ paṭic‍ca sitthāvakārakaṃ bhuñ‍jantau aikaṃ āpattiṃ āpaj‍jati| duk‍kaṭaṃ…|

…anādariyaṃ  paṭic‍ca jivhānicchārakaṃ bhuñ‍jantau aikaṃ āpattiṃ āpaj‍jati| duk‍kaṭaṃ…|

…anādariyaṃ paṭic‍ca capucapukārakaṃ bhuñ‍jantau aikaṃ āpattiṃ āpaj‍jati| duk‍kaṭaṃ…|

kabaḷavaggau pañ‍camau|

6. surusuruvaggau

180. …anādariyaṃ paṭic‍ca surusurukārakaṃ bhuñ‍jantau aikaṃ āpattiṃ āpaj‍jati| duk‍kaṭaṃ…|

…anādariyaṃ paṭic‍ca hatthanil‍laihakaṃ bhuñ‍jantau aikaṃ āpattiṃ āpaj‍jati| duk‍kaṭaṃ…|

…anādariyaṃ  paṭic‍ca pattanil‍laihakaṃ bhuñ‍jantau aikaṃ āpattiṃ āpaj‍jati| duk‍kaṭaṃ…|

…anādariyaṃ paṭic‍ca auṭṭhanil‍laihakaṃ bhuñ‍jantau aikaṃ āpattiṃ āpaj‍jati| duk‍kaṭaṃ…|

…anādariyaṃ  paṭic‍ca sāmisaina hatthaina pānīyathālakaṃ paṭiggaṇhantau aikaṃ āpattiṃ āpaj‍jati| duk‍kaṭaṃ…|

…anādariyaṃ  paṭic‍ca sasitthakaṃ pattadhauvanaṃ antaragharai chaḍḍaintau aikaṃ āpattiṃ āpaj‍jati| duk‍kaṭaṃ…|

…anādariyaṃ paṭic‍ca chattapāṇissa dhammaṃ daisaintau aikaṃ āpattiṃ āpaj‍jati| duk‍kaṭaṃ…|

…anādariyaṃ paṭic‍ca daṇḍapāṇissa dhammaṃ daisaintau aikaṃ āpattiṃ āpaj‍jati| duk‍kaṭaṃ…|

…anādariyaṃ paṭic‍ca satthapāṇissa dhammaṃ daisaintau aikaṃ āpattiṃ āpaj‍jati| duk‍kaṭaṃ…|

…anādariyaṃ paṭic‍ca āvudhapāṇissa dhammaṃ daisaintau aikaṃ āpattiṃ āpaj‍jati| duk‍kaṭaṃ…|

surusuruvaggau chaṭṭhau|

7. pādukavaggau

181. …anādariyaṃ  paṭic‍ca pādukāruḷhassa dhammaṃ daisaintau aikaṃ āpattiṃ āpaj‍jati| duk‍kaṭaṃ…|

…anādariyaṃ paṭic‍ca upāhanāruḷhassa dhammaṃ daisaintau aikaṃ  āpattiṃ āpaj‍jati| duk‍kaṭaṃ…|

…anādariyaṃ paṭic‍ca yānagatassa dhammaṃ daisaintau aikaṃ āpattiṃ āpaj‍jati| duk‍kaṭaṃ…|

…anādariyaṃ paṭic‍ca sayanagatassa dhammaṃ daisaintau aikaṃ āpattiṃ āpaj‍jati| duk‍kaṭaṃ…|

…anādariyaṃ paṭic‍ca pal‍latthikāya nisin‍nassa dhammaṃ daisaintau aikaṃ āpattiṃ āpaj‍jati| duk‍kaṭaṃ…|

…anādariyaṃ paṭic‍ca vaiṭhitasīsassa dhammaṃ daisaintau aikaṃ āpattiṃ āpaj‍jati| duk‍kaṭaṃ…|

…anādariyaṃ paṭic‍ca auguṇṭhitasīsassa dhammaṃ daisaintau aikaṃ āpattiṃ āpaj‍jati| duk‍kaṭaṃ…|

…anādariyaṃ  paṭic‍ca chamāyaṃ nisīditvā āsanai nisin‍nassa dhammaṃ daisaintau aikaṃ āpattiṃ āpaj‍jati| duk‍kaṭaṃ…|

…anādariyaṃ paṭic‍ca nīcai āsanai nisīditvā uc‍cai āsanai nisin‍nassa dhammaṃ daisaintau aikaṃ āpattiṃ āpaj‍jati| duk‍kaṭaṃ…|

…anādariyaṃ paṭic‍ca ṭhitau nisin‍nassa dhammaṃ daisaintau aikaṃ āpattiṃ āpaj‍jati| duk‍kaṭaṃ…|

…anādariyaṃ paṭic‍ca pacchatau gacchantau puratau gacchantassa dhammaṃ daisaintau aikaṃ āpattiṃ āpaj‍jati| duk‍kaṭaṃ…|

…anādariyaṃ  paṭic‍ca uppathaina gacchantau pathaina gacchantassa dhammaṃ  daisaintau aikaṃ āpattiṃ āpaj‍jati| duk‍kaṭaṃ…|

…anādariyaṃ paṭic‍ca ṭhitau uc‍cāraṃ vā passāvaṃ vā karauntau aikaṃ āpattiṃ āpaj‍jati| duk‍kaṭaṃ…|

…anādariyaṃ paṭic‍ca haritai uc‍cāraṃ vā passāvaṃ vā khaiḷaṃ vā karauntau aikaṃ āpattiṃ āpaj‍jati| duk‍kaṭaṃ…|

anādariyaṃ paṭic‍ca udakai uc‍cāraṃ vā passāvaṃ vā khaiḷaṃ vā karauntau kati āpattiyau āpaj‍jati? anādariyaṃ paṭic‍ca udakai uc‍cāraṃ vā passāvaṃ vā khaiḷaṃ vā karauntau aikaṃ āpattiṃ āpaj‍jati| duk‍kaṭaṃ – anādariyaṃ paṭic‍ca udakai uc‍cāraṃ vā passāvaṃ vā khaiḷaṃ vā karauntau imaṃ aikaṃ āpattiṃ āpaj‍jati|

pādukavaggau sattamau|

saikhiyā niṭṭhitā|

katāpattivārau niṭṭhitau dutiyau|

3. vipattivārau

182. maithunaṃ  dhammaṃ paṭisaivantassa āpattiyau catun‍naṃ vipattīnaṃ kati vipattiyau bhajanti? maithunaṃ dhammaṃ paṭisaivantassa āpattiyau catun‍naṃ vipattīnaṃ dvai vipattiyau bhajanti – siyā sīlavipattiṃ, siyā ācāravipattiṃ…pai॰…|

anādariyaṃ  paṭic‍ca udakai uc‍cāraṃ vā passāvaṃ vā khaiḷaṃ vā karauntassa āpatti catun‍naṃ vipattīnaṃ kati vipattiyau bhajati? anādariyaṃ paṭic‍ca udakai uc‍cāraṃ vā passāvaṃ vā khaiḷaṃ  vā karauntassa āpatti catun‍naṃ vipattīnaṃ aikaṃ vipattiṃ bhajati – ācāravipattiṃ|

vipattivārau niṭṭhitau tatiyau|

4. saṅgahitavārau

183. maithunaṃ  dhammaṃ paṭisaivantassa āpattiyau sattan‍naṃ āpattikkhandhānaṃ katihi āpattikkhandhaihi saṅgahitā? maithunaṃ dhammaṃ paṭisaivantassa āpattiyau sattan‍naṃ āpattikkhandhānaṃ tīhi āpattikkhandhaihi saṅgahitā – siyā pārājikāpattikkhandhaina, siyā thul‍lac‍cayāpattikkhandhaina, siyā duk‍kaṭāpattikkhandhaina…pai॰…|

anādariyaṃ  paṭic‍ca udakai uc‍cāraṃ vā passāvaṃ vā khaiḷaṃ vā karauntassa āpatti sattan‍naṃ āpattikkhandhānaṃ katihi āpattikkhandhaihi saṅgahitā? anādariyaṃ paṭic‍ca udakai uc‍cāraṃ vā passāvaṃ vā khaiḷaṃ vā karauntassa āpatti sattan‍naṃ āpattikkhandhānaṃ aikaina āpattikkhandhaina saṅgahitā – duk‍kaṭāpattikkhandhaina|

saṅgahitavārau niṭṭhitau catutthau|

5. samuṭṭhānavārau

184. maithunaṃ  dhammaṃ paṭisaivantassa āpattiyau chan‍naṃ āpattisamuṭṭhānānaṃ katihi samuṭṭhānaihi samuṭṭhanti samuṭṭhahanti (sī॰ syā॰)? maithunaṃ dhammaṃ paṭisaivantassa āpattiyau chan‍naṃ āpattisamuṭṭhānānaṃ aikaina samuṭṭhānaina samuṭṭhanti – kāyatau ca cittatau ca samuṭṭhanti, na vācatau…pai॰…|

anādariyaṃ  paṭic‍ca udakai uc‍cāraṃ vā passāvaṃ vā khaiḷaṃ vā karauntassa āpatti chan‍naṃ āpattisamuṭṭhānānaṃ katihi  samuṭṭhānaihi samuṭṭhāti? anādariyaṃ paṭic‍ca udakai uc‍cāraṃ vā passāvaṃ vā khaiḷaṃ vā karauntassa āpatti chan‍naṃ āpattisamuṭṭhānānaṃ aikaina samuṭṭhānaina samuṭṭhāti – kāyatau ca cittatau ca samuṭṭhāti, na vācatau|

samuṭṭhānavārau niṭṭhitau pañ‍camau|

6. adhikaraṇavārau

185. maithunaṃ  dhammaṃ paṭisaivantassa āpattiyau catun‍naṃ adhikaraṇānaṃ katamaṃ adhikaraṇaṃ? maithunaṃ dhammaṃ paṭisaivantassa āpattiyau catun‍naṃ adhikaraṇānaṃ āpattādhikaraṇaṃ…pai॰…|

anādariyaṃ paṭic‍ca udakai uc‍cāraṃ vā passāvaṃ vā khaiḷaṃ vā karauntassa āpatti catun‍naṃ adhikaraṇānaṃ katamaṃ adhikaraṇaṃ? anādariyaṃ paṭic‍ca udakai uc‍cāraṃ vā passāvaṃ vā khaiḷaṃ vā karauntassa āpatti catun‍naṃ adhikaraṇānaṃ āpattādhikaraṇaṃ|

adhikaraṇavārau niṭṭhitau chaṭṭhau|

7. samathavārau

186. maithunaṃ  dhammaṃ paṭisaivantassa āpattiyau sattan‍naṃ samathānaṃ katihi samathaihi sammanti? maithunaṃ dhammaṃ paṭisaivantassa āpattiyau sattan‍naṃ samathānaṃ tīhi samathaihi sammanti – siyā sammukhāvinayaina ca paṭiñ‍ñātakaraṇaina ca, siyā sammukhāvinayaina ca tiṇavatthārakaina ca…pai॰…|

anādariyaṃ paṭic‍ca udakai uc‍cāraṃ vā passāvaṃ vā khaiḷaṃ vā karauntassa āpatti sattan‍naṃ samathānaṃ katihi samathaihi sammati? anādariyaṃ paṭic‍ca udakai uc‍cāraṃ vā passāvaṃ vā khaiḷaṃ  vā karauntassa āpatti sattan‍naṃ samathānaṃ tīhi samathaihi sammati – siyā sammukhāvinayaina ca paṭiñ‍ñātakaraṇaina ca, siyā sammukhāvinayaina ca tiṇavatthārakaina ca|

samathavārau niṭṭhitau sattamau|

8. samuc‍cayavārau

187. maithunaṃ  dhammaṃ paṭisaivantau kati āpattiyau āpaj‍jati? maithunaṃ dhammaṃ paṭisaivantau tissau āpattiyau āpaj‍jati| akkhāyitai sarīrai maithunaṃ dhammaṃ paṭisaivati, āpatti pārājikassa; yaibhuyyaina khāyitai sarīrai maithunaṃ dhammaṃ paṭisaivati, āpatti thul‍lac‍cayassa; vaṭṭakatai mukhai acchupantaṃ  aṅgajātaṃ pavaisaiti, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa – maithunaṃ dhammaṃ paṭisaivantau imā tissau āpattiyau āpaj‍jati|

tā āpattiyau catun‍naṃ vipattīnaṃ kati vipattiyau bhajanti, sattan‍naṃ āpattikkhandhānaṃ katihi āpattikkhandhaihi saṅgahitā, chan‍naṃ āpattisamuṭṭhānānaṃ katihi samuṭṭhānaihi samuṭṭhanti, catun‍naṃ adhikaraṇānaṃ katamaṃ adhikaraṇaṃ, sattan‍naṃ samathānaṃ katihi samathaihi sammanti? tā āpattiyau catun‍naṃ vipattīnaṃ dvai vipattiyau bhajanti – siyā sīlavipattiṃ, siyā ācāravipattiṃ| sattan‍naṃ āpattikkhandhānaṃ tīhi āpattikkhandhaihi saṅgahitā – siyā pārājikāpattikkhandhaina, siyā thul‍lac‍cayāpattikkhandhaina, siyā duk‍kaṭāpattikkhandhaina| chan‍naṃ āpattisamuṭṭhānānaṃ aikaina samuṭṭhānaina samuṭṭhanti – kāyatau ca cittatau ca samuṭṭhanti, na vācatau| catun‍naṃ adhikaraṇānaṃ, āpattādhikaraṇaṃ| sattan‍naṃ  samathānaṃ tīhi samathaihi sammanti – siyā sammukhāvinayaina ca paṭiñ‍ñātakaraṇaina ca, siyā sammukhāvinayaina ca tiṇavatthārakaina ca…pai॰…|

anādariyaṃ paṭic‍ca udakai uc‍cāraṃ vā passāvaṃ vā khaiḷaṃ vā karauntau kati āpattiyau āpaj‍jati? anādariyaṃ paṭic‍ca udakai uc‍cāraṃ vā passāvaṃ vā khaiḷaṃ vā karauntau aikaṃ āpattiṃ āpaj‍jati| duk‍kaṭaṃ – anādariyaṃ paṭic‍ca udakai uc‍cāraṃ vā passāvaṃ vā khaiḷaṃ vā karauntau imaṃ aikaṃ āpattiṃ āpaj‍jati|

sā  āpatti catun‍naṃ vipattīnaṃ kati vipattiyau bhajati, sattan‍naṃ āpattikkhandhānaṃ katihi āpattikkhandhaihi saṅgahitā, chan‍naṃ āpattisamuṭṭhānānaṃ katihi samuṭṭhānaihi samuṭṭhāti, catun‍naṃ adhikaraṇānaṃ katamaṃ adhikaraṇaṃ, sattan‍naṃ, samathānaṃ katihi samathaihi sammati? sā āpatti catun‍naṃ vipattīnaṃ aikaṃ vipattiṃ bhajati – ācāravipattiṃ| sattan‍naṃ āpattikkhandhānaṃ aikaina āpattikkhandhaina saṅgahitā – duk‍kaṭāpattikkhandhaina| chan‍naṃ  āpattisamuṭṭhānānaṃ aikaina samuṭṭhānaina samuṭṭhāti – kāyatau ca cittatau ca samuṭṭhāti, na vācatau| catun‍naṃ adhikaraṇānaṃ, āpattādhikaraṇaṃ| sattan‍naṃ samathānaṃ tīhi samathaihi sammati – siyā sammukhāvinayaina ca paṭiñ‍ñātakaraṇaina ca, siyā sammukhāvinayaina ca tiṇavatthārakaina ca|

samuc‍cayavārau niṭṭhitau aṭṭhamau|

imai aṭṭha vārā sajjhāyamaggaina likhitā|

tassuddānaṃ –

katthapañ‍ñatti  kati ca, vipattisaṅgahaina ca|

samuṭṭhānādhikaraṇā samathau, samuc‍cayaina cāti||

1. katthapañ‍ñattivārau

1. pārājikakaṇḍaṃ

188. yaṃ  taina bhagavatā jānatā passatā arahatā sammāsambuddhaina maithunaṃ dhammaṃ paṭisaivanapac‍cayā pārājikaṃ kattha pañ‍ñattaṃ, kaṃ ārabbha, kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ…pai॰… kainābhatanti?

yaṃ taina bhagavatā jānatā passatā arahatā sammāsambuddhaina maithunaṃ dhammaṃ paṭisaivanapac‍cayā pārājikaṃ  kattha pañ‍ñattanti? vaisāliyaṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ| kaṃ ārabbhāti? sudin‍naṃ kalandaputtaṃ ārabbha| kismiṃ vatthusminti? sudin‍nau kalandaputtau purāṇadutiyikāya maithunaṃ dhammaṃ paṭisaivi, tasmiṃ vatthusmiṃ| atthi tattha pañ‍ñatti, anupañ‍ñatti, anuppan‍napañ‍ñattīti? aikā pañ‍ñatti, dvai anupañ‍ñattiyau| anuppan‍napañ‍ñatti tasmiṃ natthi| sabbattha pañ‍ñatti, padaisapañ‍ñattīti? sabbatthapañ‍ñatti| sādhāraṇapañ‍ñatti, asādhāraṇapañ‍ñattīti? sādhāraṇapañ‍ñatti| aikataupañ‍ñatti, ubhataupañ‍ñattīti? ubhataupañ‍ñatti| pañ‍can‍naṃ pātimaukkhuddaisānaṃ katthaugadhaṃ kattha pariyāpan‍nanti? nidānaugadhaṃ nidānapariyāpan‍naṃ| katamaina uddaisaina uddaisaṃ āgacchatīti? dutiyaina uddaisaina uddaisaṃ āgacchati| catun‍naṃ vipattīnaṃ katamā vipattīti? sīlavipatti| sattan‍naṃ āpattikkhandhānaṃ katamau  āpattikkhandhauti? pārājikāpattikkhandhau| chan‍naṃ āpattisamuṭṭhānānaṃ katihi samuṭṭhānaihi samuṭṭhātīti? aikaina samuṭṭhānaina samuṭṭhāti – kāyatau  ca cittatau ca samuṭṭhāti, na vācatau…pai॰… kainābhatanti? paramparābhataṃ –

upāli dāsakau caiva, sauṇakau siggavau tathā|

mauggaliputtaina pañ‍camā, aitai jambusirivhayai|| …pai॰…|

aitai  nāgā mahāpañ‍ñā, vinayañ‍ñū maggakauvidā|

vinayaṃ dīpai pakāsaisuṃ, piṭakaṃ tambapaṇṇiyāti||

189. yaṃ taina bhagavatā jānatā passatā arahatā sammāsambuddhaina adin‍naṃ ādiyanapac‍cayā pārājikaṃ kattha pañ‍ñattanti? rājagahai pañ‍ñattaṃ| kaṃ ārabbhāti? dhaniyaṃ kumbhakāraputtaṃ ārabbha| kismiṃ vatthusminti? dhaniyau kumbhakāraputtau rañ‍ñau dārūni adin‍naṃ ādiyi, tasmiṃ vatthusmiṃ| aikā pañ‍ñatti, aikā anupañ‍ñatti| chan‍naṃ āpattisamuṭṭhānānaṃ tīhi samuṭṭhānaihi samuṭṭhāti – siyā kāyatau ca cittatau ca samuṭṭhāti, na vācatau; siyā vācatau ca cittatau ca samuṭṭhāti, na kāyatau; siyā kāyatau ca vācatau ca cittatau ca samuṭṭhāti…pai॰…|

190. sañ‍cic‍ca manussaviggahaṃ jīvitā vauraupanapac‍cayā pārājikaṃ  kattha pañ‍ñattanti? vaisāliyaṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ| kaṃ ārabbhāti? sambahulai bhikkhū ārabbha| kismiṃ vatthusminti? sambahulā bhikkhū añ‍ñamañ‍ñaṃ jīvitā vauraupaisuṃ, tasmiṃ vatthusmiṃ| aikā pañ‍ñatti, aikā anupañ‍ñatti| chan‍naṃ āpattisamuṭṭhānānaṃ tīhi samuṭṭhānaihi samuṭṭhāti – siyā kāyatau ca cittatau ca samuṭṭhāti, na vācatau; siyā vācatau ca cittatau ca samuṭṭhāti, na kāyatau; siyā kāyatau ca vācatau ca cittatau ca samuṭṭhāti…pai॰…|

191. asantaṃ abhūtaṃ uttarimanussadhammaṃ ul‍lapanapac‍cayā pārājikaṃ kattha pañ‍ñattanti? vaisāliyaṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ| kaṃ ārabbhāti? vaggumudātīriyai bhikkhū ārabbha| kismiṃ vatthusminti? vaggumudātīriyā bhikkhū gihīnaṃ añ‍ñamañ‍ñassa uttarimanussadhammassa vaṇṇaṃ bhāsiṃsu, tasmiṃ vatthusmiṃ| aikā pañ‍ñatti, aikā anupañ‍ñatti| chan‍naṃ āpattisamuṭṭhānānaṃ tīhi samuṭṭhānaihi samuṭṭhāti – siyā kāyatau ca cittatau ca samuṭṭhāti, na vācatau; siyā vācatau ca cittatau ca samuṭṭhāti, na kāyatau; siyā kāyatau ca vācatau ca cittatau ca samuṭṭhāti…pai॰…|

cattārau pārājikā niṭṭhitā|

2. saṅghādisaisakaṇḍādi

192. yaṃ  taina bhagavatā jānatā passatā arahatā sammāsambuddhaina upak‍kamitvāasuciṃ maucanapac‍cayā saṅghādisaisau kattha pañ‍ñattau, kaṃ ārabbha, kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ…pai॰… kainābhatanti?

yaṃ taina bhagavatā jānatā passatā arahatā sammāsambuddhaina upak‍kamitvā asuciṃ maucanapac‍cayā saṅghādisaisau kattha pañ‍ñattauti? sāvatthiyaṃ  pañ‍ñattau| kaṃ ārabbhāti? āyasmantaṃ saiyyasakaṃ ārabbha| kismiṃ vatthusminti? āyasmā saiyyasakau upak‍kamitvā asuciṃ maucaisi, tasmiṃ vatthusmiṃ| atthi tattha pañ‍ñatti, anupañ‍ñatti, anuppan‍napañ‍ñattīti? aikā pañ‍ñatti, aikā anupañ‍ñatti| anuppan‍napañ‍ñatti tasmiṃ natthi| sabbatthapañ‍ñatti, padaisapañ‍ñattīti? sabbatthapañ‍ñatti| sādhāraṇapañ‍ñatti, asādhāraṇapañ‍ñattīti? asādhāraṇapañ‍ñatti| aikataupañ‍ñatti, ubhataupañ‍ñattīti? aikataupañ‍ñatti| pañ‍can‍naṃ pātimaukkhuddaisānaṃ katthaugadhaṃ kattha pariyāpan‍nanti? nidānaugadhaṃ nidānapariyāpan‍naṃ| katamaina uddaisaina uddaisaṃ āgacchatīti? tatiyaina uddaisaina uddaisaṃ āgacchati| catun‍naṃ vipattīnaṃ katamā vipattīti? sīlavipatti| sattan‍naṃ āpattikkhandhānaṃ katamau āpattikkhandhauti? saṅghādisaisāpattikkhandhau| chan‍naṃ āpattisamuṭṭhānānaṃ katihi samuṭṭhānaihi samuṭṭhātīti? aikaina samuṭṭhānaina samuṭṭhāti – kāyatau ca cittatau ca samuṭṭhāti, na vācatau…pai॰… kainābhatanti? paramparābhataṃ –

upāli dāsakau caiva, sauṇakau siggavau tathā|

mauggaliputtaina pañ‍camā, aitai jambusirivhayai|| …pai॰…|

aitai nāgā mahāpañ‍ñā, vinayañ‍ñū maggakauvidā|

vinayaṃ  dīpai pakāsaisuṃ, piṭakaṃ tambapaṇṇiyāti||

mātugāmaina saddhiṃ kāyasaṃsaggaṃ samāpaj‍janapac‍cayā saṅghādisaisau kattha pañ‍ñattauti? sāvatthiyaṃ pañ‍ñattau| kaṃ ārabbhāti? āyasmantaṃ udāyiṃ ārabbha| kismiṃ vatthusminti? āyasmā udāyī mātugāmaina saddhiṃ kāyasaṃsaggaṃ samāpaj‍ji, tasmiṃ vatthusmiṃ| aikā pañ‍ñatti| chan‍naṃ āpattisamuṭṭhānānaṃ aikaina samuṭṭhānaina samuṭṭhāti – kāyatau ca cittatau ca samuṭṭhāti, na vācatau…pai॰…|

mātugāmaṃ  duṭṭhul‍lāhi vācāhi aubhāsanapac‍cayā saṅghādisaisau kattha pañ‍ñattauti? sāvatthiyaṃ  pañ‍ñattau| kaṃ ārabbhāti? āyasmantaṃ udāyiṃ ārabbha| kismiṃ vatthusminti? āyasmā udāyī mātugāmaṃ duṭṭhul‍lāhi vācāhi aubhāsi, tasmiṃ vatthusmiṃ| aikā pañ‍ñatti| chan‍naṃ āpattisamuṭṭhānānaṃ tīhi samuṭṭhānaihi samuṭṭhāti – siyā kāyatau ca cittatau ca samuṭṭhāti, na vācatau; siyā vācatau ca cittatau ca samuṭṭhāti, na kāyatau; siyā kāyatau ca vācatau ca cittatau ca samuṭṭhāti…pai॰…|

mātugāmassa santikai attakāmapāricariyāya vaṇṇaṃ bhāsanapac‍cayā saṅghādisaisau kattha pañ‍ñattauti? sāvatthiyaṃ pañ‍ñattau| kaṃ ārabbhāti? āyasmantaṃ udāyiṃ ārabbha| kismiṃ vatthusminti? āyasmā udāyī mātugāmassa santikai attakāmapāricariyāya vaṇṇaṃ bhāsi, tasmiṃ vatthusmiṃ| aikā pañ‍ñatti| chan‍naṃ  āpattisamuṭṭhānānaṃ tīhi samuṭṭhānaihi samuṭṭhāti…pai॰…|

sañ‍carittaṃ samāpaj‍janapac‍cayā saṅghādisaisau kattha pañ‍ñattauti? sāvatthiyaṃ pañ‍ñattau| kaṃ ārabbhāti? āyasmantaṃ udāyiṃ ārabbha| kismiṃ vatthusminti? āyasmā udāyī sañ‍carittaṃ samāpaj‍ji, tasmiṃ vatthusmiṃ| aikā pañ‍ñatti, aikā anupañ‍ñatti| chan‍naṃ āpattisamuṭṭhānānaṃ chahi samuṭṭhānaihi samuṭṭhāti – siyā kāyatau samuṭṭhāti, na vācatau na cittatau; siyā vācatau samuṭṭhāti, na kāyatau na cittatau; siyā kāyatau ca vācatau ca samuṭṭhāti, na cittatau; siyā kāyatau ca cittatau ca samuṭṭhāti, na vācatau; siyā vācatau ca cittatau ca samuṭṭhāti, na kāyatau; siyā kāyatau ca vācatau ca cittatau ca samuṭṭhāti…pai॰…|

sañ‍ñācikāya kuṭiṃ kārāpanapac‍cayā saṅghādisaisau kattha pañ‍ñattauti? āḷaviyaṃ pañ‍ñattau| kaṃ ārabbhāti? āḷavakai bhikkhū ārabbha| kismiṃ vatthusminti? āḷavakā bhikkhū sañ‍ñācikāya kuṭiyau kārāpaisuṃ, tasmiṃ vatthusmiṃ| aikā pañ‍ñatti| chan‍naṃ āpattisamuṭṭhānānaṃ chahi samuṭṭhānaihi samuṭṭhāti…pai॰…|

mahal‍lakaṃ  vihāraṃ kārāpanapac‍cayā saṅghādisaisau kattha pañ‍ñattauti? kausambiyaṃ pañ‍ñattau| kaṃ ārabbhāti? āyasmantaṃ chan‍naṃ ārabbha| kismiṃ vatthusminti? āyasmā chan‍nau vihāravatthuṃ saudhaintau añ‍ñataraṃ caitiyarukkhaṃ chaidāpaisi, tasmiṃ vatthusmiṃ| aikā  pañ‍ñatti| chan‍naṃ āpattisamuṭṭhānānaṃ chahi samuṭṭhānaihi samuṭṭhāti…pai॰…|

bhikkhuṃ  amūlakaina pārājikaina dhammaina anuddhaṃsanapac‍cayā saṅghādisaisau kattha pañ‍ñattauti? rājagahai pañ‍ñattau| kaṃ ārabbhāti? maittiyabhūmajakai bhikkhū ārabbha| kismiṃ vatthusminti? maittiyabhūmajakā bhikkhū āyasmantaṃ dabbaṃ mal‍laputtaṃ amūlakaina pārājikaina dhammaina anuddhaṃsaisuṃ, tasmiṃ vatthusmiṃ| aikā pañ‍ñatti| chan‍naṃ āpattisamuṭṭhānānaṃ tīhi samuṭṭhānaihi samuṭṭhāti…pai॰…|

bhikkhuṃ añ‍ñabhāgiyassa adhikaraṇassa kiñ‍cidaisaṃ laisamattaṃ upādāya pārājikaina dhammaina anuddhaṃsanapac‍cayā saṅghādisaisau kattha pañ‍ñattauti? rājagahai pañ‍ñattau| kaṃ ārabbhāti? maittiyabhūmajakai bhikkhū ārabbha| kismiṃ vatthusminti? maittiyabhūmajakā bhikkhū āyasmantaṃ dabbaṃ mal‍laputtaṃ añ‍ñabhāgiyassa adhikaraṇassa kiñ‍ci daisaṃ laisamattaṃ upādāya pārājikaina dhammaina anuddhaṃsaisuṃ, tasmiṃ vatthusmiṃ| aikā pañ‍ñatti| chan‍naṃ āpattisamuṭṭhānānaṃ tīhi samuṭṭhānaihi samuṭṭhāti…pai॰…|

saṅghabhaidakassa bhikkhunau yāvatatiyaṃ samanubhāsanāya na paṭinissaj‍janapac‍cayā saṅghādisaisau kattha pañ‍ñattauti? rājagahai pañ‍ñattau| kaṃ ārabbhāti? daivadattaṃ ārabbha| kismiṃ vatthusminti? daivadattau samaggassa saṅghassa bhaidāya parak‍kami, tasmiṃ  vatthusmiṃ| aikā pañ‍ñatti| chan‍naṃ āpattisamuṭṭhānānaṃ aikaina samuṭṭhānaina samuṭṭhāti – kāyatau ca vācatau ca cittatau ca samuṭṭhāti…pai॰…|

bhaidakānuvattakānaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ yāvatatiyaṃ samanubhāsanāya na paṭinissaj‍janapac‍cayā saṅghādisaisau kattha pañ‍ñattauti? rājagahai pañ‍ñattau| kaṃ ārabbhāti? sambahulai bhikkhū ārabbha| kismiṃ vatthusminti? sambahulā bhikkhū daivadattassa saṅghabhaidāya parak‍kamantassa anuvattakā ahaisuṃ vaggavādakā, tasmiṃ vatthusmiṃ| aikā pañ‍ñatti| chan‍naṃ āpattisamuṭṭhānānaṃ aikai  samuṭṭhānaina samuṭṭhāti – kāyatau ca vācatau ca cittatau ca samuṭṭhāti…pai॰…|

dubbacassa bhikkhunau yāvatatiyaṃ samanubhāsanāya na paṭinissaj‍janapac‍cayā saṅghādisaisau kattha pañ‍ñattauti? kausambiyaṃ pañ‍ñattau| kaṃ ārabbhāti? āyasmantaṃ chan‍naṃ ārabbha| kismiṃ vatthusminti? āyasmā chan‍nau bhikkhūhi sahadhammikaṃ vuc‍camānau attānaṃ avacanīyaṃ akāsi, tasmiṃ vatthusmiṃ| aikā pañ‍ñatti| chan‍naṃ āpattisamuṭṭhānānaṃ aikaina samuṭṭhānaina samuṭṭhāti – kāyatau ca vācatau ca cittatau ca samuṭṭhāti…pai॰…|

kuladūsakassa  bhikkhunau yāvatatiyaṃ samanubhāsanāya na paṭinissaj‍janapac‍cayā saṅghādisaisau kattha pañ‍ñattauti? sāvatthiyaṃ pañ‍ñattau| kaṃ ārabbhāti? assajipunabbasukai bhikkhū ārabbha| kismiṃ  vatthusminti? assajipunabbasukā bhikkhū saṅghaina pabbājanīyakammakatā bhikkhū chandagāmitā dausagāmitā mauhagāmitā bhayagāmitā pāpaisuṃ, tasmiṃ vatthusmiṃ| aikā pañ‍ñatti| chan‍naṃ āpattisamuṭṭhānānaṃ aikaina samuṭṭhānaina samuṭṭhāti – kāyatau ca vācatau ca cittatau ca samuṭṭhāti…pai॰…|

anādariyaṃ paṭic‍ca udakai uc‍cāraṃ vā passāvaṃ vā khaiḷaṃ vā karaṇapac‍cayā duk‍kaṭaṃ kattha pañ‍ñattanti? sāvatthiyaṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ| kaṃ ārabbhāti? chabbaggiyai bhikkhū ārabbha| kismiṃ vatthusminti? chabbaggiyā bhikkhū udakai uc‍cārampi passāvampi khaiḷampi akaṃsu, tasmiṃ vatthusmiṃ| aikā pañ‍ñatti, aikā anupañ‍ñatti| chan‍naṃ āpattisamuṭṭhānānaṃ aikaina samuṭṭhānaina samuṭṭhāti – kāyatau ca cittatau ca samuṭṭhāti, na vācatau…pai॰…|

katthapañ‍ñattivārau niṭṭhitau paṭhamau|

2. katāpattivārau

1. pārājikakaṇḍaṃ

193. maithunaṃ  dhammaṃ paṭisaivanapac‍cayā kati āpattiyau āpaj‍jati? maithunaṃ dhammaṃ paṭisaivanapac‍cayā catassau āpattiyau āpaj‍jati – akkhāyitai sarīrai maithunaṃ dhammaṃ paṭisaivati, āpatti pārājikassa; yaibhuyyaina khāyitai sarīrai maithunaṃ dhammaṃ paṭisaivati, āpatti thul‍lac‍cayassa; vaṭṭakatai mukhai acchupantaṃ aṅgajātaṃ pavaisaiti, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa; jatumaṭṭhakai pācittiyaṃ – maithunaṃ dhammaṃ paṭisaivanapac‍cayā imā  catassau āpattiyau āpaj‍jati|

adin‍naṃ ādiyanapac‍cayā kati āpattiyau āpaj‍jati? adin‍naṃ ādiyanapac‍cayā tissau āpattiyau āpaj‍jati| pañ‍camāsakaṃ vā atiraikapañ‍camāsakaṃ vā agghanakaṃ adin‍naṃ thaiyyasaṅkhātaṃ ādiyati, āpatti pārājikassa; atiraikamāsakaṃ vā ūnapañ‍camāsakaṃ vā agghanakaṃ adin‍naṃ thaiyyasaṅkhātaṃ ādiyati, āpatti thul‍lac‍cayassa; māsakaṃ vā ūnamāsakaṃ vā agghanakaṃ adin‍naṃ thaiyyasaṅkhātaṃ ādiyati, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa – adin‍naṃ ādiyanapac‍cayā imā tissau āpattiyau āpaj‍jati|

sañ‍cic‍ca manussaviggahaṃ jīvitā vauraupanapac‍cayā kati āpattiyau āpaj‍jati? sañ‍cic‍ca manussaviggahaṃ jīvitā vauraupanapac‍cayā tissau āpattiyau āpaj‍jati| manussaṃ audissa aupātaṃ khaṇati ‘‘papatitvā marissatī’’ti, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa; papatitai dukkhā vaidanā uppaj‍jati, āpatti thul‍lac‍cayassa; marati, āpatti pārājikassa – sañ‍cic‍ca manussaviggahaṃ jīvitā vauraupanapac‍cayā imā tissau āpattiyau āpaj‍jati|

asantaṃ abhūtaṃ uttarimanussadhammaṃ ul‍lapanapac‍cayā kati āpattiyau āpaj‍jati? asantaṃ  abhūtaṃ uttarimanussadhammaṃ ul‍lapanapac‍cayā tissau āpattiyau āpaj‍jati – pāpicchau icchāpakatau asantaṃ abhūtaṃ uttarimanussadhammaṃ ul‍lapati, āpatti pārājikassa, ‘‘yau  tai vihārai vasati sau bhikkhu arahā’’ti bhaṇati, paṭivijānantassa āpatti thul‍lac‍cayassa; na paṭivijānantassa āpatti duk‍kaṭassa – asantaṃ abhūtaṃ uttarimanussadhammaṃ ul‍lapanapac‍cayā imā tissau āpattiyau āpaj‍jati|

cattārau pārājikā niṭṭhitā|

2. saṅghādisaisakaṇḍādi

194. upak‍kamitvā asuciṃ maucanapac‍cayā kati āpattiyau āpaj‍jati? upak‍kamitvā asucimaucanapac‍cayā tissau āpattiyau āpaj‍jati – caitaiti upak‍kamati muc‍cati, āpatti saṅghādisaisassa; caitaiti  upak‍kamati na muc‍cati, āpatti thul‍lac‍cayassa; payaugai duk‍kaṭaṃ – upak‍kamitvā asucimaucanapac‍cayā imā tissau āpattiyau āpaj‍jati|

kāyasaṃsaggaṃ samāpaj‍janapac‍cayā kati āpattiyau āpaj‍jati? kāyasaṃsaggaṃ samāpaj‍janapac‍cayā pañ‍ca āpattiyau āpaj‍jati – avassutā bhikkhunī avassutassa purisapuggalassa adhakkhakaṃ ubbhajāṇumaṇḍalaṃ gahaṇaṃ sādiyati, āpatti pārājikassa; bhikkhu kāyaina kāyaṃ āmasati, āpatti saṅghādisaisassa; kāyaina kāyapaṭibaddhaṃ āmasati, āpatti thul‍lac‍cayassa; kāyapaṭibaddhaina kāyapaṭibaddhaṃ āmasati, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa; aṅgulipataudakai pācittiyaṃ – kāyasaṃsaggaṃ samāpaj‍janapac‍cayā imā pañ‍ca āpattiyau āpaj‍jati|

mātugāmaṃ duṭṭhul‍lāhi vācāhi aubhāsanapac‍cayā tissau āpattiyau āpaj‍jati – vac‍camaggaṃ passāvamaggaṃ ādissa vaṇṇampi bhaṇati avaṇṇampi bhaṇati, āpatti saṅghādisaisassa; vac‍camaggaṃ passāvamaggaṃ ṭhapaitvā adhakkhakaṃ ubbhajāṇumaṇḍalaṃ ādissa vaṇṇampi bhaṇati  avaṇṇampi bhaṇati, āpatti thul‍lac‍cayassa; kāyapaṭibaddhaṃ ādissa vaṇṇampi bhaṇati avaṇṇampi bhaṇati, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa|

attakāmapāricariyāya vaṇṇaṃ bhāsanapac‍cayā tissau āpattiyau āpaj‍jati – mātugāmassa santikai attakāmapāricariyāya vaṇṇaṃ bhāsati, āpatti saṅghādisaisassa; paṇḍakassa  santikai attakāmapāricariyāya vaṇṇaṃ bhāsati, āpatti thul‍lac‍cayassa; tiracchānagatassa santikai attakāmapāricariyāya vaṇṇaṃ bhāsati, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa|

sañ‍carittaṃ samāpaj‍janapac‍cayā tissau āpattiyau āpaj‍jati – paṭiggaṇhāti vīmaṃsati pac‍cāharati, āpatti saṅghādisaisassa; paṭiggaṇhāti vīmaṃsati na pac‍cāharati, āpatti thul‍lac‍cayassa; paṭiggaṇhāti na vīmaṃsati na pac‍cāharati, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa|

sañ‍ñācikāya kuṭiṃ kārāpanapac‍cayā tissau āpattiyau āpaj‍jati – kārāpaiti, payaugai duk‍kaṭaṃ; aikaṃ piṇḍaṃ aikapiṇḍai (syā॰) anāgatai āpatti thul‍lac‍cayassa; tasmiṃ piṇḍai āgatai āpatti saṅghādisaisassa|

mahal‍lakaṃ  vihāraṃ kārāpanapac‍cayā tissau āpattiyau āpaj‍jati – kārāpaiti, payaugai duk‍kaṭaṃ; aikaṃ piṇḍaṃ anāgatai, āpatti thul‍lac‍cayassa; tasmiṃ piṇḍai āgatai āpatti saṅghādisaisassa|

bhikkhuṃ amūlakaina pārājikaina dhammaina anuddhaṃsanapac‍cayā tissau āpattiyau āpaj‍jati – anaukāsaṃ kārāpaitvā cāvanādhippāyau  vadaiti, āpatti saṅghādisaisaina duk‍kaṭassa; aukāsaṃ kārāpaitvā ak‍kausādhippāyau vadaiti, āpatti aumasavādassa|

bhikkhuṃ añ‍ñabhāgiyassa adhikaraṇassa kiñ‍ci daisaṃ laisamattaṃ upādāya pārājikaina dhammaina anuddhaṃsanapac‍cayā tissau āpattiyau āpaj‍jati – anaukāsaṃ kārāpaitvā cāvanādhippāyau vadaiti, āpatti saṅghādisaisaina duk‍kaṭassa; aukāsaṃ kārāpaitvā ak‍kausādhippāyau vadaiti, āpatti aumasavādassa|

saṅghabhaidakau bhikkhu yāvatatiyaṃ samanubhāsanāya na paṭinissaj‍janapac‍cayā tissau āpattiyau āpaj‍jati – ñattiyā duk‍kaṭaṃ; dvīhi kammavācāhi thul‍lac‍cayā; kammavācāpariyausānai āpatti saṅghādisaisassa|

bhaidakānuvattakā bhikkhū yāvatatiyaṃ samanubhāsanāya na paṭinissaj‍janapac‍cayā tissau āpattiyau  āpaj‍jati – ñattiyā duk‍kaṭaṃ; dvīhi kammavācāhi thul‍lac‍cayā; kammavācāpariyausānai āpatti saṅghādisaisassa|

dubbacau bhikkhu yāvatatiyaṃ samanubhāsanāya na paṭinissaj‍janapac‍cayā tissau āpattiyau āpaj‍jati – ñattiyā duk‍kaṭaṃ; dvīhi kammavācāhi thul‍lac‍cayā; kammavācāpariyausānai āpatti saṅghādisaisassa|

kuladūsakau bhikkhu yāvatatiyaṃ samanubhāsanāya na paṭinissaj‍janapac‍cayā tissau āpattiyau āpaj‍jati – ñattiyā duk‍kaṭaṃ; dvīhi kammavācāhi thul‍lac‍cayā; kammavācāpariyausānai āpatti saṅghādisaisassa…pai॰…|

anādariyaṃ paṭic‍ca udakai uc‍cāraṃ vā passāvaṃ vā khaiḷaṃ vā  karaṇapac‍cayā kati āpattiyau āpaj‍jati? anādariyaṃ paṭic‍ca udakai uc‍cāraṃ vā passāvaṃ  vā khaiḷaṃ vā karaṇapac‍cayā aikaṃ āpattiṃ āpaj‍jati| duk‍kaṭaṃ – anādariyaṃ paṭic‍ca udakai uc‍cāraṃ vā passāvaṃ vā khaiḷaṃ vā karaṇapac‍cayā imaṃ aikaṃ āpattiṃ āpaj‍jati|

katāpattivārau niṭṭhitau dutiyau|

3. vipattivārau

195. maithunaṃ  dhammaṃ paṭisaivanapac‍cayā āpattiyau catun‍naṃ vipattīnaṃ kati vipattiyau bhajanti? maithunaṃ dhammaṃ paṭisaivanapac‍cayā āpattiyau catun‍naṃ vipattīnaṃ dvai vipattiyau bhajanti – siyā sīlavipattiṃ siyā ācāravipattiṃ…pai॰…|

anādariyaṃ paṭic‍ca udakai uc‍cāraṃ vā passāvaṃ vā khaiḷaṃ vā karaṇapac‍cayā āpatti catun‍naṃ vipattīnaṃ kati vipattiyau bhajati? anādariyaṃ paṭic‍ca udakai uc‍cāraṃ vā passāvaṃ vā khaiḷaṃ vā karaṇapac‍cayā āpatti catun‍naṃ vipattīnaṃ aikaṃ vipattiṃ bhajati – ācāravipattiṃ|

vipattivārau niṭṭhitau tatiyau|

4. saṅgahitavārau

196. maithunaṃ  dhammaṃ paṭisaivanapac‍cayā āpattiyau sattan‍naṃ āpattikkhandhānaṃ katihi āpattikkhandhaihi saṅgahitā? maithunaṃ dhammaṃ paṭisaivanapac‍cayā  āpattiyau sattan‍naṃ āpattikkhandhānaṃ catūhi āpattikkhandhaihi saṅgahitā – siyā pārājikāpattikkhandhaina, siyā thul‍lac‍cayāpattikkhandhaina, siyā pācittiyāpattikkhandhaina, siyā duk‍kaṭāpattikkhandhaina…pai॰…|

anādariyaṃ paṭic‍ca udakai uc‍cāraṃ vā passāvaṃ vā khaiḷaṃ vā karaṇapac‍cayā āpatti sattan‍naṃ āpattikkhandhānaṃ katihi āpattikkhandhaihi saṅgahitā? anādariyaṃ paṭic‍ca udakai uc‍cāraṃ vā passāvaṃ vā khaiḷaṃ vā karaṇapac‍cayā āpatti sattan‍naṃ āpattikkhandhānaṃ aikaina āpattikkhandhaina saṅgahitā – duk‍kaṭāpattikkhandhaina|

saṅgahitavārau niṭṭhitau catutthau|

5. samuṭṭhānavārau

197. maithunaṃ  dhammaṃ paṭisaivanapac‍cayā āpattiyau chan‍naṃ āpattisamuṭṭhānānaṃ katihi samuṭṭhānaihi samuṭṭhanti? maithunaṃ dhammaṃ paṭisaivanapac‍cayā āpattiyau chan‍naṃ āpattisamuṭṭhānānaṃ aikaina samuṭṭhānaina samuṭṭhanti – kāyatau ca cittatau ca samuṭṭhanti, na vācatau…pai॰…|

anādariyaṃ paṭic‍ca udakai uc‍cāraṃ vā passāvaṃ vā khaiḷaṃ vā karaṇapac‍cayā āpatti chan‍naṃ āpattisamuṭṭhānānaṃ katihi samuṭṭhānaihi samuṭṭhāti? anādariyaṃ paṭic‍ca udakai uc‍cāraṃ vā passāvaṃ vā khaiḷaṃ vā karaṇapac‍cayā āpatti chan‍naṃ āpattisamuṭṭhānānaṃ aikaina samuṭṭhānaina samuṭṭhāti – kāyatau ca cittatau ca samuṭṭhāti, na vācatau…pai॰…|

samuṭṭhānavārau niṭṭhitau pañ‍camau|

6. adhikaraṇavārau

198. maithunaṃ  dhammaṃ paṭisaivanapac‍cayā āpattiyau catun‍naṃ adhikaraṇānaṃ katamaṃ adhikaraṇaṃ? maithunaṃ dhammaṃ paṭisaivanapac‍cayā āpattiyau catun‍naṃ adhikaraṇānaṃ āpattādhikaraṇaṃ…pai॰…|

anādariyaṃ paṭic‍ca udakai uc‍cāraṃ vā passāvaṃ vā khaiḷaṃ vā karaṇapac‍cayā āpatti catun‍naṃ adhikaraṇānaṃ katamaṃ adhikaraṇaṃ? anādariyaṃ paṭic‍ca udakai uc‍cāraṃ vā passāvaṃ vā khaiḷaṃ vā karaṇapac‍cayā āpatti catun‍naṃ adhikaraṇānaṃ āpattādhikaraṇaṃ|

adhikaraṇavārau niṭṭhitau chaṭṭhau|

7. samathavārau

199. maithunaṃ  dhammaṃ paṭisaivanapac‍cayā āpattiyau sattan‍naṃ samathānaṃ katihi samathaihi sammanti? maithunaṃ dhammaṃ paṭisaivanapac‍cayā āpattiyau sattan‍naṃ samathānaṃ tīhi samathaihi sammanti – siyā sammukhāvinayaina ca paṭiñ‍ñātakaraṇaina ca; siyā sammukhāvinayaina tiṇavatthārakaina ca…pai॰…|

anādariyaṃ  paṭic‍ca udakai uc‍cāraṃ vā passāvaṃ vā khaiḷaṃ vā karaṇapac‍cayā āpatti sattan‍naṃ samathānaṃ katihi samathaihi sammati? anādariyaṃ paṭic‍ca udakai uc‍cāraṃ vā passāvaṃ vā khaiḷaṃ vā karaṇapac‍cayā āpatti sattan‍naṃ samathānaṃ tīhi samathaihi sammati – siyā sammukhāvinayaina ca paṭiñ‍ñātakaraṇaina ca, siyā sammukhāvinayaina ca tiṇavatthārakaina cāti|

samathavārau niṭṭhitau sattamau|

8. samuc‍cayavārau

200. maithunaṃ  dhammaṃ paṭisaivanapac‍cayā kati āpattiyau āpaj‍jati? maithunaṃ dhammaṃ paṭisaivanapac‍cayā catassau āpattiyau  āpaj‍jati| akkhāyitai sarīrai maithunaṃ dhammaṃ paṭisaivati, āpatti pārājikassa; yaibhuyyaina khāyitai sarīrai maithunaṃ dhammaṃ paṭisaivati, āpatti thul‍lac‍cayassa; vaṭṭakatai mukhai acchupantaṃ aṅgajātaṃ pavaisaiti, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa; jatumaṭṭhakai pācittiyaṃ – maithunaṃ dhammaṃ paṭisaivanapac‍cayā imā catassau āpattiyau āpaj‍jati| tā āpattiyau catun‍naṃ vipattīnaṃ kati vipattiyau bhajanti? sattan‍naṃ āpattikkhandhānaṃ katihi āpattikkhandhaihi saṅgahitā? chan‍naṃ āpattisamuṭṭhānānaṃ katihi samuṭṭhānaihi samuṭṭhanti? catun‍naṃ adhikaraṇānaṃ katamaṃ adhikaraṇaṃ? sattan‍naṃ samathānaṃ katihi samathaihi sammanti? tā āpattiyau catun‍naṃ vipattīnaṃ dvai vipattiyau bhajanti – siyā sīlavipattiṃ siyā ācāravipattiṃ| sattan‍naṃ āpattikkhandhānaṃ catūhi āpattikkhandhaihi saṅgahitā – siyā pārājikāpattikkhandhaina, siyā thul‍lac‍cayāpattikkhandhaina, siyā pācittiyāpattikkhandhaina, siyā duk‍kaṭāpattikkhandhaina| chan‍naṃ āpattisamuṭṭhānānaṃ aikaina samuṭṭhānaina samuṭṭhanti – kāyatau ca cittatau ca samuṭṭhanti, na vācatau| catun‍naṃ adhikaraṇānaṃ, āpattādhikaraṇaṃ| sattan‍naṃ samathānaṃ, tīhi samathaihi sammanti – siyā sammukhāvinayaina ca, paṭiñ‍ñātakaraṇaina ca, siyā sammukhāvinayaina ca tiṇavatthārakaina ca…pai॰…|

anādariyaṃ paṭic‍ca  udakai uc‍cāraṃ vā passāvaṃ vā khaiḷaṃ vā karaṇapac‍cayā kati āpattiyau āpaj‍jati? anādariyaṃ paṭic‍ca udakai uc‍cāraṃ vā passāvaṃ vā khaiḷaṃ vā karaṇapac‍cayā aikaṃ āpattiṃ āpaj‍jati| duk‍kaṭaṃ – anādariyaṃ paṭic‍ca  udakai uc‍cāraṃ vā passāvaṃ vā khaiḷaṃ vā karaṇapac‍cayā imaṃ aikaṃ āpattiṃ āpaj‍jati| sā āpatti catun‍naṃ vipattīnaṃ kati vipattiyau bhajati? sattan‍naṃ āpattikkhandhānaṃ katihi āpattikkhandhaihi saṅgahitā? chan‍naṃ āpattisamuṭṭhānānaṃ katihi samuṭṭhānaihi samuṭṭhāti? catun‍naṃ adhikaraṇānaṃ katamaṃ  adhikaraṇaṃ? sattan‍naṃ samathānaṃ katihi samathaihi sammati? sā āpatti catun‍naṃ vipattīnaṃ aikaṃ vipattiṃ bhajati – ācāravipattiṃ| sattan‍naṃ āpattikkhandhānaṃ aikaina āpattikkhandhaina saṅgahitā – duk‍kaṭāpattikkhandhaina| chan‍naṃ āpattisamuṭṭhānānaṃ aikaina samuṭṭhānaina samuṭṭhāti – kāyatau ca cittatau ca samuṭṭhāti, na vācatau| catun‍naṃ adhikaraṇānaṃ, āpattādhikaraṇaṃ| sattan‍naṃ samathānaṃ tīhi samathaihi sammati – siyā sammukhāvinayaina ca paṭiñ‍ñātakaraṇaina ca, siyā sammukhāvinayaina ca tiṇavatthārakaina cāti|

samuc‍cayavārau niṭṭhitau aṭṭhamau|

aṭṭhapac‍cayavārā niṭṭhitā|

mahāvibhaṅgai sauḷasamahāvārā niṭṭhitā|

bhikkhuvibhaṅgamahāvārau niṭṭhitau|

bhikkhunīvibhaṅgau

1. katthapañ‍ñattivārau

1. pārājikakaṇḍaṃ
