201. yaṃ  taina bhagavatā jānatā passatā arahatā sammāsambuddhaina bhikkhunīnaṃ pañ‍camaṃ pārājikaṃ kattha pañ‍ñattaṃ? kaṃ ārabbha? kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ? atthi tattha pañ‍ñatti, anupañ‍ñatti, anuppan‍napañ‍ñatti? sabbatthapañ‍ñatti, padaisapañ‍ñatti? sādhāraṇapañ‍ñatti, asādhāraṇapañ‍ñatti? aikataupañ‍ñatti, ubhataupañ‍ñatti? catun‍naṃ pātimaukkhuddaisānaṃ katthaugadhaṃ kattha pariyāpan‍naṃ? katamaina uddaisaina uddaisaṃ āgacchati? catun‍naṃ vipattīnaṃ katamā vipatti? sattan‍naṃ āpattikkhandhānaṃ katamau āpattikkhandhau? chan‍naṃ āpattisamuṭṭhānānaṃ katihi samuṭṭhānaihi samuṭṭhāti? catun‍naṃ adhikaraṇānaṃ katamaṃ adhikaraṇaṃ? sattan‍naṃ samathānaṃ katihi samathaihi sammati? kau tattha vinayau? kau tattha abhivinayau? kiṃ tattha pātimaukkhaṃ? kiṃ tattha adhipātimaukkhaṃ? kā vipatti? kā sampatti? kā paṭipatti? kati atthavasai paṭic‍ca bhagavatā bhikkhunīnaṃ pañ‍camaṃ pārājikaṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ? kā sikkhanti? kā sikkhitasikkhā? kattha ṭhitaṃ? kā dhārainti? kassa vacanaṃ? kainābhatanti?

202. yaṃ taina bhagavatā jānatā passatā arahatā sammāsambuddhaina bhikkhunīnaṃ pañ‍camaṃ pārājikaṃ kattha pañ‍ñattanti? sāvatthiyaṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ| kaṃ ārabbhāti? sundarīnandaṃ bhikkhuniṃ ārabbha| kismiṃ  vatthusminti? sundarīnandā bhikkhunī avassutā avassutassa purisapuggalassa kāyasaṃsaggaṃ sādiyi, tasmiṃ vatthusmiṃ| atthi tattha pañ‍ñatti anupañ‍ñatti anuppan‍napañ‍ñattīti? aikā pañ‍ñatti| anupañ‍ñatti anuppan‍napañ‍ñatti tasmiṃ natthi| sabbatthapañ‍ñatti, padaisapañ‍ñattīti? sabbatthapañ‍ñatti| sādhāraṇapañ‍ñatti, asādhāraṇapañ‍ñattīti? asādhāraṇapañ‍ñatti| aikataupañ‍ñatti, ubhataupañ‍ñattīti? aikataupañ‍ñatti| catun‍naṃ pātimaukkhuddaisānaṃ katthaugadhaṃ kattha pariyāpan‍nanti? nidānaugadhaṃ nidānapariyāpan‍naṃ| katamaina uddaisaina uddaisaṃ āgacchatīti? dutiyaina uddaisaina uddaisaṃ āgacchati| catun‍naṃ vipattīnaṃ katamā vipattīti? sīlavipatti| sattan‍naṃ āpattikkhandhānaṃ katamau āpattikkhandhauti? pārājikāpattikkhandhau| chan‍naṃ āpattisamuṭṭhānānaṃ katihi samuṭṭhānaihi samuṭṭhātīti? aikaina samuṭṭhānai  samuṭṭhāti – kāyatau ca cittatau ca samuṭṭhāti, na vācatau| catun‍naṃ adhikaraṇānaṃ katamaṃ adhikaraṇanti? āpattādhikaraṇaṃ? sattan‍naṃ samathānaṃ katihi samathaihi sammatīti? dvīhi samathaihi sammati – sammukhāvinayaina ca paṭiñ‍ñātakaraṇaina ca| kau tattha vinayau, kau tattha abhivinayauti? pañ‍ñatti vinayau, vibhatti abhivinayau| kiṃ tattha pātimaukkhaṃ, kiṃ tattha adhipātimaukkhanti? pañ‍ñatti pātimaukkhaṃ, vibhatti adhipātimaukkhaṃ| kā vipattīti? asaṃvarau vipatti| kā sampattīti? saṃvarau sampatti| kā paṭipattīti? na aivarūpaṃ karissāmīti yāvajīvaṃ āpāṇakauṭikaṃ samādāya sikkhati sikkhāpadaisu| a॰ ni॰ 10.31 kati atthavasai paṭic‍ca bhagavatā bhikkhunīnaṃ pañ‍camaṃ pārājikaṃ pañ‍ñattanti? dasa atthavasai paṭic‍ca bhagavatā bhikkhunīnaṃ pañ‍camaṃ pārājikaṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ – saṅghasuṭṭhutāya, saṅghaphāsutāya, dummaṅkūnaṃ bhikkhunīnaṃ niggahāya, paisalānaṃ bhikkhunīnaṃ phāsuvihārāya, diṭṭhadhammikānaṃ āsavānaṃ saṃvarāya, samparāyikānaṃ āsavānaṃ paṭighātāya, appasan‍nānaṃ pasādāya, pasan‍nānaṃ bhiyyaubhāvāya, saddhammaṭṭhitiyā, vinayānuggahāya| kā sikkhantīti? saikkhā ca puthuj‍janakalyāṇikā ca sikkhanti| kā sikkhitasikkhāti? arahantiyau arahantā (ka॰) sikkhitasikkhā| kattha ṭhitanti? sikkhākāmāsu ṭhitaṃ| kā dhāraintīti? yāsaṃ vattati tā dhārainti| kassa vacananti? bhagavatau vacanaṃ arahatau sammāsambuddhassa| kainābhatanti? paramparābhataṃ –

upāli dāsakau caiva, sauṇakau siggavau tathā|

mauggaliputtaina pañ‍camā, aitai jambusirivhayai||

tatau mahindau iṭṭiyau, uttiyau sambalau tathā|

bhaddanāmau ca paṇḍitau||

aitai  nāgā mahāpañ‍ñā, jambudīpā idhāgatā|

vinayaṃ tai vācayiṃsu, piṭakaṃ tambapaṇṇiyā||

nikāyai pañ‍ca vācaisuṃ, satta caiva pakaraṇai|

tatau ariṭṭhau maidhāvī, tissadattau ca paṇḍitau||

visāradau kāḷasumanau, thairau ca dīghanāmakau|

dīghasumanau ca paṇḍitau||

punadaiva  kāḷasumanau, nāgatthairau ca buddharakkhitau|

tissatthairau ca maidhāvī, daivatthairau ca paṇḍitau||

punadaiva sumanau maidhāvī, vinayai ca visāradau|

bahussutau cūḷanāgau, gajauva duppadhaṃsiyau||

dhammapālitanāmau ca, rauhaṇai sādhupūjitau|

tassa sissau mahāpañ‍ñau, khaimanāmau tipaiṭakau||

dīpai tārakarājāva pañ‍ñāya atiraucatha|

upatissau ca maidhāvī, phussadaivau mahākathī||

punadaiva sumanau maidhāvī, pupphanāmau bahussutau|

mahākathī mahāsivau, piṭakai sabbattha kauvidau||

punadaiva upāli maidhāvī, vinayai ca visāradau|

mahānāgau mahāpañ‍ñau, saddhammavaṃsakauvidau||

punadaiva abhayau maidhāvī, piṭakai sabbattha kauvidau|

tissatthairau ca maidhāvī, vinayai ca visāradau||

tassa  sissau mahāpañ‍ñau, pupphanāmau bahussutau|

sāsanaṃ anurakkhantau, jambudīpai patiṭṭhitau||

cūḷābhayau ca maidhāvī, vinayai ca visāradau|

tissatthairau ca maidhāvī, saddhammavaṃsakauvidau||

cūḷadaivau ca maidhāvī, vinayai ca visāradau|

sivatthairau ca maidhāvī, vinayai sabbattha kauvidau||

aitai nāgā mahāpañ‍ñā, vinayañ‍ñū maggakauvidā|

vinayaṃ  dīpai pakāsaisuṃ, piṭakaṃ tambapaṇṇiyāti||

203. yaṃ  taina bhagavatā jānatā passatā arahatā sammāsambuddhaina bhikkhunīnaṃ chaṭṭhaṃ pārājikaṃ kattha pañ‍ñattanti? sāvatthiyaṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ| kaṃ ārabbhāti? thul‍lanandaṃ bhikkhuniṃ ārabbha| kismiṃ vatthusminti? thul‍lanandā bhikkhunī jānaṃ pārājikaṃ dhammaṃ ajjhāpan‍naṃ bhikkhuniṃ naivattanā paṭicaudaisi  gaṇassa āraucaisi, tasmiṃ vatthusmiṃ| aikā pañ‍ñatti| chan‍naṃ āpattisamuṭṭhānānaṃ aikaina samuṭṭhānaina samuṭṭhāti – kāyatau ca vācatau ca cittatau ca samuṭṭhāti…pai॰…|

204. bhikkhunīnaṃ sattamaṃ pārājikaṃ kattha pañ‍ñattanti? sāvatthiyaṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ| kaṃ ārabbhāti? thul‍lanandaṃ bhikkhuniṃ ārabbha| kismiṃ vatthusminti? thul‍lanandā bhikkhunī samaggaina saṅghaina ukkhittaṃ ariṭṭhaṃ bhikkhuṃ gaddhabādhipubbaṃ anuvatti, tasmiṃ vatthusmiṃ| aikā pañ‍ñatti| chan‍naṃ āpattisamuṭṭhānānaṃ aikaina samuṭṭhānaina samuṭṭhāti – dhuranikkhaipai…pai॰…|

205. bhikkhunīnaṃ aṭṭhamaṃ pārājikaṃ kattha pañ‍ñattanti? sāvatthiyaṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ| kaṃ ārabbhāti? chabbaggiyā bhikkhuniyau ārabbha| kismiṃ vatthusminti? chabbaggiyā bhikkhuniyau aṭṭhamaṃ vatthuṃ paripūraisuṃ, tasmiṃ vatthusmiṃ| aikā pañ‍ñatti| chan‍naṃ āpattisamuṭṭhānānaṃ aikaina samuṭṭhānaina samuṭṭhāti – dhuranikkhaipai…pai॰…|

aṭṭha pārājikā niṭṭhitā|

tassuddānaṃ –

maithunādin‍nādānañ‍ca, manussaviggahuttari|

kāyasaṃsaggaṃ chādaiti, ukkhittā aṭṭha vatthukā|

pañ‍ñāpaisi mahāvīrau, chaij‍javatthū asaṃsayāti||

2. saṅghādisaisakaṇḍaṃ

206. yaṃ taina bhagavatā jānatā passatā arahatā sammāsambuddhaina ussayavādikāya bhikkhuniyā aḍḍaṃ karauntiyā saṅghādisaisau kattha pañ‍ñattau? kaṃ ārabbha? kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ…pai॰… kainābhatanti?

207. yaṃ taina bhagavatā jānatā passatā arahatā sammāsambuddhaina ussayavādikāya bhikkhuniyā aḍḍaṃ karauntiyā saṅghādisaisau kattha pañ‍ñattauti? sāvatthiyaṃ pañ‍ñattau| kaṃ ārabbhāti? thul‍lanandaṃ bhikkhuniṃ ārabbha| kismiṃ vatthusminti? thul‍lanandā bhikkhunī ussayavādikā vihari, tasmiṃ  vatthusmiṃ| atthi tattha pañ‍ñatti, anupañ‍ñatti, anuppan‍napañ‍ñattīti? aikā pañ‍ñatti| anupañ‍ñatti anuppan‍napañ‍ñatti tasmiṃ natthi| sabbatthapañ‍ñatti, padaisapañ‍ñattīti? sabbatthapañ‍ñatti| sādhāraṇapañ‍ñatti, asādhāraṇapañ‍ñattīti? asādhāraṇapañ‍ñatti| aikataupañ‍ñatti, ubhataupañ‍ñattīti? aikataupañ‍ñatti| catun‍naṃ pātimaukkhuddaisānaṃ katthaugadhaṃ kattha pariyāpan‍nanti? nidānaugadhaṃ nidānapariyāpan‍naṃ| katamaina uddaisaina uddaisaṃ āgacchatīti? tatiyaina uddaisaina uddaisaṃ āgacchati| catun‍naṃ vipattīnaṃ katamā vipattīti? sīlavipatti| sattan‍naṃ āpattikkhandhānaṃ katamau āpattikkhandhauti? saṅghādisaisāpattikkhandhau| chan‍naṃ āpattisamuṭṭhānānaṃ katihi samuṭṭhānaihi samuṭṭhātīti? dvīhi samuṭṭhānaihi samuṭṭhāti – siyā kāyatau ca vācatau ca samuṭṭhāti, na cittatau; siyā kāyatau ca vācatau  ca cittatau ca samuṭṭhāti…pai॰… kainābhatanti? paramparābhataṃ –

upāli dāsakau caiva, sauṇakau siggavau tathā|

mauggaliputtaina pañ‍camā, aitai jambusirivhayai|| …pai॰…|

aitai nāgā mahāpañ‍ñā, vinayañ‍ñū maggakauvidā|

vinayaṃ dīpai pakāsaisuṃ, piṭakaṃ tambapaṇṇiyāti||

208. cauriṃ  vuṭṭhāpaintiyā saṅghādisaisau kattha pañ‍ñattauti? sāvatthiyaṃ pañ‍ñattau| kaṃ ārabbhāti? thul‍lanandaṃ bhikkhuniṃ ārabbha| kismiṃ vatthusminti? thul‍lanandā bhikkhunī cauriṃ vuṭṭhāpaisi, tasmiṃ vatthusmiṃ| aikā pañ‍ñatti| chan‍naṃ āpattisamuṭṭhānānaṃ dvīhi samuṭṭhānaihi samuṭṭhāti – siyā vācatau ca cittatau ca samuṭṭhāti, na kāyatau; siyā kāyatau ca vācatau ca cittatau ca samuṭṭhāti…pai॰…|

209. aikāya gāmantaraṃ gacchantiyā saṅghādisaisau kattha pañ‍ñattauti? sāvatthiyaṃ pañ‍ñattau| kaṃ ārabbhāti? añ‍ñataraṃ bhikkhuniṃ ārabbha| kismiṃ vatthusminti? añ‍ñatarā bhikkhunī aikā gāmantaraṃ gacchi, tasmiṃ vatthusmiṃ| aikā pañ‍ñatti, tissau anupañ‍ñattiyau| chan‍naṃ āpattisamuṭṭhānānaṃ aikaina samuṭṭhānaina samuṭṭhāti  – paṭhamapārājikai…pai॰…|

210. samaggaina saṅghaina ukkhittaṃ bhikkhuniṃ dhammaina vinayaina satthusāsanaina anapalaukaitvā kārakasaṅghaṃ anañ‍ñāya gaṇassa chandaṃ  ausāraintiyā saṅghādisaisau kattha pañ‍ñattauti? sāvatthiyaṃ pañ‍ñattau| kaṃ ārabbhāti? thul‍lanandaṃ bhikkhuniṃ ārabbha| kismiṃ vatthusminti? thul‍lanandā bhikkhunī samaggaina saṅghaina ukkhittaṃ bhikkhuniṃ dhammaina vinayaina satthusāsanaina anapalaukaitvā kārakasaṅghaṃ anañ‍ñāya gaṇassa chandaṃ ausāraisi, tasmiṃ vatthusmiṃ| aikā pañ‍ñatti| chan‍naṃ āpattisamuṭṭhānānaṃ aikaina samuṭṭhānaina samuṭṭhāti – dhuranikkhaipai…pai॰…|

211. avassutāya bhikkhuniyā avassutassa purisapuggalassa hatthatau khādanīyaṃ vā bhaujanīyaṃ vā sahatthā paṭiggahaitvā bhuñ‍jantiyā saṅghādisaisau kattha pañ‍ñattauti? sāvatthiyaṃ pañ‍ñattau| kaṃ ārabbhāti? sundarīnandaṃ bhikkhuniṃ ārabbha| kismiṃ vatthusminti? sundarīnandā bhikkhunī avassutā avassutassa purisapuggalassa hatthatau āmisaṃ paṭiggahaisi, tasmiṃ vatthusmiṃ| aikā pañ‍ñatti| chan‍naṃ āpattisamuṭṭhānānaṃ aikaina samuṭṭhānaina samuṭṭhāti – paṭhamapārājikai…pai॰…|

212. ‘‘kiṃ tai, ayyai, aisau purisapuggalau karissati avassutau vā anavassutau vā, yatau tvaṃ anavassutā! iṅgha, ayyai, yaṃ tai aisau purisapuggalau daiti khādanīyaṃ vā bhaujanīyaṃ vā, taṃ tvaṃ  sahatthā paṭiggahaitvā khāda vā bhuñ‍ja vā’’ti uyyaujaintiyā saṅghādisaisau kattha pañ‍ñattauti? sāvatthiyaṃ pañ‍ñattau| kaṃ arabbhāti? añ‍ñataraṃ bhikkhuniṃ ārabbha| kismiṃ vatthusminti? añ‍ñatarā  bhikkhunī –‘‘kiṃ tai, ayyai, aisau purisapuggalau karissati avassutau vā anavassutau vā, yatau tvaṃ anavassutā! iṅgha, ayyai, yaṃ tai aisau purisapuggalau daiti khādanīyaṃ vā bhaujanīyaṃ vā, taṃ tvaṃ sahatthā paṭiggahaitvā khāda vā bhuñ‍ja vā’’ti uyyaujaisi, tasmiṃ vatthusmiṃ| aikā pañ‍ñatti| chan‍naṃ āpattisamuṭṭhānānaṃ tīhi samuṭṭhānaihi samuṭṭhāti…pai॰…|

213. kupitāya anattamanāya bhikkhuniyā yāvatatiyaṃ samanubhāsanāya na paṭinissaj‍jantiyā  saṅghādisaisau kattha pañ‍ñattauti? sāvatthiyaṃ pañ‍ñattau| kaṃ ārabbhāti? caṇḍakāḷiṃ bhikkhuniṃ ārabbha| kismiṃ vatthusminti? caṇḍakāḷī bhikkhunī kupitā anattamanā aivaṃ avaca – ‘‘buddhaṃ pac‍cācikkhāmi, dhammaṃ pac‍cācikkhāmi, saṅghaṃ pac‍cācikkhāmi, sikkhaṃ pac‍cācikkhāmī’’ti, tasmiṃ  vatthusmiṃ| aikā pañ‍ñatti| chan‍naṃ āpattisamuṭṭhānānaṃ aikaina samuṭṭhānaina samuṭṭhāti – dhuranikkhaipai…pai॰…|

214. kismiñ‍cidaiva adhikaraṇai pac‍cākatāya bhikkhuniyā yāvatatiyaṃ samanubhāsanāya na paṭinissaj‍jantiyā saṅghādisaisau kattha pañ‍ñattauti? sāvatthiyaṃ pañ‍ñattau| kaṃ ārabbhāti? caṇḍakāḷiṃ  bhikkhuniṃ ārabbha| kismiṃ vatthusminti? caṇḍakāḷī bhikkhunī kismiñ‍cidaiva adhikaraṇai pac‍cākatā kupitā anattamanā aivaṃ avaca – ‘‘chandagāminiyau ca bhikkhuniyau, dausagāminiyau ca bhikkhuniyau, mauhagāminiyau ca bhikkhuniyau, bhayagāminiyau ca bhikkhuniyau’’ti, tasmiṃ vatthusmiṃ| aikā pañ‍ñatti| chan‍naṃ āpattisamuṭṭhānānaṃ aikaina samuṭṭhānaina samuṭṭhāti – dhuranikkhaipai…pai॰…|

215. saṃsaṭṭhānaṃ bhikkhunīnaṃ yāvatatiyaṃ samanubhāsanāya na paṭinissaj‍jantīnaṃ saṅghādisaisau kattha pañ‍ñattauti? sāvatthiyaṃ pañ‍ñattau| kaṃ ārabbhāti? sambahulā bhikkhuniyau ārabbha| kismiṃ vatthusminti? sambahulā bhikkhuniyau saṃsaṭṭhā vihariṃsu, tasmiṃ vatthusmiṃ| aikā pañ‍ñatti| chan‍naṃ āpattisamuṭṭhānānaṃ aikaina samuṭṭhānaina samuṭṭhāti – dhuranikkhaipai…pai॰…|

216. ‘‘saṃsaṭṭhāva, ayyai, tumhai viharatha| mā tumhai nānā viharitthā’’ti uyyaujaintiyā yāvatatiyaṃ samanubhāsanāya na paṭinissaj‍jantiyā saṅghādisaisau kattha pañ‍ñattauti? sāvatthiyaṃ pañ‍ñattau| kaṃ ārabbhāti? thul‍lanandaṃ bhikkhuniṃ ārabbha| kismiṃ vatthusminti? thul‍lanandā bhikkhunī – ‘‘saṃsaṭṭhāva ayyai, tumhai viharatha, mā tumhai nānā viharitthā’’ti uyyaujaisi, tasmiṃ  vatthusmiṃ| aikā pañ‍ñatti| chan‍naṃ āpattisamuṭṭhānānaṃ aikaina samuṭṭhānaina samuṭṭhāti – dhuranikkhaipai…pai॰…|

dasa saṅghādisaisā niṭṭhitā|

tassuddānaṃ –

ussayacauri  gāmantaṃ, ukkhittaṃ khādanaina ca|

kiṃ tai kupitā kismiñ‍ci, saṃsaṭṭhā ñāyatai dasāti||

3. nissaggiyakaṇḍaṃ

217. yaṃ  taina bhagavatā jānatā passatā arahatā sammāsambuddhaina pattasan‍nicayaṃ karauntiyā nissaggiyaṃ pācittiyaṃ kattha pañ‍ñattanti? sāvatthiyaṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ| kaṃ ārabbhāti? chabbaggiyā bhikkhuniyau ārabbha| kismiṃ vatthusminti? chabbaggiyā bhikkhuniyau pattasan‍nicayaṃ akaṃsu, tasmiṃ vatthusmiṃ| aikā pañ‍ñatti| chan‍naṃ āpattisamuṭṭhānānaṃ dvīhi samuṭṭhānaihi samuṭṭhāti – kathinakai…pai॰…|

akālacīvaraṃ ‘‘kālacīvara’’nti adhiṭṭhahitvā bhājāpaintiyā nissaggiyaṃ pācittiyaṃ kattha pañ‍ñattanti? sāvatthiyaṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ| kaṃ ārabbhāti? thul‍lanandaṃ bhikkhuniṃ ārabbha| kismiṃ vatthusminti? thul‍lanandā bhikkhunī  akālacīvaraṃ ‘‘kālacīvara’’nti adhiṭṭhahitvā bhājāpaisi, tasmiṃ vatthusmiṃ| aikā pañ‍ñatti| chan‍naṃ āpattisamuṭṭhānānaṃ tīhi samuṭṭhānaihi samuṭṭhāti…pai॰…|

bhikkhuniyā saddhiṃ cīvaraṃ parivattaitvā acchindantiyā nissaggiyaṃ pācittiyaṃ kattha pañ‍ñattanti? sāvatthiyaṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ| kaṃ ārabbhāti? thul‍lanandaṃ bhikkhuniṃ ārabbha| kismiṃ vatthusminti? thul‍lanandā bhikkhunī bhikkhuniyā saddhiṃ cīvaraṃ parivattaitvā acchindi, tasmiṃ  vatthusmiṃ| aikā pañ‍ñatti| chan‍naṃ āpattisamuṭṭhānānaṃ tīhi samuṭṭhānaihi samuṭṭhāti…pai॰…|

añ‍ñaṃ viñ‍ñāpaitvā añ‍ñaṃ viñ‍ñāpaintiyā nissaggiyaṃ pācittiyaṃ kattha pañ‍ñattanti? sāvatthiyaṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ| kaṃ ārabbhāti? thul‍lanandaṃ bhikkhuniṃ ārabbha| kismiṃ vatthusminti? thul‍lanandā  bhikkhunī añ‍ñaṃ viñ‍ñāpaitvā añ‍ñaṃ viñ‍ñāpaisi, tasmiṃ vatthusmiṃ| aikā pañ‍ñatti| chan‍naṃ āpattisamuṭṭhānānaṃ chahi samuṭṭhānaihi samuṭṭhāti…pai॰…|

añ‍ñaṃ caitāpaitvā añ‍ñaṃ caitāpaintiyā nissaggiyaṃ pācittiyaṃ kattha pañ‍ñattanti? sāvatthiyaṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ| kaṃ ārabbhāti? thul‍lanandaṃ bhikkhuniṃ ārabbha| kismiṃ vatthusminti? thul‍lanandā bhikkhunī añ‍ñaṃ caitāpaitvā añ‍ñaṃ caitāpaisi, tasmiṃ vatthusmiṃ| aikā pañ‍ñatti| chan‍naṃ āpattisamuṭṭhānānaṃ chahi samuṭṭhānaihi samuṭṭhāti…pai॰…|

añ‍ñadatthikaina parikkhāraina añ‍ñuddisikaina saṅghikaina añ‍ñaṃ caitāpaintiyā nissaggiyaṃ pācittiyaṃ katthaṃ pañ‍ñattanti? sāvatthiyaṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ| kaṃ ārabbhāti? sambahulā bhikkhuniyau ārabbha| kismiṃ vatthusminti? sambahulā bhikkhuniyau añ‍ñadatthikaina parikkhāraina añ‍ñuddisikaina saṅghikaina añ‍ñaṃ caitāpaisuṃ, tasmiṃ vatthusmiṃ| aikā pañ‍ñatti| chan‍naṃ āpattisamuṭṭhānānaṃ chahi samuṭṭhānaihi  samuṭṭhāti…pai॰…|

añ‍ñadatthikaina parikkhāraina añ‍ñuddisikaina saṅghikaina sañ‍ñācikaina añ‍ñaṃ caitāpaintiyā nissaggiyaṃ pācittiyaṃ kattha pañ‍ñattanti? sāvatthiyaṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ| kaṃ ārabbhāti? sambahulā bhikkhuniyau ārabbha| kismiṃ vatthusminti? sambahulā bhikkhuniyau añ‍ñadatthikaina parikkhāraina añ‍ñuddisikaina saṅghikaina sañ‍ñācikaina añ‍ñaṃ caitāpaisuṃ, tasmiṃ vatthusmiṃ| aikā pañ‍ñatti| chan‍naṃ āpattisamuṭṭhānānaṃ chahi samuṭṭhānaihi samuṭṭhāti…pai॰…|

añ‍ñadatthikaina parikkhāraina añ‍ñuddisikaina mahājanikaina añ‍ñaṃ caitāpaintiyā nissaggiyaṃ pācittiyaṃ kattha pañ‍ñattanti? sāvatthiyaṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ| kaṃ ārabbhāti? sambahulā bhikkhuniyau ārabbha| kismiṃ vatthusminti? sambahulā bhikkhuniyau añ‍ñadatthikaina parikkhāraina  añ‍ñuddisikaina mahājanikaina añ‍ñaṃ caitāpaisuṃ, tasmiṃ vatthusmiṃ| aikā pañ‍ñatti| chan‍naṃ āpattisamuṭṭhānānaṃ chahi samuṭṭhānaihi samuṭṭhāti…pai॰…|

añ‍ñadatthikaina parikkhāraina añ‍ñuddisikaina mahājanikaina sañ‍ñācikaina añ‍ñaṃ caitāpaintiyā nissaggiyaṃ pācittiyaṃ kattha pañ‍ñattanti? sāvatthiyaṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ| kaṃ ārabbhāti? sambahulā bhikkhuniyau ārabbha| kismiṃ vatthusminti? sambahulā bhikkhuniyau añ‍ñadatthikaina parikkhāraina añ‍ñuddisikaina mahājanikaina sañ‍ñācikaina  añ‍ñaṃ caitāpaisuṃ, tasmiṃ vatthusmiṃ| aikā pañ‍ñatti| chan‍naṃ āpattisamuṭṭhānānaṃ chahi samuṭṭhānaihi samuṭṭhāti…pai॰…|

añ‍ñadatthikaina parikkhāraina añ‍ñuddisikaina puggalikaina sañ‍ñācikaina añ‍ñaṃ caitāpaintiyā nissaggiyaṃ pācittiyaṃ kattha pañ‍ñattanti? sāvatthiyaṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ| kaṃ ārabbhāti? thul‍lanandaṃ bhikkhuniṃ ārabbha| kismiṃ vatthusminti? thul‍lanandā bhikkhunī añ‍ñadatthikaina parikkhāraina añ‍ñuddisikaina puggalikaina sañ‍ñācikaina añ‍ñaṃ caitāpaisi, tasmiṃ vatthusmiṃ| aikā pañ‍ñatti| chan‍naṃ āpattisamuṭṭhānānaṃ chahi samuṭṭhānaihi samuṭṭhāti…pai॰…|

atiraikacatuk‍kaṃsaparamaṃ  garupāvuraṇaṃ caitāpaintiyā nissaggiyaṃ pācittiyaṃ kattha pañ‍ñattanti? sāvatthiyaṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ| kaṃ ārabbhāti? thul‍lanandaṃ bhikkhuniṃ ārabbha| kismiṃ vatthusminti? thul‍lanandā bhikkhunī rājānaṃ kambalaṃ viñ‍ñāpaisi, tasmiṃ vatthusmiṃ| aikā pañ‍ñatti| chan‍naṃ āpattisamuṭṭhānānaṃ chahi samuṭṭhānaihi samuṭṭhāti…pai॰…|

atiraikaaḍḍhataiyyakaṃsaparamaṃ lahupāvuraṇaṃ caitāpaintiyā  nissaggiyaṃ pācittiyaṃ kattha pañ‍ñattanti? sāvatthiyaṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ| kaṃ ārabbhāti? thul‍lanandaṃ bhikkhuniṃ ārabbha| kismiṃ vatthusminti? thul‍lanandā bhikkhunī rājānaṃ khaumaṃ viñ‍ñāpaisi, tasmiṃ vatthusmiṃ| aikā pañ‍ñatti| chan‍naṃ āpattisamuṭṭhānānaṃ chahi samuṭṭhānaihi samuṭṭhāti  …pai॰…|

dvādasa nissaggiyā pācittiyā niṭṭhitā|

tassuddānaṃ –

pattaṃ akālaṃ kālañ‍ca, parivattai ca viñ‍ñāpai|

caitāpaitvā añ‍ñadatthi, saṅghikañ‍ca mahājanikaṃ|

sañ‍ñācikā puggalikā, catuk‍kaṃsaḍḍhataiyyakāti||

4. pācittiyakaṇḍaṃ

1. lasuṇavaggau

218. yaṃ taina bhagavatā jānatā passatā arahatā sammāsambuddhaina lasuṇaṃ khādantiyā pācittiyaṃ  kattha pañ‍ñattanti? sāvatthiyaṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ| kaṃ ārabbhāti? thul‍lanandaṃ bhikkhuniṃ ārabbha| kismiṃ vatthusminti? thul‍lanandā bhikkhunī na mattaṃ jānitvā lasuṇaṃ harāpaisi, tasmiṃ vatthusmiṃ| aikā pañ‍ñatti| chan‍naṃ āpattisamuṭṭhānānaṃ dvīhi samuṭṭhānaihi samuṭṭhāti aiḷakalaumakai…pai॰…|

sambādhai laumaṃ saṃharāpaintiyā pācittiyaṃ kattha pañ‍ñattanti? sāvatthiyaṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ| kaṃ ārabbhāti? chabbaggiyā bhikkhuniyau ārabbha| kismiṃ vatthusminti? chabbaggiyā bhikkhuniyau sambādhai laumaṃ saṃharāpaisuṃ, tasmiṃ vatthusmiṃ| aikā pañ‍ñatti| chan‍naṃ āpattisamuṭṭhānānaṃ catūhi samuṭṭhānaihi samuṭṭhāti…pai॰…|

talaghātakai  pācittiyaṃ kattha pañ‍ñattanti? sāvatthiyaṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ| kaṃ ārabbhāti? dvai bhikkhuniyau ārabbha| kismiṃ vatthusminti? dvai bhikkhuniyau talaghātakaṃ akaṃsu, tasmiṃ vatthusmiṃ| aikā pañ‍ñatti| chan‍naṃ āpattisamuṭṭhānānaṃ aikaina samuṭṭhānaina samuṭṭhāti – paṭhamapārājikai…pai॰…|

jatumaṭṭhakai pācittiyaṃ kattha pañ‍ñattanti? sāvatthiyaṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ| kaṃ ārabbhāti? añ‍ñataraṃ bhikkhuniṃ ārabbha| kismiṃ vatthusminti? añ‍ñatarā bhikkhunī jatumaṭṭhakaṃ ādiyi, tasmiṃ vatthusmiṃ| aikā pañ‍ñatti| chan‍naṃ āpattisamuṭṭhānānaṃ aikaina samuṭṭhānaina samuṭṭhāti – paṭhamapārājikai…pai॰…|

atiraikadvaṅgulapabbaparamaṃ udakasuddhikaṃ ādiyantiyā pācittiyaṃ kattha pañ‍ñattanti? sak‍kaisu pañ‍ñattaṃ| kaṃ ārabbhāti? añ‍ñataraṃ bhikkhuniṃ ārabbha| kismiṃ vatthusminti? añ‍ñatarā bhikkhunī atigambhīraṃ udakasuddhikaṃ ādiyi, tasmiṃ vatthusmiṃ| aikā pañ‍ñatti| chan‍naṃ āpattisamuṭṭhānānaṃ aikaina samuṭṭhānaina samuṭṭhāti – paṭhamapārājikai…pai॰…|

bhikkhussa bhuñ‍jantassa pānīyaina vā vidhūpanaina vā upatiṭṭhantiyā pācittiyaṃ kattha pañ‍ñattanti? sāvatthiyaṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ| kaṃ ārabbhāti? añ‍ñataraṃ bhikkhuniṃ ārabbha| kismiṃ vatthusminti? añ‍ñatarā bhikkhunī bhikkhussa bhuñ‍jantassa pānīyaina ca vidhūpanaina ca upatiṭṭhi, tasmiṃ vatthusmiṃ| aikā pañ‍ñatti| chan‍naṃ āpattisamuṭṭhānānaṃ dvīhi samuṭṭhānaihi samuṭṭhāti – aiḷakalaumakai…pai॰…|

āmakadhañ‍ñaṃ  viñ‍ñāpaitvā bhuñ‍jantiyā pācittiyaṃ kattha pañ‍ñattanti? sāvatthiyaṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ| kaṃ ārabbhāti? sambahulā bhikkhuniyau ārabbha| kismiṃ vatthusminti? sambahulā bhikkhuniyau āmakadhañ‍ñaṃ viñ‍ñāpaitvā bhuñ‍jiṃsu, tasmiṃ vatthusmiṃ| aikā pañ‍ñatti| chan‍naṃ āpattisamuṭṭhānānaṃ catūhi samuṭṭhānaihi samuṭṭhāti…pai॰…|

uc‍cāraṃ vā passāvaṃ vā saṅkāraṃ vā vighāsaṃ vā tiraukuṭṭai tiraukuḍḍai (sī॰ syā॰) chaḍḍaintiyā pācittiyaṃ kattha pañ‍ñattanti? sāvatthiyaṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ| kaṃ ārabbhāti? añ‍ñataraṃ bhikkhuniṃ ārabbha| kismiṃ vatthusminti? añ‍ñatarā bhikkhunī uc‍cāraṃ uc‍cārampi passāvampi saṅkārampi vighāsampi (ka॰) tiraukuṭṭai chaḍḍaisi, tasmiṃ vatthusmiṃ| aikā pañ‍ñatti| chan‍naṃ āpattisamuṭṭhānānaṃ chahi samuṭṭhānaihi samuṭṭhāti…pai॰…|

uc‍cāraṃ  vā passāvaṃ vā saṅkāraṃ vā vighāsaṃ vā haritai chaḍḍaintiyā pācittiyaṃ kattha pañ‍ñattanti? sāvatthiyaṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ| kaṃ ārabbhāti? sambahulā bhikkhuniyau ārabbha| kismiṃ vatthusminti? sambahulā bhikkhuniyau uc‍cārampi passāvampi saṅkārampi vighāsampi haritai chaḍḍaisuṃ, tasmiṃ vatthusmiṃ| aikā pañ‍ñatti| chan‍naṃ āpattisamuṭṭhānānaṃ chahi samuṭṭhānaihi samuṭṭhāti…pai॰…|

nac‍caṃ vā gītaṃ vā vāditaṃ vā dassanāya gacchantiyā pācittiyaṃ  kattha pañ‍ñattanti? rājagahai pañ‍ñattaṃ| kaṃ ārabbhāti? chabbaggiyā bhikkhuniyau ārabbha| kismiṃ vatthusminti? chabbaggiyā bhikkhuniyau nac‍campi gītampi vāditampi dassanāya agamaṃsu, tasmiṃ vatthusmiṃ| aikā pañ‍ñatti| chan‍naṃ āpattisamuṭṭhānānaṃ dvīhi samuṭṭhānaihi samuṭṭhāti – aiḷakalaumakai…pai॰…|

lasuṇavaggau paṭhamau|

2. rattandhakāravaggau

219. rattandhakārai appadīpai purisaina saddhiṃ aikainaikāya santiṭṭhantiyā pācittiyaṃ kattha pañ‍ñattanti? sāvatthiyaṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ| kaṃ ārabbhāti? añ‍ñataraṃ bhikkhuniṃ ārabbha| kismiṃ vatthusminti? añ‍ñatarā bhikkhunī rattandhakārai appadīpai purisaina saddhiṃ aikainaikā santiṭṭhi, tasmiṃ vatthusmiṃ| aikā pañ‍ñatti| chan‍naṃ āpattisamuṭṭhānānaṃ dvīhi samuṭṭhānaihi samuṭṭhāti – thaiyyasatthakai…pai॰…|

paṭicchan‍nai  aukāsai purisaina saddhiṃ aikainaikāya santiṭṭhantiyā pācittiyaṃ kattha pañ‍ñattanti? sāvatthiyaṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ| kaṃ ārabbhāti? añ‍ñataraṃ bhikkhuniṃ ārabbha| kismiṃ vatthusminti? añ‍ñatarā bhikkhunī paṭicchan‍nai aukāsai purisaina saddhiṃ aikainaikā santiṭṭhi, tasmiṃ vatthusmiṃ| aikā pañ‍ñatti| chan‍naṃ āpattisamuṭṭhānānaṃ dvīhi samuṭṭhānaihi samuṭṭhāti – thaiyyasatthakai…pai॰…|

ajjhaukāsai purisaina saddhiṃ aikainaikāya santiṭṭhantiyā pācittiyaṃ kattha pañ‍ñattanti? sāvatthiyaṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ| kaṃ ārabbhāti? añ‍ñataraṃ bhikkhuniṃ ārabbha| kismiṃ vatthusminti? añ‍ñatarā bhikkhunī ajjhaukāsai purisaina saddhiṃ aikainaikā santiṭṭhi, tasmiṃ vatthusmiṃ| aikā pañ‍ñatti| chan‍naṃ āpattisamuṭṭhānānaṃ dvīhi samuṭṭhānaihi samuṭṭhāti – thaiyyasatthakai…pai॰…|

rathikā  vā rathiyāya vā (ka॰) byūhai vā siṅghāṭakai vā purisaina saddhiṃ aikainaikāya santiṭṭhantiyā pācittiyaṃ kattha pañ‍ñattanti? sāvatthiyaṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ| kaṃ ārabbhāti? thul‍lanandaṃ bhikkhuniṃ ārabbha| kismiṃ vatthusminti? thul‍lanandā bhikkhunī rathikāyapi byūhaipi siṅghāṭakaipi purisaina saddhiṃ aikainaikā santiṭṭhi, tasmiṃ vatthusmiṃ| aikā pañ‍ñatti| chan‍naṃ āpattisamuṭṭhānānaṃ dvīhi samuṭṭhānaihi samuṭṭhāti – thaiyyasatthakai…pai॰…|

puraibhattaṃ kulāni upasaṅkamitvā āsanai nisīditvā sāmikai anāpucchā pak‍kamantiyā pācittiyaṃ kattha pañ‍ñattanti? sāvatthiyaṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ| kaṃ ārabbhāti? añ‍ñataraṃ bhikkhuniṃ ārabbha| kismiṃ vatthusminti? añ‍ñatarā bhikkhunī puraibhattaṃ kulāni upasaṅkamitvā āsanai nisīditvā sāmikai anāpucchā pak‍kāmi, tasmiṃ vatthusmiṃ| aikā pañ‍ñatti| chan‍naṃ āpattisamuṭṭhānānaṃ dvīhi samuṭṭhānaihi samuṭṭhāti – kathinakai…pai॰…|

pacchābhattaṃ kulāni upasaṅkamitvā sāmikai anāpucchā āsanai abhinisīdantiyā pācittiyaṃ kattha pañ‍ñattanti? sāvatthiyaṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ| kaṃ ārabbhāti? thul‍lanandaṃ bhikkhuniṃ ārabbha| kismiṃ vatthusminti? thul‍lanandā bhikkhunī pacchābhattaṃ kulāni upasaṅkamitvā sāmikai anāpucchā āsanai abhinisīdi, tasmiṃ vatthusmiṃ| aikā pañ‍ñatti| chan‍naṃ āpattisamuṭṭhānānaṃ dvīhi samuṭṭhānaihi samuṭṭhāti – kathinakai…pai॰…|

vikālai kulāni upasaṅkamitvā sāmikai anāpucchā saiyyaṃ santharitvā vā santharāpaitvā vā  abhinisīdantiyā pācittiyaṃ kattha pañ‍ñattanti? sāvatthiyaṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ| kaṃ ārabbhāti? sambahulā bhikkhuniyau ārabbha| kismiṃ vatthusminti? sambahulā bhikkhuniyau vikālai kulāni upasaṅkamitvā sāmikai anāpucchā saiyyaṃ santharitvā abhinisīdiṃsu, tasmiṃ vatthusmiṃ| aikā pañ‍ñatti| chan‍naṃ āpattisamuṭṭhānānaṃ dvīhi samuṭṭhānaihi samuṭṭhāti – kathinakai…pai॰…|

duggahitaina dūpadhāritaina paraṃ ujjhāpaintiyā pācittiyaṃ kattha pañ‍ñattanti? sāvatthiyaṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ| kaṃ ārabbhāti? añ‍ñataraṃ bhikkhuniṃ ārabbha| kismiṃ vatthusminti? añ‍ñatarā bhikkhunī duggahitaina dūpadhāritaina paraṃ ujjhāpaisi, tasmiṃ vatthusmiṃ| aikā pañ‍ñatti chan‍naṃ āpattisamuṭṭhānānaṃ tīhi samuṭṭhānaihi samuṭṭhāti…pai॰…|

attānaṃ  vā paraṃ vā nirayaina vā brahmacariyaina vā abhisapantiyā pācittiyaṃ kattha pañ‍ñattanti? sāvatthiyaṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ| kaṃ  ārabbhāti? caṇḍakāḷiṃ bhikkhuniṃ ārabbha| kismiṃ vatthusminti? caṇḍakāḷī bhikkhunī attānampi parampi nirayainapi brahmacariyainapi abhisapi, tasmiṃ vatthusmiṃ| aikā pañ‍ñatti| chan‍naṃ āpattisamuṭṭhānānaṃ tīhi samuṭṭhānaihi samuṭṭhāti…pai॰…|

attānaṃ vadhitvā vadhitvā raudantiyā pācittiyaṃ kattha pañ‍ñattanti? sāvatthiyaṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ| kaṃ ārabbhāti? caṇḍakāḷiṃ bhikkhuniṃ ārabbha| kismiṃ vatthusminti? caṇḍakāḷī bhikkhunī attānaṃ vadhitvā vadhitvā raudi, tasmiṃ vatthusmiṃ| aikā pañ‍ñatti| chan‍naṃ āpattisamuṭṭhānānaṃ aikaina samuṭṭhānaina samuṭṭhāti – dhuranikkhaipai…pai॰…|

rattandhakāravaggau dutiyau|

3. nahānavaggau

220. naggāya nahāyantiyā pācittiyaṃ kattha pañ‍ñattanti? sāvatthiyaṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ| kaṃ ārabbhāti? sambahulā bhikkhuniyau ārabbha| kismiṃ vatthusminti? sambahulā bhikkhuniyau naggā nahāyiṃsu, tasmiṃ vatthusmiṃ| aikā pañ‍ñatti| chan‍naṃ āpattisamuṭṭhānānaṃ dvīhi samuṭṭhānaihi samuṭṭhāti – aiḷakalaumakai…pai॰…|

pamāṇātik‍kantaṃ udakasāṭikaṃ kārāpaintiyā pācittiyaṃ kattha pañ‍ñattanti? sāvatthiyaṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ| kaṃ ārabbhāti? chabbaggiyā bhikkhuniyau ārabbha| kismiṃ vatthusminti? chabbaggiyā bhikkhuniyau appamāṇikāyau udakasāṭikāyau dhāraisuṃ, tasmiṃ vatthusmiṃ| aikā  pañ‍ñatti| chan‍naṃ āpattisamuṭṭhānānaṃ chahi samuṭṭhānaihi samuṭṭhāti…pai॰…|

bhikkhuniyā  cīvaraṃ visibbaitvā vā visibbāpaitvā vā naiva sibbaintiyā na sibbāpanāya ussuk‍kaṃ karauntiyā pācittiyaṃ kattha pañ‍ñattanti? sāvatthiyaṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ| kaṃ ārabbhāti? thul‍lanandaṃ bhikkhuniṃ ārabbha| kismiṃ vatthusminti? thul‍lanandā bhikkhunī bhikkhuniyā cīvaraṃ visibbāpaitvā naiva sibbaisi na sibbāpanāya ussuk‍kaṃ akāsi, tasmiṃ vatthusmiṃ| aikā pañ‍ñatti| chan‍naṃ āpattisamuṭṭhānānaṃ aikaina samuṭṭhānaina samuṭṭhāti – dhuranikkhaipai…pai॰…|

pañ‍cāhikaṃ  saṅghāṭicāraṃ atik‍kāmaintiyā pācittiyaṃ kattha pañ‍ñattanti? sāvatthiyaṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ| kaṃ ārabbhāti? sambahulā bhikkhuniyau ārabbha| kismiṃ vatthusminti? sambahulā bhikkhuniyau bhikkhunīnaṃ hatthai cīvaraṃ nikkhipitvā santaruttaraina janapadacārikaṃ pak‍kamiṃsu, tasmiṃ vatthusmiṃ| aikā pañ‍ñatti| chan‍naṃ āpattisamuṭṭhānānaṃ dvīhi samuṭṭhānaihi samuṭṭhāti kathinakai…pai॰…|

cīvarasaṅkamanīyaṃ dhāraintiyā pācittiyaṃ kattha pañ‍ñattanti? sāvatthiyaṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ| kaṃ ārabbhāti? añ‍ñataraṃ bhikkhuniṃ ārabbha| kismiṃ vatthusminti? añ‍ñatarā bhikkhunī añ‍ñatarāya bhikkhuniyā cīvaraṃ anāpucchā pārupi, tasmiṃ vatthusmiṃ| aikā pañ‍ñatti| chan‍naṃ āpattisamuṭṭhānānaṃ dvīhi samuṭṭhānaihi samuṭṭhāti – kathinakai…pai॰…|

gaṇassa  cīvaralābhaṃ antarāyaṃ karauntiyā pācittiyaṃ kattha pañ‍ñattanti? sāvatthiyaṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ| kaṃ ārabbhāti? thul‍lanandaṃ bhikkhuniṃ ārabbha| kismiṃ vatthusminti? thul‍lanandā bhikkhunī gaṇassa cīvaralābhaṃ antarāyaṃ akāsi, tasmiṃ vatthusmiṃ| aikā pañ‍ñatti| chan‍naṃ āpattisamuṭṭhānānaṃ tīhi samuṭṭhānaihi samuṭṭhāti…pai॰…|

dhammikaṃ cīvaravibhaṅgaṃ paṭibāhantiyā pācittiyaṃ kattha pañ‍ñattanti| sāvatthiyaṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ| kaṃ ārabbhāti? thul‍lanandaṃ bhikkhuniṃ ārabbha| kismiṃ vatthusminti? thul‍lanandā bhikkhunī dhammikaṃ  cīvaravibhaṅgaṃ paṭibāhi, tasmiṃ vatthusmiṃ| aikā pañ‍ñatti| chan‍naṃ āpattisamuṭṭhānānaṃ tīhi samuṭṭhānaihi samuṭṭhāti…pai॰…|

agārikassa vā paribbājakassa vā paribbājikāya vā samaṇacīvaraṃ daintiyā pācittiyaṃ kattha pañ‍ñattanti? sāvatthiyaṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ| kaṃ ārabbhāti? thul‍lanandaṃ bhikkhuniṃ ārabbha| kismiṃ vatthusminti? thul‍lanandā bhikkhunī agārikassa samaṇacīvaraṃ adāsi, tasmiṃ vatthusmiṃ| aikā pañ‍ñatti| chan‍naṃ āpattisamuṭṭhānānaṃ chahi samuṭṭhānaihi samuṭṭhāti…pai॰…|

dubbalacīvarapac‍cāsāya cīvarakālasamayaṃ atik‍kāmaintiyā pācittiyaṃ kattha pañ‍ñattanti? sāvatthiyaṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ| kaṃ ārabbhāti? thul‍lanandaṃ bhikkhuniṃ ārabbha| kismiṃ vatthusminti? thul‍lanandā  bhikkhunī dubbalacīvarapac‍cāsāya cīvarakālasamayaṃ atik‍kāmaisi, tasmiṃ vatthusmiṃ| aikā pañ‍ñatti| chan‍naṃ āpattisamuṭṭhānānaṃ tīhi samuṭṭhānaihi samuṭṭhāti…pai॰…|

dhammikaṃ  kathinuddhāraṃ paṭibāhantiyā pācittiyaṃ kattha pañ‍ñattanti? sāvatthiyaṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ| kaṃ ārabbhāti? thul‍lanandaṃ bhikkhuniṃ ārabbha| kismiṃ vatthusminti? thul‍lanandā bhikkhunī dhammikaṃ kathinuddhāraṃ paṭibāhi, tasmiṃ vatthusmiṃ| aikā pañ‍ñatti| chan‍naṃ āpattisamuṭṭhānānaṃ tīhi samuṭṭhānaihi samuṭṭhāti…pai॰…|

nahānavaggau tatiyau|

4. tuvaṭṭavaggau

221. dvin‍naṃ bhikkhunīnaṃ aikamañ‍cai tuvaṭṭaintīnaṃ pācittiyaṃ kattha pañ‍ñattanti? sāvatthiyaṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ| kaṃ ārabbhāti? sambahulā bhikkhuniyau ārabbha| kismiṃ vatthusminti? sambahulā bhikkhuniyau  dvai aikamañ‍cai tuvaṭṭaisuṃ, tasmiṃ vatthusmiṃ| aikā pañ‍ñatti| chan‍naṃ āpattisamuṭṭhānānaṃ dvīhi samuṭṭhānaihi samuṭṭhāti – aiḷakalaumakai…pai॰…|

dvin‍naṃ bhikkhunīnaṃ aikattharaṇapāvuraṇai tuvaṭṭaintīnaṃ pācittiyaṃ kattha pañ‍ñattanti? sāvatthiyaṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ| kaṃ ārabbhāti? sambahulā bhikkhuniyau ārabbha| kismiṃ vatthusminti? sambahulā bhikkhuniyau dvai aikattharaṇapāvuraṇā tuvaṭṭaisuṃ, tasmiṃ vatthusmiṃ| aikā pañ‍ñatti| chan‍naṃ āpattisamuṭṭhānānaṃ dvīhi samuṭṭhānaihi  samuṭṭhāti – aiḷakalaumakai…pai॰…|

bhikkhuniyā sañ‍cic‍ca aphāsuṃ karauntiyā pācittiyaṃ kattha pañ‍ñattanti? sāvatthiyaṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ| kaṃ ārabbhāti? thul‍lanandaṃ bhikkhuniṃ ārabbha| kismiṃ vatthusminti? thul‍lanandā bhikkhunī bhikkhuniyā sañ‍cic‍ca aphāsuṃ akāsi, tasmiṃ vatthusmiṃ| aikā pañ‍ñatti| chan‍naṃ āpattisamuṭṭhānānaṃ tīhi samuṭṭhānaihi samuṭṭhāti…pai॰…|

dukkhitaṃ sahajīviniṃ naiva upaṭṭhaintiyā na upaṭṭhāpanāya ussuk‍kaṃ karauntiyā pācittiyaṃ kattha pañ‍ñattanti? sāvatthiyaṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ| kaṃ ārabbhāti? thul‍lanandaṃ bhikkhuniṃ ārabbha| kismiṃ vatthusminti? thul‍lanandā bhikkhunī dukkhitaṃ sahajīviniṃ naiva upaṭṭhaisi na upaṭṭhāpanāya ussuk‍kaṃ akāsi, tasmiṃ vatthusmiṃ| aikā pañ‍ñatti| chan‍naṃ āpattisamuṭṭhānānaṃ aikaina samuṭṭhānaina samuṭṭhāti – dhuranikkhaipai…pai॰…|

bhikkhuniyā  upassayaṃ datvā kupitāya anattamanāya nik‍kaḍḍhantiyā pācittiyaṃ kattha pañ‍ñattanti? sāvatthiyaṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ| kaṃ ārabbhāti? thul‍lanandaṃ bhikkhuniṃ ārabbha| kismiṃ vatthusminti? thul‍lanandā bhikkhunī bhikkhuniyā upassayaṃ datvā kupitā anattamanā nik‍kaḍḍhi, tasmiṃ vatthusmiṃ| aikā pañ‍ñatti| chan‍naṃ āpattisamuṭṭhānānaṃ tīhi samuṭṭhānaihi samuṭṭhāti…pai॰…|

saṃsaṭṭhāya bhikkhuniyā yāvatatiyaṃ samanubhāsanāya na paṭinissaj‍jantiyā  pācittiyaṃ kattha pañ‍ñattanti? sāvatthiyaṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ| kaṃ ārabbhāti? caṇḍakāḷiṃ bhikkhuniṃ ārabbha| kismiṃ vatthusminti? caṇḍakāḷī bhikkhunī saṃsaṭṭhā vihari, tasmiṃ vatthusmiṃ| aikā pañ‍ñatti| chan‍naṃ āpattisamuṭṭhānānaṃ aikaina samuṭṭhānaina samuṭṭhāti – dhuranikkhaipai…pai॰…|

antauraṭṭhai sāsaṅkasammatai sappaṭibhayai asatthikāya cārikaṃ carantiyā pācittiyaṃ kattha pañ‍ñattanti? sāvatthiyaṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ| kaṃ ārabbhāti? sambahulā  bhikkhuniyau ārabbha| kismiṃ vatthusminti? sambahulā bhikkhuniyau antauraṭṭhai sāsaṅkasammatai sappaṭibhayai asatthikāyau cārikaṃ cariṃsu, tasmiṃ vatthusmiṃ| aikā pañ‍ñatti| chan‍naṃ āpattisamuṭṭhānānaṃ dvīhi samuṭṭhānaihi samuṭṭhāti – aiḷakalaumakai…pai॰…|

tirauraṭṭhai  sāsaṅkasammatai sappaṭibhayai asatthikāya cārikaṃ carantiyā pācittiyaṃ kattha pañ‍ñattanti? sāvatthiyaṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ| kaṃ ārabbhāti? sambahulā bhikkhuniyau ārabbha| kismiṃ vatthusminti? sambahulā bhikkhuniyau tirauraṭṭhai sāsaṅkasammatai sappaṭibhayai asatthikāyau cārikaṃ cariṃsu, tasmiṃ vatthusmiṃ| aikā pañ‍ñatti| chan‍naṃ āpattisamuṭṭhānānaṃ dvīhi samuṭṭhānaihi samuṭṭhāti – aiḷakalaumakai…pai॰…|

antauvassaṃ cārikaṃ carantiyā pācittiyaṃ kattha pañ‍ñattanti? rājagahai  pañ‍ñattaṃ| kaṃ ārabbhāti? sambahulā bhikkhuniyau ārabbha| kismiṃ vatthusminti? sambahulā bhikkhuniyau antauvassaṃ cārikaṃ cariṃsu, tasmiṃ vatthusmiṃ| aikā pañ‍ñatti| chan‍naṃ āpattisamuṭṭhānānaṃ dvīhi samuṭṭhānaihi samuṭṭhāti – aiḷakalaumakai…pai॰…|

vassaṃvuṭṭhāya vassaṃvutthāya (sī॰ syā॰) bhikkhuniyā cārikaṃ na pak‍kamantiyā pācittiyaṃ kattha pañ‍ñattanti? rājagahai pañ‍ñattaṃ| kaṃ ārabbhāti? sambahulā bhikkhuniyau ārabbha| kismiṃ vatthusminti? sambahulā bhikkhuniyau vassaṃvuṭṭhā cārikaṃ na pak‍kamiṃsu, tasmiṃ vatthusmiṃ| aikā pañ‍ñatti| chan‍naṃ āpattisamuṭṭhānānaṃ aikaina samuṭṭhānaina samuṭṭhāti – paṭhamapārājikai…pai॰…|

tuvaṭṭavaggau catutthau|

5. cittāgāravaggau

222. rājāgāraṃ vā cittāgāraṃ vā ārāmaṃ vā uyyānaṃ vā paukkharaṇiṃ vā dassanāya gacchantiyā pācittiyaṃ kattha pañ‍ñattanti? sāvatthiyaṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ| kaṃ ārabbhāti? chabbaggiyā bhikkhuniyau ārabbha| kismiṃ vatthusminti? chabbaggiyā bhikkhuniyau rājāgārampi cittāgārampi dassanāya agamaṃsu, tasmiṃ vatthusmiṃ| aikā pañ‍ñatti| chan‍naṃ āpattisamuṭṭhānānaṃ dvīhi samuṭṭhānaihi samuṭṭhāti – aiḷakalaumakai…pai॰…|

āsandiṃ vā pal‍laṅkaṃ vā paribhuñ‍jantiyā pācittiyaṃ kattha pañ‍ñattanti? sāvatthiyaṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ| kaṃ ārabbhāti? sambahulā  bhikkhuniyau ārabbha| kismiṃ vatthusminti? sambahulā bhikkhuniyau āsandimpi pal‍laṅkampi paribhuñ‍jiṃsu, tasmiṃ vatthusmiṃ| aikā pañ‍ñatti| chan‍naṃ āpattisamuṭṭhānānaṃ dvīhi samuṭṭhānaihi samuṭṭhāti – aiḷakalaumakai…pai॰…|

suttaṃ  kantantiyā pācittiyaṃ kattha pañ‍ñattanti? sāvatthiyaṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ| kaṃ ārabbhāti? chabbaggiyā bhikkhuniyau ārabbha| kismiṃ vatthusminti? chabbaggiyā bhikkhuniyau suttaṃ kantiṃsu, tasmiṃ vatthusmiṃ| aikā pañ‍ñatti| chan‍naṃ āpattisamuṭṭhānānaṃ dvīhi samuṭṭhānaihi samuṭṭhāti – aiḷakalaumakai…pai॰…|

gihivaiyyāvac‍caṃ karauntiyā pācittiyaṃ kattha pañ‍ñattanti? sāvatthiyaṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ| kaṃ ārabbhāti? sambahulā bhikkhuniyau ārabbha| kismiṃ vatthusminti? sambahulā bhikkhuniyau gihivaiyyāvac‍caṃ akaṃsu, tasmiṃ vatthusmiṃ| aikā pañ‍ñatti| chan‍naṃ āpattisamuṭṭhānānaṃ dvīhi samuṭṭhānaihi samuṭṭhāti – aiḷakalaumakai…pai॰…|

bhikkhuniyā ‘‘aihāyyai imaṃ adhikaraṇaṃ vūpasamaihī’’ti vuc‍camānāya ‘‘sādhū’’ti paṭissuṇitvā naiva vūpasamaintiyā na vūpasamāya ussuk‍kaṃ karauntiyā  pācittiyaṃ kattha pañ‍ñattanti? sāvatthiyaṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ| kaṃ ārabbhāti? thul‍lanandaṃ bhikkhuniṃ ārabbha| kismiṃ vatthusminti? thul‍lanandā  bhikkhunī bhikkhuniyā – ‘‘aihāyyai, imaṃ adhikaraṇaṃ vūpasamaihī’’ti vuc‍camānā ‘‘sādhū’’ti paṭissuṇitvā naiva vūpasamaisi  na vūpasamāya ussuk‍kaṃ akāsi, tasmiṃ vatthusmiṃ| aikā pañ‍ñatti| chan‍naṃ āpattisamuṭṭhānānaṃ aikaina samuṭṭhānaina samuṭṭhāti – dhuranikkhaipai…pai॰…|

agārikassa vā paribbājakassa vā paribbājikāya vā sahatthā khādanīyaṃ vā bhaujanīyaṃ vā daintiyā pācittiyaṃ kattha pañ‍ñattanti? sāvatthiyaṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ| kaṃ ārabbhāti? thul‍lanandaṃ bhikkhuniṃ ārabbha| kismiṃ vatthusminti? thul‍lanandā bhikkhunī agārikassa sahatthā khādanīyampi bhaujanīyampi adāsi, tasmiṃ vatthusmiṃ| aikā pañ‍ñatti| chan‍naṃ āpattisamuṭṭhānānaṃ dvīhi samuṭṭhānaihi samuṭṭhāti – aiḷakalaumakai…pai॰…|

āvasathacīvaraṃ anissaj‍jitvā paribhuñ‍jantiyā pācittiyaṃ kattha pañ‍ñattanti? sāvatthiyaṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ| kaṃ ārabbhāti? thul‍lanandaṃ bhikkhuniṃ ārabbha| kismiṃ vatthusminti? thul‍lanandā bhikkhunī āvasathacīvaraṃ anissaj‍jitvā paribhuñ‍ji, tasmiṃ vatthusmiṃ| aikā pañ‍ñatti| chan‍naṃ āpattisamuṭṭhānānaṃ dvīhi samuṭṭhānaihi samuṭṭhāti – kathinakai…pai॰…|

āvasathaṃ anissaj‍jitvā cārikaṃ pak‍kamantiyā pācittiyaṃ kattha pañ‍ñattanti? sāvatthiyaṃ  pañ‍ñattaṃ| kaṃ ārabbhāti? thul‍lanandaṃ bhikkhuniṃ ārabbha| kismiṃ vatthusminti? thul‍lanandā bhikkhunī āvasathaṃ anissaj‍jitvā cārikaṃ pak‍kāmi, tasmiṃ vatthusmiṃ| aikā pañ‍ñatti| chan‍naṃ āpattisamuṭṭhānānaṃ dvīhi samuṭṭhānaihi samuṭṭhāti  – kathinakai…pai॰…|

tiracchānavij‍jaṃ pariyāpuṇantiyā pācittiyaṃ kattha pañ‍ñattanti? sāvatthiyaṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ| kaṃ ārabbhāti? chabbaggiyā bhikkhuniyau ārabbha| kismiṃ vatthusminti? chabbaggiyā bhikkhuniyau tiracchānavij‍jaṃ pariyāpuṇiṃsu, tasmiṃ vatthusmiṃ| aikā pañ‍ñatti| chan‍naṃ āpattisamuṭṭhānānaṃ dvīhi samuṭṭhānaihi samuṭṭhāti – padasaudhammai…pai॰…|

tiracchānavij‍jaṃ vācaintiyā pācittiyaṃ kattha pañ‍ñattanti? sāvatthiyaṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ| kaṃ ārabbhāti? chabbaggiyā bhikkhuniyau ārabbha| kismiṃ vatthusminti| chabbaggiyā bhikkhuniyau  tiracchānavij‍jaṃ vācaisuṃ, tasmiṃ vatthusmiṃ| aikā pañ‍ñatti| chan‍naṃ āpattisamuṭṭhānānaṃ dvīhi samuṭṭhānaihi samuṭṭhāti – padasaudhammai…pai॰…|

cittāgāravaggau pañ‍camau|

6. ārāmavaggau

223. jānaṃ sabhikkhukaṃ ārāmaṃ anāpucchā pavisantiyā pācittiyaṃ kattha pañ‍ñattanti? sāvatthiyaṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ| kaṃ ārabbhāti? sambahulā bhikkhuniyau ārabbha| kismiṃ vatthusminti? sambahulā bhikkhuniyau ārāmaṃ anāpucchā pavisiṃsu, tasmiṃ vatthusmiṃ| aikā pañ‍ñatti, dvai anupañ‍ñattiyau| chan‍naṃ āpattisamuṭṭhānānaṃ aikaina samuṭṭhānaina samuṭṭhāti – dhuranikkhaipai…pai॰…|

bhikkhuṃ ak‍kausantiyā paribhāsantiyā pācittiyaṃ kattha pañ‍ñattanti? vaisāliyaṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ| kaṃ ārabbhāti? chabbaggiyā bhikkhuniyau  ārabbha| kismiṃ vatthusminti? chabbaggiyā bhikkhuniyau āyasmantaṃ upāliṃ ak‍kausiṃsu, tasmiṃ vatthusmiṃ| aikā pañ‍ñatti| chan‍naṃ āpattisamuṭṭhānānaṃ tīhi samuṭṭhānaihi samuṭṭhāti…pai॰…|

caṇḍīkatāya gaṇaṃ paribhāsantiyā pācittiyaṃ kattha pañ‍ñattanti? sāvatthiyaṃ  pañ‍ñattaṃ| kaṃ ārabbhāti? thul‍lanandaṃ bhikkhuniṃ ārabbha| kismiṃ vatthusminti? thul‍lanandā bhikkhunī caṇḍīkatāya  gaṇaṃ paribhāsi, tasmiṃ vatthusmiṃ| aikā pañ‍ñatti| chan‍naṃ āpattisamuṭṭhānānaṃ tīhi samuṭṭhānaihi samuṭṭhāti…pai॰…|

nimantitāya vā pavāritāya vā khādanīyaṃ vā bhaujanīyaṃ vā añ‍ñatra bhuñ‍jantiyā pācittiyaṃ kattha pañ‍ñattanti? sāvatthiyaṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ| kaṃ ārabbhāti? sambahulā bhikkhuniyau ārabbha| kismiṃ vatthusminti? sambahulā bhikkhuniyau bhuttāviniyau pavāritā añ‍ñatra bhuñ‍jiṃsu, tasmiṃ vatthusmiṃ| aikā pañ‍ñatti| chan‍naṃ āpattisamuṭṭhānānaṃ catūhi samuṭṭhānaihi samuṭṭhāti…pai॰…|

kulaṃ maccharāyantiyā pācittiyaṃ kattha pañ‍ñattanti? sāvatthiyaṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ| kaṃ ārabbhāti? añ‍ñataraṃ bhikkhuniṃ ārabbha| kismiṃ vatthusminti? añ‍ñatarā bhikkhunī kulaṃ maccharāyi, tasmiṃ vatthusmiṃ| aikā pañ‍ñatti| chan‍naṃ āpattisamuṭṭhānānaṃ tīhi samuṭṭhānaihi samuṭṭhāti…pai॰…|

abhikkhukai  āvāsai vassaṃ vasantiyā pācittiyaṃ kattha pañ‍ñattanti? sāvatthiyaṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ| kaṃ ārabbhāti? sambahulā bhikkhuniyau ārabbha| kismiṃ vatthusminti? sambahulā bhikkhuniyau abhikkhukai āvāsai vassaṃ vasiṃsu, tasmiṃ vatthusmiṃ| aikā pañ‍ñatti| chan‍naṃ āpattisamuṭṭhānānaṃ dvīhi samuṭṭhānaihi samuṭṭhāti – aiḷakalaumakai…pai॰…|

vassaṃvuṭṭhāya bhikkhuniyā ubhatausaṅghai tīhi ṭhānaihi na pavāraintiyā pācittiyaṃ kattha pañ‍ñattanti? sāvatthiyaṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ| kaṃ ārabbhāti? sambahulā bhikkhuniyau ārabbha| kismiṃ vatthusminti? sambahulā bhikkhuniyau vassaṃvuṭṭhā bhikkhusaṅghaṃ bhikkhunisaṃghai (ka॰) na pavāraisuṃ, tasmiṃ vatthusmiṃ| aikā pañ‍ñatti| chan‍naṃ āpattisamuṭṭhānānaṃ aikaina samuṭṭhānaina samuṭṭhāti – dhuranikkhaipai…pai॰…|

auvādāya vā saṃvāsāya vā na gacchantiyā pācittiyaṃ kattha pañ‍ñattanti? sak‍kaisu pañ‍ñattaṃ| kaṃ ārabbhāti? chabbaggiyā bhikkhuniyau ārabbha| kismiṃ vatthusminti? chabbaggiyā bhikkhuniyau auvādaṃ na gacchiṃsu, tasmiṃ vatthusmiṃ| aikā pañ‍ñatti| chan‍naṃ āpattisamuṭṭhānānaṃ aikaina samuṭṭhānaina samuṭṭhāti – paṭhamapārājikai…pai॰…|

upausathampi na pucchantiyā auvādampi na yācantiyā pācittiyaṃ kattha pañ‍ñattanti? sāvatthiyaṃ  pañ‍ñattaṃ| kaṃ ārabbhāti? sambahulā bhikkhuniyau ārabbha| kismiṃ vatthusminti? sambahulā bhikkhuniyau upausathampi na pucchiṃsu auvādampi na yāciṃsu, tasmiṃ  vatthusmiṃ| aikā pañ‍ñatti| chan‍naṃ āpattisamuṭṭhānānaṃ aikaina samuṭṭhānaina samuṭṭhāti – dhuranikkhaipai…pai॰…|

pasākhai jātaṃ gaṇḍaṃ vā rudhitaṃ ruhitaṃ (sī॰ syā॰) vā anapalaukaitvā saṅghaṃ vā gaṇaṃ vā purisaina saddhiṃ aikainaikāya bhaidāpaintiyā pācittiyaṃ kattha pañ‍ñattanti? sāvatthiyaṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ| kaṃ ārabbhāti? añ‍ñataraṃ bhikkhuniṃ ārabbha| kismiṃ vatthusminti? añ‍ñatarā bhikkhunī pasākhai jātaṃ gaṇḍaṃ purisaina saddhiṃ aikainaikā bhaidāpaisi, tasmiṃ vatthusmiṃ| aikā pañ‍ñatti| chan‍naṃ āpattisamuṭṭhānānaṃ dvīhi samuṭṭhānaihi samuṭṭhāti – kathinakai…pai॰…|

ārāmavaggau chaṭṭhau|

7. gabbhinīvaggau

224. gabbhiniṃ  vuṭṭhāpaintiyā pācittiyaṃ kattha pañ‍ñattanti? sāvatthiyaṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ| kaṃ ārabbhāti? sambahulā bhikkhuniyau ārabbha| kismiṃ vatthusminti? sambahulā bhikkhuniyau gabbhiniṃ vuṭṭhāpaisuṃ, tasmiṃ vatthusmiṃ| aikā pañ‍ñatti| chan‍naṃ āpattisamuṭṭhānānaṃ tīhi samuṭṭhānaihi samuṭṭhāti…pai॰…|

pāyantiṃ vuṭṭhāpaintiyā pācittiyaṃ kattha pañ‍ñattanti? sāvatthiyaṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ| kaṃ ārabbhāti? sambahulā bhikkhuniyau ārabbha| kismiṃ vatthusminti? sambahulā bhikkhuniyau pāyantiṃ vuṭṭhāpaisuṃ, tasmiṃ vatthusmiṃ| aikā pañ‍ñatti| chan‍naṃ āpattisamuṭṭhānānaṃ tīhi samuṭṭhānaihi samuṭṭhāti…pai॰…|

dvai  vassāni chasu dhammaisu asikkhitasikkhaṃ sikkhamānaṃ vuṭṭhāpaintiyā pācittiyaṃ  kattha pañ‍ñattanti? sāvatthiyaṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ| kaṃ ārabbhāti? sambahulā bhikkhuniyau ārabbha| kismiṃ vatthusminti? sambahulā bhikkhuniyau dvai vassāni chasu dhammaisu asikkhitasikkhaṃ sikkhamānaṃ vuṭṭhāpaisuṃ, tasmiṃ vatthusmiṃ| aikā pañ‍ñatti| chan‍naṃ āpattisamuṭṭhānānaṃ tīhi samuṭṭhānaihi samuṭṭhāti…pai॰…|

dvai vassāni chasu dhammaisu sikkhitasikkhaṃ sikkhamānaṃ saṅghaina asammataṃ vuṭṭhāpaintiyā pācittiyaṃ kattha pañ‍ñattanti? sāvatthiyaṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ| kaṃ ārabbhāti? sambahulā bhikkhuniyau ārabbha| kismiṃ vatthusminti? sambahulā bhikkhuniyau dvai vassāni chasu dhammaisu sikkhitasikkhaṃ sikkhamānaṃ saṅghaina asammataṃ vuṭṭhāpaisuṃ, tasmiṃ vatthusmiṃ| aikā pañ‍ñatti| chan‍naṃ āpattisamuṭṭhānānaṃ tīhi samuṭṭhānaihi samuṭṭhāti…pai॰…|

ūnadvādasavassaṃ gihigataṃ vuṭṭhāpaintiyā pācittiyaṃ kattha pañ‍ñattanti? sāvatthiyaṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ| kaṃ ārabbhāti? sambahulā bhikkhuniyau ārabbha| kismiṃ vatthusminti? sambahulā bhikkhuniyau ūnadvādasavassaṃ gihigataṃ vuṭṭhāpaisuṃ, tasmiṃ vatthusmiṃ| aikā pañ‍ñatti| chan‍naṃ āpattisamuṭṭhānānaṃ tīhi samuṭṭhānaihi samuṭṭhāti…pai॰…|

paripuṇṇadvādasavassaṃ gihigataṃ dvai vassāni chasu dhammaisu asikkhitasikkhaṃ  vuṭṭhāpaintiyā pācittiyaṃ kattha pañ‍ñattanti? sāvatthiyaṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ| kaṃ ārabbhāti? sambahulā bhikkhuniyau ārabbha| kismiṃ vatthusminti? sambahulā bhikkhuniyau paripuṇṇadvādasavassaṃ gihigataṃ dvai vassāni chasu dhammaisu asikkhitasikkhaṃ vuṭṭhāpaisuṃ, tasmiṃ vatthusmiṃ| aikā pañ‍ñatti| chan‍naṃ āpattisamuṭṭhānānaṃ tīhi samuṭṭhānaihi samuṭṭhāti…pai॰…|

paripuṇṇadvādasavassaṃ gihigataṃ dvai vassāni chasu dhammaisu sikkhitasikkhaṃ saṅghaina asammataṃ vuṭṭhāpaintiyā pācittiyaṃ kattha pañ‍ñattanti? sāvatthiyaṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ| kaṃ ārabbhāti? sambahulā bhikkhuniyau ārabbha| kismiṃ vatthusminti? sambahulā bhikkhuniyau paripuṇṇadvādasavassaṃ gihigataṃ dvai vassāni chasu dhammaisu sikkhitasikkhaṃ saṅghaina asammataṃ vuṭṭhāpaisuṃ, tasmiṃ vatthusmiṃ| aikā pañ‍ñatti| chan‍naṃ āpattisamuṭṭhānānaṃ tīhi samuṭṭhānaihi samuṭṭhāti…pai॰…|

sahajīviniṃ vuṭṭhāpaitvā dvai vassāni naiva anuggaṇhantiyā na anuggaṇhāpaintiyā pācittiyaṃ kattha pañ‍ñattanti? sāvatthiyaṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ| kaṃ ārabbhāti? thul‍lanandaṃ bhikkhuniṃ ārabbha| kismiṃ vatthusminti? thul‍lanandā bhikkhunī sahajīviniṃ vuṭṭhāpaitvā dvai vassāni naiva anuggahaisi na anuggaṇhāpaisi, tasmiṃ vatthusmiṃ| aikā pañ‍ñatti| chan‍naṃ āpattisamuṭṭhānānaṃ aikaina samuṭṭhānaina samuṭṭhāti – dhuranikkhaipai…pai॰…|

vuṭṭhāpitaṃ  pavattiniṃ dvai vassāni nānubandhantiyā pācittiyaṃ kattha pañ‍ñattanti? sāvatthiyaṃ  pañ‍ñattaṃ| kaṃ ārabbhāti? sambahulā bhikkhuniyau ārabbha| kismiṃ vatthusminti? sambahulā bhikkhuniyau vuṭṭhāpitaṃ pavattiniṃ dvai vassāni nānubandhiṃsu, tasmiṃ vatthusmiṃ| aikā pañ‍ñatti| chan‍naṃ āpattisamuṭṭhānānaṃ aikaina samuṭṭhānaina samuṭṭhāti – paṭhamapārājikai…pai॰…|

sahajīviniṃ vuṭṭhāpaitvā naiva vūpakāsaintiyā na vūpakāsāpaintiyā pācittiyaṃ kattha pañ‍ñattanti? sāvatthiyaṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ| kaṃ ārabbhāti? thul‍lanandaṃ bhikkhuniṃ ārabbha| kismiṃ vatthusminti? thul‍lanandā bhikkhunī sahajīviniṃ vuṭṭhāpaitvā naiva vūpakāsaisi na vūpakāsāpaisi, tasmiṃ vatthusmiṃ| aikā pañ‍ñatti| chan‍naṃ āpattisamuṭṭhānānaṃ aikaina samuṭṭhānaina samuṭṭhāti – dhuranikkhaipai…pai॰…|

gabbhinivaggau sattamau|

8. kumārībhūtavaggau

225. ūnavīsativassaṃ kumāribhūtaṃ vuṭṭhāpaintiyā pācittiyaṃ kattha pañ‍ñattanti? sāvatthiyaṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ| kaṃ ārabbhāti? sambahulā bhikkhuniyau ārabbha| kismiṃ vatthusminti? sambahulā bhikkhuniyau ūnavīsativassaṃ kumāribhūtaṃ vuṭṭhāpaisuṃ, tasmiṃ vatthusmiṃ| aikā pañ‍ñatti| chan‍naṃ āpattisamuṭṭhānānaṃ tīhi samuṭṭhānaihi samuṭṭhāti…pai॰…|

paripuṇṇavīsativassaṃ kumāribhūtaṃ dvai vassāni chasu dhammaisu asikkhitasikkhaṃ vuṭṭhāpaintiyā pācittiyaṃ kattha pañ‍ñattanti? sāvatthiyaṃ  pañ‍ñattaṃ| kaṃ ārabbhāti? sambahulā bhikkhuniyau ārabbha| kismiṃ vatthusminti? sambahulā bhikkhuniyau paripuṇṇavīsativassaṃ kumāribhūtaṃ dvai vassāni chasu dhammaisu asikkhitasikkhaṃ vuṭṭhāpaisuṃ, tasmiṃ vatthusmiṃ| aikā pañ‍ñatti| chan‍naṃ āpattisamuṭṭhānānaṃ tīhi samuṭṭhānaihi samuṭṭhāti…pai॰…|

paripuṇṇavīsativassaṃ  kumāribhūtaṃ dvai vassāni chasu dhammaisu sikkhitasikkhaṃ saṅghaina asammataṃ vuṭṭhāpaintiyā pācittiyaṃ kattha pañ‍ñattanti? sāvatthiyaṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ| kaṃ ārabbhāti? sambahulā bhikkhuniyau ārabbha| kismiṃ vatthusminti? sambahulā bhikkhuniyau paripuṇṇavīsativassaṃ kumāribhūtaṃ dvai vassāni chasu dhammaisu sikkhitasikkhaṃ saṅghaina asammataṃ vuṭṭhāpaisuṃ, tasmiṃ vatthusmiṃ| aikā pañ‍ñatti| chan‍naṃ āpattisamuṭṭhānānaṃ tīhi samuṭṭhānaihi samuṭṭhāti…pai॰…|

ūnadvādasavassāya  vuṭṭhāpaintiyā pācittiyaṃ kattha pañ‍ñattanti? sāvatthiyaṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ| kaṃ ārabbhāti? sambahulā bhikkhuniyau ārabbha| kismiṃ vatthusminti? sambahulā bhikkhuniyau ūnadvādasavassā vuṭṭhāpaisuṃ, tasmiṃ vatthusmiṃ| aikā pañ‍ñatti| chan‍naṃ āpattisamuṭṭhānānaṃ tīhi samuṭṭhānaihi samuṭṭhāti…pai॰…|

paripuṇṇadvādasavassāya saṅghaina asammatāya vuṭṭhāpaintiyā pācittiyaṃ kattha pañ‍ñattanti? sāvatthiyaṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ| kaṃ ārabbhāti? sambahulā bhikkhuniyau ārabbha| kismiṃ vatthusminti? sambahulā  bhikkhuniyau paripuṇṇadvādasavassā saṅghaina asammatā vuṭṭhāpaisuṃ, tasmiṃ vatthusmiṃ| aikā pañ‍ñatti| chan‍naṃ āpattisamuṭṭhānānaṃ tīhi samuṭṭhānaihi samuṭṭhāti – dutiyapārājikai…pai॰…|

‘‘alaṃ tāva tai, ayyai, vuṭṭhāpitainā’’ti vuc‍camānāya ‘‘sādhū’’ti paṭissuṇitvā pacchā khīyanadhammaṃ āpaj‍jantiyā pācittiyaṃ kattha pañ‍ñattanti? sāvatthiyaṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ| kaṃ ārabbhāti? caṇḍakāḷiṃ bhikkhuniṃ ārabbha| kismiṃ vatthusminti? caṇḍakāḷī bhikkhunī ‘‘alaṃ tāva tai, ayyai, vuṭṭhāpitainā’’ti vuc‍camānā  ‘‘sādhū’’ti paṭissuṇitvā pacchā khīyanadhammaṃ āpaj‍ji, tasmiṃ vatthusmiṃ| aikā pañ‍ñatti| chan‍naṃ āpattisamuṭṭhānānaṃ tīhi samuṭṭhānaihi samuṭṭhāti…pai॰…|

sikkhamānaṃ – ‘‘sacai mai tvaṃ, ayyai, cīvaraṃ dassasi, aivāhaṃ taṃ vuṭṭhāpaissāmī’’ti vatvā naiva vuṭṭhāpaintiyā na vuṭṭhāpanāya ussuk‍kaṃ karauntiyā pācittiyaṃ kattha pañ‍ñattanti? sāvatthiyaṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ| kaṃ ārabbhāti? thul‍lanandaṃ bhikkhuniṃ ārabbha| kismiṃ vatthusminti? thul‍lanandā bhikkhunī sikkhamānaṃ – ‘‘sacai mai tvaṃ, ayyai, cīvaraṃ dassasi, aivāhaṃ taṃ vuṭṭhāpaissāmī’’ti vatvā naiva vuṭṭhāpaisi na vuṭṭhāpanāya ussuk‍kaṃ akāsi, tasmiṃ vatthusmiṃ| aikā pañ‍ñatti| chan‍naṃ āpattisamuṭṭhānānaṃ aikaina samuṭṭhānaina samuṭṭhāti – dhuranikkhaipai…pai॰…|

sikkhamānaṃ – ‘‘sacai maṃ tvaṃ, ayyai, dvai vassāni anubandhissasi, aivāhaṃ taṃ vuṭṭhāpaissāmī’’ti vatvā naiva vuṭṭhāpaintiyā na vuṭṭhāpanāya ussuk‍kaṃ  karauntiyā pācittiyaṃ kattha pañ‍ñattanti? sāvatthiyaṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ| kaṃ ārabbhāti? thul‍lanandaṃ bhikkhuniṃ ārabbha| kismiṃ vatthusminti? thul‍lanandā bhikkhunī sikkhamānaṃ – ‘‘sacai maṃ tvaṃ, ayyai, dvai vassāni anubandhissasi, aivāhaṃ taṃ vuṭṭhāpaissāmī’’ti vatvā naiva vuṭṭhāpaisi na vuṭṭhāpanāya ussuk‍kaṃ akāsi, tasmiṃ vatthusmiṃ| aikā pañ‍ñatti| chan‍naṃ āpattisamuṭṭhānānaṃ aikaina samuṭṭhānaina samuṭṭhāti – dhuranikkhaipai…pai॰…|

purisasaṃsaṭṭhaṃ kumārakasaṃsaṭṭhaṃ caṇḍiṃ saukāvāsaṃ sikkhamānaṃ vuṭṭhāpaintiyā pācittiyaṃ kattha pañ‍ñattanti? sāvatthiyaṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ| kaṃ ārabbhāti? thul‍lanandaṃ bhikkhuniṃ ārabbha| kismiṃ vatthusminti? thul‍lanandā bhikkhunī purisasaṃsaṭṭhaṃ kumārakasaṃsaṭṭhaṃ caṇḍiṃ saukāvāsaṃ sikkhamānaṃ vuṭṭhāpaisi, tasmiṃ vatthusmiṃ| aikā pañ‍ñatti| chan‍naṃ āpattisamuṭṭhānānaṃ tīhi samuṭṭhānaihi samuṭṭhāti…pai॰…|

mātāpitūhi vā sāmikaina vā ananuñ‍ñātaṃ sikkhamānaṃ vuṭṭhāpaintiyā pācittiyaṃ kattha pañ‍ñattanti? sāvatthiyaṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ| kaṃ ārabbhāti? thul‍lanandaṃ bhikkhuniṃ ārabbha| kismiṃ vatthusminti? thul‍lanandā bhikkhunī mātāpitūhipi sāmikaināpi ananuñ‍ñātaṃ sikkhamānaṃ vuṭṭhāpaisi, tasmiṃ vatthusmiṃ| aikā pañ‍ñatti| chan‍naṃ āpattisamuṭṭhānānaṃ catūhi samuṭṭhānaihi samuṭṭhāti – siyā vācatau samuṭṭhāti, na kāyatau na cittatau; siyā kāyatau ca vācatau  ca samuṭṭhāti, na cittatau; siyā vācatau ca cittatau ca samuṭṭhāti, na  kāyatau; siyā kāyatau ca vācatau ca cittatau ca samuṭṭhāti…pai॰…|

pārivāsikachandadānaina sikkhamānaṃ vuṭṭhāpaintiyā pācittiyaṃ kattha pañ‍ñattanti? rājagahai pañ‍ñattaṃ| kaṃ ārabbhāti? thul‍lanandaṃ bhikkhuniṃ ārabbha| kismiṃ vatthusminti? thul‍lanandā bhikkhunī pārivāsikachandadānaina sikkhamānaṃ vuṭṭhāpaisi, tasmiṃ vatthusmiṃ| aikā pañ‍ñatti| chan‍naṃ āpattisamuṭṭhānānaṃ tīhi samuṭṭhānaihi samuṭṭhāti…pai॰…|

anuvassaṃ vuṭṭhāpaintiyā pācittiyaṃ kattha pañ‍ñattanti? sāvatthiyaṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ| kaṃ ārabbhāti? sambahulā bhikkhuniyau ārabbha| kismiṃ vatthusminti? sambahulā bhikkhuniyau anuvassaṃ vuṭṭhāpaisuṃ, tasmiṃ vatthusmiṃ| aikā pañ‍ñatti| chan‍naṃ āpattisamuṭṭhānānaṃ tīhi samuṭṭhānaihi samuṭṭhāti…pai॰…|

aikaṃ vassaṃ dvai vuṭṭhāpaintiyā pācittiyaṃ kattha pañ‍ñattanti? sāvatthiyaṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ| kaṃ ārabbhāti? sambahulā bhikkhuniyau ārabbha| kismiṃ vatthusminti? sambahulā bhikkhuniyau aikaṃ  vassaṃ dvai vuṭṭhāpaisuṃ, tasmiṃ vatthusmiṃ| aikā pañ‍ñatti| chan‍naṃ āpattisamuṭṭhānānaṃ tīhi samuṭṭhānaihi samuṭṭhāti…pai॰…|

kumārībhūtavaggau aṭṭhamau|

9. chattupāhanavaggau

226. chattupāhanaṃ dhāraintiyā pācittiyaṃ kattha pañ‍ñattanti? sāvatthiyaṃ  pañ‍ñattaṃ| kaṃ ārabbhāti? chabbaggiyā bhikkhuniyau ārabbha| kismiṃ vatthusminti? chabbaggiyā bhikkhuniyau chattupāhanaṃ dhāraisuṃ, tasmiṃ vatthusmiṃ| aikā pañ‍ñatti, aikā anupañ‍ñatti| chan‍naṃ āpattisamuṭṭhānānaṃ dvīhi samuṭṭhānaihi samuṭṭhāti – aiḷakalaumakai…pai॰…|

yānaina yāyantiyā pācittiyaṃ kattha pañ‍ñattanti? sāvatthiyaṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ| kaṃ ārabbhāti? chabbaggiyā bhikkhuniyau ārabbha| kismiṃ vatthusminti? chabbaggiyā bhikkhuniyau yānaina yāyiṃsu, tasmiṃ vatthusmiṃ| aikā pañ‍ñatti, aikā anupañ‍ñatti| chan‍naṃ āpattisamuṭṭhānānaṃ dvīhi samuṭṭhānaihi samuṭṭhāti – aiḷakalaumakai…pai॰…|

saṅghāṇiṃ  dhāraintiyā pācittiyaṃ kattha pañ‍ñattanti? sāvatthiyaṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ| kaṃ ārabbhāti? añ‍ñataraṃ bhikkhuniṃ ārabbha| kismiṃ vatthusminti? añ‍ñatarā bhikkhunī saṅghāṇiṃ dhāraisi, tasmiṃ vatthusmiṃ| aikā pañ‍ñatti| chan‍naṃ āpattisamuṭṭhānānaṃ dvīhi samuṭṭhānaihi samuṭṭhāti – aiḷakalaumakai…pai॰…|

itthālaṅkāraṃ dhāraintiyā pācittiyaṃ kattha pañ‍ñattanti? sāvatthiyaṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ| kaṃ ārabbhāti? chabbaggiyā bhikkhuniyau ārabbha| kismiṃ vatthusminti? chabbaggiyā bhikkhuniyau itthālaṅkāraṃ dhāraisuṃ, tasmiṃ vatthusmiṃ| aikā pañ‍ñatti| chan‍naṃ āpattisamuṭṭhānānaṃ  dvīhi samuṭṭhānaihi samuṭṭhāti – aiḷakalaumakai…pai॰…|

gandhavaṇṇakaina nahāyantiyā pācittiyaṃ kattha pañ‍ñattanti? sāvatthiyaṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ| kaṃ ārabbhāti? chabbaggiyā bhikkhuniyau ārabbha| kismiṃ vatthusminti? chabbaggiyā bhikkhuniyau gandhavaṇṇakaina nahāyiṃsu, tasmiṃ vatthusmiṃ| aikā pañ‍ñatti| chan‍naṃ āpattisamuṭṭhānānaṃ dvīhi samuṭṭhānaihi samuṭṭhāti – aiḷakalaumakai…pai॰…|

vāsitakaina  piñ‍ñākaina nahāyantiyā pācittiyaṃ kattha pañ‍ñattanti? sāvatthiyaṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ| kaṃ ārabbhāti? chabbaggiyā bhikkhuniyau ārabbha| kismiṃ vatthusminti? chabbaggiyā bhikkhuniyau vāsitakaina piñ‍ñākaina nahāyiṃsu, tasmiṃ vatthusmiṃ| aikā pañ‍ñatti| chan‍naṃ āpattisamuṭṭhānānaṃ dvīhi samuṭṭhānaihi samuṭṭhāti – aiḷakalaumakai…pai॰…|

bhikkhuniyā ummaddāpaintiyā parimaddāpaintiyā pācittiyaṃ kattha pañ‍ñattanti? sāvatthiyaṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ| kaṃ ārabbhāti? sambahulā bhikkhuniyau ārabbha| kismiṃ vatthusminti? sambahulā bhikkhuniyau bhikkhuniyā ummaddāpaisuṃ parimaddāpaisuṃ, tasmiṃ vatthusmiṃ| aikā pañ‍ñatti| chan‍naṃ āpattisamuṭṭhānānaṃ dvīhi samuṭṭhānaihi samuṭṭhāti – aiḷakalaumakai…pai॰…|

sikkhamānā  ummaddāpaintiyā parimaddāpaintiyā pācittiyaṃ kattha pañ‍ñattanti? sāvatthiyaṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ| kaṃ ārabbhāti? sambahulā  bhikkhuniyau ārabbha| kismiṃ vatthusminti? sambahulā bhikkhuniyau sikkhamānāya ummaddāpaisuṃ parimaddāpaisuṃ, tasmiṃ vatthusmiṃ| aikā pañ‍ñatti| chan‍naṃ āpattisamuṭṭhānānaṃ dvīhi samuṭṭhānaihi samuṭṭhāti – aiḷakalaumakai…pai॰…|

sāmaṇairiyā ummaddāpaintiyā parimaddāpaintiyā pācittiyaṃ kattha pañ‍ñattanti? sāvatthiyaṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ| kaṃ ārabbhāti? sambahulā bhikkhuniyau ārabbha| kismiṃ vatthusminti? sambahulā bhikkhuniyau sāmaṇairiyā ummaddāpaisuṃ parimaddāpaisuṃ, tasmiṃ vatthusmiṃ| aikā pañ‍ñatti| chan‍naṃ āpattisamuṭṭhānānaṃ dvīhi samuṭṭhānaihi samuṭṭhāti – aiḷakalaumakai…pai॰…|

gihiniyā ummaddāpaintiyā parimaddāpaintiyā pācittiyaṃ kattha pañ‍ñattanti? sāvatthiyaṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ| kaṃ ārabbhāti? sambahulā bhikkhuniyau ārabbha| kismiṃ vatthusminti? sambahulā bhikkhuniyau gihiniyā ummaddāpaisuṃ parimaddāpaisuṃ, tasmiṃ vatthusmiṃ| aikā pañ‍ñatti| chan‍naṃ āpattisamuṭṭhānānaṃ dvīhi samuṭṭhānaihi samuṭṭhāti – aiḷakalaumakai…pai॰…|

bhikkhussa puratau anāpucchā āsanai nisīdantiyā pācittiyaṃ kattha pañ‍ñattanti? sāvatthiyaṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ| kaṃ ārabbhāti? sambahulā bhikkhuniyau ārabbha| kismiṃ vatthusminti? sambahulā bhikkhuniyau bhikkhussa puratau anāpucchā āsanai nisīdiṃsu, tasmiṃ vatthusmiṃ| aikā pañ‍ñatti| chan‍naṃ āpattisamuṭṭhānānaṃ dvīhi samuṭṭhānaihi samuṭṭhāti – kathinakai…pai॰…|

anaukāsakataṃ  bhikkhuṃ pañhaṃ pucchantiyā pācittiyaṃ kattha pañ‍ñattanti? sāvatthiyaṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ| kaṃ ārabbhāti? sambahulā bhikkhuniyau ārabbha| kismiṃ vatthusminti? sambahulā bhikkhuniyau anaukāsakataṃ bhikkhuṃ pañhaṃ pucchiṃsu, tasmiṃ vatthusmiṃ| aikā pañ‍ñatti| chan‍naṃ āpattisamuṭṭhānānaṃ dvīhi samuṭṭhānaihi samuṭṭhāti – padasaudhammai…pai॰…|

asaṅkac‍cikāya asaṅkacchikāya (syā॰) gāmaṃ pavisantiyā pācittiyaṃ kattha pañ‍ñattanti? sāvatthiyaṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ| kaṃ ārabbhāti? añ‍ñataraṃ bhikkhuniṃ ārabbha| kismiṃ vatthusminti? añ‍ñatarā bhikkhunī asaṅkac‍cikā gāmaṃ pāvisi, tasmiṃ vatthusmiṃ| aikā pañ‍ñatti| chan‍naṃ āpattisamuṭṭhānānaṃ dvīhi samuṭṭhānaihi samuṭṭhāti – siyā kāyatau samuṭṭhāti, na vācatau na cittatau; siyā kāyatau ca cittatau ca samuṭṭhāti, na vācatau…pai॰…|

chattupāhanavaggau navamau|

navavaggakhuddakā niṭṭhitā|

tassuddānaṃ –

lasuṇaṃ  saṃharai laumaṃ, talamaṭṭhañ‍ca suddhikaṃ|

bhuñ‍jantāmakadhañ‍ñānaṃ, dvai vighāsaina dassanā||

andhakārai  paṭicchan‍nai, ajjhaukāsai rathikāya ca|

purai pacchā vikālai ca, duggahi nirayai vadhi||

naggaudakā visibbaitvā, pañ‍cāhikaṃ saṅkamanīyaṃ|

gaṇaṃ vibhaṅgasamaṇaṃ, dubbalaṃ kathinaina ca||

aikamañ‍cattharaṇaina, sañ‍cic‍ca sahajīvinī|

datvā saṃsaṭṭhaantau ca, tirauvassaṃ na pak‍kamai||

rājā āsandi suttañ‍ca, gihi vūpasamaina ca|

dadai cīvarāvasathaṃ, pariyāpuṇañ‍ca vācayai||

ārāmak‍kausacaṇḍī  ca, bhuñ‍jaiyya kulamaccharī|

vāsai pavāraṇauvādaṃ, dvai dhammā pasākhaina ca||

gabbhī pāyantī cha dhammai, asammatūnadvādasa|

paripuṇṇañ‍ca  saṅghaina, saha vuṭṭhā cha pañ‍ca ca||

kumārī dvai ca saṅghaina, dvādasa sammataina ca|

alaṃ sacai ca dvaivassaṃ, saṃsaṭṭhā sāmikaina ca||

pārivāsikānuvassaṃ, duvai vuṭṭhāpanaina ca|

chattayānaina saṅghāṇi, itthālaṅkāravaṇṇakai||

piñ‍ñākabhikkhunī caiva, sikkhā ca sāmaṇairikā|

gihi bhikkhussa puratau, anaukāsaṃ saṅkac‍cikāti||

taisaṃ vaggānaṃ uddānaṃ –

lasuṇandhakārā nhānā, tuvaṭṭā cittagārakā|

ārāmaṃ  gabbhinī caiva, kumārī chattupāhanāti||

5. pāṭidaisanīyakaṇḍaṃ

227. sappiṃ  viñ‍ñāpaitvā bhuñ‍jantiyā pāṭidaisanīyaṃ kattha pañ‍ñattanti? sāvatthiyaṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ| kaṃ ārabbhāti? chabbaggiyā bhikkhuniyau ārabbha| kismiṃ vatthusminti? chabbaggiyā bhikkhuniyau sappiṃ viñ‍ñāpaitvā bhuñ‍jiṃsu, tasmiṃ vatthusmiṃ| aikā pañ‍ñatti, aikā anupañ‍ñatti| chan‍naṃ āpattisamuṭṭhānānaṃ catūhi samuṭṭhānaihi samuṭṭhāti…pai॰…|

tailaṃ viñ‍ñāpaitvā bhuñ‍jantiyā pāṭidaisanīyaṃ kattha pañ‍ñattanti? sāvatthiyaṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ| kaṃ ārabbhāti? chabbaggiyā bhikkhuniyau ārabbha| kismiṃ vatthusminti? chabbaggiyā bhikkhuniyau tailaṃ viñ‍ñāpaitvā bhuñ‍jiṃsu, tasmiṃ vatthusmiṃ| aikā pañ‍ñatti, aikā anupañ‍ñatti| chan‍naṃ āpattisamuṭṭhānānaṃ catūhi samuṭṭhānaihi samuṭṭhāti…pai॰…|

madhuṃ  viñ‍ñāpaitvā bhuñ‍jantiyā pāṭidaisanīyaṃ kattha pañ‍ñattanti? sāvatthiyaṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ| kaṃ ārabbhāti? chabbaggiyā bhikkhuniyau ārabbha| kismiṃ vatthusminti? chabbaggiyā bhikkhuniyau madhuṃ viñ‍ñāpaitvā bhuñ‍jiṃsu, tasmiṃ vatthusmiṃ| aikā pañ‍ñatti, aikā anupañ‍ñatti| chan‍naṃ āpattisamuṭṭhānānaṃ catūhi samuṭṭhānaihi samuṭṭhāti…pai॰…|

phāṇitaṃ  viñ‍ñāpaitvā bhuñ‍jantiyā pāṭidaisanīyaṃ kattha pañ‍ñattanti? sāvatthiyaṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ| kaṃ ārabbhāti? chabbaggiyā bhikkhuniyau ārabbha| kismiṃ vatthusminti? chabbaggiyā bhikkhuniyau phāṇitaṃ viñ‍ñāpaitvā bhuñ‍jiṃsu, tasmiṃ vatthusmiṃ| aikā pañ‍ñatti, aikā anupañ‍ñatti| chan‍naṃ āpattisamuṭṭhānānaṃ catūhi samuṭṭhānaihi samuṭṭhāti…pai॰…|

macchaṃ viñ‍ñāpaitvā bhuñ‍jantiyā pāṭidaisanīyaṃ kattha pañ‍ñattanti? sāvatthiyaṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ| kaṃ ārabbhāti? chabbaggiyā bhikkhuniyau ārabbha| kismiṃ vatthusminti? chabbaggiyā bhikkhuniyau macchaṃ viñ‍ñāpaitvā bhuñ‍jiṃsu, tasmiṃ vatthusmiṃ| aikā pañ‍ñatti, aikā anupañ‍ñatti| chan‍naṃ āpattisamuṭṭhānānaṃ catūhi samuṭṭhānaihi samuṭṭhāti…pai॰…|

maṃsaṃ viñ‍ñāpaitvā bhuñ‍jantiyā pāṭidaisanīyaṃ kattha pañ‍ñattanti? sāvatthiyaṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ| kaṃ ārabbhāti? chabbaggiyā bhikkhuniyau ārabbha| kismiṃ vatthusminti? chabbaggiyā bhikkhuniyau| maṃsaṃ viñ‍ñāpaitvā bhuñ‍jiṃsu, tasmiṃ vatthusmiṃ| aikā pañ‍ñatti, aikā anupañ‍ñatti| chan‍naṃ āpattisamuṭṭhānānaṃ catūhi samuṭṭhānaihi samuṭṭhāti…pai॰…|

khīraṃ  viñ‍ñāpaitvā bhuñ‍jantiyā pāṭidaisanīyaṃ kattha pañ‍ñattanti? sāvatthiyaṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ| kaṃ ārabbhāti? chabbaggiyā bhikkhuniyau  ārabbha| kismiṃ vatthusminti? chabbaggiyā bhikkhuniyau khīraṃ viñ‍ñāpaitvā bhuñ‍jiṃsu, tasmiṃ vatthusmiṃ| aikā pañ‍ñatti, aikā anupañ‍ñatti| chan‍naṃ āpattisamuṭṭhānānaṃ catūhi samuṭṭhānaihi samuṭṭhāti…pai॰…|

dadhiṃ viñ‍ñāpaitvā bhuñ‍jantiyā pāṭidaisanīyaṃ kattha pañ‍ñattanti? sāvatthiyaṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ| kaṃ ārabbhāti? chabbaggiyā bhikkhuniyau ārabbha| kismiṃ vatthusminti? chabbaggiyā bhikkhuniyau dadhiṃ viñ‍ñāpaitvā bhuñ‍jiṃsu, tasmiṃ vatthusmiṃ| aikā pañ‍ñatti, aikā anupañ‍ñatti| chan‍naṃ āpattisamuṭṭhānānaṃ catūhi samuṭṭhānaihi samuṭṭhāti – siyā kāyatau samuṭṭhāti, na vācatau na cittatau; siyā kāyatau ca vācatau ca samuṭṭhāti, na cittatau; siyā  kāyatau ca cittatau ca samuṭṭhāti, na vācatau; siyā kāyatau ca vācatau ca cittatau ca samuṭṭhāti…pai॰…|

aṭṭha pāṭidaisanīyā niṭṭhitā|

tassuddānaṃ –

sappiṃ tailaṃ madhuñ‍caiva, phāṇitaṃ macchamaiva ca|

maṃsaṃ khīraṃ dadhiñ‍cāpi, viñ‍ñāpaitvāna bhikkhunī|

pāṭidaisanīyā aṭṭha, sayaṃ buddhaina daisitāti||

yai sikkhāpadā bhikkhuvibhaṅgai vitthāritā tai saṃkhittā

bhikkhunivibhaṅgai|

katthapañ‍ñattivārau niṭṭhitau paṭhamau|

2. katāpattivārau

1. pārājikakaṇḍaṃ

228. avassutā  bhikkhunī avassutassa purisapuggalassa kāyasaṃsaggaṃ sādiyantī kati āpattiyau āpaj‍jati? avassutā bhikkhunī avassutassa purisapuggalassa kāyasaṃsaggaṃ sādiyantī  tissau āpattiyau āpaj‍jati| adhakkhakaṃ ubbhajāṇumaṇḍalaṃ gahaṇaṃ sādiyati, āpatti pārājikassa; ubbhakkhakaṃ adhaujāṇumaṇḍalaṃ gahaṇaṃ sādiyati, āpatti thul‍lac‍cayassa; kāyapaṭibaddhaṃ gahaṇaṃ sādiyati, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa – avassutā bhikkhunī avassutassa purisapuggalassa kāyasaṃsaggaṃ sādiyantī imā tissau āpattiyau āpaj‍jati|

vaj‍jappaṭicchādikā bhikkhunī vaj‍jaṃ paṭicchādaintī kati āpattiyau āpaj‍jati? vaj‍jappaṭicchādikā bhikkhunī vaj‍jaṃ paṭicchādaintī tissau āpattiyau āpaj‍jati| jānaṃ pārājikaṃ dhammaṃ paṭicchādaiti, āpatti pārājikassa; vaimatikā paṭicchādaiti, āpatti thul‍lac‍cayassa; ācāravipattiṃ paṭicchādaiti, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa – vaj‍jappaṭicchādikā bhikkhunī vaj‍jaṃ paṭicchādaintī imā tissau āpattiyau āpaj‍jati|

ukkhittānuvattikā bhikkhunī yāvatatiyaṃ samanubhāsanāya na paṭinissaj‍jantī kati āpattiyau āpaj‍jati? ukkhittānuvattikā bhikkhunī yāvatatiyaṃ samanubhāsanāya na paṭinissaj‍jantī tissau āpattiyau āpaj‍jati| ñattiyā duk‍kaṭaṃ; dvīhi kammavācāhi thul‍lac‍cayā; kammavācāpariyausānai  āpatti pārājikassa – ukkhittānuvattikā bhikkhunī yāvatatiyaṃ samanubhāsanāya na paṭinissaj‍jantī imā tissau āpattiyau āpaj‍jati|

aṭṭhamaṃ vatthuṃ paripūraintī kati āpattiyau āpaj‍jati? aṭṭhamaṃ vatthuṃ paripūraintī tissau āpattiyau  āpaj‍jati| purisaina – ‘‘itthan‍nāmaṃ aukāsaṃ itthan‍nāmaṃ gahanaṃ (sī॰), itthan‍nāmaṃ gabbhaṃ (syā॰) āgacchā’’ti vuttā gacchati, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa; purisassa hatthapāsaṃ auk‍kantamattai āpatti thul‍lac‍cayassa; aṭṭhamaṃ vatthuṃ paripūraiti, āpatti pārājikassa – aṭṭhamaṃ vatthuṃ paripūraintī imā tissau āpattiyau āpaj‍jati|

pārājikā niṭṭhitā|

2. saṅghādisaisakaṇḍaṃ

229. ussayavādikā bhikkhunī aḍḍaṃ karauntī tissau āpattiyau āpaj‍jati| aikassa āraucaiti, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa; dutiyassa āraucaiti,  āpatti thul‍lac‍cayassa; aḍḍapariyausānai āpatti saṅghādisaisassa|

cauriṃ vuṭṭhāpaintī tissau āpattiyau āpaj‍jati| ñattiyā duk‍kaṭaṃ; dvīhi kammavācāhi thul‍lac‍cayā; kammavācāpariyausānai āpatti saṅghādisaisassa|

aikā gāmantaraṃ gacchantī tissau āpattiyau āpaj‍jati| gacchati, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa; paṭhamaṃ pādaṃ parikkhaipaṃ atik‍kāmaiti, āpatti thul‍lac‍cayassa; dutiyaṃ pādaṃ atik‍kāmaiti, āpatti saṅghādisaisassa|

samaggaina saṅghaina ukkhittaṃ bhikkhuniṃ dhammaina vinayaina satthusāsanaina anapalaukaitvā kārakasaṅghaṃ anañ‍ñāya gaṇassa chandaṃ ausāraintī tissau āpattiyau āpaj‍jati| ñattiyā duk‍kaṭaṃ; dvīhi kammavācāhi thul‍lac‍cayā; kammavācāpariyausānai āpatti saṅghādisaisassa|

avassutā bhikkhunī avassutassa purisapuggalassa hatthatau khādanīyaṃ vā bhaujanīyaṃ vā sahatthā paṭiggahaitvā bhuñ‍jantī  tissau āpattiyau āpaj‍jati| ‘‘khādissāmi bhuñ‍jissāmī’’ti paṭiggaṇhāti, āpatti thul‍lac‍cayassa; ajjhauhārai ajjhauhārai āpatti saṅghādisaisassa; udakadantapaunaṃ paṭiggaṇhāti, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa|

‘‘kiṃ tai, ayyai, aisau purisapuggalau karissati avassutau vā anavassutau vā, yatau tvaṃ  anavassutā! iṅgha, ayyai, yaṃ tai aisau purisapuggalau daiti khādanīyaṃ vā bhaujanīyaṃ vā, taṃ tvaṃ sahatthā paṭiggahaitvā khāda vā bhuñ‍ja vā’’ti uyyaujaintī tissau āpattiyau āpaj‍jati| tassā vacanaina khādissāmi bhuñ‍jissāmīti paṭiggaṇhāti, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa; ajjhauhārai ajjhauhārai āpatti thul‍lac‍cayassa; bhaujanapariyausānai āpatti saṅghādisaisassa|

kupitā bhikkhunī yāvatatiyaṃ samanubhāsanāya na paṭinissaj‍jantī tissau āpattiyau āpaj‍jati| ñattiyā duk‍kaṭaṃ; dvīhi kammavācāhi thul‍lac‍cayā; kammavācāpariyausānai āpatti saṅghādisaisassa|

kismiñ‍cidaiva adhikaraṇai pac‍cākatā bhikkhunī yāvatatiyaṃ samanubhāsanāya na paṭinissaj‍jantī tissau āpattiyau āpaj‍jati| ñattiyā duk‍kaṭaṃ;  dvīhi kammavācāhi thul‍lac‍cayā; kammavācāpariyausānai āpatti saṅghādisaisassa|

saṃsaṭṭhā bhikkhuniyau yāvatatiyaṃ samanubhāsanāya na paṭinissaj‍jantiyau tissau āpattiyau āpaj‍janti| ñattiyā duk‍kaṭaṃ; dvīhi kammavācāhi thul‍lac‍cayā; kammavācāpariyausānai āpatti saṅghādisaisassa|

‘‘saṃsaṭṭhāva, ayyai, tumhai viharatha| mā tumhai nānā viharitthā’’ti uyyaujaintī yāvatatiyaṃ samanubhāsanāya na paṭinissaj‍jantī tissau āpattiyau āpaj‍jati| ñattiyā duk‍kaṭaṃ; dvīhi kammavācāhi thul‍lac‍cayā; kammavācāpariyausānai āpatti saṅghādisaisassa|

saṅghādisaisā niṭṭhitā|

3. nissaggiyakaṇḍaṃ

230. pattasan‍nicayaṃ karauntī aikaṃ āpattiṃ āpaj‍jati| nissaggiyaṃ pācittiyaṃ|

akālacīvaraṃ ‘‘kālacīvara’’nti adhiṭṭhahitvā bhājāpaintī dvai āpattiyau āpaj‍jati| bhājāpaiti, payaugai duk‍kaṭaṃ; bhājāpitai nissaggiyaṃ pācittiyaṃ|

bhikkhuniyā  saddhiṃ cīvaraṃ parivattaitvā acchindantī dvai āpattiyau  āpaj‍jati| acchindati, payaugai duk‍kaṭaṃ; acchin‍nai nissaggiyaṃ pācittiyaṃ|

añ‍ñaṃ viñ‍ñāpaitvā añ‍ñaṃ viñ‍ñāpaintī dvai āpattiyau āpaj‍jati| viñ‍ñāpaiti, payaugai duk‍kaṭaṃ; viñ‍ñāpitai nissaggiyaṃ pācittiyaṃ|

añ‍ñaṃ caitāpaitvā añ‍ñaṃ caitāpaintī dvai āpattiyau āpaj‍jati| caitāpaiti, payaugai duk‍kaṭaṃ; caitāpitai nissaggiyaṃ pācittiyaṃ|

añ‍ñadatthikaina parikkhāraina añ‍ñuddisikaina saṅghikaina añ‍ñaṃ caitāpaintī dvai āpattiyau āpaj‍jati| caitāpaiti, payaugai duk‍kaṭaṃ; caitāpitai nissaggiyaṃ pācittiyaṃ|

añ‍ñadatthikai  parikkhāraina añ‍ñuddisikaina saṅghikaina saṃyācikaina añ‍ñaṃ caitāpaintī dvai āpattiyau āpaj‍jati| caitāpaiti, payaugai duk‍kaṭaṃ; caitāpitai nissaggiyaṃ pācittiyaṃ|

añ‍ñadatthikaina parikkhāraina añ‍ñuddisikaina mahājanikaina añ‍ñaṃ caitāpaintī dvai āpattiyau āpaj‍jati| caitāpaiti, payaugai duk‍kaṭaṃ; caitāpitai nissaggiyaṃ pācittiyaṃ|

añ‍ñadatthikaina parikkhāraina añ‍ñuddisikaina mahājanikaina saṃyācikaina añ‍ñaṃ caitāpaintī dvai āpattiyau āpaj‍jati| caitāpaiti, payaugai duk‍kaṭaṃ; caitāpitai nissaggiyaṃ pācittiyaṃ|

añ‍ñadatthikaina  parikkhāraina añ‍ñuddisikai  puggalikaina saṃyācikaina añ‍ñaṃ caitāpaintī dvai āpattiyau āpaj‍jati| caitāpaiti, payaugai duk‍kaṭaṃ; caitāpitai nissaggiyaṃ pācittiyaṃ|

atiraikacatuk‍kaṃsaparamaṃ garupāvuraṇaṃ caitāpaintī dvai āpattiyau āpaj‍jati| caitāpaiti, payaugai duk‍kaṭaṃ; caitāpitai nissaggiyaṃ pācittiyaṃ|

atiraikaaḍḍhataiyyakaṃsaparamaṃ lahupāvuraṇaṃ caitāpaintī dvai āpattiyau āpaj‍jati| caitāpaiti, payaugai duk‍kaṭaṃ; caitāpitai nissaggiyaṃ pācittiyaṃ|

nissaggiyā pācittiyā niṭṭhitā|

4. pācittiyakaṇḍaṃ

1. lasuṇavaggau

231. lasuṇaṃ  khādantī dvai āpattiyau āpaj‍jati| khādissāmīti paṭiggaṇhāti, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa; ajjhauhārai ajjhauhārai āpatti pācittiyassa|

sambādhai laumaṃ saṃharāpaintī dvai āpattiyau āpaj‍jati| saṃharāpaiti, payaugai duk‍kaṭaṃ; saṃharāpitai āpatti pācittiyassa|

talaghātakaṃ  karauntī dvai āpattiyau āpaj‍jati| karauti, payaugai duk‍kaṭaṃ; katai āpatti pācittiyassa|

jatumaṭṭhakaṃ ādiyantī dvai āpattiyau āpaj‍jati| ādiyati, payaugai duk‍kaṭaṃ, ādin‍nai āpatti pācittiyassa|

atiraikadvaṅgulapabbaparamaṃ udakasuddhikaṃ ādiyantī dvai āpattiyau  āpaj‍jati| ādiyati, payaugai duk‍kaṭaṃ; ādin‍nai āpatti pācittiyassa|

bhikkhussa bhuñ‍jantassa pānīyaina vā vidhūpanaina vā upatiṭṭhantī dvai āpattiyau āpaj‍jati| hatthapāsai tiṭṭhati, āpatti pācittiyassa; hatthapāsaṃ vijahitvā tiṭṭhati, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa|

āmakadhañ‍ñaṃ viñ‍ñāpaitvā bhuñ‍jantī dvai āpattiyau āpaj‍jati| bhuñ‍jissāmīti paṭiggaṇhāti, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa; ajjhauhārai ajjhauhārai āpatti pācittiyassa|

uc‍cāraṃ vā passāvaṃ vā saṅkāraṃ vā vighāsaṃ vā tiraukuṭṭai vā tiraupākārai vā chaḍḍaintī dvai āpattiyau āpaj‍jati| chaḍḍaiti, payaugai duk‍kaṭaṃ; chaḍḍitai āpatti pācittiyassa|

uc‍cāraṃ vā passāvaṃ vā saṅkāraṃ vā vighāsaṃ vā haritai chaḍḍaintī dvai āpattiyau āpaj‍jati| chaḍḍaiti, payaugai duk‍kaṭaṃ; chaḍḍitai āpatti pācittiyassa|

nac‍caṃ  vā gītaṃ vā vāditaṃ vā dassanāya gacchantī dvai āpattiyau āpaj‍jati| gacchati, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa; yattha ṭhitā passati vā suṇāti vā, āpatti pācittiyassa|

lasuṇavaggau paṭhamau|

2. rattandhakāravaggau

232. rattandhakārai appadīpai purisaina saddhiṃ aikainaikā santiṭṭhantī  dvai āpattiyau āpaj‍jati| hatthapāsai tiṭṭhati, āpatti pācittiyassa; hatthapāsaṃ vijahitvā tiṭṭhati, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa|

paṭicchan‍nai  aukāsai purisaina saddhiṃ aikainaikā santiṭṭhantī dvai āpattiyau āpaj‍jati| hatthapāsai tiṭṭhati, āpatti pācittiyassa; hatthapāsaṃ vijahitvā tiṭṭhati, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa|

ajjhaukāsai  purisaina saddhiṃ aikainaikā santiṭṭhantī dvai āpattiyau āpaj‍jati| hatthapāsai tiṭṭhati, āpatti pācittiyassa; hatthapāsaṃ vijahitvā tiṭṭhati, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa|

rathikāya vā byūhai vā siṅghāṭakai vā purisaina saddhiṃ aikainaikā santiṭṭhantī dvai āpattiyau āpaj‍jati| hatthapāsai tiṭṭhati, āpatti pācittiyassa; hatthapāsaṃ vijahitvā tiṭṭhati, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa|

puraibhattaṃ kulāni upasaṅkamitvā āsanai nisīditvā sāmikai anāpucchā pak‍kamantī dvai āpattiyau āpaj‍jati| paṭhamaṃ pādaṃ anauvassakaṃ atik‍kāmaiti, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa; dutiyaṃ pādaṃ atik‍kāmaiti, āpatti pācittiyassa|

pacchābhattaṃ kulāni upasaṅkamitvā sāmikai anāpucchā āsanai nisīdantī dvai āpattiyau āpaj‍jati| nisīdati, payaugai duk‍kaṭaṃ; nisin‍nai āpatti pācittiyassa|

vikālai  kulāni upasaṅkamitvā sāmikai anāpucchā saiyyaṃ santharitvā vā santharāpaitvā vā abhinisīdantī dvai āpattiyau āpaj‍jati| abhinisīdati, payaugai duk‍kaṭaṃ; abhinisin‍nai āpatti pācittiyassa|

duggahitaina  dūpadhāritaina paraṃ ujjhāpaintī dvai āpattiyau āpaj‍jati| ujjhāpaiti, payaugai duk‍kaṭaṃ; ujjhāpitai āpatti pācittiyassa|

attānaṃ vā paraṃ vā nirayaina vā brahmacariyaina vā abhisapantī dvai āpattiyau āpaj‍jati| abhisapati, payaugai duk‍kaṭaṃ; abhisapitai āpatti pācittiyassa|

attānaṃ vadhitvā vadhitvā raudantī dvai āpattiyau āpaj‍jati| vadhati raudati, āpatti pācittiyassa; vadhati na raudati, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa|

rattandhakāravaggau dutiyau|

3. nahānavaggau

233. naggā  nahāyantī dvai āpattiyau āpaj‍jati| nahāyati, payaugai duk‍kaṭaṃ; nahānapariyausānai āpatti pācittiyassa|

pamāṇātik‍kantaṃ udakasāṭikaṃ kārāpaintī dvai āpattiyau āpaj‍jati| kārāpaiti, payaugai duk‍kaṭaṃ; kārāpitai, āpatti pācittiyassa|

bhikkhuniyā cīvaraṃ visibbaitvā vā visibbāpaitvā vā naiva  sibbaintī na sibbāpanāya ussuk‍kaṃ karauntī aikaṃ āpattiṃ āpaj‍jati| pācittiyaṃ|

pañ‍cāhikaṃ saṅghāṭicāraṃ atik‍kāmaintī aikaṃ āpattiṃ āpaj‍jati| pācittiyaṃ| cīvarasaṅkamanīyaṃ dhāraintī dvai āpattiyau āpaj‍jati| dhāraiti, payaugai duk‍kaṭaṃ; dhāritai, āpatti pācittiyassa|

gaṇassa cīvaralābhaṃ antarāyaṃ karauntī dvai āpattiyau āpaj‍jati| karauti, payaugai duk‍kaṭaṃ; katai, āpatti pācittiyassa|

dhammikaṃ  cīvaravibhaṅgaṃ paṭibāhantī dvai āpattiyau āpaj‍jati| paṭibāhati, payaugai duk‍kaṭaṃ; paṭibāhitai, āpatti pācittiyassa|

agārikassa vā paribbājakassa vā paribbājikāya vā samaṇacīvaraṃ daintī dvai āpattiyau āpaj‍jati| daiti, payaugai duk‍kaṭaṃ; din‍nai, āpatti pācittiyassa|

dubbalacīvarapac‍cāsā  cīvarakālasamayaṃ atik‍kāmaintī dvai āpattiyau āpaj‍jati| atik‍kāmaiti, payaugai duk‍kaṭaṃ; atik‍kāmitai, āpatti pācittiyassa|

dhammikaṃ kathinuddhāraṃ paṭibāhantī dvai āpattiyau āpaj‍jati| paṭibāhati, payaugai duk‍kaṭaṃ; paṭibāhitai, āpatti pācittiyassa|

nahānavaggau tatiyau|

4. tuvaṭṭavaggau

234. dvai  bhikkhuniyau aikamañ‍cai tuvaṭṭaintiyau dvai āpattiyau āpaj‍janti| nipaj‍janti, payaugai duk‍kaṭaṃ; nipan‍nai, āpatti pācittiyassa|

dvai bhikkhuniyau aikattharaṇapāvuraṇā tuvaṭṭaintiyau dvai āpattiyau āpaj‍janti| nipaj‍janti, payaugai duk‍kaṭaṃ; nipan‍nai, āpatti pācittiyassa|

bhikkhuniyā sañ‍cic‍ca aphāsuṃ karauntī dvai āpattiyau āpaj‍jati| karauti, payaugai duk‍kaṭaṃ; katai, āpatti pācittiyassa|

dukkhitaṃ sahajīviniṃ naiva upaṭṭhaintī na upaṭṭhāpanāya ussuk‍kaṃ karauntī aikaṃ āpattiṃ āpaj‍jati| pācittiyaṃ|

bhikkhuniyā upassayaṃ datvā kupitā anattamanā nik‍kaḍḍhantī dvai āpattiyau āpaj‍jati| nik‍kaḍḍhati, payaugai duk‍kaṭaṃ; nik‍kaḍḍhitai, āpatti pācittiyassa|

saṃsaṭṭhā  bhikkhunī yāvatatiyaṃ samanubhāsanāya na paṭinissaj‍jantī dvai āpattiyau āpaj‍jati| ñattiyā duk‍kaṭaṃ; kammavācāpariyausānai āpatti pācittiyassa|

antauraṭṭhai sāsaṅkasammatai sappaṭibhayai asatthikā cārikaṃ carantī dvai āpattiyau āpaj‍jati| paṭipaj‍jati, payaugai duk‍kaṭaṃ; paṭipan‍nai, āpatti pācittiyassa|

tirauraṭṭhai  sāsaṅkasammatai sappaṭibhayai asatthikā cārikaṃ carantī dvai āpattiyau āpaj‍jati| paṭipaj‍jati, payaugai duk‍kaṭaṃ; paṭipan‍nai, āpatti pācittiyassa|

antauvassaṃ cārikaṃ carantī dvai āpattiyau āpaj‍jati| paṭipaj‍jati, payaugai duk‍kaṭaṃ; paṭipan‍nai, āpatti pācittiyassa|

vassaṃvuṭṭhā bhikkhunī cārikaṃ na pak‍kamantī aikaṃ āpattiṃ āpaj‍jati| pācittiyaṃ|

tuvaṭṭavaggau catutthau|

5. cittāgāravaggau

235. rājāgāraṃ  vā cittāgāraṃ vā ārāmaṃ vā uyyānaṃ vā paukkharaṇiṃ vā dassanāya gacchantī dvai āpattiyau āpaj‍jati| gacchati, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa; yattha ṭhitā passati, āpatti pācittiyassa|

āsandiṃ vā pal‍laṅkaṃ vā paribhuñ‍jantī dvai āpattiyau āpaj‍jati| paribhuñ‍jati, payaugai duk‍kaṭaṃ; paribhuttai, āpatti pācittiyassa|

suttaṃ kantantī dvai āpattiyau āpaj‍jati| kantati, payaugai duk‍kaṭaṃ; uj‍javuj‍javai, āpatti pācittiyassa|

gihivaiyyāvac‍caṃ karauntī dvai āpattiyau āpaj‍jati| karauti, payaugai duk‍kaṭaṃ; katai, āpatti pācittiyassa|

bhikkhuniyā  – ‘‘aihāyyai imaṃ adhikaraṇaṃ vūpasamaihī’’ti vuc‍camānā – ‘‘sādhū’’ti  paṭissuṇitvā naiva vūpasamaintī na vūpasamāya ussuk‍kaṃ karauntī aikaṃ āpattiṃ āpaj‍jati| pācittiyaṃ|

agārikassa vā paribbājakassa vā paribbājikāya vā sahatthā khādanīyaṃ vā bhaujanīyaṃ vā daintī dvai āpattiyau āpaj‍jati| daiti, payaugai duk‍kaṭaṃ; din‍nai, āpatti pācittiyassa|

āvasathacīvaraṃ anissaj‍jitvā paribhuñ‍jantī dvai āpattiyau āpaj‍jati| paribhuñ‍jati, payaugai duk‍kaṭaṃ; paribhuttai, āpatti pācittiyassa|

āvasathaṃ anissaj‍jitvā cārikaṃ pak‍kamantī dvai āpattiyau āpaj‍jati| paṭhamaṃ pādaṃ parikkhaipaṃ atik‍kāmaiti, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa; dutiyaṃ pādaṃ atik‍kāmaiti, āpatti pācittiyassa|

tiracchānavij‍jaṃ pariyāpuṇantī dvai āpattiyau āpaj‍jati| pariyāpuṇāti, payaugai duk‍kaṭaṃ; padai padai āpatti pācittiyassa|

tiracchānavij‍jaṃ vācaintī dvai āpattiyau āpaj‍jati| vācaiti, payaugai duk‍kaṭaṃ; padai padai āpatti pācittiyassa|

cittāgāravaggau pañ‍camau|

6. ārāmavaggau

236. jānaṃ  sabhikkhukaṃ ārāmaṃ anāpucchā pavisantī dvai āpattiyau  āpaj‍jati| paṭhamaṃ pādaṃ parikkhaipaṃ atik‍kāmaiti, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa; dutiyaṃ pādaṃ atik‍kāmaiti, āpatti pācittiyassa|

bhikkhuṃ ak‍kausantī paribhāsantī dvai āpattiyau āpaj‍jati| ak‍kausati, payaugai duk‍kaṭaṃ; ak‍kausitai, āpatti pācittiyassa|

caṇḍīkatā  gaṇaṃ paribhāsantī dvai āpattiyau āpaj‍jati| paribhāsati, payaugai duk‍kaṭaṃ; paribhāsitai, āpatti pācittiyassa|

nimantitā vā pavāritā vā khādanīyaṃ vā bhaujanīyaṃ vā bhuñ‍jantī dvai āpattiyau āpaj‍jati| ‘‘khādissāmi bhuñ‍jissāmī’’ti paṭiggaṇhāti, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa; ajjhauhārai ajjhauhārai āpatti pācittiyassa|

kulaṃ maccharāyantī dvai āpattiyau āpaj‍jati| maccharāyati, payaugai duk‍kaṭaṃ; maccharitai, āpatti pācittiyassa|

abhikkhukai āvāsai vassaṃ vasantī dvai āpattiyau āpaj‍jati| ‘‘vassaṃ vasissāmī’’ti saināsanaṃ pañ‍ñapaiti pānīyaṃ paribhaujanīyaṃ upaṭṭhapaiti parivaiṇaṃ sammaj‍jati, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa; saha aruṇuggamanā āpatti pācittiyassa|

vassaṃvuṭṭhā bhikkhunī ubhatausaṅghai tīhi ṭhānaihi na pavāraintī aikaṃ āpattiṃ āpaj‍jati| pācittiyaṃ|

auvādāya vā saṃvāsāya vā na gacchantī aikaṃ āpattiṃ āpaj‍jati| pācittiyaṃ|

upausathampi  na pucchantī auvādampi na yācantī aikaṃ āpattiṃ āpaj‍jati| pācittiyaṃ|

pasākhai jātaṃ gaṇḍaṃ vā rudhitaṃ vā anapalaukaitvā saṅghaṃ vā gaṇaṃ vā purisaina saddhiṃ aikainaikā bhaidāpaintī dvai  āpattiyau āpaj‍jati| bhaidāpaiti, payaugai duk‍kaṭaṃ; bhin‍nai, āpatti pācittiyassa|

ārāmavaggau chaṭṭhau|

7. gabbhinīvaggau

237. gabbhiniṃ  vuṭṭhāpaintī dvai āpattiyau āpaj‍jati| vuṭṭhāpaiti, payaugai duk‍kaṭaṃ; vuṭṭhāpitai, āpatti pācittiyassa|

pāyantiṃ  vuṭṭhāpaintī dvai āpattiyau āpaj‍jati| vuṭṭhāpaiti, payaugai duk‍kaṭaṃ; vuṭṭhāpitai, āpatti pācittiyassa|

dvai vassāni chasu dhammaisu asikkhitasikkhaṃ sikkhamānaṃ vuṭṭhāpaintī dvai āpattiyau āpaj‍jati| vuṭṭhāpaiti, payaugai duk‍kaṭaṃ; vuṭṭhāpitai, āpatti pācittiyassa|

dvai vassāni chasu dhammaisu sikkhitasikkhaṃ sikkhamānaṃ saṅghaina asammataṃ vuṭṭhāpaintī dvai āpattiyau āpaj‍jati| vuṭṭhāpaiti, payaugai duk‍kaṭaṃ; vuṭṭhāpitai, āpatti pācittiyassa|

ūnadvādasavassaṃ gihigataṃ vuṭṭhāpaintī dvai āpattiyau āpaj‍jati| vuṭṭhāpaiti, payaugai duk‍kaṭaṃ; vuṭṭhāpitai, āpatti pācittiyassa|

paripuṇṇadvādasavassaṃ gihigataṃ dvai vassāni chasu dhammaisu  asikkhitasikkhaṃ vuṭṭhāpaintī dvai āpattiyau āpaj‍jati| vuṭṭhāpaiti, payaugai duk‍kaṭaṃ; vuṭṭhāpitai, āpatti pācittiyassa|

paripuṇṇadvādasavassaṃ gihigataṃ dvai vassāni chasu dhammaisu sikkhitasikkhaṃ saṅghaina asammataṃ vuṭṭhāpaintī dvai āpattiyau āpaj‍jati| vuṭṭhāpaiti, payaugai duk‍kaṭaṃ; vuṭṭhāpitai, āpatti pācittiyassa|

sahajīviniṃ vuṭṭhāpaitvā dvai vassāni naiva anuggaṇhantī nānuggaṇhāpaintī aikaṃ āpattiṃ āpaj‍jati| pācittiyaṃ|

vuṭṭhāpitaṃ pavattiniṃ dvai vassāni nānubandhantī aikaṃ āpattiṃ āpaj‍jati| pācittiyaṃ|

sahajīviniṃ vuṭṭhāpaitvā naiva vūpakāsaintī na vūpakāsāpaintī aikaṃ āpattiṃ āpaj‍jati| pācittiyaṃ|

gabbhinīvaggau sattamau|

8. kumārībhūtavaggau

238. ūnavīsativassaṃ  kumāribhūtaṃ vuṭṭhāpaintī dvai āpattiyau āpaj‍jati| vuṭṭhāpaiti, payaugai duk‍kaṭaṃ; vuṭṭhāpitai, āpatti pācittiyassa|

paripuṇṇavīsativassaṃ kumāribhūtaṃ dvai vassāni chasu dhammaisu asikkhitasikkhaṃ vuṭṭhāpaintī dvai āpattiyau āpaj‍jati| vuṭṭhāpaiti, payaugai duk‍kaṭaṃ; vuṭṭhāpitai, āpatti pācittiyassa|

paripuṇṇavīsativassaṃ kumāribhūtaṃ dvai vassāni chasu dhammaisu sikkhitasikkhaṃ saṅghaina asammataṃ vuṭṭhāpaintī dvai āpattiyau āpaj‍jati| vuṭṭhāpaiti, payaugai duk‍kaṭaṃ; vuṭṭhāpitai, āpatti pācittiyassa|

ūnadvādasavassā vuṭṭhāpaintī dvai āpattiyau āpaj‍jati| vuṭṭhāpaiti, payaugai duk‍kaṭaṃ; vuṭṭhāpitai, āpatti pācittiyassa|

paripuṇṇadvādasavassā saṅghaina asammatā vuṭṭhāpaintī dvai āpattiyau āpaj‍jati| vuṭṭhāpaiti, payaugai duk‍kaṭaṃ; vuṭṭhāpitai, āpatti pācittiyassa|

‘‘alaṃ tāva tai, ayyai, vuṭṭhāpitainā’’ti vuc‍camānā ‘‘sādhū’’ti paṭissuṇitvā pacchā khīyanadhammaṃ āpaj‍jantī dvai āpattiyau āpaj‍jati| khiyyati, payaugai duk‍kaṭaṃ; khiyyitai, āpatti pācittiyassa|

sikkhamānaṃ – ‘‘sacai mai tvaṃ, ayyai, cīvaraṃ dassasi, aivāhaṃ taṃ vuṭṭhāpaissāmī’’ti vatvā naiva vuṭṭhāpaintī na vuṭṭhāpanāya ussuk‍kaṃ karauntī aikaṃ āpattiṃ āpaj‍jati| pācittiyaṃ|

sikkhamānaṃ  – ‘‘sacai maṃ tvaṃ, ayyai, dvai vassāni anubandhissasi, aivāhaṃ taṃ vuṭṭhāpaissāmī’’ti vatvā naiva vuṭṭhāpaintī na vuṭṭhāpanāya ussuk‍kaṃ karauntī aikaṃ āpattiṃ āpaj‍jati| pācittiyaṃ|

purisasaṃsaṭṭhaṃ  kumārakasaṃsaṭṭhaṃ caṇḍiṃ saukāvāsaṃ sikkhamānaṃ vuṭṭhāpaintī dvai āpattiyau āpaj‍jati| vuṭṭhāpaiti, payaugai duk‍kaṭaṃ; vuṭṭhāpitai, āpatti pācittiyassa|

mātāpitūhi vā sāmikaina vā ananuñ‍ñātaṃ sikkhamānaṃ vuṭṭhāpaintī  dvai āpattiyau āpaj‍jati| vuṭṭhāpaiti, payaugai duk‍kaṭaṃ; vuṭṭhāpitai, āpatti pācittiyassa|

pārivāsikachandadānai  sikkhamānaṃ vuṭṭhāpaintī dvai āpattiyau āpaj‍jati| vuṭṭhāpaiti, payaugai duk‍kaṭaṃ; vuṭṭhāpitai, āpatti pācittiyassa|

anuvassaṃ vuṭṭhāpaintī dvai āpattiyau āpaj‍jati| vuṭṭhāpaiti, payaugai duk‍kaṭaṃ; vuṭṭhāpitai, āpatti pācittiyassa|

aikaṃ vassaṃ dvai vuṭṭhāpaintī dvai āpattiyau āpaj‍jati| vuṭṭhāpaiti, payaugai duk‍kaṭaṃ; vuṭṭhāpitai, āpatti pācittiyassa|

kumārībhūtavaggau aṭṭhamau|

9. chattupāhanavaggau

239. chattupāhanaṃ dhāraintī dvai āpattiyau āpaj‍jati| dhāraiti, payaugai duk‍kaṭaṃ; dhāritai, āpatti pācittiyassa|

yānaina yāyantī dvai āpattiyau āpaj‍jati| yāyati, payaugai duk‍kaṭaṃ; yāyitai, āpatti pācittiyassa|

saṅghāṇiṃ dhāraintī dvai āpattiyau āpaj‍jati| dhāraiti, payaugai duk‍kaṭaṃ; dhāritai, āpatti pācittiyassa|

itthālaṅkāraṃ dhāraintī dvai āpattiyau āpaj‍jati| dhāraiti, payaugai duk‍kaṭaṃ; dhāritai, āpatti pācittiyassa|

gandhavaṇṇakaina  nahāyantī dvai āpattiyau āpaj‍jati| nahāyati, payaugai duk‍kaṭaṃ; nahānapariyausānai, āpatti pācittiyassa|

vāsitakaina piñ‍ñākaina nahāyantī dvai āpattiyau āpaj‍jati| nahāyati, payaugai duk‍kaṭaṃ; nahānapariyausānai, āpatti pācittiyassa|

bhikkhuniyā ummaddāpaintī parimaddāpaintī dvai āpattiyau āpaj‍jati| ummaddāpaiti, payaugai duk‍kaṭaṃ; ummadditai, āpatti pācittiyassa|

sikkhamānāya ummaddāpaintī parimaddāpaintī dvai āpattiyau āpaj‍jati| ummaddāpaiti, payaugai duk‍kaṭaṃ; ummadditai, āpatti pācittiyassa|

sāmaṇairiyā  ummaddāpaintī parimaddāpaintī dvai āpattiyau āpaj‍jati| ummaddāpaiti, payaugai duk‍kaṭaṃ; ummadditai, āpatti pācittiyassa|

gihiniyā ummaddāpaintī parimaddāpaintī dvai āpattiyau āpaj‍jati| ummaddāpaiti, payaugai duk‍kaṭaṃ; ummadditai, āpatti pācittiyassa|

bhikkhussa puratau anāpucchā āsanai nisīdantī dvai āpattiyau āpaj‍jati| nisīdati, payaugai duk‍kaṭaṃ; nisin‍nai, āpatti pācittiyassa|

anaukāsakataṃ bhikkhuṃ pañhaṃ pucchantī dvai āpattiyau āpaj‍jati| pucchati, payaugai duk‍kaṭaṃ; pucchitai, āpatti pācittiyassa|

asaṅkac‍cikā gāmaṃ pavisantī dvai āpattiyau āpaj‍jati| paṭhamaṃ  pādaṃ parikkhaipaṃ atik‍kāmaiti, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa; dutiyaṃ pādaṃ atik‍kāmaiti, āpatti pācittiyassa|

chattupāhanavaggau navamau|

khuddakaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

5. pāṭidaisanīyakaṇḍaṃ

240. sappiṃ  viñ‍ñāpaitvā bhuñ‍jantī dvai āpattiyau āpaj‍jati| bhuñ‍jissāmīti paṭiggaṇhāti, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa; ajjhauhārai ajjhauhārai āpatti pāṭidaisanīyassa|

tailaṃ viñ‍ñāpaitvā bhuñ‍jantī dvai āpattiyau āpaj‍jati| bhuñ‍jissāmīti paṭiggaṇhāti, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa; ajjhauhārai ajjhauhārai āpatti pāṭidaisanīyassa|

madhuṃ viñ‍ñāpaitvā bhuñ‍jantī dvai āpattiyau āpaj‍jati| bhuñ‍jissāmīti paṭiggaṇhāti, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa; ajjhauhārai ajjhauhārai āpatti pāṭidaisanīyassa|

phāṇitaṃ viñ‍ñāpaitvā bhuñ‍jantī dvai āpattiyau āpaj‍jati| bhuñ‍jissāmīti paṭiggaṇhāti, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa; ajjhauhārai ajjhauhārai āpatti pāṭidaisanīyassa|
