macchaṃ  viñ‍ñāpaitvā bhuñ‍jantī dvai āpattiyau āpaj‍jati| bhuñ‍jissāmīti paṭiggaṇhāti, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa; ajjhauhārai ajjhauhārai āpatti pāṭidaisanīyassa|

maṃsaṃ viñ‍ñāpaitvā bhuñ‍jantī dvai āpattiyau āpaj‍jati| bhuñ‍jissāmīti  paṭiggaṇhāti, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa; ajjhauhārai ajjhauhārai āpatti pāṭidaisanīyassa|

khīraṃ viñ‍ñāpaitvā bhuñ‍jantī dvai āpattiyau āpaj‍jati| bhuñ‍jissāmīti paṭiggaṇhāti, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa; ajjhauhārai ajjhauhārai āpatti pāṭidaisanīyassa|

dadhiṃ viñ‍ñāpaitvā bhuñ‍jantī dvai āpattiyau āpaj‍jati| bhuñ‍jissāmīti paṭiggaṇhāti, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa; ajjhauhārai ajjhauhārai āpatti pāṭidaisanīyassa|

aṭṭha pāṭidaisanīyā niṭṭhitā|

katāpattivārau niṭṭhitau dutiyau|

3. vipattivārau

241. avassutā  bhikkhuniyā avassutassa purisapuggalassa kāyasaṃsaggaṃ sādiyantiyā āpattiyau catun‍naṃ vipattīnaṃ kati vipattiyau bhajanti? avassutāya bhikkhuniyā avassutassa purisapuggalassa kāyasaṃsaggaṃ sādiyantiyā āpattiyau catun‍naṃ vipattīnaṃ dvai vipattiyau bhajanti – siyā sīlavipattiṃ, siyā ācāravipattiṃ…pai॰…|

dadhiṃ viñ‍ñāpaitvā bhuñ‍jantiyā āpattiyau catun‍naṃ vipattīnaṃ kati vipattiyau bhajanti? dadhiṃ viñ‍ñāpaitvā bhuñ‍jantiyā āpattiyau catun‍naṃ vipattīnaṃ aikaṃ vipattiṃ bhajanti – ācāravipattiṃ|

vipattivārau niṭṭhitau tatiyau|

4. saṅgahavārau

242. avassutā  bhikkhuniyā avassutassa purisapuggalassa kāyasaṃsaggaṃ  sādiyantiyā āpattiyau sattan‍naṃ āpattikkhandhānaṃ katihi āpattikkhandhaihi saṅgahitā? avassutāya bhikkhuniyā avassutassa purisapuggalassa kāyasaṃsaggaṃ sādiyantiyā āpattiyau sattan‍naṃ āpattikkhandhānaṃ tīhi āpattikkhandhaihi saṅgahitā – siyā pārājikāpattikkhandhaina, siyā thul‍lac‍cayāpattikkhandhaina, siyā duk‍kaṭāpattikkhandhaina…pai॰…|

dadhiṃ viñ‍ñāpaitvā bhuñ‍jantiyā āpattiyau sattan‍naṃ āpattikkhandhānaṃ katihi āpattikkhandhaihi saṅgahitā? dadhiṃ viñ‍ñāpaitvā bhuñ‍jantiyā āpattiyau sattan‍naṃ āpattikkhandhānaṃ dvīhi āpattikkhandhaihi saṅgahitā – siyā pāṭidaisanīyāpattikkhandhaina, siyā duk‍kaṭāpattikkhandhaina|

saṅgahavārau niṭṭhitau catutthau|

5. samuṭṭhānavārau

243. avassutāya  bhikkhuniyā avassutassa purisapuggalassa kāyasaṃsaggaṃ sādiyantiyā āpattiyau chan‍naṃ āpattisamuṭṭhānānaṃ katihi samuṭṭhānaihi samuṭṭhanti? avassutāya bhikkhuniyā avassutassa purisapuggalassa kāyasaṃsaggaṃ sādiyantiyā āpattiyau chan‍naṃ āpattisamuṭṭhānānaṃ aikaina samuṭṭhānaina samuṭṭhanti – kāyatau ca cittatau ca samuṭṭhanti, na vācatau…pai॰…|

dadhiṃ viñ‍ñāpaitvā bhuñ‍jantiyā āpattiyau chan‍naṃ āpattisamuṭṭhānānaṃ katihi samuṭṭhānaihi samuṭṭhanti? dadhiṃ viñ‍ñāpaitvā  bhuñ‍jantiyā āpattiyau chan‍naṃ āpattisamuṭṭhānānaṃ catūhi samuṭṭhānaihi samuṭṭhanti – siyā kāyatau samuṭṭhanti, na vācatau na cittatau; siyā kāyatau ca vācatau ca samuṭṭhanti, na cittatau; siyā kāyatau ca cittatau ca samuṭṭhanti, na vācatau; siyā kāyatau ca vācatau ca cittatau ca samuṭṭhanti|

samuṭṭhānavārau niṭṭhitau pañ‍camau|

6. adhikaraṇavārau

244. avassutā  bhikkhuniyā avassutassa purisapuggalassa kāyasaṃsaggaṃ sādiyantiyā āpattiyau catun‍naṃ adhikaraṇānaṃ katamaṃ adhikaraṇaṃ? avassutāya bhikkhuniyā avassutassa purisapuggalassa kāyasaṃsaggaṃ sādiyantiyā āpattiyau catun‍naṃ adhikaraṇānaṃ – āpattādhikaraṇaṃ…pai॰…|

dadhiṃ viñ‍ñāpaitvā bhuñ‍jantiyā āpattiyau catun‍naṃ adhikaraṇānaṃ katamaṃ adhikaraṇaṃ? dadhiṃ viñ‍ñāpaitvā bhuñ‍jantiyā āpattiyau catun‍naṃ adhikaraṇānaṃ – āpattādhikaraṇaṃ|

adhikaraṇavārau niṭṭhitau chaṭṭhau|

7. samathavārau

245. avassutāya  bhikkhuniyā avassutassa purisapuggalassa kāyasaṃsaggaṃ sādiyantiyā āpattiyau sattan‍naṃ samathānaṃ katihi samathaihi sammanti? avassutāya bhikkhuniyā avassutassa purisapuggalassa kāyasaṃsaggaṃ sādiyantiyā āpattiyau sattan‍naṃ samathānaṃ tīhi samathaihi sammanti – siyā sammukhāvinayaina ca paṭiñ‍ñātakaraṇaina ca, siyā sammukhāvinayaina ca tiṇavatthārakaina ca…pai॰…|

dadhiṃ  viñ‍ñāpaitvā bhuñ‍jantiyā āpattiyau sattan‍naṃ samathānaṃ katihi samathaihi sammanti? dadhiṃ viñ‍ñāpaitvā bhuñ‍jantiyā āpattiyau sattan‍naṃ samathānaṃ tīhi samathaihi sammanti – siyā sammukhāvinayaina ca paṭiñ‍ñātakaraṇaina ca, siyā sammukhāvinayaina ca tiṇavatthārakaina ca|

samathavārau niṭṭhitau sattamau|

8. samuc‍cayavārau

246. avassutā  bhikkhunī avassutassa purisapuggalassa kāyasaṃsaggaṃ sādiyantī kati āpattiyau āpaj‍jati? avassutā bhikkhunī avassutassa purisapuggalassa  kāyasaṃsaggaṃ sādiyantī tissau āpattiyau āpaj‍jati| adhakkhakaṃ ubbhajāṇumaṇḍalaṃ gahaṇaṃ sādiyati, āpatti pārājikassa; ubbhakkhakaṃ adhaujāṇumaṇḍalaṃ gahaṇaṃ sādiyati, āpatti thul‍lac‍cayassa; kāyapaṭibaddhaṃ gahaṇaṃ sādiyati, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa – avassutā bhikkhunī avassutassa purisapuggalassa kāyasaṃsaggaṃ sādiyantī imā tissau āpattiyau āpaj‍jati|

tā  āpattiyau catun‍naṃ vipattīnaṃ kati vipattiyau bhajanti? sattan‍naṃ āpattikkhandhānaṃ katihi āpattikkhandhaihi saṅgahitā? chan‍naṃ āpattisamuṭṭhānānaṃ katihi samuṭṭhānaihi samuṭṭhanti? catun‍naṃ adhikaraṇānaṃ katamaṃ adhikaraṇaṃ? sattan‍naṃ samathānaṃ katihi samathaihi sammanti? tā āpattiyau catun‍naṃ vipattīnaṃ dvai vipattiyau bhajanti – siyā sīlavipattiṃ, siyā ācāravipattiṃ| sattan‍naṃ āpattikkhandhānaṃ tīhi āpattikkhandhaihi saṅgahitā – siyā  pārājikāpattikkhandhaina, siyā thul‍lac‍cayāpattikkhandhaina, siyā duk‍kaṭāpattikkhandhaina| chan‍naṃ āpattisamuṭṭhānānaṃ aikaina samuṭṭhānaina samuṭṭhanti – kāyatau ca cittatau ca samuṭṭhanti, na vācatau| catun‍naṃ adhikaraṇānaṃ āpattādhikaraṇaṃ| sattan‍naṃ samathānaṃ tīhi samathaihi sammanti – siyā sammukhāvinayaina ca paṭiñ‍ñātakaraṇaina ca, siyā sammukhāvinayaina ca tiṇavatthārakaina ca…pai॰…|

dadhiṃ viñ‍ñāpaitvā bhuñ‍jantī kati āpattiyau āpaj‍jati? dadhiṃ viñ‍ñāpaitvā bhuñ‍jantī dvai āpattiyau āpaj‍jati| bhuñ‍jissāmīti paṭiggaṇhāti, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa; ajjhauhārai ajjhauhārai āpatti pāṭidaisanīyassa – dadhiṃ viñ‍ñāpaitvā bhuñ‍jantī imā dvai āpattiyau āpaj‍jati|

tā āpattiyau catun‍naṃ vipattīnaṃ kati vipattiyau bhajanti? sattan‍naṃ āpattikkhandhānaṃ katihi āpattikkhandhaihi saṅgahitā? chan‍naṃ āpattisamuṭṭhānānaṃ katihi samuṭṭhānaihi samuṭṭhanti? catun‍naṃ adhikaraṇānaṃ katamaṃ adhikaraṇaṃ? sattan‍naṃ samathānaṃ katihi samathaihi sammanti? tā āpattiyau catun‍naṃ vipattīnaṃ aikaṃ vipattiṃ bhajanti – ācāravipattiṃ| sattan‍naṃ āpattikkhandhānaṃ dvīhi āpattikkhandhaihi saṅgahitā – siyā pāṭidaisanīyāpattikkhandhaina, siyā duk‍kaṭāpattikkhandhaina| chan‍naṃ āpattisamuṭṭhānānaṃ catūhi samuṭṭhānaihi samuṭṭhanti – siyā kāyatau samuṭṭhanti na vācatau na cittatau, siyā kāyatau ca vācatau ca samuṭṭhanti na cittatau, siyā  kāyatau  ca cittatau ca samuṭṭhanti na vācatau, siyā kāyatau ca vācatau ca cittatau ca samuṭṭhanti| catun‍naṃ adhikaraṇānaṃ – āpattādhikaraṇaṃ| sattan‍naṃ samathānaṃ tīhi samathaihi sammanti – siyā sammukhāvinayaina ca paṭiñ‍ñātakaraṇaina ca, siyā sammukhāvinayaina ca tiṇavatthārakaina ca|

samuc‍cayavārau niṭṭhitau aṭṭhamau|

1. katthapañ‍ñattivārau

1. pārājikakaṇḍaṃ

247. yaṃ  taina bhagavatā jānatā passatā arahatā sammāsambuddhaina kāyasaṃsaggaṃ sādiyanapac‍cayā pārājikaṃ kattha pañ‍ñattaṃ? kaṃ ārabbha? kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ…pai॰… kainābhatanti?

yaṃ taina bhagavatā jānatā passatā arahatā sammāsambuddhaina kāyasaṃsaggaṃ sādiyanapac‍cayā pārājikaṃ kattha pañ‍ñattanti? sāvatthiyaṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ| kaṃ ārabbhāti? sundarīnandaṃ bhikkhuniṃ ārabbha| kismiṃ vatthusminti? sundarīnandā bhikkhunī avassutā avassutassa purisapuggalassa kāyasaṃsaggaṃ sādiyi, tasmiṃ vatthusmiṃ| atthi tattha pañ‍ñatti, anupañ‍ñatti, anuppan‍napañ‍ñattīti? aikā pañ‍ñatti| anupañ‍ñatti anuppan‍napañ‍ñatti tasmiṃ natthi| sabbatthapañ‍ñatti, padaisapañ‍ñattīti? sabbatthapañ‍ñatti| sādhāraṇapañ‍ñatti, asādhāraṇapañ‍ñattīti? asādhāraṇapañ‍ñatti| aikataupañ‍ñatti, ubhataupañ‍ñattīti? aikataupañ‍ñatti| catun‍naṃ pātimaukkhuddaisānaṃ katthaugadhaṃ kattha pariyāpan‍nanti? nidānaugadhaṃ nidānapariyāpan‍naṃ? katamaina uddaisaina uddaisaṃ āgacchatīti? dutiyaina uddaisaina uddaisaṃ āgacchati| catun‍naṃ vipattīnaṃ katamā vipattīti? sīlavipatti| sattan‍naṃ āpattikkhandhānaṃ katamau āpattikkhandhauti? pārājikāpattikkhandhau| chan‍naṃ āpattisamuṭṭhānānaṃ katihi samuṭṭhānaihi samuṭṭhātīti? aikaina samuṭṭhānaina samuṭṭhāti – kāyatau ca cittatau ca samuṭṭhāti, na vācatau…pai॰… kainābhatanti? paramparābhataṃ –

upāli  dāsakau caiva, sauṇakau siggavau tathā|

mauggaliputtaina pañ‍camā, aitai jambusirivhayai|| …pai॰…|

aitai  nāgā mahāpañ‍ñā, vinayañ‍ñū maggakauvidā|

vinayaṃ dīpai pakāsaisuṃ, piṭakaṃ tambapaṇṇiyāti||

vaj‍jappaṭicchādanapac‍cayā  pārājikaṃ kattha pañ‍ñattanti? sāvatthiyaṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ| kaṃ ārabbhāti? thul‍lanandaṃ bhikkhuniṃ ārabbha| kismiṃ vatthusminti? thul‍lanandā bhikkhunī jānaṃ pārājikaṃ dhammaṃ ajjhāpan‍naṃ bhikkhuniṃ naivattanā paṭicaudaisi na gaṇassa āraucaisi, tasmiṃ vatthusmiṃ| aikā pañ‍ñatti| chan‍naṃ āpattisamuṭṭhānānaṃ aikaina samuṭṭhānaina samuṭṭhāti – dhuranikkhaipai…pai॰…|

yāvatatiyaṃ samanubhāsanāya na paṭinissaj‍janapac‍cayā pārājikaṃ kattha pañ‍ñattanti? sāvatthiyaṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ| kaṃ ārabbhāti? thul‍lanandaṃ bhikkhuniṃ ārabbha| kismiṃ vatthusminti? thul‍lanandā bhikkhunī samaggaina saṅghaina ukkhittaṃ ariṭṭhaṃ bhikkhuṃ gaddhabādhipubbaṃ anuvatti, tasmiṃ vatthusmiṃ| aikā pañ‍ñatti| chan‍naṃ āpattisamuṭṭhānānaṃ aikaina samuṭṭhānaina samuṭṭhāti – dhuranikkhaipai…pai॰…|

aṭṭhamaṃ vatthuṃ paripūraṇapac‍cayā pārājikaṃ kattha pañ‍ñattanti? sāvatthiyaṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ| kaṃ ārabbhāti? chabbaggiyā bhikkhuniyau ārabbha| kismiṃ vatthusminti? chabbaggiyā bhikkhuniyau aṭṭhamaṃ vatthuṃ paripūraisuṃ, tasmiṃ vatthusmiṃ| aikā pañ‍ñatti| chan‍naṃ āpattisamuṭṭhānānaṃ  aikaina samuṭṭhānaina samuṭṭhāti – dhuranikkhaipai…pai॰…|

pārājikā niṭṭhitā|

2. saṅghādisaisakaṇḍādi

248. yaṃ taina bhagavatā jānatā passatā arahatā sammāsambuddhaina ussayavādikāya bhikkhuniyā aḍḍaṃ karaṇapac‍cayā saṅghādisaisau kattha pañ‍ñattau? kaṃ ārabbha? kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ…pai॰… kainābhatanti?

yaṃ taina bhagavatā jānatā passatā arahatā sammāsambuddhaina ussayavādikāya bhikkhuniyā aḍḍaṃ karaṇapac‍cayā saṅghādisaisau kattha pañ‍ñattauti? sāvatthiyaṃ pañ‍ñattau| kaṃ ārabbhāti? thul‍lanandaṃ bhikkhuniṃ ārabbha| kismiṃ vatthusminti? thul‍lanandā bhikkhunī ussayavādikā vihari, tasmiṃ vatthusmiṃ| atthi tattha pañ‍ñatti, anupañ‍ñatti, anuppan‍napañ‍ñattīti? aikā pañ‍ñatti| anupañ‍ñatti anuppan‍napañ‍ñatti tasmiṃ natthi| sabbatthapañ‍ñatti, padaisapañ‍ñattīti? sabbatthapañ‍ñatti| sādhāraṇapañ‍ñatti, asādhāraṇapañ‍ñattīti? asādhāraṇapañ‍ñatti| aikataupañ‍ñatti, ubhataupañ‍ñattīti? aikataupañ‍ñatti| catun‍naṃ pātimaukkhuddaisānaṃ katthaugadhaṃ kattha pariyāpan‍nanti? nidānaugadhaṃ nidānapariyāpan‍naṃ| katamaina uddaisaina uddaisaṃ āgacchatīti? tatiyaina uddaisaina uddaisaṃ āgacchati| catun‍naṃ vipattīnaṃ katamā vipattīti? sīlavipatti| sattan‍naṃ āpattikkhandhānaṃ katamau āpattikkhandhauti? saṅghādisaisāpattikkhandhau| chan‍naṃ āpattisamuṭṭhānānaṃ katihi samuṭṭhānaihi samuṭṭhātīti? dvīhi samuṭṭhānaihi samuṭṭhāti – siyā kāyatau ca vācatau ca samuṭṭhāti, na cittatau; siyā kāyatau ca vācatau cittatau ca samuṭṭhāti…pai॰… kainābhatanti? paramparābhataṃ –

upāli dāsakau caiva, sauṇakau siggavau tathā|

mauggaliputtaina pañ‍camā, aitai jambusirivhayai|| …pai॰…|

aitai nāgā mahāpañ‍ñā, vinayañ‍ñū maggakauvidā|

vinayaṃ dīpai pakāsaisuṃ, piṭakaṃ tambapaṇṇiyāti||

cauriṃ  vuṭṭhāpanapac‍cayā saṅghādisaisau kattha pañ‍ñattauti? sāvatthiyaṃ pañ‍ñattau| kaṃ ārabbhāti? thul‍lanandaṃ bhikkhuniṃ ārabbha| kismiṃ vatthusminti? thul‍lanandā bhikkhunī cauriṃ vuṭṭhāpaisi, tasmiṃ vatthusmiṃ| aikā pañ‍ñatti| chan‍naṃ āpattisamuṭṭhānānaṃ dvīhi samuṭṭhānaihi samuṭṭhāti – siyā vācatau ca cittatau ca samuṭṭhāti, na kāyatau; siyā kāyatau ca vācatau ca cittatau ca samuṭṭhāti…pai॰…|

aikā gāmantaraṃ gamanapac‍cayā saṅghādisaisau kattha pañ‍ñattauti? sāvatthiyaṃ pañ‍ñattau| kaṃ ārabbhāti? añ‍ñataraṃ bhikkhuniṃ ārabbha| kismiṃ vatthusminti? añ‍ñatarā bhikkhunī aikā gāmantaraṃ gacchi, tasmiṃ vatthusmiṃ| aikā pañ‍ñatti, tissau anupañ‍ñattiyau| chan‍naṃ āpattisamuṭṭhānānaṃ aikaina samuṭṭhānaina samuṭṭhāti – paṭhamapārājikai…pai॰…|

samaggaina  saṅghaina ukkhittaṃ bhikkhuniṃ dhammaina vinayaina satthusāsanaina anapalaukaitvā kārakasaṅghaṃ  anañ‍ñāya gaṇassa chandaṃ ausāraṇapac‍cayā saṅghādisaisau kattha pañ‍ñattauti? sāvatthiyaṃ pañ‍ñattau| kaṃ ārabbhāti? thul‍lanandaṃ bhikkhuniṃ ārabbha| kismiṃ vatthusminti? thul‍lanandā bhikkhunī samaggaina saṅghaina ukkhittaṃ bhikkhuniṃ  dhammaina vinayaina satthusāsanaina anapalaukaitvā kārakasaṅghaṃ anañ‍ñāya gaṇassa chandaṃ ausāraisi, tasmiṃ vatthusmiṃ| aikā pañ‍ñatti| chan‍naṃ āpattisamuṭṭhānānaṃ aikaina samuṭṭhānaina samuṭṭhāti – dhuranikkhaipai…pai॰…|

avassutāya bhikkhuniyā avassutassa purisapuggalassa hatthatau khādanīyaṃ vā bhaujanīyaṃ vā sahatthā paṭiggahaitvā bhuñ‍janapac‍cayā saṅghādisaisau kattha pañ‍ñattauti? sāvatthiyaṃ pañ‍ñattau| kaṃ ārabbhāti? sundarīnandaṃ bhikkhuniṃ ārabbha| kismiṃ vatthusminti? sundarīnandā bhikkhunī avassutā avassutassa purisapuggalassa hatthatau āmisaṃ paṭiggahaisi, tasmiṃ vatthusmiṃ| aikā pañ‍ñatti| chan‍naṃ āpattisamuṭṭhānānaṃ aikaina samuṭṭhānaina samuṭṭhāti – paṭhamapārājikai…pai॰…|

‘‘kiṃ tai, ayyai, aisau purisapuggalau karissati avassutau vā anavassutau vā, yatau tvaṃ anavassutā! iṅgha, ayyai, yaṃ tai aisau purisapuggalau daiti khādanīyaṃ vā bhaujanīyaṃ vā taṃ tvaṃ sahatthā paṭiggahaitvā khāda vā bhuñ‍ja vā’’ti uyyaujanapac‍cayā saṅghādisaisau kattha pañ‍ñattauti? sāvatthiyaṃ pañ‍ñattau| kaṃ ārabbhāti? añ‍ñataraṃ bhikkhuniṃ ārabbha| kismiṃ vatthusminti? añ‍ñatarā bhikkhunī – ‘‘kiṃ tai, ayyai, aisau purisapuggalau karissati avassutau vā anavassutau vā, yatau tvaṃ anavassutā! iṅgha, ayyai, yaṃ tai aisau purisapuggalau daiti khādanīyaṃ vā bhaujanīyaṃ vā taṃ tvaṃ sahatthā paṭiggahaitvā khāda vā bhuñ‍ja vā’’ti uyyaujaisi, tasmiṃ  vatthusmiṃ| aikā pañ‍ñatti| chan‍naṃ āpattisamuṭṭhānānaṃ tīhi samuṭṭhānaihi samuṭṭhāti…pai॰…|

kupitāya anattamanāya bhikkhuniyā yāvatatiyaṃ samanubhāsanāya na paṭinissaj‍janapac‍cayā saṅghādisaisau kattha pañ‍ñattauti? sāvatthiyaṃ pañ‍ñattau| kaṃ ārabbhāti? caṇḍakāḷiṃ bhikkhuniṃ ārabbha| kismiṃ vatthusminti? caṇḍakāḷī bhikkhunī kupitā anattamanā aivaṃ avaca – ‘‘buddhaṃ pac‍cācikkhāmi, dhammaṃ pac‍cācikkhāmi, saṅghaṃ pac‍cācikkhāmi, sikkhaṃ pac‍cācikkhāmī’’ti, tasmiṃ vatthusmiṃ| aikā pañ‍ñatti| chan‍naṃ āpattisamuṭṭhānānaṃ aikaina samuṭṭhānaina samuṭṭhāti – dhuranikkhaipai…pai॰…|

kismiñ‍cidaiva  adhikaraṇai pac‍cākatāya bhikkhuniyā yāvatatiyaṃ samanubhāsanāya na paṭinissaj‍janapac‍cayā saṅghādisaisau kattha pañ‍ñattauti? sāvatthiyaṃ pañ‍ñattau| kaṃ ārabbhāti? caṇḍakāḷiṃ bhikkhuniṃ ārabbha| kismiṃ vatthusminti? caṇḍakāḷī bhikkhunī kismiñ‍cidaiva adhikaraṇai pac‍cākatā kupitā anattamanā aivaṃ avaca – ‘‘chandagāminiyau ca bhikkhuniyau, dausagāminiyau ca bhikkhuniyau, mauhagāminiyau ca bhikkhuniyau, bhayagāminiyau ca bhikkhuniyau’’ti, tasmiṃ vatthusmiṃ| aikā pañ‍ñatti| chan‍naṃ āpattisamuṭṭhānānaṃ aikaina samuṭṭhānaina samuṭṭhāti – dhuranikkhaipai…pai॰…|

saṃsaṭṭhānaṃ bhikkhunīnaṃ yāvatatiyaṃ samanubhāsanāya na paṭinissaj‍janapac‍cayā  saṅghādisaisau kattha pañ‍ñattauti? sāvatthiyaṃ pañ‍ñattau| kaṃ ārabbhāti? sambahulā bhikkhuniyau ārabbha| kismiṃ vatthusminti? sambahulā bhikkhuniyau saṃsaṭṭhā vihariṃsu, tasmiṃ vatthusmiṃ| aikā pañ‍ñatti| chan‍naṃ āpattisamuṭṭhānānaṃ aikaina samuṭṭhānaina samuṭṭhāti – dhuranikkhaipai…pai॰…|

‘‘saṃsaṭṭhā, ayyai, tumhai viharatha| mā tumhai nānā viharitthā’’ti uyyaujaintiyā yāvatatiyaṃ samanubhāsanāya na paṭinissaj‍janapac‍cayā saṅghādisaisau kattha pañ‍ñattauti? sāvatthiyaṃ pañ‍ñattau| kaṃ ārabbhāti? thul‍lanandaṃ bhikkhuniṃ ārabbha| kismiṃ vatthusminti? thul‍lanandā bhikkhunī – ‘‘saṃsaṭṭhāva ayyai, tumhai viharatha| mā tamhai nānā viharitthā’’ti uyyaujaisi, tasmiṃ vatthusmiṃ| aikā pañ‍ñatti| chan‍naṃ āpattisamuṭṭhānānaṃ aikaina samuṭṭhānaina samuṭṭhāti – dhuranikkhaipai…pai॰…|

dadhiṃ viñ‍ñāpaitvā bhuñ‍janapac‍cayā pāṭidaisanīyaṃ kattha pañ‍ñattanti? sāvatthiyaṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ| kaṃ ārabbhāti? chabbaggiyā bhikkhuniyau ārabbha| kismiṃ vatthusminti? chabbaggiyā bhikkhuniyau dadhiṃ viñ‍ñāpaitvā bhuñ‍jiṃsu, tasmiṃ vatthusmiṃ| aikā pañ‍ñatti, aikā anupañ‍ñatti| chan‍naṃ āpattisamuṭṭhānānaṃ catūhi samuṭṭhānaihi samuṭṭhāti…pai॰…|

katthapañ‍ñattivārau niṭṭhitau paṭhamau|

2. katāpattivārau

1. pārājikakaṇḍaṃ

249. kāyasaṃsaggaṃ  sādiyanapac‍cayā kati āpattiyau āpaj‍jati? kāyasaṃsaggaṃ sādiyanapac‍cayā pañ‍ca āpattiyau āpaj‍jati| avassutā bhikkhunī avassutassa purisapuggalassa adhakkhakaṃ ubbhajāṇumaṇḍalaṃ gahaṇaṃ sādiyati, āpatti pārājikassa; bhikkhu kāyaina kāyaṃ āmasati, āpatti saṅghādisaisassa; kāyaina kāyapaṭibaddhaṃ āmasati, āpatti thul‍lac‍cayassa; kāyapaṭibaddhaina kāyapaṭibaddhaṃ āmasati, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa; aṅgulipataudakai pācittiyaṃ – kāyasaṃsaggaṃ sādiyanapac‍cayā imā pañ‍ca āpattiyau āpaj‍jati|

vaj‍jappaṭicchādanapac‍cayā kati āpattiyau āpaj‍jati? vaj‍jappaṭicchādanapac‍cayā catassau āpattiyau āpaj‍jati| bhikkhunī jānaṃ pārājikaṃ dhammaṃ dhammaṃ ajjhāpan‍naṃ (syā॰) paṭicchādaiti, āpatti pārājikassa; vaimatikā paṭicchādaiti, āpatti thul‍lac‍cayassa; bhikkhu saṅghādisaisaṃ paṭicchādaiti, āpatti pācittiyassa; ācāravipattiṃ paṭicchādaiti, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa – vaj‍jappaṭicchādanapac‍cayā imā catassau āpattiyau āpaj‍jati|

yāvatatiyaṃ samanubhāsanāya na paṭinissaj‍janapac‍cayā kati āpattiyau āpaj‍jati? yāvatatiyaṃ samanubhāsanāya na paṭinissaj‍janapac‍cayā pañ‍ca āpattiyau āpaj‍jati| ukkhittānuvattikā bhikkhunī yāvatatiyaṃ  samanubhāsanāya na paṭinissaj‍jati, ñattiyā duk‍kaṭaṃ; dvīhi kammavācāhi thul‍lac‍cayā; kammavācāpariyausānai āpatti pārājikassa; bhaidakānuvattikā bhikkhunī yāvatatiyaṃ samanubhāsanāya na paṭinissaj‍jati, āpatti saṅghādisaisassa; pāpikāya diṭṭhiyā yāvatatiyaṃ samanubhāsanāya na paṭinissaj‍jati, āpatti pācittiyassa  – yāvatatiyaṃ samanubhāsanāya na paṭinissaj‍janapac‍cayā imā pañ‍ca āpattiyau āpaj‍jati|

aṭṭhamaṃ vatthuṃ paripūraṇapac‍cayā kati āpattiyau āpaj‍jati? aṭṭhamaṃ vatthuṃ paripūraṇapac‍cayā tissau āpattiyau āpaj‍jati| purisaina – ‘‘itthan‍nāmaṃ aukāsaṃ āgacchā’’ti vuttā gacchati, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa; purisassa  hatthapāsaṃ auk‍kantamattai āpatti thul‍lac‍cayassa; aṭṭhamaṃ vatthuṃ paripūraiti, āpatti pārājikassa – aṭṭhamaṃ vatthuṃ paripūraṇapac‍cayā imā tissau āpattiyau āpaj‍jati|

pārājikā niṭṭhitā|

2. saṅghādisaisakaṇḍādi

250. ussayavādikā bhikkhunī aḍḍaṃ karaṇapac‍cayā tissau āpattiyau āpaj‍jati| aikassa āraucaiti, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa; dutiyassa āraucaiti, āpatti thul‍lac‍cayassa; aḍḍapariyausānai āpatti saṅghādisaisassa|

cauriṃ vuṭṭhāpanapac‍cayā  tissau āpattiyau āpaj‍jati| ñattiyā duk‍kaṭaṃ; dvīhi kammavācāhi thul‍lac‍cayā; kammavācāpariyausānai  āpatti saṅghādisaisassa|

aikā gāmantaraṃ gamanapac‍cayā tissau āpattiyau āpaj‍jati| gacchati, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa; paṭhamaṃ pādaṃ parikkhaipaṃ atik‍kāmaiti, āpatti thul‍lac‍cayassa; dutiyaṃ pādaṃ atik‍kāmaiti, āpatti saṅghādisaisassa|

samaggaina saṅghaina ukkhittaṃ bhikkhuniṃ dhammaina vinayaina satthusāsanaina anapalaukaitvā kārakasaṅghaṃ anañ‍ñāya gaṇassa chandaṃ ausāraṇapac‍cayā tissau āpattiyau āpaj‍jati| ñattiyā duk‍kaṭaṃ; dvīhi kammavācāhi thul‍lac‍cayā; kammavācāpariyausānai āpatti saṅghādisaisassa|

avassutā bhikkhunī avassutassa purisapuggalassa hatthatau khādanīyaṃ vā bhaujanīyaṃ vā sahatthā  paṭiggahaitvā bhuñ‍janapac‍cayā tissau āpattiyau āpaj‍jati| ‘‘khādissāmi bhuñ‍jissāmī’’ti paṭiggaṇhāti, āpatti thul‍lac‍cayassa; ajjhauhārai ajjhauhārai āpatti saṅghādisaisassa; udakadantapaunaṃ paṭiggaṇhāti, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa|

‘‘kiṃ tai, ayyai, aisau purisapuggalau karissati avassutau vā anavassutau vā, yatau tvaṃ anavassutā! iṅgha, ayyai, yaṃ tai aisau purisapuggalau  daiti khādanīyaṃ vā bhaujanīyaṃ vā taṃ tvaṃ sahatthā paṭiggahaitvā khāda vā bhuñ‍ja vā’’ti, uyyaujanapac‍cayā tissau āpattiyau āpaj‍jati| tassā vacanaina khādissāmi bhuñ‍jissāmīti  paṭiggaṇhāti, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa; ajjhauhārai ajjhauhārai āpatti thul‍lac‍cayassa; bhaujanapariyausānai āpatti saṅghādisaisassa|

kupitā bhikkhunī yāvatatiyaṃ samanubhāsanāya na paṭinissaj‍janapac‍cayā tissau āpattiyau āpaj‍jati| ñattiyā duk‍kaṭaṃ; dvīhi kammavācāhi thul‍lac‍cayā; kammavācāpariyausānai āpatti saṅghādisaisassa|

kismiñ‍cidaiva adhikaraṇai pac‍cākatā bhikkhunī yāvatatiyaṃ samanubhāsanāya na paṭinissaj‍janapac‍cayā tissau āpattiyau āpaj‍jati| ñattiyā duk‍kaṭaṃ; dvīhi kammavācāhi thul‍lac‍cayā; kammavācāpariyausānai āpatti saṅghādisaisassa|

saṃsaṭṭhā bhikkhunī yāvatatiyaṃ samanubhāsanāya na paṭinissaj‍janapac‍cayā tissau āpattiyau āpaj‍jati| ñattiyā duk‍kaṭaṃ; dvīhi kammavācāhi thul‍lac‍cayā; kammavācāpariyausānai āpatti saṅghādisaisassa|

‘‘saṃsaṭṭhāva, ayyai, tumhai viharatha, mā tumhai nānā viharitthā’’ti uyyaujaintī yāvatatiyaṃ samanubhāsanāya na paṭinissaj‍janapac‍cayā tissau āpattiyau āpaj‍jati| ñattiyā duk‍kaṭaṃ; dvīhi kammavācāhi thul‍lac‍cayā; kammavācāpariyausānai āpatti saṅghādisaisassa|

dasa saṅghādisaisā niṭṭhitā…pai॰…|

(yathā haiṭṭhā tathā vitthāraitabbā pac‍cayamaiva nānākaraṇaṃ)

dadhiṃ  viñ‍ñāpaitvā bhuñ‍janapac‍cayā kati āpattiyau āpaj‍jati? dadhiṃ viñ‍ñāpaitvā bhuñ‍janapac‍cayā dvai āpattiyau āpaj‍jati| bhuñ‍jissāmīti paṭiggaṇhāti, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa; ajjhauhārai ajjhauhārai āpatti pāṭidaisanīyassa – dadhiṃ viñ‍ñāpaitvā bhuñ‍janapac‍cayā imā dvai āpattiyau āpaj‍jati|

katāpattivārau niṭṭhitau dutiyau|

3. vipattivārau

251. kāyasaṃsaggaṃ  sādiyanapac‍cayā āpattiyau catun‍naṃ vipattīnaṃ kati vipattiyau bhajanti? kāyasaṃsaggaṃ sādiyanapac‍cayā āpattiyau catun‍naṃ vipattīnaṃ dvai vipattiyau bhajanti – siyā sīlavipattiṃ, siyā ācāravipattiṃ…pai॰… dadhiṃ viñ‍ñāpaitvā bhuñ‍janapac‍cayā āpattiyau catun‍naṃ vipattīnaṃ kati vipattiyau bhajanti? dadhiṃ viñ‍ñāpaitvā bhuñ‍janapac‍cayā āpattiyau catun‍naṃ vipattīnaṃ aikaṃ vipattiṃ bhajanti – ācāravipattiṃ|

vipattivārau niṭṭhitau tatiyau|

4. saṅgahavārau

252. kāyasaṃsaggaṃ  sādiyanapac‍cayā āpattiyau sattan‍naṃ āpattikkhandhānaṃ  katihi āpattikkhandhaihi saṅgahitā? kāyasaṃsaggaṃ sādiyanapac‍cayā āpattiyau sattan‍naṃ āpattikkhandhānaṃ pañ‍cahi āpattikkhandhaihi saṅgahitā – siyā pārājikāpattikkhandhaina, siyā saṅghādisaisāpattikkhandhaina, siyā thul‍lac‍cayāpattikkhandhaina, siyā pācittiyāpattikkhandhaina, siyā duk‍kaṭāpattikkhandhaina…pai॰…|

dadhiṃ viñ‍ñāpaitvā  bhuñ‍janapac‍cayā āpattiyau sattan‍naṃ āpattikkhandhānaṃ katihi āpattikkhandhaihi saṅgahitā? dadhiṃ viñ‍ñāpaitvā bhuñ‍janapac‍cayā āpattiyau sattan‍naṃ āpattikkhandhānaṃ dvīhi āpattikkhandhaihi saṅgahitā – siyā pāṭidaisanīyāpattikkhandhaina, siyā duk‍kaṭāpattikkhandhaina|

saṅgahavārau niṭṭhitau catutthau|

5. samuṭṭhānavārau

253. kāyasaṃsaggaṃ  sādiyanapac‍cayā āpattiyau chan‍naṃ āpattisamuṭṭhānānaṃ katihi samuṭṭhānaihi samuṭṭhanti? kāyasaṃsaggaṃ sādiyanapac‍cayā āpattiyau chan‍naṃ āpattisamuṭṭhānānaṃ aikaina samuṭṭhānaina samuṭṭhanti – kāyatau ca cittatau ca samuṭṭhanti, na vācatau…pai॰…|

dadhiṃ  viñ‍ñāpaitvā bhuñ‍janapac‍cayā āpattiyau chan‍naṃ āpattisamuṭṭhānānaṃ katihi samuṭṭhānaihi samuṭṭhanti? dadhiṃ viñ‍ñāpaitvā bhuñ‍janapac‍cayā āpattiyau chan‍naṃ āpattisamuṭṭhānānaṃ catūhi samuṭṭhānaihi samuṭṭhanti – siyā kāyatau samuṭṭhanti, na vācatau na cittatau; siyā kāyatau ca vācatau ca samuṭṭhanti, na cittatau; siyā kāyatau ca cittatau ca samuṭṭhanti, na vācatau; siyā kāyatau ca vācatau ca cittatau ca samuṭṭhanti|

samuṭṭhānavārau niṭṭhitau pañ‍camau|

6. adhikaraṇavārau

254. kāyasaṃsaggaṃ  sādiyanapac‍cayā āpattiyau catun‍naṃ adhikaraṇānaṃ katamaṃ adhikaraṇaṃ? kāyasaṃsaggaṃ sādiyanapac‍cayā āpattiyau catun‍naṃ  adhikaraṇānaṃ, āpattādhikaraṇaṃ…pai॰…|

dadhiṃ viñ‍ñāpaitvā bhuñ‍janapac‍cayā āpattiyau catun‍naṃ adhikaraṇānaṃ katamaṃ adhikaraṇaṃ? dadhiṃ viñ‍ñāpaitvā bhuñ‍janapac‍cayā āpattiyau catun‍naṃ adhikaraṇānaṃ – āpattādhikaraṇaṃ|

adhikaraṇavārau niṭṭhitau chaṭṭhau|

7. samathavārau

255. kāyasaṃsaggaṃ  sādiyanapac‍cayā āpattiyau sattan‍naṃ samathānaṃ katihi samathaihi sammanti? kāyasaṃsaggaṃ sādiyanapac‍cayā āpattiyau sattan‍naṃ samathānaṃ tīhi samathaihi sammanti – siyā sammukhāvinayaina ca paṭiñ‍ñātakaraṇaina ca, siyā sammukhāvinayaina ca tiṇavatthārakaina ca…pai॰…|

dadhiṃ viñ‍ñāpaitvā bhuñ‍janapac‍cayā āpattiyau sattan‍naṃ samathānaṃ katihi samathaihi sammanti? dadhiṃ viñ‍ñāpaitvā bhuñ‍janapac‍cayā āpattiyau sattan‍naṃ samathānaṃ tīhi samathaihi sammanti – siyā sammukhāvinayaina ca paṭiñ‍ñātakaraṇaina ca, siyā sammukhāvinayaina ca tiṇavatthārakaina ca|

samathavārau niṭṭhitau sattamau|

8. samuc‍cayavārau

256. kāyasaṃsaggaṃ  sādiyanapac‍cayā kati āpattiyau āpaj‍jati? kāyasaṃsaggaṃ sādiyanapac‍cayā pañ‍ca āpattiyau āpaj‍jati| avassutā bhikkhunī avassutassa purisapuggalassa adhakkhakaṃ ubbhajāṇumaṇḍalaṃ gahaṇaṃ sādiyati, āpatti pārājikassa; bhikkhu kāyaina kāyaṃ āmasati, āpatti saṅghādisaisassa; kāyaina kāyapaṭibaddhaṃ āmasati, āpatti thul‍lac‍cayassa; kāyapaṭibaddhaina kāyapaṭibaddhaṃ āmasati, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa; aṅgulipataudakai pācittiyaṃ – kāyasaṃsaggaṃ sādiyanapac‍cayā imā pañ‍ca āpattiyau āpaj‍jati|

tā  āpattiyau catun‍naṃ vipattīnaṃ kati vipattiyau bhajanti? sattan‍naṃ āpattikkhandhānaṃ katihi āpattikkhandhaihi saṅgahitā? chan‍naṃ āpattisamuṭṭhānānaṃ katihi samuṭṭhānaihi samuṭṭhanti? catun‍naṃ adhikaraṇānaṃ katamaṃ adhikaraṇaṃ? sattan‍naṃ samathānaṃ katihi samathaihi sammanti? tā āpattiyau catun‍naṃ vipattīnaṃ dvai vipattiyau bhajanti – siyā sīlavipattiṃ, siyā ācāravipattiṃ| sattan‍naṃ āpattikkhandhānaṃ pañ‍cahi āpattikkhandhaihi saṅgahitā – siyā pārājikāpattikkhandhaina, siyā saṅghādisaisāpattikkhandhaina, siyā thul‍lac‍cayāpattikkhandhaina, siyā pācittiyāpattikkhandhaina, siyā duk‍kaṭāpattikkhandhaina| chan‍naṃ āpattisamuṭṭhānānaṃ aikaina samuṭṭhānaina samuṭṭhanti – kāyatau ca cittatau ca samuṭṭhanti, na vācatau| catun‍naṃ adhikaraṇānaṃ āpattādhikaraṇaṃ| sattan‍naṃ samathānaṃ tīhi samathaihi sammanti – siyā sammukhāvinayaina ca paṭiñ‍ñātakaraṇaina ca, siyā sammukhāvinayaina ca tiṇavatthārakaina ca…pai॰…|

dadhiṃ viñ‍ñāpaitvā bhuñ‍janapac‍cayā kati āpattiyau āpaj‍jati? dadhiṃ viñ‍ñāpaitvā bhuñ‍janapac‍cayā dvai āpattiyau āpaj‍jati| bhuñ‍jissāmīti paṭiggaṇhāti, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa; ajjhauhārai  ajjhauhārai āpatti pāṭidaisanīyassa – dadhiṃ viñ‍ñāpaitvā bhuñ‍janapac‍cayā imā dvai āpattiyau āpaj‍jati|

tā āpattiyau catun‍naṃ vipattīnaṃ kati vipattiyau bhajanti? sattan‍naṃ āpattikkhandhānaṃ katihi āpattikkhandhaihi saṅgahitā? chan‍naṃ āpattisamuṭṭhānānaṃ katihi samuṭṭhānaihi samuṭṭhanti? catun‍naṃ adhikaraṇānaṃ katamaṃ adhikaraṇaṃ? sattan‍naṃ samathānaṃ katihi samathaihi sammanti? tā āpattiyau catun‍naṃ vipattīnaṃ aikaṃ vipattiṃ bhajanti – ācāravipattiṃ| sattan‍naṃ āpattikkhandhānaṃ dvīhi āpattikkhandhaihi saṅgahitā – siyā pāṭidaisanīyāpattikkhandhaina, siyā duk‍kaṭāpattikkhandhaina| chan‍naṃ āpattisamuṭṭhānānaṃ catūhi samuṭṭhānaihi samuṭṭhanti – siyā kāyatau samuṭṭhanti, na vācatau na cittatau; siyā kāyatau ca vācatau ca samuṭṭhanti, na cittatau; siyā kāyatau ca cittatau ca samuṭṭhanti na vācatau; siyā kāyatau ca vācatau ca cittatau ca samuṭṭhanti| catun‍naṃ adhikaraṇānaṃ, āpattādhikaraṇaṃ| sattan‍naṃ samathānaṃ tīhi samathaihi sammanti – siyā sammukhāvinayaina ca paṭiñ‍ñātakaraṇaina ca, siyā sammukhāvinayaina ca tiṇavatthārakaina cāti|

samuc‍cayavārau niṭṭhitau aṭṭhamau| imassānantaraṃ paurāṇasīhaḷapautthakai aivampi§dissati –§‘‘taisamuddānaṃ –§kativārau ca vipattivārau ca, saṅgahavārau ca samuṭṭhānavārau| adhikaraṇavārau ca samathavārau ca, samuc‍cayavārau ca imai satta vārā| āditau pañ‍ñattivāraina saha aṭṭhavārāti’’iti|

aṭṭha pac‍cayavārā niṭṭhitā|

bhikkhunīvibhaṅgai sauḷasa mahāvārā niṭṭhitā|



samuṭṭhānasīsasaṅkhaipau

samuṭṭhānassuddānaṃ

257.

 anic‍cā  sabbai saṅkhārā, dukkhānattā ca saṅkhatā|

nibbānañ‍caiva pañ‍ñatti, anattā iti nicchayā||

buddhacandai anuppan‍nai, buddhādic‍cai anuggatai|

taisaṃ sabhāgadhammānaṃ, nāmamattaṃ na nāyati||

duk‍karaṃ vividhaṃ katvā, pūrayitvāna pāramī|

uppaj‍janti mahāvīrā, cakkhubhūtā sabrahmakai||

tai daisayanti saddhammaṃ, dukkhahāniṃ sukhāvahaṃ|

aṅgīrasau sakyamuni, sabbabhūtānukampakau||

sabbasattuttamau sīhau, piṭakai tīṇi daisayi|

suttantamabhidhammañ‍ca, vinayañ‍ca mahāguṇaṃ||

aivaṃ nīyati saddhammau, vinayau yadi tiṭṭhati|

ubhatau ca vibhaṅgāni, khandhakā yā ca mātikā||

mālā suttaguṇainaiva, parivāraina ganthitā|

tassaiva parivārassa, samuṭṭhānaṃ niyatau kataṃ||

sambhaidaṃ  nidānaṃ cañ‍ñaṃ, suttai dissanti upari|

tasmā sikkhai parivāraṃ, dhammakāmau supaisalauti||

tairasasamuṭṭhānaṃ

vibhaṅgai dvīsu pañ‍ñattaṃ, uddisanti upausathai|

pavakkhāmi samuṭṭhānaṃ, yathāñāyaṃ suṇātha mai||

pārājikaṃ  yaṃ paṭhamaṃ, dutiyañ‍ca tatau paraṃ|

sañ‍carittānubhāsanañ‍ca, atiraikañ‍ca cīvaraṃ||

laumāni padasaudhammau, bhūtaṃ saṃvidhānaina ca|

thaiyyadaisanacaurī ca, ananuñ‍ñātāya tairasa||

tairasaitai samuṭṭhāna nayā, viñ‍ñūhi cintitā||

aikaikasmiṃ samuṭṭhānai, sadisā idha dissarai||

1. paṭhamapārājikasamuṭṭhānaṃ

258.

 maithunaṃ  suk‍kasaṃsaggau, aniyatā paṭhamikā|

pubbūpaparipācitā, rahau bhikkhuniyā saha||

sabhaujanai rahau dvai ca, aṅguli udakai hasaṃ|

pahārai  uggirai caiva, taipañ‍ñāsā ca saikhiyā||

adhakkhagāmāvassutā, talamaṭṭhañ‍ca suddhikā|

vassaṃvuṭṭhā ca auvādaṃ, nānubandhai pavattiniṃ||

chasattati imai sikkhā, kāyamānasikā katā|

sabbai aikasamuṭṭhānā, paṭhamaṃ pārājikaṃ yathā||

paṭhamapārājikasamuṭṭhānaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

2. dutiyapārājikasamuṭṭhānaṃ

259.

 adin‍naṃ  viggahuttari, duṭṭhul‍lā attakāminaṃ|

amūlā añ‍ñabhāgiyā, aniyatā dutiyikā||

acchindai pariṇāmanai, musā aumasapaisuṇā|

duṭṭhul‍lā pathavīkhaṇai, bhūtaṃ añ‍ñāya ujjhāpai||

nik‍kaḍḍhanaṃ  siñ‍canañ‍ca, āmisahaitu bhuttāvī|

aihi anādari bhiṃsā, apanidhai ca jīvitaṃ||

jānaṃ sappāṇakaṃ kammaṃ, ūnasaṃvāsanāsanā|

sahadhammikavilaikhā, mauhau amūlakaina ca||

kuk‍kuc‍caṃ dhammikaṃ cīvaraṃ, datvā kuk‍kuc‍caṃ dhammikaṃ cīvaraṃ (sī॰), kuk‍kuc‍caṃ dhammikaṃ datvā (syā॰) pariṇāmaiyya puggalai|

kiṃ tai akālaṃ acchindai, duggahī nirayaina ca||

gaṇaṃ vibhaṅgaṃ dubbalaṃ, kathināphāsupassayaṃ|

ak‍kausacaṇḍī maccharī, gabbhinī ca pāyantiyā||

dvaivassaṃ  sikkhā saṅghaina, tayau caiva gihīgatā|

kumāribhūtā tissau ca, ūnadvādasasammatā||

alaṃ tāva saukāvāsaṃ, chandā anuvassā ca dvai|

sikkhāpadā sattatimai, samuṭṭhānā tikā katā||

kāyacittaina na vācā, vācācittaṃ na kāyikaṃ|

tīhi dvāraihi jāyanti, pārājikaṃ dutiyaṃ yathā||

dutiyapārājikasamuṭṭhānaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

3. sañ‍carittasamuṭṭhānaṃ

260.

 sañ‍carī  kuṭi vihārau, dhauvanañ‍ca paṭiggahau|

viñ‍ñattuttari abhihaṭṭhuṃ, ubhin‍naṃ dūtakaina ca||

kausiyā suddhadvaibhāgā, chabbassāni nisīdanaṃ|

riñ‍canti rūpikā caiva, ubhau nānappakārakā||

ūnabandhanavassikā, suttaṃ vikappanaina ca|

dvāradānasibbāni  dvāradānasibbinī (sī॰ syā॰) ca, pūvapac‍cayajauti ca||

ratanaṃ sūci mañ‍cau ca, tūlaṃ nisīdanakaṇḍu ca|

vassikā ca sugataina, viñ‍ñatti añ‍ñaṃ caitāpanā||

dvai saṅghikā mahājanikā, dvai puggalalahukā garu|

dvai vighāsā sāṭikā ca, samaṇacīvaraina ca||

samapañ‍ñāsimai dhammā, chahi ṭhānaihi jāyarai|

kāyatau na vācācittā, vācatau na kāyamanā||

kāyavācā na ca cittā na cittatau (sī॰ syā॰), kāyacittā na vācikā na vācatau (sī॰ syā॰)|

vācācittā  na kāyaina, tīhi dvāraihi tīhi ṭhānaihi (syā॰) jāyarai||

chasamuṭṭhānikā aitai, sañ‍carittaina sādisā||

sañ‍carittasamuṭṭhānaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

4. samanubhāsanāsamuṭṭhānaṃ

261.

 bhaidānuvattadubbaca, dūsaduṭṭhul‍ladiṭṭhi ca|

chandaṃ uj‍jagghikā dvai ca, dvai ca saddā na byāharai||

chamā nīcāsanai ṭhānaṃ, pacchatau uppathaina ca|

vaj‍jānuvattigahaṇā, ausārai pac‍cācikkhanā||

kismiṃ saṃsaṭṭhā dvai vadhi, visibbai dukkhitāya ca|

puna saṃsaṭṭhā na vūpasamai, ārāmañ‍ca pavāraṇā||

anvaddhaṃ anvaddhamāsaṃ (sī॰ syā॰) saha jīviniṃ, dvai cīvaraṃ anubandhanā|

sattatiṃsa  imai dhammā, kāyavācāya cittatau||

sabbai aikasamuṭṭhānā, samanubhāsanā yathā||

samanubhāsanāsamuṭṭhānaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

5. kathinasamuṭṭhānaṃ

262.

 ubbhataṃ kathinaṃ tīṇi, paṭhamaṃ pattabhaisaj‍jaṃ|

ac‍caikaṃ cāpi sāsaṅkaṃ, pak‍kamantaina vā duvai||

upassayaṃ paramparā, anatirittaṃ nimantanā|

vikappaṃ rañ‍ñau vikālai, vausāsārañ‍ñakaina ca||

ussayāsan‍nicayañ‍ca, purai pacchā vikālai ca|

pañ‍cāhikā saṅkamanī, dvaipi āvasathaina ca||

pasākhai  āsanai caiva, tiṃsa aikūnakā imai|

kāyavācā na ca cittā na cittatau (syā॰), tīhi dvāraihi jāyarai||

dvisamuṭṭhānikā sabbai, kathinaina sahāsamā||

kathinasamuṭṭhānaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

6. aiḷakalaumasamuṭṭhānaṃ

263.

 aiḷakalaumā  dvai saiyyā, āhac‍ca piṇḍabhaujanaṃ|

gaṇavikālasan‍nidhi, dantapaunaina cailakā||

uyyuttaṃ sainaṃ uyyuttaṃ vasai (syā॰) uyyaudhi, surā auraina nhāyanā|

dubbaṇṇai dvai daisanikā, lasuṇupatiṭṭhai nac‍canā||

nhānamattharaṇaṃ saiyyā, antauraṭṭhai tathā bahi|

antauvassaṃ  cittāgāraṃ, āsandi suttakantanā||

vaiyyāvac‍caṃ sahatthā ca, abhikkhukāvāsaina ca|

chattaṃ yānañ‍ca saṅghāṇiṃ, alaṅkāraṃ gandhavāsitaṃ||

bhikkhunī sikkhamānā ca, sāmaṇairī gihiniyā|

asaṃkac‍cikā āpatti, cattārīsā catuttari||

kāyaina na vācācittaina, kāyacittaina na vācatau|

dvisamuṭṭhānikā sabbai, samā aiḷakalaumikāti||

aiḷakalaumasamuṭṭhānaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

7. padasaudhammasamuṭṭhānaṃ

264.

 padañ‍ñatra  asammatā, tathā atthaṅgataina ca|

tiracchānavij‍jā  dvai vuttā, anaukāsau anaukāsai (sī॰ syā॰) ca pucchanā||

satta sikkhāpadā aitai, vācā na kāyacittatau kāyacittakā (ka॰)|

vācācittaina jāyanti, na tu kāyaina jāyarai||

dvisamuṭṭhānikā sabbai, padasaudhammasadisā||

padasaudhammasamuṭṭhānaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

8. addhānasamuṭṭhānaṃ

265.

 addhānanāvaṃ  paṇītaṃ, mātugāmaina saṃharai|

dhañ‍ñaṃ nimantitā caiva, aṭṭha ca pāṭidaisanī||

sikkhā pan‍narasa aitai, kāyā na vācā na manā|

kāyavācāhi jāyanti, na tai cittaina jāyarai||

kāyacittaina jāyanti, na tai jāyanti vācatau|

kāyavācāhi cittaina, samuṭṭhānā catubbidhā||

pañ‍ñattā  buddhañāṇaina, addhānaina sahā samā samānayā (syā॰)||

addhānasamuṭṭhānaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

9. thaiyyasatthasamuṭṭhānaṃ

266.

 thaiyyasatthaṃ  upassuti, sūpaviñ‍ñāpanaina ca|

rattichan‍nañ‍ca aukāsaṃ, aitai byūhaina sattamā||

kāyacittaina jāyanti, na tai jāyanti vācatau|

tīhi dvāraihi jāyanti, dvisamuṭṭhānikā imai||

thaiyyasatthasamuṭṭhānā, daisitādic‍cabandhunā||

thaiyyasatthasamuṭṭhānaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

10. dhammadaisanāsamuṭṭhānaṃ

267.

 chattapāṇissa saddhammaṃ, na daisainti tathāgatā|

aivamaiva tathaiva (sī॰ syā॰) daṇḍapāṇissa, satthaāvudhapāṇinaṃ||

pādukupāhanā yānaṃ, saiyyapal‍latthikāya ca|

vaiṭhitauguṇṭhitau caiva, aikādasamanūnakā||

vācācittaina jāyanti, na tai jāyanti kāyatau|

sabbai aikasamuṭṭhānā, samakā dhammadaisanai||

dhammadaisanāsamuṭṭhānaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

11. bhūtāraucanasamuṭṭhānaṃ

268.

 bhūtaṃ  kāyaina jāyati, na vācā na ca cittatau|

vācatau ca samuṭṭhāti, na kāyā na ca cittatau||

kāyavācāya  jāyati, na tu jāyati cittatau|

bhūtāraucanakā  nāma, tīhi ṭhānaihi jāyati||

bhūtāraucanasamuṭṭhānaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

12. caurivuṭṭhāpanasamuṭṭhānaṃ

269.

 caurī vācāya cittaina, na taṃ jāyati kāyatau|

jāyati tīhi dvāraihi, caurivuṭṭhāpanaṃ idaṃ|

akataṃ dvisamuṭṭhānaṃ, dhammarājaina bhāsitaṃ ṭhapitaṃ (syā॰)||

caurivuṭṭhāpanasamuṭṭhānaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

13. ananuñ‍ñātasamuṭṭhānaṃ

270.

 ananuñ‍ñātaṃ vācāya, na kāyā na ca cittatau|

jāyati kāyavācāya, na taṃ jāyati cittatau||

jāyati vācācittaina, na taṃ jāyati kāyatau|

jāyati  tīhi dvāraihi, akataṃ catuṭhānikaṃ||

ananuñ‍ñātasamuṭṭhānaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

samuṭṭhānañhi saṅkhaipaṃ, dasa tīṇi sudaisitaṃ|

asammauhakaraṃ ṭhānaṃ, naittidhammānulaumikaṃ|

dhārayantau imaṃ viñ‍ñū, samuṭṭhānai na muyhatīti||

samuṭṭhānasīsasaṅkhaipau niṭṭhitau|



antarapaiyyālaṃ

katipucchāvārau

271. kati  āpattiyau? kati āpattikkhandhā? kati vinītavatthūni? kati agāravā? kati gāravā? kati vinītavatthūni? kati vipattiyau? kati āpattisamuṭṭhānā? kati vivādamūlāni? kati anuvādamūlāni? kati sāraṇīyā dhammā? kati bhaidakaravatthūni? kati adhikaraṇāni? kati samathā?

pañ‍ca āpattiyau| pañ‍ca āpattikkhandhā| pañ‍ca vinītavatthūni| satta āpattiyau| satta āpattikkhandhā| satta vinītavatthūni| cha agāravā| cha gāravā| cha vinītavatthūni| catassau vipattiyau| cha āpattisamuṭṭhānā| cha vivādamūlāni| cha anuvādamūlāni| cha sāraṇīyā dhammā| aṭṭhārasa bhaidakaravatthūni| cattāri adhikaraṇāni| satta samathā|

tattha katamā pañ‍ca āpattiyau? pārājikāpatti, saṅghādisaisāpatti, pācittiyāpatti, pāṭidaisanīyāpatti, duk‍kaṭāpatti – imā pañ‍ca āpattiyau|

tattha katamai pañ‍ca āpattikkhandhā? pārājikāpattikkhandhau, saṅghādisaisāpattikkhandhau, pācittiyāpattikkhandhau, pāṭidaisanīyāpattikkhandhau, duk‍kaṭāpattikkhandhau – imai pañ‍ca āpattikkhandhā|

tattha  katamāni pañ‍ca vinītavatthūni? pañ‍cahi āpattikkhandhaihi ārati virati paṭivirati vairamaṇī akiriyā akaraṇaṃ anajjhāpatti vailāanatik‍kamau saitughātau – imāni pañ‍ca vinītavatthūni|

tattha  katamā satta āpattiyau? pārājikāpatti, saṅghādisaisāpatti, thul‍lac‍cayāpatti, pācittiyāpatti, pāṭidaisanīyāpatti, duk‍kaṭāpatti, dubbhāsitāpatti – imā satta āpattiyau|

tattha katamai satta āpattikkhandhā? pārājikāpattikkhandhau, saṅghādisaisāpattikkhandhau, thul‍lac‍cayāpattikkhandhau, pācittiyāpattikkhandhau, pāṭidaisanīyāpattikkhandhau, duk‍kaṭāpattikkhandhau, dubbhāsitāpattikkhandhau – imai satta āpattikkhandhā|

tattha  katamāni satta vinītavatthūni? sattahi āpattikkhandhaihi ārati virati paṭivirati vairamaṇī akiriyā akaraṇaṃ anajjhāpatti vailāanatik‍kamau saitughātau – imāni satta vinītavatthūni|

tattha  katamai cha agāravā? buddhai agāravau, dhammai agāravau, saṅghai agāravau, sikkhāya agāravau, appamādai agāravau, paṭisandhārai agāravau – imai cha agāravā|

tattha katamai cha gāravā? buddhai gāravau, dhammai gāravau, saṅghai gāravau, sikkhāya gāravau, appamādai gāravau, paṭisandhārai gāravau – imai cha gāravā|

tattha katamāni cha vinītavatthūni? chahi agāravaihi ārati virati paṭivirati  vairamaṇī akiriyā akaraṇaṃ anajjhāpatti vailāanatik‍kamau saitughātau – imāni cha vinītavatthūni|

tattha katamā catassau vipattiyau? sīlavipatti, ācāravipatti, diṭṭhivipatti, ājīvavipatti – imā catassau vipattiyau|

tattha katamai cha āpattisamuṭṭhānā? atthāpatti kāyatau samuṭṭhāti, na vācatau na cittatau; atthāpatti vācatau samuṭṭhāti, na kāyatau na cittatau; atthāpatti kāyatau ca vācatau ca samuṭṭhāti, na cittatau; atthāpatti kāyatau ca cittatau ca samuṭṭhāti, na vācatau; atthāpatti vācatau ca cittatau ca samuṭṭhāti, na kāyatau; atthāpatti kāyatau ca vācatau ca cittatau ca samuṭṭhāti – imai cha āpattisamuṭṭhānā|

272. tattha  cūḷava॰ 216; a॰ ni॰ 6.36; ma॰ ni॰ 3.44; dī॰ ni॰ 3.325 katamāni cha vivādamūlāni? idha bhikkhu kaudhanau hauti upanāhī| yau sau bhikkhu kaudhanau hauti upanāhī sau sattharipi agāravau viharati appatissau, dhammaipi agāravau viharati appatissau, saṅghaipi agāravau viharati appatissau, sikkhāyapi na paripūrakārī hauti| yau sau bhikkhu satthari agāravau viharati appatissau, dhammai…pai॰… saṅghai…pai॰… sikkhāya na paripūrakārī, sau saṅghai vivādaṃ janaiti| yau hauti vivādau bahujanāhitāya bahujanāsukhāya bahunau janassa anatthāya ahitāya dukkhāya daivamanussānaṃ| aivarūpaṃ cai tumhai vivādamūlaṃ ajjhattaṃ vā  bahiddhā vā samanupassaiyyātha tatra tumhai  tassaiva pāpakassa vivādamūlassa pahānāya vāyamaiyyātha| aivarūpaṃ cai tumhai vivādamūlaṃ ajjhattaṃ vā bahiddhā vā na samanupassaiyyātha tatra tumhai tassaiva pāpakassa vivādamūlassa āyatiṃ anavassavāya paṭipaj‍jaiyyātha| aivamaitassa pāpakassa vivādamūlassa pahānaṃ hauti| aivamaitassa pāpakassa vivādamūlassa āyatiṃ anavassavau hauti|

cūḷava॰ 216 puna caparaṃ bhikkhu makkhī hauti paḷāsī…pai॰… issukī hauti maccharī, saṭhau hauti māyāvī, pāpicchau hauti micchādiṭṭhi, sandiṭṭhiparāmāsī hauti ādhānaggāhī duppaṭinissaggī| yau sau bhikkhu sandiṭṭhiparāmāsī hauti ādhānaggāhī duppaṭinissaggī sau sattharipi agāravau viharati appatissau, dhammaipi agāravau viharati appatissau, saṅghaipi agāravau viharati appatissau, sikkhāyapi na paripūrakārī hauti| yau sau bhikkhu satthari agāravau viharati appatissau dhammai…pai॰… saṅghai…pai॰… sikkhāya na paripūrakārī, sau saṅghai vivādaṃ janaiti| yau sau hauti vivādau bahujanāhitāya bahujanāsukhāya bahunau janassa anatthāya ahitāya dukkhāya daivamanussānaṃ| aivarūpaṃ cai tumhai vivādamūlaṃ ajjhattaṃ vā bahiddhā vā samanupassaiyyātha tatra tumhai tassaiva pāpakassa vivādamūlassa  pahānāya vāyamaiyyātha| aivarūpaṃ cai tumhai vivādamūlaṃ ajjhattaṃ vā bahiddhā vā na samanupassaiyyātha tatra tumhai tassaiva pāpakassa vivādamūlassa āyatiṃ anavassavāya paṭipaj‍jaiyyātha| aivamaitassa pāpakassa vivādamūlassa pahānaṃ hauti| aivamaitassa pāpakassa vivādamūlassa āyatiṃ anavassavau hauti| imāni cha vivādamūlāni|

273. cūḷava॰ 217 tattha katamāni cha anuvādamūlāni? idha bhikkhu kaudhanau hauti upanāhī| yau sau bhikkhu kaudhanau hauti upanāhī sau sattharipi agāravau viharati appatissau, dhammaipi agāravau viharati appatissau, saṅghaipi agāravau viharati appatissau, sikkhāyapi na paripūrakārī hauti| yau sau bhikkhu satthari agāravau viharati appatissau dhammai…pai॰… saṅghai  …pai॰… sikkhāya na paripūrakārī sau saṅghai anuvādaṃ janaiti| yau hauti anuvādau bahujanāhitāya bahujanāsukhāya bahunau janassa anatthāya ahitāya dukkhāya daivamanussānaṃ| aivarūpaṃ  cai tumhai anuvādamūlaṃ ajjhattaṃ vā bahiddhā vā samanupassaiyyātha tatra tumhai tassaiva pāpakassa anuvādamūlassa pahānāya vāyamaiyyātha| aivarūpaṃ cai tumhai anuvādamūlaṃ ajjhattaṃ vā bahiddhā vā na samanupassaiyyātha tatra tumhai tassaiva pāpakassa anuvādamūlassa āyatiṃ anavassavāya paṭipaj‍jaiyyātha| aivamaitassa pāpakassa anuvādamūlassa pahānaṃ hauti| aivamaitassa pāpakassa anuvādamūlassa āyatiṃ anavassavau hauti|

puna caparaṃ bhikkhu makkhī hauti palāsī…pai॰… issukī hauti maccharī, saṭhau hauti māyāvī, pāpicchau hauti micchādiṭṭhi, sandiṭṭhiparāmāsī  hauti ādhānaggāhī duppaṭinissaggī| yau sau bhikkhu sandiṭṭhiparāmāsī hauti ādhānaggāhī duppaṭinissaggī sau sattharipi agāravau viharati appatissau, dhammaipi agāravau viharati appatissau, saṅghaipi agāravau viharati appatissau, sikkhāyapi na paripūrakārī hauti| yau bhikkhu satthari agāravau viharati appatissau dhammai…pai॰… saṅghai…pai॰… sikkhāya na paripūrakārī sau saṅghai anuvādaṃ janaiti| yau hauti anuvādau bahujanāhitāya bahujanāsukhāya bahunau janassa anatthāya ahitāya dukkhāya daivamanussānaṃ| aivarūpaṃ cai tumhai anuvādamūlaṃ ajjhattaṃ vā bahiddhā vā samanupassaiyyātha tatra tumhai tassaiva pāpakassa anuvādamūlassa pahānāya vāyamaiyyātha| aivarūpaṃ cai tumhai anuvādamūlaṃ ajjhattaṃ vā bahiddhā vā na samanupassaiyyātha tatra tumhai tassaiva pāpakassa anuvādamūlassa āyatiṃ anavassavāya paṭipaj‍jaiyyātha| aivamaitassa pāpakassa anuvādamūlassa pahānaṃ hauti| aivamaitassa pāpakassa anuvādamūlassa āyatiṃ anavassavau hauti| imāni cha anuvādamūlāni|

274. dī॰ ni॰ 3.324; a॰ ni॰ 6.11 tattha katamai cha sāraṇīyā dhammā? idha bhikkhunau maittaṃ kāyakammaṃ pac‍cupaṭṭhitaṃ hauti sabrahmacārīsu āvi caiva rahau ca| ayampi dhammau sāraṇīyau piyakaraṇau garukaraṇau saṅgahāya avivādāya sāmaggiyā aikībhāvāya saṃvattati|

puna caparaṃ bhikkhunau maittaṃ  vacīkammaṃ pac‍cupaṭṭhitaṃ hauti sabrahmacārīsu āvi caiva rahau ca| ayampi dhammau sāraṇīyau piyakaraṇau garukaraṇau saṅgahāya avivādāya sāmaggiyā aikībhāvāya saṃvattati|

puna  caparaṃ bhikkhunau maittaṃ manaukammaṃ pac‍cupaṭṭhitaṃ hauti sabrahmacārīsu āvi caiva rahau ca| ayampi dhammau sāraṇīyau piyakaraṇau garukaraṇau saṅgahāya avivādāya sāmaggiyā aikībhāvāya saṃvattati|

puna caparaṃ bhikkhu yai tai lābhā dhammikā dhammaladdhā antamasau pattapariyāpan‍namattampi tathārūpaihi lābhaihi appaṭivibhattabhaugī hauti sīlavantaihi sabrahmacārīhi sādhāraṇabhaugī| ayampi dhammau sāraṇīyau piyakaraṇau garukaraṇau saṅgahāya avivādāya sāmaggiyā aikībhāvāya saṃvattati|

puna caparaṃ bhikkhu yāni tāni sīlāni akhaṇḍāni acchiddāni asabalāni akammāsāni bhujissāni viñ‍ñupasatthāni aparāmaṭṭhāni samādhisaṃvattanikāni, tathārūpaisu sīlaisu sīlasāmañ‍ñagatau viharati sabrahmacārīhi āvi caiva rahau ca| ayampi dhammau sāraṇīyau piyakaraṇau garukaraṇau saṅgahāya avivādāya sāmaggiyā aikībhāvāya saṃvattati|

puna caparaṃ bhikkhu yāyaṃ diṭṭhi ariyā niyyānikā niyyāti tak‍karassa sammā dukkhakkhayāya tathārūpāya diṭṭhiyā diṭṭhisāmañ‍ñagatau viharati sabrahmacārīhi āvi caiva rahau ca| ayampi dhammau sāraṇīyau piyakaraṇau garukaraṇau saṅgahāya avivādāya sāmaggiyā aikībhāvāya saṃvattati| imai cha sāraṇīyā dhammā|

275. tattha  katamāni aṭṭhārasa bhaidakaravatthūni? idha bhikkhu adhammaṃ ‘‘dhammau’’ti dīpaiti, dhammaṃ ‘‘adhammau’’ti dīpaiti, avinayaṃ ‘‘vinayau’’ti dīpaiti, vinayaṃ ‘‘avinayau’’ti dīpaiti, abhāsitaṃ alapitaṃ tathāgataina ‘‘bhāsitaṃ lapitaṃ tathāgatainā’’ti dīpaiti, bhāsitaṃ lapitaṃ tathāgataina ‘‘abhāsitaṃ alapitaṃ tathāgatainā’’ti dīpaiti, anāciṇṇaṃ tathāgataina ‘‘āciṇṇaṃ tathāgatainā’’ti dīpaiti, āciṇṇaṃ tathāgataina ‘‘anāciṇṇaṃ tathāgatainā’’ti dīpaiti, apañ‍ñattaṃ tathāgataina ‘‘pañ‍ñattaṃ tathāgatainā’’ti dīpaiti, pañ‍ñattaṃ tathāgataina ‘‘apañ‍ñattaṃ tathāgatainā’’ti dīpaiti, āpattiṃ ‘‘anāpattī’’ti dīpaiti, anāpattiṃ ‘‘āpattī’’ti dīpaiti, lahukaṃ āpattiṃ ‘‘garukā āpattī’’ti dīpaiti, garukaṃ āpattiṃ ‘‘lahukā āpattī’’ti dīpaiti, sāvasaisaṃ āpattiṃ ‘‘anavasaisā āpattī’’ti  dīpaiti, anavasaisaṃ āpattiṃ ‘‘sāvasaisā āpattī’’ti  dīpaiti, duṭṭhul‍laṃ āpattiṃ ‘‘aduṭṭhul‍lā āpattī’’ti dīpaiti, aduṭṭhul‍laṃ āpattiṃ ‘‘duṭṭhul‍lā āpattī’’ti dīpaiti| imāni aṭṭhārasa bhaidakaravatthūni|

cūḷava॰ 215; pari॰ 340 tattha katamāni cattāri adhikaraṇāni? vivādādhikaraṇaṃ, anuvādādhikaraṇaṃ, āpattādhikaraṇaṃ, kic‍cādhikaraṇaṃ – imāni cattāri adhikaraṇāni|

tattha katamai satta samathā? sammukhāvinayau, sativinayau, amūḷhavinayau, paṭiñ‍ñātakaraṇaṃ, yaibhuyyasikā, tassapāpiyasikā, tiṇavatthārakau  – imai satta samathā|

katipucchāvārau niṭṭhitau|

tassuddānaṃ –

āpatti āpattikkhandhā, vinītā sattadhā puna|

vinītāgāravā caiva, gāravā mūlamaiva ca||

puna vinītā vipatti, samuṭṭhānā vivādanā|

anuvādā sāraṇīyaṃ, bhaidādhikaraṇaina ca||

sattaiva samathā vuttā, padā sattarasā imaiti||

1. chaāpattisamuṭṭhānavārau

276. paṭhamaina  āpattisamuṭṭhānaina pārājikaṃ āpaj‍jaiyyāti? na hīti vattabbaṃ| saṅghādisaisaṃ āpaj‍jaiyyāti? siyāti vattabbaṃ| thul‍lac‍cayaṃ āpaj‍jaiyyāti? siyāti vattabbaṃ| pācittiyaṃ āpaj‍jaiyyāti? siyāti vattabbaṃ| pāṭidaisanīyaṃ āpaj‍jaiyyāti? siyāti vattabbaṃ| duk‍kaṭaṃ āpaj‍jaiyyāti? siyāti vattabbaṃ| dubbhāsitaṃ āpaj‍jaiyyāti? na hīti vattabbaṃ|

dutiyaina āpattisamuṭṭhānaina pārājikaṃ āpaj‍jaiyyāti? na hīti vattabbaṃ| saṅghādisaisaṃ āpaj‍jaiyyāti? siyāti vattabbaṃ| thul‍lac‍cayaṃ āpaj‍jaiyyāti? siyāti vattabbaṃ| pācittiyaṃ āpaj‍jaiyyāti? siyāti vattabbaṃ| pāṭidaisanīyaṃ āpaj‍jaiyyāti? na hīti vattabbaṃ| duk‍kaṭaṃ āpaj‍jaiyyāti? siyāti vattabbaṃ| dubbhāsitaṃ āpaj‍jaiyyāti? na hīti vattabbaṃ|

tatiyaina  āpattisamuṭṭhānaina pārājikaṃ āpaj‍jaiyyāti? na hīti vattabbaṃ| saṅghādisaisaṃ āpaj‍jaiyyāti? siyāti vattabbaṃ| thul‍lac‍cayaṃ āpaj‍jaiyyāti? siyāti vattabbaṃ| pācittiyaṃ āpaj‍jaiyyāti? siyāti vattabbaṃ| pāṭidaisanīyaṃ āpaj‍jaiyyāti? siyāti vattabbaṃ| duk‍kaṭaṃ āpaj‍jaiyyāti? siyāti vattabbaṃ| dubbhāsitaṃ āpaj‍jaiyyāti? na hīti vattabbaṃ|

catutthai  āpattisamuṭṭhānaina pārājikaṃ āpaj‍jaiyyāti? siyāti vattabbaṃ| saṅghādisaisaṃ āpaj‍jaiyyāti? siyāti vattabbaṃ| thul‍lac‍cayaṃ āpaj‍jaiyyāti? siyāti vattabbaṃ| pācittiyaṃ āpaj‍jaiyyāti? siyāti vattabbaṃ| pāṭidaisanīyaṃ āpaj‍jaiyyāti? siyāti vattabbaṃ| duk‍kaṭaṃ āpaj‍jaiyyāti? siyāti vattabbaṃ| dubbhāsitaṃ āpaj‍jaiyyāti? na hīti vattabbaṃ|

pañ‍camaina āpattisamuṭṭhānaina pārājikaṃ āpaj‍jaiyyāti? siyāti vattabbaṃ| saṅghādisaisaṃ āpaj‍jaiyyāti? siyāti vattabbaṃ| thul‍lac‍cayaṃ āpaj‍jaiyyāti? siyāti vattabbaṃ| pācittiyaṃ āpaj‍jaiyyāti? siyāti vattabbaṃ| pāṭidaisanīyaṃ āpaj‍jaiyyāti? na hīti vattabbaṃ| duk‍kaṭaṃ āpaj‍jaiyyāti? siyāti vattabbaṃ| dubbhāsitaṃ āpaj‍jaiyyāti? siyāti vattabbaṃ|

chaṭṭhaina āpattisamuṭṭhānaina pārājikaṃ āpaj‍jaiyyāti? siyāti vattabbaṃ| saṅghādisaisaṃ āpaj‍jaiyyāti? siyāti vattabbaṃ| thul‍lac‍cayaṃ āpaj‍jaiyyāti? siyāti vattabbaṃ| pācittiyaṃ āpaj‍jaiyyāti? siyāti vattabbaṃ| pāṭidaisanīyaṃ āpaj‍jaiyyāti? siyāti vattabbaṃ| duk‍kaṭaṃ āpaj‍jaiyyāti? siyāti vattabbaṃ| dubbhāsitaṃ āpaj‍jaiyyāti? na hīti vattabbaṃ|

chaāpattisamuṭṭhānavārau niṭṭhitau paṭhamau|

2. katāpattivārau

277. paṭhamaina āpattisamuṭṭhānaina kati āpattiyau āpaj‍jati? paṭhamaina  āpattisamuṭṭhānaina pañ‍ca āpattiyau āpaj‍jati| bhikkhu kappiyasañ‍ñī sañ‍ñācikāya kuṭiṃ karauti adaisitavatthukaṃ pamāṇātik‍kantaṃ sārambhaṃ aparik‍kamanaṃ, payaugai duk‍kaṭaṃ; aikaṃ piṇḍaṃ anāgatai āpatti  thul‍lac‍cayassa; tasmiṃ piṇḍai āgatai āpatti saṅghādisaisassa; bhikkhu kappiyasañ‍ñī vikālai bhaujanaṃ bhuñ‍jati, āpatti pācittiyassa; bhikkhu kappiyasañ‍ñī añ‍ñātikāya  bhikkhuniyā antaragharaṃ paviṭṭhāya hatthatau khādanīyaṃ vā bhaujanīyaṃ vā sahatthā paṭiggahaitvā bhuñ‍jati, āpatti pāṭidaisanīyassa – paṭhamaina āpattisamuṭṭhānaina imā pañ‍ca āpattiyau āpaj‍jati|

tā āpattiyau catun‍naṃ vipattīnaṃ kati vipattiyau bhajanti? sattan‍naṃ āpattikkhandhānaṃ katihi āpattikkhandhaihi saṅgahitā? chan‍naṃ āpattisamuṭṭhānānaṃ katihi samuṭṭhānaihi samuṭṭhanti? catun‍naṃ adhikaraṇānaṃ katamaṃ adhikaraṇaṃ? sattan‍naṃ samathānaṃ katihi samathaihi sammanti? tā āpattiyau catun‍naṃ vipattīnaṃ dvai vipattiyau bhajanti – siyā sīlavipattiṃ, siyā ācāravipattiṃ| sattan‍naṃ āpattikkhandhānaṃ pañ‍cahi āpattikkhandhaihi saṅgahitā – siyā saṅghādisaisāpattikkhandhaina, siyā thul‍lac‍cayāpattikkhandhaina, siyā pācittiyāpattikkhandhaina, siyā pāṭidaisanīyāpattikkhandhaina, siyā duk‍kaṭāpattikkhandhaina| chan‍naṃ āpattisamuṭṭhānānaṃ aikaina samuṭṭhānaina samuṭṭhanti – kāyatau samuṭṭhanti, na vācatau na cittatau| catun‍naṃ adhikaraṇānaṃ āpattādhikaraṇaṃ| sattan‍naṃ samathānaṃ tīhi samathaihi sammanti – siyā sammukhāvinayaina ca paṭiñ‍ñātakaraṇaina ca, siyā sammukhāvinayaina ca tiṇavatthārakaina ca|

278. dutiyaina āpattisamuṭṭhānaina kati āpattiyau āpaj‍jati? dutiyaina āpattisamuṭṭhānaina catassau āpattiyau āpaj‍jati – bhikkhu kappiyasañ‍ñī samādisati  – ‘‘kuṭiṃ mai karauthā’’ti| tassa kuṭiṃ karaunti adaisitavatthukaṃ pamāṇātik‍kantaṃ sārambhaṃ aparik‍kamanaṃ| payaugai duk‍kaṭaṃ; aikaṃ piṇḍaṃ anāgatai āpatti thul‍lac‍cayassa; tasmiṃ piṇḍai āgatai āpatti saṅghādisaisassa| bhikkhu kappiyasañ‍ñī anupasampan‍naṃ padasau dhammaṃ vācaiti, āpatti pācittiyassa – dutiyaina āpattisamuṭṭhānaina imā catassau āpattiyau āpaj‍jati|

tā āpattiyau catun‍naṃ vipattīnaṃ kati vipattiyau bhajanti…pai॰… sattan‍naṃ samathānaṃ katihi samathaihi sammanti? tā āpattiyau catun‍naṃ vipattīnaṃ dvai vipattiyau bhajanti – siyā sīlavipattiṃ, siyā ācāravipattiṃ| sattan‍naṃ āpattikkhandhānaṃ  catūhi āpattikkhandhaihi saṅgahitā – siyā saṅghādisaisāpattikkhandhaina, siyā thul‍lac‍cayāpattikkhandhaina, siyā pācittiyāpattikkhandhaina, siyā duk‍kaṭāpattikkhandhaina| chan‍naṃ āpattisamuṭṭhānānaṃ aikaina samuṭṭhānaina samuṭṭhanti – vācatau samuṭṭhanti, na kāyatau na cittatau| catun‍naṃ adhikaraṇānaṃ, āpattādhikaraṇaṃ| sattan‍naṃ samathānaṃ tīhi samathaihi sammanti – siyā sammukhāvinayaina  ca paṭiñ‍ñātakaraṇaina ca, siyā sammukhāvinayaina ca tiṇavatthārakaina ca|

279. tatiyaina  āpattisamuṭṭhānaina kati āpattiyau āpaj‍jati? tatiyaina āpattisamuṭṭhānaina pañ‍ca āpattiyau āpaj‍jati| bhikkhu kappiyasañ‍ñī saṃvidahitvā kuṭiṃ karauti adaisitavatthukaṃ pamāṇātik‍kantaṃ sārambhaṃ aparik‍kamanaṃ| payaugai duk‍kaṭaṃ; aikaṃ piṇḍaṃ anāgatai āpatti thul‍lac‍cayassa; tasmiṃ piṇḍai āgatai āpatti saṅghādisaisassa| bhikkhu kappiyasañ‍ñī paṇītabhaujanāni attanau atthāya viñ‍ñāpaitvā bhuñ‍jati, āpatti pācittiyassa| bhikkhu kappiyasañ‍ñī bhikkhuniyā vausāsantiyā na nivāraitvā bhuñ‍jati, āpatti pāṭidaisanīyassa – tatiyaina āpattisamuṭṭhānaina imā pañ‍ca āpattiyau āpaj‍jati|

tā āpattiyau catun‍naṃ vipattīnaṃ kati vipattiyau bhajanti…pai॰… sattan‍naṃ samathānaṃ katihi samathaihi sammanti? tā āpattiyau catun‍naṃ vipattīnaṃ dvai vipattiyau bhajanti – siyā sīlavipattiṃ, siyā ācāravipattiṃ| sattan‍naṃ āpattikkhandhānaṃ pañ‍cahi āpattikkhandhaihi saṅgahitā – siyā saṅghādisaisāpattikkhandhaina, siyā thul‍lac‍cayāpattikkhandhaina, siyā pācittiyāpattikkhandhaina, siyā pāṭidaisanīyāpattikkhandhaina, siyā duk‍kaṭāpattikkhandhaina| chan‍naṃ āpattisamuṭṭhānānaṃ aikaina samuṭṭhānaina samuṭṭhanti – kāyatau ca vācatau ca samuṭṭhanti, na cittatau| catun‍naṃ adhikaraṇānaṃ, āpattādhikaraṇaṃ| sattan‍naṃ samathānaṃ tīhi samathaihi sammanti – siyā sammukhāvinayaina ca paṭiñ‍ñātakaraṇaina ca, siyā sammukhāvinayaina ca tiṇavatthārakaina ca|

280. catutthaina āpattisamuṭṭhānaina kati āpattiyau āpaj‍jati? catutthaina āpattisamuṭṭhānaina cha āpattiyau āpaj‍jati – bhikkhu maithunaṃ dhammaṃ paṭisaivati, āpatti pārājikassa; bhikkhu akappiyasañ‍ñī sañ‍ñācikāya kuṭiṃ karauti  adaisitavatthukaṃ pamāṇātik‍kantaṃ sārambhaṃ aparik‍kamanaṃ, payaugai  duk‍kaṭaṃ; aikaṃ piṇḍaṃ anāgatai āpatti thul‍lac‍cayassa; tasmiṃ piṇḍai āgatai āpatti saṅghādisaisassa| bhikkhu akappiyasañ‍ñī vikālai bhaujanaṃ bhuñ‍jati, āpatti pācittiyassa| bhikkhu akappiyasañ‍ñī añ‍ñātikāya bhikkhuniyā antaragharaṃ paviṭṭhāya hatthatau khādanīyaṃ vā bhaujanīyaṃ vā sahatthā paṭiggahaitvā bhuñ‍jati, āpatti pāṭidaisanīyassa| catutthaina āpattisamuṭṭhānaina imā cha āpattiyau āpaj‍jati|

tā āpattiyau catun‍naṃ vipattīnaṃ kati vipattiyau bhajanti…pai॰… sattan‍naṃ samathānaṃ katihi samathaihi sammanti? tā āpattiyau catun‍naṃ vipattīnaṃ dvai vipattiyau bhajanti – siyā sīlavipattiṃ, siyā ācāravipattiṃ| sattan‍naṃ āpattikkhandhānaṃ chahi āpattikkhandhaihi saṅgahitā – siyā pārājikāpattikkhandhaina, siyā saṅghādisaisāpattikkhandhaina, siyā thul‍lac‍cayāpattikkhandhaina, siyā pācittiyāpattikkhandhaina, siyā pāṭidaisanīyāpattikkhandhaina, siyā duk‍kaṭāpattikkhandhaina| chan‍naṃ āpattisamuṭṭhānānaṃ aikaina samuṭṭhānaina samuṭṭhanti – kāyatau ca cittatau ca samuṭṭhanti, na vācatau| catun‍naṃ adhikaraṇānaṃ, āpattādhikaraṇaṃ| sattan‍naṃ samathānaṃ tīhi samathaihi sammanti – siyā sammukhāvinayaina ca paṭiñ‍ñātakaraṇaina ca, siyā sammukhāvinayaina ca tiṇavatthārakaina ca|

281. pañ‍camaina āpattisamuṭṭhānaina kati āpattiyau āpaj‍jati? pañ‍camaina āpattisamuṭṭhānaina cha āpattiyau āpaj‍jati| bhikkhu pāpicchau icchāpakatau asantaṃ abhūtaṃ uttarimanussadhammaṃ ul‍lapati, āpatti pārājikassa; bhikkhu akappiyasañ‍ñī samādisati – ‘‘kuṭiṃ mai karauthā’’ti| tassa kuṭiṃ karaunti adaisitavatthukaṃ pamāṇātik‍kantaṃ sārambhaṃ aparik‍kamanaṃ| payaugai duk‍kaṭaṃ; aikaṃ piṇḍaṃ anāgatai āpatti thul‍lac‍cayassa; tasmiṃ piṇḍai āgatai āpatti saṅghādisaisassa| bhikkhu akappiyasañ‍ñī anupasampan‍naṃ padasau dhammaṃ vācaiti, āpatti pācittiyassa| na khuṃsaitukāmau na vambhaitukāmau na maṅkukattukāmau davakamyatā hīnaina hīnaṃ vadaiti, āpatti dubbhāsitassa – pañ‍camaina āpattisamuṭṭhānaina imā cha āpattiyau āpaj‍jati|

tā āpattiyau catun‍naṃ vipattīnaṃ kati vipattiyau bhajanti…pai॰… sattan‍naṃ samathānaṃ katihi samathaihi sammanti? tā āpattiyau catun‍naṃ vipattīnaṃ  dvai vipattiyau bhajanti – siyā sīlavipattiṃ, siyā ācāravipattiṃ| sattan‍naṃ āpattikkhandhānaṃ chahi āpattikkhandhaihi  saṅgahitā – siyā pārājikāpattikkhandhaina, siyā saṅghādisaisāpattikkhandhaina, siyā thul‍lac‍cayāpattikkhandhaina, siyā pācittiyāpattikkhandhaina, siyā duk‍kaṭāpattikkhandhaina, siyā dubbhāsitāpattikkhandhaina| chan‍naṃ āpattisamuṭṭhānānaṃ aikaina samuṭṭhānaina samuṭṭhanti – vācatau ca cittatau ca samuṭṭhanti, na kāyatau| catun‍naṃ adhikaraṇānaṃ, āpattādhikaraṇaṃ| sattan‍naṃ samathānaṃ tīhi samathaihi sammanti – siyā sammukhāvinayaina ca paṭiñ‍ñātakaraṇaina ca, siyā sammukhāvinayaina ca tiṇavatthārakaina ca|

282. chaṭṭhaina āpattisamuṭṭhānaina kati āpattiyau āpaj‍jati? chaṭṭhaina āpattisamuṭṭhānaina cha āpattiyau āpaj‍jati – bhikkhu saṃvidahitvā bhaṇḍaṃ avaharati, āpatti  pārājikassa; bhikkhu akappiyasañ‍ñī saṃvidahitvā kuṭiṃ karauti adaisitavatthukaṃ pamāṇātik‍kantaṃ sārambhaṃ aparik‍kamanaṃ, payaugai duk‍kaṭaṃ; aikaṃ piṇḍaṃ anāgatai āpatti thul‍lac‍cayassa; tasmiṃ piṇḍai āgatai, āpatti saṅghādisaisassa| bhikkhu akappiyasañ‍ñī paṇītabhaujanāni attanau atthāya viñ‍ñāpaitvā bhuñ‍jati, āpatti pācittiyassa| bhikkhu akappiyasañ‍ñī bhikkhuniyā vausāsantiyā na nivāraitvā bhuñ‍jati, āpatti pāṭidaisanīyassa – chaṭṭhaina āpattisamuṭṭhānaina imā cha āpattiyau āpaj‍jati|

tā āpattiyau catun‍naṃ vipattīnaṃ kati vipattiyau bhajanti? sattan‍naṃ āpattikkhandhānaṃ katihi āpattikkhandhaihi saṅgahitā? chan‍naṃ āpattisamuṭṭhānānaṃ katihi āpattisamuṭṭhānaihi samuṭṭhanti? catun‍naṃ adhikaraṇānaṃ katamaṃ adhikaraṇaṃ? sattan‍naṃ samathānaṃ katihi samathaihi sammanti? tā āpattiyau catun‍naṃ vipattīnaṃ dvai vipattiyau bhajanti – siyā sīlavipattiṃ, siyā ācāravipattiṃ| sattan‍naṃ āpattikkhandhānaṃ chahi āpattikkhandhaihi saṅgahitā – siyā pārājikāpattikkhandhaina, siyā saṅghādisaisāpattikkhandhaina, siyā thul‍lac‍cayāpattikkhandhaina, siyā pācittiyāpattikkhandhaina, siyā pāṭidaisanīyāpattikkhandhaina, siyā duk‍kaṭāpattikkhandhaina| chan‍naṃ āpattisamuṭṭhānānaṃ aikaina samuṭṭhānaina samuṭṭhanti – kāyatau ca vācatau ca cittatau ca samuṭṭhanti| catun‍naṃ adhikaraṇānaṃ, āpattādhikaraṇaṃ| sattan‍naṃ  samathānaṃ tīhi samathaihi sammanti – siyā sammukhāvinayaina ca paṭiñ‍ñātakaraṇaina ca, siyā sammukhāvinayaina ca tiṇavatthārakaina cāti|

chan‍naṃ āpattisamuṭṭhānānaṃ

katāpattivārau niṭṭhitau dutiyau|

3. āpattisamuṭṭhānagāthā

283.

 samuṭṭhānā kāyikā anantadassinā|

akkhātā laukahitaina vivaikadassinā|

āpattiyau taina samuṭṭhitā kati|

pucchāmi taṃ brūhi vibhaṅgakauvida||

samuṭṭhānā  kāyikā anantadassinā|

akkhātā laukahitaina vivaikadassinā|

āpattiyau taina samuṭṭhitā pañ‍ca|

aitaṃ  tai akkhāmi vibhaṅgakauvida||

samuṭṭhānā vācasikā anantadassinā|

akkhātā laukahitaina vivaikadassinā|

āpattiyau taina samuṭṭhitā kati|

pucchāmi taṃ brūhi vibhaṅgakauvida||

samuṭṭhānā vācasikā anantadassinā|

akkhātā laukahitaina vivaikadassinā|

āpattiyau taina samuṭṭhitā catassau|

aitaṃ tai akkhāmi vibhaṅgakauvida||

samuṭṭhānā kāyikā vācasikā anantadassinā|

akkhātā laukahitaina vivaikadassinā|

āpattiyau taina samuṭṭhitā kati|

pucchāmi  taṃ brūhi vibhaṅgakauvida||

samuṭṭhānā  kāyikā vācasikā anantadassinā|

akkhātā laukahitaina vivaikadassinā|

āpattiyau taina samuṭṭhitā pañ‍ca|

aitaṃ tai akkhāmi vibhaṅgakauvida||

samuṭṭhānā kāyikā mānasikā anantadassinā|

akkhātā laukahitaina vivaikadassinā|

āpattiyau taina samuṭṭhitā kati|

pucchāmi taṃ brūhi vibhaṅgakauvida||

samuṭṭhānā  kāyikā mānasikā anantadassinā|

akkhātā laukahitaina vivaikadassinā|

āpattiyau taina samuṭṭhitā cha|

aitaṃ tai akkhāmi vibhaṅgakauvida||

samuṭṭhānā vācasikā mānasikā anantadassinā|

akkhātā laukahitaina vivaikadassinā|

āpattiyau taina samuṭṭhitā kati|

pucchāmi taṃ brūhi vibhaṅgakauvida||

samuṭṭhānā vācasikā mānasikā anantadassinā|

akkhātā laukahitaina vivaikadassinā|

āpattiyau taina samuṭṭhitā cha|

aitaṃ tai akkhāmi vibhaṅgakauvida||

samuṭṭhānā kāyikā vācasikā mānasikā anantadassinā|

akkhātā laukahitaina vivaikadassinā|

āpattiyau taina samuṭṭhitā kati|

pucchāmi taṃ brūhi vibhaṅgakauvida||

samuṭṭhānā  kāyikā vācasikā mānasikā anantadassinā|

akkhātā laukahitaina vivaikadassinā|

āpattiyau taina samuṭṭhitā cha|

aitaṃ  tai akkhāmi vibhaṅgakauvidāti||

āpattisamuṭṭhānagāthā niṭṭhitā tatiyā|

4. vipattipac‍cayavārau

284. sīlavipattipac‍cayā  kati āpattiyau āpaj‍jati? sīlavipattipac‍cayā catassau  āpattiyau āpaj‍jati – bhikkhunī jānaṃ pārājikaṃ dhammaṃ paṭicchādaiti, āpatti pārājikassa; vaimatikā paṭicchādaiti, āpatti thul‍lac‍cayassa; bhikkhu saṅghādisaisaṃ paṭicchādaiti, āpatti pācittiyassa; attanau duṭṭhul‍laṃ āpattiṃ paṭicchādaiti, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa – sīlavipattipac‍cayā imā catassau āpattiyau āpaj‍jati|

tā āpattiyau catun‍naṃ vipattīnaṃ kati vipattiyau bhajanti…pai॰… sattan‍naṃ samathānaṃ katihi samathaihi sammanti? tā āpattiyau catun‍naṃ vipattīnaṃ dvai vipattiyau bhajanti – siyā sīlavipattiṃ, siyā ācāravipattiṃ| sattan‍naṃ āpattikkhandhānaṃ catūhi āpattikkhandhaihi saṅgahitā – siyā pārājikāpattikkhandhaina, siyā thul‍lac‍cayāpattikkhandhaina, siyā pācittiyāpattikkhandhaina, siyā duk‍kaṭāpattikkhandhaina| chan‍naṃ āpattisamuṭṭhānānaṃ aikaina samuṭṭhānaina samuṭṭhanti – kāyatau ca vācatau ca cittatau ca samuṭṭhanti| catun‍naṃ adhikaraṇānaṃ, āpattādhikaraṇaṃ| sattan‍naṃ samathānaṃ tīhi samathaihi sammanti – siyā sammukhāvinayaina ca paṭiñ‍ñātakaraṇaina ca, siyā sammukhāvinayaina ca tiṇavatthārakaina ca|

285. ācāravipattipac‍cayā kati āpattiyau āpaj‍jati? ācāravipattipac‍cayā  aikaṃ āpattiṃ āpaj‍jati| ācāravipattiṃ paṭicchādaiti, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa – ācāravipattipac‍cayā imaṃ aikaṃ āpattiṃ āpaj‍jati|

sā āpatti catun‍naṃ vipattīnaṃ kati vipattiyau bhajati …pai॰… sattan‍naṃ samathānaṃ katihi samathaihi sammati? sā āpatti catun‍naṃ vipattīnaṃ aikaṃ vipattiṃ bhajati – ācāravipattiṃ| sattan‍naṃ āpattikkhandhānaṃ aikaina āpattikkhandhaina saṅgahitā – duk‍kaṭāpattikkhandhaina| chan‍naṃ āpattisamuṭṭhānānaṃ aikaina samuṭṭhānaina samuṭṭhāti – kāyatau ca vācatau ca cittatau ca samuṭṭhāti| catun‍naṃ adhikaraṇānaṃ āpattādhikaraṇaṃ| sattan‍naṃ samathānaṃ tīhi samathaihi sammati – siyā sammukhāvinayaina ca paṭiñ‍ñātakaraṇaina ca, siyā sammukhāvinayaina ca tiṇavatthārakaina ca|

286. diṭṭhivipattipac‍cayā  kati āpattiyau āpaj‍jati? diṭṭhivipattipac‍cayā dvai āpattiyau āpaj‍jati| pāpikāya diṭṭhiyā yāvatatiyaṃ samanubhāsanāya na paṭinissaj‍jati, ñattiyā duk‍kaṭaṃ ñattiyā duk‍kaṭaṃ, dvīhi kammavācāhi duk‍kaṭā (syā॰ ka॰); kammavācāpariyausānai āpatti pācittiyassa – diṭṭhivipattipac‍cayā imā dvai āpattiyau āpaj‍jati|

tā  āpattiyau catun‍naṃ vipattīnaṃ kati vipattiyau bhajanti…pai॰… sattan‍naṃ samathānaṃ katihi samathaihi sammanti? tā āpattiyau catun‍naṃ vipattīnaṃ aikaṃ vipattiṃ bhajanti – ācāravipattiṃ| sattan‍naṃ āpattikkhandhānaṃ dvīhi āpattikkhandhaihi saṅgahitā  – siyā pācittiyāpattikkhandhaina, siyā duk‍kaṭāpattikkhandhaina| chan‍naṃ āpattisamuṭṭhānānaṃ aikaina samuṭṭhānaina samuṭṭhanti – kāyatau ca vācatau ca cittatau ca samuṭṭhanti| catun‍naṃ adhikaraṇānaṃ, āpattādhikaraṇaṃ| sattan‍naṃ samathānaṃ tīhi samathaihi sammanti – siyā sammukhāvinayaina ca paṭiñ‍ñātakaraṇaina ca, siyā sammukhāvinayaina ca tiṇavatthārakaina ca|

287. ājīvavipattipac‍cayā kati āpattiyau āpaj‍jati? ājīvavipattipac‍cayā cha āpattiyau āpaj‍jati – ājīvahaitu ājīvakāraṇā pāpicchau icchāpakatau asantaṃ abhūtaṃ uttarimanussadhammaṃ ul‍lapati, āpatti pārājikassa; ājīvahaitu ājīvakāraṇā sañ‍carittaṃ samāpaj‍jati, āpatti saṅghādisaisassa; ājīvahaitu ājīvakāraṇā ‘‘yau tai vihārai vasati, sau bhikkhu arahā’’ti bhaṇati, paṭivijānantassa āpatti thul‍lac‍cayassa; ājīvahaitu ājīvakāraṇā bhikkhu paṇītabhaujanāni attanau atthāya viñ‍ñāpaitvā bhuñ‍jati, āpatti pācittiyassa; ājīvahaitu ājīvakāraṇā bhikkhunī paṇītabhaujanāni attanau atthāya viñ‍ñāpaitvā bhuñ‍jati, āpatti pāṭidaisanīyassa; ājīvahaitu ājīvakāraṇā sūpaṃ vā audanaṃ vā agilānau attanau atthāya viñ‍ñāpaitvā bhuñ‍jati, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa – ājīvavipattipac‍cayā imā cha āpattiyau āpaj‍jati|

tā āpattiyau catun‍naṃ vipattīnaṃ kati vipattiyau bhajanti…pai॰… sattan‍naṃ samathānaṃ katihi samathaihi sammanti| tā āpattiyau catun‍naṃ vipattīnaṃ dvai  vipattiyau  bhajanti – siyā sīlavipattiṃ, siyā ācāravipattiṃ| sattan‍naṃ āpattikkhandhānaṃ chahi āpattikkhandhaihi saṅgahitā – siyā pārājikāpattikkhandhaina, siyā saṅghādisaisāpattikkhandhaina, siyā thul‍lac‍cayāpattikkhandhaina, siyā pācittiyāpattikkhandhaina, siyā pāṭidaisanīyāpattikkhandhaina, siyā duk‍kaṭāpattikkhandhaina| chan‍naṃ āpattisamuṭṭhānānaṃ chahi samuṭṭhānaihi samuṭṭhanti – siyā kāyatau samuṭṭhanti, na vācatau na cittatau; siyā vācatau samuṭṭhanti, na kāyatau na cittatau; siyā kāyatau ca vācatau ca samuṭṭhanti, na cittatau; siyā kāyatau ca cittatau ca samuṭṭhanti, na vācatau; siyā vācatau ca cittatau ca samuṭṭhanti, na kāyatau; siyā kāyatau ca vācatau ca cittatau ca samuṭṭhanti| catun‍naṃ adhikaraṇānaṃ, āpattādhikaraṇaṃ| sattan‍naṃ samathānaṃ tīhi  samathaihi sammanti – siyā sammukhāvinayaina ca paṭiñ‍ñātakaraṇaina ca, siyā sammukhāvinayaina ca tiṇavatthārakaina ca|

vipattipac‍cayavārau niṭṭhitau catutthau|

5. adhikaraṇapac‍cayavārau

288. vivādādhikaraṇapac‍cayā kati āpattiyau āpaj‍jati? vivādādhikaraṇapac‍cayā dvai āpattiyau āpaj‍jati – upasampan‍naṃ aumasati, āpatti pācittiyassa; anupasampan‍naṃ aumasati, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa – vivādādhikaraṇapac‍cayā imā dvai āpattiyau āpaj‍jati|

tā āpattiyau catun‍naṃ  vipattīnaṃ kati vipattiyau bhajanti…pai॰… sattan‍naṃ samathānaṃ katihi samathaihi sammanti? tā āpattiyau catun‍naṃ vipattīnaṃ aikaṃ vipattiṃ bhajanti – ācāravipattiṃ| sattan‍naṃ āpattikkhandhānaṃ dvīhi āpattikkhandhaihi saṅgahitā – siyā pācittiyāpattikkhandhaina, siyā duk‍kaṭāpattikkhandhaina| chan‍naṃ āpattisamuṭṭhānānaṃ tīhi samuṭṭhānaihi samuṭṭhanti – siyā kāyatau ca cittatau ca samuṭṭhanti, na vācatau;  siyā vācatau ca cittatau ca samuṭṭhanti, na kāyatau; siyā kāyatau ca vācatau ca cittatau  ca samuṭṭhanti| catun‍naṃ adhikaraṇānaṃ, āpattādhikaraṇaṃ| sattan‍naṃ samathānaṃ tīhi samathaihi sammanti – siyā sammukhāvinayaina ca paṭiñ‍ñātakaraṇaina ca, siyā sammukhāvinayaina ca tiṇavatthārakaina ca|

289. anuvādādhikaraṇapac‍cayā kati āpattiyau āpaj‍jati? anuvādādhikaraṇapac‍cayā tissau āpattiyau āpaj‍jati| bhikkhuṃ amūlakaina pārājikaina dhammaina anuddhaṃsaiti, āpatti saṅghādisaisassa; amūlakaina saṅghādisaisaina anuddhaṃsaiti, āpatti pācittiyassa; amūlikāya ācāravipattiyā anuddhaṃsaiti, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa – anuvādādhikaraṇapac‍cayā imā tissau āpattiyau āpaj‍jati|

tā āpattiyau catun‍naṃ vipattīnaṃ kati vipattiyau bhajanti…pai॰… sattan‍naṃ samathānaṃ katihi samathaihi sammanti? tā āpattiyau catun‍naṃ vipattīnaṃ dvai vipattiyau bhajanti – siyā sīlavipattiṃ, siyā ācāravipattiṃ| sattan‍naṃ āpattikkhandhānaṃ tīhi āpattikkhandhaihi saṅgahitā – siyā saṅghādisaisāpattikkhandhaina, siyā pācittiyāpattikkhandhaina, siyā duk‍kaṭāpattikkhandhaina| chan‍naṃ āpattisamuṭṭhānānaṃ tīhi samuṭṭhānaihi samuṭṭhanti – siyā kāyatau ca cittatau ca samuṭṭhanti, na vācatau; siyā vācatau ca cittatau ca samuṭṭhanti, na kāyatau; siyā kāyatau ca vācatau ca cittatau ca samuṭṭhanti| catun‍naṃ adhikaraṇānaṃ, āpattādhikaraṇaṃ| sattan‍naṃ samathānaṃ tīhi samathaihi sammanti – siyā sammukhāvinayaina ca paṭiñ‍ñātakaraṇaina ca, siyā sammukhāvinayaina ca tiṇavatthārakaina ca|

290. āpattādhikaraṇapac‍cayā kati āpattiyau āpaj‍jati? āpattādhikaraṇapac‍cayā catassau āpattiyau āpaj‍jati| bhikkhunī jānaṃ pārājikaṃ dhammaṃ paṭicchādaiti, āpatti pārājikassa; vaimatikā paṭicchādaiti, āpatti thul‍lac‍cayassa; bhikkhu saṅghādisaisaṃ paṭicchādaiti; āpatti pācittiyassa; ācāravipattiṃ paṭicchādaiti, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa – āpattādhikaraṇapac‍cayā imā catassau āpattiyau āpaj‍jati|

tā āpattiyau catun‍naṃ vipattīnaṃ kati vipattiyau bhajanti…pai॰… sattan‍naṃ samathānaṃ katihi samathaihi sammanti? tā āpattiyau catun‍naṃ vipattīnaṃ dvai vipattiyau bhajanti – siyā sīlavipattiṃ, siyā ācāravipattiṃ| sattan‍naṃ  āpattikkhandhānaṃ catūhi āpattikkhandhaihi saṅgahitā – siyā pārājikāpattikkhandhaina, siyā thul‍lac‍cayāpattikkhandhaina, siyā pācittiyāpattikkhandhaina, siyā duk‍kaṭāpattikkhandhaina| chan‍naṃ āpattisamuṭṭhānānaṃ aikaina samuṭṭhānaina  samuṭṭhanti – kāyatau ca vācatau ca cittatau ca samuṭṭhanti| catun‍naṃ adhikaraṇānaṃ, āpattādhikaraṇaṃ| sattan‍naṃ samathānaṃ tīhi samathaihi sammanti – siyā sammukhāvinayaina ca paṭiñ‍ñātakaraṇaina ca, siyā sammukhāvinayaina ca tiṇavatthārakaina ca|

291. kic‍cādhikaraṇapac‍cayā kati āpattiyau āpaj‍jati? kic‍cādhikaraṇapac‍cayā pañ‍ca āpattiyau āpaj‍jati| ukkhittānuvattikā bhikkhunī yāvatatiyaṃ samanubhāsanāya na paṭinissaj‍jati, ñattiyā duk‍kaṭaṃ; dvīhi kammavācāhi thul‍lac‍cayā; kammavācāpariyausānai āpatti pārājikassa| bhaidakānuvattakā bhikkhū yāvatatiyaṃ samanubhāsanāya na paṭinissaj‍janti, āpatti saṅghādisaisassa; pāpikāya diṭṭhiyā yāvatatiyaṃ samanubhāsanāya na paṭinissaj‍jati, āpatti pācittiyassa  – kic‍cādhikaraṇapac‍cayā imā pañ‍ca āpattiyau āpaj‍jati|

tā āpattiyau catun‍naṃ vipattīnaṃ kati vipattiyau bhajanti…pai॰… sattan‍naṃ samathānaṃ katihi  samathaihi sammanti? tā āpattiyau catun‍naṃ vipattīnaṃ dvai vipattiyau bhajanti – siyā sīlavipattiṃ, siyā ācāravipattiṃ| sattan‍naṃ āpattikkhandhānaṃ pañ‍cahi āpattikkhandhaihi saṅgahitā – siyā pārājikāpattikkhandhaina, siyā saṅghādisaisāpattikkhandhaina, siyā thul‍lac‍cayāpattikkhandhaina, siyā pācittiyāpattikkhandhaina, siyā duk‍kaṭāpattikkhandhaina| chan‍naṃ āpattisamuṭṭhānānaṃ aikaina samuṭṭhānaina samuṭṭhanti – kāyatau ca vācatau ca cittatau ca samuṭṭhanti| catun‍naṃ adhikaraṇānaṃ, āpattādhikaraṇaṃ| sattan‍naṃ samathānaṃ tīhi samathaihi sammanti – siyā sammukhāvinayaina ca paṭiñ‍ñātakaraṇaina ca, siyā sammukhāvinayaina ca tiṇavatthārakaina ca|

ṭhapaitvā satta āpattiyau satta āpattikkhandhai, avasaisā āpattiyau catun‍naṃ vipattīnaṃ kati vipattiyau bhajanti? sattan‍naṃ āpattikkhandhānaṃ katihi āpattikkhandhaihi saṅgahitā? chan‍naṃ āpattisamuṭṭhānānaṃ katihi samuṭṭhānaihi samuṭṭhanti? catun‍naṃ adhikaraṇānaṃ katamaṃ adhikaraṇaṃ? sattan‍naṃ samathānaṃ katihi samathaihi sammanti? ṭhapaitvā satta āpattiyau satta  āpattikkhandhai avasaisā āpattiyau catun‍naṃ vipattīnaṃ na katamaṃ vipattiṃ bhajanti? sattan‍naṃ āpattikkhandhānaṃ na katamaina āpattikkhandhaina saṅgahitā| chan‍naṃ āpattisamuṭṭhānānaṃ na katamaina āpattisamuṭṭhānaina samuṭṭhanti| catun‍naṃ adhikaraṇānaṃ na katamaṃ adhikaraṇaṃ| sattan‍naṃ samathānaṃ na katamaina samathaina sammanti| taṃ kissa haitu? ṭhapaitvā satta āpattiyau satta āpattikkhandhai, natthañ‍ñā āpattiyauti|

adhikaraṇapac‍cayavārau niṭṭhitau pañ‍camau|

antarapaiyyālaṃ anantarapaiyyālaṃ (sī॰ syā॰) niṭṭhitaṃ|

tassuddānaṃ –

katipucchā samuṭṭhānā, katāpatti tathaiva ca|

samuṭṭhānā vipatti ca, tathādhikaraṇaina cāti||

samathabhaidau

6. adhikaraṇapariyāyavārau

292. vivādādhikaraṇassa  – kiṃ pubbaṅgamaṃ? kati ṭhānāni? kati vatthūni? kati bhūmiyau? kati haitū? kati mūlāni? katihākāraihi vivadati? vivādādhikaraṇaṃ katihi samathaihi sammati?

anuvādādhikaraṇassa – kiṃ pubbaṅgamaṃ? kati ṭhānāni? kati vatthūni? kati bhūmiyau? kati haitū? kati mūlāni? katihākāraihi anuvadati? anuvādādhikaraṇaṃ katihi samathaihi sammati?

āpattādhikaraṇassa – kiṃ pubbaṅgamaṃ? kati ṭhānāni? kati vatthūni? kati bhūmiyau? kati haitū? kati mūlāni? katihākāraihi āpattiṃ āpaj‍jati? āpattādhikaraṇaṃ katihi samathaihi sammati?

kic‍cādhikaraṇassa – kiṃ pubbaṅgamaṃ? kati ṭhānāni? kati vatthūni? kati bhūmiyau? kati haitū? kati mūlāni? katihākāraihi kic‍caṃ jāyati? kic‍cādhikaraṇaṃ katihi samathaihi sammati?

293. vivādādhikaraṇassa  kiṃ pubbaṅgamanti? laubhau pubbaṅgamau, dausau pubbaṅgamau, mauhau pubbaṅgamau, alaubhau pubbaṅgamau, adausau pubbaṅgamau, amauhau pubbaṅgamau| kati ṭhānānīti? aṭṭhārasa bhaidakaravatthūni ṭhānāni| kati vatthūnīti? aṭṭhārasa bhaidakaravatthūni| kati bhūmiyauti? aṭṭhārasa bhaidakaravatthūni bhūmiyau| kati haitūti? nava haitū – tayau kusalahaitū, tayau akusalahaitū, tayau abyākatahaitū| kati mūlānīti? dvādasa mūlāni| katihākāraihi vivadatīti? dvīhākāraihi vivadati – dhammadiṭṭhi vā adhammadiṭṭhi vā| cūḷava॰ 228 vivādādhikaraṇaṃ katihi samathaihi sammatīti? vivādādhikaraṇaṃ  dvīhi samathaihi sammati – sammukhāvinayaina ca yaibhuyyasikāya ca|

294. anuvādādhikaraṇassa kiṃ pubbaṅgamanti? laubhau pubbaṅgamau, dausau pubbaṅgamau, mauhau pubbaṅgamau, alaubhau pubbaṅgamau, adausau pubbaṅgamau, amauhau pubbaṅgamau| kati ṭhānānīti? catassau vipattiyau  ṭhānāni| kati vatthūnīti? catassau vipattiyau vatthūni| kati bhūmiyauti? catassau vipattiyau bhūmiyau| kati haitūti? nava haitū – tayau kusalahaitū, tayau akusalahaitū, tayau abyākatahaitū| kati mūlānīti? cuddasa mūlāni| katihākāraihi anuvadatīti? dvīhākāraihi anuvadati – vatthutau vā āpattitau vā| cūḷava॰ 236 anuvādādhikaraṇaṃ katihi samathaihi sammatīti? anuvādādhikaraṇaṃ catūhi samathaihi sammati – sammukhāvinayaina ca sativinayaina ca amūḷhavinayaina ca tassapāpiyasikāya ca|

295. āpattādhikaraṇassa kiṃ pubbaṅgamanti? laubhau pubbaṅgamau, dausau pubbaṅgamau, mauhau pubbaṅgamau, alaubhau pubbaṅgamau, adausau pubbaṅgamau, amauhau pubbaṅgamau| kati ṭhānānīti? satta āpattikkhandhā ṭhānāni| kati vatthūnīti? satta āpattikkhandhā vatthūni| kati bhūmiyauti? satta āpattikkhandhā bhūmiyau| kati haitūti? cha haitū kati haitūti nava haitū, tayau kusalahaitū (sī॰ syā॰) – tayau akusalahaitū, tayau abyākatahaitū| kati mūlānīti? cha āpattisamuṭṭhānāni mūlāni| katihākāraihi āpattiṃ āpaj‍jatīti? chahākāraihi āpattiṃ āpaj‍jati – alaj‍jitā, añ‍ñāṇatā, kuk‍kuc‍capakatatā, akappiyai kappiyasañ‍ñitā, kappiyai akappiyasañ‍ñitā, satisammausā| cūḷava॰ 239 āpattādhikaraṇaṃ katihi samathaihi sammatīti? āpattādhikaraṇaṃ tīhi samathaihi sammati – sammukhāvinayaina ca paṭiñ‍ñātakaraṇaina ca, sammukhāvinayaina ca tiṇavatthārakaina ca|

296. kic‍cādhikaraṇassa kiṃ pubbaṅgamanti? laubhau pubbaṅgamau, dausau pubbaṅgamau, mauhau pubbaṅgamau, alaubhau pubbaṅgamau, adausau pubbaṅgamau, amauhau pubbaṅgamau| kati ṭhānānīti? cattāri kammāni ṭhānāni| kati vatthūnīti? cattāri kammāni vatthūni| kati bhūmiyauti? cattāri kammāni bhūmiyau| kati haitūti? nava haitū – tayau kusalahaitū, tayau akusalahaitū, tayau abyākatahaitū| kati mūlānīti? aikaṃ mūlaṃ – saṅghau| katihākāraihi kic‍caṃ jāyatīti? dvīhākāraihi kic‍caṃ jāyati – ñattitau vā apalaukanatau vā| cūḷava॰ 242 kic‍cādhikaraṇaṃ  katihi samathaihi sammatīti? kic‍cādhikaraṇaṃ aikaina samathaina sammati – sammukhāvinayaina|

kati  samathā? satta samathā| sammukhāvinayau, sativinayau, amūḷhavinayau, paṭiñ‍ñātakaraṇaṃ, yaibhuyyasikā, tassapāpiyasikā, tiṇavatthārakau – imai satta samathā|

siyā imai satta samathā dasa samathā haunti, dasa samathā satta samathā haunti vatthuvasaina pariyāyaina siyāti|

kathañ‍ca siyā? vivādādhikaraṇassa dvai samathā, anuvādādhikaraṇassa cattārau samathā, āpattādhikaraṇassa tayau samathā, kic‍cādhikaraṇassa aikau samathau| aivaṃ imai satta samathā dasa samathā haunti, dasa samathā satta samathā haunti vatthuvasaina pariyāyaina|

pariyāyavārau niṭṭhitau chaṭṭhau|

7. sādhāraṇavārau

297. kati samathā vivādādhikaraṇassa sādhāraṇā? kati samathā vivādādhikaraṇassa asādhāraṇā? kati samathā anuvādādhikaraṇassa sādhāraṇā? kati samathā anuvādādhikaraṇassa asādhāraṇā? kati samathā āpattādhikaraṇassa sādhāraṇā? kati samathā āpattādhikaraṇassa asādhāraṇā? kati samathā kic‍cādhikaraṇassa sādhāraṇā? kati samathā kic‍cādhikaraṇassa asādhāraṇā?

dvai samathā vivādādhikaraṇassa sādhāraṇā – sammukhāvinayau, yaibhuyyasikā| pañ‍ca samathā vivādādhikaraṇassa asādhāraṇā – sativinayau, amūḷhavinayau, paṭiñ‍ñātakaraṇaṃ, tassapāpiyasikā, tiṇavatthārakau|

cattārau samathā anuvādādhikaraṇassa sādhāraṇā – sammukhāvinayau; sativinayau, amūḷhavinayau, tassapāpiyasikā| tayau samathā anuvādādhikaraṇassa asādhāraṇā – yaibhuyyasikā, paṭiñ‍ñātakaraṇaṃ, tiṇavatthārakau|

tayau  samathā āpattādhikaraṇassa sādhāraṇā – sammukhāvinayau, paṭiñ‍ñātakaraṇaṃ, tiṇavatthārakau| cattārau samathā āpattādhikaraṇassa asādhāraṇā – yaibhuyyasikā, sativinayau, amūḷhavinayau, tassapāpiyasikā|

aikau samathau kic‍cādhikaraṇassa sādhāraṇau – sammukhāvinayau| cha samathā kic‍cādhikaraṇassa asādhāraṇā – yaibhuyyasikā, sativinayau, amūḷhavinayau, paṭiñ‍ñātakaraṇaṃ, tassapāpiyasikā, tiṇavatthārakau|

sādhāraṇavārau niṭṭhitau sattamau|

8. tabbhāgiyavārau

298. kati samathā vivādādhikaraṇassa tabbhāgiyā? kati samathā vivādādhikaraṇassa añ‍ñabhāgiyā? kati samathā anuvādādhikaraṇassa tabbhāgiyā? kati samathā anuvādādhikaraṇassa añ‍ñabhāgiyā? kati samathā āpattādhikaraṇassa tabbhāgiyā? kati samathā āpattādhikaraṇassa añ‍ñabhāgiyā? kati samathā kic‍cādhikaraṇassa tabbhāgiyā? kati samathā kic‍cādhikaraṇassa añ‍ñabhāgiyā?

dvai  samathā vivādādhikaraṇassa tabbhāgiyā – sammukhāvinayau, yaibhuyyasikā| pañ‍ca samathā vivādādhikaraṇassa añ‍ñabhāgiyā – sativinayau, amūḷhavinayau, paṭiñ‍ñātakaraṇaṃ, tassapāpiyasikā, tiṇavatthārakau|

cattārau samathā anuvādādhikaraṇassa tabbhāgiyā – sammukhāvinayau, sativinayau, amūḷhavinayau, tassapāpiyasikā| tayau samathā anuvādādhikaraṇassa añ‍ñabhāgiyā – yaibhuyyasikā, paṭiñ‍ñātakaraṇaṃ, tiṇavatthārakau|

tayau samathā āpattādhikaraṇassa tabbhāgiyā – sammukhāvinayau, paṭiñ‍ñātakaraṇaṃ, tiṇavatthārakau| cattārau samathā āpattādhikaraṇassa añ‍ñabhāgiyā – yaibhuyyasikā, sativinayau, amūḷhavinayau, tassapāpiyasikā|

aikau  samathau kic‍cādhikaraṇassa tabbhāgiyau – sammukhāvinayau| cha samathā kic‍cādhikaraṇassa añ‍ñabhāgiyā – yaibhuyyasikā, sativinayau, amūḷhavinayau, paṭiñ‍ñātakaraṇaṃ, tassapāpiyasikā, tiṇavatthārakau|

tabbhāgiyavārau niṭṭhitau aṭṭhamau|

9. samathā samathassa sādhāraṇavārau

299. samathā samathassa sādhāraṇā, samathā samathassa asādhāraṇā| siyā samathā samathassa sādhāraṇā, siyā samathā samathassa asādhāraṇā|

kathaṃ siyā samathā samathassa sādhāraṇā? kathaṃ siyā samathā samathassa asādhāraṇā? yaibhuyyasikā sammukhāvinayassa sādhāraṇā, sativinayassa amūḷhavinayassa paṭiñ‍ñātakaraṇassa tassapāpiyasikāya tiṇavatthārakassa asādhāraṇā|

sativinayau sammukhāvinayassa sādhāraṇau, amūḷhavinayassa paṭiñ‍ñātakaraṇassa tassapāpiyasikāya tiṇavatthārakassa yaibhuyyasikāya asādhāraṇau|

amūḷhavinayau sammukhāvinayassa sādhāraṇau, paṭiñ‍ñātakaraṇassa tassapāpiyasikāya tiṇavatthārakassa yaibhuyyasikāya sativinayassa asādhāraṇau|

paṭiñ‍ñātakaraṇaṃ sammukhāvinayassa sādhāraṇaṃ, tassapāpiyasikāya tiṇavatthārakassa yaibhuyyasikāya sativinayassa amūḷhavinayassa asādhāraṇaṃ|

tassapāpiyasikā sammukhāvinayassa sādhāraṇā, tiṇavatthārakassa yaibhuyyasikāya sativinayassa amūḷhavinayassa paṭiñ‍ñātakaraṇassa asādhāraṇā|

tiṇavatthārakau sammukhāvinayassa sādhāraṇau, yaibhuyyasikāya sativinayassa amūḷhavinayassa  paṭiñ‍ñātakaraṇassa tassapāpiyasikāya asādhāraṇau| aivaṃ  siyā samathā samathassa sādhāraṇā; aivaṃ siyā samathā samathassa asādhāraṇā|

samathā samathassa sādhāraṇavārau niṭṭhitau navamau|

10. samathā samathassa tabbhāgiyavārau

300. samathā samathassa tabbhāgiyā, samathā samathassa añ‍ñabhāgiyā| siyā samathā samathassa tabbhāgiyā, siyā samathā samathassa añ‍ñabhāgiyā|

kathaṃ siyā samathā samathassa tabbhāgiyā? kathaṃ siyā samathā samathassa añ‍ñabhāgiyā? yaibhuyyasikā sammukhāvinayassa tabbhāgiyā, sativinayassa amūḷhavinayassa paṭiñ‍ñātakaraṇassa tassapāpiyasikāya tiṇavatthārakassa añ‍ñabhāgiyā|

sativinayau sammukhāvinayassa tabbhāgiyau, amūḷhavinayassa paṭiñ‍ñātakaraṇassa tassapāpiyasikāya tiṇavatthārakassa yaibhuyyasikāya añ‍ñabhāgiyau|

amūḷhavinayau sammukhāvinayassa tabbhāgiyau, paṭiñ‍ñātakaraṇassa tassapāpiyasikāya tiṇavatthārakassa yaibhuyyasikāya sativinayassa añ‍ñabhāgiyau|

paṭiñ‍ñātakaraṇaṃ sammukhāvinayassa tabbhāgiyaṃ, tassapāpiyasikāya tiṇavatthārakassa yaibhuyyasikāya sativinayassa amūḷhavinayassa añ‍ñabhāgiyaṃ|

tassapāpiyasikā sammukhāvinayassa tabbhāgiyā, tiṇavatthārakassa yaibhuyyasikāya sativinayassa amūḷhavinayassa paṭiñ‍ñātakaraṇassa añ‍ñabhāgiyā|

tiṇavatthārakau sammukhāvinayassa tabbhāgiyau, yaibhuyyasikāya sativinayassa amūḷhavinayassa paṭiñ‍ñātakaraṇassa tassapāpiyasikāya añ‍ñabhāgiyau| aivaṃ siyā samathā samathassa tabbhāgiyā, aivaṃ siyā  samathā samathassa añ‍ñabhāgiyā|

samathā samathassa tabbhāgiyavārau niṭṭhitau dasamau|

11. samathasammukhāvinayavārau

301. samathau  sammukhāvinayau, sammukhāvinayau samathau? samathau yaibhuyyasikā, yaibhuyyasikā samathau? samathau sativinayau, sativinayau samathau? samathau amūḷhavinayau, amūḷhavinayau samathau? samathau paṭiñ‍ñātakaraṇaṃ, paṭiñ‍ñātakaraṇaṃ samathau? samathau tassapāpiyasikā, tassapāpiyasikā samathau? samathau tiṇavatthārakau, tiṇavatthārakau samathau?

yaibhuyyasikā sativinayau amūḷhavinayau paṭiñ‍ñātakaraṇaṃ tassapāpiyasikā tiṇavatthārakau – imai samathā samathā, nau sammukhāvinayau| sammukhāvinayau samathau caiva sammukhāvinayau ca|

sativinayau amūḷhavinayau paṭiñ‍ñātakaraṇaṃ tassapāpiyasikā tiṇavatthārakau sammukhāvinayau – imai samathā samathā, nau yaibhuyyasikā| yaibhuyyasikā samathau caiva yaibhuyyasikā ca|

amūḷhavinayau paṭiñ‍ñātakaraṇaṃ tassapāpiyasikā tiṇavatthārakau sammukhāvinayau yaibhuyyasikā – imai samathā samathā, nau sativinayau| sativinayau samathau caiva sativinayau ca|

paṭiñ‍ñātakaraṇaṃ tassapāpiyasikā tiṇavatthārakau sammukhāvinayau yaibhuyyasikā sativinayau – imai samathā samathā, nau amūḷhavinayau| amūḷhavinayau samathau caiva amūḷhavinayau ca|

tassapāpiyasikā tiṇavatthārakau sammukhāvinayau yaibhuyyasikā sativinayau amūḷhavinayau – imai samathā samathā, nau paṭiñ‍ñātakaraṇaṃ| paṭiñ‍ñātakaraṇaṃ  samathau caiva paṭiñ‍ñātakaraṇañ‍ca|

tiṇavatthārakau sammukhāvinayau yaibhuyyasikā sativinayau amūḷhavinayau paṭiñ‍ñātakaraṇaṃ – imai samathā samathā, nau tassapāpiyasikā| tassapāpiyasikā samathau caiva tassapāpiyasikā ca|

sammukhāvinayau  yaibhuyyasikā sativinayau amūḷhavinayau paṭiñ‍ñātakaraṇaṃ tassapāpiyasikā – imai samathā samathā, nau tiṇavatthārakau| tiṇavatthārakau samathau caiva tiṇavatthārakau ca|

samathasammukhāvinayavārau niṭṭhitau aikādasamau|

12. vinayavārau

302. vinayau  sammukhāvinayau, sammukhāvinayau vinayau? vinayau yaibhuyyasikā, yaibhuyyasikā vinayau? vinayau sativinayau, sativinayau vinayau? vinayau amūḷhavinayau, amūḷhavinayau vinayau? vinayau paṭiñ‍ñātakaraṇaṃ, paṭiñ‍ñātakaraṇaṃ vinayau? vinayau tassapāpiyasikā, tassapāpiyasikā vinayau? vinayau tiṇavatthārakau, tiṇavatthārakau vinayau?

vinayau siyā sammukhāvinayau siyā na sammukhāvinayau| sammukhāvinayau vinayau caiva sammukhāvinayau ca|
