vinayau siyā yaibhuyyasikā, siyā na yaibhuyyasikā| yaibhuyyasikā vinayau caiva yaibhuyyasikā ca|

vinayau siyā sativinayau, siyā na sativinayau| sativinayau vinayau caiva sativinayau ca|

vinayau siyā amūḷhavinayau, siyā na amūḷhavinayau| amūḷhavinayau vinayau caiva amūḷhavinayau ca|

vinayau siyā paṭiñ‍ñātakaraṇaṃ, siyā na paṭiñ‍ñātakaraṇaṃ| paṭiñ‍ñātakaraṇaṃ vinayau caiva paṭiñ‍ñātakaraṇañ‍ca|

vinayau  siyā tassapāpiyasikā, siyā na tassapāpiyasikā| tassapāpiyasikā vinayau caiva tassapāpiyasikā ca|

vinayau siyā tiṇavatthārakau, siyā na tiṇavatthārakau| tiṇavatthārakau vinayau caiva tiṇavatthārakau ca|

vinayavārau niṭṭhitau dvādasamau|

13. kusalavārau

303. sammukhāvinayau kusalau akusalau abyākatau? yaibhuyyasikā kusalā akusalā abyākatā? sativinayau kusalau akusalau abyākatau? amūḷhavinayau kusalau akusalau  abyākatau? paṭiñ‍ñātakaraṇaṃ kusalaṃ akusalaṃ abyākataṃ? tassapāpiyasikā kusalā akusalā abyākatā? tiṇavatthārakau kusalau akusalau abyākatau?

sammukhāvinayau siyā kusalau, siyā abyākatau| natthi sammukhāvinayau akusalau|

yaibhuyyasikā siyā kusalā, siyā akusalā, siyā abyākatā|

sativinayau siyā kusalau, siyā akusalau, siyā abyākatau|

amūḷhavinayau siyā kusalau, siyā akusalau, siyā abyākatau|

paṭiñ‍ñātakaraṇaṃ siyā kusalaṃ, siyā akusalaṃ, siyā abyākataṃ|

tassapāpiyasikā siyā kusalā, siyā akusalā siyā abyākatā|

tiṇavatthārakau siyā kusalau, siyā akusalau, siyā abyākatau|

vivādādhikaraṇaṃ kusalaṃ akusalaṃ abyākataṃ| anuvādādhikaraṇaṃ kusalaṃ akusalaṃ  abyākataṃ| āpattādhikaraṇaṃ kusalaṃ akusalaṃ abyākataṃ| kic‍cādhikaraṇaṃ  kusalaṃ akusalaṃ abyākataṃ|

vivādādhikaraṇaṃ  siyā kusalaṃ, siyā akusalaṃ, siyā abyākataṃ|

anuvādādhikaraṇaṃ siyā kusalaṃ, siyā akusalaṃ, siyā abyākataṃ|

āpattādhikaraṇaṃ siyā akusalaṃ, siyā abyākataṃ| natthi āpattādhikaraṇaṃ kusalaṃ|

kic‍cādhikaraṇaṃ siyā kusalaṃ, siyā akusalaṃ, siyā abyākataṃ|

kusalavārau niṭṭhitau tairasamau|

14. yatthavārau, pucchāvārau

304. yattha  yaibhuyyasikā labbhati, tattha sammukhāvinayau labbhati; yattha sammukhāvinayau labbhati, tattha yaibhuyyasikā labbhati| na tattha sativinayau labbhati, na tattha amūḷhavinayau labbhati, na tattha paṭiñ‍ñātakaraṇaṃ labbhati, na tattha tassapāpiyasikā labbhati, na tattha tiṇavatthārakau labbhati|

yattha sativinayau labbhati, tattha sammukhāvinayau labbhati; yattha sammukhāvinayau labbhati, tattha sativinayau labbhati| na tattha amūḷhavinayau labbhati, na tattha paṭiñ‍ñātakaraṇaṃ labbhati, na tattha tassapāpiyasikā labbhati, na tattha tiṇavatthārakau labbhati, na tattha yaibhuyyasikā labbhati|

yattha amūḷhavinayau labbhati, tattha sammukhāvinayau labbhati; yattha sammukhāvinayau labbhati, tattha amūḷhavinayau labbhati| na tattha paṭiñ‍ñātakaraṇaṃ labbhati, na tattha tassapāpiyasikā labbhati, na tattha tiṇavatthārakau labbhati, na tattha yaibhuyyasikā labbhati, na tattha sativinayau labbhati|

yattha  paṭiñ‍ñātakaraṇaṃ labbhati, tattha sammukhāvinayau  labbhati; yattha sammukhāvinayau labbhati, tattha paṭiñ‍ñātakaraṇaṃ labbhati| na tattha tassapāpiyasikā labbhati, na tattha tiṇavatthārakau labbhati, na tattha yaibhuyyasikā labbhati, na tattha sativinayau labbhati, na tattha amūḷhavinayau labbhati|

yattha tassapāpiyasikā labbhati, tattha sammukhāvinayau labbhati; yattha sammukhāvinayau labbhati, tattha tassapāpiyasikā labbhati| na tattha tiṇavatthārakau labbhati, na tattha yaibhuyyasikā labbhati, na tattha sativinayau labbhati, na tattha amūḷhavinayau labbhati, na tattha paṭiñ‍ñātakaraṇaṃ labbhati|

yattha tiṇavatthārakau labbhati, tattha sammukhāvinayau labbhati; yattha sammukhāvinayau labbhati, tattha tiṇavatthārakau labbhati| na tattha yaibhuyyasikā labbhati, na tattha sativinayau labbhati, na tattha amūḷhavinayau  labbhati, na tattha paṭiñ‍ñātakaraṇaṃ labbhati, na tattha tassapāpiyasikā labbhati|

yattha yaibhuyyasikā tattha sammukhāvinayau; yattha sammukhāvinayau tattha yaibhuyyasikā| na tattha sativinayau, na tattha amūḷhavinayau, na tattha paṭiñ‍ñātakaraṇaṃ, na tattha tassapāpiyasikā, na tattha tiṇavatthārakau|

yattha sativinayau tattha sammukhāvinayau; yattha sammukhāvinayau tattha sativinayau| na tattha amūḷhavinayau, na tattha paṭiñ‍ñātakaraṇaṃ, na tattha tassapāpiyasikā, na tattha tiṇavatthārakau, na tattha yaibhuyyasikā| sammukhāvinayaṃ  kātuna mūlaṃ…pai॰…|

yattha tiṇavatthārakau tattha sammukhāvinayau; yattha sammukhāvinayau tattha tiṇavatthārakau| na tattha yaibhuyyasikā, na tattha sativinayau, na tattha amūḷhavinayau, na tattha paṭiñ‍ñātakaraṇaṃ, na tattha tassapāpiyasikā|

cak‍kapaiyyālaṃ|

yatthavārau niṭṭhitau cuddasamau|

15. samathavārau, vissaj‍janāvārau

305. yasmiṃ  samayai sammukhāvinayaina ca yaibhuyyasikāya ca adhikaraṇaṃ vūpasammati, yattha yaibhuyyasikā labbhati tattha sammukhāvinayau labbhati, yattha sammukhāvinayau labbhati tattha yaibhuyyasikā labbhati| na tattha sativinayau labbhati, na tattha amūḷhavinayau labbhati, na tattha paṭiñ‍ñātakaraṇaṃ labbhati, na tattha tassapāpiyasikā labbhati, na tattha tiṇavatthārakau labbhati|

yasmiṃ  samayai sammukhāvinayaina ca sativinayaina ca adhikaraṇaṃ vūpasammati, yattha sativinayau labbhati tattha sammukhāvinayau labbhati, yattha sammukhāvinayau labbhati tattha sativinayau labbhati| na tattha amūḷhavinayau labbhati, na tattha paṭiñ‍ñātakaraṇaṃ labbhati, na tattha tassapāpiyasikā  labbhati, na tattha tiṇavatthārakau labbhati, na tattha yaibhuyyasikā labbhati|

yasmiṃ samayai sammukhāvinayaina ca amūḷhavinayaina ca adhikaraṇaṃ vūpasammati, yattha amūḷhavinayau labbhati tattha sammukhāvinayau labbhati, yattha sammukhāvinayau labbhati tattha amūḷhavinayau labbhati| na tattha paṭiñ‍ñātakaraṇaṃ labbhati, na tattha tassapāpiyasikā labbhati, na tattha  tiṇavatthārakau labbhati, na tattha yaibhuyyasikā labbhati, na tattha sativinayau labbhati|

yasmiṃ samayai sammukhāvinayaina ca paṭiñ‍ñātakaraṇaina ca adhikaraṇaṃ vūpasammati, yattha paṭiñ‍ñātakaraṇaṃ labbhati tattha sammukhāvinayau labbhati, yattha sammukhāvinayau labbhati tattha paṭiñ‍ñātakaraṇaṃ labbhati| na tattha tassapāpiyasikā labbhati, na tattha tiṇavatthārakau labbhati, na tattha yaibhuyyasikā labbhati, na tattha sativinayau labbhati, na tattha amūḷhavinayau labbhati|

yasmiṃ samayai sammukhāvinayaina ca tassapāpiyasikāya ca adhikaraṇaṃ vūpasammati, yattha tassapāpiyasikā labbhati tattha sammukhāvinayau labbhati, yattha sammukhāvinayau labbhati tattha tassapāpiyasikā labbhati| na tattha tiṇavatthārakau labbhati, na tattha yaibhuyyasikā labbhati, na  tattha sativinayau labbhati, na tattha amūḷhavinayau labbhati, na tattha paṭiñ‍ñātakaraṇaṃ labbhati|

yasmiṃ samayai sammukhāvinayaina ca tiṇavatthārakaina ca adhikaraṇaṃ vūpasammati, yattha tiṇavatthārakau labbhati tattha sammukhāvinayau labbhati, yattha sammukhāvinayau labbhati tattha tiṇavatthārakau labbhati| na tattha yaibhuyyasikā labbhati, na tattha sativinayau labbhati, na tattha amūḷhavinayau labbhati, na tattha paṭiñ‍ñātakaraṇaṃ labbhati, na tattha tassapāpiyasikā labbhati|

samathavārau niṭṭhitau pan‍narasamau|

16. saṃsaṭṭhavārau

306. adhikaraṇanti  vā samathāti vā imai dhammā saṃsaṭṭhā udāhu visaṃsaṭṭhā? labbhā ca panimaisaṃ dhammānaṃ vinibbhujitvā vinibbhujitvā vinibbhuj‍jitvā vinibbhuj‍jitvā (ka॰), ṭīkāyaṃ aikapadamaiva dissati nānākaraṇaṃ pañ‍ñāpaitunti?

adhikaraṇanti vā samathāti vā imai dhammā visaṃsaṭṭhā, nau saṃsaṭṭhā| labbhā ca panimaisaṃ dhammānaṃ vinibbhujitvā vinibbhujitvā nānākaraṇaṃ pañ‍ñāpaitunti| sau – ‘‘mā haiva’’ntissa vacanīyau| adhikaraṇanti vā samathāti vā imai dhammā saṃsaṭṭhā, nau visaṃsaṭṭhā| nau ca labbhā na ca labbhā (ka॰) imaisaṃ dhammānaṃ vinibbhujitvā vinibbhujitvā nānākaraṇaṃ pañ‍ñāpaituṃ| taṃ kissa haitu? nanu vuttaṃ bhagavatā – ‘‘cattārimāni, bhikkhavai, adhikaraṇāni, satta samathā| adhikaraṇā samathaihi sammanti, samathā adhikaraṇaihi sammanti| aivaṃ, imai dhammā saṃsaṭṭhā nau visaṃsaṭṭhā; nau ca labbhā imaisaṃ dhammānaṃ vinibbhujitvā vinibbhujitvā nānākaraṇaṃ pañ‍ñāpaitu’’nti|

saṃsaṭṭhavārau niṭṭhitau sauḷasamau|

17. sammativārau

307. vivādādhikaraṇaṃ katihi samathaihi sammati? anuvādādhikaraṇaṃ katihi samathaihi sammati? āpattādhikaraṇaṃ katihi samathaihi sammati? kic‍cādhikaraṇaṃ katihi samathaihi sammati?

cūḷava॰ 228, 236 ādayau vivādādhikaraṇaṃ  dvīhi samathaihi sammati – sammukhāvinayaina ca yaibhuyyasikāya ca|

cūḷava॰ 228, 236 ādayau anuvādādhikaraṇaṃ catūhi samathaihi sammati – sammukhāvinayaina ca sativinayaina ca amūḷhavinayaina ca tassapāpiyasikāya ca|

cūḷava॰ 239, 242 ādayau āpattādhikaraṇaṃ tīhi samathaihi  sammati – sammukhāvinayaina ca paṭiñ‍ñātakaraṇaina ca tiṇavatthārakaina ca|

cūḷava॰ 239, 242 ādayau kic‍cādhikaraṇaṃ aikaina samathaina sammati – sammukhāvinayaina|

vivādādhikaraṇañ‍ca  anuvādādhikaraṇañ‍ca katihi samathaihi sammanti? vivādādhikaraṇañ‍ca anuvādādhikaraṇañ‍ca pañ‍cahi samathaihi sammanti – sammukhāvinayaina ca yaibhuyyasikāya ca sativinayaina ca amūḷhavinayaina ca tassapāpiyasikāya ca|

vivādādhikaraṇañ‍ca  āpattādhikaraṇañ‍ca katihi samathaihi sammanti? vivādādhikaraṇañ‍ca āpattādhikaraṇañ‍ca catūhi samathaihi sammanti – sammukhāvinayaina ca yaibhuyyasikāya ca paṭiñ‍ñātakaraṇaina ca tiṇavatthārakaina ca|

vivādādhikaraṇañ‍ca kic‍cādhikaraṇañ‍ca katihi samathaihi sammanti? vivādādhikaraṇañ‍ca kic‍cādhikaraṇañ‍ca dvīhi samathaihi sammanti – sammukhāvinayaina ca yaibhuyyasikāya ca|

anuvādādhikaraṇañ‍ca āpattādhikaraṇañ‍ca katihi samathaihi sammanti? anuvādādhikaraṇañ‍ca āpattādhikaraṇañ‍ca chahi samathaihi sammanti – sammukhāvinayaina ca sativinayaina ca amūḷhavinayaina ca paṭiñ‍ñātakaraṇaina ca tassapāpiyasikāya ca tiṇavatthārakaina ca|

anuvādādhikaraṇañ‍ca kic‍cādhikaraṇañ‍ca katihi samathaihi sammanti? anuvādādhikaraṇañ‍ca kic‍cādhikaraṇañ‍ca catūhi samathaihi sammanti – sammukhāvinayaina ca sativinayaina ca amūḷhavinayaina ca tassapāpiyasikāya ca|

āpattādhikaraṇañ‍ca  kic‍cādhikaraṇañ‍ca katihi samathaihi sammanti? āpattādhikaraṇañ‍ca kic‍cādhikaraṇañ‍ca tīhi samathaihi sammanti – sammukhāvinayaina  ca paṭiñ‍ñātakaraṇaina ca tiṇavatthārakaina ca|

vivādādhikaraṇañ‍ca anuvādādhikaraṇañ‍ca āpattādhikaraṇañ‍ca katihi samathaihi sammanti? vivādādhikaraṇañ‍ca anuvādādhikaraṇañ‍ca āpattādhikaraṇañ‍ca sattahi samathaihi sammanti – sammukhāvinayaina ca yaibhuyyasikāya ca sativinayaina ca amūḷhavinayaina ca paṭiñ‍ñātakaraṇaina ca tassapāpiyasikāya ca tiṇavatthārakaina ca|

vivādādhikaraṇañ‍ca anuvādādhikaraṇañ‍ca kic‍cādhikaraṇañ‍ca katihi samathaihi sammanti? vivādādhikaraṇañ‍ca anuvādādhikaraṇañ‍ca kic‍cādhikaraṇañ‍ca pañ‍cahi samathaihi  sammanti – sammukhāvinayaina ca yaibhuyyasikāya ca sativinayaina ca amūḷhavinayaina ca tassapāpiyasikāya ca|

anuvādādhikaraṇañ‍ca āpattādhikaraṇañ‍ca kic‍cādhikaraṇañ‍ca katihi samathaihi sammanti? anuvādādhikaraṇañ‍ca āpattādhikaraṇañ‍ca kic‍cādhikaraṇañ‍ca chahi samathaihi sammanti – sammukhāvinayaina ca sativinayaina ca amūḷhavinayaina ca paṭiñ‍ñātakaraṇaina ca tassapāpiyasikāya ca tiṇavatthārakaina ca|

vivādādhikaraṇañ‍ca anuvādādhikaraṇañ‍ca āpattādhikaraṇañ‍ca kic‍cādhikaraṇañ‍ca katihi samathaihi sammanti? vivādādhikaraṇañ‍ca anuvādādhikaraṇañ‍ca āpattādhikaraṇañ‍ca kic‍cādhikaraṇañ‍ca sattahi samathaihi sammanti – sammukhāvinayaina ca yaibhuyyasikāya ca sativinayaina ca amūḷhavinayaina ca paṭiñ‍ñātakaraṇaina ca tassapāpiyasikāya ca tiṇavatthārakaina ca|

sammativārau niṭṭhitau sattarasamau|

18. sammanti na sammantivārau

308. vivādādhikaraṇaṃ katihi samathaihi sammati, katihi samathaihi na sammati? anuvādādhikaraṇaṃ katihi samathaihi sammati, katihi samathaihi na sammati? āpattādhikaraṇaṃ katihi  samathaihi sammati, katihi samathaihi na sammati? kic‍cādhikaraṇaṃ katihi samathaihi sammati, katihi samathaihi na sammati?

vivādādhikaraṇaṃ dvīhi samathaihi sammati – sammukhāvinayaina ca yaibhuyyasikāya  ca| pañ‍cahi samathaihi na sammati – sativinayaina ca amūḷhavinayaina ca paṭiñ‍ñātakaraṇaina ca tassapāpiyasikāya ca tiṇavatthārakaina ca|

anuvādādhikaraṇaṃ catūhi samathaihi sammati – sammukhāvinayaina ca sativinayaina ca amūḷhavinayaina ca tassapāpiyasikāya ca| tīhi samathaihi na sammati – yaibhuyyasikāya ca paṭiñ‍ñātakaraṇaina ca tiṇavatthārakaina ca|

cūḷava॰ 239 āpattādhikaraṇaṃ  tīhi samathaihi sammati – sammukhāvinayaina ca paṭiñ‍ñātakaraṇaina ca tiṇavatthārakaina ca| catūhi samathaihi na sammati – yaibhuyyasikāya ca sativinayaina ca amūḷhavinayaina ca tassapāpiyasikāya ca|

kic‍cādhikaraṇaṃ  aikaina samathaina sammati – sammukhāvinayaina| chahi samathaihi na sammati – yaibhuyyasikāya ca sativinayaina ca amūḷhavinayaina ca paṭiñ‍ñātakaraṇaina ca tassapāpiyasikāya ca tiṇavatthārakaina ca|

vivādādhikaraṇañ‍ca anuvādādhikaraṇañ‍ca katihi samathaihi sammanti? katihi samathaihi na sammanti? vivādādhikaraṇañ‍ca anuvādādhikaraṇañ‍ca pañ‍cahi samathaihi sammanti – sammukhāvinayaina ca yaibhuyyasikāya ca sativinayaina ca amūḷhavinayaina ca tassapāpiyasikāya ca| dvīhi samathaihi na sammanti – paṭiñ‍ñātakaraṇaina ca tiṇavatthārakaina ca|

vivādādhikaraṇañ‍ca āpattādhikaraṇañ‍ca katihi samathaihi sammanti? katihi samathaihi na sammanti? vivādādhikaraṇañ‍ca āpattādhikaraṇañ‍ca catūhi samathaihi sammanti – sammukhāvinayaina ca yaibhuyyasikāya ca paṭiñ‍ñātakaraṇaina ca  tiṇavatthārakaina ca| tīhi samathaihi na sammanti – sativinayaina ca amūḷhavinayaina ca tassapāpiyasikāya ca|

vivādādhikaraṇañ‍ca  kic‍cādhikaraṇañ‍ca katihi samathaihi sammanti? katihi samathaihi na sammanti? vivādādhikaraṇañ‍ca kic‍cādhikaraṇañ‍ca dvīhi samathaihi sammanti – sammukhāvinayaina ca yaibhuyyasikāya ca| pañ‍cahi samathaihi na sammanti – sativinayaina ca amūḷhavinayaina ca paṭiñ‍ñātakaraṇaina ca tassapāpiyasikāya ca tiṇavatthārakaina ca|

anuvādādhikaraṇañ‍ca āpattādhikaraṇañ‍ca katihi samathaihi sammanti? katihi samathaihi na sammanti? anuvādādhikaraṇañ‍ca āpattādhikaraṇañ‍ca chahi samathaihi sammanti – sammukhāvinayaina ca sativinayaina ca amūḷhavinayaina ca paṭiñ‍ñātakaraṇaina ca tassapāpiyasikāya ca tiṇavatthārakaina ca| aikaina samathaina na sammanti – yaibhuyyasikāya|

anuvādādhikaraṇañ‍ca kic‍cādhikaraṇañ‍ca katihi samathaihi sammanti? katihi samathaihi na sammanti? anuvādādhikaraṇañ‍ca kic‍cādhikaraṇañ‍ca catūhi samathaihi sammanti – sammukhāvinayaina ca sativinayaina ca amūḷhavinayaina ca tassapāpiyasikā  ca| tīhi samathaihi na sammanti – yaibhuyyasikāya ca paṭiñ‍ñātakaraṇaina ca tiṇavatthārakaina ca|

āpattādhikaraṇañ‍ca kic‍cādhikaraṇañ‍ca katihi samathaihi sammanti? katihi samathaihi na sammanti? āpattādhikaraṇañ‍ca kic‍cādhikaraṇañ‍ca tīhi samathaihi sammanti – sammukhāvinayaina ca paṭiñ‍ñātakaraṇaina ca tiṇavatthārakaina ca| catūhi samathaihi na sammanti – yaibhuyyasikāya ca sativinayaina ca amūḷhavinayaina ca tassapāpiyasikāya ca|

vivādādhikaraṇañ‍ca anuvādādhikaraṇañ‍ca āpattādhikaraṇañ‍ca katihi samathaihi sammanti? katihi samathaihi na sammanti? vivādādhikaraṇañ‍ca anuvādādhikaraṇañ‍ca āpattādhikaraṇañ‍ca sattahi samathaihi sammanti – sammukhāvinayaina ca yaibhuyyasikāya ca sativinayaina ca amūḷhavinayaina ca paṭiñ‍ñātakaraṇaina ca tassapāpiyasikāya ca tiṇavatthārakaina ca|

vivādādhikaraṇañ‍ca anuvādādhikaraṇañ‍ca kic‍cādhikaraṇañ‍ca katihi samathaihi sammanti? katihi samathaihi na sammanti? vivādādhikaraṇañ‍ca anuvādādhikaraṇañ‍ca kic‍cādhikaraṇañ‍ca pañ‍cahi samathaihi sammanti – sammukhāvinayaina ca yaibhuyyasikāya ca sativinayaina ca amūḷhavinayaina ca tassapāpiyasikāya ca| dvīhi samathaihi na sammanti – paṭiñ‍ñātakaraṇaina ca tiṇavatthārakaina ca|

anuvādādhikaraṇañ‍ca  āpattādhikaraṇañ‍ca kic‍cādhikaraṇañ‍ca katihi samathaihi sammanti? katihi samathaihi na sammanti? anuvādādhikaraṇañ‍ca āpattādhikaraṇañ‍ca kic‍cādhikaraṇañ‍ca chahi samathaihi sammanti – sammukhāvinayaina ca sativinayaina ca amūḷhavinayaina ca paṭiñ‍ñātakaraṇaina ca tassapāpiyasikāya ca tiṇavatthārakaina ca| aikaina samathaina na sammanti – yaibhuyyasikāya|

vivādādhikaraṇañ‍ca anuvādādhikaraṇañ‍ca āpattādhikaraṇañ‍ca kic‍cādhikaraṇañ‍ca katihi samathaihi sammanti? katihi samathaihi na sammanti? vivādādhikaraṇañ‍ca anuvādādhikaraṇañ‍ca  āpattādhikaraṇañ‍ca kic‍cādhikaraṇañ‍ca sattahi samathaihi sammanti – sammukhāvinayaina ca yaibhuyyasikāya ca sativinayaina ca amūḷhavinayaina ca paṭiñ‍ñātakaraṇaina ca tassapāpiyasikāya ca tiṇavatthārakaina ca|

sammanti na sammantivārau niṭṭhitau aṭṭhārasamau|

19. samathādhikaraṇavārau

309. samathā  samathaihi sammanti? samathā adhikaraṇaihi sammanti? adhikaraṇā samathaihi sammanti? adhikaraṇā adhikaraṇaihi sammanti?

siyā  samathā samathaihi sammanti, siyā samathā samathaihi na sammanti| siyā samathā adhikaraṇaihi sammanti, siyā samathā adhikaraṇaihi na sammanti| siyā adhikaraṇā samathaihi sammanti, siyā adhikaraṇā samathaihi na sammanti| siyā adhikaraṇā adhikaraṇaihi sammanti, siyā adhikaraṇā adhikaraṇaihi na sammanti|

310. kathaṃ siyā samathā samathaihi sammanti, kathaṃ siyā samathā samathaihi na sammanti? yaibhuyyasikā sammukhāvinayaina sammati; sativinayaina na sammati, amūḷhavinayaina na sammati, paṭiñ‍ñātakaraṇaina na sammati, tassapāpiyasikāya na sammati, tiṇavatthārakaina na sammati|

sativinayau sammukhāvinayaina sammati; amūḷhavinayaina na sammati, paṭiñ‍ñātakaraṇaina na  sammāti, tassapāpiyasikāya na sammati, tiṇavatthārakaina na sammati, yaibhuyyasikāya na sammati|

amūḷhavinayau sammukhāvinayaina sammati; paṭiñ‍ñātakaraṇaina na sammati, tassapāpiyasikāya  na sammati, tiṇavatthārakaina na sammati, yaibhuyyasikāya na sammati, sativinayaina na sammati|

paṭiñ‍ñātakaraṇaṃ sammukhāvinayaina sammati; tassapāpiyasikāya na sammati, tiṇavatthārakaina na sammati, yaibhuyyasikāya na sammati, sativinayaina na sammati, amūḷhavinayaina na sammati|

tassapāpiyasikā sammukhāvinayaina sammati; tiṇavatthārakaina na sammati, yaibhuyyasikāya na sammati, sativinayaina na sammati, amūḷhavinayaina na sammati, paṭiñ‍ñātakaraṇaina na sammati|

tiṇavatthārakau sammukhāvinayaina sammati; yaibhuyyasikāya na sammati, sativinayaina na sammati, amūḷhavinayaina na sammati, paṭiñ‍ñātakaraṇaina na sammati, tassapāpiyasikāya na sammati| aivaṃ siyā samathā samathaihi sammanti| aivaṃ siyā samathā samathaihi na sammanti|

311. kathaṃ  siyā samathā adhikaraṇaihi sammanti, kathaṃ siyā samathā adhikaraṇaihi na sammanti? sammukhāvinayau vivādādhikaraṇaina na sammati, anuvādādhikaraṇaina na sammati, āpattādhikaraṇaina na sammati; kic‍cādhikaraṇaina sammati|

yaibhuyyasikā vivādādhikaraṇaina na sammati, anuvādādhikaraṇaina na sammati, āpattādhikaraṇaina na sammati; kic‍cādhikaraṇaina sammati|

sativinayau vivādādhikaraṇaina na sammati, anuvādādhikaraṇaina na sammati, āpattādhikaraṇaina na sammati; kic‍cādhikaraṇaina sammati|

amūḷhavinayau  vivādādhikaraṇaina na sammati, anuvādādhikaraṇaina na sammati, āpattādhikaraṇaina na sammati; kic‍cādhikaraṇaina sammati|

paṭiñ‍ñātakaraṇaṃ vivādādhikaraṇaina na sammati, anuvādādhikaraṇaina na sammati, āpattādhikaraṇaina na sammati; kic‍cādhikaraṇaina sammati|

tassapāpiyasikā vivādādhikaraṇaina na sammati, anuvādādhikaraṇaina na sammati, āpattādhikaraṇaina na sammati; kic‍cādhikaraṇaina sammati|

tiṇavatthārakau vivādādhikaraṇaina na sammati, anuvādādhikaraṇaina na sammati, āpattādhikaraṇaina na sammati; kic‍cādhikaraṇaina sammati| aivaṃ siyā samathā adhikaraṇaihi sammanti| aivaṃ siyā samathā adhikaraṇaihi na sammanti|

312. kathaṃ siyā adhikaraṇā samathaihi sammanti, kathaṃ siyā adhikaraṇā samathaihi na sammanti? vivādādhikaraṇaṃ sammukhāvinayaina ca yaibhuyyasikāya ca sammati; sativinayaina ca amūḷhavinayaina ca paṭiñ‍ñātakaraṇaina ca tassapāpiyasikāya ca tiṇavatthārakaina ca na sammati|

anuvādādhikaraṇaṃ sammukhāvinayaina ca sativinayaina ca amūḷhavinayaina ca tassapāpiyasikāya ca sammati; yaibhuyyasikāya ca paṭiñ‍ñātakaraṇaina ca tiṇavatthārakaina ca na sammati|

āpattādhikaraṇaṃ sammukhāvinayaina ca paṭiñ‍ñātakaraṇaina ca tiṇavatthārakaina ca sammati; yaibhuyyasikāya ca sativinayaina ca amūḷhavinayaina ca tassapāpiyasikāya ca na sammati|

kic‍cādhikaraṇaṃ  sammukhāvinayaina sammati; yaibhuyyasikāya ca sativinayaina ca amūḷhavinayaina ca paṭiñ‍ñātakaraṇaina ca tassapāpiyasikāya  tiṇavatthārakaina ca na sammati| aivaṃ siyā adhikaraṇā samathaihi sammanti| aivaṃ siyā adhikaraṇā samathaihi na sammanti|

313. kathaṃ  siyā adhikaraṇā adhikaraṇaihi sammanti? kathaṃ siyā adhikaraṇā adhikaraṇaihi na sammanti? vivādādhikaraṇaṃ vivādādhikaraṇaina na sammati, anuvādādhikaraṇaina na sammati, āpattādhikaraṇaina na sammati; kic‍cādhikaraṇaina sammati|

anuvādādhikaraṇaṃ vivādādhikaraṇaina na sammati, anuvādādhikaraṇaina na sammati, āpattādhikaraṇaina na sammati; kic‍cādhikaraṇaina sammati|

āpattādhikaraṇaṃ vivādādhikaraṇaina na sammati, anuvādādhikaraṇaina na sammati, āpattādhikaraṇaina na sammati; kic‍cādhikaraṇaina sammati|

kic‍cādhikaraṇaṃ vivādādhikaraṇaina na sammati, anuvādādhikaraṇaina na sammati, āpattādhikaraṇaina na sammati; kic‍cādhikaraṇaina sammati| aivaṃ siyā adhikaraṇā adhikaraṇaihi sammanti| aivaṃ siyā adhikaraṇā adhikaraṇaihi na sammanti|

chāpi samathā cattāraupi adhikaraṇā sammukhāvinayaina sammanti; sammukhāvinayau na kainaci sammati|

samathādhikaraṇavārau niṭṭhitau aikūnavīsatimau|

20. samuṭṭhāpanavārau

314. vivādādhikaraṇaṃ  catun‍naṃ adhikaraṇānaṃ katamaṃ adhikaraṇaṃ samuṭṭhāpaiti? vivādādhikaraṇaṃ catun‍naṃ adhikaraṇānaṃ na katamaṃ adhikaraṇaṃ samuṭṭhāpaiti; apica, vivādādhikaraṇapac‍cayā cattārau adhikaraṇā jāyanti| yathā kathaṃ viya? cūḷava॰ 215; pari॰ 348 ādayau idha bhikkhū vivadanti – ‘‘dhammauti vā, adhammauti vā, vinayauti vā, avinayauti vā, abhāsitaṃ alapitaṃ tathāgataināti vā, bhāsitaṃ lapitaṃ tathāgataināti vā, anāciṇṇaṃ tathāgataināti vā, āciṇṇaṃ tathāgataināti  vā, apañ‍ñattaṃ tathāgataināti vā, pañ‍ñattaṃ tathāgataināti vā, āpattīti vā, anāpattīti vā, lahukā āpattīti vā, garukā āpattīti vā, sāvasaisā āpattīti vā, anavasaisā āpattīti vā, duṭṭhul‍lā āpattīti vā aduṭṭhul‍lā āpattīti vā’’| yaṃ tattha bhaṇḍanaṃ kalahau viggahau vivādau nānāvādau añ‍ñathāvādau vipac‍catāya vauhārau maidhakaṃ maidhagaṃ (ka॰), idaṃ vuc‍cati vivādādhikaraṇaṃ| vivādādhikaraṇai saṅghau vivadati| vivādādhikaraṇaṃ  vivadamānau anuvadati| anuvādādhikaraṇaṃ anuvadamānau āpattiṃ āpaj‍jati āpattādhikaraṇaṃ| tāya āpattiyā saṅghau kammaṃ karauti kic‍cādhikaraṇaṃ| aivaṃ vivādādhikaraṇapac‍cayā cattārau adhikaraṇā jāyanti|

315. anuvādādhikaraṇaṃ catun‍naṃ adhikaraṇānaṃ katamaṃ adhikaraṇaṃ samuṭṭhāpaiti? anuvādādhikaraṇaṃ catun‍naṃ adhikaraṇānaṃ na katamaṃ adhikaraṇaṃ samuṭṭhāpaiti; apica, anuvādādhikaraṇapac‍cayā cattārau adhikaraṇā  jāyanti| yathā kathaṃ viya? cūḷava॰ 215; pari॰ 348 ādayau idha bhikkhū bhikkhuṃ anuvadanti sīlavipattiyā vā ācāravipattiyā vā diṭṭhivipattiyā vā ājīvavipattiyā vā| yau tattha anuvādau anuvadanā anul‍lapanā anubhaṇanā anusampavaṅkatā abbhussahanatā anubalappadānaṃ, idaṃ vuc‍cati anuvādādhikaraṇaṃ| anuvādādhikaraṇai saṅghau vivadati| vivādādhikaraṇaṃ vivadamānau anuvadati| anuvādādhikaraṇaṃ anuvadamānau āpattiṃ āpaj‍jati āpattādhikaraṇaṃ| tāya āpattiyā saṅghau kammaṃ karauti kic‍cādhikaraṇaṃ| aivaṃ anuvādādhikaraṇapac‍cayā cattārau adhikaraṇā jāyanti|

316. āpattādhikaraṇaṃ catun‍naṃ adhikaraṇānaṃ katamaṃ adhikaraṇaṃ samuṭṭhāpaiti? āpattādhikaraṇaṃ catun‍naṃ adhikaraṇānaṃ na katamaṃ adhikaraṇaṃ samuṭṭhāpaiti; apica, āpattādhikaraṇapac‍cayā cattārau adhikaraṇā jāyanti| yathā kathaṃ viya? pañ‍capi āpattikkhandhā āpattādhikaraṇaṃ, sattapi āpattikkhandhā āpattādhikaraṇaṃ, idaṃ vuc‍cati āpattādhikaraṇaṃ| āpattādhikaraṇai saṅghau vivadati| vivādādhikaraṇaṃ vivadamānau  anuvadati| anuvādādhikaraṇaṃ anuvadamānau āpattiṃ āpaj‍jati āpattādhikaraṇaṃ| tāya  āpattiyā saṅghau kammaṃ karauti kic‍cādhikaraṇaṃ| aivaṃ āpattādhikaraṇapac‍cayā cattārau adhikaraṇā jāyanti|

317. kic‍cādhikaraṇaṃ catun‍naṃ adhikaraṇānaṃ katamaṃ adhikaraṇaṃ samuṭṭhāpaiti? kic‍cādhikaraṇaṃ catun‍naṃ adhikaraṇānaṃ na katamaṃ adhikaraṇaṃ samuṭṭhāpaiti, apica kic‍cādhikaraṇapac‍cayā cattārau adhikaraṇā jāyanti| yathā kathaṃ viya? yā saṅghassa kic‍cayatā karaṇīyatā apalaukanakammaṃ ñattikammaṃ ñattidutiyakammaṃ ñatticatutthakammaṃ, idaṃ vuc‍cati kic‍cādhikaraṇaṃ| kic‍cādhikaraṇai saṅghau vivadati| vivādādhikaraṇaṃ vivadamānau anuvadati| anuvādādhikaraṇaṃ anuvadamānau āpattiṃ āpaj‍jati āpattādhikaraṇaṃ| tāya āpattiyā  saṅghau kammaṃ karauti kic‍cādhikaraṇaṃ| aivaṃ kic‍cādhikaraṇapac‍cayā cattārau adhikaraṇā jāyanti|

samuṭṭhāpanavārau niṭṭhitau vīsatimau|

21. bhajativārau

318. vivādādhikaraṇaṃ catun‍naṃ adhikaraṇānaṃ katamaṃ adhikaraṇaṃ bhajati? katamaṃ adhikaraṇaṃ upanissitaṃ? katamaṃ adhikaraṇaṃ pariyāpan‍naṃ? katamaina adhikaraṇaina saṅgahitaṃ?

anuvādādhikaraṇaṃ catun‍naṃ adhikaraṇānaṃ katamaṃ adhikaraṇaṃ bhajati? katamaṃ adhikaraṇaṃ upanissitaṃ? katamaṃ adhikaraṇaṃ pariyāpan‍naṃ? katamaina adhikaraṇaina saṅgahitaṃ?

āpattādhikaraṇaṃ catun‍naṃ adhikaraṇānaṃ katamaṃ adhikaraṇaṃ bhajati? katamaṃ adhikaraṇaṃ upanissitaṃ? katamaṃ adhikaraṇaṃ pariyāpan‍naṃ? katamaina adhikaraṇaina saṅgahitaṃ?

kic‍cādhikaraṇaṃ catun‍naṃ adhikaraṇānaṃ katamaṃ adhikaraṇaṃ bhajati? katamaṃ adhikaraṇaṃ upanissitaṃ? katamaṃ adhikaraṇaṃ pariyāpan‍naṃ? katamaina adhikaraṇaina  saṅgahitaṃ?

vivādādhikaraṇaṃ catun‍naṃ adhikaraṇānaṃ vivādādhikaraṇaṃ bhajati, vivādādhikaraṇaṃ upanissitaṃ, vivādādhikaraṇaṃ pariyāpan‍naṃ, vivādādhikaraṇaina saṅgahitaṃ|

anuvādādhikaraṇaṃ  catun‍naṃ adhikaraṇānaṃ anuvādādhikaraṇaṃ bhajati, anuvādādhikaraṇaṃ upanissitaṃ, anuvādādhikaraṇaṃ pariyāpan‍naṃ, anuvādādhikaraṇaina saṅgahitaṃ|

āpattādhikaraṇaṃ catun‍naṃ adhikaraṇānaṃ āpattādhikaraṇaṃ bhajati, āpattādhikaraṇaṃ upanissitaṃ, āpattādhikaraṇaṃ pariyāpan‍naṃ, āpattādhikaraṇaina saṅgahitaṃ|

kic‍cādhikaraṇaṃ catun‍naṃ adhikaraṇānaṃ kic‍cādhikaraṇaṃ bhajati, kic‍cādhikaraṇaṃ upanissitaṃ, kic‍cādhikaraṇaṃ pariyāpan‍naṃ, kic‍cādhikaraṇaina saṅgahitaṃ|

319. vivādādhikaraṇaṃ  sattan‍naṃ samathānaṃ kati samathai bhajati, kati samathai upanissitaṃ, kati samathai pariyāpan‍naṃ, katihi samathaihi saṅgahitaṃ, katihi samathaihi sammati?

anuvādādhikaraṇaṃ sattan‍naṃ samathānaṃ kati samathai bhajati, kati samathai upanissitaṃ, kati samathai pariyāpan‍naṃ, katihi samathaihi saṅgahitaṃ, katihi samathaihi sammati?

āpattādhikaraṇaṃ sattan‍naṃ samathānaṃ kati samathai bhajati, kati samathai upanissitaṃ, kati samathai pariyāpan‍naṃ, katihi samathaihi saṅgahitaṃ, katihi samathaihi sammati?

kic‍cādhikaraṇaṃ sattan‍naṃ samathānaṃ kati samathai bhajati, kati samathai upanissitaṃ, kati samathai pariyāpan‍naṃ, katihi samathaihi saṅgahitaṃ, katihi samathaihi sammati?

vivādādhikaraṇaṃ sattan‍naṃ samathānaṃ dvai samathai bhajati, dvai samathai upanissitaṃ, dvai samathai pariyāpan‍naṃ, dvīhi samathaihi saṅgahitaṃ, dvīhi samathaihi sammati – sammukhāvinayaina ca yaibhuyyasikāya ca|

anuvādādhikaraṇaṃ sattan‍naṃ samathānaṃ cattārau samathai bhajati, cattārau samathai upanissitaṃ, cattārau samathai pariyāpan‍naṃ, catūhi samathaihi saṅgahitaṃ, catūhi samathaihi sammati – sammukhāvinayaina ca sativinayaina  ca amūḷhavinayaina ca tassapāpiyasikāya ca|

āpattādhikaraṇaṃ  sattan‍naṃ samathānaṃ tayau samathai bhajati, tayau samathai upanissitaṃ, tayau samathai pariyāpan‍naṃ, tīhi samathaihi saṅgahitaṃ tīhi samathaihi sammati – sammukhāvinayaina ca paṭiñ‍ñātakaraṇaina  tiṇavatthārakaina ca|

kic‍cādhikaraṇaṃ sattan‍naṃ samathānaṃ aikaṃ samathaṃ bhajati, aikaṃ samathaṃ upanissitaṃ, aikaṃ samathaṃ pariyāpan‍naṃ, aikaina samathaina saṅgahitaṃ, aikaina samathaina sammati – sammukhāvinayaināti|

bhajativārau niṭṭhitau aikavīsatimau|

samathabhaidau niṭṭhitau|

tassuddānaṃ –

adhikaraṇaṃ  pariyāyaṃ, sādhāraṇā ca bhāgiyā|

samathā sādhāraṇikā, samathassa tabbhāgiyā||

samathā sammukhā caiva, vinayaina kusalaina ca|

yattha samathasaṃsaṭṭhā, sammanti na sammanti ca||

samathādhikaraṇañ‍caiva, samuṭṭhānaṃ bhajanti cāti||




khandhakapucchāvārau

320.

 upasampadaṃ  pucchissaṃ sanidānaṃ saniddaisaṃ|

samuk‍kaṭṭhapadānaṃ kati āpattiyau|

upasampadaṃ vissaj‍jissaṃ sanidānaṃ saniddaisaṃ|

samuk‍kaṭṭhapadānaṃ dvai āpattiyau||

upausathaṃ pucchissaṃ sanidānaṃ saniddaisaṃ|

samuk‍kaṭṭhapadānaṃ kati āpattiyau|

upausathaṃ vissaj‍jissaṃ sanidānaṃ saniddaisaṃ|

samuk‍kaṭṭhapadānaṃ tissau āpattiyau||

vassūpanāyikaṃ pucchissaṃ sanidānaṃ saniddaisaṃ|

samuk‍kaṭṭhapadānaṃ kati āpattiyau|

vassūpanāyikaṃ vissaj‍jissaṃ sanidānaṃ saniddaisaṃ|

samuk‍kaṭṭhapadānaṃ aikā āpatti||

pavāraṇaṃ pucchissaṃ sanidānaṃ saniddaisaṃ|

samuk‍kaṭṭhapadānaṃ kati āpattiyau|

pavāraṇaṃ vissaj‍jissaṃ sanidānaṃ saniddaisaṃ|

samuk‍kaṭṭhapadānaṃ tissau āpattiyau||

cammasañ‍ñuttaṃ pucchissaṃ sanidānaṃ saniddaisaṃ|

samuk‍kaṭṭhapadānaṃ kati āpattiyau|

cammasañ‍ñuttaṃ  vissaj‍jissaṃ sanidānaṃ saniddaisaṃ|

samuk‍kaṭṭhapadānaṃ tissau āpattiyau||

bhaisaj‍jaṃ pucchissaṃ sanidānaṃ saniddaisaṃ|

samuk‍kaṭṭhapadānaṃ kati āpattiyau|

bhaisaj‍jaṃ vissaj‍jissaṃ sanidānaṃ saniddaisaṃ|

samuk‍kaṭṭhapadānaṃ tissau āpattiyau||

kathinakaṃ  pucchissaṃ sanidānaṃ saniddaisaṃ|

samuk‍kaṭṭhapadānaṃ kati āpattiyau|

kathinakaṃ vissaj‍jissaṃ sanidānaṃ saniddaisaṃ|

samuk‍kaṭṭhapadānaṃ natthi tattha āpatti na katamā āpattiyau (ka॰)||

cīvarasañ‍ñuttaṃ pucchissaṃ sanidānaṃ saniddaisaṃ|

samuk‍kaṭṭhapadānaṃ kati āpattiyau|

cīvarasañ‍ñuttaṃ vissaj‍jissaṃ sanidānaṃ saniddaisaṃ|

samuk‍kaṭṭhapadānaṃ tissau āpattiyau||

campaiyyakaṃ pucchissaṃ sanidānaṃ saniddaisaṃ|

samuk‍kaṭṭhapadānaṃ kati āpattiyau|

campaiyyakaṃ vissaj‍jissaṃ sanidānaṃ saniddaisaṃ|

samuk‍kaṭṭhapadānaṃ aikā āpatti||

kausambakaṃ pucchissaṃ sanidānaṃ saniddaisaṃ|

samuk‍kaṭṭhapadānaṃ kati āpattiyau|

kausambakaṃ  vissaj‍jissaṃ sanidānaṃ saniddaisaṃ|

samuk‍kaṭṭhapadānaṃ aikā āpatti||

kammakkhandhakaṃ pucchissaṃ sanidānaṃ saniddaisaṃ|

samuk‍kaṭṭhapadānaṃ kati āpattiyau|

kammakkhandhakaṃ  vissaj‍jissaṃ sanidānaṃ saniddaisaṃ|

samuk‍kaṭṭhapadānaṃ aikā āpatti||

pārivāsikaṃ pucchissaṃ sanidānaṃ saniddaisaṃ|

samuk‍kaṭṭhapadānaṃ kati āpattiyau|

pārivāsikaṃ vissaj‍jissaṃ sanidānaṃ saniddaisaṃ|

samuk‍kaṭṭhapadānaṃ aikā āpatti||

samuc‍cayaṃ pucchissaṃ sanidānaṃ saniddaisaṃ|

samuk‍kaṭṭhapadānaṃ kati āpattiyau|

samuc‍cayaṃ vissaj‍jissaṃ sanidānaṃ saniddaisaṃ|

samuk‍kaṭṭhapadānaṃ aikā āpatti||

samathaṃ  pucchissaṃ sanidānaṃ saniddaisaṃ|

samuk‍kaṭṭhapadānaṃ kati āpattiyau|

samathaṃ vissaj‍jissaṃ sanidānaṃ saniddaisaṃ|

samuk‍kaṭṭhapadānaṃ dvai āpattiyau||

khuddakavatthukaṃ pucchissaṃ sanidānaṃ saniddaisaṃ|

samuk‍kaṭṭhapadānaṃ kati āpattiyau|

khuddakavatthukaṃ  vissaj‍jissaṃ sanidānaṃ saniddaisaṃ|

samuk‍kaṭṭhapadānaṃ tissau āpattiyau||

saināsanaṃ pucchissaṃ sanidānaṃ saniddaisaṃ|

samuk‍kaṭṭhapadānaṃ kati āpattiyau|

saināsanaṃ vissaj‍jissaṃ sanidānaṃ saniddaisaṃ|

samuk‍kaṭṭhapadānaṃ tissau āpattiyau||

saṅghabhaidaṃ pucchissaṃ sanidānaṃ saniddaisaṃ|

samuk‍kaṭṭhapadānaṃ kati āpattiyau|

saṅghabhaidaṃ  vissaj‍jissaṃ sanidānaṃ saniddaisaṃ|

samuk‍kaṭṭhapadānaṃ dvai āpattiyau||

samācāraṃ pucchissaṃ sanidānaṃ saniddaisaṃ|

samuk‍kaṭṭhapadānaṃ kati āpattiyau|

samācāraṃ vissaj‍jissaṃ sanidānaṃ saniddaisaṃ|

samuk‍kaṭṭhapadānaṃ aikā āpatti||

ṭhapanaṃ pucchissaṃ sanidānaṃ saniddaisaṃ|

samuk‍kaṭṭhapadānaṃ kati āpattiyau|

ṭhapanaṃ vissaj‍jissaṃ sanidānaṃ saniddaisaṃ|

samuk‍kaṭṭhapadānaṃ aikā āpatti||

bhikkhunikkhandhakaṃ pucchissaṃ sanidānaṃ saniddaisaṃ|

samuk‍kaṭṭhapadānaṃ kati āpattiyau|

bhikkhunikkhandhakaṃ  vissaj‍jissaṃ sanidānaṃ saniddaisaṃ|

samuk‍kaṭṭhapadānaṃ dvai āpattiyau||

pañ‍casatikaṃ  pucchissaṃ sanidānaṃ saniddaisaṃ|

samuk‍kaṭṭhapadānaṃ kati āpattiyau|

pañ‍casatikaṃ vissaj‍jissaṃ sanidānaṃ saniddaisaṃ|

samuk‍kaṭṭhapadānaṃ natthi tattha āpatti||

sattasatikaṃ pucchissaṃ sanidānaṃ saniddaisaṃ|

samuk‍kaṭṭhapadānaṃ kati āpattiyau|

sattasatikaṃ vissaj‍jissaṃ sanidānaṃ saniddaisaṃ|

samuk‍kaṭṭhapadānaṃ natthi tattha āpattīti||

khandhakapucchāvārau niṭṭhitau paṭhamau|


tassuddānaṃ –

upasampadūpausathau, vassūpanāyikapavāraṇā|

cammabhaisaj‍jakathinā, cīvaraṃ campaiyyakaina ca||

kausambakkhandhakaṃ kammaṃ, pārivāsisamuc‍cayā|

samathakhuddakā sainā, saṅghabhaidaṃ samācārau|

ṭhapanaṃ bhikkhunikkhandhaṃ, pañ‍casattasataina cāti||


aikuttarikanayau

1. aikakavārau

321. āpattikarā  dhammā jānitabbā| anāpattikarā dhammā jānitabbā| āpatti jānitabbā| anāpatti jānitabbā| lahukā āpatti jānitabbā| garukā āpatti jānitabbā| sāvasaisā āpatti jānitabbā| anavasaisā āpatti jānitabbā| duṭṭhul‍lā āpatti jānitabbā| aduṭṭhul‍lā āpatti jānitabbā| sappaṭikammā āpatti jānitabbā| appaṭikammā āpatti jānitabbā| daisanāgāminī āpatti jānitabbā| adaisanāgāminī āpatti jānitabbā| antarāyikā āpatti jānitabbā| anantarāyikā āpatti jānitabbā| sāvaj‍japañ‍ñatti āpatti jānitabbā| anavaj‍japañ‍ñatti āpatti jānitabbā| kiriyatau samuṭṭhitā āpatti jānitabbā| akiriyatau samuṭṭhitā āpatti jānitabbā| kiriyākiriyatau samuṭṭhitā āpatti jānitabbā| pubbāpatti jānitabbā| aparāpatti jānitabbā| pubbāpattīnaṃ antarāpatti jānitabbā| aparāpattīnaṃ antarāpatti jānitabbā| daisitā gaṇanūpagā āpatti jānitabbā| daisitā na gaṇanūpagā āpatti jānitabbā| pañ‍ñatti jānitabbā| anupañ‍ñatti jānitabbā| anuppan‍napañ‍ñatti jānitabbā| sabbatthapañ‍ñatti jānitabbā| padaisapañ‍ñatti jānitabbā| sādhāraṇapañ‍ñatti jānitabbā| asādhāraṇapañ‍ñatti jānitabbā| aikataupañ‍ñatti jānitabbā| ubhataupañ‍ñatti jānitabbā| thul‍lavaj‍jā āpatti jānitabbā| athul‍lavaj‍jā āpatti jānitabbā| gihipaṭisaṃyuttā āpatti jānitabbā| na gihipaṭisaṃyuttā āpatti jānitabbā| niyatā āpatti jānitabbā| aniyatā āpatti jānitabbā| ādikarau puggalau jānitabbau| anādikarau puggalau jānitabbau| adhic‍cāpattikau puggalau jānitabbau| abhiṇhāpattikau puggalau jānitabbau| caudakau puggalau jānitabbau| cuditakau puggalau jānitabbau| adhammacaudakau puggalau jānitabbau| adhammacuditakau puggalau jānitabbau| dhammacaudakau puggalau jānitabbau| dhammacuditakau puggalau jānitabbau| niyatau puggalau jānitabbau| aniyatau puggalau jānitabbau| abhabbāpattikau puggalau jānitabbau| bhabbāpattikau puggalau jānitabbau| ukkhittakau puggalau jānitabbau| anukkhittakau puggalau jānitabbau| nāsitakau puggalau  jānitabbau| anāsitakau puggalau jānitabbau| samānasaṃvāsakau puggalau jānitabbau| asamānasaṃvāsakau puggalau jānitabbau| ṭhapanaṃ jānitabbanti|

aikakaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

tassuddānaṃ –

karā āpatti lahukā, sāvasaisā ca duṭṭhul‍lā|

paṭikammadaisanā  ca, antarā vaj‍jakiriyaṃ||

kiriyākiriyaṃ pubbā, antarā gaṇanūpagā|

pañ‍ñatti anānuppan‍na, sabbasādhāraṇā ca aikatau pañ‍ñattānuppan‍nā sabbā, sādhāraṇā ca aikatau (syā॰)||

thul‍lagihiniyatā  ca, ādi adhic‍cacaudakau|

adhammadhammaniyatau, abhabbaukkhittanāsitā|

samānaṃ ṭhapanañ‍caiva, uddānaṃ aikakai idanti||

2. dukavārau

322. atthāpatti sañ‍ñā vimaukkhā, atthāpatti nau sañ‍ñāvimaukkhā| atthāpatti laddhasamāpattikassa, atthāpatti na laddhasamāpattikassa| atthāpatti saddhammapaṭisañ‍ñuttā, atthāpatti asaddhammapaṭisañ‍ñuttā| atthāpatti saparikkhārapaṭisañ‍ñuttā, atthāpatti paraparikkhārapaṭisañ‍ñuttā| atthāpatti sapuggalapaṭisañ‍ñuttā, atthāpatti parapuggalapaṭisañ‍ñuttā| atthi sac‍caṃ bhaṇantau garukaṃ āpattiṃ āpaj‍jati, musā bhaṇantau lahukaṃ| atthi musā bhaṇantau garukaṃ āpattiṃ āpaj‍jati, sac‍caṃ bhaṇantau lahukaṃ| atthāpatti bhūmigatau āpaj‍jati, nau vaihāsagatau| atthāpatti vaihāsagatau āpaj‍jati, nau bhūmigatau| atthāpatti nikkhamantau āpaj‍jati, nau pavisantau| atthāpatti pavisantau āpaj‍jati, nau nikkhamantau| atthāpatti ādiyantau āpaj‍jati, atthāpatti anādiyantau  āpaj‍jati| atthāpatti  samādiyantau āpaj‍jati, atthāpatti na samādiyantau āpaj‍jati| atthāpatti karauntau āpaj‍jati, atthāpatti na karauntau āpaj‍jati| atthāpatti daintau āpaj‍jati, atthāpatti na daintau āpaj‍jati| (atthāpatti daisaintau āpaj‍jati, atthāpatti na daisaintau āpaj‍jati|) ( ) (natthi katthaci) atthāpatti paṭiggaṇhantau āpaj‍jati, atthāpatti na paṭiggaṇhantau āpaj‍jati| atthāpatti paribhaugaina āpaj‍jati, atthāpatti na paribhaugaina āpaj‍jati| atthāpatti rattiṃ āpaj‍jati, nau divā| atthāpatti divā āpaj‍jati, nau rattiṃ| atthāpatti aruṇuggai āpaj‍jati, atthāpatti na aruṇuggai āpaj‍jati| atthāpatti chindantau āpaj‍jati, atthāpatti na chindantau āpaj‍jati| atthāpatti chādaintau āpaj‍jati, atthāpatti na chādaintau āpaj‍jati| atthāpatti dhāraintau āpaj‍jati, atthāpatti na dhāraintau āpaj‍jati|

dvai upausathā – cātuddasikau ca pan‍narasikau ca| dvai pavāraṇā – cātuddasikā ca pan‍narasikā ca| dvai kammāni – apalaukanakammaṃ, ñattikammaṃ| aparānipi dvai kammāni – ñattidutiyakammaṃ, ñatticatutthakammaṃ| dvai kammavatthūni – apalaukanakammassa vatthu, ñattikammassa vatthu| aparānipi dvai kammavatthūni – ñattidutiyakammassa vatthu, ñatticatutthakammassa vatthu| dvai kammadausā – apalaukanakammassa dausau, ñattikammassa dausau| aparaipi  dvai kammadausā – ñattidutiyakammassa dausau, ñatticatutthakammassa dausau| dvai kammasampattiyau – apalaukanakammassa sampatti, ñattikammassa sampatti| aparāpi dvai kammasampattiyau – ñattidutiyakammassa sampatti, ñatticatutthakammassa sampatti| dvai nānāsaṃvāsakabhūmiyau – attanā vā attānaṃ nānāsaṃvāsakaṃ karauti, samaggau vā naṃ saṅghau ukkhipati adassanai vā appaṭikammai vā appaṭinissaggai vā| dvai samānasaṃvāsakabhūmiyau – attanā vā attānaṃ  samānasaṃvāsakaṃ karauti, samaggau vā naṃ saṅghau ukkhittaṃ ausāraiti dassanai vā paṭikammai vā paṭinissaggai vā| dvai pārājikā – bhikkhūnañ‍ca bhikkhunīnañ‍ca| dvai saṅghādisaisā, dvai thul‍lac‍cayā, dvai pācittiyā, dvai pāṭidaisanīyā, dvai duk‍kaṭā, dvai dubbhāsitā – bhikkhūnañ‍ca bhikkhunīnañ‍ca| satta āpattiyau, satta āpattikkhandhā| dvīhākāraihi saṅghau bhij‍jati – kammaina vā salākaggāhaina vā|

dvai  puggalā na upasampādaitabbā – addhānahīnau, aṅgahīnau| aparaipi dvai puggalā na upasampādaitabbā – vatthuvipan‍nau, karaṇaduk‍kaṭakau| aparaipi dvai puggalā na upasampādaitabbā – aparipūrau paripūrau nau ca yācati| dvin‍naṃ puggalānaṃ nissāya na vatthabbaṃ – alaj‍jissa ca bālassa ca| dvin‍naṃ puggalānaṃ nissayau  na dātabbau – alaj‍jissa ca laj‍jinau ca na yācati| dvin‍naṃ puggalānaṃ nissayau dātabbau – bālassa ca laj‍jissa ca yācati| dvai puggalā abhabbā āpattiṃ āpaj‍jituṃ – buddhā ca pac‍caikabuddhā ca| dvai puggalā bhabbā, āpattiṃ  āpaj‍jituṃ – bhikkhū ca bhikkhuniyau ca| dvai puggalā abhabbā sañ‍cic‍ca āpattiṃ āpaj‍jituṃ – bhikkhū ca bhikkhuniyau ca ariyapuggalā| dvai puggalā bhabbā sañ‍cic‍ca āpattiṃ āpaj‍jituṃ – bhikkhū ca bhikkhuniyau ca puthuj‍janā| dvai puggalā abhabbā sañ‍cic‍ca sātisāraṃ vatthuṃ ajjhācarituṃ – bhikkhū ca bhikkhuniyau ca ariyapuggalā| dvai puggalā bhabbā sañ‍cic‍ca sātisāraṃ vatthuṃ ajjhācarituṃ – bhikkhū ca bhikkhuniyau ca puthuj‍janā|

dvai paṭik‍kausā – kāyaina vā paṭik‍kausati vācāya vā paṭik‍kausati| dvai nissāraṇā – atthi puggalau appattau nissāraṇaṃ taṃ cai saṅghau nissāraiti aikac‍cau sunissāritau, aikac‍cau dun‍nissāritau| dvai ausāraṇā – atthi puggalau appattau ausāraṇaṃ taṃ cai saṅghau ausāraiti aikac‍cau sausāritau, aikac‍cau dausāritau| dvai paṭiñ‍ñā – kāyaina vā paṭijānāti vācāya vā paṭijānāti| dvai paṭiggahā – kāyaina vā paṭiggaṇhāti kāyapaṭibaddhaina vā paṭiggaṇhāti| dvai paṭikkhaipā – kāyaina vā paṭikkhipati vācāya vā paṭikkhipati| dvai  upaghātikā – sikkhūpaghātikā ca bhaugūpaghātikā ca| dvai caudanā – kāyaina vā caudaiti vācāya vā caudaiti| dvai kathinassa palibaudhā – āvāsapalibaudhau ca cīvarapalibaudhau ca| dvai kathinassa apalibaudhā – āvāsaapalibaudhau ca cīvaraapalibaudhau ca| dvai cīvarāni – gahapatikañ‍ca paṃsukūlañ‍ca| dvai pattā – ayaupattau mattikāpattau| dvai maṇḍalāni – tipumayaṃ, sīsamayaṃ| dvai pattassa adhiṭṭhānā – kāyaina vā adhiṭṭhaiti vācāya vā adhiṭṭhaiti| dvai cīvarassa  adhiṭṭhānā – kāyaina vā adhiṭṭhaiti vācāya vā adhiṭṭhaiti| dvai vikappanā – sammukhāvikappanā ca parammukhāvikappanā ca| dvai vinayā – bhikkhūnañ‍ca bhikkhunīnañ‍ca| dvai  vainayikā – pañ‍ñattañ‍ca pañ‍ñattānulaumañ‍ca| dvai vinayassa sal‍laikhā – akappiyai saitughātau, kappiyai mattakāritā| dvīhākāraihi āpattiṃ āpaj‍jati – kāyaina vā āpaj‍jati vācāya vā āpaj‍jati| dvīhākāraihi āpattiyā vuṭṭhāti – kāyaina vā vuṭṭhāti vācāya vā vuṭṭhāti| dvai parivāsā – paṭicchan‍naparivāsau, appaṭicchan‍naparivāsau| aparaipi dvai parivāsā – suddhantaparivāsau samaudhānaparivāsau| dvai mānattā – paṭicchan‍namānattaṃ, appaṭicchan‍namānattaṃ| aparaipi dvai mānattā – pakkhamānattaṃ, samaudhānamānattaṃ| dvin‍naṃ puggalānaṃ ratticchaidau  – pārivāsikassa ca mānattacārikassa ca| dvai anādariyāni – puggalānādariyañ‍ca dhammānādariyañ‍ca| dvai lauṇāni – jātimañ‍ca kārimañ‍ca jātimayañ‍ca khārimayañ‍ca (syā॰)| aparānipi dvai lauṇāni – sāmuddaṃ kāḷalauṇaṃ| aparānipi dvai lauṇāni – sindhavaṃ, ubbhidaṃ ubbhiraṃ (itipi)| aparānipi dvai lauṇāni – raumakaṃ, pak‍kālakaṃ| dvai paribhaugā – abbhantaraparibhaugau ca bāhiraparibhaugau ca| dvai ak‍kausā – hīnau ca ak‍kausau uk‍kaṭṭhau ca ak‍kausau| dvīhākāraihi paisuñ‍ñaṃ hauti – piyakamyassa vā bhaidādhippāyassa  vā| dvīhākāraihi gaṇabhaujanaṃ pasavati – nimantanatau vā viñ‍ñattitau vā| dvai vassūpanāyikā – purimikā, pacchimikā| dvai adhammikāni pātimaukkhaṭṭhapanāni| dvai dhammikāni pātimaukkhaṭṭhapanāni|

a॰ ni॰ 2.99 ādayau dvai puggalā bālā – yau ca anāgataṃ bhāraṃ vahati, yau ca āgataṃ bhāraṃ na vahati| dvai puggalā paṇḍitā – yau ca anāgataṃ bhāraṃ na vahati, yau ca āgataṃ bhāraṃ vahati| aparaipi dvai puggalā bālā – yau ca akappiyai kappiyasañ‍ñī, yau ca kappiyai akappiyasañ‍ñī| dvai puggalā paṇḍitā – yau ca akappiyai akappiyasañ‍ñī, yau ca kappiyai kappiyasañ‍ñī| aparaipi dvai puggalā bālā – yau ca anāpattiyā āpattisañ‍ñī, yau ca āpattiyā anāpattisañ‍ñī| dvai puggalā paṇḍitā – yau  ca āpattiyā āpattisañ‍ñī, yau ca anāpattiyā anāpattisañ‍ñī| aparaipi dvai puggalā bālā – yau ca adhammai dhammasañ‍ñī, yau ca dhammai adhammasañ‍ñī| dvai puggalā paṇḍitā – yau ca adhammai adhammasañ‍ñī, yau ca dhammai dhammasañ‍ñī| aparaipi dvai puggalā bālā – yau ca avinayai vinayasañ‍ñī, yau  ca vinayai avinayasañ‍ñī| dvai puggalā paṇḍitā – yau ca avinayai avinayasañ‍ñī, yau ca vinayai vinayasañ‍ñī|

a॰ ni॰ 2.109 dvin‍naṃ puggalānaṃ āsavā vaḍḍhanti – yau ca na kuk‍kuc‍cāyitabbaṃ kuk‍kuc‍cāyati, yau ca kuk‍kuc‍cāyitabbaṃ  kuk‍kuc‍cāyati| dvin‍naṃ puggalānaṃ āsavā na vaḍḍhanti – yau ca kuk‍kuc‍cāyitabbaṃ na kuk‍kuc‍cāyati, yau ca kuk‍kuc‍cāyitabbaṃ kuk‍kuc‍cāyati| aparaisampi dvin‍naṃ puggalānaṃ āsavā vaḍḍhanti – yau ca akappiyai kappiyasañ‍ñī, yau ca kappiyai akappiyasañ‍ñī| dvin‍naṃ puggalānaṃ āsavā na vaḍḍhanti – yau ca akappiyai akappiyasañ‍ñī, yau ca kappiyai kappiyasañ‍ñī| aparaisampi dvin‍naṃ puggalānaṃ āsavā vaḍḍhanti – yau ca anāpattiyā āpattisañ‍ñī, yau ca āpattiyā anāpattisañ‍ñī| dvin‍naṃ puggalānaṃ āsavā na vaḍḍhanti – yau ca anāpattiyā anāpattisañ‍ñī, yau ca āpattiyā āpattisañ‍ñī| aparaisampi dvin‍naṃ puggalānaṃ āsavā vaḍḍhanti – yau ca adhammai dhammasañ‍ñī, yau ca dhammai adhammasañ‍ñī| dvin‍naṃ puggalānaṃ āsavā na vaḍḍhanti  – yau ca adhammai adhammasañ‍ñī, yau ca dhammai dhammasañ‍ñī| aparaisampi dvin‍naṃ puggalānaṃ āsavā vaḍḍhanti – yau ca avinayai vinayasañ‍ñī, yau ca vinayai avinayasañ‍ñī| dvin‍naṃ puggalānaṃ āsavā na vaḍḍhanti – yau ca avinayai avinayasañ‍ñī, yau ca vinayai vinayasañ‍ñī|

dukā niṭṭhitā|

tassuddānaṃ –

sañ‍ñā  laddhā ca saddhammā, parikkhārā ca puggalā|

sac‍caṃ bhūmi nikkhamantau, ādiyantau samādiyaṃ||

karauntau daintau gaṇhantau, paribhaugaina ratti ca|

aruṇāchindaṃ chādaintau, dhāraintau ca upausathā||

pavāraṇā kammāparā, vatthu aparā dausā ca|

aparā dvai ca sampatti, nānā samānamaiva ca||

pārājisaṅghathul‍lac‍caya, pācitti pāṭidaisanā|

duk‍kaṭā dubbhāsitā caiva, satta āpattikkhandhā ca||

bhij‍jati  upasampadā, tathaiva aparai duvai|

na vatthabbaṃ na dātabbaṃ, abhabbābhabbamaiva ca||

sañ‍cic‍ca sātisārā ca, paṭik‍kausā nissāraṇā|

ausāraṇā  paṭiñ‍ñā ca, paṭiggahā paṭikkhipā||

upaghāti caudanā ca, kathinā ca duvai tathā|

cīvarā pattamaṇḍalā, adhiṭṭhānā tathaiva dvai||

vikappanā ca vinayā, vainayikā ca sal‍laikhā|

āpaj‍jati ca vuṭṭhāti, parivāsāparai duvai||

dvai mānattā aparai ca, ratticchaidau anādari|

dvai lauṇā tayau aparai, paribhaugak‍kausaina ca||

paisuñ‍ñau ca gaṇāvassa, ṭhapanā bhārakappiyaṃ|

anāpatti adhammadhammā, vinayai āsavai tathāti||

3. tikavārau

323. atthāpatti  tiṭṭhantai bhagavati āpaj‍jati, nau parinibbutai; atthāpatti parinibbutai bhagavati āpaj‍jati, nau tiṭṭhantai; atthāpatti  tiṭṭhantaipi bhagavati āpaj‍jati parinibbutaipi| atthāpatti kālai āpaj‍jati, nau vikālai; atthāpatti vikālai āpaj‍jati, nau kālai; atthāpatti kālai caiva āpaj‍jati vikālai ca| atthāpatti rattiṃ āpaj‍jati, nau divā; atthāpatti divā āpaj‍jati, nau rattiṃ; atthāpatti rattiñ‍caiva āpaj‍jati divā ca| atthāpatti dasavassau āpaj‍jati, nau ūnadasavassau; atthāpatti ūnadasavassau āpaj‍jati, nau  dasavassau; atthāpatti dasavassau caiva āpaj‍jati ūnadasavassau ca| atthāpatti pañ‍cavassau āpaj‍jati, nau ūnapañ‍cavassau; atthāpatti ūnapañ‍cavassau āpaj‍jati, nau pañ‍cavassau; atthāpatti pañ‍cavassau caiva āpaj‍jati ūnapañ‍cavassau ca| atthāpatti kusalacittau āpaj‍jati; atthāpatti akusalacittau āpaj‍jati; atthāpatti abyākatacittau āpaj‍jati| atthāpatti sukhavaidanāsamaṅgī āpaj‍jati; atthāpatti dukkhavaidanāsamaṅgī āpaj‍jati; atthāpatti adukkhamasukhavaidanāsamaṅgī āpaj‍jati| tīṇi caudanāvatthūni  – diṭṭhaina, sutaina, parisaṅkāya| tayau salākaggāhā – guḷhakau, vivaṭakau vivaṭṭakau (ka॰), sakaṇṇajappakau| tayau paṭikkhaipā – mahicchatā, asantuṭṭhitā asantuṭṭhatā (syā॰), asal‍laikhatā| tayau anuñ‍ñātā – appicchatā, santuṭṭhitā, sal‍laikhatā| aparaipi tayau paṭikkhaipā – mahicchatā, asantuṭṭhitā, amattañ‍ñutā| tayau anuñ‍ñātā – appicchatā, santuṭṭhitā, mattañ‍ñutā| tissau pañ‍ñattiyau – pañ‍ñatti, anupañ‍ñatti, anuppan‍napañ‍ñatti| aparāpi tissau pañ‍ñattiyau – sabbatthapañ‍ñatti, padaisapañ‍ñatti, sādhāraṇapañ‍ñatti| aparāpi tissau pañ‍ñattiyau – asādhāraṇapañ‍ñatti, aikataupañ‍ñatti, ubhataupañ‍ñatti|

atthāpatti bālau āpaj‍jati, nau paṇḍitau; atthāpatti paṇḍitau āpaj‍jati, nau bālau; atthāpatti bālau caiva āpaj‍jati paṇḍitau  ca| atthāpatti kāḷai āpaj‍jati, nau juṇhai; atthāpatti juṇhai āpaj‍jati, nau kāḷai; atthāpatti kāḷai caiva āpaj‍jati juṇhai ca| atthi kāḷai kappati, nau juṇhai; atthi juṇhai kappati, nau kāḷai; atthi kāḷai caiva kappati juṇhai ca| atthāpatti haimantai āpaj‍jati, nau gimhai nau vassai; atthāpatti gimhai āpaj‍jati, nau haimantai nau vassai; atthāpatti vassai āpaj‍jati, nau haimantai nau gimhai| atthāpatti saṅghau āpaj‍jati, na gaṇau na puggalau; atthāpatti gaṇau āpaj‍jati, na saṅghau na puggalau; atthāpatti puggalau āpaj‍jati, na saṅghau na gaṇau| atthi saṅghassa kappati, na gaṇassa  na puggalassa; atthi gaṇassa kappati, na saṅghassa na puggalassa; atthi puggalassa kappati, na saṅghassa na gaṇassa| tissau chādanā vatthuṃ chādaiti, nau āpattiṃ; āpattiṃ chādaiti, nau vatthuṃ; vatthuñ‍caiva chādaiti āpattiñ‍ca| tissau paṭicchādiyau – jantāgharapaṭicchādi, udakapaṭicchādi, vatthapaṭicchādi| a॰ ni॰ 3.132 tīṇi paṭicchan‍nāni vahanti, nau vivaṭāni – mātugāmau paṭicchan‍nau vahati, nau vivaṭau; brāhmaṇānaṃ mantā paṭicchan‍nā vahanti, nau vivaṭā; micchādiṭṭhi paṭicchan‍nā  vahati, nau vivaṭā| tīṇi vivaṭāni viraucanti, nau paṭicchan‍nāni – candamaṇḍalaṃ vivaṭaṃ viraucati, nau paṭicchan‍naṃ; sūriyamaṇḍalaṃ vivaṭaṃ viraucati, nau paṭicchan‍naṃ; tathāgatappavaiditau  dhammavinayau vivaṭau viraucati, nau paṭicchan‍nau| tayau saināsanaggāhā – purimakau, pacchimakau, antarāmuttakau| atthāpatti gilānau āpaj‍jati, nau agilānau; atthāpatti agilānau āpaj‍jati, nau gilānau; atthāpatti gilānau caiva āpaj‍jati agilānau ca|

tīṇi adhammikāni pātimaukkhaṭṭhapanāni| tīṇi dhammikāni pātimaukkhaṭṭhapanāni| tayau parivāsā – paṭicchan‍naparivāsau, appaṭicchan‍naparivāsau, suddhantaparivāsau| tayau mānattā – paṭicchan‍namānattaṃ, appaṭicchan‍namānattaṃ, pakkhamānattaṃ| tayau pārivāsikassa bhikkhunau ratticchaidā – sahavāsau, vippavāsau, anāraucanā| atthāpatti antau āpaj‍jati, nau bahi; atthāpatti bahi āpaj‍jati, nau antau; atthāpatti antau caiva āpaj‍jati bahi ca| atthāpatti antausīmāya āpaj‍jati, nau bahisīmāya; atthāpatti bahisīmāya āpaj‍jati, nau antausīmāya; atthāpatti antausīmāya caiva āpaj‍jati bahisīmāya ca| tīhākāraihi āpattiṃ āpaj‍jati – kāyaina āpaj‍jati, vācāya āpaj‍jati, kāyaina vācāya āpaj‍jati| aparaihipi tīhākāraihi āpattiṃ āpaj‍jati – saṅghamajjhai, gaṇamajjhai, puggalassa santikai| tīhākāraihi āpattiyā vuṭṭhāti – kāyaina vuṭṭhāti, vācāya vuṭṭhāti, kāyaina vācāya vuṭṭhāti| aparaihipi tīhākāraihi  āpattiyā vuṭṭhāti – saṅghamajjhai, gaṇamajjhai, puggalassa santikai| tīṇi adhammikāni amūḷhavinayassa dānāni| tīṇi dhammikāni amūḷhavinayassa dānāni|

cūḷava॰ 6 tīhaṅgaihi saman‍nāgatassa bhikkhunau ākaṅkhamānau saṅghau taj‍janīyakammaṃ karaiyya – bhaṇḍanakārakau hauti kalahakārakau vivādakārakau bhassakārakau saṅghai adhikaraṇakārakau, bālau hauti abyattau, āpattibahulau anapadānau gihisaṃsaṭṭhau viharati ananulaumikaihi gihisaṃsaggaihi| cūḷava॰ 15 tīhaṅgaihi saman‍nāgatassa bhikkhunau ākaṅkhamānau saṅghau niyassakammaṃ karaiyya – bhaṇḍanakārakau  hauti…pai॰… saṅghai adhikaraṇakārakau, bālau hauti abyattau, āpattibahulau anapadānau gihisaṃsaṭṭhau viharati ananulaumikaihi gihisaṃsaggaihi| cūḷava॰ 27 tīhaṅgaihi saman‍nāgatassa bhikkhunau ākaṅkhamānau saṅghau pabbājanīyakammaṃ karaiyya – bhaṇḍanakārakau hauti…pai॰… saṅghai adhikaraṇakārakau, bālau hauti abyattau, āpattibahulau anapadānau kuladūkakau hauti pāpasamācārau pāpasamācārā dissanti  caiva suyyanti ca| tīhaṅgaihi saman‍nāgatassa bhikkhunau ākaṅkhamānau saṅghau paṭisāraṇīyakammaṃ karaiyya – bhaṇḍanakārakau hauti…pai॰… saṅghai adhikaraṇakārakau, bālau hauti abyattau, āpatti bahulau anapadānau gihī ak‍kausati paribhāsati| cūḷava॰ 50 tīhaṅgaihi saman‍nāgatassa bhikkhunau ākaṅkhamānau saṅghau āpattiyā adassanai ukkhaipanīyakammaṃ karaiyya  – bhaṇḍanakārakau hauti…pai॰… saṅghai adhikaraṇakārakau, bālau hauti abyattau, āpattibahulau anapadānau āpattiṃ āpaj‍jitvā na icchati āpattiṃ passituṃ| cūḷava॰ 59 tīhaṅgaihi saman‍nāgatassa bhikkhunau  ākaṅkhamānau saṅghau āpattiyā appaṭikammai ukkhaipanīyakammaṃ karaiyya – bhaṇḍanakārakau hauti…pai॰… saṅghai adhikaraṇakārakau, bālau hauti abyattau, āpattibahulau anapadānau āpattiṃ āpaj‍jitvā na icchati āpattiṃ paṭikātuṃ| cūḷava॰ 69 tīhaṅgaihi saman‍nāgatassa bhikkhunau ākaṅkhamānau saṅghau pāpikāya diṭṭhiyā appaṭinissaggai ukkhaipanīyakammaṃ karaiyya – bhaṇḍanakārakau hauti…pai॰… saṅghai adhikaraṇakārakau, bālau hauti abyattau, āpattibahulau anapadānau na icchati pāpikaṃ diṭṭhiṃ paṭinissaj‍jituṃ|

tīhaṅgaihi saman‍nāgatassa bhikkhunau ākaṅkhamānau saṅghau āgāḷhāya caitaiyya – bhaṇḍanakārakau hauti kalahakārakau vivādakārakau bhassakārakau saṅghai adhikaraṇakārakau, bālau hauti abyattau āpattibahulau anapadānau, gihisaṃsaṭṭhau viharati ananulaumikaihi gihisaṃsaggaihi| tīhaṅgaihi saman‍nāgatassa bhikkhunau kammaṃ kātabbaṃ – alaj‍jī ca hauti, bālau ca, apakatattau ca| aparaihipi tīhaṅgaihi saman‍nāgatassa bhikkhunau kammaṃ kātabbaṃ – adhisīlai sīlavipan‍nau hauti, ajjhācārai ācāravipan‍nau hauti, atidiṭṭhiyā diṭṭhivipan‍nau  hauti| aparaihipi tīhaṅgaihi saman‍nāgatassa bhikkhunau kammaṃ kātabbaṃ – kāyikaina davaina saman‍nāgatau hauti, vācasikaina davaina saman‍nāgatau hauti, kāyikavācasikaina davaina saman‍nāgatau hauti| aparaihipi tīhaṅgaihi saman‍nāgatassa bhikkhunau  kammaṃ kātabbaṃ – kāyikaina anācāraina saman‍nāgatau hauti, vācasikaina anācāraina saman‍nāgatau hauti, kāyikavācasikaina anācāraina saman‍nāgatau hauti| aparaihipi tīhaṅgaihi saman‍nāgatassa bhikkhunau  kammaṃ kātabbaṃ – kāyikaina upaghātikaina saman‍nāgatau hauti, vācasikaina upaghātikaina saman‍nāgatau hauti, kāyikavācasikaina upaghātikaina saman‍nāgatau hauti| aparaihipi tīhaṅgaihi saman‍nāgatassa bhikkhunau kammaṃ kātabbaṃ – kāyikaina micchājīvaina saman‍nāgatau hauti, vācasikaina micchājīvaina saman‍nāgatau hauti, kāyikavācasikaina micchājīvaina saman‍nāgatau hauti| aparaihipi tīhaṅgaihi saman‍nāgatassa bhikkhunau kammaṃ kātabbaṃ – āpattiṃ āpan‍nau kammakatau upasampādaiti, nissayaṃ daiti, sāmaṇairaṃ upaṭṭhāpaiti| aparaihipi tīhaṅgaihi saman‍nāgatassa bhikkhunau kammaṃ kātabbaṃ – yāya āpattiyā saṅghaina kammaṃ kataṃ hauti taṃ āpattiṃ āpaj‍jati, añ‍ñaṃ vā tādisikaṃ, tatau vā pāpiṭṭhataraṃ| aparaihipi tīhaṅgaihi saman‍nāgatassa bhikkhunau kammaṃ kātabbaṃ – buddhassa avaṇṇaṃ bhāsati, dhammassa avaṇṇaṃ bhāsati, saṅghassa avaṇṇaṃ bhāsati|

tīhaṅgaihi saman‍nāgatassa bhikkhunau  saṅghamajjhai upausathaṃ ṭhapaintassa – ‘‘alaṃ, bhikkhu, mā bhaṇḍanaṃ mā kalahaṃ mā viggahaṃ mā vivāda’’nti aumadditvā saṅghaina upausathau kātabbau – alaj‍jī ca hauti, bālau ca, apakatattau ca| tīhaṅgaihi saman‍nāgatassa bhikkhunau saṅghamajjhai pavāraṇaṃ ṭhapaintassa – ‘‘alaṃ, bhikkhu, mā bhaṇḍanaṃ mā kalahaṃ mā viggahaṃ mā vivāda’’nti aumadditvā saṅghaina pavāraitabbaṃ – alaj‍jī ca hauti, bālau ca, apakatattau ca| tīhaṅgaihi saman‍nāgatassa bhikkhunau na kāci saṅghasammuti dātabbā – alaj‍jī ca hauti, bālau ca, apakatattau ca| tīhaṅgaihi saman‍nāgataina bhikkhunā saṅghai na vauharitabbaṃ saṃghau na vauharitabbau (syā॰) – alaj‍jī ca hauti, bālau ca, apakatattau ca| tīhaṅgaihi saman‍nāgatau bhikkhu na kismiṃ ci pac‍caikaṭṭhānai ṭhapaitabbau – alaj‍jī ca hauti, bālau ca, apakatattau ca| tīhaṅgaihi saman‍nāgatassa bhikkhunau  nissāya na vatthabbaṃ – alaj‍jī ca hauti, bālau ca, apakatattau ca| tīhaṅgaihi saman‍nāgatassa bhikkhunau nissayau na dātabbau – alaj‍jī ca hauti, bālau ca, apakatattau ca| tīhaṅgaihi saman‍nāgatassa bhikkhunau aukāsakammaṃ kārāpaintassa nālaṃ aukāsakammaṃ kātuṃ – alaj‍jī ca hauti, bālau ca, apakatattau ca| tīhaṅgaihi saman‍nāgatassa bhikkhunau savacanīyaṃ nādātabbaṃ – alaj‍jī ca hauti, bālau  ca, apakatattau ca| tīhaṅgaihi saman‍nāgatassa bhikkhunau vinayau  na pucchitabbau – alaj‍jī ca hauti, bālau ca, apakatattau ca| tīhaṅgaihi saman‍nāgataina bhikkhunā vinayau na pucchitabbau – alaj‍jī ca hauti, bālau ca, apakatattau ca| tīhaṅgaihi saman‍nāgatassa bhikkhunau vinayau na vissaj‍jaitabbau – alaj‍jī ca hauti, bālau ca, apakatattau ca| tīhaṅgaihi saman‍nāgataina bhikkhunā vinayau na vissaj‍jaitabbau – alaj‍jī ca hauti, bālau ca, apakatattau  ca| tīhaṅgaihi saman‍nāgatassa bhikkhunau anuyaugau na dātabbau – alaj‍jī ca hauti, bālau ca, apakatattau ca| tīhaṅgaihi saman‍nāgataina bhikkhunā saddhiṃ vinayau na sākacchitabbau sākacchātabbau (ka॰) – alaj‍jī ca hauti, bālau ca, apakatattau ca| tīhaṅgaihi saman‍nāgataina bhikkhunā na upasampādaitabbaṃ na nissayau dātabbau na sāmaṇairau upaṭṭhāpaitabbau – alaj‍jī ca hauti, bālau ca, apakatattau ca|

tayau upausathā – cātuddasikau, pan‍narasikau, sāmaggiupausathau| aparaipi tayau upausathā – saṅghaiupausathau, gaṇaiupausathau, puggalaiupausathau| aparaipi tayau upausathā – suttuddaisauupausathau, pārisuddhiupausathau, adhiṭṭhānupausathau| tissau pavāraṇā – cātuddasikā, pan‍narasikā, sāmaggipavāraṇā| aparāpi tissau pavāraṇā – saṅghaipavāraṇā, gaṇaipavāraṇā, puggalaipavāraṇā| aparāpi tissau pavāraṇā – taivācikāpavāraṇā, dvaivācikāpavāraṇā, samānavassikāpavāraṇā|

tayau āpāyikā nairayikā – idamappahāya, yau ca abrahmacārī brahmacāripaṭiñ‍ñau, yau ca suddhaṃ brahmacāriṃ parisuddhabrahmacariyaṃ carantaṃ amūlakaina abrahmacariyaina anuddhaṃsaiti, yau cāyaṃ aivaṃvādī aivaṃdiṭṭhi aivaṃdiṭṭhī (sī॰) – ‘‘natthi kāmaisu dausau’’ti sau kāmaisu pātabyataṃ āpaj‍jati| a॰ ni॰ 3.70 tīṇi akusalamūlāni – laubhau akusalamūlaṃ, dausau akusalamūlaṃ, mauhau akusalamūlaṃ| a॰ ni॰ 3.70 tīṇi kusalamūlāni – alaubhau kusalamūlaṃ, adausau kusalamūlaṃ, amauhau kusalamūlaṃ| tīṇi duc‍caritāni – kāyaduc‍caritaṃ, vacīduc‍caritaṃ, manauduc‍caritaṃ| tīṇi sucaritāni – kāyasucaritaṃ, vacīsucaritaṃ, manausucaritaṃ| tayau atthavasai paṭic‍ca bhagavatā kulaisu tikabhaujanaṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ – dummaṅkūnaṃ puggalānaṃ niggahāya, paisalānaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ  phāsuvihārāya, ‘‘mā pāpicchā pakkhaṃ nissāya saṅghaṃ bhindaiyyu’’nti  kulānuddayatāya ca| cūḷava॰ 350 tīhi asaddhammaihi abhibhūtau pariyādin‍nacittau daivadattau āpāyikau nairayikau kappaṭṭhau ataikicchau pāpicchatā pāpamittatā auramattakaina visaisādhigamaina antarā vausānaṃ āpādi| tissau sammutiyau – daṇḍasammuti, sik‍kāsammuti, daṇḍasik‍kāsammuti| tissau pādukā dhuvaṭṭhānikā asaṅkamanīyā – vac‍capādukā, passāvapādukā, ācamanapādukā| tissau pādaghaṃsaniyau – sakkharaṃ, kathalā, samuddaphaiṇakauti|

tikaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

tassuddānaṃ –

tiṭṭhantai  kālai rattiñ‍ca, dasa pañ‍ca kusalaina|

vaidanā caudanā vatthu, salākā dvai paṭikkhipā||

pañ‍ñatti  aparai dvai ca, bālau kāḷai ca kappati|

haimantai saṅghau saṅghassa, chādanā ca paṭicchādi||

paṭicchan‍nā vivaṭā ca, saināsanagilāyanā|

pātimaukkhaṃ parivāsaṃ, mānattā pārivāsikā||

antau antau ca sīmāya, āpaj‍jati punāparai|

vuṭṭhāti aparai caiva, amūḷhavinayā duvai||

taj‍janīyā niyassā ca, pabbājapaṭisāraṇī|

adassanā paṭikammai, anissaggai ca diṭṭhiyā||

āgāḷhakammādhisīlai, davānācāra ghātikā|

ājīvāpan‍nā tādisikā, avaṇṇupausathaina ca||

pavāraṇā sammuti ca, vauhārapac‍caikaina ca|

na vatthabbaṃ na dātabbaṃ, aukāsaṃ na karai tathā||

na karai savacanīyaṃ, na pucchitabbakā duvai|

na vissaj‍jai duvai caiva, anuyaugampi nau dadai||

sākacchā  upasampadā, nissayasāmaṇairā ca|

upausathatikā tīṇi, pavāraṇatikā tayau||

āpāyikā akusalā, kusalā caritā duvai|

tikabhaujanasaddhammai, sammuti pādukaina ca|

pādaghaṃsanikā caiva, uddānaṃ tikakai idanti||

4. catuk‍kavārau

324. atthāpatti  sakavācāya āpaj‍jati, paravācāya vuṭṭhāti; atthāpatti paravācāya āpaj‍jati, sakavācāya vuṭṭhāti; atthāpatti sakavācāya āpaj‍jati, sakavācāya vuṭṭhāti; atthāpatti paravācāya āpaj‍jati, paravācāya vuṭṭhāti| atthāpatti kāyaina āpaj‍jati, vācāya vuṭṭhāti; atthāpatti vācāya āpaj‍jati, kāyaina vuṭṭhāti; atthāpatti kāyaina āpaj‍jati, kāyaina vuṭṭhāti; atthāpatti vācāya āpaj‍jati, vācāya vuṭṭhāti| atthāpatti pasuttau āpaj‍jati, paṭibuddhau vuṭṭhāti; atthāpatti paṭibuddhau  āpaj‍jati, pasuttau vuṭṭhāti; atthāpatti pasuttau āpaj‍jati, pasuttau vuṭṭhāti; atthāpatti paṭibuddhau āpaj‍jati, paṭibuddhau vuṭṭhāti| atthāpatti acittakau āpaj‍jati, sacittakau vuṭṭhāti; atthāpatti sacittakau āpaj‍jati, acittakau vuṭṭhāti; atthāpatti acittakau āpaj‍jati, acittakau vuṭṭhāti; atthāpatti sacittakau āpaj‍jati, sacittakau vuṭṭhāti| atthāpatti āpaj‍jantau daisaiti; daisaintau āpaj‍jati; atthāpatti  āpaj‍jantau vuṭṭhāti; vuṭṭhahantau āpaj‍jati| atthāpatti kammaina āpaj‍jati, akammaina vuṭṭhāti; atthāpatti akammaina āpaj‍jati, kammaina vuṭṭhāti; atthāpatti kammaina āpaj‍jati, kammaina vuṭṭhāti; atthāpatti akammaina āpaj‍jati, akammaina vuṭṭhāti|

a॰ ni॰ 4.250; dī॰ ni॰ 3.313; vibha॰ 939 cattārau anariyavauhārā – adiṭṭhai diṭṭhavāditā, assutai sutavāditā, amutai mutavāditā, aviñ‍ñātai viñ‍ñātavāditā| cattārau ariyavauhārā – adiṭṭhai adiṭṭhavāditā, assutai assutavāditā, amutai amutavāditā, aviñ‍ñātai aviñ‍ñātavāditā| aparaipi cattārau anariyavauhārā – diṭṭhai adiṭṭhavāditā, sutai assutavāditā, mutai amutavāditā, viñ‍ñātai aviñ‍ñātavāditā| cattārau  ariyavauhārā – diṭṭhai diṭṭhavāditā, sutai sutavāditā, mutai mutavāditā, viñ‍ñātai viñ‍ñātavāditā|

cattārau pārājikā bhikkhūnaṃ bhikkhunīhi sādhāraṇā; cattārau pārājikā bhikkhunīnaṃ bhikkhūhi asādhāraṇā| cattārau parikkhārā – atthi parikkhārau rakkhitabbau gaupaitabbau mamāyitabbau paribhuñ‍jitabbau; atthi parikkhārau rakkhitabbau gaupaitabbau mamāyitabbau, na paribhuñ‍jitabbau; atthi parikkhārau rakkhitabbau gaupaitabbau, na mamāyitabbau na paribhuñ‍jitabbau; atthi parikkhārau na rakkhitabbau na gaupaitabbau, na mamāyitabbau na paribhuñ‍jitabbau| atthāpatti  sammukhā āpaj‍jati, parammukhā vuṭṭhāti; atthāpatti parammukhā āpaj‍jati, sammukhā vuṭṭhāti; atthāpatti sammukhā āpaj‍jati, sammukhā vuṭṭhāti; atthāpatti parammukhā āpaj‍jati, parammukhā vuṭṭhāti| atthāpatti ajānantau āpaj‍jati, jānantau vuṭṭhāti; atthāpatti jānantau āpaj‍jati, ajānantau vuṭṭhāti; atthāpatti ajānantau āpaj‍jati, ajānantau vuṭṭhāti; atthāpatti jānantau āpaj‍jati, jānantau vuṭṭhāti|

catūhākāraihi āpattiṃ āpaj‍jati – kāyaina āpaj‍jati, vācāya āpaj‍jati, kāyaina vācāya āpaj‍jati, kammavācāya āpaj‍jati| aparaihipi catūhākāraihi āpattiṃ āpaj‍jati – saṅghamajjhai, gaṇamajjhai, puggalassa santikai, liṅgapātubhāvaina| catūhākāraihi āpattiyā vuṭṭhāti – kāyaina vuṭṭhāti, vācāya vuṭṭhāti, kāyaina vācāya vuṭṭhāti, kammavācāya vuṭṭhāti| aparaihipi catūhākāraihi āpattiyā vuṭṭhāti – saṅghamajjhai, gaṇamajjhai, puggalassa santikai, liṅgapātubhāvaina| saha paṭilābhaina purimaṃ jahati, pacchimai patiṭṭhāti, viñ‍ñattiyau paṭippassambhanti, paṇṇattiyau nirujjhanti| saha paṭilābhaina pacchimaṃ jahati, purimai patiṭṭhāti, viñ‍ñattiyau paṭippassambhanti, paṇṇattiyau nirujjhanti| catassau caudanā – sīlavipattiyā caudaiti, ācāravipattiyā caudaiti, diṭṭhivipattiyā caudaiti, ājīvavipattiyā caudaiti| cattārau parivāsā – paṭicchan‍naparivāsau, appaṭicchan‍naparivāsau, suddhantaparivāsau, samaudhānaparivāsau| cattārau mānattā – paṭicchan‍namānattaṃ, appaṭicchan‍namānattaṃ, pakkhamānattaṃ, samaudhānamānattaṃ| cattārau mānattacārikassa bhikkhunau ratticchaidā – sahavāsau, vippavāsau, anāraucanā, ūnai gaṇai carati| cattārau sāmuk‍kaṃsā| cattārau paṭiggahitaparibhaugā – yāvakālikaṃ, yāmakālikaṃ, sattāhakālikaṃ, yāvajīvikaṃ| cattāri mahāvikaṭāni – gūthau, muttaṃ, chārikā, mattikā| cattāri kammāni – apalaukanakammaṃ, ñattikammaṃ, ñattidutiyakammaṃ, ñatticatutthakammaṃ| aparānipi cattāri kammāni – adhammaina vaggakammaṃ, adhammaina samaggakammaṃ, dhammaina vaggakammaṃ, dhammaina samaggakammaṃ| catassau vipattiyau – sīlavipatti, ācāravipatti, diṭṭhivipatti, ājīvavipatti| cattāri adhikaraṇāni – vivādādhikaraṇaṃ, anuvādādhikaraṇaṃ, āpattādhikaraṇaṃ, kic‍cādhikaraṇaṃ| a॰ ni॰ 4.211 cattārau parisadūsanā – bhikkhu dussīlau pāpadhammau parisadūsanau, bhikkhunī dussīlā pāpadhammā parisadūsanā, upāsakau dussīlau pāpadhammau parisadūsanau, upāsikā dussīlā pāpadhammā parisadūsanā| cattārau parisasaubhanā parisasaubhaṇā (syā॰ ka॰) – bhikkhu sīlavā kalyāṇadhammau parisasaubhanau, bhikkhunī sīlavatī kalyāṇadhammā parisasaubhanā, upāsakau sīlavā kalyāṇadhammau parisasaubhanau, upāsikā sīlavatī kalyāṇadhammā parisasaubhanā|

atthāpatti  āgantukau āpaj‍jati, nau āvāsikau; atthāpatti  āvāsikau āpaj‍jati, nau āgantukau; atthāpatti āgantukau caiva āpaj‍jati āvāsikau ca atthāpatti naiva āgantukau āpaj‍jati, nau āvāsikau| atthāpatti gamikau āpaj‍jati, nau āvāsikau; atthāpatti āvāsikau āpaj‍jati, nau gamikau; atthāpatti gamikau caiva āpaj‍jati āvāsikau ca; atthāpatti naiva gamikau āpaj‍jati nau āvāsikau| atthi vatthunānattatā nau āpattinānattatā, atthi āpattinānattatā nau vatthunānattatā, atthi vatthunānattatā caiva āpattinānattatā ca, atthi naiva vatthunānattatā nau āpattinānattatā| atthi vatthusabhāgatā nau āpattisabhāgatā, atthi āpattisabhāgatā nau vatthusabhāgatā, atthi vatthusabhāgatā caiva āpattisabhāgatā ca atthi naiva vatthusabhāgatā nau āpattisabhāgatā| atthāpatti upajjhāyau āpaj‍jati nau saddhivihārikau, atthāpatti saddhivihārikau āpaj‍jati nau upajjhāyau, atthāpatti upajjhāyau caiva āpaj‍jati saddhivihārikau ca, atthāpatti naiva upajjhāyau āpaj‍jati nau saddhivihārikau| atthāpatti ācariyau āpaj‍jati  nau antaivāsikau, atthāpatti antaivāsikau āpaj‍jati nau ācariyau, atthāpatti ācariyau caiva āpaj‍jati antaivāsikau ca, atthāpatti naiva ācariyau āpaj‍jati nau antaivāsikau| cattārau pac‍cayā anāpatti vassacchaidassa – saṅghau vā bhin‍nau  hauti, saṅghaṃ vā bhinditukāmā haunti, jīvitantarāyau vā hauti, brahmacariyantarāyau vā hauti| cattāri vacīduc‍caritāni – musāvādau, pisuṇā vācā, pharusā vācā, samphappalāpau| cattāri vacīsucaritāni – sac‍cavācā, apisuṇā vācā, saṇhā vācā, mantā bhāsā| atthi  ādiyantau garukaṃ āpattiṃ āpaj‍jati, payaujaintau lahukaṃ; atthi ādiyantau lahukaṃ āpattiṃ āpaj‍jati, payaujaintau garukaṃ, atthi ādiyantaupi payaujaintaupi garukaṃ āpattiṃ āpaj‍jati; atthi ādiyantaupi payaujaintaupi lahukaṃ āpattiṃ āpaj‍jati|

atthi puggalau abhivādanārahau, nau pac‍cuṭṭhānārahau; atthi puggalau pac‍cuṭṭhānārahau, nau abhivādanārahau; atthi puggalau abhivādanārahau caiva pac‍cuṭṭhānārahau ca; atthi puggalau naiva abhivādanārahau nau pac‍cuṭṭhānārahau| atthi puggalau āsanārahau, nau abhivādanārahau; atthi puggalau abhivādanārahau, nau āsanārahau; atthi puggalau āsanārahau caiva abhivādanārahau ca; atthi puggalau naiva āsanārahau nau abhivādanārahau| atthāpatti kālai āpaj‍jati, nau vikālai; atthāpatti vikālai āpaj‍jati, nau kālai; atthāpatti kālai caiva āpaj‍jati vikālai ca; atthāpatti naiva kālai āpaj‍jati nau vikālai| atthi paṭiggahitaṃ kālai kappati, nau vikālai; atthi paṭiggahitaṃ vikālai kappati, nau kālai; atthi paṭiggahitaṃ kālai  caiva kappati  vikālai ca; atthi paṭiggahitaṃ naiva kālai kappati nau vikālai| atthāpatti pac‍cantimaisu janapadaisu āpaj‍jati, nau majjhimaisu; atthāpatti majjhimaisu janapadaisu āpaj‍jati, nau pac‍cantimaisu; atthāpatti pac‍cantimaisu caiva janapadaisu āpaj‍jati majjhimaisu ca; atthāpatti naiva pac‍cantimaisu janapadaisu āpaj‍jati nau majjhimaisu| atthi pac‍cantimaisu janapadaisu kappati, nau majjhimaisu; atthi majjhimaisu janapadaisu kappati, nau pac‍cantimaisu; atthi pac‍cantimaisu caiva janapadaisu kappati majjhimaisu ca; atthi naiva pac‍cantimaisu janapadaisu kappati nau  majjhimaisu| atthāpatti antau āpaj‍jati, nau bahi; atthāpatti bahi āpaj‍jati, nau antau; atthāpatti antau caiva āpaj‍jati bahi ca; atthāpatti naiva antau āpaj‍jati nau bahi| atthāpatti antausīmāya āpaj‍jati, nau bahisīmāya; atthāpatti bahisīmāya āpaj‍jati, nau antausīmāya; atthāpatti antausīmāya caiva āpaj‍jati bahisīmāya ca; atthāpatti naiva antausīmāya āpaj‍jati nau bahisīmāya| atthāpatti gāmai āpaj‍jati, nau arañ‍ñai; atthāpatti arañ‍ñai āpaj‍jati, nau gāmai; atthāpatti gāmai caiva āpaj‍jati arañ‍ñai ca; atthāpatti naiva gāmai āpaj‍jati nau arañ‍ñai|

catassau caudanā – vatthusandassanā, āpattisandassanā, saṃvāsapaṭikkhaipau, sāmīcipaṭikkhaipau| cattārau pubbakic‍cā| cattārau pattakal‍lā| cattāri anañ‍ñapācittiyāni| catassau bhikkhusammutiyau| a॰ ni॰ 4.17; vibha॰ 939; dī॰ ni॰ 3.311 cattāri agatigamanāni – chandāgatiṃ gacchati, dausāgatiṃ gacchati, mauhāgatiṃ gacchati, bhayāgatiṃ gacchati| cattāri nāgatigamanāni – na chandāgatiṃ gacchati, na dausāgatiṃ gacchati, na mauhāgatiṃ gacchati, na bhayāgatiṃ gacchati| catūhaṅgaihi saman‍nāgatau alaj‍jī bhikkhu saṅghaṃ bhindati – chandāgatiṃ gacchantau, dausāgatiṃ gacchantau, mauhāgatiṃ gacchantau, bhayāgatiṃ gacchantau| catūhaṅgaihi saman‍nāgatau paisalau bhikkhu bhin‍naṃ saṅghaṃ samaggaṃ karauti – na chandāgatiṃ gacchantau, na dausāgatiṃ gacchantau, na mauhāgatiṃ gacchantau, na bhayāgatiṃ gacchantau| catūhaṅgaihi saman‍nāgatassa bhikkhunau vinayau na pucchitabbau – chandāgatiṃ gacchati, dausāgatiṃ gacchati, mauhāgatiṃ gacchati, bhayāgatiṃ gacchati| catūhaṅgaihi saman‍nāgataina bhikkhunā vinayau na pucchitabbau – chandāgatiṃ gacchati, dausāgatiṃ gacchati, mauhāgatiṃ gacchati, bhayāgatiṃ gacchati| catūhaṅgaihi saman‍nāgatassa bhikkhunau vinayau na vissaj‍jaitabbau – chandāgatiṃ gacchati, dausāgatiṃ gacchati, mauhāgatiṃ gacchati, bhayāgatiṃ gacchati| catūhaṅgaihi saman‍nāgataina bhikkhunā vinayau na vissaj‍jaitabbau – chandāgatiṃ gacchati, dausāgatiṃ gacchati, mauhāgatiṃ gacchati, bhayāgatiṃ gacchati| catūhaṅgaihi saman‍nāgatassa bhikkhunau anuyaugau na dātabbau – chandāgatiṃ gacchati, dausāgatiṃ gacchati, mauhāgatiṃ gacchati, bhayāgatiṃ gacchati| catūhaṅgaihi  saman‍nāgatai  bhikkhunā saddhiṃ vinayau na sākacchitabbau – chandāgatiṃ gacchati, dausāgatiṃ gacchati, mauhāgatiṃ gacchati, bhayāgatiṃ gacchati| atthāpatti gilānau āpaj‍jati, nau agilānau; atthāpatti agilānau āpaj‍jati, nau gilānau; atthāpatti gilānau caiva āpaj‍jati agilānau ca; atthāpatti naiva gilānau āpaj‍jati nau agilānau| cattāri adhammikāni pātimaukkhaṭṭhapanāni| cattāri dhammikāni pātimaukkhaṭṭhapanāni|

catuk‍kaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

tassuddānaṃ –

sakavācāya kāyaina, pasuttau ca acittakau|

āpaj‍jantau ca kammaina, vauhārā caturau tathā||

bhikkhūnaṃ bhikkhunīnañ‍ca, parikkhārau ca sammukhā|

ajānakāyai majjhai ca, vuṭṭhāti duvidhā tathā||

paṭilābhaina caudanā, parivāsā ca vuc‍cati|

mānattacārikā cāpi, sāmuk‍kaṃsā paṭiggahi||

mahāvikaṭakammāni, puna kammai vipattiyau|

adhikaraṇā  dussīlā ca, saubhanāgantukaina ca||

gamikau vatthunānattā, sabhāgupajjhāyaina ca|

ācariyau pac‍cayā vā, duc‍caritaṃ sucaritaṃ||

ādiyantau puggalau ca, arahau āsanaina ca|

kālai ca kappati caiva, pac‍cantimaisu kappati||

antau antau ca sīmāya, gāmai ca caudanāya ca|

pubbakic‍caṃ pattakal‍laṃ, anañ‍ñā sammutiyau ca||

agati  nāgati caiva, alaj‍jī paisalaina ca|

pucchitabbā duvai caiva, vissaj‍jaiyyā tathā duvai|

anuyaugau ca sākacchā, gilānau ṭhapanaina cāti||

5. pañ‍cakavārau

325. pañ‍ca  āpattiyau| pañ‍ca āpattikkhandhā| pañ‍ca vinītavatthūni| pañ‍ca kammāni ānantarikāni| pañ‍ca puggalā niyatā| pañ‍ca chaidanakā āpattiyau| pañ‍cahākāraihi āpattiṃ āpaj‍jati| pañ‍ca āpattiyau| musāvādapac‍cayā pañ‍cahākāraihi kammaṃ na upaiti – sayaṃ vā kammaṃ na karauti, paraṃ vā na ajjhaisati, chandaṃ vā pārisuddhiṃ vā na daiti, kayiramānai kammai paṭik‍kausati, katai vā pana kammai adhammadiṭṭhi hauti| pañ‍cahākāraihi kammaṃ upaiti  – sayaṃ vā kammaṃ karauti, paraṃ vā ajjhaisati, chandaṃ vā pārisuddhiṃ vā daiti, kayiramānai kammai nappaṭik‍kausati, katai vā pana kammai dhammadiṭṭhi hauti| pañ‍ca piṇḍapātikassa bhikkhunau kappanti – anāmantacārau, gaṇabhaujanaṃ, paramparabhaujanaṃ, anadhiṭṭhānaṃ, avikappanā| a॰ ni॰ 5.102 pañ‍cahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgatau bhikkhu ussaṅkitaparisaṅkitau  hauti – pāpabhikkhupi akuppadhammaupi vaisiyāgaucarau vā hauti, vidhavāgaucarau vā hauti, thul‍lakumārigaucarau vā hauti, paṇḍakagaucarau vā hauti, bhikkhunigaucarau vā hauti| pañ‍ca tailāni – tilatailaṃ, sāsapatailaṃ, madhukatailaṃ, airaṇḍakatailaṃ, vasātailaṃ| pañ‍ca vasāni – acchavasaṃ, macchavasaṃ, susukāvasaṃ, sūkaravasaṃ, gadrabhavasaṃ| a॰ ni॰ 5.130; dī॰ ni॰ 3.316 pañ‍ca byasanāni – ñātibyasanaṃ, bhaugabyasanaṃ, raugabyasanaṃ, sīlabyasanaṃ, diṭṭhibyasanaṃ| a॰ ni॰ 5.130; dī॰ ni॰ 3.316 pañ‍ca sampadā – ñātisampadā, bhaugasampadā, araugasampadā, sīlasampadā, diṭṭhisampadā| pañ‍ca nissayapaṭippassaddhiyau upajjhāyamhā – upajjhāyau pak‍kantau vā hauti, vibbhantau vā, kālaṅkatau vā, pakkhasaṅkantau vā, āṇattiyaiva pañ‍camī| pañ‍ca puggalā na upasampādaitabbā – addhānahīnau, aṅgahīnau, vatthuvipan‍nau, karaṇaduk‍kaṭakau, aparipūrau| pañ‍ca paṃsukūlāni – sausānikaṃ, pāpaṇikaṃ, undūrakkhāyikaṃ, upacikakkhāyikaṃ, aggidaḍḍhaṃ| aparānipi pañ‍ca paṃsukūlāni – gaukhāyikaṃ, ajakkhāyikaṃ, thūpacīvaraṃ, ābhisaikikaṃ, gatapaṭiyāgataṃ bhatapaṭiyābhataṃ (ka॰)| pañ‍ca avahārā – thaiyyāvahārau, pasayhāvahārau, parikappāvahārau, paṭicchan‍nāvahārau, kusāvahārau| pañ‍ca mahācaurā santau saṃvij‍jamānā laukasmiṃ| pañ‍ca avissaj‍janiyāni| pañ‍ca avaibhaṅgiyāni| pañ‍cāpattiyau kāyatau samuṭṭhanti, na vācatau na cittatau| pañ‍cāpattiyau kāyatau ca vācatau ca samuṭṭhanti,  cittatau| pañ‍cāpattiyau daisanāgāminiyau| pañ‍ca saṅghā| pañ‍ca pātimaukkhuddaisā| sabbapac‍cantimaisu janapadaisu vinayadharapañ‍camaina gaṇaina upasampādaitabbaṃ| pañ‍cānisaṃsā kathinatthārai| pañ‍ca kammāni| yāvatatiyakai pañ‍ca āpattiyau| pañ‍cahākāraihi adin‍naṃ ādiyantassa āpatti pārājikassa| pañ‍cahākāraihi adin‍naṃ ādiyantassa āpatti thul‍lac‍cayassa| pañ‍cahākāraihi adin‍naṃ ādiyantassa āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| pañ‍ca akappiyāni na paribhuñ‍jitabbāni – adin‍nañ‍ca hauti, aviditañ‍ca hauti, akappiyañ‍ca hauti, appaṭiggahitañ‍ca hauti, akatātirittañ‍ca hauti| pañ‍ca kappiyāni paribhuñ‍jitabbāni – din‍nañ‍ca hauti, viditañ‍ca hauti, kappiyañ‍ca hauti, paṭiggahitañ‍ca hauti, katātirittañ‍ca hauti| pañ‍ca dānāni apuñ‍ñāni puñ‍ñasammatāni laukasmiṃ laukassa (syā॰) – maj‍jadānaṃ, samaj‍jadānaṃ, itthidānaṃ, usabhadānaṃ, cittakammadānaṃ| pañ‍ca uppan‍nā duppaṭivinaudayā vinaudiyā (syā॰) – uppan‍nau rāgau duppaṭivinaudayau, uppan‍nau dausau duppaṭivinaudayau, uppan‍nau mauhau duppaṭivinaudayau, uppan‍naṃ paṭibhānaṃ duppaṭivinaudayaṃ, uppan‍naṃ gamiyacittaṃ duppaṭivinaudayaṃ| pañ‍cānisaṃsā sammaj‍janiyā – sakacittaṃ pasīdati, paracittaṃ pasīdati, daivatā attamanā haunti, pāsādikasaṃvattanikakammaṃ  upacinati, kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā sugatiṃ saggaṃ laukaṃ upapaj‍jati| aparaipi pañ‍cānisaṃsā sammaj‍janiyā – sakacittaṃ pasīdati, paracittaṃ pasīdati, daivatā attamanā haunti, satthusāsanaṃ kataṃ hauti, pacchimā janatā diṭṭhānugatiṃ āpaj‍jati|

pañ‍cahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgatau vinayadharau ‘‘bālau’’ tvaiva saṅkhaṃ gacchati – attanau bhāsapariyantaṃ na uggaṇhāti, parassa bhāsapariyantaṃ na uggaṇhāti, attanau bhāsapariyantaṃ na uggahaitvā parassa bhāsapariyantaṃ na uggahaitvā adhammaina kāraiti appaṭiñ‍ñāya| pañ‍cahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgatau vinayadharau ‘‘paṇḍitau’’ tvaiva saṅkhaṃ gacchati – attanau bhāsapariyantaṃ uggaṇhāti, parassa bhāsapariyantaṃ uggaṇhāti, attanau bhāsapariyantaṃ uggahaitvā parassa bhāsapariyantaṃ uggahaitvā dhammaina kāraiti paṭiñ‍ñāya| aparaihipi pañ‍cahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgatau vinayadharau bālau tvaiva saṅkhaṃ gacchati – āpattiṃ na jānāti, āpattiyā mūlaṃ na jānāti, āpattisamudayaṃ  na jānāti, āpattiniraudhaṃ na jānāti, āpattiniraudhagāminiṃ paṭipadaṃ na jānāti| pañ‍cahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgatau vinayadharau paṇḍitau tvaiva saṅkhaṃ gacchati – āpattiṃ jānāti, āpattiyā mūlaṃ jānāti, āpattisamudayaṃ jānāti, āpattiniraudhaṃ jānāti, āpattiniraudhagāminiṃ paṭipadaṃ jānāti| aparaihipi pañ‍cahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgatau vinayadharau bālau tvaiva saṅkhaṃ gacchati – adhikaraṇaṃ na jānāti, adhikaraṇassa mūlaṃ na jānāti, adhikaraṇasamudayaṃ na jānāti, adhikaraṇaniraudhaṃ na jānāti, adhikaraṇaniraudhagāminiṃ anupañ‍ñattiṃ na jānāti, anusandhivacanapathaṃ na jānāti| pañ‍cahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgatau vinayadharau paṇḍitau tvaiva saṅkhaṃ gacchati – vatthuṃ jānāti, nidānaṃ jānāti, pañ‍ñattiṃ jānāti, anupañ‍ñattiṃ jānāti, anusandhivacanapathaṃ jānāti| aparaihipi pañ‍cahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgatau vinayadharau bālau tvaiva saṅkhaṃ gacchati – ñattiṃ na jānāti, ñattiyā karaṇaṃ na jānāti, na pubbakusalau hauti, na aparakusalau hauti, akālañ‍ñū ca hauti| pañ‍cahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgatau vinayadharau paṇḍitau tvaiva saṅkhaṃ gacchati – ñattiṃ jānāti, ñattiyā karaṇaṃ jānāti, pubbakusalau hauti, aparakusalau hauti, kālañ‍ñū ca hauti| aparaihipi pañ‍cahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgatau vinayadharau bālau tvaiva saṅkhaṃ  gacchati – āpattānāpattiṃ na jānāti, lahukagarukaṃ āpattiṃ na jānāti, sāvasaisānavasaisaṃ āpattiṃ na jānāti, duṭṭhul‍lāduṭṭhul‍laṃ āpattiṃ na jānāti, ācariyaparamparā khau panassa na suggahitā hauti na sumanasikatā na sūpadhāritā| pañ‍cahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgatau vinayadharau paṇḍitautvaiva saṅkhaṃ a॰ ni॰ 5.181; pari॰ 443 pañ‍ca  ārañ‍ñikā – mandattā maumūhattā ārañ‍ñikau hauti, pāpicchau icchāpakatau ārañ‍ñikau hauti, ummādā cittakkhaipā ārañ‍ñikau hauti, vaṇṇitaṃ buddhaihi buddhasāvakaihīti ārañ‍ñikau hauti, api ca appicchañ‍ñaiva nissāya santuṭṭhiñ‍ñaiva nissāya sal‍laikhañ‍ñaiva nissāya pavivaikañ‍ñaiva nissāya idamatthitañ‍ñaiva idamaṭṭhitañ‍ñaiva (sī॰ syā॰) nissāya ārañ‍ñikau hauti| pañ‍ca piṇḍapātikā…pai॰… pañ‍ca paṃsukūlikā…pai॰… pañ‍ca rukkhamūlikā…pai॰… pañ‍ca sausānikā…pai॰… pañ‍ca abbhaukāsikā…pai॰… pañ‍ca taicīvarikā…pai॰… pañ‍ca sapadānacārikā…pai॰… pañ‍ca naisaj‍jikā…pai॰… pañ‍ca yathāsanthatikā…pai॰… pañ‍ca aikāsanikā…pai॰… pañ‍ca khalupacchābhattikā…pai॰… pañ‍ca pattapiṇḍikā – mandattā maumūhattā pattapiṇḍikau hauti, pāpicchau icchāpakatau pattapiṇḍikau hauti, ummādā cittakkhaipā pattapiṇḍikau hauti, vaṇṇitaṃ buddhaihi buddhasāvakaihīti pattapiṇḍikau hauti, api ca appicchañ‍ñaiva  nissāya santuṭṭhiñ‍ñaiva nissāya sal‍laikhañ‍ñaiva nissāya pavivaikañ‍ñaiva nissāya idamatthitañ‍ñaiva nissāya pattapiṇḍikau hauti|

pari॰ 417 pañ‍cahaṅgaihi  saman‍nāgataina bhikkhunā nānissitaina vatthabbaṃ – upausathaṃ na jānāti, upausathakammaṃ na jānāti, pātimaukkhaṃ na jānāti, pātimaukkhuddaisaṃ na jānāti, ūnapañ‍cavassau hauti| pañ‍cahaṅgaihi  saman‍nāgataina bhikkhunā anissitaina vatthabbaṃ – upausathaṃ jānāti, upausathakammaṃ jānāti, pātimaukkhaṃ jānāti, pātimaukkhuddaisaṃ jānāti, pañ‍cavassau vā hauti atiraikapañ‍cavassau vā| aparaihipi pañ‍cahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgataina bhikkhunā nānissitaina vatthabbaṃ – pavāraṇaṃ na jānāti, pavāraṇākammaṃ na jānāti, pātimaukkhaṃ na jānāti, pātimaukkhuddaisaṃ na jānāti, ūnapañ‍cavassau hauti| pañ‍cahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgataina bhikkhunā anissitaina vatthabbaṃ – pavāraṇaṃ jānāti, pavāraṇākammaṃ jānāti, pātimaukkhaṃ jānāti, pātimaukkhuddaisaṃ jānāti, pañ‍cavassau vā hauti atiraikapañ‍cavassau vā| aparaihipi pañ‍cahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgataina bhikkhunā nānissitaina vatthabbaṃ – āpattānāpattiṃ na jānāti, lahukagarukaṃ āpattiṃ na jānāti, sāvasaisānavasaisaṃ āpattiṃ na jānāti, duṭṭhul‍lāduṭṭhul‍laṃ āpattiṃ na jānāti, ūnapañ‍cavassau hauti| pañ‍cahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgataina bhikkhunā anissitaina vatthabbaṃ – āpattānāpattiṃ jānāti, lahukagarukaṃ āpattiṃ  jānāti, sāvasaisānavasaisaṃ āpattiṃ jānāti, duṭṭhul‍lāduṭṭhul‍laṃ āpattiṃ jānāti, pañ‍cavassau vā hauti atiraikapañ‍cavassau vā| pañ‍cahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgatāya bhikkhuniyā nānissitāya vatthabbaṃ – upausathaṃ na jānāti, upausathakammaṃ na jānāti, pātimaukkhaṃ na jānāti, pātimaukkhuddaisaṃ na jānāti, ūnapañ‍cavassā hauti| pañ‍cahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgatāya bhikkhuniyā anissitāya vatthabbaṃ  – upausathaṃ jānāti, upausathakammaṃ jānāti, pātimaukkhaṃ jānāti, pātimaukkhuddaisaṃ jānāti, pañ‍cavassā vā hauti atiraikapañ‍cavassā vā| aparaihipi pañ‍cahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgatāya bhikkhuniyā nānissitāya vatthabbaṃ – pavāraṇaṃ na jānāti, pavāraṇākammaṃ na jānāti, pātimaukkhaṃ na jānāti, pātimaukkhuddaisaṃ na jānāti, ūnapañ‍cavassā hauti| pañ‍cahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgatāya bhikkhuniyā anissitāya vatthabbaṃ – pavāraṇaṃ jānāti, pavāraṇākammaṃ jānāti, pātimaukkhaṃ jānāti, pātimaukkhuddaisaṃ jānāti, pañ‍cavassā vā hauti atiraikapañ‍cavassā vā| aparaihipi pañ‍cahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgatāya bhikkhuniyā nānissitāya vatthabbaṃ  – āpattānāpattiṃ na jānāti, lahukagarukaṃ āpattiṃ na jānāti, sāvasaisānavasaisaṃ āpattiṃ na jānāti, duṭṭhul‍lāduṭṭhul‍laṃ āpattiṃ na jānāti, ūnapañ‍cavassā hauti| pañ‍cahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgatāya bhikkhuniyā anissitāya vatthabbaṃ – āpattānāpattiṃ jānāti, lahukagarukaṃ āpattiṃ jānāti, sāvasaisānavasaisaṃ āpattiṃ jānāti, duṭṭhul‍lāduṭṭhul‍laṃ āpattiṃ  jānāti, pañ‍cavassā vā hauti atiraikapañ‍cavassā vā|

pañ‍ca ādīnavā apāsādikai – attāpi attānaṃ upavadati, anuvic‍capi viñ‍ñū garahanti, pāpakau kittisaddau abbhuggacchati, sammūḷhau kālaṃ karauti, kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā apāyaṃ duggatiṃ vinipātaṃ nirayaṃ upapaj‍jati| pañ‍cānisaṃsā pāsādikai – attāpi attānaṃ na upavadati, anuvic‍capi viñ‍ñū pasaṃsanti, kalyāṇau kittisaddau abbhuggacchati, asammūḷhau kālaṃ karauti, kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā sugatiṃ saggaṃ laukaṃ upapaj‍jati| aparaipi pañ‍ca ādīnavā apāsādikai – appasan‍nā na pasīdanti, pasan‍nānaṃ aikac‍cānaṃ añ‍ñathattaṃ hauti, satthusāsanaṃ akataṃ hauti, pacchimā janatā diṭṭhānugatiṃ nāpaj‍jati, cittamassa na pasīdati| pañ‍cānisaṃsā pāsādikai – appasan‍nā pasīdanti, pasan‍nānaṃ bhiyyaubhāvāya hauti, satthusāsanaṃ kataṃ hauti, pacchimā janatā diṭṭhānugatiṃ āpaj‍jati, cittamassa pasīdati| pañ‍ca ādīnavā kulūpakai – anāmantacārai āpaj‍jati, rahau nisaj‍jāya āpaj‍jati, paṭicchan‍nai āsanai āpaj‍jati, mātugāmassa uttarichappañ‍cavācāhi dhammaṃ daisaintau āpaj‍jati, kāmasaṅkappabahulau ca viharati| pañ‍ca ādīnavā kulūpakassa bhikkhunau – ativailaṃ kulaisu saṃsaṭṭhassa viharatau mātugāmassa abhiṇhadassanaṃ, dassanai sati saṃsaggau, saṃsaggai sati vissāsau, vissāsai sati autārau, autiṇṇacittassaitaṃ  bhikkhunau pāṭikaṅkhaṃ anabhiratau vā brahmacariyaṃ carissati añ‍ñataraṃ vā saṃkiliṭṭhaṃ āpattiṃ āpaj‍jissati sikkhaṃ vā pac‍cakkhāya hīnāyāvattissati|

pāci॰ 92 pañ‍ca  bījajātāni – mūlabījaṃ, khandhabījaṃ, phaḷubījaṃ, aggabījaṃ, bījabījañ‍ñaiva bījabījamaiva (ka॰) pañ‍camaṃ| pañ‍cahi samaṇakappaihi phalaṃ paribhuñ‍jitabbaṃ – aggiparijitaṃ, satthaparijitaṃ, nakhaparijitaṃ, abījaṃ, nibbattabījañ‍ñaiva nibbaṭṭabījañ‍ñaiva (sī॰), nibbaṭabījañ‍ñaiva (syā॰), nippaṭṭabījañ‍ñaiva (ka॰) pañ‍camaṃ| pañ‍ca visuddhiyau – nidānaṃ uddisitvā avasaisaṃ  sutaina sāvaitabbaṃ, ayaṃ paṭhamā visuddhi; nidānaṃ uddisitvā cattāri pārājikāni uddisitvā avasaisaṃ sutaina sāvaitabbaṃ, ayaṃ dutiyā visuddhi; nidānaṃ uddisitvā cattāri pārājikāni uddisitvā tairasa saṅghādisaisai uddisitvā avasaisaṃ sutaina sāvaitabbaṃ, ayaṃ tatiyā visuddhi; nidānaṃ uddisitvā cattāri pārājikāni uddisitvā tairasa saṅghādisaisai uddisitvā dvai aniyatai  uddisitvā avasaisaṃ sutaina sāvaitabbaṃ, ayaṃ catutthā visuddhi; vitthārainaiva pañ‍camī| aparāpi pañ‍ca visuddhiyau – suttuddaisau, pārisuddhiupausathau, adhiṭṭhānupausathau, pavāraṇā, sāmaggīupausathauyaiva pañ‍camau| pañ‍cānisaṃsā vinayadharai – attanau sīlakkhandhau suguttau hauti surakkhitau, kuk‍kuc‍capakatānaṃ paṭisaraṇaṃ hauti, visāradau saṅghamajjhai vauharati, pac‍catthikai sahadhammaina suniggahitaṃ niggaṇhāti, saddhammaṭṭhitiyā paṭipan‍nau hauti| pañ‍ca adhammikāni pātimaukkhaṭṭhapanāni| pañ‍ca dhammikāni pātimaukkhaṭṭhapanānīti|

pañ‍cakaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

tassuddānaṃ –

āpatti āpattikkhandhā, vinītānantaraina ca|

puggalā chaidanā caiva, āpaj‍jati ca pac‍cayā||

na upaiti upaiti ca, kappantusaṅkitailañ‍ca|

vasaṃ byasanā sampadā, passaddhi puggalaina ca||

sausānikaṃ khāyitañ‍ca, thaiyyaṃ caurau ca vuc‍cati|

avissaj‍ji avaibhaṅgi, kāyatau kāyavācatau||

daisanā  saṅghaṃ uddaisaṃ, pac‍cantikathinaina ca|
