kammāni yāvatatiyaṃ, pārājithul‍laduk‍kaṭaṃ||

akappiyaṃ kappiyañ‍ca, apuñ‍ñaduvinaudayā|

sammaj‍janī aparai ca, bhāsaṃ āpattimaiva ca||

adhikaraṇaṃ vatthu ñatti, āpattā ubhayāni ca|

lahukaṭṭhamakā aitai, kaṇhasuk‍kā vijānatha||

arañ‍ñaṃ piṇḍapātañ‍ca, paṃsurukkhasusānikā|

abbhaukāsau cīvarañ‍ca, sapadānau nisaj‍jikau||

santhati  khalu pacchāpi, pattapiṇḍikamaiva ca|

upausathaṃ  pavāraṇaṃ, āpattānāpattipi ca||

kaṇhasuk‍kapadā aitai, bhikkhunīnampi tai tathā|

apāsādikapāsādi, tathaiva aparai duvai||

kulūpakai ativailaṃ, bījaṃ samaṇakappi ca|

visuddhi aparai caiva, vinayādhammikaina ca|

dhammikā ca tathā vuttā, niṭṭhitā suddhipañ‍cakāti||

6. chak‍kavārau

326. dī॰ ni॰ 3.324 cha agāravā| cha gāravā| cha vinītavatthūni| cha sāmīciyau| cha āpattisamuṭṭhānā| chacchaidanakā āpattiyau| chahākāraihi āpattiṃ āpaj‍jati| chānisaṃsā vinayadharai| cha paramāni| chārattaṃ ticīvaraina vippavasitabbaṃ| cha cīvarāni| cha rajanāni| cha āpattiyau kāyatau ca cittatau ca samuṭṭhanti na vācatau| cha āpattiyau vācatau ca cittatau ca samuṭṭhanti na kāyatau| cha āpattiyau kāyatau ca vācatau ca cittatau ca samuṭṭhanti| cha kammāni| cha vivādamūlāni| cha anuvādamūlāni| cha sāraṇīyā dhammā dīghasau| cha vidatthiyau, sugatavidatthiyā, tiriyaṃ cha vidatthiyau| cha nissayapaṭippassaddhiyau ācariyamhā| cha nahānai anupañ‍ñattiyau – vippakatacīvaraṃ  ādāya pak‍kamati, vippakatacīvaraṃ samādāya pak‍kamati|

mahāva॰ 85 ādayau chahaṅgaihi  saman‍nāgataina bhikkhunā upasampādaitabbaṃ nissayau dātabbau sāmaṇairau upaṭṭhāpaitabbau – asaikkhaina sīlakkhandhaina saman‍nāgatau hauti, asaikkhaina samādhikkhandhaina saman‍nāgatau hauti, asaikkhaina pañ‍ñakkhandhaina saman‍nāgatau hauti, asaikkhaina vimuttikkhandhaina saman‍nāgatau hauti, asaikkhaina vimuttiñāṇadassanakkhandhaina saman‍nāgatau hauti, dasavassau vā hauti atiraikadasavassau vā|

aparaihipi chahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgataina bhikkhunā upasampādaitabbaṃ nissayau dātabbau sāmaṇairau upaṭṭhāpaitabbau – attanā asaikkhaina sīlakkhandhai  saman‍nāgatau hauti, paraṃ asaikkhai sīlakkhandhai samādapaitā; attanā asaikkhaina samādhikkhandhaina saman‍nāgatau hauti, paraṃ asaikkhai samādhikkhandhai samādapaitā; attanā asaikkhaina pañ‍ñakkhandhaina saman‍nāgatau hauti, paraṃ asaikkhai pañ‍ñakkhandhai samādapaitā; attanā asaikkhaina vimuttikkhandhaina saman‍nāgatau hauti, paraṃ asaikkhai vimuttikkhandhai samādapaitā; attanā asaikkhaina vimuttiñāṇadassanakkhandhaina saman‍nāgatau hauti, paraṃ asaikkhai vimuttiñāṇadassanakkhandhai samādapaitā; dasavassau vā hauti atiraikadasavassau vā|

aparaihipi chahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgataina bhikkhunā upasampādaitabbaṃ nissayau dātabbau sāmaṇairau upaṭṭhāpaitabbau – saddhau hauti, hirimā hauti, auttappī hauti, āraddhavīriyau hauti, upaṭṭhitassati hauti, dasavassau vā hauti atiraikadasavassau vā|

aparaihipi chahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgataina bhikkhunā upasampādaitabbaṃ nissayau dātabbau sāmaṇairau upaṭṭhāpaitabbau – na adhisīlai sīlavipan‍nau hauti, na ajjhācārai ācāravipan‍nau hauti, na atidiṭṭhiyā diṭṭhivipan‍nau hauti, bahussutau hauti, pañ‍ñavā hauti, dasavassau vā hauti atiraikadasavassau vā|

aparaihipi chahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgataina bhikkhunā upasampādaitabbaṃ nissayau dātabbau sāmaṇairau upaṭṭhāpaitabbau – paṭibalau hauti antaivāsiṃ vā saddhivihāriṃ vā gilānaṃ upaṭṭhātuṃ vā upaṭṭhāpaituṃ vā, anabhirataṃ vūpakāsaituṃ vā vūpakāsāpaituṃ vā anabhiratiṃ (ka॰), uppan‍naṃ kuk‍kuc‍caṃ dhammatau vinaudaituṃ vinaudaituṃ vā vinaudāpaituṃ vā (syā॰) mahāva॰ 85, āpattiṃ jānāti, āpattivuṭṭhānaṃ jānāti, dasavassau vā hauti atiraikadasavassau vā|

aparaihipi  chahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgataina bhikkhunā upasampādaitabbaṃ nissayau dātabbau sāmaṇairau upaṭṭhāpaitabbau – paṭibalau hauti antaivāsiṃ vā saddhivihāriṃ vā ābhisamācārikāya sikkhāya sikkhāpaituṃ, ādibrahmacāriyakāya sikkhāya vinaituṃ, abhidhammai vinaituṃ, abhivinayai vinaituṃ, uppan‍naṃ diṭṭhigataṃ dhammatau vivaicaituṃ, dasavassau vā hauti atiraikadasavassau vā|

aparaihipi  chahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgataina bhikkhunā upasampādaitabbaṃ nissayau dātabbau sāmaṇairau upaṭṭhāpaitabbau – āpattiṃ jānāti, anāpattiṃ jānāti, lahukaṃ  āpattiṃ jānāti, garukaṃ āpattiṃ jānāti, ubhayāni khau panassa pātimaukkhāni vitthāraina svāgatāni haunti suvibhattāni suppavattīni suvinicchitāni suttasau anubyañ‍janasau, dasavassau vā hauti atiraikadasavassau vā|

cha adhammikāni pātimaukkhaṭṭhapanāni, cha dhammikāni pātimaukkhaṭṭhapanānīti|

chak‍kaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

tassuddānaṃ –

agāravā gāravā ca, vinītā sāmīcipi ca|

samuṭṭhānā chaidanā caiva, ākārānisaṃsaina ca||

paramāni ca chārattaṃ, cīvaraṃ rajanāni ca|

kāyatau cittatau cāpi, vācatau cittataupi ca||

kāyavācācittatau ca, kammavivādamaiva ca|

anuvādā dīghasau ca, tiriyaṃ nissayaina ca||

anupañ‍ñatti ādāya, samādāya tathaiva ca|

asaikkhai samādapaitā, saddhau adhisīlaina ca|

gilānābhisamācārī, āpattādhammadhammikāti||

7. sattakavārau

327. sattāpattiyau| sattāpattikkhandhā| satta vinītavatthūni| satta sāmīciyau| satta adhammikā paṭiñ‍ñātakaraṇā| satta dhammikā paṭiñ‍ñātakaraṇā| sattan‍naṃ anāpatti sattāhakaraṇīyaina  gantuṃ| sattānisaṃsā vinayadharai| satta paramāni| sattamai aruṇuggamanai nissaggiyaṃ hauti| satta samathā| satta kammāni| satta āmakadhañ‍ñāni| tiriyaṃ sattantarā| gaṇabhaujanai  satta anupañ‍ñattiyau| bhaisaj‍jāni paṭiggahaitvā sattāhaparamaṃ san‍nidhikārakaṃ paribhuñ‍jitabbāni| katacīvaraṃ ādāya pak‍kamati| katacīvaraṃ samādāya pak‍kamati| bhikkhussa na hauti āpatti daṭṭhabbā| bhikkhussa hauti āpatti daṭṭhabbā| bhikkhussa hauti āpatti daṭṭhabbā paṭikātabbā (sabbattha) aṭṭhakathā ca campaiyyakkhandhakai adhammakammādikathā ca aulaukaitabbā| satta adhammikāni pātimaukkhaṭṭhapanāni| satta dhammikāni pātimaukkhaṭṭhapanāni|

a॰ ni॰ 7.75 sattahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgatau bhikkhu vinayadharau hauti – āpattiṃ jānāti, anāpattiṃ jānāti, lahukaṃ āpattiṃ jānāti, garukaṃ āpattiṃ jānāti, sīlavā hauti, pātimaukkhasaṃvarasaṃvutau viharati ācāragaucarasampan‍nau aṇumattaisu vaj‍jaisu bhayadassāvī samādāya sikkhati sikkhāpadaisu, catun‍naṃ jhānānaṃ ābhicaitasikānaṃ diṭṭhadhammasukhavihārānaṃ nikāmalābhī hauti akicchalābhī akasiralābhī, āsavānañ‍ca khayā anāsavaṃ caitauvimuttiṃ pañ‍ñāvimuttiṃ diṭṭhaiva dhammai sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā upasampaj‍ja viharati|

aparaihipi sattahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgatau bhikkhu vinayadharau hauti – āpattiṃ jānāti, anāpattiṃ jānāti, lahukaṃ āpattiṃ jānāti, garukaṃ āpattiṃ jānāti, bahussutau hauti sutadharau sutasan‍nicayau yai tai dhammā ādikalyāṇā  majjhaikalyāṇā pariyausānakalyāṇā sātthaṃ sabyañ‍janaṃ kaivalaparipuṇṇaṃ parisuddhaṃ brahmacariyaṃ abhivadanti tathārūpassa dhammā bahussutā haunti dhātā dhatā (sī॰ syā॰) vacasā paricitā manasānupaikkhitā diṭṭhiyā suppaṭividdhā, catun‍naṃ jhānānaṃ ābhicaitasikānaṃ diṭṭhadhammasukhavihārānaṃ nikāmalābhī hauti akicchalābhī akasiralābhī, āsavānañ‍ca khayā anāsavaṃ caitauvimuttiṃ pañ‍ñāvimuttiṃ diṭṭhaiva dhammai sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā upasampaj‍ja viharati|

aparaihipi sattahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgatau bhikkhu vinayadharau hauti – āpattiṃ jānāti, anāpattiṃ jānāti, lahukaṃ āpattiṃ jānāti, garukaṃ āpattiṃ jānāti, ubhayāni khau panassa  pātimaukkhāni vitthāraina svāgatāni haunti suvibhattāni suppavattīni suvinicchitāni suttasau anubyañ‍janasau, catun‍naṃ jhānānaṃ ābhicaitasikānaṃ diṭṭhadhammasukhavihārānaṃ nikāmalābhī hauti akicchalābhī akasiralābhī, āsavānañ‍ca  khayā anāsavaṃ caitauvimuttiṃ pañ‍ñāvimuttiṃ diṭṭhaiva dhammai sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā upasampaj‍ja viharati|

aparaihipi sattahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgatau bhikkhu vinayadharau hauti – āpattiṃ jānāti; anāpattiṃ jānāti; lahukaṃ āpattiṃ jānāti; garukaṃ āpattiṃ jānāti; anaikavihitaṃ pubbainivāsaṃ anussarati, saiyyathidaṃ – aikampi jātiṃ dvaipi jātiyau tissaupi jātiyau catassaupi jātiyau pañ‍capi jātiyau dasapi jātiyau vīsampi jātiyau tiṃsampi jātiyau cattālīsampi jātiyau pañ‍ñāsampi jātiyau jātisatampi  jātisahassampi jātisatasahassampi anaikaipi saṃvaṭṭakappai anaikaipi vivaṭṭakappai anaikaipi saṃvaṭṭavivaṭṭakappai – ‘‘amutrāsiṃ aivaṃnāmau aivaṃgauttau aivaṃvaṇṇau aivamāhārau aivaṃsukhadukkhappaṭisaṃvaidī aivamāyupariyantau, sau tatau cutau amutra udapādiṃ; tatrāpāsiṃ aivaṃnāmau aivaṃgauttau aivaṃvaṇṇau aivamāhārau aivaṃsukhadukkhappaṭisaṃvaidī aivamāyupariyantau, sau tatau cutau idhūpapan‍nau’’ti iti sākāraṃ sauddaisaṃ anaikavihitaṃ pubbainivāsaṃ anussarati; dibbaina cakkhunā visuddhaina atik‍kantamānusakaina atik‍kantamānussakaina (ka॰) sattai passati cavamānai upapaj‍jamānai hīnai paṇītai suvaṇṇai dubbaṇṇai, sugatai duggatai yathākammūpagai sattai pajānāti – ‘‘imai vata bhauntau sattā kāyaduc‍caritaina saman‍nāgatā vacīduc‍caritaina saman‍nāgatā manauduc‍caritaina saman‍nāgatā ariyānaṃ upavādakā micchādiṭṭhikā micchādiṭṭhikammasamādānā, tai kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā apāyaṃ duggatiṃ vinipātaṃ nirayaṃ upapan‍nā, imai vā pana bhauntau sattā kāyasucaritaina saman‍nāgatā vacīsucaritaina saman‍nāgatā manausucaritaina saman‍nāgatā ariyānaṃ anupavādakā sammādiṭṭhikā sammādiṭṭhikammasamādānā, tai kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā sugatiṃ saggaṃ laukaṃ upapan‍nā’’ti iti dibbaina cakkhunā visuddhaina atik‍kantamānusakaina sattai passati cavamānai upapaj‍jamānai hīnai paṇītai suvaṇṇai dubbaṇṇai sugatai duggatai yathākammūpagai sattai pajānāti; āsavānañ‍ca khayā anāsavaṃ caitauvimuttiṃ pañ‍ñāvimuttiṃ diṭṭhaiva dhammai sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā  sacchikatvā upasampaj‍ja viharati|

sattahaṅgaihi  saman‍nāgatau vinayadharau saubhati – āpattiṃ jānāti, anāpattiṃ jānāti, lahukaṃ āpattiṃ jānāti, garukaṃ  āpattiṃ jānāti, sīlavā hauti…pai॰… samādāya sikkhati sikkhāpadaisu, catun‍naṃ jhānānaṃ ābhicaitasikānaṃ diṭṭhadhammasukhavihārānaṃ nikāmalābhī hauti  akicchalābhī akasiralābhī, āsavānañ‍ca khayā anāsavaṃ caitauvimuttiṃ pañ‍ñāvimuttiṃ diṭṭhaiva dhammai sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā upasampaj‍ja viharati|

aparaihipi sattahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgatau vinayadharau| saubhati – āpattiṃ jānāti, anāpattiṃ jānāti, lahukaṃ āpattiṃ jānāti, garukaṃ āpattiṃ jānāti, bahussutau hauti…pai॰… diṭṭhiyā suppaṭividdhau catun‍naṃ jhānānaṃ ābhicaitasikānaṃ diṭṭhadhammasukhavihārānaṃ nikāmalābhī hauti akicchalābhī akasiralābhī, āsavānañ‍ca khayā anāsavaṃ caitauvimuttiṃ pañ‍ñāvimuttiṃ diṭṭhaiva dhammai sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā upasampaj‍ja viharati|

aparaihipi sattahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgatau vinayadharau saubhati – āpattiṃ jānāti, anāpattiṃ jānāti, lahukaṃ āpattiṃ jānāti, garukaṃ āpattiṃ jānāti, ubhayāni khau panassa pātimaukkhāni vitthāraina svāgatāni haunti suvibhattāni suppavattīni suvinicchitāni suttasau anubyañ‍janasau, catun‍naṃ jhānānaṃ ābhicaitasikānaṃ diṭṭhadhammasukhavihārānaṃ nikāmalābhī hauti akicchalābhī akasiralābhī, āsavānañ‍ca khayā anāsavaṃ caitauvimuttiṃ pañ‍ñāvimuttiṃ diṭṭhaiva dhammai sayaṃ  abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā upasampaj‍ja viharati|

aparaihipi sattahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgatau vinayadharau saubhati – āpattiṃ jānāti; anāpattiṃ jānāti; lahukaṃ āpattiṃ jānāti; garukaṃ āpattiṃ jānāti; anaikavihitaṃ pubbainivāsaṃ anussarati, saiyyathidaṃ – aikampi jātiṃ dvaipi jātiyau…pai॰… iti sākāraṃ sauddaisaṃ anaikavihitaṃ pubbainivāsaṃ anussarati, dibbaina cakkhunā visuddhaina atik‍kantamānusakaina sattai passati cavamānai upapaj‍jamānai hīnai paṇītai suvaṇṇai dubbaṇṇai sugatai duggatai yathākammūpagai sattai pajānāti…pai॰… iti dibbaina cakkhunā visuddhaina atik‍kantamānusakaina sattai passati cavamānai upapaj‍jamānai hīnai paṇītai suvaṇṇai dubbaṇṇai sugatai duggatai yathākammūpagai  sattai pajānāti; āsavānañ‍ca khayā anāsavaṃ caitauvimuttiṃ pañ‍ñāvimuttiṃ diṭṭhaiva dhammai sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā upasampaj‍ja viharati|

dī॰ ni॰ 3.330 satta asaddhammā – assaddhau hauti, ahirikau hauti, anauttappī hauti, appassutau hauti, kusītau hauti, muṭṭhassati hauti, duppañ‍ñau hauti|

dī॰ ni॰ 3.330 satta  saddhammā – saddhau hauti, hirimā hauti, auttappī hauti, bahussutau hauti, āraddhavīriyau hauti, upaṭṭhitassati hauti, pañ‍ñavā hautīti|

sattakaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

tassuddānaṃ –

āpatti āpattikkhandhā, vinītā sāmīcipi ca|

adhammikā dhammikā ca, anāpatti ca sattāhaṃ||

ānisaṃsā paramāni, aruṇasamathaina ca|

kammā āmakadhañ‍ñā ca, tiriyaṃ gaṇabhaujanai||

sattāhaparamaṃ  ādāya, samādāya tathaiva ca|

na hauti hauti hauti ca, adhammā dhammikāni ca||

caturau vinayadharā, catubhikkhū ca saubhanai|

satta caiva asaddhammā, satta saddhammā daisitāti||

8. aṭṭhakavārau

328. aṭṭhānisaṃsai sampassamānaina na sau bhikkhu āpattiyā adassanai ukkhipitabbau| aṭṭhānisaṃsai sampassamānaina paraisampi saddhāya sā āpatti daisaitabbā| aṭṭha yāvatatiyakā| aṭṭhahākāraihi kulāni dūsaiti| aṭṭha mātikā cīvarassa uppādāya| aṭṭha mātikā kathinassa ubbhārāya| aṭṭha pānāni| cūḷava॰ 348; a॰ ni॰ 8.7 aṭṭhahi asaddhammaihi abhibhūtau pariyādin‍nacittau daivadattau āpāyikau nairayikau kappaṭṭhau ataikicchau| aṭṭha laukadhammā| aṭṭha garudhammā| aṭṭha pāṭidaisanīyā| aṭṭhaṅgikau musāvādau| aṭṭha upausathaṅgāni| aṭṭha dūtaiyyaṅgāni| aṭṭha titthiyavattāni| aṭṭha acchariyā abbhutadhammā mahāsamuddai| aṭṭha acchariyā abbhutadhammā imasmiṃ dhammavinayai| aṭṭha anatirittā| aṭṭha atirittā| aṭṭhamai aruṇuggamanai nissaggiyaṃ hauti| aṭṭha pārājikā| aṭṭhamaṃ vatthuṃ paripūraintī nāsaitabbā| aṭṭhamaṃ vatthuṃ paripūraintiyā daisitāpi adaisitā hauti| aṭṭhavācikā  upasampadā| aṭṭhan‍naṃ pac‍cuṭṭhātabbaṃ| aṭṭhan‍naṃ  āsanaṃ dātabbaṃ| upāsikā aṭṭha varāni yācati| aṭṭhahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgatau bhikkhu bhikkhunauvādakau samman‍nitabbau sammanitabbau (ka॰)| aṭṭhānisaṃsā vinayadharai| aṭṭha paramāni| tassapāpiyasikakammakataina bhikkhunā aṭṭhasu dhammaisu sammā vattitabbaṃ| aṭṭha adhammikāni pātimaukkhaṭṭhapanāni aṭṭha dhammikāni pātimaukkhaṭṭhapanānīti|

aṭṭhakaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

tassuddānaṃ –

na sau bhikkhu paraisampi, yāvatatiyadūsanā|

mātikā kathinubbhārā, pānā abhibhūtaina ca||

laukadhammā garudhammā, pāṭidaisanīyā musā|

upausathā ca dūtaṅgā, titthiyā samuddaipi ca||

abbhutā anatirittaṃ, atirittaṃ nissaggiyaṃ|

pārājikaṭṭhamaṃ vatthu, adaisitūpasampadā||

pac‍cuṭṭhānāsanañ‍caiva, varaṃ auvādakaina ca|

ānisaṃsā paramāni, aṭṭhadhammaisu vattanā|

adhammikā dhammikā ca, aṭṭhakā suppakāsitāti||

9. navakavārau

329. nava āghātavatthūni| nava āghātapaṭivinayā| nava vinītavatthūni| nava paṭhamāpattikā| navahi saṅghau bhij‍jati| nava paṇītabhaujanāni| navamaṃsaihi  duk‍kaṭaṃ| nava pātimaukkhuddaisā| nava paramāni| nava taṇhāmūlakā dhammā| nava vidhamānā| nava cīvarāni adhiṭṭhātabbāni| nava cīvarāni na vikappaitabbāni| dīghasau nava vidatthiyau sugatavidatthiyā| nava adhammikāni dānāni| nava adhammikā paṭiggahā| nava adhammikā paribhaugā – tīṇi dhammikāni dānāni, tayau dhammikā paṭiggahā, tayau dhammikā paribhaugā| nava adhammikā sañ‍ñattiyau  pañ‍ñattiyau (sabbattha) aṭṭhakathā ca samathakkhandhakai kaṇhapakkhanavakañ‍ca aulaukaitabbaṃ| nava dhammikā sañ‍ñattiyau| adhammakammai dvai navakāni| dhammakammai dvai  navakāni| nava adhammikāni pātimaukkhaṭṭhapanāni| nava dhammikāni pātimaukkhaṭṭhapanānīti|

navakaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

tassuddānaṃ –

āghātavatthuvinayā, vinītā paṭhamaina ca|

bhij‍jati ca paṇītañ‍ca, maṃsuddaisaparamāni ca||

taṇhā mānā adhiṭṭhānā, vikappai ca vidatthiyau|

dānā paṭiggahā bhaugā, tividhā puna dhammikā||

adhammadhammasañ‍ñatti, duvai dvai navakāni ca|

pātimaukkhaṭṭhapanāni, adhammadhammikāni cāti||

10. dasakavārau

330. dasa āghātavatthūni| dasa āghātapaṭivinayā| dasa vinītavatthūni| dasavatthukā micchādiṭṭhi| dasavatthukā sammādiṭṭhi| dasa antaggāhikā diṭṭhi| dasa micchattā| dasa sammattā| dasa  akusalakammapathā| dasa kusalakammapathā| dasa adhammikā salākaggāhā| dasa dhammikā salākaggāhā| sāmaṇairānaṃ dasa sikkhāpadāni| dasahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgatau sāmaṇairau nāsaitabbau|

dasahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgatau vinayadharau bālau tvaiva saṅkhaṃ gacchati – attanau bhāsapariyantaṃ na uggaṇhāti, parassa bhāsapariyantaṃ na uggaṇhāti, attanau bhāsapariyantaṃ anuggahaitvā parassa bhāsapariyantaṃ anuggahaitvā adhammaina kāraiti, appaṭiñ‍ñāya āpattiṃ na jānāti, āpattiyā mūlaṃ na jānāti, āpattisamudayaṃ na jānāti, āpattiniraudhaṃ na jānāti, āpattiniraudhagāminiṃ paṭipadaṃ na jānāti|

dasahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgatau vinayadharau paṇḍitau tvaiva saṅkhaṃ gacchati – attanau bhāsapariyantaṃ uggaṇhāti, parassa bhāsapariyantaṃ uggaṇhāti, attanau bhāsapariyantaṃ uggahaitvā parassa bhāsapariyantaṃ  uggahaitvā dhammaina kāraiti, paṭiñ‍ñāya āpattiṃ jānāti, āpattiyā mūlaṃ jānāti, āpattisamudayaṃ jānāti, āpattiniraudhaṃ jānāti, āpattiniraudhagāminiṃ paṭipadaṃ jānāti|

aparaihipi dasahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgatau vinayadharau bālau tvaiva saṅkhaṃ gacchati – adhikaraṇaṃ na jānāti, adhikaraṇassa mūlaṃ na jānāti, adhikaraṇasamudayaṃ na jānāti, adhikaraṇaniraudhaṃ na jānāti, adhikaraṇaniraudhagāminiṃ paṭipadaṃ na jānāti, vatthuṃ na jānāti, nidānaṃ uddānaṃ (ka॰) na jānāti, pañ‍ñattiṃ na jānāti, anupañ‍ñattiṃ na jānāti, anusandhivacanapathaṃ na jānāti|

dasahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgatau vinayadharau paṇḍitau tvaiva saṅkhaṃ gacchati – adhikaraṇaṃ jānāti, adhikaraṇassa mūlaṃ jānāti, adhikaraṇasamudayaṃ jānāti, adhikaraṇaniraudhaṃ jānāti, adhikaraṇaniraudhagāminiṃ paṭipadaṃ jānāti, vatthuṃ jānāti, nidānaṃ jānāti, pañ‍ñattiṃ jānāti, anupañ‍ñattiṃ jānāti, anusandhivacanapathaṃ jānāti|

aparaihipi dasahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgatau  vinayadharau bālau tvaiva saṅkhaṃ gacchati – ñattiṃ na jānāti, ñattiyā karaṇaṃ na jānāti, na pubbakusalau hauti, na aparakusalau hauti, akālañ‍ñū ca hauti, āpattānāpattiṃ na jānāti, lahukagarukaṃ āpattiṃ na jānāti, sāvasaisānavasaisaṃ āpattiṃ na jānāti, duṭṭhul‍lāduṭṭhul‍laṃ āpattiṃ na jānāti, ācariyaparamparā khau panassa na suggahitā hauti na sumanasikatā na sūpadhāritā|

dasahaṅgaihi  saman‍nāgatau vinayadharau paṇḍitau tvaiva saṅkhaṃ gacchati – ñattiṃ jānāti, ñattiyā karaṇaṃ jānāti, pubbakusalau hauti, aparakusalau hauti, kālañ‍ñū ca hauti, āpattānāpattiṃ jānāti, lahukagarukaṃ āpattiṃ jānāti, sāvasaisānavasaisaṃ āpattiṃ jānāti, duṭṭhul‍lāduṭṭhul‍laṃ āpattiṃ jānāti, ācariyaparamparā khau panassa suggahitā hauti sumanasikatā sūpadhāritā|

aparaihipi dasahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgatau vinayadharau bālau tvaiva saṅkhaṃ gacchati – āpattānāpattiṃ na jānāti, lahukagarukaṃ āpattiṃ na jānāti, sāvasaisānavasaisaṃ āpattiṃ na  jānāti, duṭṭhul‍lāduṭṭhul‍laṃ āpattiṃ na jānāti, ubhayāni khau panassa pātimaukkhāni vitthāraina na svāgatāni haunti na suvibhattāni na suppavattīni na suvinicchitāni suttasau anubyañ‍janasau, āpattānāpattiṃ na jānāti, lahukagarukaṃ āpattiṃ na jānāti, sāvasaisānavasaisaṃ āpattiṃ na jānāti, duṭṭhul‍lāduṭṭhul‍laṃ āpattiṃ na jānāti, adhikaraṇai ca na vinicchayakusalau hauti|

dasahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgatau vinayadharau paṇḍitau tvaiva saṅkhaṃ gacchati – āpattānāpattiṃ jānāti, lahukagarukaṃ āpattiṃ jānāti, sāvasaisānavasaisaṃ āpattiṃ jānāti, duṭṭhul‍lāduṭṭhul‍laṃ āpattiṃ jānāti, ubhayāni khau panassa pātimaukkhāni vitthāraina svāgatāni haunti suvibhattāni suppavattīni suvinicchitāni suttasau anubyañ‍janasau, āpattānāpattiṃ jānāti, lahukagarukaṃ āpattiṃ jānāti, sāvasaisānavasaisaṃ āpattiṃ jānāti, duṭṭhul‍lāduṭṭhul‍laṃ āpattiṃ jānāti, adhikaraṇai ca vinicchayakusalau hauti|

a॰ ni॰ 10.33 dasahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgatau bhikkhu ubbāhikāya samman‍nitabbau| dasa atthavasai paṭic‍ca tathāgataina sāvakānaṃ sikkhāpadaṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ| dasa ādīnavā rājantaipurappavaisanai| dasa dānavatthūni| dasa ratanāni| dasavaggau bhikkhusaṅghau| dasavaggaina gaṇaina upasampādaitabbaṃ| dasa paṃsukūlāni| dasa cīvaradhāraṇā cīvaradhāraṇāni (syā॰)| dasāhaparamaṃ atiraikacīvaraṃ dhāraitabbaṃ| dasa suk‍kāni| dasa itthiyau| dasa  bhariyāyau| vaisāliyā dasa vatthūni dīpainti| dasa puggalā avandiyā| dasa ak‍kausavatthūni| dasahākāraihi paisuñ‍ñaṃ upasaṃharati| dasa saināsanāni| dasa varāni yāciṃsu| dasa adhammikāni pātimaukkhaṭṭhapanāni| dasa dhammikāni pātimaukkhaṭṭhapanāni| dasānisaṃsā yāguyā| dasa maṃsā akappiyā| dasa paramāni| dasavassaina bhikkhunā byattaina paṭibalaina pabbājaitabbaṃ upasampādaitabbaṃ nissayau dātabbau sāmaṇairau upaṭṭhāpaitabbau| dasavassāya bhikkhuniyā byattāya paṭibalāya pabbājaitabbaṃ upasampādaitabbaṃ nissayau dātabbau sāmaṇairī upaṭṭhāpaitabbā| dasavassāya bhikkhuniyā byattāya paṭibalāya vuṭṭhāpanasammuti sāditabbā| dasavassāya gihigatāya sikkhā dātabbāti|

dasakaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

tassuddānaṃ –

āghātaṃ  vinayaṃ vatthu, micchā sammā ca antagā|

micchattā caiva sammattā, akusalā kusalāpi ca||

salākā  adhammā dhammā, sāmaṇairā ca nāsanā|

bhāsādhikaraṇañ‍caiva, ñattilahukamaiva ca||

lahukā garukā aitai, kaṇhasuk‍kā vijānatha|

ubbāhikā ca sikkhā ca, antaipurai ca vatthūni||

ratanaṃ  dasavaggau ca, tathaiva upasampadā|

paṃsukūladhāraṇā ca, dasāhasuk‍kaitthiyau||

bhariyā dasa vatthūni, avandiyak‍kausaina ca|

paisuñ‍ñañ‍caiva saināni, varāni ca adhammikā||

dhammikā yāgumaṃsā ca, paramā bhikkhu bhikkhunī|

vuṭṭhāpanā gihigatā, dasakā suppakāsitāti||

11. aikādasakavārau

331. aikāda  puggalā anupasampan‍nā na upasampādaitabbā, upasampan‍nā nāsaitabbā| aikādasa pādukā akappiyā| aikādasa pattā akappiyā| aikādasa cīvarāni akappiyāni| aikādasa yāvatatiyakā| bhikkhunīnaṃ aikādasa antarāyikā dhammā pucchitabbā| aikādasa cīvarāni adhiṭṭhātabbāni| aikādasa cīvarāni na vikappaitabbāni| aikādasai aruṇuggamanai nissaggiyaṃ hauti| aikādasa gaṇṭhikā kappiyā| aikādasa vidhā vīthā (syā॰) kappiyā| aikādasa pathavī akappiyā| aikādasa pathavī kappiyā| aikādasa nissayapaṭippassaddhiyau| aikādasa puggalā avandiyā| aikādasa paramāni| aikādasa varāni yāciṃsu| aikādasa sīmādausā| ak‍kausakaparibhāsakai puggalai aikādasādīnavā pāṭikaṅkhā| a॰ ni॰ 11.15; mi॰ pa॰ 4.4.6 maittāya caitauvimuttiyā āsaivitāya bhāvitāya bahulīkatāya yānīkatāya vatthukatāya anuṭṭhitāya paricitāya susamāraddhāya aikādasānisaṃsā pāṭikaṅkhā| sukhaṃ supati, sukhaṃ paṭibujjhati, na pāpakaṃ supinaṃ passati, manussānaṃ piyau hauti, amanussānaṃ piyau hauti, daivatā rakkhanti, nāssa aggi vā visaṃ vā satthaṃ vā kamati, tuvaṭṭaṃ cittaṃ samādhiyati, mukhavaṇṇau vippasīdati, asammūḷhau kālaṅkarauti, uttari appaṭivijjhantau brahmalaukūpagau hauti – maittāya caitauvimuttiyā  āsaivitāya bhāvitāya bahulīkatāya yānīkatāya vatthukatāya anuṭṭhitāya paricitāya susamāraddhāya imai aikādasānisaṃsā pāṭikaṅkhāti|

aikādasakaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

tassuddānaṃ –

nāsaitabbā pādukā ca, pattā ca cīvarāni ca|

tatiyā pucchitabbā ca, adhiṭṭhānavikappanā||

aruṇā  gaṇṭhikā vidhā, akappiyā ca kappiyaṃ|

nissayāvandiyā caiva, paramāni varāni ca|

sīmādausā ca ak‍kausā, maittāyaikādasā katāti||

aikuttarikanayau|

tassuddānaṃ –

aikakā  ca dukā caiva, tikā ca catupañ‍cakā|

chasattaṭṭhanavakā ca, dasa aikādasāni ca||

hitāya sabbasattānaṃ, ñātadhammaina tādinā|

aikuttarikā vimalā, mahāvīraina daisitāti||

aikuttarikanayau niṭṭhitau|



upausathādipucchāvissaj‍janā

ādimajjhantapucchanaṃ

332. upausathakammassa  kau ādi, kiṃ majjhai, kiṃ pariyausānaṃ? pavāraṇākammassa kau ādi, kiṃ majjhai, kiṃ pariyausānaṃ? taj‍janīyakammassa kau ādi, kiṃ majjhai, kiṃ pariyausānaṃ? niyassakammassa…pai॰… pabbājanīyakammassa…pai॰… paṭisāraṇīyakammassa…pai॰… ukkhaipanīyakammassa…pai॰… parivāsadānassa…pai॰… mūlāyapaṭikassanāya…pai॰… mānattadānassa…pai॰… abbhānassa…pai॰… upasampadākammassa kau ādi, kiṃ majjhai, kiṃ pariyausānaṃ? taj‍janīyakammassa paṭippassaddhiyā kau ādi, kiṃ majjhai, kiṃ pariyausānaṃ? niyassakammassa paṭippassaddhiyā kau ādi, kiṃ majjhai, kiṃ pariyausānaṃ? pabbājanīyakammassa paṭippassaddhiyā kau ādi, kiṃ majjhai, kiṃ pariyausānaṃ? paṭisāraṇīyakammassa paṭippassaddhiyā kau ādi, kiṃ majjhai, kiṃ pariyausānaṃ? ukkhaipanīyakammassa paṭippassaddhiyā kau ādi, kiṃ majjhai, kiṃ pariyausānaṃ? sativinayassa kau ādi, kiṃ maj‍jai, kiṃ pariyausānaṃ? amūḷhavinayassa kau ādi, kiṃ majjhai, kiṃ pariyausānaṃ? tassapāpiyasikāya kau ādi, kiṃ majjhai, kiṃ pariyausānaṃ? tiṇavatthārakassa kau ādi, kiṃ majjhai, kiṃ pariyausānaṃ? bhikkhunauvādakasammutiyā kau ādi, kiṃ majjhai, kiṃ pariyausānaṃ? ticīvaraina avippavāsasammutiyā kau ādi, kiṃ majjhai, kiṃ pariyausānaṃ? santhatasammutiyā kau ādi, kiṃ majjhai, kiṃ pariyausānaṃ? rūpiyachaḍḍakasammutiyā kau ādi, kiṃ majjhai, kiṃ pariyausānaṃ? sāṭiyaggāhāpakasammutiyau kau ādi, kiṃ majjhai, kiṃ pariyausānaṃ? pattaggāhāpakasammutiyā kau ādi, kiṃ majjhai, kiṃ pariyausānaṃ? daṇḍasammutiyā kau ādi, kiṃ majjhai, kiṃ pariyausānaṃ? sik‍kāsammutiyā kau ādi, kiṃ majjhai, kiṃ pariyausānaṃ? daṇḍasik‍kāsammutiyā kau ādi, kiṃ majjhai, kiṃ pariyausānaṃ?

ādimajjhantavissaj‍janā

333. upausathakammassa  kau ādi, kiṃ majjhai, kiṃ pariyausānanti? upausathakammassa sāmaggī ādi, kiriyā majjhai, niṭṭhānaṃ pariyausānaṃ|

pavāraṇākammassa  kau ādi, kiṃ majjhai, kiṃ pariyausānanti? pavāraṇākammassa sāmaggī ādi, kiriyā majjhai, niṭṭhānaṃ pariyausānaṃ|

taj‍janīyakammassa kau ādi, kiṃ majjhai, kiṃ pariyausānanti? taj‍janīyakammassa vatthu ca puggalau ca ādi, ñatti majjhai, kammavācā pariyausānaṃ|

niyassakammassa…pai॰… pabbājanīyakammassa…pai॰… paṭisāraṇīyakammassa…pai॰… ukkhaipanīyakammassa…pai॰… parivāsadānassa…pai॰… mūlāyapaṭikassanāya…pai॰… mānattadānassa…pai॰… abbhānassa kau ādi, kiṃ majjhai, kiṃ pariyausānanti? abbhānassa vatthu ca puggalau ca ādi, ñatti majjhai, kammavācā pariyausānaṃ|

upasampadākammassa kau ādi, kiṃ majjhai, kiṃ pariyausānanti? upasampadākammassa puggalau ādi, ñatti majjhai, kammavācā pariyausānaṃ|

taj‍janīyakammassa paṭippassaddhiyā kau ādi, kiṃ majjhai, kiṃ pariyausānanti? taj‍janīyakammassa paṭippassaddhiyā sammāvattanā ādi, ñatti majjhai, kammavācā pariyausānaṃ|

niyassakammassa…pai॰… pabbājanīyakammassa…pai॰… paṭisāraṇīyakammassa…pai॰… ukkhaipanīyakammassa paṭippassaddhiyā kau ādi, kiṃ majjhai, kiṃ pariyausānanti? ukkhaipanīyakammassa paṭippassaddhiyā sammāvattanā ādi, ñatti majjhai, kammavācā pariyausānaṃ|

sativinayassa kau ādi, kiṃ majjhai, kiṃ pariyausānanti? sativinayassa vatthu  ca puggalau ca ādi, ñatti majjhai, kammavācā pariyausānaṃ|

amūḷhavinayassa  …pai॰… tassapāpiyasikāya…pai॰… tiṇavatthārakassa…pai॰… bhikkhunauvādakasammutiyā…pai॰… ticīvaraina avippavāsasammutiyā…pai॰… santhatasammutiyā…pai॰… rūpiyachaḍḍakasammutiyā…pai॰… sāṭiyaggāhāpakasammutiyā…pai॰… pattaggāhāpakasammutiyā…pai॰… daṇḍasammutiyā…pai॰… sik‍kāsammutiyā…pai॰… daṇḍasik‍kāsammutiyā kau ādi, kiṃ majjhai, kiṃ pariyausānanti? daṇḍasik‍kāsammutiyā vatthu ca puggalau ca ādi, ñatti majjhai, kammavācā pariyausānaṃ|

upausathādipucchāvissaj‍janā niṭṭhitā|


atthavasapakaraṇaṃ

334. a॰ ni॰ 10.334; pari॰ 22 dasa  atthavasai paṭic‍ca tathāgataina sāvakānaṃ sikkhāpadaṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ – saṅghasuṭṭhutāya, saṅghaphāsutāya, dummaṅkūnaṃ puggalānaṃ niggahāya, paisalānaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ phāsuvihārāya, diṭṭhadhammikānaṃ āsavānaṃ saṃvarāya, samparāyikānaṃ āsavānaṃ paṭighātāya, appasan‍nānaṃ pasādāya, pasan‍nānaṃ bhiyyaubhāvāya, saddhammaṭṭhitiyā vinayānuggahāya|

yaṃ saṅghasuṭṭhu taṃ saṅghaphāsu| yaṃ saṅghaphāsu taṃ dummaṅkūnaṃ puggalānaṃ niggahāya| yaṃ dummaṅkūnaṃ puggalānaṃ niggahāya taṃ paisalānaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ phāsuvihārāya| yaṃ paisalānaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ phāsuvihārāya taṃ diṭṭhadhammikānaṃ āsavānaṃ saṃvarāya| yaṃ diṭṭhadhammikānaṃ āsavānaṃ saṃvarāya taṃ samparāyikānaṃ āsavānaṃ paṭighātāya| yaṃ samparāyikānaṃ āsavānaṃ paṭighātāya taṃ appasan‍nānaṃ pasādāya| yaṃ appasan‍nānaṃ pasādāya taṃ pasan‍nānaṃ bhiyyaubhāvāya| yaṃ pasan‍nānaṃ bhiyyaubhāvāya taṃ saddhammaṭṭhitiyā| yaṃ saddhammaṭṭhitiyā taṃ vinayānuggahāya|

yaṃ saṅghasuṭṭhu taṃ saṅghaphāsu| yaṃ saṅghasuṭṭhu taṃ dummaṅkūnaṃ puggalānaṃ niggahāya| yaṃ saṅghasuṭṭhu taṃ paisalānaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ phāsuvihārāya| yaṃ saṅghasuṭṭhu taṃ diṭṭhadhammikānaṃ āsavānaṃ saṃvarāya| yaṃ saṅghasuṭṭhu taṃ samparāyikānaṃ āsavānaṃ paṭighātāya| yaṃ saṅghasuṭṭhu taṃ  appasan‍nānaṃ pasādāya| yaṃ saṅghasuṭṭhu taṃ pasan‍nānaṃ bhiyyaubhāvāya| yaṃ saṅghasuṭṭhu taṃ saddhammaṭṭhitiyā| yaṃ saṅghasuṭṭhu taṃ vinayānuggahāya|

yaṃ saṅghaphāsu taṃ dummaṅkūnaṃ puggalānaṃ niggahāya| yaṃ saṅghaphāsu taṃ paisalānaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ phāsuvihārāya| yaṃ saṅghaphāsu taṃ diṭṭhadhammikānaṃ āsavānaṃ saṃvarāya| yaṃ saṅghaphāsu taṃ samparāyikānaṃ āsavānaṃ paṭighātāya| yaṃ saṅghaphāsu taṃ appasan‍nānaṃ pasādāya| yaṃ saṅghaphāsu taṃ pasan‍nānaṃ bhiyyaubhāvāya| yaṃ saṅghaphāsu taṃ saddhammaṭṭhitiyā| yaṃ saṅghaphāsu taṃ vinayānuggahāya| yaṃ saṅghaphāsu taṃ saṅghasuṭṭhu|

yaṃ dummaṅkūnaṃ puggalānaṃ niggahāya…pai॰… yaṃ paisalānaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ phāsuvihārāya… yaṃ diṭṭhadhammikānaṃ āsavānaṃ saṃvarāya… yaṃ samparāyikānaṃ āsavānaṃ  paṭighātāya… yaṃ appasan‍nānaṃ pasādāya… yaṃ pasan‍nānaṃ bhiyyaubhāvāya… yaṃ saddhammaṭṭhitiyā… yaṃ vinayānuggahāya taṃ saṅghasuṭṭhu| yaṃ vinayānuggahāya taṃ saṅghaphāsu| yaṃ vinayānuggahāya taṃ dummaṅkūnaṃ puggalānaṃ niggahāya| yaṃ vinayānuggahāya taṃ paisalānaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ phāsuvihārāya| yaṃ vinayānuggahāya taṃ diṭṭhadhammikānaṃ āsavānaṃ saṃvarāya| yaṃ vinayānuggahāya taṃ samparāyikānaṃ āsavānaṃ paṭighātāya| yaṃ vinayānuggahāya taṃ appasan‍nānaṃ pasādāya| yaṃ vinayānuggahāya taṃ pasan‍nānaṃ bhiyyaubhāvāya| yaṃ vinayānuggahāya taṃ  saddhammaṭṭhitiyāti|

atthasataṃ dhammasataṃ, dvai ca niruttisatāni|

cattāri ñāṇasatāni, atthavasai pakaraṇaiti||

atthavasapakaraṇaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

mahāvaggau niṭṭhitau|

tassuddānaṃ –

paṭhamaṃ aṭṭhapucchāyaṃ, pac‍cayaisu punaṭṭha ca|

bhikkhūnaṃ sauḷasa aitai, bhikkhunīnañ‍ca sauḷasa||

paiyyālaantarā bhaidā, aikuttarikamaiva ca|

pavāraṇatthavasikā, mahāvaggassa saṅgahauti||

atthavasapakaraṇaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|


gāthāsaṅgaṇikaṃ

1. sattanagaraisu pañ‍ñattasikkhāpadaṃ

335.

 aikaṃsaṃ  cīvaraṃ katvā, paggaṇhitvāna añ‍jaliṃ|

āsīsamānarūpauva āsiṃsamānarūpauva (sī॰ syā॰), kissa tvaṃ idha māgatau||

dvīsu vinayaisu yai pañ‍ñattā|

uddaisaṃ āgacchanti upausathaisu|

kati tai sikkhāpadā haunti|

katisu nagaraisu pañ‍ñattā||

bhaddakau tai ummaṅgau, yaunisau paripucchasi|

taggha tai ahamakkhissaṃ, yathāsi kusalau tathā||

dvīsu vinayaisu yai pañ‍ñattā|

uddaisaṃ āgacchanti upausathaisu|

aḍḍhuḍḍhasatāni tai haunti|

sattasu nagaraisu pañ‍ñattā||

katamaisu sattasu nagaraisu pañ‍ñattā|

iṅgha mai tvaṃ byākara naṃ iṅgha mai taṃ byākara (ka॰)|

taṃ vacanapathaṃ tava vacanapathaṃ (syā॰) nisāmayitvā|

paṭipaj‍jaima hitāya nau siyā||

vaisāliyaṃ  rājagahai, sāvatthiyañ‍ca āḷaviyaṃ|

kausambiyañ‍ca sak‍kaisu, bhaggaisu caiva pañ‍ñattā||

kati  vaisāliyaṃ pañ‍ñattā, kati rājagahai katā|

sāvatthiyaṃ kati haunti, kati āḷaviyaṃ katā||

kati kausambiyaṃ pañ‍ñattā, kati sak‍kaisu vuc‍canti|

kati bhaggaisu pañ‍ñattā, taṃ mai akkhāhi pucchitau||

dasa  vaisāliyaṃ pañ‍ñattā, aikavīsa rājagahai katā|

chaūna tīṇisatāni, sabbai sāvatthiyaṃ katā||

cha āḷaviyaṃ pañ‍ñattā, aṭṭha kausambiyaṃ katā|

aṭṭha sak‍kaisu vuc‍canti, tayau bhaggaisu pañ‍ñattā||

yai vaisāliyaṃ pañ‍ñattā, tai suṇauhi yathātathaṃ yathākathaṃ (sī॰ syā॰ aivamuparipi)|

maithunaviggahuttari, atiraikañ‍ca kāḷakaṃ||

bhūtaṃ paramparabhattaṃ, dantapaunaina dantapauṇaina (ka॰) acailakau|

bhikkhunīsu ca ak‍kausau, dasaitai vaisāliyaṃ katā||

yai rājagahai pañ‍ñattā, tai suṇauhi yathātathaṃ|

adin‍nādānaṃ rājagahai, dvai anuddhaṃsanā dvaipi ca bhaidā||

antaravāsakaṃ rūpiyaṃ suttaṃ, ujjhāpanaina ca pācitapiṇḍaṃ|

gaṇabhaujanaṃ vikālai ca, cārittaṃ nahānaṃ ūnavīsati||

cīvaraṃ datvā vausāsanti, aitai rājagahai katā|

giraggacariyā tatthaiva, chandadānaina aikavīsati||

yai  sāvatthiyaṃ pañ‍ñattā, tai suṇauhi yathātathaṃ|

pārājikāni cattāri, saṅghādisaisā bhavanti sauḷasa||

aniyatā ca dvai haunti, nissaggiyā catuvīsati|

chapañ‍ñāsasatañ‍caiva, khuddakāni pavuc‍canti||

dasayaiva ca gārayhā, dvaisattati ca saikhiyā|

chaūna tīṇisatāni, sabbai sāvatthiyaṃ katā||

yai āḷaviyaṃ pañ‍ñattā, tai suṇauhi yathātathaṃ|

kuṭikausiyasaiyyā ca, khaṇanai gaccha daivatai|

sappāṇakañ‍ca siñ‍canti, cha aitai āḷaviyaṃ katā||

yai kausambiyaṃ pañ‍ñattā, tai suṇauhi yathātathaṃ|

mahāvihārau dauvacassaṃ, añ‍ñaṃ dvāraṃ surāya ca|

anādariyaṃ sahadhammau, payaupānaina aṭṭhamaṃ||

yai  sak‍kaisu pañ‍ñattā, tai suṇauhi yathātathaṃ|

aiḷakalaumāni pattau ca, auvādau caiva bhaisaj‍jaṃ||

sūci ārañ‍ñikau caiva, aṭṭhaitai cha aitai (sabbattha) kāpilavatthavai|

udakasuddhiyā  auvādau, bhikkhunīsu pavuc‍canti||

yai bhaggaisu pañ‍ñattā, tai suṇauhi yathātathaṃ|

samādahitvā visibbainti, sāmisaina sasitthakaṃ||

pārājikāni cattāri, saṅghādisaisāni bhavanti|

satta ca nissaggiyāni, aṭṭha dvattiṃsa khuddakā||

dvai  gārayhā tayau saikkhā, chappañ‍ñāsa sikkhāpadā|

chasu nagaraisu pañ‍ñattā, buddhainādic‍cabandhunā||

chaūna tīṇisatāni, sabbai sāvatthiyaṃ katā|

kāruṇikaina buddhaina, gautamaina yasassinā||

2. catuvipattiṃ

336.

 yaṃ taṃ yaṃ yaṃ (ka॰) pucchimha akittayi nau|

taṃ taṃ byākataṃ anañ‍ñathā|

añ‍ñaṃ taṃ pucchāmi tadiṅgha brūhi|

garuka lahukañ‍cāpi sāvasaisaṃ|

anavasaisaṃ duṭṭhul‍lañ‍ca aduṭṭhul‍laṃ|

yai ca yāvatatiyakā||

sādhāraṇaṃ asādhāraṇaṃ|

vibhattiyau ca vipattiyau ca (sī॰ syā॰) yaihi samathaihi sammanti|

sabbānipaitāni viyākarauhi|

handa vākyaṃ suṇauma tai||

aikatiṃsā  yai garukā, aṭṭhaittha anavasaisā|

yai  garukā tai duṭṭhul‍lā, yai duṭṭhul‍lā sā sīlavipatti|

pārājikaṃ saṅghādisaisau, ‘‘sīlavipattī’’ti vuc‍cati||

thul‍lac‍cayaṃ  pācittiyā, pāṭidaisanīyaṃ duk‍kaṭaṃ|

dubbhāsitaṃ yau cāyaṃ, ak‍kausati hasādhippāyau|

ayaṃ sā ācāravipattisammatā||

viparītadiṭṭhiṃ  gaṇhanti, asaddhammaihi purakkhatā|

abbhācikkhanti sambuddhaṃ, duppañ‍ñā mauhapārutā|

ayaṃ sā diṭṭhivipattisammatā||

ājīvahaitu ājīvakāraṇā pāpicchau icchāpakatau asantaṃ abhūtaṃ uttarimanussadhammaṃ ul‍lapati, ājīvahaitu ājīvakāraṇā sañ‍carittaṃ samāpaj‍jati, ājīvahaitu ājīvakāraṇā – ‘‘yau tai vihārai vasati, sau bhikkhu arahā’’ti bhaṇati, ājīvahaitu ājīvakāraṇā bhikkhu paṇītabhaujanāni attanau atthāya viñ‍ñāpaitvā bhuñ‍jati, ājīvahaitu ājīvakāraṇā bhikkhunī paṇītabhaujanāni attanau atthāya viñ‍ñāpaitvā bhuñ‍jati, ājīvahaitu ājīvakāraṇā sūpaṃ vā audanaṃ vā agilānau attanau atthāya viñ‍ñāpaitvā bhuñ‍jati| ayaṃ sā ājīvavipatti sammatā|

aikādasa yāvatatiyakā, tai suṇauhi yathātathaṃ|

ukkhittānuvattikā, aṭṭha yāvatatiyakā|

ariṭṭhau caṇḍakāḷī ca, imai tai yāvatatiyakā||

3. chaidanakādi

337. kati  chaidanakāni? kati bhaidanakāni? kati uddālanakāni? kati anañ‍ñapācittiyāni? kati bhikkhusammutiyau? kati sāmīciyau? kati paramāni?

kati jānanti pañ‍ñattā, buddhainādic‍cabandhunā||

cha chaidanakāni| aikaṃ bhaidanakaṃ| aikaṃ uddālanakaṃ| cattāri anañ‍ñapācittiyāni| catassau bhikkhusammutiyau| satta sāmīciyau| cuddasa paramāni|

saudasa sauḷasa (sī॰ syā॰) aṭṭhakathā aulaukaitabbā jānanti pañ‍ñattā, buddhainādic‍cabandhunā||

4. asādhāraṇādi

338.

 vīsaṃ  dvai satāni bhikkhūnaṃ sikkhāpadāni|

uddaisaṃ āgacchanti upausathaisu|

tīṇi satāni cattāri bhikkhunīnaṃ sikkhāpadāni|

uddaisaṃ āgacchanti upausathaisu||

chacattārīsā bhikkhūnaṃ, bhikkhunīhi asādhāraṇā|

sataṃ tiṃsā ca bhikkhunīnaṃ, bhikkhūhi asādhāraṇā||

sataṃ sattati chac‍caiva, ubhin‍naṃ asādhāraṇā|

sataṃ sattati cattāri, ubhin‍naṃ samasikkhatā||

vīsaṃ  dvai satāni bhikkhūnaṃ sikkhāpadāni|

uddaisaṃ āgacchanti upausathaisu|

tai suṇauhi yathātathaṃ||

pārājikāni cattāri, saṅghādisaisāni bhavanti tairasa|

aniyatā dvai haunti||

nissaggiyāni tiṃsaiva, dvainavuti ca khuddakā|

cattārau  pāṭidaisanīyā, pañ‍casattati saikhiyā||

vīsaṃ dvai satāni cimai haunti bhikkhūnaṃ sikkhāpadāni|

uddaisaṃ āgacchanti upausathaisu||

tīṇi satāni cattāri, bhikkhunīnaṃ sikkhāpadāni|

uddaisaṃ āgacchanti upausathaisu, tai suṇauhi yathātathaṃ||

pārājikāni  aṭṭha, saṅghādisaisāni bhavanti sattarasa|

nissaggiyāni tiṃsaiva, sataṃ saṭṭhi cha caiva khuddakāni pavuc‍canti||

aṭṭha pāṭidaisanīyā, pañ‍casattati saikhiyā|

tīṇi satāni cattāri cimai haunti bhikkhunīnaṃ sikkhāpadāni|

uddaisaṃ āgacchanti upausathaisu||

chacattārīsā bhikkhūnaṃ, bhikkhunīhi asādhāraṇā|

tai suṇauhi yathātathaṃ||

 saṅghādisaisā, dvai aniyataihi aṭṭha|

nissaggiyāni  dvādasa, taihi tai haunti vīsati||

dvaivīsati khuddakā, caturau pāṭidaisanīyā|

chacattārīsā cimai haunti, bhikkhūnaṃ bhikkhunīhi asādhāraṇā||

sataṃ tiṃsā ca bhikkhunīnaṃ, bhikkhūhi asādhāraṇā|

tai suṇauhi yathātathaṃ||

pārājikāni  cattāri, saṅghamhā dasa nissarai|

nissaggiyāni dvādasa, chan‍navuti ca khuddakā|

aṭṭha pāṭidaisanīyā||

sataṃ tiṃsā cimai haunti bhikkhunīnaṃ, bhikkhūhi asādhāraṇā|

sataṃ sattati chac‍caiva, ubhin‍naṃ asādhāraṇā|

tai suṇauhi yathātathaṃ||

pārājikāni cattāri, saṅghādisaisāni bhavanti sauḷasa|

aniyatā dvai haunti, nissaggiyāni catuvīsati|

sataṃ aṭṭhārasā caiva, khuddakāni pavuc‍canti|

dvādasa pāṭidaisanīyā||

sataṃ sattati chac‍caivimai haunti, ubhin‍naṃ asādhāraṇā|

sataṃ sattati cattāri, ubhin‍naṃ samasikkhatā|

tai suṇauhi yathātathaṃ||

pārājikāni cattāri, saṅghādisaisāni bhavanti satta|

nissaggiyāni aṭṭhārasa, samasattati khuddakā|

pañ‍casattati saikhiyāni||

sataṃ sattati cattāri cimai haunti, ubhin‍naṃ samasikkhatā|

aṭṭhai  pārājikā yai durāsadā, tālavatthusamūpamā||

paṇḍupalāsau puthusilā, sīsacchin‍nauva sau narau|

tālauva matthakacchin‍nau, aviruḷhī bhavanti tai||

taivīsati  saṅghādisaisā, dvai aniyatā|

dvai  cattārīsa nissaggiyā|

aṭṭhāsītisataṃ pācittiyā, dvādasa pāṭidaisanīyā||

pañ‍casattati saikhiyā, tīhi samathaihi sammanti|

sammukhā ca paṭiñ‍ñāya, tiṇavatthārakaina ca||

dvai upausathā dvai pavāraṇā|

cattāri kammāni jinaina daisitā|

pañ‍caiva uddaisā caturau bhavanti|

anañ‍ñathā āpattikkhandhā ca bhavanti satta||

adhikaraṇāni  cattāri sattahi samathaihi sammanti|

dvīhi catūhi tīhi kic‍caṃ aikaina sammati||

5. pārājikādiāpatti

339.

 ‘pārājika’nti yaṃ vuttaṃ, taṃ suṇauhi yathātathaṃ|

cutau paraddhau bhaṭṭhau ca, saddhammā hi niraṅkatau|

saṃvāsaupi tahiṃ natthi, tainaitaṃ iti vuc‍cati||

‘saṅghādisaisau’ti yaṃ vuttaṃ, taṃ suṇauhi yathātathaṃ|

saṅghauva daiti parivāsaṃ, mūlāya paṭikassati|

mānattaṃ daiti abbhaiti, tainaitaṃ iti vuc‍cati||

‘aniyatau’ti yaṃ vuttaṃ, taṃ suṇauhi yathātathaṃ|

aniyatau na niyatau, anaikaṃsikataṃ padaṃ|

tiṇṇamañ‍ñataraṃ ṭhānaṃ, ‘aniyatau’ti pavuc‍cati||

‘thul‍lac‍caya’nti yaṃ vuttaṃ, taṃ suṇauhi yathātathaṃ|

aikassa  mūlai yau daisaiti, yau ca taṃ paṭigaṇhati|

ac‍cayau taina samau natthi, tainaitaṃ iti vuc‍cati||

‘nissaggiya’nti yaṃ vuttaṃ, taṃ suṇauhi yathātathaṃ|

saṅghamajjhai gaṇamajjhai, aikassaiva ca aikatau|

nissaj‍jitvāna daisaiti, tainaitaṃ iti vuc‍cati||

‘pācittiya’nti  yaṃ vuttaṃ, taṃ suṇauhi yathātathaṃ|

pātaiti kusalaṃ dhammaṃ, ariyamaggaṃ aparajjhati|

cittasaṃmauhanaṭṭhānaṃ, tainaitaṃ iti vuc‍cati||

‘pāṭidaisanīya’nti  yaṃ vuttaṃ, taṃ suṇauhi yathātathaṃ|

bhikkhu añ‍ñātakau santau, kicchā laddhāya bhaujanaṃ|

sāmaṃ gahaitvā bhuñ‍jaiyya, ‘gārayha’nti pavuc‍cati||

nimantanāsu bhuñ‍jantā chandāya, vausāsati tattha bhikkhuniṃ|

anivāraitvā tahiṃ bhuñ‍jai, gārayhanti pavuc‍cati||

saddhācittaṃ  kulaṃ gantvā, appabhaugaṃ anāḷiyaṃ anāḷhiyaṃ (sī॰ syā॰)|

agilānau tahiṃ bhuñ‍jai, gārayhanti pavuc‍cati||

yau cai arañ‍ñai viharantau, sāsaṅkai sabhayānakai|

aviditaṃ tahiṃ bhuñ‍jai, gārayhanti pavuc‍cati||

bhikkhunī añ‍ñātikā santā, yaṃ paraisaṃ mamāyitaṃ|

sappi tailaṃ madhuṃ phāṇitaṃ, macchamaṃsaṃ athau khīraṃ|

dadhiṃ sayaṃ  viñ‍ñāpaiyya bhikkhunī, gārayhapattā sugatassa sāsanai||

‘duk‍kaṭa’nti yaṃ vuttaṃ, taṃ suṇauhi yathātathaṃ|

aparaddhaṃ viraddhañ‍ca, khalitaṃ yañ‍ca duk‍kaṭaṃ||

yaṃ manussau karai pāpaṃ, āvi vā yadi vā rahau|

‘duk‍kaṭa’nti pavaidainti, tainaitaṃ iti vuc‍cati||

‘dubbhāsita’nti yaṃ vuttaṃ, taṃ suṇauhi yathātathaṃ|

dubbhāsitaṃ durābhaṭṭhaṃ, saṃkiliṭṭhañ‍ca yaṃ padaṃ|

yañ‍ca viñ‍ñū garahanti, tainaitaṃ iti vuc‍cati||

‘saikhiya’nti yaṃ vuttaṃ, taṃ suṇauhi yathātathaṃ|

saikkhassa sikkhamānassa, ujumaggānusārinau||

ādi  caitaṃ caraṇañ‍ca, mukhaṃ sañ‍ñamasaṃvarau|

sikkhā aitādisī natthi, tainaitaṃ iti vuc‍cati||

udā॰ 45 udānaipi chan‍namativassati, vivaṭaṃ nātivassati|

tasmā chan‍naṃ vivaraitha, aivaṃ taṃ nātivassati||

gati migānaṃ pavanaṃ, ākāsau pakkhinaṃ gati|

vibhavau gati dhammānaṃ, nibbānaṃ arahatau gatīti||

gāthāsaṅgaṇikaṃ|

tassuddānaṃ –

sattanagaraisu pañ‍ñattā, vipatti caturaupi ca|

bhikkhūnaṃ  bhikkhunīnañ‍ca, sādhāraṇā asādhāraṇā|

sāsanaṃ anuggahāya, gāthāsaṅgaṇikaṃ idanti||

gāthāsaṅgaṇikaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|



adhikaraṇabhaidau

1. uk‍kauṭanabhaidādi

340. cūḷava॰ 215; pari॰ 275 cattāri  adhikaraṇāni| vivādādhikaraṇaṃ, anuvādādhikaraṇaṃ, āpattādhikaraṇaṃ, kic‍cādhikaraṇaṃ – imāni cattāri adhikaraṇāni|

imaisaṃ catun‍naṃ adhikaraṇānaṃ kati uk‍kauṭā? imaisaṃ catun‍naṃ adhikaraṇānaṃ dasa uk‍kauṭā| vivādādhikaraṇassa dvai uk‍kauṭā, anuvādādhikaraṇassa cattārau uk‍kauṭā, āpattādhikaraṇassa tayau uk‍kauṭā, kic‍cādhikaraṇassa aikau uk‍kauṭau – imaisaṃ catun‍naṃ adhikaraṇānaṃ imai dasa uk‍kauṭā|

vivādādhikaraṇaṃ uk‍kauṭaintau kati samathai uk‍kauṭaiti? anuvādādhikaraṇaṃ uk‍kauṭaintau kati samathai uk‍kauṭaiti? āpattādhikaraṇaṃ uk‍kauṭaintau kati samathai uk‍kauṭaiti? kic‍cādhikaraṇaṃ uk‍kauṭaintau kati samathai uk‍kauṭaiti?

vivādādhikaraṇaṃ uk‍kauṭaintau dvai samathai uk‍kauṭaiti| anuvādādhikaraṇaṃ uk‍kauṭaintau cattārau samathai uk‍kauṭaiti| āpattādhikaraṇaṃ uk‍kauṭaintau tayau samathai uk‍kauṭaiti| kic‍cādhikaraṇaṃ uk‍kauṭaintau aikaṃ samathaṃ uk‍kauṭaiti|

341. kati uk‍kauṭā? katihākāraihi uk‍kauṭanaṃ pasavati? katihaṅgaihi saman‍nāgatau puggalau adhikaraṇaṃ  uk‍kauṭaiti? kati puggalā adhikaraṇaṃ uk‍kauṭaintā āpattiṃ āpaj‍janti?

dvādasa  uk‍kauṭā| dasahākāraihi uk‍kauṭanaṃ pasavati| catūhaṅgaihi saman‍nāgatau puggalau adhikaraṇaṃ uk‍kauṭaiti| cattārau puggalā adhikaraṇaṃ uk‍kauṭaintā āpattiṃ āpaj‍janti?

katamai dvādasa uk‍kauṭā? akataṃ kammaṃ, duk‍kaṭaṃ kammaṃ, puna kātabbaṃ kammaṃ, anihataṃ, dun‍nihataṃ, puna nihanitabbaṃ, avinicchitaṃ, duvinicchitaṃ, puna vinicchitabbaṃ, avūpasantaṃ, duvūpasantaṃ, puna vūpasamaitabbanti – imai dvādasa uk‍kauṭā|

katamaihi  dasahākāraihi uk‍kauṭanaṃ pasavati? tattha jātakaṃ adhikaraṇaṃ uk‍kauṭaiti, tattha jātakaṃ vūpasantaṃ adhikaraṇaṃ uk‍kauṭaiti, antarāmaggai adhikaraṇaṃ uk‍kauṭaiti, antarāmaggai vūpasantaṃ adhikaraṇaṃ uk‍kauṭaiti, tattha gataṃ adhikaraṇaṃ uk‍kauṭaiti, tattha gataṃ vūpasantaṃ adhikaraṇaṃ uk‍kauṭaiti, sativinayaṃ uk‍kauṭaiti, amūḷhavinayaṃ uk‍kauṭaiti, tassapāpiyasikaṃ  uk‍kauṭaiti, tiṇavatthārakaṃ uk‍kauṭaiti – imaihi dasahākāraihi uk‍kauṭanaṃ pasavati|

katamaihi catūhaṅgaihi saman‍nāgatau puggalau adhikaraṇaṃ uk‍kauṭaiti? chandāgatiṃ gacchantau adhikaraṇaṃ uk‍kauṭaiti, dausāgatiṃ gacchantau adhikaraṇaṃ uk‍kauṭaiti, mauhāgatiṃ gacchantau adhikaraṇaṃ uk‍kauṭaiti, bhayāgatiṃ gacchantau adhikaraṇaṃ uk‍kauṭaiti – imaihi catūhaṅgaihi saman‍nāgatau puggalau adhikaraṇaṃ uk‍kauṭaiti|

katamai cattārau puggalā adhikaraṇaṃ uk‍kauṭaintā āpattiṃ āpaj‍janti? tadahupasampan‍nau uk‍kauṭaiti uk‍kauṭanakaṃ pācittiyaṃ, āgantukau uk‍kauṭaiti uk‍kauṭanakaṃ pācittiyaṃ, kārakau uk‍kauṭaiti uk‍kauṭanakaṃ pācittiyaṃ, chandadāyakau uk‍kauṭaiti uk‍kauṭanakaṃ pācittiyaṃ – imai cattārau puggalā adhikaraṇaṃ uk‍kauṭaintā āpattiṃ āpaj‍janti|

2. adhikaraṇanidānādi

342. vivādādhikaraṇaṃ  kiṃnidānaṃ, kiṃsamudayaṃ, kiṃjātikaṃ, kiṃpabhavaṃ, kiṃsambhāraṃ, kiṃsamuṭṭhānaṃ? anuvādādhikaraṇaṃ kiṃnidānaṃ, kiṃsamudayaṃ, kiṃjātikaṃ, kiṃpabhavaṃ, kiṃsambhāraṃ, kiṃsamuṭṭhānaṃ? āpattādhikaraṇaṃ kiṃnidānaṃ, kiṃsamudayaṃ, kiṃjātikaṃ, kiṃpabhavaṃ, kiṃsambhāraṃ, kiṃsamuṭṭhānaṃ? kic‍cādhikaraṇaṃ kiṃnidānaṃ, kiṃsamudayaṃ, kiṃjātikaṃ, kiṃpabhavaṃ, kiṃsambhāraṃ, kiṃsamuṭṭhānaṃ?

vivādādhikaraṇaṃ vivādanidānaṃ vivādasamudayaṃ vivādajātikaṃ vivādapabhavaṃ vivādasambhāraṃ vivādasamuṭṭhānaṃ| anuvādādhikaraṇaṃ anuvādanidānaṃ anuvādasamudayaṃ anuvādajātikaṃ anuvādapabhavaṃ anuvādasambhāraṃ anuvādasamuṭṭhānaṃ| āpattādhikaraṇaṃ āpattinidānaṃ āpattisamudayaṃ āpattijātikaṃ āpattipabhavaṃ āpattisambhāraṃ āpattisamuṭṭhānaṃ| kic‍cādhikaraṇaṃ kic‍cayanidānaṃ kic‍cayasamudayaṃ kic‍cayajātikaṃ kic‍cayapabhavaṃ kic‍cayasambhāraṃ kic‍cayasamuṭṭhānaṃ|

vivādādhikaraṇaṃ  kiṃnidānaṃ, kiṃsamudayaṃ, kiṃjātikaṃ, kiṃpabhavaṃ, kiṃsambhāraṃ, kiṃsamuṭṭhānaṃ? anuvādādhikaraṇaṃ…pai॰… āpattādhikaraṇaṃ…pai॰… kic‍cādhikaraṇaṃ kiṃnidānaṃ, kiṃsamudayaṃ, kiṃjātikaṃ, kiṃpabhavaṃ, kiṃsambhāraṃ, kiṃsamuṭṭhānaṃ?

vivādādhikaraṇaṃ haitunidānaṃ, haitusamudayaṃ, haitujātikaṃ, haitupabhavaṃ, haitusambhāraṃ, haitusamuṭṭhānaṃ| anuvādādhikaraṇaṃ…pai॰… āpattādhikaraṇaṃ…pai॰… kic‍cādhikaraṇaṃ haitunidānaṃ, haitusamudayaṃ, haitujātikaṃ, haitupabhavaṃ, haitusambhāraṃ, haitusamuṭṭhānaṃ|

vivādādhikaraṇaṃ kiṃnidānaṃ, kiṃsamudayaṃ, kiṃjātikaṃ, kiṃpabhavaṃ, kiṃsambhāraṃ, kiṃsamuṭṭhānaṃ? anuvādādhikaraṇaṃ  …pai॰… āpattādhikaraṇaṃ…pai॰… kic‍cādhikaraṇaṃ kiṃnidānaṃ, kiṃsamudayaṃ, kiṃjātikaṃ, kiṃpabhavaṃ, kiṃsambhāraṃ, kiṃsamuṭṭhānaṃ?

vivādādhikaraṇaṃ pac‍cayanidānaṃ, pac‍cayasamudayaṃ, pac‍cayajātikaṃ, pac‍cayapabhavaṃ, pac‍cayasambhāraṃ, pac‍cayasamuṭṭhānaṃ| anuvādādhikaraṇaṃ…pai॰… āpattādhikaraṇaṃ…pai॰… kic‍cādhikaraṇaṃ pac‍cayanidānaṃ, pac‍cayasamudayaṃ, pac‍cayajātikaṃ, pac‍cayapabhavaṃ, pac‍cayasambhāraṃ, pac‍cayasamuṭṭhānaṃ|

3. adhikaraṇamūlādi

343. catun‍naṃ  adhikaraṇānaṃ kati mūlāni, kati samuṭṭhānā? catun‍naṃ adhikaraṇānaṃ taittiṃsa mūlāni, taittiṃsa samuṭṭhānā|

catun‍naṃ adhikaraṇānaṃ katamāni taittiṃsa mūlāni? vivādādhikaraṇassa dvādasa mūlāni, anuvādādhikaraṇassa cuddasa mūlāni, āpattādhikaraṇassa cha mūlāni, kic‍cādhikaraṇassa aikaṃ mūlaṃ, saṅghau – catun‍naṃ adhikaraṇānaṃ imāni taittiṃsa mūlāni|

catun‍naṃ  adhikaraṇānaṃ katamai taittiṃsa samuṭṭhānā? vivādādhikaraṇassa aṭṭhārasabhaidakaravatthūni samuṭṭhānā, anuvādādhikaraṇassa catassau vipattiyau samuṭṭhānā, āpattādhikaraṇassa sattāpattikkhandhā samuṭṭhānā, kic‍cādhikaraṇassa cattāri kammāni samuṭṭhānā – catun‍naṃ adhikaraṇānaṃ imai taittiṃsa samuṭṭhānā|

4. adhikaraṇapac‍cayāpatti

344. vivādādhikaraṇaṃ  āpattānāpattīti? vivādādhikaraṇaṃ na āpatti| kiṃ pana vivādādhikaraṇapac‍cayā āpattiṃ āpaj‍jaiyyāti? āma, vivādādhikaraṇapac‍cayā āpattiṃ āpaj‍jaiyya| vivādādhikaraṇapac‍cayā  kati āpattiyau āpaj‍jati? vivādādhikaraṇapac‍cayā dvai āpattiyau āpaj‍jati| upasampan‍naṃ aumasati, āpatti pācittiyassa; anupasampan‍naṃ aumasati, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa – vivādādhikaraṇapac‍cayā imā dvai āpattiyau āpaj‍jati|

tā āpattiyau catun‍naṃ vipattīnaṃ kati vipattiyau bhajanti? catun‍naṃ adhikaraṇānaṃ katamaṃ adhikaraṇaṃ? sattan‍naṃ āpattikkhandhānaṃ katihi āpattikkhandhaihi saṅgahitā? chan‍naṃ āpattisamuṭṭhānānaṃ katihi samuṭṭhānaihi samuṭṭhanti? katihi adhikaraṇaihi katisu ṭhānaisu katihi samathaihi sammanti?

tā āpattiyau catun‍naṃ vipattīnaṃ aikaṃ vipattiṃ bhajanti – ācāravipattiṃ| catun‍naṃ adhikaraṇānaṃ, āpattādhikaraṇaṃ| sattan‍naṃ āpattikkhandhānaṃ dvīhi āpattikkhandhaihi saṅgahitā – siyā pācittiyāpattikkhandhaina, siyā duk‍kaṭāpattikkhandhaina| chan‍naṃ āpattisamuṭṭhānānaṃ tīhi samuṭṭhānaihi  samuṭṭhanti| aikaina adhikaraṇaina – kic‍cādhikaraṇaina; tīsu ṭhānaisu – saṅghamajjhai, gaṇamajjhai, puggalassa santikai; tīhi samathaihi sammanti – siyā sammukhāvinayaina ca paṭiñ‍ñātakaraṇaina ca, siyā sammukhāvinayaina ca tiṇavatthārakaina ca|

345. anuvādādhikaraṇaṃ āpattānāpattīti? anuvādādhikaraṇaṃ na āpatti| kiṃ pana anuvādādhikaraṇapac‍cayā āpattiṃ āpaj‍jaiyyāti? āma, anuvādādhikaraṇapac‍cayā āpattiṃ āpaj‍jaiyya| anuvādādhikaraṇapac‍cayā, kati āpattiyau āpaj‍jati? anuvādādhikaraṇapac‍cayā tissau  āpattiyau āpaj‍jati| bhikkhuṃ amūlakaina pārājikaina dhammaina anuddhaṃsaiti, āpatti saṅghādisaisassa; amūlakaina saṅghādisaisaina anuddhaṃsaiti, āpatti pācittiyassa; amūlikāya ācāravipattiyā anuddhaṃsaiti, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa – anuvādādhikaraṇapac‍cayā imā tissau āpattiyau āpaj‍jati|

tā  āpattiyau catun‍naṃ vipattīnaṃ kati vipattiyau bhajanti? catun‍naṃ adhikaraṇānaṃ katamaṃ adhikaraṇaṃ? sattan‍naṃ āpattikkhandhānaṃ katihi āpattikkhandhaihi saṅgahitā? chan‍naṃ āpattisamuṭṭhānānaṃ katihi samuṭṭhānaihi samuṭṭhanti? katihi adhikaraṇaihi katisu ṭhānaisu katihi samathaihi sammanti?

tā āpattiyau catun‍naṃ vipattīnaṃ dvai vipattiyau bhajanti – siyā sīlavipattiṃ, siyā ācāravipattiṃ| catun‍naṃ adhikaraṇānaṃ, āpattādhikaraṇaṃ| sattan‍naṃ āpattikkhandhānaṃ tīhi āpattikkhandhaihi saṅgahitā – siyā saṅghādisaisāpattikkhandhaina, siyā pācittiyāpattikkhandhaina, siyā duk‍kaṭāpattikkhandhaina| chan‍naṃ āpattisamuṭṭhānānaṃ tīhi samuṭṭhānaihi samuṭṭhanti| yā tā āpattiyau garukā tā āpattiyau aikaina adhikaraṇaina – kic‍cādhikaraṇaina; aikamhi ṭhānai – saṅghamajjhai; dvīhi samathaihi sammanti – sammukhāvinayaina ca paṭiñ‍ñātakaraṇaina ca| yā tā āpattiyau lahukā tā āpattiyau aikaina adhikaraṇaina – kic‍cādhikaraṇaina; tīsu ṭhānaisu – saṅghamajjhai gaṇamajjhai puggalassa santikai; tīhi samathaihi sammanti – siyā sammukhāvinayaina ca paṭiñ‍ñātakaraṇaina ca, siyā sammukhāvinayaina ca tiṇavatthārakaina ca|

346. āpattādhikaraṇaṃ āpattānāpattīti? āpattādhikaraṇaṃ āpatti| kiṃ pana āpattādhikaraṇapac‍cayā āpattiṃ āpaj‍jaiyyāti? āma, āpattādhikaraṇapac‍cayā āpattiṃ āpaj‍jaiyya| āpattādhikaraṇapac‍cayā kati āpattiyau āpaj‍jati? āpattādhikaraṇapac‍cayā catassau  āpattiyau āpaj‍jati| bhikkhunī jānaṃ pārājikaṃ dhammaṃ pārājikaṃ dhammaṃ ajjhāpan‍naṃ (syā॰) paṭicchādaiti, āpatti pārājikassa; vaimatikā paṭicchādaiti, āpatti thul‍lac‍cayassa; bhikkhu saṅghādisaisaṃ paṭicchādaiti, āpatti pācittiyassa; ācāravipattiṃ paṭicchādaiti, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa – āpattādhikaraṇapac‍cayā imā catassau āpattiyau āpaj‍jati|

tā āpattiyau catun‍naṃ vipattīnaṃ kati vipattiyau bhajanti? catun‍naṃ adhikaraṇānaṃ katamaṃ adhikaraṇaṃ? sattan‍naṃ āpattikkhandhānaṃ katihi āpattikkhandhaihi saṅgahitā? chan‍naṃ āpattisamuṭṭhānānaṃ katihi samuṭṭhānaihi samuṭṭhanti? katihi adhikaraṇaihi katisu ṭhānaisu katihi samathaihi sammanti?

tā āpattiyau catun‍naṃ vipattīnaṃ dvai vipattiyau bhajanti – siyā sīlavipattiṃ siyā ācāravipattiṃ| catun‍naṃ adhikaraṇānaṃ – āpattādhikaraṇaṃ| sattan‍naṃ āpattikkhandhānaṃ catūhi āpattikkhandhaihi saṅgahitā – siyā pārājikāpattikkhandhaina, siyā thul‍lac‍cayāpattikkhandhaina, siyā pācittiyāpattikkhandhaina, siyā duk‍kaṭāpattikkhandhaina| chan‍naṃ āpattisamuṭṭhānānaṃ aikaina samuṭṭhānaina samuṭṭhanti  – kāyatau ca vācatau ca cittatau ca samuṭṭhanti| yā sā āpatti anavasaisā sā āpatti na katamaina adhikaraṇaina, na katamamhi ṭhānai, na katamaina samathaina sammati| yā tā āpattiyau lahukā tā āpattiyau aikaina adhikaraṇaina – kic‍cādhikaraṇaina; tīsu ṭhānaisu – saṅghamajjhai, gaṇamajjhai, puggalassa santikai; tīhi samathaihi sammanti – siyā sammukhāvinayaina ca paṭiñ‍ñātakaraṇaina ca, siyā sammukhāvinayaina ca tiṇavatthārakaina ca|

347. kic‍cādhikaraṇaṃ āpattānāpattīti? kic‍cādhikaraṇaṃ na āpatti| kiṃ pana kic‍cādhikaraṇapac‍cayā āpattiṃ āpaj‍jaiyyāti? āma, kic‍cādhikaraṇapac‍cayā āpattiṃ āpaj‍jaiyya| kic‍cādhikaraṇapac‍cayā kati āpattiyau āpaj‍jati? kic‍cādhikaraṇapac‍cayā pañ‍ca āpattiyau āpaj‍jati| ukkhittānuvattikā bhikkhunī yāvatatiyaṃ samanubhāsanāya na paṭinissaj‍jati, ñattiyā duk‍kaṭaṃ; dvīhi kammavācāhi thul‍lac‍cayā; kammavācāpariyausānai āpatti pārājikassa; bhaidakānuvattakā bhikkhū yāvatatiyaṃ samanubhāsanāya na paṭinissaj‍janti, āpatti saṅghādisaisassa; pāpikāya diṭṭhiyā yāvatatiyaṃ samanubhāsanāya na paṭinissaj‍janti, āpatti pācittiyassa – kic‍cādhikaraṇapac‍cayā imā pañ‍ca āpattiyau āpaj‍jati|

tā  āpattiyau catun‍naṃ vipattīnaṃ kati vipattiyau bhajanti? catun‍naṃ adhikaraṇānaṃ katamaṃ adhikaraṇaṃ? sattan‍naṃ āpattikkhandhānaṃ katihi āpattikkhandhaihi saṅgahitā? chan‍naṃ āpattisamuṭṭhānānaṃ  katihi samuṭṭhānaihi samuṭṭhanti? katihi adhikaraṇaihi katisu ṭhānaisu katihi samathaihi sammanti?

tā  āpattiyau catun‍naṃ vipattīnaṃ dvai vipattiyau bhajanti – siyā sīlavipattiṃ siyā ācāravipattiṃ| catun‍naṃ adhikaraṇānaṃ – āpattādhikaraṇaṃ| sattan‍naṃ āpattikkhandhānaṃ pañ‍cahi āpattikkhandhaihi saṅgahitā – siyā pārājikāpattikkhandhaina, siyā saṅghādisaisāpattikkhandhaina, siyā thul‍lac‍cayāpattikkhandhaina, siyā pācittiyāpattikkhandhaina, siyā duk‍kaṭāpattikkhandhaina| chan‍naṃ āpattisamuṭṭhānānaṃ aikaina samuṭṭhānaina samuṭṭhanti – kāyatau ca vācatau ca cittatau ca samuṭṭhanti| yā sā āpatti anavasaisā sā āpatti na katamaina adhikaraṇaina, na katamamhi ṭhānai, na katamaina samathaina sammati| yā sā āpatti garukā sā āpatti aikaina adhikaraṇaina – kic‍cādhikaraṇaina; aikamhi ṭhānai – saṅghamajjhai; dvīhi samathaihi sammati – sammukhāvinayaina ca paṭiñ‍ñātakaraṇaina ca| yā tā āpattiyau lahukā tā āpattiyau aikaina adhikaraṇaina – kic‍cādhikaraṇaina; tīsu ṭhānaisu – saṅghamajjhai, gaṇamajjhai, puggalassa santikai; tīhi samathaihi sammanti – siyā sammukhāvinayaina ca paṭiñ‍ñātakaraṇaina ca, siyā sammukhāvinayaina ca tiṇavatthārakaina ca|

5. adhikaraṇādhippāyau

348. vivādādhikaraṇaṃ hauti anuvādādhikaraṇaṃ, hauti āpattādhikaraṇaṃ, hauti kic‍cādhikaraṇaṃ| vivādādhikaraṇaṃ na hauti anuvādādhikaraṇaṃ, na hauti āpattādhikaraṇaṃ, na hauti kic‍cādhikaraṇaṃ; api  ca, vivādādhikaraṇapac‍cayā hauti anuvādādhikaraṇaṃ, hauti āpattādhikaraṇaṃ, hauti kic‍cādhikaraṇaṃ| yathā kathaṃ viya? cūḷava॰ 215; pari॰ 314 idha bhikkhū vivadanti – dhammauti vā adhammauti vā duṭṭhul‍lāpattīti vā aduṭṭhul‍lāpattīti vā| yaṃ tattha bhaṇḍanaṃ kalahau viggahau vivādau nānāvādau añ‍ñathāvādau vipac‍catāya vauhārau maidhakaṃ, idaṃ vuc‍cati vivādādhikaraṇaṃ| vivādādhikaraṇai saṅghau vivadati vivādādhikaraṇaṃ| vivadamānau anuvadati anuvādādhikaraṇaṃ| anuvadamānau āpattiṃ āpaj‍jati āpattādhikaraṇaṃ| tāya āpattiyā saṅghau kammaṃ karauti kic‍cādhikaraṇaṃ| aivaṃ vivādādhikaraṇapac‍cayā hauti anuvādādhikaraṇaṃ, hauti āpattādhikaraṇaṃ, hauti kic‍cādhikaraṇaṃ|

anuvādādhikaraṇaṃ  hauti āpattādhikaraṇaṃ, hauti kic‍cādhikaraṇaṃ, hauti vivādādhikaraṇaṃ| anuvādādhikaraṇaṃ na hauti āpattādhikaraṇaṃ, na hauti kic‍cādhikaraṇaṃ, na hauti vivādādhikaraṇaṃ; api ca, anuvādādhikaraṇapac‍cayā hauti āpattādhikaraṇaṃ, hauti kic‍cādhikaraṇaṃ, hauti vivādādhikaraṇaṃ| yathā kathaṃ viya? idha bhikkhū bhikkhuṃ anuvadanti sīlavipattiyā vā ācāravipattiyā vā diṭṭhivipattiyā vā ājīvavipattiyā vā| yau tattha anuvādau anuvadanā anul‍lapanā anubhaṇanā anusampavaṅkatā abbhussahanatā anubalappadānaṃ, idaṃ vuc‍cati anuvādādhikaraṇaṃ| anuvādādhikaraṇai saṅghau vivadati, vivādādhikaraṇaṃ| vivadamānau anuvadati, anuvādādhikaraṇaṃ| anuvadamānau āpattiṃ āpaj‍jati, āpattādhikaraṇaṃ| tāya āpattiyā saṅghau kammaṃ karauti, kic‍cādhikaraṇaṃ| aivaṃ anuvādādhikaraṇapac‍cayā hauti āpattādhikaraṇaṃ, hauti kic‍cādhikaraṇaṃ, hauti vivādādhikaraṇaṃ|

āpattādhikaraṇaṃ hauti kic‍cādhikaraṇaṃ, hauti vivādādhikaraṇaṃ, hauti anuvādādhikaraṇaṃ| āpattādhikaraṇaṃ na hauti kic‍cādhikaraṇaṃ, na hauti vivādādhikaraṇaṃ, na hauti anuvādādhikaraṇaṃ; api ca, āpattādhikaraṇapac‍cayā hauti kic‍cādhikaraṇaṃ, hauti vivādādhikaraṇaṃ, hauti anuvādādhikaraṇaṃ| yathā kathaṃ viya? cūḷava॰ 215; pari॰ 348 pañ‍capi āpattikkhandhā āpattādhikaraṇaṃ, sattapi āpattikkhandhā āpattādhikaraṇaṃ, idaṃ vuc‍cati āpattādhikaraṇaṃ| āpattādhikaraṇai saṅghau vivadati vivādādhikaraṇaṃ| vivadamānau anuvadati anuvādādhikaraṇaṃ| anuvadamānau āpattiṃ āpaj‍jati āpattādhikaraṇaṃ| tāya āpattiyā saṅghau kammaṃ karauti kic‍cādhikaraṇaṃ| aivaṃ āpattādhikaraṇapac‍cayā hauti kic‍cādhikaraṇaṃ, hauti vivādādhikaraṇaṃ, hauti anuvādādhikaraṇaṃ|

kic‍cādhikaraṇaṃ hauti vivādādhikaraṇaṃ, hauti anuvādādhikaraṇaṃ, hauti āpattādhikaraṇaṃ| kic‍cādhikaraṇaṃ na hauti vivādādhikaraṇaṃ, na hauti anuvādādhikaraṇaṃ, na hauti āpattādhikaraṇaṃ; api ca, kic‍cādhikaraṇapac‍cayā hauti vivādādhikaraṇaṃ, hauti anuvādādhikaraṇaṃ, hauti  āpattādhikaraṇaṃ| yathā kathaṃ viya? yā saṅghassa kic‍cayatā karaṇīyatā apalaukanakammaṃ ñattikammaṃ ñattidutiyakammaṃ ñatticatutthakammaṃ, idaṃ  vuc‍cati kic‍cādhikaraṇaṃ| kic‍cādhikaraṇai saṅghau vivadati vivādādhikaraṇaṃ| vivadamānau anuvadati anuvādādhikaraṇaṃ| anuvadamānau āpattiṃ āpaj‍jati āpattādhikaraṇaṃ| tāya āpattiyā saṅghau kammaṃ karauti kic‍cādhikaraṇaṃ| aivaṃ kic‍cādhikaraṇapac‍cayā hauti vivādādhikaraṇaṃ, hauti anuvādādhikaraṇaṃ, hauti āpattādhikaraṇaṃ|

6. pucchāvārau

349. yattha  sativinayau tattha sammukhāvinayau? yattha sammukhāvinayau  tattha sativinayau? yattha amūḷhavinayau tattha sammukhāvinayau? yattha sammukhāvinayau tattha amūḷhavinayau? yattha paṭiñ‍ñātakaraṇaṃ tattha sammukhāvinayau? yattha sammukhāvinayau tattha paṭiñ‍ñātakaraṇaṃ? yattha yaibhuyyasikā tattha sammukhāvinayau? yattha sammukhāvinayau tattha yaibhuyyasikā? yattha tassapāpiyasikā tattha sammukhāvinayau? yattha sammukhāvinayau tattha tassapāpiyasikā? yattha tiṇavatthārakau tattha sammukhāvinayau? yattha sammukhāvinayau tattha tiṇavatthārakau?

7. vissaj‍janāvārau

350. yasmiṃ samayai sammukhāvinayaina ca sativinayaina ca adhikaraṇaṃ vūpasammati – yattha sativinayau tattha sammukhāvinayau, yattha sammukhāvinayau tattha sativinayau, na tattha amūḷhavinayau, na tattha  paṭiñ‍ñātakaraṇaṃ, na tattha yaibhuyyasikā, na tattha tassapāpiyasikā, na tattha tiṇavatthārakau| yasmiṃ samayai sammukhāvinayaina ca amūḷhavinayaina ca…pai॰… sammukhāvinayaina ca paṭiñ‍ñātakaraṇaina ca…pai॰… sammukhāvinayaina ca yaibhuyyasikāya ca…pai॰… sammukhāvinayaina ca tassapāpiyasikāya ca…pai॰… sammukhāvinayaina ca tiṇavatthārakaina ca adhikaraṇaṃ vūpasammati – yattha tiṇavatthārakau tattha sammukhāvinayau, yattha sammukhāvinayau tattha tiṇavatthārakau, na tattha sativinayau, na tattha amūḷhavinayau, na tattha paṭiñ‍ñātakaraṇaṃ, na tattha yaibhuyyasikā, na tattha tassapāpiyasikā|

8. saṃsaṭṭhavārau

351. sammukhāvinayauti vā sativinayauti vā – imai dhammā saṃsaṭṭhā udāhu visaṃsaṭṭhā? labbhā ca panimaisaṃ dhammānaṃ vinibbhujitvā vinibbhujitvā nānākaraṇaṃ  pañ‍ñāpaituṃ? sammukhāvinayauti vā amūḷhavinayauti vā…pai॰… sammukhāvinayauti vā paṭiñ‍ñātakaraṇanti vā… sammukhāvinayauti vā yaibhuyyasikāti vā… sammukhāvinayauti vā tassapāpiyasikāti vā… sammukhāvinayauti vā tiṇavatthārakauti – imai dhammā saṃsaṭṭhā udāhu visaṃsaṭṭhā? labbhā ca panimaisaṃ dhammānaṃ vinibbhujitvā vinibbhujitvā nānākaraṇaṃ pañ‍ñāpaituṃ?

sammukhāvinayauti vā sativinayauti vā – imai dhammā saṃsaṭṭhā, nau visaṃsaṭṭhā; na ca labbhā imaisaṃ dhammānaṃ vinibbhujitvā vinibbhujitvā nānākaraṇaṃ pañ‍ñāpaituṃ| sammukhāvinayauti vā amūḷhavinayauti  vā…pai॰… sammukhāvinayauti vā paṭiñ‍ñātakaraṇanti vā… sammukhāvinayauti vā yaibhuyyasikāti vā… sammukhāvinayauti vā tassapāpiyasikāti vā… sammukhāvinayauti vā tiṇavatthārakauti vā – imai dhammā saṃsaṭṭhā, nau visaṃsaṭṭhā; na ca labbhā imaisaṃ dhammānaṃ vinibbhujitvā vinibbhujitvā nānākaraṇaṃ pañ‍ñāpaituṃ|

9. sattasamathanidānaṃ

352. sammukhāvinayau kiṃnidānau, kiṃsamudayau, kiṃjātikau, kiṃpabhavau, kiṃsambhārau kiṃsamuṭṭhānau? sativinayau kiṃnidānau, kiṃsamudayau, kiṃjātikau, kiṃpabhavau, kiṃsambhārau, kiṃsamuṭṭhānau? amūḷhavinayau kiṃnidānau, kiṃsamudayau, kiṃjātikau, kiṃpabhavau, kiṃsambhārau, kiṃsamuṭṭhānau? paṭiñ‍ñātakaraṇaṃ kiṃnidānaṃ, kiṃsamudayaṃ, kiṃjātikaṃ, kiṃpabhavaṃ, kiṃsambhāraṃ, kiṃsamuṭṭhānaṃ? yaibhuyyasikā kiṃnidānā, kiṃsamudayā, kiṃjātikā, kiṃpabhavā, kiṃsambhārā, kiṃsamuṭṭhānā? tassapāpiyasikā kiṃnidānā, kiṃsamudayā, kiṃjātikā, kiṃpabhavā, kiṃsambhārā, kiṃsamuṭṭhānā tiṇavatthārakau kiṃnidānau, kiṃsamudayau, kiṃjātikau, kiṃpabhavau, kiṃsambhārau, kiṃsamuṭṭhānau?

sammukhāvinayau nidānanidānau, nidānasamudayau, nidānajātikau, nidānapabhavau, nidānasambhārau, nidānasamuṭṭhānau| sativinayau…pai॰… amūḷhavinayau…pai॰… paṭiñ‍ñātakaraṇaṃ nidānanidānaṃ, nidānasamudayaṃ, nidānajātikaṃ, nidānapabhavaṃ, nidānasambhāraṃ, nidānasamuṭṭhānaṃ| yaibhuyyasikā…pai॰… tassapāpiyasikā nidānanidānā, nidānasamudayā, nidānajātikā, nidānapabhavā, nidānasambhārā, nidānasamuṭṭhānā| tiṇavatthārakau nidānanidānau, nidānasamudayau, nidānajātikau, nidānapabhavau, nidānasambhārau, nidānasamuṭṭhānau|

sammukhāvinayau  kiṃnidānau, kiṃsamudayau, kiṃjātikau, kiṃpabhavau, kiṃsambhārau, kiṃsamuṭṭhānau? sativinayau…pai॰… amūḷhavinayau…pai॰… paṭiñ‍ñātakaraṇaṃ…pai॰… yaibhuyyasikā…pai॰… tassapāpiyasikā…pai॰… tiṇavatthārakau kiṃnidānau, kiṃsamudayau, kiṃjātikau, kiṃpabhavau, kiṃsambhārau, kiṃsamuṭṭhānau?

sammukhāvinayau haitunidānau, haitusamudayau, haitujātikau, haitupabhavau, haitusambhārau, haitusamuṭṭhānau| sativinayau…pai॰… amūḷhavinayau…pai॰… paṭiñ‍ñātakaraṇaṃ haitunidānaṃ, haitusamudayaṃ, haitujātikaṃ, haitupabhavaṃ, haitusambhāraṃ, haitusamuṭṭhānaṃ| yaibhuyyasikā…pai॰… tassapāpiyasikā haitunidānā, haitusamudayā, haitujātikā, haitupabhavā, haitusambhārā, haitusamuṭṭhānā| tiṇavatthārakau haitunidānau, haitusamudayau, haitujātikau, haitupabhavau, haitusambhārau, haitusamuṭṭhānau|

sammukhāvinayau kiṃnidānau, kiṃsamudayau, kiṃjātikau, kiṃpabhavau, kiṃsambhārau, kiṃsamuṭṭhānau? sativinayau…pai॰… amūḷhavinayau…pai॰… paṭiñ‍ñātakaraṇaṃ…pai॰… yaibhuyyasikā…pai॰… tassapāpiyasikā…pai॰… tiṇavatthārakau kiṃnidānau, kiṃsamudayau, kiṃjātikau, kiṃpabhavau, kiṃsambhārau, kiṃsamuṭṭhānau? sammukhāvinayau pac‍cayanidānau, pac‍cayasamudayau, pac‍cayajātikau, pac‍cayapabhavau, pac‍cayasambhārau, pac‍cayasamuṭṭhānau| sativinayau…pai॰… amūḷhavinayau…pai॰… paṭiñ‍ñātakaraṇaṃ pac‍cayanidānaṃ, pac‍cayasamudayaṃ, pac‍cayajātikaṃ, pac‍cayapabhavaṃ, pac‍cayasambhāraṃ, pac‍cayasamuṭṭhānaṃ| yaibhuyyasikā…pai॰… tassapāpiyasikā pac‍cayanidānā, pac‍cayasamudayā, pac‍cayajātikā, pac‍cayapabhavā, pac‍cayasambhārā, pac‍cayasamuṭṭhānā| tiṇavatthārakau pac‍cayanidānau, pac‍cayasamudayau, pac‍cayajātikau, pac‍cayapabhavau, pac‍cayasambhārau, pac‍cayasamuṭṭhānau|

353. sattan‍naṃ samathānaṃ kati mūlāni, kati samuṭṭhānā? sattan‍naṃ samathānaṃ chabbīsa mūlāni, chattiṃsa samuṭṭhānā| sattan‍naṃ samathānaṃ katamāni chabbī  mūlāni? sammukhāvinayassa cattāri mūlāni| saṅghasammukhatā, dhammasammukhatā, vinayasammukhatā, puggalasammukhatā; sativinayassa cattāri mūlāni; amūḷhavinayassa cattāri mūlāni; paṭiñ‍ñātakaraṇassa dvai mūlāni – yau ca daisaiti yassa ca daisaiti; yaibhuyyasikāya cattāri mūlāni; tassapāpiyasikāya cattāri  mūlāni; tiṇavatthārakassa cattāri mūlāni – saṅghasammukhatā, dhammasammukhatā, vinayasammukhatā, puggalasammukhatā – sattan‍naṃ samathānaṃ imāni chabbīsa mūlāni|

sattan‍naṃ  samathānaṃ katamai chattiṃsa samuṭṭhānā? sativinayassa kammassa kiriyā, karaṇaṃ, upagamanaṃ, ajjhupagamanaṃ, adhivāsanā, appaṭik‍kausanā| amūḷhavinayassa kammassa…pai॰… paṭiñ‍ñātakaraṇassa kammassa… yaibhuyyasikāya kammassa… tassapāpiyasikāya kammassa… tiṇavatthārakassa kammassa kiriyā, karaṇaṃ, upagamanaṃ, ajjhupagamanaṃ, adhivāsanā, appaṭik‍kausanā – sattan‍naṃ samathānaṃ imai chattiṃsa samuṭṭhānā|

10. sattasamathanānatthādi

354. sammukhāvinayauti vā sativinayauti vā – imai dhammā nānatthā nānābyañ‍janā udāhu aikatthā byañ‍janamaiva nānaṃ? sammukhāvinayauti vā amūḷhavinayauti vā…pai॰… sammukhāvinayauti vā paṭiñ‍ñātakaraṇanti vā… sammukhāvinayauti vā yaibhuyyasikāti vā… sammukhāvinayauti vā tassapāpiyasikāti vā… sammukhāvinayauti vā tiṇavatthārakauti vā – imai dhammā nānatthā nānābyañ‍janā udāhu aikatthā byañ‍janamaiva nānaṃ? sammukhāvinayauti vā sativinayauti vā – imai dhammā nānatthā caiva nānābyañ‍janā ca| sammukhāvinayauti vā amūḷhavinayauti vā…pai॰… sammukhāvinayauti vā paṭiñ‍ñātakaraṇanti vā… sammukhāvinayauti vā yaibhuyyasikāti vā… sammukhāvinayauti vā tassapāpiyasikāti vā… sammukhāvinayauti vā  tiṇavatthārakauti vā – imai dhammā nānatthā caiva nānā byañ‍janā ca|

355. cūḷava॰ 224 vivādau  vivādādhikaraṇaṃ, vivādau nau adhikaraṇaṃ, adhikaraṇaṃ nau vivādau, adhikaraṇañ‍caiva vivādau ca? siyā vivādau vivādādhikaraṇaṃ, siyā vivādau nau adhikaraṇaṃ, siyā adhikaraṇaṃ nau vivādau, siyā adhikaraṇañ‍caiva vivādau ca|

tattha  katamau vivādau vivādādhikaraṇaṃ? idha bhikkhū vivadanti dhammauti vā adhammauti vā…pai॰… duṭṭhul‍lā āpattīti vā aduṭṭhul‍lā āpattīti vā| yaṃ tattha bhaṇḍanaṃ, kalahau, viggahau, vivādau, nānāvādau, añ‍ñathāvādau, vipac‍catāya vauhārau, maidhakaṃ – ayaṃ vivādau vivādādhikaraṇaṃ|

tattha katamau vivādau nau adhikaraṇaṃ? mātāpi puttaina vivadati, puttaupi mātarā vivadati, pitāpi puttaina vivadati, puttaupi pitarā vivadati, bhātāpi bhātarā vivadati, bhātāpi  bhaginiyā vivadati, bhaginīpi bhātarā vivadati, sahāyaupi sahāyaina vivadati – ayaṃ vivādau nau adhikaraṇaṃ|

tattha katamaṃ adhikaraṇaṃ nau vivādau? anuvādādhikaraṇaṃ, āpattādhikaraṇaṃ, kic‍cādhikaraṇaṃ – idaṃ adhikaraṇaṃ nau vivādau|

tattha katamaṃ adhikaraṇañ‍caiva vivādau ca? vivādādhikaraṇaṃ adhikaraṇañ‍caiva vivādau ca|

356. cūḷava॰ 224 ādayau anuvādau anuvādādhikaraṇaṃ, anuvādau nau adhikaraṇaṃ, adhikaraṇaṃ nau anuvādau, adhikaraṇañ‍caiva anuvādau ca? siyā anuvādau  anuvādādhikaraṇaṃ, siyā anuvādau nau adhikaraṇaṃ, siyā adhikaraṇaṃ nau anuvādau, siyā adhikaraṇañ‍caiva anuvādau ca|

tattha katamau anuvādau anuvādādhikaraṇaṃ? idha bhikkhū bhikkhuṃ anuvadanti sīlavipattiyā vā ācāravipattiyā vā diṭṭhivipattiyā vā ājīvavipattiyā vā| yau tattha anuvādau, anuvadanā anul‍lapanā anubhaṇanā anusampavaṅkatā abbhussahanatā anubalappadānaṃ – ayaṃ anuvādau anuvādādhikaraṇaṃ|

tattha katamau anuvādau nau adhikaraṇaṃ? mātāpi puttaṃ anuvadati, puttaupi mātaraṃ anuvadati, pitāpi puttaṃ anuvadati, puttaupi pitaraṃ anuvadati, bhātāpi bhātaraṃ anuvadati, bhātāpi bhaginiṃ anuvadati, bhaginīpi bhātaraṃ anuvadati, sahāyaupi sahāyaṃ anuvadati – ayaṃ anuvādau nau adhikaraṇaṃ|

tattha katamaṃ adhikaraṇaṃ nau anuvādau? āpattādhikaraṇaṃ kic‍cādhikaraṇaṃ vivādādhikaraṇaṃ – idaṃ adhikaraṇaṃ nau anuvādau|

tattha  katamaṃ adhikaraṇañ‍caiva anuvādau ca? anuvādādhikaraṇaṃ adhikaraṇañ‍caiva anuvādau ca|

357. āpatti  āpattādhikaraṇaṃ, āpatti nau adhikaraṇaṃ, adhikaraṇaṃ nau āpatti, adhikaraṇañ‍caiva āpatti ca? siyā āpatti āpattādhikaraṇaṃ, siyā āpatti nau adhikaraṇaṃ, siyā adhikaraṇaṃ nau āpatti, siyā adhikaraṇañ‍caiva āpatti ca|

tattha katamā āpatti āpattādhikaraṇaṃ? pañ‍capi āpattikkhandhā āpattādhikaraṇaṃ| sattapi āpattikkhandhā āpattādhikaraṇaṃ| ayaṃ āpatti āpattādhikaraṇaṃ|

tattha katamā āpatti nau adhikaraṇaṃ? sautāpatti samāpatti – ayaṃ āpatti nau adhikaraṇaṃ|

tattha katamaṃ adhikaraṇaṃ nau āpatti? kic‍cādhikaraṇaṃ vivādādhikaraṇaṃ anuvādādhikaraṇaṃ – idaṃ adhikaraṇaṃ nau āpatti|

tattha katamaṃ adhikaraṇañ‍caiva āpatti ca? āpattādhikaraṇaṃ adhikaraṇañ‍caiva āpatti ca|

358. cūḷava॰ 223 kic‍caṃ kic‍cādhikaraṇaṃ, kic‍caṃ nau adhikaraṇaṃ, adhikaraṇaṃ nau kic‍caṃ, adhikaraṇañ‍caiva kic‍cañ‍ca? siyā kic‍caṃ kic‍cādhikaraṇaṃ, siyā kic‍caṃ nau adhikaraṇaṃ, siyā adhikaraṇaṃ nau kic‍caṃ, siyā adhikaraṇañ‍caiva kic‍cañ‍ca|

tattha katamaṃ kic‍caṃ kic‍cādhikaraṇaṃ? yā saṅghassa kic‍cayatā karaṇīyatā apalaukanakammaṃ ñattikammaṃ ñattidutiyakammaṃ ñatticatutthakammaṃ – idaṃ kic‍caṃ kic‍cādhikaraṇaṃ|

tattha katamaṃ kic‍caṃ nau adhikaraṇaṃ? ācariyakic‍caṃ upajjhāyakic‍caṃ upajjhāyakic‍caṃ sakic‍caṃ (ka॰) samānupajjhāyakic‍caṃ samānācariyakic‍caṃ – idaṃ kic‍caṃ nau adhikaraṇaṃ|

tattha katamaṃ adhikaraṇaṃ nau kic‍caṃ? vivādādhikaraṇaṃ anuvādādhikaraṇaṃ āpattādhikaraṇaṃ – idaṃ adhikaraṇaṃ nau kic‍caṃ|

tattha  katamaṃ adhikaraṇañ‍caiva kic‍cañ‍ca? kic‍cādhikaraṇaṃ adhikaraṇañ‍caiva kic‍caṃ cāti|

adhikaraṇabhaidau niṭṭhitau|


tassuddānaṃ –

adhikaraṇaṃ  uk‍kauṭā, ākārā puggalaina ca|

nidānahaitupac‍cayā, mūlaṃ samuṭṭhānaina ca||

āpatti hauti yattha ca, saṃsaṭṭhā nidānaina ca saṃsaṭṭhā nidānapabhavā (sī॰)|

haitupac‍cayamūlāni, samuṭṭhānaina byañ‍janā|

vivādau adhikaraṇanti, bhaidādhikaraṇai idanti||


aparagāthāsaṅgaṇikaṃ

1. caudanādipucchāvissaj‍janā

359.

 caudanā  kimatthāya, sāraṇā kissa kāraṇā|

saṅghau kimatthāya, matikammaṃ pana kissa kāraṇā||

caudanā sāraṇatthāya, niggahatthāya sāraṇā|

saṅghau pariggahatthāya, matikammaṃ pana pāṭiyaik‍kaṃ||

mā khau turitau abhaṇi, mā khau caṇḍikatau bhaṇi|

mā khau paṭighaṃ janayi, sacai anuvij‍jakau tuvaṃ||

mā khau sahasā abhaṇi, kathaṃ viggāhikaṃ anatthasaṃhitaṃ|

suttai vinayai anulaumai, pañ‍ñattai anulaumikai||

anuyaugavattaṃ nisāmaya, kusalaina buddhimatā kataṃ|

suvuttaṃ sikkhāpadānulaumikaṃ, gatiṃ na nāsaintau samparāyikaṃ|

hitaisī anuyuñ‍jassu, kālainatthūpasaṃhitaṃ||

cuditassa ca caudakassa ca|

sahasā vauhāraṃ mā padhāraisi|

caudakau āha āpan‍nauti|

cuditakau āha anāpan‍nauti||

ubhau  anukkhipantau, paṭiñ‍ñānusandhitaina  kārayai|

paṭiñ‍ñā laj‍jīsu katā, alaj‍jīsu aivaṃ na vij‍jati|

bahumpi alaj‍jī bhāsaiyya, vattānusandhitaina vuttānusandhitaina (sī॰ syā॰ ka॰) kārayai||

alaj‍jī kīdisau hauti, paṭiñ‍ñā yassa na rūhati|

aitañ‍ca aivañ‍ca (ka॰) tāhaṃ pucchāmi, kīdisau vuc‍cati alaj‍jī puggalau||

sañ‍cic‍ca āpattiṃ āpaj‍jati, āpattiṃ parigūhati|

agatigamanañ‍ca gacchati, aidisau vuc‍cati alaj‍jīpuggalau||

sac‍caṃ ahampi jānāmi, aidisau vuc‍cati alaj‍jīpuggalau|

añ‍ñañ‍ca tāhaṃ pucchāmi, kīdisau vuc‍cati laj‍jīpuggalau||

sañ‍cic‍ca āpattiṃ nāpaj‍jati, āpattiṃ na parigūhati|

agatigamanaṃ  na gacchati, aidisau vuc‍cati laj‍jīpuggalau||

sac‍caṃ  ahampi jānāmi, aidisau vuc‍cati laj‍jīpuggalau|

añ‍ñañ‍ca tāhaṃ pucchāmi, kīdisau vuc‍cati adhammacaudakau||

akālai  caudaiti abhūtaina|

pharusaina anatthasaṃhitaina|

dausantarau caudaiti nau maittācittau|

aidisau vuc‍cati adhammacaudakau||

sac‍caṃ ahampi jānāmi, aidisau vuc‍cati adhammacaudakau|

añ‍ñañ‍ca tāhaṃ pucchāmi, kīdisau vuc‍cati dhammacaudakau||

kālaina caudaiti bhūtaina, saṇhaina atthasaṃhitaina|

maittācittau caudaiti nau dausantarau|

aidisau vuc‍cati dhammacaudakau||

sac‍caṃ  ahampi jānāmi, aidisau vuc‍cati dhammacaudakau|

añ‍ñañ‍ca tāhaṃ pucchāmi, kīdisau vuc‍cati bālacaudakau||

pubbāparaṃ na jānāti, pubbāparassa akauvidau|

anusandhivacanapathaṃ na jānāti|

anusandhivacanapathassa akauvidau|

aidisau vuc‍cati bālacaudakau||

sac‍caṃ ahampi jānāmi, aidisau vuc‍cati bālacaudakau|

añ‍ñañ‍ca  tāhaṃ pucchāmi, kīdisau vuc‍cati paṇḍitacaudakau||

pubbāparampi jānāti, pubbāparassa kauvidau|

anusandhivacanapathaṃ jānāti, anusandhivacanapathassa kauvidau|

aidisau vuc‍cati paṇḍitacaudakau||

sac‍caṃ ahampi jānāmi, aidisau vuc‍cati paṇḍitacaudakau|

añ‍ñañ‍ca tāhaṃ pucchāmi, caudanā kinti vuc‍cati||

sīlavipattiyā caudaiti, athau ācāradiṭṭhiyā|

ājīvainapi caudaiti, caudanā taina vuc‍catīti||

aparaṃ gāthāsaṅgaṇikaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|



caudanākaṇḍaṃ

1. anuvij‍jakaanuyaugau

360. anuvij‍jakai  caudakau pucchitabbau – ‘‘yaṃ khau tvaṃ, āvusau, imaṃ bhikkhuṃ caudaisi, kimhi naṃ caudaisi, sīlavipattiyā vā caudaisi, ācāravipattiyā vā caudaisi, diṭṭhivipattiyā vā caudaisī’’ti? sau cai aivaṃ vadaiyya – ‘‘sīlavipattiyā vā caudaimi, ācāravipattiyā vā caudaimi, diṭṭhivipattiyā vā caudaimī’’ti, sau aivamassa vacanīyau – ‘‘jānāsi panāyasmā sīlavipattiṃ, jānāsi ācāravipattiṃ, jānāsi diṭṭhivipatti’’nti? sau cai aivaṃ vadaiyya – ‘‘jānāmi khau ahaṃ, āvusau, sīlavipattiṃ, jānāmi ācāravipattiṃ, jānāmi diṭṭhivipatti’’nti, sau aivamassa vacanīyau – ‘‘katamā panāvusau, sīlavipatti? katamā ācāravipatti? katamā diṭṭhivipattī’’ti? sau cai aivaṃ vadaiyya – ‘‘cattāri ca pārājikāni, tairasa ca saṅghādisaisā – ayaṃ sīlavipatti| thul‍lac‍cayaṃ, pācittiyaṃ, pāṭidaisanīyaṃ, duk‍kaṭaṃ, dubbhāsitaṃ, ayaṃ ācāravipatti| micchādiṭṭhi, antaggāhikā diṭṭhi – ayaṃ diṭṭhivipattī’’ti, sau aivamassa vacanīyau – ‘‘yaṃ khau tvaṃ, āvusau, imaṃ bhikkhuṃ caudaisi, diṭṭhaina vā caudaisi sutaina vā caudaisi parisaṅkāya vā caudaisī’’ti? sau cai aivaṃ vadaiyya – ‘‘diṭṭhaina vā caudaimi sutaina vā caudaimi parisaṅkāya vā caudaimī’’ti, sau aivamassa vacanīyau – ‘‘yaṃ khau tvaṃ, āvusau, imaṃ bhikkhuṃ diṭṭhaina caudaisi, kiṃ tai diṭṭhaṃ kinti tai diṭṭhaṃ, kadā  tai diṭṭhaṃ, kattha tai diṭṭhaṃ pārājikaṃ ajjhāpaj‍jantau diṭṭhau, saṅghādisaisaṃ ajjhāpaj‍jantau diṭṭhau, thul‍lac‍cayaṃ… pācittiyaṃ… pāṭidaisanīyaṃ… duk‍kaṭaṃ… dubbhāsitaṃ ajjhāpaj‍jantau diṭṭhau, kattha ca tvaṃ ahausi, kattha cāyaṃ bhikkhu ahausi, kiñ‍ca tvaṃ karausi, kiṃ cāyaṃ bhikkhu karautī’’ti? sau cai aivaṃ vadaiyya – ‘‘na khau ahaṃ, āvusau, imaṃ bhikkhuṃ diṭṭhaina caudaimi, api ca sutaina caudaimī’’ti, sau aivamassa vacanīyau – ‘‘yaṃ khau tvaṃ, āvusau, imaṃ bhikkhuṃ sutaina caudaisi, kiṃ tai sutaṃ, kinti tai sutaṃ, kadā tai sutaṃ, kattha tai sutaṃ, pārājikaṃ ajjhāpan‍nauti sutaṃ, saṅghādisaisaṃ… thul‍lac‍cayaṃ… pācittiyaṃ… pāṭidaisanīyaṃ… duk‍kaṭaṃ… dubbhāsitaṃ ajjhāpan‍nauti sutaṃ, bhikkhussa sutaṃ, bhikkhuniyā sutaṃ, sikkhamānāya sutaṃ, sāmaṇairassa sutaṃ, sāmaṇairiyā sutaṃ, upāsakassa sutaṃ, upāsikāya sutaṃ, rājūnaṃ sutaṃ, rājamahāmattānaṃ sutaṃ, titthiyānaṃ sutaṃ, titthiyasāvakānaṃ suta’’nti? sau cai aivaṃ vadaiyya – ‘‘na khau ahaṃ, āvusau, imaṃ bhikkhuṃ sutaina caudaimi, api ca parisaṅkāya caudaimī’’ti, sau aivamassa vacanīyau – ‘‘yaṃ khau tvaṃ, āvusau, imaṃ bhikkhuṃ parisaṅkāya caudaisi, kiṃ parisaṅkasi, kinti parisaṅkasi, kadā parisaṅkasi, kattha parisaṅkasi, pārājikaṃ ajjhāpan‍nauti  parisaṅkasi, saṅghādisaisaṃ ajjhāpan‍nauti parisaṅkasi, thul‍lac‍cayaṃ… pācittiyaṃ… pāṭidaisanīyaṃ… duk‍kaṭaṃ… dubbhāsitaṃ ajjhāpan‍nauti parisaṅkasi, bhikkhussa sutvā parisaṅkasi, bhikkhuniyā sutvā parisaṅkasi, sikkhamānāya sutvā parisaṅkasi, sāmaṇairassa sutvā parisaṅkasi, sāmaṇairiyā sutvā parisaṅkasi, upāsakassa sutvā parisaṅkasi, upāsikāya sutvā parisaṅkasi, rājūnaṃ sutvā parisaṅkasi, rājamahāmattānaṃ sutvā parisaṅkasi, titthiyānaṃ sutvā parisaṅkasi, titthiyasāvakānaṃ sutvā parisaṅkasī’’ti?

361.

 diṭṭhaṃ diṭṭhaina samaiti diṭṭhaina saṃsandatai diṭṭhaṃ|

diṭṭhaṃ paṭic‍ca na upaiti asuddhaparisaṅkitau|

sau puggalau paṭiñ‍ñāya kātabbau tainupausathau||

sutaṃ sutaina samaiti sutaina saṃsandatai sutaṃ|

sutaṃ paṭic‍ca na upaiti asuddhaparisaṅkitau|

sau puggalau paṭiñ‍ñāya kātabbau tainupausathau||

mutaṃ mutaina samaiti mutaina saṃsandatai mutaṃ|

mutaṃ paṭic‍ca na upaiti asuddhaparisaṅkitau|

sau puggalau paṭiñ‍ñāya kātabbau tainupausathau||

362. caudanāya kau ādi, kiṃ majjhai, kiṃ pariyausānaṃ? caudanāya aukāsakammaṃ ādi, kiriyā majjhai, samathau pariyausānaṃ| caudanāya  kati mūlāni, kati vatthūni, kati bhūmiyau, katihākāraihi caudaiti? caudanāya dvai mūlāni, tīṇi vatthūni, pañ‍ca bhūmiyau, dvīhākāraihi caudaiti| caudanāya katamāni dvai mūlāni? samūlikā vā amūlikā vā – caudanāya  imāni dvai mūlāni| caudanāya katamāni tīṇi vatthūni? diṭṭhaina  sutaina parisaṅkāya – caudanāya imāni tīṇi vatthūni| caudanā  katamā pañ‍ca bhūmiyau? kālaina vakkhāmi nau akālaina, bhūtaina vakkhāmi nau abhūtaina, saṇhaina vakkhāmi nau pharusaina, atthasaṃhitaina vakkhāmi nau anatthasaṃhitaina, maittācittau vakkhāmi nau dausantarauti – caudanāya imā pañ‍ca bhūmiyau|

katamaihi dvīhākāraihi caudaiti? kāyaina vā caudaiti vācāya vā caudaiti – imaihi dvīhākāraihi caudaiti|

2. caudakādipaṭipatti

363. caudakaina kathaṃ paṭipaj‍jitabbaṃ? cuditakaina kathaṃ paṭipaj‍jitabbaṃ? saṅghaina kathaṃ paṭipaj‍jitabbaṃ? anuvij‍jakaina kathaṃ paṭipaj‍jitabbaṃ? caudakaina kathaṃ paṭipaj‍jitabbanti? caudakaina pañ‍casu dhammaisu patiṭṭhāya parau caudaitabbau| kālaina vakkhāmi nau akālaina, bhūtaina vakkhāmi nau abhūtaina, saṇhaina vakkhāmi nau pharusaina, atthasaṃhitaina vakkhāmi nau anatthasaṃhitaina, maittācittau vakkhāmi nau dausantarauti – caudakaina aivaṃ paṭipaj‍jitabbaṃ| cuditakaina kathaṃ paṭipaj‍jitabbanti? cuditakaina dvīsu dhammaisu paṭipaj‍jitabbaṃ| sac‍cai ca akuppai ca – cuditakaina aivaṃ paṭipaj‍jitabbaṃ| saṅghaina kathaṃ paṭipaj‍jitabbanti? saṅghaina  autiṇṇānautiṇṇaṃ jānitabbaṃ| saṅghaina aivaṃ paṭipaj‍jitabbaṃ| anuvij‍jakaina kathaṃ paṭipaj‍jitabbanti? anuvij‍jakaina yaina dhammaina yaina vinayaina yaina satthusāsanaina taṃ adhikaraṇaṃ vūpasammati tathā taṃ adhikaraṇaṃ vūpasamaitabbaṃ| anuvij‍jakaina aivaṃ paṭipaj‍jitabbaṃ|

364.

 upausathau kimatthāya, pavāraṇā kissa kāraṇā|

parivāsau kimatthāya, mūlāyapaṭikassanā kissa kāraṇā|

mānattaṃ kimatthāya, abbhānaṃ kissa kāraṇā||

upausathau sāmaggatthāya, visuddhatthāya pavāraṇā|

parivāsau mānattatthāya, mūlāyapaṭikassanā niggahatthāya|

mānattaṃ abbhānatthāya, visuddhatthāya abbhānaṃ||

chandā  dausā bhayā mauhā, thairai ca paribhāsati|

kāyassa bhaidā duppañ‍ñau, khatau upahatindriyau|

nirayaṃ gacchati dummaidhau, na ca sikkhāya gāravau||

na ca āmisaṃ nissāya|

na ca nissāya puggalaṃ|

ubhau aitai vivaj‍jaitvā|

yathādhammau tathā karai||

3. caudakassaattajhāpanaṃ

kaudhanau upanāhī ca|

caṇḍau ca paribhāsakau|

anāpattiyā āpattīti raupaiti|

tādisau caudakau jhāpaiti attānaṃ||

upakaṇṇakaṃ jappati jimhaṃ paikkhati|

vītiharati  kummaggaṃ paṭisaivati|

anāpattiyā  āpattīti raupaiti|

tādisau caudakau jhāpaiti attānaṃ||

akālaina caudaiti abhūtaina|

pharusaina anatthasaṃhitaina|

dausantarau caudaiti nau maittācittau|

anāpattiyā āpattīti raupaiti|

tādisau caudakau jhāpaiti attānaṃ||

dhammādhammaṃ na jānāti|

dhammādhammassa akauvidau|

anāpattiyā  āpattīti raupaiti|

tādisau caudakau jhāpaiti attānaṃ||

vinayāvinayaṃ na jānāti|

vinayāvinayassa akauvidau|

anāpattiyā āpattīti raupaiti|

tādisau caudakau jhāpaiti attānaṃ||

bhāsitābhāsitaṃ  na jānāti|

bhāsitābhāsitassa akauvidau|

anāpattiyā āpattīti raupaiti|

tādisau caudakau jhāpaiti attānaṃ||

āciṇṇānāciṇṇaṃ  na jānāti|

āciṇṇānāciṇṇassa akauvidau|

anāpattiyā āpattīti raupaiti|

tādisau caudakau jhāpaiti attānaṃ||

pañ‍ñattāpañ‍ñattaṃ na jānāti|

pañ‍ñattāpañ‍ñattassa akauvidau|

anāpattiyā āpattīti raupaiti|

tādisau caudakau jhāpaiti attānaṃ||

āpattānāpattiṃ na jānāti|

āpattānāpattiyā akauvidau|

anāpattiyā āpattīti raupaiti|

tādisau caudakau jhāpaiti attānaṃ||

lahukagarukaṃ na jānāti|

lahukagarukassa akauvidau|

anāpattiyā  āpattīti raupaiti|

tādisau caudakau jhāpaiti attānaṃ||

sāvasaisānavasaisaṃ na jānāti|

sāvasaisānavasaisassa akauvidau|

anāpattiyā āpattīti raupaiti|

tādisau caudakau jhāpaiti attānaṃ||

duṭṭhul‍lāduṭṭhul‍laṃ  na jānāti|

duṭṭhul‍lāduṭṭhul‍lassa akauvidau|

anāpattiyā āpattīti raupaiti|

tādisau caudakau jhāpaiti attānaṃ||

pubbāparaṃ  na jānāti|

pubbāparassa akauvidau|

anāpattiyā āpattīti raupaiti|

tādisau caudakau jhāpaiti attānaṃ||

anusandhivacanapathaṃ na jānāti|

anusandhivacanapathassa akauvidau|

anāpattiyā āpattīti raupaiti|

tādisau caudakau jhāpaiti attānanti||

caudanākaṇḍaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|


tassuddānaṃ –

caudanā anuvij‍jā ca, ādi mūlainupausathau|

gati caudanakaṇḍamhi, sāsanaṃ patiṭṭhāpayanti||


cūḷasaṅgāmau

1. anuvij‍jakassapaṭipatti

365. pari॰ 421 saṅgāmāvacarai  bhikkhunā saṅghaṃ upasaṅkamantaina nīcacittaina saṅghau upasaṅkamitabbau rajauharaṇasamaina cittaina; āsanakusalaina bhavitabbaṃ nisaj‍jakusalaina; thairai bhikkhū anupakhaj‍jantaina, navai bhikkhū āsanaina appaṭibāhantaina, yathāpatirūpai āsanai nisīditabbaṃ; anānākathikaina bhavitabbaṃ atiracchānakathikaina; sāmaṃ vā dhammau bhāsitabbau parau vā ajjhaisitabbau ariyau vā tuṇhībhāvau nātimañ‍ñitabbau|

saṅghaina anumataina puggalaina anuvij‍jakaina anuvij‍jitukāmaina na upajjhāyau pucchitabbau, na ācariyau pucchitabbau, na saddhivihārikau pucchitabbau, na antaivāsikau pucchitabbau, na samānupajjhāyakau pucchitabbau, na samānācariyakau pucchitabbau, na jāti pucchitabbā, na nāmaṃ pucchitabbaṃ, na gauttaṃ pucchitabbaṃ, na āgamau pucchitabbau, na kulapadaisau pucchitabbau, na jātibhūmi pucchitabbā| taṃ kiṃ kāraṇā? atrassa paimaṃ vā dausau vā| paimai vā sati dausai vā, chandāpi gacchaiyya dausāpi gacchaiyya mauhāpi gacchaiyya bhayāpi gacchaiyya|

saṅghaina anumataina puggalaina anuvij‍jakaina anuvij‍jitukāmaina saṅghagarukaina bhavitabbaṃ nau puggalagarukaina, saddhammagarukaina bhavitabbaṃ nau āmisagarukaina, atthavasikaina bhavitabbaṃ nau parisakappikaina, kālaina anuvij‍jitabbaṃ nau akālaina, bhūtaina anuvij‍jitabbaṃ  nau abhūtaina, saṇhaina anuvij‍jitabbaṃ nau pharusaina, atthasaṃhitaina anuvij‍jitabbaṃ nau anatthasaṃhitaina, maittācittaina anuvij‍jitabbaṃ nau dausantaraina, na upakaṇṇakajappinā bhavitabbaṃ, na  jimhaṃ paikkhitabbaṃ, na akkhi nikhaṇitabbaṃ, na bhamukaṃ ukkhipitabbaṃ, na sīsaṃ ukkhipitabbaṃ, na hatthavikārau kātabbau, na hatthamuddā dassaitabbā|

āsanakusalaina bhavitabbaṃ nisaj‍jakusalaina, yugamattaṃ paikkhantaina atthaṃ anuvidhiyantaina sakai āsanai nisīditabbaṃ, na ca āsanā vuṭṭhātabbaṃ, na vītihātabbaṃ, na kummaggau saivitabbau, na bāhāvikkhaipakaṃ na vācā vikkhaipakaṃ (syā॰) bhaṇitabbaṃ, aturitaina bhavitabbaṃ asāhasikaina, acaṇḍikataina bhavitabbaṃ vacanakkhamai, maittācittaina bhavitabbaṃ hitānukampinā, kāruṇikaina bhavitabbaṃ hitaparisak‍kinā, asamphappalāpinā bhavitabbaṃ pariyantabhāṇinā, avairavasikaina bhavitabbaṃ anasuruttaina, attā pariggahaitabbau, parau pariggahaitabbau, caudakau pariggahaitabbau, cuditakau pariggahaitabbau, adhammacaudakau pariggahaitabbau, adhammacuditakau pariggahaitabbau, dhammacaudakau pariggahaitabbau, dhammacuditakau pariggahaitabbau, vuttaṃ ahāpaintaina avuttaṃ apakāsaintaina autiṇṇāni padabyañ‍janāni sādhukaṃ pariggahaitvā parau paṭipucchitvā yathā paṭiñ‍ñāya kāraitabbau, mandau hāsaitabbau vaipau pahāsaitabbau (syā॰), bhīrū  assāsaitabbau, caṇḍau nisaidhaitabbau, asuci vibhāvaitabbau, ujumaddavaina na chandāgatiṃ gantabbaṃ, na dausāgatiṃ gantabbaṃ, na mauhāgatiṃ gantabbaṃ, na bhayāgatiṃ gantabbaṃ, majjhattaina bhavitabbaṃ dhammaisu ca puggalaisu ca| aivañ‍ca pana anuvij‍jakau anuvij‍jamānau satthu caiva sāsanakarau hauti, viñ‍ñūnañ‍ca sabrahmacārīnaṃ piyau ca hauti manāpau ca garu ca bhāvanīyau ca|

366. suttaṃ saṃsandanatthāya, aupammaṃ nidassanatthāya, atthau viñ‍ñāpanatthāya, paṭipucchā ṭhapanatthāya, aukāsakammaṃ caudanatthāya, caudanā sāraṇatthāya, sāraṇā savacanīyatthāya, savacanīyaṃ palibaudhatthāya, palibaudhau vinicchayatthāya, vinicchayau santīraṇatthāya, santīraṇaṃ ṭhānāṭhānagamanatthāya, ṭhānāṭhānagamanaṃ dummaṅkūnaṃ puggalānaṃ niggahatthāya, paisalānaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ sampaggahatthāya, saṅghau sampariggahasampaṭicchanatthāya, saṅghaina anumatā puggalā pac‍caikaṭṭhāyinau avisaṃvādakaṭṭhāyinau|
