vinayau saṃvaratthāya, saṃvarau avippaṭisāratthāya, avippaṭisārau pāmuj‍jatthāya, pāmuj‍jaṃ pītatthāya, pīti passaddhatthāya, passaddhi sukhatthāya, sukhaṃ samādhatthāya, samādhi yathābhūtañāṇadassanatthāya, yathābhūtañāṇadassanaṃ nibbidatthāya, nibbidā virāgatthāya, virāgau vimuttatthāya, vimutti vimuttiñāṇadassanatthāya, vimuttiñāṇadassanaṃ anupādāparinibbānatthāya| aitadatthā  kathā, aitadatthā mantanā, aitadatthā upanisā, aitadatthaṃ sautāvadhānaṃ – yadidaṃ  anupādācittassa vimaukkhauti|

367.

 anuyaugavattaṃ nisāmaya, kusalaina buddhimatā kataṃ|

suvuttaṃ sikkhāpadānulaumikaṃ, gatiṃ na nāsaintau samparāyikaṃ||

vatthuṃ  vipattiṃ āpattiṃ, nidānaṃ ākāraakauvidau|

pubbāparaṃ na jānāti, katākataṃ samaina ca||

kammañ‍ca adhikaraṇañ‍ca, samathai cāpi akauvidau|

rattau duṭṭhau ca mūḷhau ca, bhayā mauhā ca gacchati||

na ca sañ‍ñattikusalau, nijjhattiyā ca akauvidau|

laddhapakkhau ahirikau, kaṇhakammau anādarau|

sa vai sacai (ka॰) tādisakau bhikkhu, appaṭikkhauti vuc‍cati||

vatthuṃ vipattiṃ āpattiṃ, nidānaṃ ākārakauvidau|

pubbāparañ‍ca jānāti, katākataṃ samaina ca||

kammañ‍ca adhikaraṇañ‍ca, samathai cāpi kauvidau|

arattau aduṭṭhau amūḷhau, bhayā mauhā na gacchati||

sañ‍ñattiyā ca kusalau, nijjhattiyā ca kauvidau|

laddhapakkhau hirimanau, suk‍kakammau sagāravau|

sa vai tādisakau bhikkhu, sappaṭikkhauti vuc‍catīti||

cūḷasaṅgāmau niṭṭhitau|


tassuddānaṃ –

nīcacittaina  pucchaiyya, garu saṅghai na puggalai|

suttaṃ saṃsandanatthāya, vinayānuggahaina ca|

uddānaṃ cūḷasaṅgāmai, aikuddaisau aikuddaisaṃ (sī॰ syā॰) idaṃ katanti||


mahāsaṅgāmau

1. vauharantaina jānitabbādi

368. saṅgāmāvacarai  bhikkhunā saṅghai vauharantaina vatthu jānitabbaṃ, vipatti jānitabbā, āpatti jānitabbā, nidānaṃ jānitabbaṃ, ākārau jānitabbau, pubbāparaṃ jānitabbaṃ, katākataṃ jānitabbaṃ, kammaṃ jānitabbaṃ, adhikaraṇaṃ jānitabbaṃ, samathau jānitabbau, na chandāgati gantabbā, na dausāgati gantabbā, na mauhāgati gantabbā, na bhayāgati gantabbā, sañ‍ñāpanīyai ṭhānai sañ‍ñāpaitabbaṃ, nijjhāpanīyai ṭhānai nijjhāpaitabbaṃ, paikkhanīyai ṭhānai paikkhitabbaṃ, pasādanīyai ṭhānai pasādaitabbaṃ, laddhapakkhaumhīti parapakkhau nāvajānitabbau, bahussutaumhīti appassutau nāvajānitabbau, thairataraumhīti navakatarau nāvajānitabbau, asampattaṃ na byāhātabbaṃ, sampattaṃ dhammatau vinayatau na parihāpaitabbaṃ, yaina dhammaina yaina vinayaina yaina satthusāsanaina taṃ adhikaraṇaṃ vūpasammati, tathā taṃ adhikaraṇaṃ vūpasamaitabbaṃ|

369. vatthu jānitabbanti aṭṭhapārājikānaṃ aṭṭhan‍naṃ pārājikānaṃ (sī॰ ka॰) vatthu jānitabbaṃ, taivīsasaṅghādisaisānaṃ vatthu jānitabbaṃ, dvaianiyatānaṃ vatthu jānitabbaṃ, dvaicattārīsanissaggiyānaṃ vatthu jānitabbaṃ, aṭṭhāsītisatapācittiyānaṃ vatthu jānitabbaṃ, dvādasapāṭidaisanīyānaṃ vatthu jānitabbaṃ, duk‍kaṭānaṃ vatthu  jānitabbaṃ, dubbhāsitānaṃ vatthu jānitabbaṃ|

370. vipatti jānitabbāti sīlavipatti jānitabbā, ācāravipatti jānitabbā, diṭṭhivipatti jānitabbā, ājīvavipatti jānitabbā|

371. āpatti jānitabbāti pārājikāpatti jānitabbā, saṅghādisaisāpatti jānitabbā, thul‍lac‍cayāpatti  jānitabbā, pācittiyāpatti jānitabbā, pāṭidaisanīyāpatti jānitabbā, duk‍kaṭāpatti jānitabbā, dubbhāsitāpatti jānitabbā|

372. nidānaṃ  jānitabbanti aṭṭhapārājikānaṃ nidānaṃ jānitabbaṃ, taivīsasaṅghādisaisānaṃ nidānaṃ jānitabbaṃ, dvaianiyatānaṃ nidānaṃ jānitabbaṃ, dvaicattārīsanissaggiyānaṃ nidānaṃ jānitabbaṃ, aṭṭhāsītisatapācittiyānaṃ nidānaṃ jānitabbaṃ, dvādasapāṭidaisanīyānaṃ nidānaṃ jānitabbaṃ, duk‍kaṭānaṃ nidānaṃ jānitabbaṃ, dubbhāsitānaṃ nidānaṃ jānitabbaṃ|

373. ākārau jānitabbauti saṅghau ākāratau jānitabbau, gaṇau ākāratau jānitabbau, puggalau ākāratau jānitabbau, caudakau ākāratau jānitabbau, cuditakau ākāratau jānitabbau| saṅghau ākāratau jānitabbauti paṭibalau nu khau ayaṃ saṅghau imaṃ adhikaraṇaṃ vūpasamaituṃ dhammaina vinayaina satthusāsanaina udāhu nauti, aivaṃ saṅghau ākāratau jānitabbau| gaṇau ākāratau jānitabbauti paṭibalau nu khau ayaṃ gaṇau imaṃ adhikaraṇaṃ vūpasamaituṃ dhammaina vinayaina satthusāsanaina udāhu nauti, aivaṃ gaṇau ākāratau jānitabbau| puggalau ākāratau jānitabbauti paṭibalau nu khau ayaṃ puggalau imaṃ adhikaraṇaṃ vūpasamaituṃ dhammaina vinayaina satthusāsanaina udāhu nauti, aivaṃ puggalau ākāratau jānitabbau| caudakau ākāratau jānitabbauti kac‍ci nu khau ayamāyasmā pañ‍casu dhammaisu patiṭṭhāya paraṃ caudaiti udāhu nauti, aivaṃ caudakau ākāratau jānitabbau| cuditakau ākāratau jānitabbauti kac‍ci nu khau ayamāyasmā dvīsu dhammaisu patiṭṭhitau sac‍cai ca akuppai ca udāhu nauti, aivaṃ cuditakau ākāratau jānitabbau|

374. pubbāparaṃ jānitabbanti kac‍ci nu khau ayamāyasmā vatthutau vā vatthuṃ saṅkamati, vipattitau vā vipattiṃ saṅkamati, āpattitau vā āpattiṃ saṅkamati, avajānitvā vā paṭijānāti, paṭijānitvā vā avajānāti, añ‍ñaina vā añ‍ñaṃ paṭicarati, udāhu nauti, aivaṃ pubbāparaṃ jānitabbaṃ|

375. katākataṃ jānitabbanti maithunadhammau jānitabbau, maithunadhammassa anulaumaṃ jānitabbaṃ, maithunadhammassa pubbabhāgau jānitabbau| maithunadhammau jānitabbauti dvayaṃdvayasamāpatti jānitabbā| maithunadhammassa anulaumaṃ  jānitabbanti bhikkhu attanau mukhaina parassa aṅgajātaṃ gaṇhāti| maithunadhammassa pubbabhāgau jānitabbauti vaṇṇāvaṇṇau vaṇṇau avaṇṇau (syā॰), kāyasaṃsaggau, duṭṭhul‍lavācā, attakāmapāricariyā, vacanamanuppadānaṃ  dhanamanuppadānaṃ (ka॰)|

376. kammaṃ jānitabbanti sauḷasakammāni jānitabbāni – cattāri apalaukanakammāni jānitabbāni, cattāri ñattikammāni jānitabbāni, cattāri ñattidutiyakammāni jānitabbāni, cattāri ñatticatutthakammāni jānitabbāni|

377. adhikaraṇaṃ jānitabbanti cattāri adhikaraṇāni jānitabbāni – vivādādhikaraṇaṃ jānitabbaṃ, anuvādādhikaraṇaṃ jānitabbaṃ, āpattādhikaraṇaṃ jānitabbaṃ, kic‍cādhikaraṇaṃ jānitabbaṃ|

378. samathau jānitabbauti satta samathā jānitabbā – sammukhāvinayau jānitabbau, sativinayau jānitabbau, amūḷhavinayau jānitabbau, paṭiñ‍ñātakaraṇaṃ jānitabbaṃ, yaibhuyyasikā jānitabbā, tassapāpiyasikā jānitabbā, tiṇavatthārakau jānitabbau|

2. agatiagantabbau

379. na chandāgati gantabbāti chandāgatiṃ gacchantau kathaṃ chandāgatiṃ gacchati? idhaikac‍cau – ‘‘ayaṃ mai upajjhāyau vā ācariyau vā saddhivihārikau vā antaivāsikau vā samānupajjhāyakau vā samānācariyakau vā sandiṭṭhau vā sambhattau vā ñātisālauhitau vā’’ti, tassānukampāya tassānurakkhāya adhammaṃ dhammauti dīpaiti, dhammaṃ adhammauti dīpaiti, avinayaṃ vinayauti dīpaiti, vinayaṃ avinayauti dīpaiti, abhāsitaṃ alapitaṃ tathāgataina bhāsitaṃ lapitaṃ tathāgataināti  dīpaiti, bhāsitaṃ lapitaṃ tathāgataina abhāsitaṃ alapitaṃ tathāgataināti dīpaiti, anāciṇṇaṃ tathāgataina āciṇṇaṃ tathāgataināti dīpaiti, āciṇṇaṃ tathāgataina anāciṇṇaṃ tathāgataināti dīpaiti, apañ‍ñattaṃ tathāgataina pañ‍ñattaṃ tathāgataināti dīpaiti, pañ‍ñattaṃ tathāgataina apañ‍ñattaṃ tathāgataināti dīpaiti, anāpattiṃ  āpattīti dīpaiti, āpattiṃ anāpattīti dīpaiti, lahukaṃ āpattiṃ garukā āpattīti dīpaiti, garukaṃ āpattiṃ lahukā āpattīti dīpaiti, sāvasaisaṃ āpattiṃ anavasaisā āpattīti dīpaiti, anavasaisaṃ āpattiṃ sāvasaisā āpattīti dīpaiti, duṭṭhul‍laṃ āpattiṃ aduṭṭhul‍lā āpattīti dīpaiti, aduṭṭhul‍laṃ  āpattiṃ duṭṭhul‍lā āpattīti dīpaiti| imaihi aṭṭhārasahi vatthūhi chandāgatiṃ gacchantau bahujanāhitāya  paṭipan‍nau hauti bahujanāsukhāya bahunau janassa anatthāya ahitāya dukkhāya daivamanussānaṃ| imaihi aṭṭhārasahi vatthūhi chandāgatiṃ gacchantau khataṃ upahataṃ attānaṃ pariharati, sāvaj‍jau ca hauti sānuvaj‍jau ca viñ‍ñūnaṃ, bahuñ‍ca apuñ‍ñaṃ pasavati| chandāgatiṃ gacchantau aivaṃ chandāgatiṃ gacchati|

380. na dausāgati gantabbāti dausāgatiṃ gacchantau kathaṃ dausāgatiṃ gacchati? idhaikac‍cau anatthaṃ mai acarīti āghātaṃ bandhati, anatthaṃ mai caratīti āghātaṃ bandhati, anatthaṃ mai carissatīti  āghātaṃ bandhati, piyassa mai manāpassa anatthaṃ acari… anatthaṃ carati… anatthaṃ carissatīti āghātaṃ bandhati, appiyassa mai amanāpassa atthaṃ acari… atthaṃ carati… atthaṃ carissatīti āghātaṃ bandhati| imaihi navahi āghātavatthūhi āghātau paṭighātau kuddhau kaudhābhibhūtau adhammaṃ dhammauti dīpaiti, dhammaṃ adhammauti dīpaiti…pai॰… duṭṭhul‍laṃ āpattiṃ aduṭṭhul‍lā āpattīti dīpaiti, aduṭṭhul‍laṃ āpattiṃ duṭṭhul‍lā āpattīti dīpaiti| imaihi aṭṭhārasahi vatthūhi dausāgatiṃ gacchantau bahujanāhitāya paṭipan‍nau hauti bahujanāsukhāya bahunau janassa anatthāya ahitāya dukkhāya daivamanussānaṃ| imaihi aṭṭhārasahi vatthūhi dausāgatiṃ gacchantau khataṃ upahataṃ attānaṃ pariharati, sāvaj‍jau ca hauti sānuvaj‍jau ca viñ‍ñūnaṃ bahuñ‍ca apuñ‍ñaṃ pasavati| dausāgatiṃ gacchantau aivaṃ dausāgatiṃ gacchati|

381. na mauhāgati gantabbāti mauhāgatiṃ gacchantau kathaṃ mauhāgatiṃ gacchati? rattau rāgavasaina gacchati, duṭṭhau dausavasaina gacchati, mūḷhau mauhavasaina gacchati, parāmaṭṭhau diṭṭhivasaina gacchati, mūḷhau saṃmūḷhau mauhābhibhūtau adhammaṃ dhammauti dīpaiti, dhammaṃ adhammauti  dīpaiti…pai॰… duṭṭhul‍laṃ āpattiṃ aduṭṭhul‍lā āpattīti dīpaiti, aduṭṭhul‍laṃ āpattiṃ duṭṭhul‍lā āpattīti dīpaiti| imaihi aṭṭhārasahi vatthūhi mauhāgatiṃ gacchantau bahujanāhitāya paṭipan‍nau hauti bahujanāsukhāya bahunau janassa anatthāya ahitāya  dukkhāya daivamanussānaṃ| imaihi aṭṭhārasahi vatthūhi mauhāgatiṃ gacchantau khataṃ upahataṃ attānaṃ pariharati, sāvaj‍jau ca hauti sānuvaj‍jau ca viñ‍ñūnaṃ, bahuñ‍ca apuñ‍ñaṃ pasavati| mauhāgatiṃ gacchantau aivaṃ mauhāgatiṃ gacchati|

382. na bhayāgati gantabbāti bhayāgatiṃ gacchantau kathaṃ bhayāgatiṃ gacchati? idhaikac‍cau – ‘‘ayaṃ visamanissitau vā gahananissitau vā balavanissitau vā kakkhaḷau pharusau jīvitantarāyaṃ vā brahmacariyantarāyaṃ vā karissatī’’ti, tassa bhayā bhītau adhammaṃ dhammauti  dīpaiti, dhammaṃ adhammauti dīpaiti, avinayaṃ vinayauti dīpaiti, vinayaṃ avinayauti dīpaiti, abhāsitaṃ alapitaṃ tathāgataina bhāsitaṃ lapitaṃ tathāgataināti dīpaiti, bhāsitaṃ lapitaṃ tathāgataina abhāsitaṃ alapitaṃ tathāgataināti dīpaiti, anāciṇṇaṃ tathāgataina āciṇṇaṃ tathāgataināti dīpaiti, āciṇṇaṃ tathāgataina anāciṇṇaṃ tathāgataināti dīpaiti, apañ‍ñattaṃ tathāgataina pañ‍ñattaṃ tathāgataināti dīpaiti, pañ‍ñattaṃ tathāgataina apañ‍ñattaṃ tathāgataināti dīpaiti, anāpattiṃ āpattīti dīpaiti, āpattiṃ anāpattīti dīpaiti, lahukaṃ āpattiṃ garukā āpattīti dīpaiti, garukaṃ āpattiṃ lahukā āpattīti dīpaiti, sāvasaisaṃ āpattiṃ anavasaisā āpattīti dīpaiti, anavasaisaṃ āpattiṃ sāvasaisā āpattīti dīpaiti, duṭṭhul‍laṃ āpattiṃ aduṭṭhul‍lā āpattīti dīpaiti, aduṭṭhul‍laṃ āpattiṃ duṭṭhul‍lā āpattīti dīpaiti| imaihi aṭṭhārasahi vatthūhi bhayāgatiṃ gacchantau bahujanāhitāya paṭipan‍nau hauti bahujanāsukhāya bahunau janassa anatthāya ahitāya dukkhāya daivamanussānaṃ| imaihi aṭṭhārasahi vatthūhi bhayāgatiṃ gacchantau khataṃ upahataṃ attānaṃ pariharati, sāvaj‍jau ca hauti sānuvaj‍jau ca viñ‍ñūnaṃ, bahuñ‍ca apuñ‍ñaṃ pasavati| bhayāgatiṃ gacchantau aivaṃ bhayāgatiṃ gacchati|

chandā dausā bhayā mauhā, yau dhammaṃ ativattati|

nihīyati tassa yasau, kāḷapakkhaiva candimāti||

3. agatiagamanaṃ

383. kathaṃ  na chandāgatiṃ gacchati? adhammaṃ adhammauti dīpaintau na chandāgatiṃ gacchati, dhammaṃ dhammauti dīpaintau  na chandāgatiṃ  gacchati, avinayaṃ avinayauti dīpaintau na chandāgatiṃ gacchati, vinayaṃ vinayauti dīpaintau na chandāgatiṃ gacchati, abhāsitaṃ alapitaṃ tathāgataina abhāsitaṃ alapitaṃ tathāgataināti dīpaintau na chandāgatiṃ gacchati, bhāsitaṃ lapitaṃ tathāgataina bhāsitaṃ lapitaṃ tathāgataināti dīpaintau na chandāgatiṃ gacchati, anāciṇṇaṃ tathāgataina anāciṇṇaṃ tathāgataināti dīpaintau na chandāgatiṃ gacchati, āciṇṇaṃ tathāgataina āciṇṇaṃ tathāgataināti dīpaintau na chandāgatiṃ gacchati, apañ‍ñattaṃ tathāgataina apañ‍ñattaṃ tathāgataināti dīpaintau na chandāgatiṃ gacchati, pañ‍ñattaṃ tathāgataina pañ‍ñattaṃ tathāgataināti dīpaintau na chandāgatiṃ gacchati, anāpattiṃ anāpattīti dīpaintau na chandāgatiṃ gacchati, āpattiṃ āpattīti dīpaintau na chandāgatiṃ gacchati, lahukaṃ āpattiṃ lahukā āpattīti dīpaintau na chandāgatiṃ gacchati, garukaṃ āpattiṃ garukā āpattīti dīpaintau na chandāgatiṃ gacchati, sāvasaisaṃ āpattiṃ sāvasaisā āpattīti dīpaintau na chandāgatiṃ gacchati, anavasaisaṃ  āpattiṃ anavasaisā āpattīti dīpaintau na chandāgatiṃ gacchati, duṭṭhul‍laṃ āpattiṃ duṭṭhul‍lā āpattīti dīpaintau na chandāgatiṃ gacchati, aduṭṭhul‍laṃ āpattiṃ aduṭṭhul‍lā āpattīti dīpaintau na chandāgatiṃ gacchati| aivaṃ na chandāgatiṃ gacchati|

384. kathaṃ na dausāgatiṃ gacchati? adhammaṃ adhammauti dīpaintau  na dausāgatiṃ gacchati, dhammaṃ dhammauti dīpaintau na dausāgatiṃ gacchati…pai॰… duṭṭhul‍laṃ āpattiṃ duṭṭhul‍lā āpattīti dīpaintau na dausāgatiṃ gacchati, aduṭṭhul‍laṃ āpattiṃ aduṭṭhul‍lā āpattīti dīpaintau na dausāgatiṃ gacchati| aivaṃ na dausāgatiṃ gacchati|

385. kathaṃ na mauhāgatiṃ gacchati? adhammaṃ adhammauti dīpaintau na mauhāgatiṃ gacchati, dhammaṃ dhammauti dīpaintau na mauhāgatiṃ gacchati…pai॰… duṭṭhul‍laṃ āpattiṃ duṭṭhul‍lā āpattīti dīpaintau na mauhāgatiṃ  gacchati, aduṭṭhul‍laṃ āpattiṃ aduṭṭhul‍lā āpattīti dīpaintau na mauhāgatiṃ gacchati| aivaṃ na mauhāgatiṃ gacchati|

386. kathaṃ na bhayāgatiṃ gacchati? adhammaṃ adhammauti dīpaintau na bhayāgatiṃ gacchati, dhammaṃ dhammauti dīpaintau na bhayāgatiṃ gacchati, avinayaṃ avinayauti dīpaintau na bhayāgatiṃ gacchati, vinayaṃ vinayauti dīpaintau na bhayāgatiṃ gacchati, abhāsitaṃ alapitaṃ tathāgataina abhāsitaṃ alapitaṃ tathāgataināti dīpaintau na bhayāgatiṃ gacchati, bhāsitaṃ lapitaṃ tathāgataina bhāsitaṃ lapitaṃ tathāgataināti dīpaintau na bhayāgatiṃ gacchati, anāciṇṇaṃ tathāgataina anāciṇṇaṃ tathāgataināti dīpaintau na bhayāgatiṃ gacchati, āciṇṇaṃ tathāgataina āciṇṇaṃ tathāgataināti dīpaintau na bhayāgatiṃ gacchati, apañ‍ñattaṃ tathāgataina apañ‍ñattaṃ tathāgataināti dīpaintau na bhayāgatiṃ gacchati, pañ‍ñattaṃ tathāgataina pañ‍ñattaṃ tathāgataināti dīpaintau na bhayāgatiṃ gacchati, anāpattiṃ anāpattīti dīpaintau na bhayāgatiṃ gacchati, āpattiṃ āpattīti dīpaintau na bhayāgatiṃ gacchati, lahukaṃ āpattiṃ lahukā āpattīti dīpaintau na bhayāgatiṃ gacchati, garukaṃ āpattiṃ garukā āpattīti dīpaintau na bhayāgatiṃ gacchati, sāvasaisaṃ āpattiṃ sāvasaisā āpattīti dīpaintau na bhayāgatiṃ gacchati, anavasaisaṃ āpattiṃ anavasaisā āpattīti dīpaintau na bhayāgatiṃ gacchati, duṭṭhul‍laṃ āpattiṃ duṭṭhul‍lā āpattīti dīpaintau na bhayāgatiṃ gacchati, aduṭṭhul‍laṃ āpattiṃ aduṭṭhul‍lā āpattīti dīpaintau na bhayāgatiṃ gacchati| aivaṃ na bhayāgatiṃ gacchati|

chandā  dausā bhayā mauhā, yau dhammaṃ nātivattati|

āpūrati tassa yasau, suk‍kapakkhaiva candimāti||

4. sañ‍ñāpanīyādi

387. kathaṃ sañ‍ñāpanīyai ṭhānai sañ‍ñāpaiti? adhammaṃ adhammauti dīpaintau sañ‍ñāpanīyai ṭhānai sañ‍ñāpaiti, dhammaṃ dhammauti dīpaintau  sañ‍ñāpanīyai ṭhānai sañ‍ñāpaiti…pai॰… duṭṭhul‍laṃ āpattiṃ duṭṭhul‍lā āpattīti dīpaintau sañ‍ñāpanīyai ṭhānai sañ‍ñāpaiti, aduṭṭhul‍laṃ āpattiṃ aduṭṭhul‍lā  āpattīti dīpaintau sañ‍ñāpanīyai ṭhānai sañ‍ñāpaiti| aivaṃ sañ‍ñāpanīyai ṭhānai sañ‍ñāpaiti|

388. kathaṃ nijjhāpanīyai ṭhānai nijjhāpaiti? adhammaṃ adhammauti dīpaintau nijjhāpanīyai ṭhānai nijjhāpaiti, dhammaṃ dhammauti dīpaintau nijjhāpanīyai ṭhānai nijjhāpaiti…pai॰… duṭṭhul‍laṃ āpattiṃ duṭṭhul‍lā āpattīti dīpaintau nijjhāpanīyai ṭhānai nijjhāpaiti, aduṭṭhul‍laṃ āpattiṃ aduṭṭhul‍lā āpattīti dīpaintau nijjhāpanīyai ṭhānai nijjhāpaiti| aivaṃ nijjhāpanīyai ṭhānai nijjhāpaiti|

389. kathaṃ paikkhanīyai ṭhānai paikkhati? adhammaṃ adhammauti dīpaintau paikkhanīyai ṭhānai paikkhati, dhammaṃ dhammauti dīpaintau paikkhanīyai ṭhānai paikkhati…pai॰… duṭṭhul‍laṃ āpattiṃ duṭṭhul‍lā āpattīti dīpaintau paikkhanīyai ṭhānai paikkhati, aduṭṭhul‍laṃ āpattiṃ aduṭṭhul‍lā āpattīti dīpaintau paikkhanīyai ṭhānai paikkhati| aivaṃ paikkhanīyai ṭhānai paikkhati|

390. kathaṃ pasādanīyai ṭhānai pasādaiti? adhammaṃ adhammauti dīpaintau pasādanīyai ṭhānai pasādaiti, dhammaṃ dhammauti dīpaintau pasādanīyai ṭhānai pasādaiti…pai॰… duṭṭhul‍laṃ āpattiṃ duṭṭhul‍lā āpattīti dīpaintau pasādanīyai ṭhānai pasādaiti, aduṭṭhul‍laṃ āpattiṃ  aduṭṭhul‍lā āpattīti dīpaintau pasādanīyai ṭhānai pasādaiti| aivaṃ pasādanīyai ṭhānai pasādaiti|

5. parapakkhādiavajānanaṃ

391. kathaṃ laddhapakkhaumhīti parapakkhaṃ avajānāti? idhaikac‍cau laddhapakkhau hauti laddhaparivārau  pakkhavā ñātimā| ‘‘ayaṃ aladdhapakkhau aladdhaparivārau na pakkhavā na ñātimā’’ti tassa avajānantau adhammaṃ dhammauti dīpaiti, dhammaṃ adhammauti dīpaiti…pai॰… duṭṭhul‍laṃ āpattiṃ aduṭṭhul‍lā āpattīti dīpaiti, aduṭṭhul‍laṃ āpattiṃ duṭṭhul‍lā āpattīti dīpaiti| aivaṃ laddhapakkhaumhīti parapakkhaṃ avajānāti|

392. kathaṃ bahussutaumhīti appassutaṃ avajānāti? idhaikac‍cau bahussutau hauti sutadharau sutasan‍nicayau| ‘‘ayaṃ appassutau appāgamau  appadharau’’ti tassa avajānantau adhammaṃ dhammauti dīpaiti, dhammaṃ adhammauti dīpaiti…pai॰… duṭṭhul‍laṃ āpattiṃ aduṭṭhul‍lā āpattīti dīpaiti, aduṭṭhul‍laṃ āpattiṃ duṭṭhul‍lā āpattīti dīpaiti| aivaṃ bahussutaumhīti appassutaṃ avajānāti|

393. kathaṃ thairataraumhīti navakataraṃ avajānāti? idhaikac‍cau thairau hauti rattañ‍ñū cirapabbajitau ayaṃ navakau appañ‍ñātau appakatañ‍ñū imassa vacanaṃ akataṃ bhavissatī’’ti tassa avajānantau adhammaṃ dhammauti dīpaiti, dhammaṃ adhammauti dīpaiti  …pai॰… duṭṭhul‍laṃ āpattiṃ aduṭṭhul‍lā āpattīti dīpaiti, aduṭṭhul‍laṃ  āpattiṃ duṭṭhul‍lā āpattīti dīpaiti| aivaṃ thairataraumhīti navakataraṃ avajānāti|

394. asampattaṃ na byāharitabbanti anautiṇṇaṃ bhāraṃ bhāsaṃ (syā॰) na autāraitabbaṃ| sampattaṃ dhammatau vinayatau na parihāpaitabbanti yaṃatthāya saṅghau san‍nipatitau hauti taṃ atthaṃ dhammatau vinayatau na parihāpaitabbaṃ|

395. yaina dhammaināti bhūtaina vatthunā| yaina vinayaināti caudaitvā sāraitvā| yaina satthusāsanaināti ñattisampadāya anussāvanasampadāya, yaina dhammaina yaina vinayaina yaina satthusāsanaina taṃ adhikaraṇaṃ vūpasammati, tathā taṃ adhikaraṇaṃ vūpasamaitabbanti|

6. anuvij‍jakassa anuyaugaṃ

396. anuvij‍jakaina caudakau pucchitabbau – ‘‘yaṃ khau tvaṃ, āvusau, imassa bhikkhunau pavāraṇaṃ ṭhapaisi, kimhi naṃ ṭhapaisi, sīlavipattiyā vā ṭhapaisi, ācāravipattiyā vā ṭhapaisi, diṭṭhivipattiyā vā ṭhapaisī’’ti? sau cai aivaṃ vadaiyya – ‘‘sīlavipattiyā vā ṭhapaimi  ācāravipattiyā vā ṭhapaimi diṭṭhivipattiyā vā ṭhapaimī’’ti, sau aivamassa vacanīyau – ‘‘jānāti panāyasmā sīlavipattiṃ, jānāti ācāravipattiṃ, jānāti diṭṭhivipatti’’nti? sau cai aivaṃ vadaiyya – ‘‘jānāmi khau ahaṃ, āvusau, sīlavipattiṃ, jānāmi ācāravipattiṃ, jānāmi diṭṭhivipatti’’nti, sau aivamassa vacanīyau – ‘‘katamā  panāvusau, sīlavipatti katamā ācāravipatti katamā diṭṭhivipattī’’ti? sau cai aivaṃ vadaiyya – ‘‘cattāri pārājikāni tairasa saṅghādisaisā – ayaṃ sīlavipatti| thul‍lac‍cayaṃ pācittiyaṃ pāṭidaisanīyaṃ duk‍kaṭaṃ dubbhāsitaṃ – ayaṃ ācāravipatti| micchādiṭṭhi antaggāhikādiṭṭhi – ayaṃ diṭṭhivipattī’’ti, sau aivamassa vacanīyau – ‘‘yaṃ khau tvaṃ, āvusau, imassa bhikkhunau pavāraṇaṃ ṭhapaisi, diṭṭhaina vā ṭhapaisi, sutaina vā ṭhapaisi, parisaṅkāya vā ṭhapaisī’’ti? sau cai aivaṃ vadaiyya – ‘‘diṭṭhaina vā ṭhapaimi, sutaina vā ṭhapaimi, parisaṅkāya vā ṭhapaimī’’ti, sau aivamassa vacanīyau – ‘‘yaṃ khau tvaṃ, āvusau, imassa bhikkhunau diṭṭhaina pavāraṇaṃ ṭhapaisi, kiṃ tai diṭṭhaṃ, kinti tai diṭṭhaṃ, kadā tai diṭṭhaṃ, kattha tai diṭṭhaṃ, pārājikaṃ ajjhāpaj‍jantau diṭṭhau, saṅghādisaisaṃ ajjhāpaj‍jantau diṭṭhau, thul‍lac‍cayaṃ… pācittiyaṃ… pāṭidaisanīyaṃ… duk‍kaṭaṃ… dubbhāsitaṃ ajjhāpaj‍jantau diṭṭhau, kattha ca tvaṃ ahausi, kattha cāyaṃ bhikkhu ahausi, kiñ‍ca tvaṃ karausi, kiṃ cāyaṃ bhikkhu karautī’’ti? sau cai aivaṃ vadaiyya – ‘‘na khau ahaṃ, āvusau, imassa bhikkhunau diṭṭhaina pavāraṇaṃ ṭhapaimi, api ca sutaina pavāraṇaṃ ṭhapaimī’’ti, sau aivamassa vacanīyau – ‘‘yaṃ khau tvaṃ, āvusau, imassa bhikkhunau sutaina pavāraṇaṃ ṭhapaisi  kiṃ tai sutaṃ, kinti tai sutaṃ, kadā tai sutaṃ, kattha tai sutaṃ, pārājikaṃ ajjhāpan‍nauti sutaṃ, saṅghādisaisaṃ ajjhāpan‍nauti sutaṃ, thul‍lac‍cayaṃ… pācittiyaṃ… pāṭidaisanīyaṃ… duk‍kaṭaṃ… dubbhāsitaṃ ajjhāpan‍nauti sutaṃ, bhikkhussa sutaṃ, bhikkhuniyā sutaṃ, sikkhamānāya sutaṃ, sāmaṇairassa sutaṃ, sāmaṇairiyā sutaṃ, upāsakassa sutaṃ, upāsikāya sutaṃ, rājūnaṃ sutaṃ, rājamahāmattānaṃ sutaṃ, titthiyānaṃ sutaṃ, titthiyasāvakānaṃ suta’’nti? sau cai aivaṃ vadaiyya – ‘‘na khau ahaṃ, āvusau, imassa bhikkhunau sutaina pavāraṇaṃ ṭhapaimi, api ca parisaṅkāya pavāraṇaṃ ṭhapaimī’’ti, sau aivamassa vacanīyau – ‘‘yaṃ khau tvaṃ, āvusau, imassa bhikkhunau parisaṅkāya pavāraṇaṃ ṭhapaisi, kiṃ parisaṅkasi, kinti parisaṅkasi, kadā parisaṅkasi, kattha parisaṅkasi, pārājikaṃ ajjhāpan‍nauti parisaṅkasi, saṅghādisaisaṃ ajjhāpan‍nauti parisaṅkasi, thul‍lac‍cayaṃ… pācittiyaṃ… pāṭidaisanīyaṃ… duk‍kaṭaṃ… dubbhāsitaṃ ajjhāpan‍nauti parisaṅkasi, bhikkhussa sutvā parisaṅkasi, bhikkhuniyā sutvā parisaṅkasi, sikkhamānāya sutvā parisaṅkasi, sāmaṇairassa sutvā parisaṅkasi, sāmaṇairiyā sutvā parisaṅkasi, upāsakassa sutvā parisaṅkasi, upāsikāya sutvā  parisaṅkasi, rājūnaṃ sutvā  parisaṅkasi rājamahāmattānaṃ  sutvā parisaṅkasi, titthiyānaṃ sutvā parisaṅkasi, titthiyasāvakānaṃ sutvā parisaṅkasī’’ti?

397.

 diṭṭhaṃ diṭṭhaina samaiti, diṭṭhaina saṃsandatai diṭṭhaṃ|

diṭṭhaṃ paṭic‍ca na upaiti, asuddhaparisaṅkitau|

sau puggalau paṭiñ‍ñāya, kātabbā taina pavāraṇā||

sutaṃ sutaina samaiti, sutaina saṃsandatai sutaṃ|

sutaṃ paṭic‍ca na upaiti, asuddhaparisaṅkitau|

sau puggalau paṭiñ‍ñāya, kātabbā taina pavāraṇā||

mutaṃ mutaina samaiti, mutaina saṃsandatai mutaṃ|

mutaṃ paṭic‍ca na upaiti, asuddhaparisaṅkitau|

sau puggalau paṭiñ‍ñāya, kātabbā taina pavāraṇāti||

7. pucchāvibhāgau

398. kiṃ tai diṭṭhanti katamā pucchā? kinti tai diṭṭhanti katamā pucchā? kadā tai diṭṭhanti katamā pucchā? kattha tai diṭṭhanti katamā pucchā?

399. kiṃ tai diṭṭhanti vatthupucchā, vipattipucchā, āpattipucchā, ajjhācārapucchā| vatthupucchāti – aṭṭhapārājikānaṃ vatthupucchā, taivīsasaṅghādisaisānaṃ vatthupucchā, dvaianiyatānaṃ vatthupucchā, dvaicattārīsanissaggiyānaṃ vatthupucchā, aṭṭhāsītisatapācittiyānaṃ vatthupucchā, dvādasapāṭidaisanīyānaṃ vatthupucchā, duk‍kaṭānaṃ vatthupucchā, dubbhāsitānaṃ vatthupucchā| vipattipucchāti – sīlavipattipucchā, ācāravipattipucchā, diṭṭhivipattipucchā, ājīvavipattipucchā| āpattipucchāti – pārājikāpattipucchā, saṅghādisaisāpattipucchā, thul‍lac‍cayāpattipucchā, pācittiyāpattipucchā, pāṭidaisanīyāpattipucchā, duk‍kaṭāpattipucchā, dubbhāsitāpattipucchā| ajjhācārapucchāti – dvayaṃdvayasamāpattipucchā|

400. kinti tai diṭṭhanti liṅgapucchā, iriyāpathapucchā, ākārapucchā, vippakārapucchā| liṅgapucchāti – dīghaṃ vā rassaṃ vā kaṇhaṃ vā audātaṃ vā| iriyāpathapucchāti gacchantaṃ vā ṭhitaṃ  vā nisin‍naṃ vā nipan‍naṃ vā| ākārapucchāti gihiliṅgai  vā titthiyaliṅgai vā pabbajitaliṅgai vā| vippakārapucchāti gacchantaṃ vā ṭhitaṃ vā nisin‍naṃ vā nipan‍naṃ vā|

401. kadā  tai diṭṭhanti kālapucchā, samayapucchā, divasapucchā, utupucchā| kālapucchāti pubbaṇhakālai vā majjhanhikakālai vā sāyanhakālai vā| samayapucchāti pubbaṇhasamayai vā majjhanhikasamayai vā sāyanhasamayai vā| divasapucchāti puraibhattaṃ vā pacchābhattaṃ vā rattiṃ vā divā vā kāḷai vā juṇhai vā| utupucchāti haimantai vā gimhai vā vassai vā vassānai vā (sī॰)|

402. kattha tai diṭṭhanti ṭhānapucchā, bhūmipucchā, aukāsapucchā, padaisapucchā| ṭhānapucchāti bhūmiyā vā pathaviyā vā dharaṇiyā vā jagatiyā vā| bhūmipucchāti bhūmiyā vā pathaviyā vā pabbatai vā pāsāṇai vā pāsādai vā| aukāsapucchāti puratthimai vā aukāsai pacchimai  vā aukāsai uttarai vā aukāsai dakkhiṇai vā aukāsai| padaisapucchāti puratthimai vā padaisai pacchimai vā padaisai uttarai vā padaisai dakkhiṇai vā padaisaiti|

mahāsaṅgāmau niṭṭhitau|


tassuddānaṃ –

vatthu nidānaṃ ākārau, pubbāparaṃ katākataṃ|

kammādhikaraṇañ‍caiva, samathau chandagāmi ca||

dausā mauhā bhayā caiva, sañ‍ñā nijjhāpanaina ca|

paikkhā pasādai pakkhaumhi, sutathairataraina ca||

asampattañ‍ca sampattaṃ, dhammaina vinayaina ca|

satthussa sāsanaināpi, mahāsaṅgāmañāpanāti||


kathinabhaidau

1. kathinaatthatādi

403. kassa  kathinaṃ kaṭhinaṃ (sī॰ syā॰) anatthataṃ? kassa kathinaṃ atthataṃ? kinti kathinaṃ anatthataṃ? kinti kathinaṃ atthataṃ?

kassa kathinaṃ anatthatanti? dvin‍naṃ puggalānaṃ anatthataṃ hauti kathinaṃ – anatthārakassa ca ananumaudakassa ca| imaisaṃ dvin‍naṃ puggalānaṃ anatthataṃ hauti kathinaṃ|

kassa kathinaṃ atthatanti? dvin‍naṃ puggalānaṃ atthataṃ hauti kathinaṃ – atthārakassa ca anumaudakassa ca| imaisaṃ dvin‍naṃ puggalānaṃ atthataṃ hauti kathinaṃ|

kinti kathinaṃ anatthatanti? catuvīsatiyā ākāraihi anatthataṃ hauti kathinaṃ, na ul‍likhitamattaina atthataṃ hauti kathinaṃ, na dhauvanamattaina atthataṃ hauti kathinaṃ, na cīvaravicāraṇamattaina atthataṃ hauti kathinaṃ, na chaidanamattaina atthataṃ hauti kathinaṃ, na bandhanamattaina atthataṃ hauti kathinaṃ, na auvaṭṭiyakaraṇamattaina atthataṃ hauti kathinaṃ, na kaṇḍusakaraṇamattaina na gaṇḍusakaraṇamattaina (ka॰) atthataṃ hauti kathinaṃ, na daḷhīkammakaraṇamattaina atthataṃ hauti kathinaṃ, na anuvātakaraṇamattaina atthataṃ hauti kathinaṃ, na paribhaṇḍakaraṇamattaina atthataṃ hauti kathinaṃ, na auvaddhaiyyakaraṇamattaina na auvaṭṭaiyyakaraṇamattaina (sī॰ syā॰), na auvadaiyyakaraṇamattaina (ka॰) atthataṃ hauti kathinaṃ, na kambalamaddanamattaina atthataṃ hauti kathinaṃ, na nimittakataina atthataṃ hauti kathinaṃ, na  parikathākataina atthataṃ hauti kathinaṃ, na kuk‍kukataina atthataṃ hauti kathinaṃ, na san‍nidhikataina atthataṃ hauti kathinaṃ, na nissaggiyaina atthataṃ hauti kathinaṃ, na akappakataina atthataṃ hauti kathinaṃ, na añ‍ñatra saṅghāṭiyā atthataṃ hauti kathinaṃ, na añ‍ñatra uttarāsaṅgaina atthataṃ hauti kathinaṃ, na añ‍ñatra antaravāsakaina atthataṃ hauti kathinaṃ, na añ‍ñatra pañ‍cakaina vā atiraikapañ‍cakaina vā tadahaiva sañchin‍naina samaṇḍalīkataina atthataṃ hauti kathinaṃ, na añ‍ñatra puggalassa atthārā atthataṃ  hauti kathinaṃ, sammā cai atthataṃ hauti kathinaṃ taṃ cai nissīmaṭṭhau anumaudati| aivampi anatthataṃ hauti kathinaṃ|

nimittakammaṃ nāma nimittaṃ karauti – ‘‘iminā dussaina kathinaṃ attharissāmī’’ti| parikathā nāma parikathaṃ karauti – ‘‘imāya parikathāya kathinadussaṃ nibbattaissāmī’’ti| kuk‍kukataṃ nāma anādiyadānaṃ vuc‍cati| san‍nidhi nāma dvai san‍nidhiyau – karaṇasan‍nidhi vā nicayasan‍nidhi vā| nissaggiyaṃ nāma kayiramānai aruṇaṃ uṭṭhahati udriyati (sī॰ syā॰)| imaihi catuvīsatiyā ākāraihi anatthataṃ hauti kathinaṃ|

kinti kathinaṃ atthatanti? sattarasahi ākāraihi atthataṃ hauti kathinaṃ| ahataina atthataṃ hauti kathinaṃ, ahatakappaina atthataṃ hauti kathinaṃ, pilautikāya atthataṃ hauti kathinaṃ, paṃsukūlaina atthataṃ hauti kathinaṃ, pāpaṇikaina atthataṃ hauti kathinaṃ, animittakataina atthataṃ  hauti kathinaṃ, aparikathākataina atthataṃ hauti kathinaṃ, akuk‍kukataina atthataṃ hauti kathinaṃ, asan‍nidhikataina atthataṃ hauti kathinaṃ, anissaggiyaina atthataṃ hauti kathinaṃ, kappakataina atthataṃ hauti kathinaṃ, saṅghāṭiyā atthataṃ hauti kathinaṃ, uttarāsaṅgaina atthataṃ hauti kathinaṃ, antaravāsakaina atthataṃ hauti kathinaṃ, pañ‍cakaina vā atiraikapañ‍cakaina vā tadahaiva sañchin‍naina samaṇḍalīkataina atthataṃ hauti kathinaṃ, puggalassa atthārā atthataṃ hauti kathinaṃ, sammā cai atthataṃ hauti kathinaṃ, taṃ cai sīmaṭṭhau anumaudati, aivampi atthataṃ hauti kathinaṃ| imaihi sattarasahi ākāraihi atthataṃ hauti kathinaṃ|

saha kathinassa atthārā kati dhammā jāyanti? saha kathinassa atthārā pan‍narasa dhammā jāyanti – aṭṭha mātikā, dvai palibaudhā, pañ‍cānisaṃsā| saha kathinassa atthārā imai pan‍narasa dhammā jāyanti|

2. kathinaanantarapac‍cayādi

404. payaugassa  katamai dhammā anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau, samanantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau, nissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau, upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau, puraijātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau, pacchājātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau, sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau? pubbakaraṇassa katamai dhammā anantarapac‍cayaina  pac‍cayau…pai॰… pac‍cuddhārassa katamai dhammā… adhiṭṭhānassa katamai dhammā… atthārassa katamai dhammā… mātikānañ‍ca palibaudhānañ‍ca katamai dhammā… vatthussa katamai dhammā anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau, samanantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau, nissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau, upanissayapac‍cayaina  pac‍cayau, puraijātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau, pacchājātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau, sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau?

pubbakaraṇaṃ payaugassa anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau, samanantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau, nissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau, upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| payaugau pubbakaraṇassa puraijātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| pubbakaraṇaṃ payaugassa pacchājātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| pan‍narasa dhammā sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| pac‍cuddhārau pubbakaraṇassa anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau, samanantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau, nissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau, upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| pubbakaraṇaṃ pac‍cuddhārassa puraijātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| pac‍cuddhārau pubbakaraṇassa pacchājātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| pan‍narasa dhammā sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| adhiṭṭhānaṃ pac‍cuddhārassa anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau, samanantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau, nissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau, upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| pac‍cuddhārau adhiṭṭhānassa puraijātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| adhiṭṭhānaṃ pac‍cuddhārassa pacchājātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| pan‍narasa dhammā sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| atthārau adhiṭṭhānassa anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau, samanantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau, nissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau, upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| adhiṭṭhānaṃ atthārassa puraijātapac‍cayaina  pac‍cayau| atthārau adhiṭṭhānassa pacchājātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| pan‍narasa dhammā sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| mātikā ca palibaudhā ca atthārassa anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau, samanantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau, nissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau, upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| atthārau mātikānañ‍ca palibaudhānañ‍ca puraijātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| mātikā ca palibaudhā ca atthārassa pacchājātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| pan‍narasa dhammā sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| āsā  ca anāsā ca vatthussa anantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau, samanantarapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau, nissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau, upanissayapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| vatthu āsānañ‍ca anāsānañ‍ca puraijātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| āsā ca anāsā ca vatthussa pacchājātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau| pan‍narasa dhammā sahajātapac‍cayaina pac‍cayau|

3. pubbakaraṇanidānādivibhāgau

405. pubbakaraṇaṃ kiṃnidānaṃ, kiṃsamudayaṃ, kiṃjātikaṃ, kiṃpabhavaṃ, kiṃsambhāraṃ, kiṃsamuṭṭhānaṃ? pac‍cuddhārau kiṃnidānau, kiṃsamudayau, kiṃjātikau, kiṃpabhavau, kiṃsambhārau, kiṃsamuṭṭhānau? adhiṭṭhānaṃ kiṃnidānaṃ, kiṃsamudayaṃ, kiṃjātikaṃ, kiṃpabhavaṃ, kiṃsambhāraṃ, kiṃsamuṭṭhānaṃ? atthārau kiṃnidānau, kiṃsamudayau, kiṃjātikau, kiṃpabhavau, kiṃsambhārau, kiṃsamuṭṭhānau? mātikā ca palibaudhā ca kiṃnidānā, kiṃsamudayā, kiṃjātikā, kiṃpabhavā, kiṃsambhārā kiṃsamuṭṭhānā? āsā ca anāsā  ca kiṃnidānā, kiṃsamudayā, kiṃjātikā, kiṃpabhavā, kiṃsambhārā, kiṃsamuṭṭhānā?

pubbakaraṇaṃ payauganidānaṃ, payaugasamudayaṃ, payaugajātikaṃ, payaugapabhavaṃ, payaugasambhāraṃ, payaugasamuṭṭhānaṃ| pac‍cuddhārau pubbakaraṇanidānau, pubbakaraṇasamudayau, pubbakaraṇajātikau, pubbakaraṇapabhavau, pubbakaraṇasambhārau, pubbakaraṇasamuṭṭhānau| adhiṭṭhānaṃ pac‍cuddhāranidānaṃ, pac‍cuddhārasamudayaṃ, pac‍cuddhārajātikaṃ, pac‍cuddhārapabhavaṃ, pac‍cuddhārasambhāraṃ, pac‍cuddhārasamuṭṭhānaṃ| atthārau adhiṭṭhānanidānau, adhiṭṭhānasamudayau, adhiṭṭhānajātikau, adhiṭṭhānapabhavau, adhiṭṭhānasambhārau, adhiṭṭhānasamuṭṭhānau| mātikā ca palibaudhā ca atthāranidānā, atthārasamudayā, atthārajātikā, atthārapabhavā, atthārasambhārā, atthārasamuṭṭhānā| āsā ca anāsā ca vatthunidānā, vatthusamudayā, vatthujātikā, vatthupabhavā, vatthusambhārā, vatthusamuṭṭhānā|

406. payaugau kiṃnidānau, kiṃsamudayau, kiṃjātikau, kiṃpabhavau, kiṃsambhārau, kiṃsamuṭṭhānau, pubbakaraṇaṃ…pai॰… pac‍cuddhārau… adhiṭṭhānaṃ… atthārau… mātikā ca palibaudhā ca… vatthu… āsā ca anāsā ca kiṃnidānā, kiṃsamudayā, kiṃjātikā, kiṃpabhavā, kiṃsambhārā, kiṃsamuṭṭhānā?

payaugau  haitunidānau, haitusamudayau, haitujātikau, haitupabhavau, haitusambhārau, haitusamuṭṭhānau| pubbakaraṇaṃ…pai॰… pac‍cuddhārau… adhiṭṭhānaṃ… atthārau … mātikā ca palibaudhā ca… vatthu… āsā ca anāsā ca haitunidānā, haitusamudayā, haitujātikā, haitupabhavā, haitusambhārā, haitusamuṭṭhānā|

407. payaugau kiṃnidānau, kiṃsamudayau, kiṃjātikau, kiṃpabhavau, kiṃsambhārau, kiṃsamuṭṭhānau? pubbakaraṇaṃ…pai॰… pac‍cuddhārau… adhiṭṭhānaṃ… atthārau… mātikā ca palibaudhā ca… vatthu… āsā ca anāsā ca kiṃnidānā, kiṃsamudayā, kiṃjātikā, kiṃpabhavā, kiṃsambhārā, kiṃsamuṭṭhānā?

payaugau  pac‍cayanidānau, pac‍cayasamudayau, pac‍cayajātikau, pac‍cayapabhavau, pac‍cayasambhārau, pac‍cayasamuṭṭhānau| pubbakaraṇaṃ…pai॰… pac‍cuddhārau… adhiṭṭhānaṃ… atthārau… mātikā ca palibaudhā ca… vatthu… āsā ca anāsā ca pac‍cayanidānā, pac‍cayasamudayā, pac‍cayajātikā, pac‍cayapabhavā, pac‍cayasambhārā, pac‍cayasamuṭṭhānā|

408. pubbakaraṇaṃ katihi dhammaihi saṅgahitaṃ? pubbakaraṇaṃ sattahi dhammaihi saṅgahitaṃ| dhauvanaina, vicāraṇaina, chaidanaina, bandhanaina, sibbanaina, rajanaina, kappakaraṇaina – pubbakaraṇaṃ imaihi sattahi dhammaihi saṅgahitaṃ|

pac‍cuddhārau katihi dhammaihi saṅgahitau? pac‍cuddhārau tīhi dhammaihi saṅgahitau – saṅghāṭiyā, uttarāsaṅgaina, antaravāsakaina|

adhiṭṭhānaṃ katihi dhammaihi saṅgahitaṃ? adhiṭṭhānaṃ tīhi dhammaihi saṅgahitaṃ – saṅghāṭiyā, uttarāsaṅgaina, antaravāsakaina|

atthārau katihi dhammaihi saṅgahitau? atthārau aikaina  dhammaina saṅgahitau – vacībhaidaina|

kathinassa kati mūlāni, kati vatthūni, kati bhūmiyau? kathinassa  aikaṃ mūlaṃ – saṅghau; tīṇi vatthūni – saṅghāṭi, uttarāsaṅgau, antaravāsakau, cha bhūmiyau – khaumaṃ, kappāsikaṃ, kausaiyyaṃ, kambalaṃ, sāṇaṃ, bhaṅgaṃ|

kathinassa  kau ādi, kiṃ majjhai, kiṃ pariyausānaṃ? kathinassa pubbakaraṇaṃ ādi, kriyā majjhai, atthārau pariyausānaṃ|

409. katihaṅgaihi saman‍nāgatau puggalau abhabbau kathinaṃ attharituṃ? katihaṅgaihi saman‍nāgatau puggalau bhabbau kathinaṃ attharituṃ? aṭṭhahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgatau puggalau abhabbau kathinaṃ attharituṃ| aṭṭhahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgatau puggalau bhabbau kathinaṃ attharituṃ| katamaihi aṭṭhahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgatau puggalau abhabbau kathinaṃ attharituṃ? pubbakaraṇaṃ na jānāti, pac‍cuddhāraṃ na jānāti, adhiṭṭhānaṃ na jānāti, atthāraṃ na jānāti, mātikaṃ na jānāti, palibaudhaṃ na jānāti, uddhāraṃ na jānāti, ānisaṃsaṃ na jānāti – imaihi aṭṭhahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgatau puggalau  abhabbau kathinaṃ attharituṃ| katamaihi aṭṭhahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgatau puggalau bhabbau kathinaṃ attharituṃ? pubbakaraṇaṃ jānāti, pac‍cuddhāraṃ jānāti, adhiṭṭhānaṃ jānāti, atthāraṃ jānāti, mātikaṃ jānāti, palibaudhaṃ jānāti, uddhāraṃ jānāti, ānisaṃsaṃ jānāti – imaihi aṭṭhahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgatau puggalau bhabbau kathinaṃ attharituṃ|

410. katinaṃ  puggalānaṃ kathinatthārā na ruhanti? katinaṃ puggalānaṃ kathinatthārā ruhanti? tiṇṇaṃ puggalānaṃ kathinatthārā na ruhanti| tiṇṇaṃ puggalānaṃ kathinatthārā ruhanti| katamaisaṃ tiṇṇaṃ puggalānaṃ kathinatthārā na ruhanti? nissīmaṭṭhau anumaudati, anumaudaintau na vācaṃ bhindati, vācaṃ bhindantau na paraṃ viñ‍ñāpaiti – imaisaṃ tiṇṇaṃ puggalānaṃ kathinatthārā na ruhanti| katamaisaṃ tiṇṇaṃ puggalānaṃ kathinatthārā ruhanti? sīmaṭṭhau anumaudati, anumaudaintau vācaṃ bhindati, vācaṃ bhindantau paraṃ viñ‍ñāpaiti – imaisaṃ tiṇṇaṃ puggalānaṃ kathinatthārā ruhanti|

411. kati kathinatthārā na ruhanti? kati kathinatthārā ruhanti? tayau kathinatthārā na ruhanti| tayau kathinatthārā ruhanti| katamai tayau kathinatthārā na ruhanti? vatthuvipan‍nañ‍caiva hauti, kālavipan‍nañ‍ca, karaṇavipan‍nañ‍ca – imai tayau kathinatthārā na ruhanti| katamai tayau kathinatthārā ruhanti? vatthusampan‍nañ‍caiva hauti, kālasampan‍nañ‍ca, karaṇasampan‍nañ‍ca – imai tayau kathinatthārā ruhanti|

4. kathinādijānitabbavibhāgau

412. kathinaṃ  jānitabbaṃ, kathinatthārau jānitabbau, kathinassa atthāramāsau jānitabbau, kathinassa atthāravipatti jānitabbā, kathinassa atthārasampatti jānitabbā, nimittakammaṃ jānitabbaṃ, parikathā jānitabbā, kuk‍kukataṃ jānitabbaṃ, san‍nidhi jānitabbā, nissaggiyaṃ  jānitabbaṃ|

kathinaṃ  jānitabbanti taisañ‍ñaiva dhammānaṃ saṅgahau samavāyau nāmaṃ nāmakammaṃ nāmadhaiyyaṃ nirutti byañ‍janaṃ abhilāpau yadidaṃ kathinanti|

kathinassa  atthāramāsau jānitabbauti vassānassa pacchimau māsau jānitabbau|

kathinassa atthāravipatti jānitabbāti catuvīsatiyā ākāraihi kathinassa atthāravipatti jānitabbā|

kathinassa atthārasampatti jānitabbāti sattarasahi ākāraihi kathinassa atthārasampatti jānitabbā|

nimittakammaṃ jānitabbanti nimittaṃ karauti iminā dussaina kathinaṃ attharissāmīti|

parikathā jānitabbāti parikathaṃ karauti imāya parikathāya kathinadussaṃ nibbattaissāmīti|

kuk‍kukataṃ jānitabbanti anādiyadānaṃ jānitabbaṃ|

san‍nidhi jānitabbāti dvai san‍nidhiyau jānitabbā – karaṇasan‍nidhi vā nicayasan‍nidhi vā|

nissaggiyaṃ jānitabbanti kariyamānai aruṇaṃ uṭṭhahati|

kathinatthārau jānitabbauti sacai saṅghassa kathinadussaṃ uppan‍naṃ hauti, saṅghaina kathaṃ paṭipaj‍jitabbaṃ, atthārakaina kathaṃ paṭipaj‍jitabbaṃ, anumaudakaina kathaṃ paṭipaj‍jitabbaṃ|

413. saṅghaina ñattidutiyaina kammaina kathinatthārakassa bhikkhunau dātabbaṃ, taina kathinatthārakaina bhikkhunā tadahaiva dhauvitvā vimaj‍jitvā vicāraitvā chinditvā sibbaitvā rajitvā kappaṃ katvā kathinaṃ attharitabbaṃ| sacai  saṅghāṭiyā kathinaṃ attharitukāmau hauti, paurāṇikā saṅghāṭi pac‍cuddharitabbā, navā saṅghāṭi adhiṭṭhātabbā| imāya saṅghāṭiyā kathinaṃ attharāmīti vācā bhinditabbā| sacai uttarāsaṅgaina kathinaṃ attharitukāmau hauti, paurāṇakau uttarāsaṅgau pac‍cuddharitabbau, navau uttarāsaṅgau adhiṭṭhātabbau| iminā uttarāsaṅgaina kathinaṃ attharāmīti vācā bhinditabbā| sacai antaravāsakaina kathinaṃ attharitukāmau hauti, paurāṇakau antaravāsakau pac‍cuddharitabbau, navau antaravāsakau adhiṭṭhātabbau| iminā antaravāsakaina kathinaṃ attharāmīti vācā bhinditabbā| taina kathinatthārakaina bhikkhunā saṅghaṃ upasaṅkamitvā aikaṃsaṃ uttarāsaṅgaṃ  karitvā añ‍jaliṃ paggahaitvā aivamassa vacanīyau – ‘‘atthataṃ, bhantai, saṅghassa kathinaṃ, dhammikau kathinatthārau, anumaudathā’’ti| taihi anumaudakaihi bhikkhūhi aikaṃsaṃ uttarāsaṅgaṃ karitvā añ‍jaliṃ  paggahaitvā aivamassa vacanīyau – ‘‘atthataṃ, āvusau, saṅghassa kathinaṃ, dhammikau kathinatthārau, anumaudāmā’’ti| taina kathinatthārakaina bhikkhunā sambahulai bhikkhū upasaṅkamitvā aikaṃsaṃ uttarāsaṅgaṃ karitvā añ‍jaliṃ  paggahaitvā aivamassu vacanīyā – ‘‘atthataṃ, bhantai, saṅghassa kathinaṃ, dhammikau kathinatthārau, anumaudathā’’ti| taihi anumaudakaihi bhikkhūhi aikaṃsaṃ uttarāsaṅgaṃ karitvā añ‍jaliṃ paggahaitvā aivamassa vacanīyau – ‘‘atthataṃ, āvusau, saṅghassa kathinaṃ, dhammikau kathinatthārau anumaudāmā’’ti| taina kathinatthārakaina bhikkhunā aikaṃ bhikkhuṃ upasaṅkamitvā aikaṃsaṃ uttarāsaṅgaṃ karitvā añ‍jaliṃ paggahaitvā aivamassa vacanīyau – ‘‘atthataṃ, āvusau, saṅghassa kathinaṃ, dhammikau kathinatthārau, anumaudāhī’’ti| taina anumaudakaina bhikkhunā aikaṃsaṃ uttarāsaṅgaṃ karitvā añ‍jaliṃ paggahaitvā aivamassa vacanīyau – ‘‘atthataṃ, āvusau, saṅghassa kathinaṃ, dhammikau kathinatthārau, anumaudāmī’’ti|

5. puggalassaivakathinatthārau

414. saṅghau kathinaṃ attharati, gaṇau kathinaṃ attharati, puggalau kathinaṃ attharatīti| na saṅghau kathinaṃ attharati, na gaṇau kathinaṃ attharati, puggalau kathinaṃ attharatīti| hañ‍ci na saṅghau kathinaṃ attharati, na gaṇau kathinaṃ attharati, puggalau kathinaṃ attharati| saṅghassa anatthataṃ  hauti kathinaṃ, gaṇassa anatthataṃ hauti kathinaṃ, puggalassa atthataṃ hauti kathinaṃ| saṅghau pātimaukkhaṃ uddisati gaṇau pātimaukkhaṃ uddisati puggalau pātimaukkhaṃ uddisatīti na saṅghau pātimaukkhaṃ uddisati, na gaṇau pātimaukkhaṃ uddisati, puggalau pātimaukkhaṃ uddisatīti| hañ‍ci na saṅghau pātimaukkhaṃ uddisati, na gaṇau pātimaukkhaṃ uddisati, puggalau pātimaukkhaṃ uddisati| saṅghassa anuddiṭṭhaṃ hauti pātimaukkhaṃ, gaṇassa anuddiṭṭhaṃ hauti pātimaukkhaṃ, puggalassa uddiṭṭhaṃ hauti pātimaukkhaṃ| saṅghassa sāmaggiyā gaṇassa sāmaggiyā puggalassa uddaisā saṅghassa uddiṭṭhaṃ hauti pātimaukkhaṃ, gaṇassa uddiṭṭhaṃ hauti pātimaukkhaṃ, puggalassa uddiṭṭhaṃ hauti pātimaukkhaṃ| aivamaiva na saṅghau kathinaṃ attharati, na  gaṇau kathinaṃ attharati, puggalau kathinaṃ attharati| saṅghassa anumaudanāya gaṇassa anumaudanāya puggalassa atthārā saṅghassa atthataṃ hauti kathinaṃ, gaṇassa atthataṃ hauti kathinaṃ, puggalassa atthataṃ hauti kathinanti|

6. palibaudhapañhābyākaraṇaṃ

415.

 pak‍kamanantikau  kathinuddhārau, vuttau ādic‍cabandhunā|

aitañ‍ca tāhaṃ pucchāmi, katamau palibaudhau paṭhamaṃ chij‍jati||

pak‍kamanantikau  kathinuddhārau, vuttau ādic‍cabandhunā|

aitañ‍ca tāhaṃ vissaj‍jissaṃ, cīvarapalibaudhau paṭhamaṃ chij‍jati|

tassa saha bahisīmagamanā, āvāsapalibaudhau chij‍jati||

niṭṭhānantikau kathinuddhārau, vuttau ādic‍cabandhunā|

aitañ‍ca tāhaṃ pucchāmi, katamau palibaudhau paṭhamaṃ chij‍jati||

niṭṭhānantikau kathinuddhārau, vuttau ādic‍cabandhunā|

aitañ‍ca tāhaṃ vissaj‍jissaṃ, āvāsapalibaudhau  paṭhamaṃ chij‍jati|

cīvarai niṭṭhitai cīvarapalibaudhau chij‍jati||

san‍niṭṭhānantikau kathinuddhārau, vuttau ādic‍cabandhunā|

aitañ‍ca tāhaṃ pucchāmi, katamau palibaudhau paṭhamaṃ chij‍jati||

san‍niṭṭhānantikau kathinuddhārau, vuttau ādic‍cabandhunā|

aitañ‍ca tāhaṃ vissaj‍jissaṃ, dvai palibaudhā apubbaṃ acarimaṃ chij‍janti||

nāsanantikau  kathinuddhārau, vuttau ādic‍cabandhunā|

aitañ‍ca tāhaṃ pucchāmi, katamau palibaudhau paṭhamaṃ chij‍jati||

nāsanantikau kathinuddhārau, vuttau ādic‍cabandhunā|

aitañ‍ca tāhaṃ vissaj‍jissaṃ, āvāsapalibaudhau paṭhamaṃ chij‍jati|

cīvarai naṭṭhai cīvarapalibaudhau chij‍jati||

savanantikau  kathinuddhārau, vuttau ādic‍cabandhunā|

aitañ‍ca tāhaṃ pucchāmi, katamau palibaudhau paṭhamaṃ chij‍jati||

savanantikau kathinuddhārau, vuttau ādic‍cabandhunā|

aitañ‍ca tāhaṃ vissaj‍jissaṃ, cīvarapalibaudhau paṭhamaṃ chij‍jati|

tassa saha savanaina, āvāsapalibaudhau chij‍jati||

āsāvacchaidikau kathinuddhārau, vuttau ādic‍cabandhunā|

aitañ‍ca tāhaṃ pucchāmi, katamau palibaudhau paṭhamaṃ chij‍jati||

āsāvacchaidikau kathinuddhārau, vuttau ādic‍cabandhunā|

aitañ‍ca tāhaṃ vissaj‍jissaṃ, āvāsapalibaudhau paṭhamaṃ chij‍jati|

cīvarāsāya upacchin‍nāya cīvarapalibaudhau chij‍jati||

sīmātik‍kamanantikau kathinuddhārau, vuttau ādic‍cabandhunā|

aitañ‍ca tāhaṃ pucchāmi, katamau  palibaudhau paṭhamaṃ chij‍jati||

sīmātik‍kamanantikau kathinuddhārau, vuttau ādic‍cabandhunā|

aitañ‍ca tāhaṃ vissaj‍jissaṃ, cīvarapalibaudhau paṭhamaṃ chij‍jati|

tassa bahisīmai bahisīmagatassa (sī॰ syā॰) āvāsapalibaudhau chij‍jati||

sahubbhārau kathinuddhārau saubbhārau (ka॰), vuttau ādic‍cabandhunā|

aitañ‍ca tāhaṃ pucchāmi, katamau palibaudhau paṭhamaṃ chij‍jati||

sahubbhārau kathinuddhārau, vuttau ādic‍cabandhunā|

aitañ‍ca tāhaṃ vissaj‍jissaṃ, dvai palibaudhā apubbaṃ acarimaṃ chij‍jantīti||

416. kati kathinuddhārā saṅghādhīnā? kati kathinuddhārā puggalādhīnā? kati kathinuddhārā naiva saṅghādhīnā na puggalādhīnā? aikau kathinuddhārau saṅghādhīnau  – antarubbhārau| cattārau kathinuddhārā puggalādhīnā – pak‍kamanantikau, niṭṭhānantikau, san‍niṭṭhānantikau, sīmātik‍kamanantikau| cattārau kathinuddhārā naiva saṅghādhīnā na puggalādhīnā – nāsanantikau, savanantikau, āsāvacchaidikau, sahubbhārau| kati  kathinuddhārā antausīmāya uddhariyyanti? kati kathinuddhārā bahisīmāya uddhariyyanti? kati kathinuddhārā siyā antausīmāya uddhariyyanti siyā bahisīmāya uddhariyyanti? dvai kathinuddhārā antausīmāya uddhariyyanti – antarubbhārau, sahubbhārau| tayau kathinuddhārā bahisīmāya uddhariyyanti – pak‍kamanantikau, savanantikau, sīmātik‍kamanantikau| cattārau kathinuddhārā siyā antausīmāya uddhariyyanti siyā bahisīmāya uddhariyyanti – niṭṭhānantikau, san‍niṭṭhānantikau, nāsanantikau, āsāvacchaidikau|

kati kathinuddhārā aikuppādā aikaniraudhā? kati kathinuddhārā aikuppādā nānāniraudhā? dvai kathinuddhārā aikuppādā aikaniraudhā – antarubbhārau, sahubbhārau| avasaisā kathinuddhārā aikuppādā nānāniraudhāti|

kathinabhaidau niṭṭhitau|


tassuddānaṃ –

kassa kinti pan‍narasa, dhammā nidānahaitu ca|

pac‍cayasaṅgahamūlā, ādi ca atthārapuggalā aṭṭhapuggalā (sī॰)||

tiṇṇaṃ bhaidatau tiṇṇaṃ (ka॰) tayau jānitabbaṃ, atthāraṃ uddaisaina ca|

palibaudhādhinā, sīmāya uppādaniraudhaina cāti itau paraṃ ‘‘parivāraṃ niṭṭhitaṃ’’ itipāṭhau kaisuci pautthakaisu dissati||


upālipañ‍cakaṃ

1. anissitavaggau

417. taina  samayaina buddhau bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| atha khau āyasmā upāli yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau āyasmā upāli bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘katihi nu khau, bhantai, aṅgaihi saman‍nāgataina bhikkhunā yāvajīvaṃ nānissitaina vatthabba’’nti?

‘‘pañ‍cahupāli, aṅgaihi saman‍nāgataina bhikkhunā yāvajīvaṃ nānissitaina vatthabbaṃ| pari॰ 325 katamaihi pañ‍cahi? upausathaṃ na jānāti, upausathakammaṃ na jānāti, pātimaukkhaṃ na jānāti, pātimaukkhuddaisaṃ na jānāti, ūnapañ‍cavassau hauti – imaihi khau, upāli, pañ‍cahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgataina bhikkhunā yāvajīvaṃ nānissitaina vatthabbaṃ| pañ‍cahupāli, aṅgaihi saman‍nāgataina bhikkhunā yāvajīvaṃ anissitaina vatthabbaṃ| katamaihi pañ‍cahi? upausathaṃ jānāti, upausathakammaṃ jānāti, pātimaukkhaṃ jānāti, pātimaukkhuddaisaṃ jānāti, pañ‍cavassau vā hauti atiraikapañ‍cavassau vā – imaihi khau, upāli, pañ‍cahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgataina bhikkhunā yāvajīvaṃ anissitaina vatthabbaṃ|

‘‘aparaihipi, upāli, pañ‍cahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgataina bhikkhunā yāvajīvaṃ nānissitaina vatthabbaṃ| katamaihi pañ‍cahi? pavāraṇaṃ na jānāti, pavāraṇākammaṃ na jānāti, pātimaukkhaṃ  na jānāti, pātimaukkhuddaisaṃ na jānāti, ūnapañ‍cavassau hauti – imaihi khau, upāli, pañ‍cahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgataina bhikkhunā yāvajīvaṃ nānissitaina vatthabbaṃ| pañ‍cahupāli, aṅgaihi saman‍nāgataina bhikkhunā yāvajīvaṃ anissitaina vatthabbaṃ| katamaihi pañ‍cahi? pavāraṇaṃ jānāti, pavāraṇākammaṃ jānāti, pātimaukkhaṃ jānāti, pātimaukkhuddaisaṃ jānāti, pañ‍cavassau vā hauti atiraikapañ‍cavassau vā – imaihi khau, upāli, pañ‍cahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgataina bhikkhunā yāvajīvaṃ anissitaina vatthabbaṃ|

‘‘aparaihipi, upāli, pañ‍cahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgataina bhikkhunā yāvajīvaṃ nānissitaina vatthabbaṃ| katamaihi pañ‍cahi? āpattānāpattiṃ na jānāti, lahukagarukaṃ  āpattiṃ na jānāti, sāvasaisānavasaisaṃ āpattiṃ na jānāti, duṭṭhul‍lāduṭṭhul‍laṃ āpattiṃ na jānāti, ūnapañ‍cavassau hauti – imaihi khau, upāli, pañ‍cahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgataina bhikkhunā yāvajīvaṃ nānissitaina vatthabbaṃ| pañ‍cahupāli, aṅgaihi saman‍nāgataina bhikkhunā yāvajīvaṃ anissitaina vatthabbaṃ| katamaihi pañ‍cahi? āpattānāpattiṃ jānāti, lahukagarukaṃ āpattiṃ jānāti, sāvasaisānavasaisaṃ āpattiṃ jānāti, duṭṭhul‍lāduṭṭhul‍laṃ āpattiṃ jānāti, pañ‍cavassau vā hauti atiraikapañ‍cavassau vā – imaihi khau, upāli, pañ‍cahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgataina bhikkhunā yāvajīvaṃ anissitaina vatthabbaṃ’’|

418. ‘‘katihi  nu khau, bhantai, aṅgaihi saman‍nāgataina bhikkhunā na  upasampādaitabbaṃ, na nissayau dātabbau, na sāmaṇairau upaṭṭhāpaitabbau’’ti?

mahāva॰ 84 ‘‘pañ‍cahupāli, aṅgaihi saman‍nāgataina bhikkhunā na upasampādaitabbaṃ, na nissayau dātabbau, na sāmaṇairau upaṭṭhāpaitabbau| katamaihi pañ‍cahi? na paṭibalau hauti antaivāsiṃ vā saddhivihāriṃ vā gilānaṃ upaṭṭhātuṃ vā upaṭṭhāpaituṃ vā, anabhirataṃ vūpakāsaituṃ vā vūpakāsāpaituṃ vā, uppan‍naṃ kuk‍kuc‍caṃ dhammatau vinaudaituṃ imasmiṃ ṭhānai sabbattha ‘‘vinaudaituṃ vā vinaudāpaituṃ vā’’ti pāṭhau dissati, vimativinaudanīṭīkāya mahāvaggavaṇṇanā aulaukaitabbā, abhidhammai vinaituṃ, abhivinayai vinaituṃ – imaihi khau, upāli, pañ‍cahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgataina bhikkhunā na upasampādaitabbaṃ, na nissayau dātabbau, na sāmaṇairau upaṭṭhāpaitabbau| pañ‍cahupāli, aṅgaihi saman‍nāgataina bhikkhunā upasampādaitabbaṃ, nissayau dātabbau, sāmaṇairau upaṭṭhāpaitabbau| katamaihi pañ‍cahi? paṭibalau hauti antaivāsiṃ vā saddhivihāriṃ vā gilānaṃ upaṭṭhātuṃ vā upaṭṭhāpaituṃ vā, anabhirataṃ vūpakāsaituṃ vā vūpakāsāpaituṃ vā, uppan‍naṃ kuk‍kuc‍caṃ dhammatau vinaudaituṃ, abhidhammai vinaituṃ, abhivinayai vinaituṃ – imaihi khau, upāli, pañ‍cahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgataina bhikkhunā upasampādaitabbaṃ, nissayau dātabbau, sāmaṇairau upaṭṭhāpaitabbau|

‘‘aparaihipi, upāli, pañ‍cahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgataina bhikkhunā na upasampādaitabbaṃ, na nissayau  dātabbau, na sāmaṇairau upaṭṭhāpaitabbau| katamaihi pañ‍cahi? na paṭibalau hauti  antaivāsiṃ vā saddhivihāriṃ vā abhisamācārikāya sikkhāya sikkhāpaituṃ, ādibrahmacāriyakāya sikkhāya vinaituṃ, adhisīlai vinaituṃ, adhicittai vinaituṃ, adhipañ‍ñāya vinaituṃ – imaihi khau, upāli, pañ‍cahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgataina bhikkhunā na upasampādaitabbaṃ, na nissayau dātabbau, na sāmaṇairau upaṭṭhāpaitabbau| pañ‍cahupāli, aṅgaihi saman‍nāgataina bhikkhunā upasampādaitabbaṃ, nissayau dātabbau, sāmaṇairau upaṭṭhāpaitabbau| katamaihi pañ‍cahi? paṭibalau hauti antaivāsiṃ vā saddhivihāriṃ vā abhisamācārikāya sikkhāya sikkhāpaituṃ, ādibrahmacāriyakāya sikkhāya vinaituṃ, adhisīlai vinaituṃ, adhicittai vinaituṃ, adhipañ‍ñāya vinaituṃ – imaihi khau, upāli, pañ‍cahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgataina bhikkhunā upasampādaitabbaṃ, nissayau dātabbau, sāmaṇairau upaṭṭhāpaitabbau’’ti|

419. ‘‘katihi nu khau, bhantai, aṅgaihi saman‍nāgatassa bhikkhunau kammaṃ kātabba’’nti?

pañ‍cahupāli, aṅgaihi saman‍nāgatassa bhikkhunau kammaṃ kātabbaṃ| katamaihi pañ‍cahi? alaj‍jī ca hauti, bālau ca, apakatattau ca, micchādiṭṭhikau ca hauti, ājīvavipan‍nau ca – imaihi khau, upāli, pañ‍cahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgatassa bhikkhunau kammaṃ kātabbaṃ|

‘‘aparaihipi, upāli, pañ‍cahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgatassa bhikkhunau kammaṃ kātabbaṃ| katamaihi pañ‍cahi? adhisīlai sīlavipan‍nau hauti, ajjhācārai ācāravipan‍nau hauti, atidiṭṭhiyā diṭṭhivipan‍nau hauti, micchādiṭṭhikau ca hauti, ājīvavipan‍nau ca – imaihi khau, upāli, pañ‍cahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgatassa bhikkhunau kammaṃ kātabbaṃ|

‘‘aparaihipi, upāli, pañ‍cahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgatassa bhikkhunau kammaṃ kātabbaṃ| katamaihi pañ‍cahi? kāyikaina davaina saman‍nāgatau hauti, vācasikaina davaina saman‍nāgatau hauti, kāyikavācasikaina davaina saman‍nāgatau hauti, micchādiṭṭhikau  ca hauti, ājīvavipan‍nau ca – imaihi khau, upāli, pañ‍cahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgatassa bhikkhunau kammaṃ kātabbaṃ|

‘‘aparaihipi, upāli, pañ‍cahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgatassa bhikkhunau kammaṃ kātabbaṃ| katamaihi pañ‍cahi? kāyikaina anācāraina saman‍nāgatau hauti, vācasikaina anācāraina saman‍nāgatau hauti, kāyikavācasikaina anācāraina saman‍nāgatau hauti, micchādiṭṭhikau ca hauti, ājīvavipan‍nau ca – imaihi khau, upāli, pañ‍cahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgatassa bhikkhunau kammaṃ kātabbaṃ|

‘‘aparaihipi, upāli, pañ‍cahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgatassa bhikkhunau kammaṃ kātabbaṃ| katamaihi pañ‍cahi? kāyikaina upaghātikaina saman‍nāgatau hauti, vācasikaina upaghātikaina saman‍nāgatau hauti, kāyikavācasikaina upaghātikaina saman‍nāgatau hauti, micchādiṭṭhikau ca hauti, ājīvavipan‍nau ca – imaihi khau, upāli, pañ‍cahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgatassa bhikkhunau kammaṃ kātabbaṃ|

‘‘aparaihipi, upāli, pañ‍cahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgatassa bhikkhunau kammaṃ kātabbaṃ| katamaihi pañ‍cahi? kāyikaina micchājīvaina saman‍nāgatau  hauti, vācasikaina micchājīvaina saman‍nāgatau hauti, kāyikavācasikaina micchājīvaina saman‍nāgatau hauti, micchādiṭṭhikau ca hauti, ājīvavipan‍nau ca – imaihi khau, upāli, pañ‍cahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgatassa bhikkhunau kammaṃ kātabbaṃ|

‘‘aparaihipi, upāli, pañ‍cahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgatassa bhikkhunau kammaṃ kātabbaṃ| katamaihi pañ‍cahi? āpattiṃ āpan‍nau kammakatau upasampādaiti, nissayaṃ daiti, sāmaṇairaṃ upaṭṭhāpaiti, bhikkhunauvādakasammutiṃ sādiyati, sammataupi bhikkhuniyau auvadati – imaihi khau, upāli, pañ‍cahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgatassa bhikkhunau kammaṃ kātabbaṃ|

‘‘aparaihipi, upāli, pañ‍cahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgatassa bhikkhunau kammaṃ kātabbaṃ| katamaihi pañ‍cahi? yāya āpattiyā saṅghaina kammaṃ kataṃ hauti taṃ āpattiṃ āpaj‍jati, añ‍ñaṃ vā tādisikaṃ, tatau vā pāpiṭṭhataraṃ, kammaṃ garahati, kammikai garahati – imaihi khau, upāli, pañ‍cahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgatassa bhikkhunau kammaṃ kātabbaṃ|

‘‘aparaihipi, upāli, pañ‍cahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgatassa bhikkhunau kammaṃ kātabbaṃ| katamaihi pañ‍cahi? buddhassa avaṇṇaṃ bhāsati, dhammassa avaṇṇaṃ bhāsati, saṅghassa avaṇṇaṃ bhāsati, micchādiṭṭhikau ca hauti, ājīvavipan‍nau ca – imaihi khau, upāli, pañ‍cahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgatassa bhikkhunau kammaṃ kātabba’’nti|

anissitavaggau niṭṭhitau paṭhamau|

tassuddānaṃ –

upausathaṃ  pavāraṇaṃ, āpatti ca gilānakaṃ|

abhisamācāralaj‍jī ca, adhisīlai davaina ca||

anācāraṃ upaghāti, micchā āpattimaiva ca|

yāyāpattiyā buddhassa, paṭhamau vaggasaṅgahauti||

2. nappaṭippassambhanavaggau

420. ‘‘katihi nu khau, bhantai, aṅgaihi saman‍nāgatassa bhikkhunau kammaṃ nappaṭippassambhaitabba’’nti?

‘‘pañ‍cahupāli, aṅgaihi saman‍nāgatassa bhikkhunau kammaṃ nappaṭippassambhaitabbaṃ| katamaihi pañ‍cahi? āpattiṃ āpan‍nau kammakatau upasampādaiti, nissayaṃ daiti, sāmaṇairaṃ upaṭṭhāpaiti, bhikkhunauvādakasammutiṃ sādiyati, sammataupi bhikkhuniyau auvadati – imaihi khau, upāli, pañ‍cahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgatassa bhikkhunau kammaṃ nappaṭippassambhaitabbaṃ|

cūḷava॰ 8-9, 18, 30-31, 43, 53, 62, 72 ‘‘aparaihipi, upāli, pañ‍cahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgatassa bhikkhunau kammaṃ nappaṭippassambhaitabbaṃ| katamaihi pañ‍cahi? yāya āpattiyā saṅghaina kammaṃ kataṃ hauti taṃ āpattiṃ āpaj‍jati, añ‍ñaṃ vā tādisikaṃ, tatau vā pāpiṭṭhataraṃ, kammaṃ garahati, kammikai garahati – imaihi khau, upāli, pañ‍cahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgatassa bhikkhunau kammaṃ nappaṭippassambhaitabbaṃ|

‘‘aparaihipi, upāli, pañ‍cahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgatassa bhikkhunau kammaṃ nappaṭippassambhaitabbaṃ| katamaihi pañ‍cahi? buddhassa avaṇṇaṃ bhāsati, dhammassa avaṇṇaṃ bhāsati, saṅghassa avaṇṇaṃ bhāsati, micchādiṭṭhikau ca  hauti, ājīvavipan‍nau  ca – imaihi khau, upāli, pañ‍cahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgatassa bhikkhunau kammaṃ nappaṭippassambhaitabbaṃ|

‘‘aparaihipi, upāli, pañ‍cahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgatassa bhikkhunau kammaṃ nappaṭippassambhaitabbaṃ| katamaihi pañ‍cahi? alaj‍jī ca hauti, bālau ca, apakatattau ca, aumaddakārakau ca hauti, vattaisu  sikkhāya ca na paripūrakārī – imaihi khau, upāli, pañ‍cahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgatassa bhikkhunau kammaṃ nappaṭippassambhaitabba’’nti|

421. ‘‘saṅgāmāvacaraina, bhantai, bhikkhunā saṅghaṃ upasaṅkamantaina kati dhammai ajjhattaṃ upaṭṭhāpaitvā saṅghau upasaṅkamitabbau’’ti?

pari॰ 365 ‘‘saṅgāmāvacaraina, upāli, bhikkhunā saṅghaṃ upasaṅkamantaina pañ‍ca dhammai ajjhattaṃ upaṭṭhāpaitvā saṅghau upasaṅkamitabbau| katamai pañ‍ca? saṅgāmāvacaraina, upāli, bhikkhunā saṅghaṃ upasaṅkamantaina nīcacittaina saṅghau upasaṅkamitabbau, rajauharaṇasamaina cittaina, āsanakusalaina bhavitabbaṃ nissaj‍jakusalaina, thairai bhikkhū anupakhaj‍jantaina, navai bhikkhū āsanaina appaṭibāhantaina yathāpatirūpai āsanai nisīditabbaṃ, anānākathikaina bhavitabbaṃ atiracchānakathikaina, sāmaṃ vā dhammau bhāsitabbau, parau vā ajjhaisitabbau, ariyau vā tuṇhibhāvau nātimañ‍ñitabbau, sacai, upāli, saṅghau samaggakaraṇīyāni kammāni karauti tatra cai, upāli, bhikkhunau nakkhamati, api diṭṭhāvikammaṃ katvā ñāpaitabbā sāmaggī| taṃ kissahaitu? māhaṃ saṅghaina nānattau assanti| saṅgāmāvacarainupāli, bhikkhunā saṅghaṃ upasaṅkamantaina imai pañ‍ca dhammai ajjhattaṃ upaṭṭhāpaitvā saṅghau upasaṅkamitabbau’’ti|

422. ‘‘katihi nu khau, bhantai, aṅgaihi saman‍nāgatau bhikkhu saṅghai vauharantau bahujanaakantau ca hauti bahujanaamanāpau ca bahujanaarucitau cā’’ti?

‘‘pañ‍cahupāli, aṅgaihi saman‍nāgatau bhikkhu saṅghai vauharantau bahujanaakantau ca hauti bahujanaamanāpau ca bahujanaarucitau ca| katamaihi pañ‍cahi? ussitamantī ca hauti, nissitajappī ca, na ca bhāsānusandhikusalau  hauti, na yathādhammai yathāvinayai yathāpattiyā caudaitā hauti, na yathādhammai yathāvinayai yathāpattiyā kāraitā hauti – imaihi khau, upāli, pañ‍cahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgatau bhikkhu saṅghai vauharantau bahujanaakantau ca hauti bahujanaamanāpau ca bahujanaarucitau ca| pañ‍cahupāli, aṅgaihi saman‍nāgatau bhikkhu saṅghai vauharantau bahujanakantau ca hauti bahujanamanāpau ca bahujanaarucitau ca| katamaihi pañ‍cahi? na ussitamantī ca hauti, na nissitajappī ca, bhāsānusandhikusalau ca hauti, yathādhammai yathāvinayai yathāpattiyā caudaitā hauti, yathādhammai yathāvinayai yathāpattiyā kāraitā hauti – imaihi khau, upāli, pañ‍cahaṅgaihi  saman‍nāgatau bhikkhu saṅghai vauharantau bahujanakantau ca hauti bahujanamanāpau ca bahujanarucitau ca|

‘‘aparaihipi, upāli, pañ‍cahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgatau bhikkhu saṅghai vauharantau bahujanaakantau ca hauti bahujanaamanāpau ca  bahujanaarucitau ca| katamaihi pañ‍cahi? ussādaitā ca hauti, apasādaitā ca, adhammaṃ gaṇhāti, dhammaṃ paṭibāhati, samphañ‍ca bahuṃ bhāsati – imaihi khau, upāli, pañ‍cahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgatau bhikkhu saṅghai vauharantau bahujanaakantau ca hauti bahujanaamanāpau ca bahujanaarucitau ca| pañ‍cahupāli, aṅgaihi saman‍nāgatau bhikkhu saṅghai vauharantau bahujanakantau ca hauti bahujanamanāpau ca bahujanarucitau  ca| katamaihi pañ‍cahi? na ussādaitā ca hauti, na apasādaitā ca, dhammaṃ gaṇhāti, adhammaṃ paṭibāhati, samphañ‍ca na bahuṃ bhāsati – imaihi khau, upāli, pañ‍cahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgatau bhikkhu saṅghai vauharantau bahujanakantau ca hauti bahujanamanāpau ca bahujanarucitau ca|

‘‘aparaihipi, upāli, pañ‍cahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgatau bhikkhu saṅghai vauharantau bahujanaakantau ca hauti bahujanaamanāpau ca bahujanaarucitau ca| katamaihi pañ‍cahi? pasayhapavattā hauti, anaukāsakammaṃ kāraitvā pavattā hauti, na yathādhammai yathāvinayai yathāpattiyā caudaitā hauti, na yathādhammai yathāvinayai yathāpattiyā kāraitā hauti, na yathādiṭṭhiyā byākatā hauti – imaihi khau, upāli, pañ‍cahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgatau bhikkhu saṅghai vauharantau bahujanaakantau ca hauti bahujanaamanāpau  ca bahujanaarucitau ca| pañ‍cahupāli, aṅgaihi saman‍nāgatau bhikkhu saṅghai vauharantau bahujanakantau ca hauti bahujanamanāpau ca bahujanarucitau ca| katamaihi pañ‍cahi? na  pasayhapavattā hauti, aukāsakammaṃ kāraitvā pavattā hauti, yathādhammai yathāvinayai yathāpattiyā caudaitā hauti, yathādhammai yathāvinayai yathāpattiyā kāraitā hauti, yathādiṭṭhiyā byākatā hauti – imaihi khau, upāli, pañ‍cahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgatau bhikkhu saṅghai vauharantau bahujanakantau ca hauti bahujanamanāpau ca bahujanarucitau cā’’ti|

423. ‘‘kati nu khau, bhantai, ānisaṃsā vinayapariyattiyā’’ti?

‘‘pañ‍cimai, upāli, ānisaṃsā vinayapariyattiyā| katamai pañ‍ca? attanau sīlakkhandhau suguttau hauti surakkhitau, kuk‍kuc‍capakatānaṃ paṭisaraṇaṃ hauti, visāradau saṅghamajjhai  vauharati, pac‍catthikai sahadhammaina suniggahitaṃ niggaṇhāti, saddhammaṭṭhitiyā paṭipan‍nau hauti – imaihi khau, upāli, pañ‍cānisaṃsā vinayapariyattiyā’’|

nappaṭippassambhanavaggau niṭṭhitau dutiyau|

tassuddānaṃ –

āpan‍nau yāyavaṇṇañ‍ca, alaj‍jī saṅgāmaina ca|

ussitā ussādaitā ca, pasayha pariyattiyāti||

paṭhamayamakapañ‍ñatti|

3. vauhāravaggau

424. ‘‘katihi  nu khau, bhantai, aṅgaihi saman‍nāgataina bhikkhunā saṅghai na vauharitabba’’nti?

‘‘pañ‍cahupāli, aṅgaihi saman‍nāgataina bhikkhunā saṅghai na vauharitabbaṃ| katamaihi pañ‍cahi? āpattiṃ na jānāti, āpattisamuṭṭhānaṃ na jānāti, āpattiyā payaugaṃ na  jānāti, āpattiyā vūpasamaṃ na jānāti, āpattiyā na vinicchayakusalau hauti – imaihi khau, upāli, pañ‍cahaṅgaihi  saman‍nāgataina bhikkhunā saṅghai na vauharitabbaṃ| pañ‍cahupāli, aṅgaihi saman‍nāgataina bhikkhunā saṅghai vauharitabbaṃ| katamaihi pañ‍cahi? āpattiṃ jānāti, āpattisamuṭṭhānaṃ jānāti, āpattiyā payaugaṃ jānāti, āpattiyā vūpasamaṃ jānāti, āpattiyā vinicchayakusalau hauti – imaihi khau, upāli, pañ‍cahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgataina bhikkhunā saṅghai vauharitabbaṃ|

‘‘aparaihipi, upāli, pañ‍cahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgataina bhikkhunā saṅghai na vauharitabbaṃ| katamaihi pañ‍cahi? adhikaraṇaṃ na jānāti, adhikaraṇasamuṭṭhānaṃ na jānāti, adhikaraṇassa payaugaṃ na jānāti, adhikaraṇassa vūpasamaṃ na jānāti, adhikaraṇassa na vinicchayakusalau hauti – imaihi khau, upāli, pañ‍cahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgataina bhikkhunā saṅghai na vauharitabbaṃ| pañ‍cahupāli, aṅgaihi saman‍nāgataina bhikkhunā saṅghai vauharitabbaṃ| katamaihi pañ‍cahi? adhikaraṇaṃ jānāti, adhikaraṇasamuṭṭhānaṃ jānāti, adhikaraṇassa payaugaṃ jānāti, adhikaraṇassa vūpasamaṃ jānāti, adhikaraṇassa vinicchayakusalau hauti – imaihi khau, upāli pañ‍cahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgataina bhikkhunā saṅghai vauharitabbaṃ|

‘‘aparaihipi, upāli, pañ‍cahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgataina  bhikkhunā saṅghai na vauharitabbaṃ| katamaihi pañ‍cahi? pasayhapavattā hauti, anaukāsakammaṃ kāraitvā pavattā hauti, na yathādhammai yathāvinayai yathāpattiyā caudaitā hauti, na yathādhammai yathāvinayai yathāpattiyā kāraitā hauti, na yathādiṭṭhiyā byākatā hauti – imaihi khau, upāli, pañ‍cahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgataina bhikkhunā saṅghai na vauharitabbaṃ| pañ‍cahupāli, aṅgaihi saman‍nāgataina bhikkhunā saṅghai vauharitabbaṃ| katamaihi pañ‍cahi? na pasayhapavattā hauti, aukāsakammaṃ kāraitvā pavattā hauti, yathādhammai yathāvinayai yathāpattiyā caudaitā hauti, yathādhammai yathāvinayai yathāpattiyā kāraitā hauti, yathādiṭṭhiyā byākatā hauti – imaihi khau, upāli, pañ‍cahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgataina bhikkhunā saṅghai vauharitabbaṃ|

‘‘aparaihipi, upāli, pañ‍cahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgataina bhikkhunā saṅghai na vauharitabbaṃ| katamaihi pañ‍cahi? āpattānāpattiṃ na jānāti, lahukagarukaṃ āpattiṃ na jānāti, sāvasaisānavasaisaṃ āpattiṃ na jānāti, duṭṭhul‍lāduṭṭhul‍laṃ  āpattiṃ na jānāti, sappaṭikammaṃ appaṭikammaṃ āpattiṃ na jānāti – imaihi  khau, upāli, pañ‍cahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgataina bhikkhunā saṅghai na vauharitabbaṃ| pañ‍cahupāli, aṅgaihi saman‍nāgataina bhikkhunā saṅghai vauharitabbaṃ| katamaihi pañ‍cahi? āpattānāpattiṃ jānāti, lahukagarukaṃ āpattiṃ jānāti, sāvasaisānavasaisaṃ āpattiṃ jānāti, duṭṭhul‍lāduṭṭhul‍laṃ āpattiṃ jānāti, sappaṭikammaṃ appaṭikammaṃ āpattiṃ jānāti – imaihi khau, upāli, pañ‍cahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgataina bhikkhunā saṅghai vauharitabbaṃ|

‘‘aparaihipi, upāli, pañ‍cahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgataina bhikkhunā saṅghai na vauharitabbaṃ| katamaihi pañ‍cahi? kammaṃ na jānāti, kammassa karaṇaṃ na jānāti, kammassa vatthuṃ na jānāti, kammassa vattaṃ na jānāti, kammassa vūpasamaṃ na jānāti – imaihi khau, upāli, pañ‍cahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgataina bhikkhunā saṅghai na vauharitabbaṃ| pañ‍cahupāli, aṅgaihi saman‍nāgataina bhikkhunā saṅghai vauharitabbaṃ| katamaihi pañ‍cahi? kammaṃ jānāti, kammassa karaṇaṃ jānāti, kammassa vatthuṃ jānāti, kammassa vattaṃ jānāti, kammassa vūpasamaṃ jānāti – imaihi khau, upāli, pañ‍cahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgataina bhikkhunā saṅghai vauharitabbaṃ|

‘‘aparaihipi, upāli, pañ‍cahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgataina bhikkhunā saṅghai na vauharitabbaṃ| katamaihi pañ‍cahi? vatthuṃ na jānāti, nidānaṃ na jānāti, pañ‍ñattiṃ na jānāti, padapac‍cābhaṭṭhaṃ na jānāti, anusandhivacanapathaṃ na jānāti – imaihi khau, upāli, pañ‍cahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgataina bhikkhunā saṅghai na vauharitabbaṃ| pañ‍cahupāli, aṅgaihi saman‍nāgataina bhikkhunā saṅghai vauharitabbaṃ| katamaihi pañ‍cahi? vatthuṃ jānāti, nidānaṃ jānāti, pañ‍ñattiṃ jānāti, padapac‍cābhaṭṭhaṃ jānāti, anusandhivacanapathaṃ jānāti – imaihi khau, upāli, pañ‍cahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgataina bhikkhunā saṅghai vauharitabbaṃ|

‘‘aparaihipi, upāli, pañ‍cahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgataina bhikkhunā saṅghai na vauharitabbaṃ| katamaihi pañ‍cahi? chandāgatiṃ gacchati, dausāgatiṃ gacchati, mauhāgatiṃ gacchati, bhayāgatiṃ gacchati, alaj‍jī ca hauti – imaihi khau, upāli, pañ‍cahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgataina bhikkhunā saṅghai na vauharitabbaṃ| pañ‍cahupāli, aṅgaihi saman‍nāgataina bhikkhunā saṅghai vauharitabbaṃ| katamaihi pañ‍cahi? na chandāgatiṃ gacchati, na dausāgatiṃ gacchati, na mauhāgatiṃ  gacchati, na bhayāgatiṃ gacchati, laj‍jī ca hauti – imaihi khau, upāli, pañ‍cahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgataina bhikkhunā saṅghai vauharitabbaṃ|

‘‘aparaihipi, upāli, pañ‍cahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgataina bhikkhunā saṅghai na vauharitabbaṃ| katamaihi pañ‍cahi? chandāgatiṃ gacchati, dausāgatiṃ gacchati, mauhāgatiṃ gacchati, bhayāgatiṃ gacchati, akusalau ca hauti vinayai – imaihi khau, upāli, pañ‍cahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgataina bhikkhunā saṅghai na vauharitabbaṃ| pañ‍cahupāli, aṅgaihi saman‍nāgataina bhikkhunā saṅghai vauharitabbaṃ| katamaihi pañ‍cahi? na chandāgatiṃ gacchati, na dausāgatiṃ gacchati, na mauhāgatiṃ gacchati, na bhayāgatiṃ gacchati, kusalau ca hauti vinayai – imaihi khau, upāli, pañ‍cahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgataina bhikkhunā saṅghai vauharitabbaṃ|

‘‘aparaihipi, upāli, pañ‍cahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgataina bhikkhunā saṅghai na vauharitabbaṃ| katamaihi pañ‍cahi? ñattiṃ na jānāti, ñattiyā karaṇaṃ na jānāti, ñattiyā anussāvanaṃ na jānāti, ñattiyā samathaṃ na jānāti, ñattiyā vūpasamaṃ na jānāti – imaihi khau, upāli, pañ‍cahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgataina bhikkhunā saṅghai na vauharitabbaṃ| pañ‍cahupāli, aṅgaihi saman‍nāgataina bhikkhunā saṅghai vauharitabbaṃ| katamaihi pañ‍cahi? ñattiṃ jānāti, ñattiyā karaṇaṃ  jānāti, ñattiyā anussāvanaṃ jānāti, ñattiyā samathaṃ jānāti, ñattiyā vūpasamaṃ jānāti – imaihi khau, upāli, pañ‍cahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgataina bhikkhunā saṅghai vauharitabbaṃ|

‘‘aparaihipi, upāli, pañ‍cahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgataina bhikkhunā saṅghai na vauharitabbaṃ| katamaihi pañ‍cahi? suttaṃ na jānāti, suttānulaumaṃ na jānāti, vinayaṃ na jānāti, vinayānulaumaṃ na jānāti, na ca ṭhānāṭhānakusalau hauti – imaihi khau, upāli, pañ‍cahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgataina bhikkhunā saṅghai na vauharitabbaṃ| pañ‍cahupāli, aṅgaihi saman‍nāgataina bhikkhunā saṅghai vauharitabbaṃ| katamaihi pañ‍cahi? suttaṃ jānāti, suttānulaumaṃ jānāti, vinayaṃ jānāti, vinayānulaumaṃ jānāti, ṭhānāṭhānakusalau ca hauti – imaihi khau, upāli, pañ‍cahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgataina bhikkhunā saṅghai vauharitabbaṃ|

‘‘aparaihipi, upāli, pañ‍cahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgataina bhikkhunā saṅghai na vauharitabbaṃ| katamaihi pañ‍cahi? dhammaṃ na jānāti, dhammānulaumaṃ na jānāti, vinayaṃ na jānāti, vinayānulaumaṃ na jānāti, na ca pubbāparakusalau hauti – imaihi khau, upāli, pañ‍cahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgataina bhikkhunā saṅghai na vauharitabbaṃ| pañ‍cahupāli, aṅgaihi saman‍nāgataina bhikkhunā saṅghai vauharitabbaṃ| katamaihi  pañ‍cahi? dhammaṃ jānāti, dhammānulaumaṃ jānāti, vinayaṃ jānāti, vinayānulaumaṃ jānāti, pubbāparakusalau ca hauti – imaihi  khau, upāli, pañ‍cahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgataina bhikkhunā saṅghai vauharitabba’’nti|

vauhāravaggau niṭṭhitau tatiyau|

tassuddānaṃ –

āpatti adhikaraṇaṃ, pasayhāpatti jānanā|

kammaṃ vatthuṃ alaj‍jī ca, akusalau ca ñattiyā|

suttaṃ na jānāti dhammaṃ, tatiyau vaggasaṅgahauti||

4. diṭṭhāvikammavaggau

425. ‘‘kati nu khau, bhantai, adhammikā diṭṭhāvikammā’’ti? ‘‘pañ‍cimai, upāli, adhammikā diṭṭhāvikammā| katamai pañ‍ca? anāpattiyā diṭṭhiṃ āvi karauti, adaisanāgāminiyā  āpattiyā diṭṭhiṃ āvi karauti, daisitāya āpattiyā diṭṭhiṃ āvi karauti, catūhi pañ‍cahi diṭṭhiṃ āvi karauti, manau mānasaina diṭṭhiṃ āvi karauti – imai khau, upāli, pañ‍ca adhammikā diṭṭhāvikammā|

‘‘pañ‍cimai, upāli, dhammikā diṭṭhāvikammā| katamai pañ‍ca? āpattiyā diṭṭhiṃ āvi karauti, daisanāgāminiyā āpattiyā diṭṭhiṃ āvi karauti, adaisitāya āpattiyā diṭṭhiṃ āvi karauti, na catūhi pañ‍cahi diṭṭhiṃ āvi karauti, na manau mānasaina diṭṭhiṃ āvi karauti – imai khau, upāli, pañ‍ca dhammikā diṭṭhāvikammā|

‘‘aparaipi, upāli, pañ‍ca adhammikā diṭṭhāvikammā| katamai  pañ‍ca? nānāsaṃvāsakassa santikai diṭṭhiṃ āvi karauti, nānāsīmāya ṭhitassa santikai diṭṭhiṃ āvi karauti, apakatattassa santikai diṭṭhiṃ āvi karauti, catūhi pañ‍cahi diṭṭhiṃ āvi karauti, manau mānasaina diṭṭhiṃ āvi karauti – imai khau, upāli, pañ‍ca adhammikā diṭṭhāvikammā|

‘‘pañ‍cimai, upāli, dhammikā diṭṭhāvikammā| katamai pañ‍ca? samānasaṃvāsakassa santikai diṭṭhiṃ āvi karauti, samānasīmāya ṭhitassa santikai diṭṭhiṃ āvi karauti, pakatattassa santikai diṭṭhiṃ āvi karauti, na catūhi pañ‍cahi diṭṭhiṃ āvi karauti, na manau mānasaina diṭṭhiṃ āvi karauti – imai khau, upāli, pañ‍ca dhammikā diṭṭhāvikammāti|

426. ‘‘kati nu khau, bhantai, adhammikā paṭiggahā’’ti? ‘‘pañ‍cimai, upāli, adhammikā paṭiggahā| katamai pañ‍ca? kāyaina diyyamānaṃ kāyaina appaṭiggahitaṃ, kāyaina diyyamānaṃ kāyappaṭibaddhaina appaṭiggahitaṃ, kāyappaṭibaddhaina diyyamānaṃ kāyaina appaṭiggahitaṃ, kāyappaṭibaddhaina diyyamānaṃ kāyappaṭibaddhaina appaṭiggahitaṃ, nissaggiyaina diyyamānaṃ kāyaina vā kāyappaṭibaddhaina vā appaṭiggahitaṃ – imai khau, upāli, pañ‍ca adhammikā paṭiggahā|

‘‘pañ‍cimai, upāli, dhammikā paṭiggahā| katamai pañ‍ca? kāyaina diyyamānaṃ kāyaina paṭiggahitaṃ, kāyaina diyyamānaṃ kāyappaṭibaddhaina paṭiggahitaṃ, kāyappaṭibaddhaina diyyamānaṃ kāyaina paṭiggahitaṃ, kāyappaṭibaddhaina diyyamānaṃ kāyappaṭibaddhaina paṭiggahitaṃ, nissaggiyaina diyyamānaṃ kāyaina vā kāyappaṭibaddhaina vā paṭiggahitaṃ – imai  khau, upāli, pañ‍ca dhammikā paṭiggahā’’ti|

427. ‘‘kati  nu khau, bhantai, anatirittā’’ti? ‘‘pañ‍cimai, upāli, anatirittā| katamai pañ‍ca? pāci॰ 239 akappiyakataṃ hauti, appaṭiggahitakataṃ hauti anuc‍cāritakataṃ hauti, ahatthapāsai kataṃ hauti, alamaitaṃ sabbanti avuttaṃ hauti – imai khau, upāli, pañ‍ca anatirittā|

‘‘pañ‍cimai, upāli, atirittā| katamai pañ‍ca? pāci॰ 239 kappiyakataṃ hauti, paṭiggahitakataṃ hauti, uc‍cāritakataṃ hauti, hatthapāsai kataṃ hauti, alamaitaṃ sabbanti vuttaṃ hauti – imai khau, upāli, pañ‍ca atirittā’’ti|

428. ‘‘katihi  nu khau, bhantai, ākāraihi pavāraṇā pañ‍ñāyatī’’ti? ‘‘pañ‍cahupāli, ākāraihi pavāraṇā pañ‍ñāyati| katamaihi pañ‍cahi? pāci॰ 239 asanaṃ pañ‍ñāyati, bhaujanaṃ pañ‍ñāyati, hatthapāsai ṭhitau abhiharati, paṭikkhaipau pañ‍ñāyati – imaihi khau, upāli, pañ‍cahākāraihi pavāraṇā pañ‍ñāyatī’’ti|

429. ‘‘kati nu khau, bhantai, adhammikā paṭiñ‍ñātakaraṇā’’ti? ‘‘pañ‍cimai, upāli, adhammikā paṭiñ‍ñātakaraṇā| katamai pañ‍ca? bhikkhu pārājikaṃ ajjhāpan‍nau hauti, pārājikaina caudiyamānau saṅghādisaisaṃ ajjhāpan‍nau paṭijānāti, taṃ saṅghau saṅghādisaisaina kāraiti, adhammikaṃ paṭiñ‍ñātakaraṇaṃ| bhikkhu pārājikaṃ ajjhāpan‍nau hauti, pārājikaina caudiyamānau pācittiyaṃ…pai॰… pāṭidaisanīyaṃ… duk‍kaṭaṃ ajjhāpan‍nau paṭijānāti, taṃ saṅghau duk‍kaṭaina kāraiti, adhammikaṃ paṭiñ‍ñātakaraṇaṃ| bhikkhu saṅghādisaisaṃ…pai॰… pācittiyaṃ… pāṭidaisanīyaṃ… duk‍kaṭaṃ ajjhāpan‍nau hauti| duk‍kaṭaina caudiyamānau pārājikaṃ ajjhāpan‍nau paṭijānāti, taṃ saṅghau pārājikaina kāraiti, adhammikaṃ paṭiñ‍ñātakaraṇaṃ| bhikkhu duk‍kaṭaṃ ajjhāpan‍nau hauti, duk‍kaṭaina caudiyamānau saṅghādisaisaṃ…pai॰… pācittiyaṃ… pāṭidaisanīyaṃ ajjhāpan‍nau paṭijānāti, taṃ saṅghau pāṭidaisanīyaina kāraiti – adhammikaṃ paṭiñ‍ñātakaraṇaṃ| imai khau, upāli, pañ‍ca adhammikā paṭiñ‍ñātakaraṇā|

‘‘pañ‍cimai, upāli, dhammikā paṭiñ‍ñātakaraṇā| katamai pañ‍ca? bhikkhu pārājikaṃ ajjhāpan‍nau hauti, pārājikaina caudiyamānau pārājikaṃ ajjhāpan‍nau paṭijānāti, taṃ saṅghau pārājikaina kāraiti, dhammikaṃ paṭiñ‍ñātakaraṇaṃ| bhikkhu saṅghādisaisaṃ…pai॰… pācittiyaṃ… pāṭidaisanīyaṃ… duk‍kaṭaṃ ajjhāpan‍nau hauti, duk‍kaṭaina caudiyamānau duk‍kaṭaṃ ajjhāpan‍nau paṭijānāti, taṃ saṅghau duk‍kaṭaina kāraiti, dhammikaṃ paṭiñ‍ñātakaraṇaṃ| imai khau, upāli, pañ‍ca dhammikā paṭiñ‍ñātakaraṇā’’ti|

430. ‘‘katihi  nu khau, bhantai, aṅgaihi saman‍nāgatassa bhikkhunau aukāsakammaṃ kārāpaintassa nālaṃ aukāsakammaṃ kātu’’nti? ‘‘pañ‍cahupāli, aṅgaihi saman‍nāgatassa bhikkhunau aukāsakammaṃ kārāpaintassa nālaṃ aukāsakammaṃ  kātuṃ| katamaihi pañ‍cahi? alaj‍jī ca hauti, bālau ca, apakatattau ca, cāvanādhippāyau vattā hauti, nau vuṭṭhānādhippāyau – imaihi khau, upāli, pañ‍cahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgatassa bhikkhunau  aukāsakammaṃ kārāpaintassa nālaṃ aukāsakammaṃ kātuṃ|

‘‘pañ‍cahupāli, aṅgaihi saman‍nāgatassa bhikkhunau aukāsakammaṃ kārāpaintassa alaṃ aukāsakammaṃ kātuṃ| katamaihi pañ‍cahi? laj‍jī ca hauti, paṇḍitau ca, pakatattau ca, vuṭṭhānādhippāyau vattā hauti, nau cāvanādhippāyau – imaihi khau, upāli, pañ‍cahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgatassa bhikkhunau aukāsakammaṃ kārāpaintassa alaṃ aukāsakammaṃ kātu’’nti|

431. ‘‘katihi nu khau, bhantai, aṅgaihi saman‍nāgataina bhikkhunā saddhiṃ vinayau na sākacchitabbau’’ti? ‘‘pañ‍cahupāli, aṅgaihi saman‍nāgataina bhikkhunā saddhiṃ vinayau na sākacchitabbau| katamaihi pañ‍cahi? vatthuṃ na jānāti, nidānaṃ na jānāti, pañ‍ñattiṃ na jānāti, padapac‍cābhaṭṭhaṃ na jānāti, anusandhivacanapathaṃ na jānāti – imaihi khau, upāli, pañ‍cahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgataina bhikkhunā saddhiṃ vinayau na sākacchitabbau|

‘‘pañ‍cahupāli, aṅgaihi saman‍nāgataina bhikkhunā saddhiṃ vinayau sākacchitabbau| katamaihi pañ‍cahi? vatthuṃ jānāti, nidānaṃ jānāti, pañ‍ñattiṃ jānāti, padapac‍cābhaṭṭhaṃ jānāti, anusandhivacanapathaṃ jānāti – imaihi khau, upāli, pañ‍cahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgataina bhikkhunā saddhiṃ vinayau sākacchitabbau’’ti|

432. ‘‘kati nu khau, bhantai, pañhāpucchā’’ti? ‘‘pañ‍cimā, upāli, pañhāpucchā| katamā pañ‍ca? mandattā maumūhattā pañhaṃ pucchati, pāpicchau icchāpakatau pañhaṃ pucchati, paribhavā pañhaṃ pucchati, añ‍ñātukāmau pañhaṃ pucchati, sacai mai pañhaṃ puṭṭhau sammadaiva byākarissati  ic‍caitaṃ kusalaṃ nau cai pañhaṃ puṭṭhau sammadaiva byākarissati ahamassa sammadaiva byākarissāmīti pañhaṃ pucchati – imā khau, upāli, pañ‍ca pañhāpucchā’’ti|

433. ‘‘kati nu khau, bhantai, añ‍ñabyākaraṇā’’ti? ‘‘pañ‍cimai, upāli, añ‍ñabyākaraṇā| katamai pañ‍ca? mandattā maumūhattā añ‍ñaṃ byākarauti, pāpicchau icchāpakatau añ‍ñaṃ byākarauti, ummādā cittakkhaipā añ‍ñaṃ byākarauti, adhimānaina añ‍ñaṃ byākarauti, bhūtaṃ añ‍ñaṃ byākarauti – imai khau, upāli, pañ‍ca añ‍ñabyākaraṇā’’ti|

434. ‘‘kati nu khau, bhantai, visuddhiyau’’ti? ‘‘pañ‍cimā, upāli, visuddhiyau| katamā pañ‍ca? nidānaṃ uddisitvā avasaisaṃ sutaina sāvaitabbaṃ ayaṃ paṭhamā visuddhi, nidānaṃ uddisitvā cattāri pārājikāni uddisitvā avasaisaṃ sutaina sāvaitabbaṃ ayaṃ dutiyā visuddhi, nidānaṃ uddisitvā cattāri pārājikāni uddisitvā tairasa saṅghādisaisai uddisitvā avasaisaṃ sutaina sāvaitabbaṃ ayaṃ tatiyā visuddhi, nidānaṃ uddisitvā cattāri pārājikāni uddisitvā tairasa saṅghādisaisai uddisitvā dvai aniyatai uddisitvā avasaisaṃ sutaina sāvaitabbaṃ ayaṃ catutthā visuddhi, vitthārainaiva pañ‍camī – imā khau, upāli, pañ‍ca visuddhiyau’’ti|

435. ‘‘kati  nu khau, bhantai, bhaujanā’’ti? ‘‘pañ‍cimai, upāli, bhaujanā| katamai pañ‍ca? audanau, kummāsau, sattu, macchau, maṃsaṃ – imai khau, upāli, pañ‍ca bhaujanā’’ti|

diṭṭhāvikammavaggau niṭṭhitau catutthau|

tassuddānaṃ –

diṭṭhāvikammā aparai, paṭiggahānatirittā|

pavāraṇā paṭiñ‍ñātaṃ, aukāsaṃ sākacchaina ca|

pañhaṃ añ‍ñabyākaraṇā, visuddhi cāpi bhaujanāti||

5. attādānavaggau

436. cūḷava॰ 399; a॰ ni॰ 10.44 ‘‘caudakaina, bhantai, bhikkhunā paraṃ caudaitukāmaina kati dhammai ajjhattaṃ pac‍cavaikkhitvā parau caudaitabbau’’ti? ‘‘caudakainupāli, bhikkhunā paraṃ caudaitukāmaina pañ‍ca dhammai ajjhattaṃ  pac‍cavaikkhitvā parau caudaitabbau| katamai pañ‍ca? caudakainupāli, bhikkhunā paraṃ caudaitukāmaina aivaṃ pac‍cavaikkhitabbaṃ – parisuddhakāyasamācārau nu khaumhi, parisuddhainamhi kāyasamācāraina saman‍nāgatau acchiddaina appaṭimaṃsaina, saṃvij‍jati nu khau mai  aisau dhammau udāhu nau’’ti| nau cai, upāli, bhikkhu parisuddhakāyasamācārau hauti, parisuddhaina kāyasamācāraina saman‍nāgatau acchiddaina appaṭimaṃsaina, tassa bhavanti vattārau – ‘iṅgha, tāva āyasmā kāyikaṃ sikkhassū’ti itissa bhavanti vattārau|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, upāli, caudakaina bhikkhunā paraṃ caudaitukāmaina aivaṃ pac‍cavaikkhitabbaṃ – ‘parisuddhavacīsamācārau nu khaumhi, parisuddhainamhi vacīsamācāraina  saman‍nāgatau acchiddaina appaṭimaṃsaina, saṃvij‍jati nu khau mai aisau dhammau udāhu nau’ti| nau cai, upāli, bhikkhu parisuddhavacīsamācārau hauti, parisuddhaina vacīsamācāraina saman‍nāgatau acchiddaina appaṭimaṃsaina, tassa bhavanti vattārau – ‘iṅgha, tāva āyasmā vācasikaṃ sikkhassū’ti itissa bhavanti vattārau|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, upāli, caudakaina bhikkhunā paraṃ caudaitukāmaina aivaṃ pac‍cavaikkhitabbaṃ – ‘maittaṃ nu khau mai cittaṃ pac‍cupaṭṭhitaṃ sabrahmacārīsu anāghātaṃ, saṃvij‍jati nu khau mai aisau dhammau udāhu nau’ti| nau cai, upāli, bhikkhunau maittaṃ cittaṃ pac‍cupaṭṭhitaṃ hauti sabrahmacārīsu anāghātaṃ, tassa bhavanti vattārau – ‘iṅgha, tāva āyasmā sabrahmacārīsu maittaṃ cittaṃ upaṭṭhāpaihī’ti itissa bhavanti vattārau|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, upāli, caudakaina bhikkhunā paraṃ caudaitukāmaina aivaṃ pac‍cavaikkhitabbaṃ – ‘bahussutau nu khaumhi sutadharau sutasan‍nicayau, yai tai dhammā ādikalyāṇā majjhaikalyāṇā pariyausānakalyāṇā sātthaṃ sabyañ‍janaṃ kaivalaparipuṇṇaṃ parisuddhaṃ brahmacariyaṃ abhivadanti, tathārūpā mai dhammā bahussutā haunti dhātā vacasā paricitā manasānupaikkhitā diṭṭhiyā suppaṭividdhā, saṃvij‍jati nu khau mai aisau dhammau udāhu nau’ti| nau cai, upāli, bhikkhu bahussutau hauti sutadharau sutasan‍nicayau, yai tai dhammā ādikalyāṇā majjhaikalyāṇā pariyausānakalyāṇā sātthaṃ  sabyañ‍janaṃ kaivalaparipuṇṇaṃ parisuddhaṃ brahmacariyaṃ abhivadanti, tathārūpassa dhammā na bahussutā haunti dhātā vacasā paricitā manasānupaikkhitā diṭṭhiyā suppaṭividdhā, tassa bhavanti vattārau – ‘iṅgha, tāva āyasmā āgamaṃ pariyāpuṇassū’ti itissa bhavanti vattārau|

‘‘puna  caparaṃ, upāli, caudakaina bhikkhunā paraṃ caudaitukāmaina aivaṃ pac‍cavaikkhitabbaṃ – ‘ubhayāni khau mai pātimaukkhāni vitthāraina svāgatāni haunti suvibhattāni suppavattīni suvinicchitāni suttasau anubyañ‍janasau, saṃvij‍jati nu khau mai aisau dhammau udāhu nau’ti| nau cai, upāli, bhikkhunau ubhayāni pātimaukkhāni vitthāraina svāgatāni haunti suvibhattāni suppavattīni suvinicchitāni suttasau anubyañ‍janasau, ‘idaṃ panāvusau kattha vuttaṃ bhagavatā’ti iti puṭṭhau na sampāyati na sampādayati (ka॰), na sampādaiti (syā॰), tassa bhavanti vattārau – ‘iṅgha, tāva āyasmā vinayaṃ pariyāpuṇassū’ti itissa bhavanti vattārau| caudakainupāli, bhikkhunā paraṃ caudaitukāmaina imai pañ‍ca dhammai ajjhattaṃ pac‍cavaikkhitvā parau caudaitabbau’’ti|

437. ‘‘caudakaina, bhantai, bhikkhunā paraṃ caudaitukāmaina kati dhammai ajjhattaṃ upaṭṭhāpaitvā parau caudaitabbau’’ti? ‘‘caudakainupāli, bhikkhunā paraṃ caudaitukāmaina pañ‍ca dhammai ajjhattaṃ upaṭṭhāpaitvā parau caudaitabbau| katamai pañ‍ca? kālaina vakkhāmi nau akālaina, bhūtaina vakkhāmi nau abhūtaina, saṇhaina vakkhāmi nau pharusaina, atthasaṃhitaina vakkhāmi nau anatthasaṃhitaina, maittācittau vakkhāmi nau dausantarauti – caudakainupāli, bhikkhunā paraṃ caudaitukāmaina imai pañ‍ca dhammai ajjhattaṃ upaṭṭhāpaitvā parau caudaitabbau’’ti|

438. cūḷava॰ 400 ‘‘caudakaina, bhantai, bhikkhunā paraṃ caudaitukāmaina kati dhammai ajjhattaṃ manasi karitvā parau caudaitabbau’’ti? ‘‘caudakainupāli, bhikkhunā paraṃ caudaitukāmaina pañ‍ca dhammai ajjhattaṃ manasi karitvā parau caudaitabbau| katamai pañ‍ca? kāruñ‍ñatā, hitaisitā, anukampatā, āpattivuṭṭhānatā, vinayapuraikkhāratā – caudakainupāli, bhikkhunā paraṃ caudaitukāmaina imai pañ‍ca dhammai ajjhattaṃ manasi karitvā parau caudaitabbau’’ti|

439. ‘‘katihi nu khau, bhantai, aṅgaihi saman‍nāgatassa bhikkhunau aukāsakammaṃ kārāpaintassa nālaṃ aukāsakammaṃ kātu’’nti? ‘‘pañ‍cahupāli, aṅgaihi saman‍nāgatassa bhikkhunau aukāsakammaṃ kārāpaintassa nālaṃ aukāsakammaṃ kātuṃ| katamaihi pañ‍cahi? aparisuddhakāyasamācārau hauti, aparisuddhavacīsamācārau hauti, aparisuddhājīvau hauti, bālau hauti abyattau, na paṭibalau anuyuñ‍jiyamānau anuyaugaṃ dātuṃ – imaihi  khau, upāli, pañ‍cahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgatassa bhikkhunau aukāsakammaṃ kārāpaintassa nālaṃ aukāsakammaṃ kātuṃ|

‘‘pañ‍cahupāli, aṅgaihi  saman‍nāgatassa bhikkhunau aukāsakammaṃ kārāpaintassa alaṃ aukāsakammaṃ kātuṃ| katamaihi pañ‍cahi? parisuddhakāyasamācārau hauti, parisuddhavacīsamācārau hauti, parisuddhājīvau hauti, paṇḍitau hauti byattau paṭibalau anuyuñ‍jiyamānau anuyaugaṃ dātuṃ – imaihi khau, upāli, pañ‍cahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgatassa bhikkhunau aukāsakammaṃ kārāpaintassa alaṃ aukāsakammaṃ kātu’’nti|

440. cūḷava॰ 398 ‘‘attādānaṃ ādātukāmaina, bhantai, bhikkhunā katihaṅgaihi saman‍nāgataṃ attādānaṃ ādātabba’’nti? ‘‘attādānaṃ ādātukāmainupāli, bhikkhunā pañ‍caṅgasaman‍nāgataṃ pañ‍cahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgataṃ (sī॰ syā॰) attādānaṃ ādātabbaṃ| katamai pañ‍ca katamaihi pañ‍cahi (syā॰)? attādānaṃ ādātukāmaina, upāli, bhikkhunā aivaṃ pac‍cavaikkhitabbaṃ – ‘yaṃ khau ahaṃ imaṃ attādānaṃ ādātukāmau, kālau nu khau imaṃ attādānaṃ ādātuṃ udāhu nau’ti| sacai, upāli, bhikkhu pac‍cavaikkhamānau aivaṃ jānāti – ‘akālau imaṃ attādānaṃ ādātuṃ nau kālau’ti, na taṃ, upāli, attādānaṃ ādātabbaṃ|

‘‘sacai panupāli, bhikkhu pac‍cavaikkhamānau aivaṃ jānāti – ‘kālau imaṃ attādānaṃ ādātuṃ nau akālau’ti, tainupāli bhikkhunā uttari pac‍cavaikkhitabbaṃ – ‘yaṃ khau ahaṃ imaṃ attādānaṃ ādātukāmau bhūtaṃ nu khau idaṃ attādānaṃ udāhu nau’ti| sacai, upāli, bhikkhu pac‍cavaikkhamānau aivaṃ jānāti – ‘abhūtaṃ idaṃ attādānaṃ nau bhūta’nti, na taṃ, upāli, attādānaṃ ādātabbaṃ|

‘‘sacai panupāli bhikkhu pac‍cavaikkhamānau  aivaṃ jānāti – ‘bhūtaṃ idaṃ attādānaṃ nau abhūta’nti, tainupāli bhikkhunā uttari pac‍cavaikkhitabbaṃ – ‘yaṃ khau ahaṃ imaṃ attādānaṃ ādātukāmau, atthasaṃhitaṃ nu khau idaṃ attādānaṃ udāhu nau’ti| sacai, upāli, bhikkhu pac‍cavaikkhamānau aivaṃ jānāti  –’ anatthasaṃhitaṃ idaṃ attādānaṃ nau atthasaṃhita’nti, na taṃ, upāli, attādānaṃ ādātabbaṃ|

‘‘sacai panupāli bhikkhu pac‍cavaikkhamānau aivaṃ jānāti – ‘atthasaṃhitaṃ idaṃ attādānaṃ nau anatthasaṃhita’nti, tainupāli bhikkhunā uttari pac‍cavaikkhitabbaṃ – ‘imaṃ khau ahaṃ attādānaṃ ādiyamānau labhissāmi sandiṭṭhai sambhattai bhikkhū dhammatau vinayatau pakkhai udāhu nau’ti| sacai, upāli, bhikkhu pac‍cavaikkhamānau aivaṃ jānāti – ‘imaṃ khau ahaṃ attādānaṃ ādiyamānau  na labhissāmi sandiṭṭhai sambhattai bhikkhū dhammatau vinayatau pakkhai’ti, na taṃ, upāli, attādānaṃ ādātabbaṃ|
