‘‘sacai panupāli bhikkhu pac‍cavaikkhamānau aivaṃ jānāti – ‘idaṃ khau ahaṃ attādānaṃ ādiyamānau labhissāmi sandiṭṭhai sambhattai bhikkhū dhammatau vinayatau pakkhai’ti, tainupāli bhikkhunā uttari pac‍cavaikkhitabbaṃ – ‘imaṃ khau mai attādānaṃ ādiyatau bhavissati saṅghassa tataunidānaṃ bhaṇḍanaṃ kalahau viggahau vivādau saṅghabhaidau saṅgharāji saṅghavavatthānaṃ saṅghanānākaraṇaṃ udāhu nau’ti| sacai, upāli, bhikkhu pac‍cavaikkhamānau aivaṃ jānāti – ‘imaṃ khau mai attādānaṃ ādiyatau bhavissati saṅghassa tataunidānaṃ bhaṇḍanaṃ kalahau viggahau vivādau  saṅghabhaidau saṅgharāji saṅghavavatthānaṃ saṅghanānākaraṇa’nti, na taṃ, upāli, attādānaṃ ādātabbaṃ|

‘‘sacai panupāli bhikkhu pac‍cavaikkhamānau aivaṃ jānāti – ‘imaṃ khau mai attādānaṃ ādiyatau na bhavissati saṅghassa tataunidānaṃ bhaṇḍanaṃ kalahau viggahau vivādau saṅghabhaidau saṅgharāji saṅghavavatthānaṃ saṅghanānākaraṇa’nti, taṃ ādātabbaṃ, upāli, attādānaṃ| aivaṃ pañ‍caṅgasaman‍nāgataṃ khau, upāli, attādānaṃ ādin‍naṃ pacchāpi avippaṭisārakaraṃ bhavissatī’’ti|

441. ‘‘katihi nu khau, bhantai, aṅgaihi saman‍nāgatau bhikkhu adhikaraṇajātānaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ bahūpakārau hautī’’ti? ‘‘pañ‍cahupāli, aṅgaihi saman‍nāgatau bhikkhu adhikaraṇajātānaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ bahūpakārau hauti| katamaihi pañ‍cahi? sīlavā hauti, pātimaukkhasaṃvarasaṃvutau viharati ācāragaucarasampan‍nau aṇumattaisu vaj‍jaisu bhayadassāvī, samādāya sikkhati  sikkhāpadaisu; bahussutau hauti sutadharau sutasan‍nicayau; yai tai dhammā ādikalyāṇā majjhaikalyāṇā pariyausānakalyāṇā sātthaṃ sabyañ‍janaṃ kaivalaparipuṇṇaṃ parisuddhaṃ brahmacariyaṃ abhivadanti tathārūpassa dhammā bahussutā haunti dhātā vacasā paricitā manasānupaikkhitā diṭṭhiyā suppaṭividdhā, ubhayāni khau panassa pātimaukkhāni vitthāraina svāgatāni haunti suvibhattāni suppavattīni suvinicchitāni suttasau anubyañ‍janasau; vinayai khau pana ṭhitau hauti asaṃhīrau; paṭibalau hauti  ubhau atthapac‍catthikai assāsaituṃ sañ‍ñāpaituṃ nijjhāpaituṃ paikkhaituṃ pasādaituṃ – imaihi khau, upāli, pañ‍cahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgatau bhikkhu adhikaraṇajātānaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ bahūpakārau hauti|

‘‘aparaihipi, upāli, pañ‍cahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgatau bhikkhu adhikaraṇajātānaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ bahūpakārau hauti| katamaihi pañ‍cahi? parisuddhakāyasamācārau hauti, parisuddhavacīsamācārau hauti, parisuddhājīvau hauti, paṇḍitau hauti byattau, paṭibalau anuyuñ‍jiyamānau anuyaugaṃ dātuṃ – imaihi khau, upāli, pañ‍cahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgatau bhikkhu adhikaraṇajātānaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ bahūpakārau hauti|

‘‘aparaihipi, upāli, pañ‍cahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgatau bhikkhu adhikaraṇajātānaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ bahūpakārau hauti| katamaihi pañ‍cahi? vatthuṃ jānāti, nidānaṃ jānāti, pañ‍ñattiṃ jānāti, padapac‍cābhaṭṭhaṃ jānāti, anusandhivacanapathaṃ jānāti – imaihi khau, upāli, pañ‍cahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgatau bhikkhu adhikaraṇajātānaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ bahūpakārau hautī’’ti|

442. ‘‘katihi nu khau, bhantai, aṅgaihi saman‍nāgataina bhikkhunā nānuyuñ‍jitabba’’nti? ‘‘pañ‍cahupāli, aṅgaihi saman‍nāgataina bhikkhunā nānuyuñ‍jitabbaṃ| katamaihi pañ‍cahi? suttaṃ na jānāti, suttānulaumaṃ na jānāti, vinayaṃ na jānāti, vinayānulaumaṃ na jānāti, na ca ṭhānāṭhānakusalau hauti – imaihi khau, upāli, pañ‍cahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgataina bhikkhunā nānuyuñ‍jitabbaṃ|

‘‘pañ‍cahupāli, aṅgaihi saman‍nāgataina  bhikkhunā anuyuñ‍jitabbaṃ| katamaihi pañ‍cahi? suttaṃ jānāti, suttānulaumaṃ jānāti, vinayaṃ jānāti, vinayānulaumaṃ  jānāti, ṭhānāṭhānakusalau ca hauti – imaihi khau, upāli, pañ‍cahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgataina bhikkhunā anuyuñ‍jitabbaṃ|

‘‘aparaihipi, upāli, pañ‍cahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgataina bhikkhunā nānuyuñ‍jitabbaṃ| katamaihi pañ‍cahi? dhammaṃ na jānāti, dhammānulaumaṃ na jānāti, vinayaṃ na jānāti, vinayānulaumaṃ na jānāti, na ca pubbāparakusalau hauti – imaihi khau, upāli, pañ‍cahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgataina bhikkhunā nānuyuñ‍jitabbaṃ|

‘‘pañ‍cahupāli, aṅgaihi saman‍nāgataina bhikkhunā anuyuñ‍jitabbaṃ| katamaihi pañ‍cahi? dhammaṃ jānāti, dhammānulaumaṃ jānāti, vinayaṃ jānāti, vinayānulaumaṃ jānāti, pubbāparakusalau  ca hauti – imaihi khau, upāli, pañ‍cahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgataina bhikkhunā anuyuñ‍jitabbaṃ|

‘‘aparaihipi, upāli, pañ‍cahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgataina bhikkhunā nānuyuñ‍jitabbaṃ| katamaihi pañ‍cahi? vatthuṃ na jānāti, nidānaṃ na jānāti, pañ‍ñattiṃ na jānāti, padapac‍cābhaṭṭhaṃ na jānāti, anusandhivacanapathaṃ na jānāti – imaihi khau, upāli, pañ‍cahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgataina bhikkhunā nānuyuñ‍jitabbaṃ|

‘‘pañ‍cahupāli, aṅgaihi saman‍nāgataina bhikkhunā anuyuñ‍jitabbaṃ| katamaihi pañ‍cahi? vatthuṃ jānāti, nidānaṃ jānāti, pañ‍ñattiṃ jānāti, padapac‍cābhaṭṭhaṃ jānāti, anusandhivacanapathaṃ jānāti – imaihi khau, upāli, pañ‍cahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgataina bhikkhunā  anuyuñ‍jitabbaṃ|

‘‘aparaihipi, upāli, pañ‍cahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgataina bhikkhunā nānuyuñ‍jitabbaṃ| katamaihi pañ‍cahi? āpattiṃ na jānāti, āpattisamuṭṭhānaṃ na jānāti, āpattiyā payaugaṃ na jānāti, āpattiyā vūpasamaṃ na jānāti, āpattiyā na vinicchayakusalau hauti – imaihi khau, upāli, pañ‍cahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgataina bhikkhunā nānuyuñ‍jitabbaṃ|

‘‘pañ‍cahupāli, aṅgaihi saman‍nāgataina bhikkhunā anuyuñ‍jitabbaṃ| katamaihi pañ‍cahi? āpattiṃ jānāti, āpattisamuṭṭhānaṃ jānāti, āpattiyā payaugaṃ jānāti, āpattiyā vūpasamaṃ jānāti, āpattiyā vinicchayakusalau hauti – imaihi khau, upāli, pañ‍cahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgataina bhikkhunā anuyuñ‍jitabbaṃ|

‘‘aparaihipi, upāli, pañ‍cahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgataina bhikkhunā nānuyuñ‍jitabbaṃ| katamaihi pañ‍cahi? adhikaraṇaṃ na jānāti, adhikaraṇasamuṭṭhānaṃ na jānāti, adhikaraṇassa payaugaṃ na jānāti, adhikaraṇassa vūpasamaṃ na jānāti, adhikaraṇassa na vinicchayakusalau hauti – imaihi khau, upāli, pañ‍cahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgataina bhikkhunā nānuyuñ‍jitabbaṃ|

‘‘pañ‍cahupāli, aṅgaihi saman‍nāgataina bhikkhunā anuyuñ‍jitabbaṃ| katamaihi pañ‍cahi? adhikaraṇaṃ jānāti, adhikaraṇasamuṭṭhānaṃ jānāti, adhikaraṇassa payaugaṃ jānāti, adhikaraṇassa  vūpasamaṃ jānāti, adhikaraṇassa vinicchayakusalau hauti – imaihi khau, upāli, pañ‍cahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgataina bhikkhunā anuyuñ‍jitabba’’nti|

attādānavaggau niṭṭhitau pañ‍camau|

tassuddānaṃ –

parisuddhañ‍ca  kālaina, kāruñ‍ñaṃ aukāsaina ca|

attādānaṃ adhikaraṇaṃ, aparaihipi vatthuñ‍ca|

suttaṃ dhammaṃ puna vatthuñ‍ca, āpatti adhikaraṇaina cāti||

6. dhutaṅgavaggau

443. ‘‘kati  nu khau, bhantai, ārañ‍ñikā’’ti? ‘‘pañ‍cimai, upāli, ārañ‍ñikā a॰ ni॰ 5.181; pari॰ 325| katamai pañ‍ca? mandattā maumūhattā ārañ‍ñikau hauti, pāpicchau icchāpakatau ārañ‍ñikau hauti, ummādā cittakkhaipā ārañ‍ñikau hauti, vaṇṇitaṃ buddhaihi buddhasāvakaihīti ārañ‍ñikau hauti, api ca appicchañ‍ñaiva nissāya santuṭṭhiñ‍ñaiva nissāya – sal‍laikhañ‍ñaiva nissāya pavivaikañ‍ñaiva nissāya  idamatthitañ‍ñaiva nissāya ārañ‍ñikau hauti – imai khau, upāli, pañ‍ca ārañ‍ñikā’’ti|

‘‘kati nu khau, bhantai, piṇḍapātikāti…pai॰… kati nu khau, bhantai, paṃsukūlikāti…pai॰… kati nu khau, bhantai, rukkhamūlikāti…pai॰… kati nu khau, bhantai, sausānikāti…pai॰… kati nu khau, bhantai, abbhaukāsikāti…pai॰… kati  nu khau, bhantai, taicīvarikāti…pai॰… kati nu khau, bhantai, sapadānacārikāti…pai॰… kati nu khau, bhantai, naisaj‍jikāti…pai॰… kati nu khau, bhantai, yathāsanthatikāti…pai॰… kati nu khau, bhantai, aikāsanikāti…pai॰… kati nu khau, bhantai, khalupacchābhattikāti…pai॰… kati  nu khau, bhantai, pattapiṇḍikāti? pañ‍cimai, upāli, pattapiṇḍikā| katamai pañ‍ca? mandattā maumūhattā pattapiṇḍikau hauti, pāpicchau icchāpakatau pattapiṇḍikau hauti, ummādā cittakkhaipā pattapiṇḍikau hauti, vaṇṇitaṃ buddhaihi buddhasāvakaihīti pattapiṇḍikau hauti, api ca appicchañ‍ñaiva nissāya santuṭṭhiñ‍ñaiva nissāya sal‍laikhañ‍ñaiva nissāya pavivaikañ‍ñaiva nissāya idamatthitañ‍ñaiva nissāya pattapiṇḍikau hauti – imai khau, upāli, pañ‍ca pattapiṇḍikā’’ti|

dhutaṅgavaggau niṭṭhitau chaṭṭhau|

tassuddānaṃ –

ārañ‍ñikau piṇḍipaṃsu, rukkhasusānapañ‍camaṃ|

abbhau taicīvarañ‍caiva, sapadānanaisaj‍jikā|

santhataikāsanañ‍caiva, khalupacchā pattapiṇḍikāti||

7. musāvādavaggau

444. ‘‘kati nu khau, bhantai, musāvādā’’ti? ‘‘pañ‍cimai, upāli, musāvādā| katamai pañ‍ca? atthi musāvādau pārājikagāmī, atthi musāvādau saṅghādisaisagāmī, atthi musāvādau thul‍lac‍cayagāmī, atthi musāvādau pācittiyagāmī, atthi musāvādau duk‍kaṭagāmī – imai khau, upāli, pañ‍ca musāvādā’’ti|

445. ‘‘katihi nu khau, bhantai, aṅgaihi saman‍nāgatassa bhikkhunau saṅghamajjhai upausathaṃ vā pavāraṇaṃ vā ṭhapaintassa – ‘alaṃ, bhikkhu, mā  bhaṇḍanaṃ, mā kalahaṃ, mā viggahaṃ, mā vivāda’nti  aumadditvā saṅghaina upausathau vā pavāraṇā vā kātabbā’’ti? ‘‘pañ‍cahupāli, aṅgaihi saman‍nāgatassa bhikkhunau saṅghamajjhai  upausathaṃ vā pavāraṇaṃ vā ṭhapaintassa – ‘alaṃ, bhikkhu, mā bhaṇḍanaṃ, mā kalahaṃ, mā viggahaṃ, mā vivāda’nti aumadditvā saṅghaina upausathau vā pavāraṇā vā kātabbā| katamaihi pañ‍cahi? alaj‍jī ca hauti, bālau ca, apakatattau ca, cāvanādhippāyau vattā hauti, nau vuṭṭhānādhippāyau – imaihi khau, upāli, pañ‍cahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgatassa bhikkhunau saṅghamajjhai upausathaṃ vā pavāraṇaṃ vā ṭhapaintassa – ‘alaṃ, bhikkhu, mā bhaṇḍanaṃ, mā kalahaṃ, mā viggahaṃ, mā vivāda’nti aumadditvā saṅghaina upausathau vā pavāraṇā vā kātabbā|

‘‘aparaihipi, upāli, pañ‍cahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgatassa bhikkhunau saṅghamajjhai upausathaṃ vā pavāraṇaṃ vā ṭhapaintassa – ‘alaṃ, bhikkhu, mā bhaṇḍanaṃ, mā kalahaṃ, mā viggahaṃ, mā vivāda’nti aumadditvā saṅghaina upausathau vā pavāraṇā vā kātabbā| katamaihi pañ‍cahi? aparisuddhakāyasamācārau hauti, aparisuddhavacīsamācārau  hauti, aparisuddhājīvau hauti, bālau hauti abyattau, bhaṇḍanakārakau hauti kalahakārakau – imaihi khau, upāli, pañ‍cahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgatassa bhikkhunau saṅghamajjhai upausathaṃ vā pavāraṇaṃ vā ṭhapaintassa – ‘alaṃ, bhikkhu, mā bhaṇḍanaṃ, mā kalahaṃ, mā viggahaṃ, mā vivāda’nti aumadditvā saṅghaina upausathau  vā pavāraṇā vā kātabbā’’ti|

446. ‘‘katihi nu khau, bhantai, aṅgaihi saman‍nāgatassa bhikkhunau anuyaugau na dātabbau’’ti? ‘‘pañ‍cahupāli, aṅgaihi saman‍nāgatassa bhikkhunau anuyaugau na dātabbau| katamaihi pañ‍cahi? āpattānāpattiṃ na jānāti, lahukagarukaṃ āpattiṃ na jānāti, sāvasaisānavasaisaṃ āpattiṃ na jānāti, duṭṭhul‍lāduṭṭhul‍laṃ āpattiṃ na jānāti, sappaṭikammāpaṭikammaṃ āpattiṃ na jānāti – imaihi khau, upāli, pañ‍cahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgatassa bhikkhunau anuyaugau na dātabbau|

‘‘pañ‍cahupāli, aṅgaihi saman‍nāgatassa bhikkhunau anuyaugau dātabbau| katamaihi pañ‍cahi? āpattānāpattiṃ jānāti, lahukagarukaṃ āpattiṃ jānāti, sāvasaisānavasaisaṃ āpattiṃ jānāti, duṭṭhul‍lāduṭṭhul‍laṃ āpattiṃ jānāti, sappaṭikammāpaṭikammaṃ āpattiṃ jānāti – imaihi khau, upāli, pañ‍cahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgatassa bhikkhunau anuyaugau dātabbau’’ti|

447. ‘‘katihi  nu khau, bhantai, ākāraihi bhikkhu āpattiṃ āpaj‍jatī’’ti? ‘‘pañ‍cahupāli, ākāraihi bhikkhu āpattiṃ āpaj‍jati| katamaihi pañ‍cahi? alaj‍jitā, añ‍ñāṇatā, kuk‍kuc‍capakatatā, akappiyai kappiyasañ‍ñitā, kappiyai akappiyasañ‍ñitā – imaihi khau, upāli, pañ‍cahākāraihi bhikkhu āpattiṃ āpaj‍jati|

‘‘aparaihipi, upāli, pañ‍cahākāraihi bhikkhu āpattiṃ āpaj‍jati| katamaihi pañ‍cahi? adassanaina, assavanaina, pasuttakatā, tathāsañ‍ñī, satisammausā – imaihi khau, upāli, pañ‍cahākāraihi bhikkhu āpattiṃ āpaj‍jatī’’ti|

448. ‘‘kati nu khau, bhantai, vairā’’ti? ‘‘pañ‍cimai, upāli, vairā| katamai pañ‍ca? pāṇātipātau, adin‍nādānaṃ, kāmaisumicchācārau, musāvādau, surāmairayamaj‍jappamādaṭṭhānaṃ – imai khau, upāli, pañ‍ca vairā’’ti|

‘‘kati nu khau, bhantai, vairamaṇiyau’’ti? ‘‘pañ‍cimā, upāli, vairamaṇiyau| katamā pañ‍ca? pāṇātipātā vairamaṇī vairamaṇi (ka॰), adin‍nādānā vairamaṇī, kāmaisumicchācārā vairamaṇī, musāvādā vairamaṇī, surāmairayamaj‍jappamādaṭṭhānā vairamaṇī – imā khau, upāli, pañ‍ca vairamaṇiyau’’ti|

449. ‘‘kati nu khau, bhantai, byasanānī’’ti? ‘‘pañ‍cimāni, upāli, byasanāni| pari॰ 325; a॰ ni॰ 5.130 katamāni pañ‍ca? ñātibyasanaṃ, bhaugabyasanaṃ, raugabyasanaṃ, sīlabyasanaṃ, diṭṭhibyasanaṃ – imāni khau, upāli, pañ‍ca byasanānī’’ti|

‘‘kati nu khau, bhantai, sampadā’’ti? ‘‘pañ‍cimā, upāli, sampadā| pari॰ 325; a॰ ni॰ 5.130 katamā pañ‍ca? ñātisampadā, bhaugasampadā, āraugyasampadā, sīlasampadā, diṭṭhisampadā – imā khau, upāli, pañ‍ca sampadā’’ti|

musāvādavaggau niṭṭhitau sattamau|

tassuddānaṃ –

musāvādau  ca aumaddi, aparaihi anuyaugau|

āpattiñ‍ca aparaihi, vairā vairamaṇīpi ca|

byasanaṃ sampadā caiva, sattamau vaggasaṅgahauti||

8. bhikkhunauvādavaggau

450. ‘‘katihi  nu khau, bhantai, aṅgaihi saman‍nāgatassa bhikkhunau bhikkhunisaṅghainaiva kammaṃ kātabba’’nti? ‘‘pañ‍cahupāli, aṅgaihi saman‍nāgatassa bhikkhunau bhikkhunisaṅghainaiva kammaṃ kātabbaṃ, avandiyau sau bhikkhu bhikkhunisaṅghaina| katamaihi pañ‍cahi? vivaritvā kāyaṃ bhikkhunīnaṃ dassaiti, ūruṃ dassaiti, aṅgajātaṃ dassaiti, ubhau aṃsakūṭai dassaiti, aubhāsati, gihī  sampayaujaiti – imaihi khau, upāli, pañ‍cahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgatassa bhikkhunau bhikkhunisaṅghainaiva kammaṃ kātabbaṃ| avandiyau sau bhikkhu bhikkhunisaṅghaina|

‘‘aparaihipi, upāli, pañ‍cahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgatassa bhikkhunau bhikkhunisaṅghainaiva kammaṃ kātabbaṃ, avandiyau sau bhikkhu bhikkhunisaṅghaina| katamaihi pañ‍cahi? bhikkhunīnaṃ alābhāya parisak‍kati, bhikkhunīnaṃ anatthāya parisak‍kati, bhikkhunīnaṃ avāsāya parisak‍kati, bhikkhuniyau ak‍kausati paribhāsati, bhikkhū bhikkhunīhi bhaidaiti – imaihi khau, upāli, pañ‍cahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgatassa bhikkhunau bhikkhunisaṅghainaiva kammaṃ kātabbaṃ, avandiyau sau bhikkhu bhikkhunisaṅghaina|

‘‘aparaihipi, upāli, pañ‍cahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgatassa bhikkhunau bhikkhunisaṅghainaiva kammaṃ kātabbaṃ, avandiyau sau  bhikkhu bhikkhunisaṅghaina| katamaihi pañ‍cahi? bhikkhunīnaṃ alābhāya parisak‍kati, bhikkhunīnaṃ anatthāya parisak‍kati, bhikkhunīnaṃ avāsāya parisak‍kati, bhikkhuniyau ak‍kausati paribhāsati, bhikkhū bhikkhunīhi sampayaujaiti – imaihi khau, upāli, pañ‍cahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgatassa bhikkhunau bhikkhunisaṅghainaiva kammaṃ kātabbaṃ, avandiyau sau bhikkhu bhikkhunisaṅghainā’’ti|

451. ‘‘katihi nu khau, bhantai, aṅgaihi saman‍nāgatāya bhikkhuniyā kammaṃ kātabba’’nti? pañ‍cahupāli, aṅgaihi saman‍nāgatāya bhikkhuniyā kammaṃ kātabbaṃ| katamaihi pañ‍cahi? vivaritvā kāyaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ dassaiti, ūruṃ dassaiti, aṅgajātaṃ dassaiti, ubhau aṃsakūṭai dassaiti, aubhāsati, gihī sampayaujaiti – imaihi khau, upāli, pañ‍cahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgatāya bhikkhuniyā kammaṃ kātabbaṃ|

‘‘aparaihipi, upāli, pañ‍cahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgatāya bhikkhuniyā kammaṃ kātabbaṃ| katamaihi pañ‍cahi? bhikkhūnaṃ alābhāya parisak‍kati, bhikkhūnaṃ anatthāya parisak‍kati, bhikkhūnaṃ avāsāya parisak‍kati, bhikkhū ak‍kausati paribhāsati, bhikkhuniyau bhikkhūhi bhaidaiti – imaihi khau, upāli, pañ‍cahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgatāya bhikkhuniyā kammaṃ kātabbaṃ|

‘‘aparaihipi, upāli, pañ‍cahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgatāya bhikkhuniyā kammaṃ kātabbaṃ| katamaihi pañ‍cahi? bhikkhūnaṃ alābhāya parisak‍kati, bhikkhūnaṃ anatthāya parisak‍kati, bhikkhūnaṃ avāsāya  parisak‍kati, bhikkhū ak‍kausati  paribhāsati, bhikkhuniyau bhikkhūhi sampayaujaiti – imaihi khau, upāli, pañ‍cahaṅgaihi  saman‍nāgatāya bhikkhuniyā kammaṃ kātabba’’nti|

452. ‘‘katihi nu khau, bhantai, aṅgaihi saman‍nāgataina bhikkhunā bhikkhunīnaṃ auvādau na ṭhapaitabbau’’ti? ‘‘pañ‍cahupāli, aṅgaihi saman‍nāgataina bhikkhunā bhikkhunīnaṃ auvādau na ṭhapaitabbau| katamaihi pañ‍cahi? alaj‍jī ca hauti, bālau ca, apakatattau ca, cāvanādhippāyau vattā hauti, nau vuṭṭhānādhippāyau – imaihi khau, upāli, pañ‍cahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgataina bhikkhunā bhikkhunīnaṃ auvādau na ṭhapaitabbau|

‘‘aparaihipi, upāli, pañ‍cahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgataina bhikkhunā bhikkhunīnaṃ auvādau na ṭhapaitabbau| katamaihi pañ‍cahi? aparisuddhakāyasamācārau hauti, aparisuddhavacīsamācārau hauti, aparisuddhājīvau hauti, bālau hauti, abyattau, na paṭibalau anuyuñ‍jiyamānau anuyaugaṃ dātuṃ – imaihi khau, upāli, pañ‍cahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgataina bhikkhunā bhikkhunīnaṃ auvādau na ṭhapaitabbau|

‘‘aparaihipi, upāli, pañ‍cahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgataina bhikkhunā bhikkhunīnaṃ auvādau na ṭhapaitabbau| katamaihi pañ‍cahi? kāyikaina anācāraina saman‍nāgatau hauti, vācasikaina anācāraina saman‍nāgatau hauti, kāyikavācasikaina anācāraina saman‍nāgatau hauti, bhikkhunīnaṃ ak‍kausakaparibhāsakau hauti, bhikkhunīhi saddhiṃ saṃsaṭṭhau viharati ananulaumikaina saṃsaggaina – imaihi khau, upāli, pañ‍cahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgataina  bhikkhunā bhikkhunīnaṃ auvādau na ṭhapaitabbau|

‘‘aparaihipi, upāli, pañ‍cahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgataina bhikkhunā bhikkhunīnaṃ auvādau na ṭhapaitabbau| katamaihi pañ‍cahi? alaj‍jī ca hauti, bālau ca, apakatattau ca, bhaṇḍanakārakau ca hauti kalahakārakau, sikkhāya ca na paripūrikārī – imaihi khau, upāli, pañ‍cahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgataina bhikkhunā bhikkhunīnaṃ auvādau na ṭhapaitabbau’’ti|

453. ‘‘katihi nu khau, bhantai, aṅgaihi saman‍nāgataina bhikkhunā bhikkhunīnaṃ auvādau na gahaitabbau’’ti? ‘‘pañ‍cahupāli, aṅgaihi saman‍nāgataina bhikkhunā bhikkhunīnaṃ auvādau na gahaitabbau| katamaihi pañ‍cahi? kāyikaina anācāraina saman‍nāgatau hauti, vācasikaina anācāraina saman‍nāgatau hauti, kāyikavācasikaina anācāraina saman‍nāgatau hauti, bhikkhunīnaṃ ak‍kausakaparibhāsakau  hauti, bhikkhunīhi saddhiṃ saṃsaṭṭhau viharati ananulaumikaina saṃsaggaina – imaihi khau, upāli, pañ‍cahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgataina bhikkhunā bhikkhunīnaṃ auvādau na gahaitabbau|

‘‘aparaihipi, upāli, pañ‍cahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgataina bhikkhunā bhikkhunīnaṃ auvādau na gahaitabbau| katamaihi pañ‍cahi? alaj‍jī ca hauti, bālau ca, apakatattau ca, gamikau vā hauti, gilānau vā – imaihi khau, upāli, pañ‍cahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgataina bhikkhunā bhikkhunīnaṃ auvādau na gahaitabbau’’ti|

454. ‘‘katihi  nu khau, bhantai, aṅgaihi saman‍nāgataina bhikkhunā saddhiṃ na sākacchitabbau’’ti? ‘‘pañ‍cahupāli, aṅgaihi saman‍nāgataina bhikkhunā saddhiṃ na sākacchitabbau| katamaihi pañ‍cahi? na asaikkhaina sīlakkhandhaina saman‍nāgatau hauti, na asaikkhaina samādhikkhandhaina saman‍nāgatau hauti, na asaikkhaina pañ‍ñākkhandhaina saman‍nāgatau hauti, na asaikkhaina vimuttikkhandhaina saman‍nāgatau hauti, na asaikkhaina vimuttiñāṇadassanakkhandhaina saman‍nāgatau hauti – imaihi khau, upāli, pañ‍cahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgataina bhikkhunā saddhiṃ na sākacchitabbau| pañ‍cahupāli, aṅgaihi saman‍nāgataina bhikkhunā saddhiṃ sākacchitabbau| katamaihi pañ‍cahi? asaikkhaina sīlakkhandhaina saman‍nāgatau hauti, asaikkhaina samādhikkhandhaina saman‍nāgatau hauti, asaikkhaina pañ‍ñākkhandhaina saman‍nāgatau hauti, asaikkhaina vimuttikkhandhaina saman‍nāgatau hauti, asaikkhaina vimuttiñāṇadassanakkhandhaina  saman‍nāgatau hauti – imaihi khau, upāli, pañ‍cahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgataina bhikkhunā saddhiṃ sākacchitabbau|

‘‘aparaihipi, upāli, pañ‍cahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgataina bhikkhunā saddhiṃ na sākacchitabbau| katamaihi pañ‍cahi? na atthapaṭisambhidāpattau hauti, na dhammapaṭisambhidāpattau hauti, na niruttipaṭisambhidāpattau hauti, na paṭibhānapaṭisambhidāpattau hauti, yathāvimuttaṃ cittaṃ na pac‍cavaikkhitā – imaihi khau, upāli, pañ‍cahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgataina bhikkhunā saddhiṃ na sākacchitabbau| pañ‍cahupāli, aṅgaihi saman‍nāgataina bhikkhunā saddhiṃ sākacchitabbau| katamaihi pañ‍cahi? atthapaṭisambhidāpattau hauti, dhammapaṭisambhidāpattau hauti, niruttipaṭisambhidāpattau hauti, paṭibhānapaṭisambhidāpattau hauti, yathāvimuttaṃ cittaṃ pac‍cavaikkhitā – imaihi khau, upāli, pañ‍cahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgataina bhikkhunā saddhiṃ sākacchitabbau’’ti|

bhikkhunauvādavaggau niṭṭhitau aṭṭhamau|

tassuddānaṃ –

bhikkhunīhaiva  kātabbaṃ, aparaihi tathā duvai|

bhikkhunīnaṃ tayau kammā, na ṭhapaitabbā dvai dukā|

na gahaitabbā dvai vuttā, sākacchāsu ca dvai dukāti||

9. ubbāhikavaggau

455. ‘‘katihi nu khau, bhantai, aṅgaihi saman‍nāgatau bhikkhu ubbāhikāya na samman‍nitabbau’’ti? ‘‘pañ‍cahupāli, aṅgaihi saman‍nāgatau bhikkhu ubbāhikāya na samman‍nitabbau| katamaihi pañ‍cahi? na atthakusalau hauti, na dhammakusalau hauti, na niruttikusalau hauti, na byañ‍janakusalau hauti, na pubbāparakusalau hauti – imaihi khau, upāli, pañ‍cahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgatau bhikkhu ubbāhikāya na samman‍nitabbau| pañ‍cahupāli, aṅgaihi saman‍nāgatau bhikkhu ubbāhikāya samman‍nitabbau| katamaihi pañ‍cahi? atthakusalau hauti, dhammakusalau hauti, niruttikusalau  hauti, byañ‍janakusalau hauti, pubbāparakusalau hauti  – imaihi khau, upāli, pañ‍cahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgatau bhikkhu ubbāhikāya samman‍nitabbau|

‘‘aparaihipi, upāli, pañ‍cahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgatau bhikkhu ubbāhikāya na samman‍nitabbau| katamaihi pañ‍cahi? kaudhanau hauti kaudhābhibhūtau, makkhī hauti makkhābhibhūtau, paḷāsī hauti paḷāsābhibhūtau, issukī hauti issābhibhūtau, sandiṭṭhiparāmāsī hauti ādhānaggāhī duppaṭinissaggī – imaihi khau, upāli, pañ‍cahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgatau bhikkhu ubbāhikāya na samman‍nitabbau| pañ‍cahupāli, aṅgaihi saman‍nāgatau bhikkhu ubbāhikāya samman‍nitabbau| katamaihi pañ‍cahi? na kaudhanau hauti na kaudhābhibhūtau, na makkhī hauti na makkhābhibhūtau, na paḷāsī hauti na paḷāsābhibhūtau, na issukī hauti na issābhibhūtau, asandiṭṭhiparāmāsī hauti anādhānaggāhī suppaṭinissaggī – imaihi khau, upāli, pañ‍cahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgatau bhikkhu ubbāhikāya samman‍nitabbau|

‘‘aparaihipi, upāli, pañ‍cahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgatau bhikkhu ubbāhikāya na samman‍nitabbau| katamaihi pañ‍cahi? kuppati, byāpaj‍jati, patiṭṭhiyati, kaupaṃ janaiti, akhamau hauti apadakkhiṇaggāhī anusāsaniṃ – imaihi khau, upāli, pañ‍cahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgatau bhikkhu ubbāhikāya na samman‍nitabbau| pañ‍cahupāli, aṅgaihi saman‍nāgatau bhikkhu ubbāhikāya samman‍nitabbau| katamaihi pañ‍cahi? na kuppati, na byāpaj‍jati, na patiṭṭhiyati, na kaupaṃ janaiti, khamau hauti padakkhiṇaggāhī anusāsaniṃ – imaihi khau, upāli, pañ‍cahaṅgaihi  saman‍nāgatau bhikkhu ubbāhikāya samman‍nitabbau|

‘‘aparaihipi, upāli, pañ‍cahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgatau bhikkhu ubbāhikāya na samman‍nitabbau| katamaihi pañ‍cahi? pasāraitā hauti nau sāraitā, anaukāsakammaṃ kāraitvā pavattā hauti, na yathādhammai yathāvinayai yathāpattiyā caudaitā hauti, na yathādhammai yathāvinayai yathāpattiyā kāraitā hauti, na yathādiṭṭhiyā byākatā hauti – imaihi khau, upāli, pañ‍cahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgatau bhikkhu ubbāhikāya na samman‍nitabbau| pañ‍cahupāli, aṅgaihi saman‍nāgatau bhikkhu ubbāhikāya samman‍nitabbau| katamaihi pañ‍cahi? sāraitā hauti nau pasāraitā, aukāsakammaṃ  kāraitvā pavattā hauti, yathādhammaṃ yathāvinayai yathāpattiyā caudaitā hauti, yathādhammai yathāvinayai yathāpattiyā kāraitā hauti, yathādiṭṭhiyā byākatā hauti – imaihi khau, upāli, pañ‍cahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgatau bhikkhu ubbāhikāya samman‍nitabbau|

‘‘aparaihipi, upāli, pañ‍cahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgatau bhikkhu ubbāhikāya na samman‍nitabbau| katamaihi pañ‍cahi? chandāgatiṃ gacchati, dausāgatiṃ gacchati, mauhāgatiṃ gacchati, bhayāgatiṃ gacchati, alaj‍jī ca hauti – imaihi khau, upāli, pañ‍cahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgatau bhikkhu ubbāhikāya na samman‍nitabbau| pañ‍cahupāli, aṅgaihi saman‍nāgatau bhikkhu ubbāhikāya samman‍nitabbau| katamaihi pañ‍cahi? na chandāgatiṃ gacchati, na dausāgatiṃ gacchati, na mauhāgatiṃ gacchati, na bhayāgatiṃ gacchati, laj‍jī  ca hauti – imaihi khau, upāli, pañ‍cahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgatau bhikkhu ubbāhikāya samman‍nitabbau|

‘‘aparaihipi, upāli, pañ‍cahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgatau bhikkhu ubbāhikāya na samman‍nitabbau| katamaihi pañ‍cahi? chandāgatiṃ gacchati, dausāgatiṃ gacchati, mauhāgatiṃ gacchati, bhayāgatiṃ gacchati, akusalau ca hauti vinayai – imaihi khau, upāli, pañ‍cahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgatau bhikkhu ubbāhikāya na samman‍nitabbau| pañ‍cahupāli, aṅgaihi saman‍nāgatau bhikkhu ubbāhikāya samman‍nitabbau| katamaihi pañ‍cahi? na chandāgatiṃ gacchati, na dausāgatiṃ gacchati, na mauhāgatiṃ gacchati, na bhayāgatiṃ gacchati, kusalau ca hauti vinayai – imaihi khau, upāli, pañ‍cahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgatau bhikkhu ubbāhikāya samman‍nitabbau’’ti|

456. ‘‘katihi  nu khau, bhantai, aṅgaihi saman‍nāgatau bhikkhu bālautvaiva saṅkhaṃ gacchatī’’ti? ‘‘pañ‍cahupāli, aṅgaihi saman‍nāgatau bhikkhu bālautvaiva saṅkhaṃ gacchati| katamaihi pañ‍cahi? suttaṃ na jānāti, suttānulaumaṃ na jānāti, vinayaṃ na jānāti, vinayānulaumaṃ na jānāti, na ca ṭhānaṭhānakusalau hauti – imaihi khau, upāli, pañ‍cahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgatau bhikkhu bālautvaiva saṅkhaṃ gacchati| pañ‍cahupāli, aṅgaihi saman‍nāgatau bhikkhu paṇḍitautvaiva saṅkhaṃ gacchati| katamaihi pañ‍cahi? suttaṃ jānāti, suttānulaumaṃ jānāti, vinayaṃ jānāti, vinayānulaumaṃ  jānāti, ṭhānāṭhānakusalau ca  hauti – imaihi khau, upāli, pañ‍cahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgatau bhikkhu paṇḍitautvaiva saṅkhaṃ gacchati|

‘‘aparaihipi, upāli, pañ‍cahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgatau bhikkhu bālautvaiva saṅkhaṃ gacchati| katamaihi pañ‍cahi? dhammaṃ na jānāti, dhammānulaumaṃ  jānāti, vinayaṃ na jānāti, vinayānulaumaṃ na jānāti, na ca pubbāparakusalau hauti – imaihi khau, upāli, pañ‍cahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgatau bhikkhu bālautvaiva saṅkhaṃ gacchati| pañ‍cahupāli, aṅgaihi saman‍nāgatau bhikkhu paṇḍitautvaiva saṅkhaṃ gacchati| katamaihi pañ‍cahi? dhammaṃ jānāti, dhammānulaumaṃ jānāti, vinayaṃ jānāti, vinayānulaumaṃ jānāti, pubbāparakusalau ca hauti – imaihi khau, upāli, pañ‍cahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgatau bhikkhu paṇḍitautvaiva saṅkhaṃ gacchati|

‘‘aparaihipi, upāli, pañ‍cahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgatau bhikkhu bālautvaiva saṅkhaṃ gacchati| katamaihi pañ‍cahi? vatthuṃ na jānāti, nidānaṃ na jānāti, pañ‍ñattiṃ na jānāti, padapac‍cābhaṭṭhaṃ na jānāti, anusandhivacanapathaṃ na jānāti – imaihi khau, upāli, pañ‍cahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgatau bhikkhu bālautvaiva saṅkhaṃ gacchati| pañ‍cahupāli, aṅgaihi saman‍nāgatau bhikkhu paṇḍitautvaiva saṅkhaṃ gacchati| katamaihi pañ‍cahi? vatthuṃ jānāti, nidānaṃ jānāti, pañ‍ñattiṃ jānāti, padapac‍cābhaṭṭhaṃ jānāti, anusandhivacanapathaṃ jānāti – imaihi khau, upāli, pañ‍cahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgatau bhikkhu paṇḍitautvaiva saṅkhaṃ gacchati|

‘‘aparaihipi, upāli, pañ‍cahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgatau  bhikkhu bālautvaiva saṅkhaṃ gacchati| katamaihi pañ‍cahi? āpattiṃ na jānāti, āpattisamuṭṭhānaṃ na jānāti, āpattiyā payaugaṃ na jānāti, āpattiyā vūpasamaṃ na jānāti, āpattiyā na vinicchayakusalau hauti – imaihi khau, upāli, pañ‍cahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgatau bhikkhu bālautvaiva saṅkhaṃ gacchati| pañ‍cahupāli, aṅgaihi saman‍nāgatau bhikkhu paṇḍitautvaiva saṅkhaṃ gacchati| katamaihi pañ‍cahi? āpattiṃ jānāti, āpattisamuṭṭhānaṃ jānāti, āpattiyā payaugaṃ jānāti, āpattiyā vūpasamaṃ jānāti, āpattiyā vinicchayakusalau hauti – imaihi khau, upāli, pañ‍cahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgatau bhikkhu paṇḍitautvaiva saṅkhaṃ gacchati|

‘‘aparaihipi, upāli, pañ‍cahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgatau bhikkhu bālautvaiva saṅkhaṃ gacchati| katamaihi pañ‍cahi? adhikaraṇaṃ na jānāti, adhikaraṇasamuṭṭhānaṃ na jānāti, adhikaraṇassa payaugaṃ na jānāti, adhikaraṇassa vūpasamaṃ na jānāti, adhikaraṇassa na vinicchayakusalau hauti – imaihi khau, upāli, pañ‍cahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgatau bhikkhu bālautvaiva saṅkhaṃ gacchati| pañ‍cahupāli, aṅgaihi saman‍nāgatau bhikkhu paṇḍitautvaiva saṅkhaṃ gacchati| katamaihi pañ‍cahi? adhikaraṇaṃ jānāti, adhikaraṇasamuṭṭhānaṃ jānāti, adhikaraṇassa payaugaṃ jānāti, adhikaraṇassa vūpasamaṃ jānāti, adhikaraṇassa vinicchayakusalau hauti – imaihi khau, upāli, pañ‍cahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgatau bhikkhu paṇḍitautvaiva saṅkhaṃ gacchatī’’ti|

ubbāhikavaggau niṭṭhitau navamau|

tassuddānaṃ –

na  atthakusalau caiva, kaudhanau kuppatī ca yau|

pasāraitā chandāgatiṃ, akusalau tathaiva ca||

suttaṃ dhammañ‍ca vatthuñ‍ca, āpatti adhikaraṇaṃ|

dvai dvai pakāsitā sabbai, kaṇhasuk‍kaṃ vijānathāti||

10. adhikaraṇavūpasamavaggau

457. ‘‘katihi nu khau, bhantai, aṅgaihi saman‍nāgatau bhikkhu nālaṃ adhikaraṇaṃ vūpasamaitu’’nti? ‘‘pañ‍cahupāli, aṅgaihi saman‍nāgatau bhikkhu nālaṃ adhikaraṇaṃ vūpasamaituṃ| katamaihi pañ‍cahi? āpattiṃ  na jānāti, āpattisamuṭṭhānaṃ na jānāti, āpattiyā payaugaṃ na jānāti, āpattiyā vūpasamaṃ na jānāti, āpattiyā na vinicchayakusalau hauti – imaihi khau, upāli, pañ‍cahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgatau bhikkhu nālaṃ adhikaraṇaṃ vūpasamaituṃ| pañ‍cahupāli, aṅgaihi saman‍nāgatau bhikkhu alaṃ adhikaraṇaṃ vūpasamaituṃ| katamaihi pañ‍cahi? āpattiṃ  jānāti, āpattisamuṭṭhānaṃ jānāti, āpattiyā payaugaṃ jānāti, āpattiyā vūpasamaṃ jānāti, āpattiyā  vinicchayakusalau hauti – imaihi khau, upāli, pañ‍cahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgatau bhikkhu alaṃ adhikaraṇaṃ vūpasamaituṃ|

‘‘aparaihipi, upāli, pañ‍cahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgatau bhikkhu nālaṃ  adhikaraṇaṃ vūpasamaituṃ| katamaihi pañ‍cahi? adhikaraṇaṃ na jānāti, adhikaraṇasamuṭṭhānaṃ na jānāti, adhikaraṇassa payaugaṃ na jānāti, adhikaraṇassa vūpasamaṃ na jānāti, adhikaraṇassa na vinicchayakusalau hauti – imaihi khau, upāli, pañ‍cahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgatau bhikkhu nālaṃ adhikaraṇaṃ vūpasamaituṃ|

‘‘pañ‍cahupāli, aṅgaihi saman‍nāgatau bhikkhu alaṃ adhikaraṇaṃ vūpasamaituṃ| katamaihi pañ‍cahi? adhikaraṇaṃ jānāti, adhikaraṇasamuṭṭhānaṃ jānāti, adhikaraṇassa payaugaṃ jānāti, adhikaraṇassa vūpasamaṃ jānāti, adhikaraṇassa vinicchayakusalau hauti – imaihi khau, upāli, pañ‍cahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgatau bhikkhu alaṃ adhikaraṇaṃ vūpasamaituṃ|

‘‘aparaihipi, upāli, pañ‍cahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgatau bhikkhu nālaṃ adhikaraṇaṃ vūpasamaituṃ| katamaihi pañ‍cahi? chandāgatiṃ gacchati, dausāgatiṃ gacchati, mauhāgatiṃ gacchati, bhayāgatiṃ gacchati, alaj‍jī ca hauti – imaihi khau, upāli, pañ‍cahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgatau bhikkhu nālaṃ adhikaraṇaṃ vūpasamaituṃ| pañ‍cahupāli, aṅgaihi saman‍nāgatau bhikkhu alaṃ adhikaraṇaṃ vūpasamaituṃ| katamaihi pañ‍cahi? na chandāgatiṃ gacchati, na dausāgatiṃ gacchati, na mauhāgatiṃ gacchati, na bhayāgatiṃ gacchati, laj‍jī ca hauti – imaihi khau, upāli, pañ‍cahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgatau bhikkhu alaṃ adhikaraṇaṃ vūpasamaituṃ|

‘‘aparaihipi, upāli, pañ‍cahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgatau bhikkhu nālaṃ adhikaraṇaṃ vūpasamaituṃ| katamaihi pañ‍cahi? chandāgatiṃ gacchati, dausāgatiṃ gacchati, mauhāgatiṃ gacchati, bhayāgatiṃ gacchati, appassutau ca hauti – imaihi khau, upāli, pañ‍cahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgatau bhikkhu nālaṃ adhikaraṇaṃ vūpasamaituṃ| pañ‍cahupāli, aṅgaihi saman‍nāgatau bhikkhu alaṃ adhikaraṇaṃ vūpasamaituṃ| katamaihi pañ‍cahi? na chandāgatiṃ gacchati, na dausāgatiṃ gacchati, na mauhāgatiṃ gacchati, na bhayāgatiṃ gacchati, bahussutau ca hauti – imaihi khau, upāli, pañ‍cahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgatau bhikkhu alaṃ adhikaraṇaṃ vūpasamaituṃ|

‘‘aparaihipi, upāli, pañ‍cahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgatau bhikkhu nālaṃ adhikaraṇaṃ vūpasamaituṃ| katamaihi pañ‍cahi? vatthuṃ na jānāti, nidānaṃ na jānāti, pañ‍ñattiṃ na jānāti, padapac‍cābhaṭṭhaṃ na jānāti, anusandhivacanapathaṃ na jānāti – imaihi khau, upāli, pañ‍cahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgatau bhikkhu nālaṃ adhikaraṇaṃ vūpasamaituṃ| pañ‍cahupāli, aṅgaihi saman‍nāgatau bhikkhu alaṃ adhikaraṇaṃ vūpasamaituṃ| katamaihi pañ‍cahi? vatthuṃ jānāti, nidānaṃ jānāti, pañ‍ñattiṃ jānāti, padapac‍cābhaṭṭhaṃ jānāti, anusandhivacanapathaṃ jānāti – imaihi khau, upāli, pañ‍cahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgatau bhikkhu alaṃ adhikaraṇaṃ vūpasamaituṃ|

‘‘aparaihipi, upāli, pañ‍cahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgatau bhikkhu nālaṃ adhikaraṇaṃ vūpasamaituṃ| katamaihi pañ‍cahi? chandāgatiṃ gacchati, dausāgatiṃ gacchati, mauhāgatiṃ gacchati, bhayāgatiṃ gacchati, akusalau ca hauti vinayai – imaihi khau, upāli, pañ‍cahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgatau bhikkhu nālaṃ adhikaraṇaṃ vūpasamaituṃ| pañ‍cahupāli, aṅgaihi saman‍nāgatau bhikkhu alaṃ adhikaraṇaṃ vūpasamaituṃ| katamaihi pañ‍cahi? na chandāgatiṃ gacchati, na dausāgatiṃ gacchati, na mauhāgatiṃ gacchati, na bhayāgatiṃ gacchati, kusalau ca hauti vinayai – imaihi khau, upāli, pañ‍cahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgatau bhikkhu alaṃ adhikaraṇaṃ vūpasamaituṃ|

‘‘aparaihipi, upāli, pañ‍cahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgatau bhikkhu nālaṃ adhikaraṇaṃ vūpasamaituṃ| katamaihi pañ‍cahi? chandāgatiṃ gacchati, dausāgatiṃ gacchati, mauhāgatiṃ gacchati, bhayāgatiṃ gacchati, puggalagaru hauti nau saṅghagaru – imaihi khau, upāli, pañ‍cahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgatau bhikkhu nālaṃ adhikaraṇaṃ vūpasamaituṃ| pañ‍cahupāli, aṅgaihi saman‍nāgatau bhikkhu alaṃ adhikaraṇaṃ vūpasamaituṃ| katamaihi pañ‍cahi? na chandāgatiṃ gacchati, na dausāgatiṃ gacchati, na mauhāgatiṃ gacchati, na bhayāgatiṃ gacchati, saṅghagaru hauti nau puggalagaru – imaihi khau, upāli, pañ‍cahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgatau bhikkhu alaṃ adhikaraṇaṃ vūpasamaituṃ|

‘‘aparaihipi, upāli, pañ‍cahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgatau bhikkhu nālaṃ adhikaraṇaṃ vūpasamaituṃ| katamaihi pañ‍cahi? chandāgatiṃ gacchati, dausāgatiṃ gacchati, mauhāgatiṃ gacchati, bhayāgatiṃ gacchati, āmisagaru hauti nau saddhammagaru – imaihi khau, upāli, pañ‍cahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgatau bhikkhu nālaṃ adhikaraṇaṃ  vūpasamaituṃ| pañ‍cahupāli, aṅgaihi saman‍nāgatau bhikkhu alaṃ adhikaraṇaṃ vūpasamaituṃ| katamaihi pañ‍cahi? na chandāgatiṃ gacchati, na dausāgatiṃ gacchati, na  mauhāgatiṃ gacchati, na bhayāgatiṃ gacchati, saddhammagaru hauti nau  āmisagaru – imaihi khau, upāli, pañ‍cahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgatau bhikkhu alaṃ adhikaraṇaṃ vūpasamaitu’’nti|

458. ‘‘katihi nu khau, bhantai, ākāraihi saṅghau bhij‍jatī’’ti? ‘‘pañ‍cahupāli, ākāraihi saṅghau bhij‍jati| katamaihi pañ‍cahi? kammaina, uddaisaina, vauharantau, anussāvanaina, salākaggāhaina – imaihi khau, upāli, pañ‍cahākāraihi saṅghau bhij‍jatī’’ti|

‘‘saṅgharāji saṅgharājīti, bhantai, vuc‍cati| kittāvatā nu khau, bhantai, saṅgharāji hauti nau ca saṅghabhaidau? kittāvatā ca pana saṅgharāji caiva hauti saṅghabhaidau cā’’ti? ‘‘pañ‍ñattaitaṃ, upāli, mayā āgantukānaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ āgantukavattaṃ| aivaṃ supañ‍ñattai khau, upāli, mayā sikkhāpadai āgantukā bhikkhū āgantukavattai na vattanti| aivampi khau, upāli, saṅgharāji hauti nau ca saṅghabhaidau| pañ‍ñattaitaṃ, upāli, mayā āvāsikānaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ āvāsikavattaṃ| aivaṃ supañ‍ñattai khau, upāli, mayā sikkhāpadai āvāsikā bhikkhū āvāsikavattai na vattanti| aivampi khau, upāli, saṅgharāji hauti nau ca saṅghabhaidau| pañ‍ñattaitaṃ upāli mayā bhikkhūnaṃ bhattaggai bhattaggavattaṃ – yathāvuḍḍhaṃ yathārattaṃ yathāpatirūpaṃ aggāsanaṃ aggaudakaṃ aggapiṇḍaṃ| aivaṃ supañ‍ñattai khau, upāli, mayā sikkhāpadai navā bhikkhū bhattaggai thairānaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ āsanaṃ paṭibāhanti| aivampi khau, upāli, saṅgharāji hauti nau ca saṅghabhaidau| pañ‍ñattaitaṃ, upāli, mayā bhikkhūnaṃ saināsanai  saināsanavattaṃ – yathāvuḍḍhaṃ yathārattaṃ yathāpatirūpaṃ| aivaṃ supañ‍ñattai khau, upāli, mayā sikkhāpadai navā bhikkhū thairānaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ saināsanaṃ paṭibāhanti| aivampi khau, upāli, saṅgharāji hauti nau ca saṅghabhaidau| pañ‍ñattaitaṃ, upāli, mayā bhikkhūnaṃ antausīmāya aikaṃ upausathaṃ aikaṃ pavāraṇaṃ aikaṃ saṅghakammaṃ aikaṃ kammākammaṃ| aivaṃ supañ‍ñattai khau, upāli, mayā sikkhāpadai tatthaiva antausīmāya āvainibhāvaṃ āvaiṇibhāvaṃ (sī॰ syā॰) karitvā gaṇaṃ bandhitvā  āvainiṃ āvaiṇi (sī॰ syā॰) upausathaṃ karaunti āvainiṃ pavāraṇaṃ  karaunti āvainiṃ saṅghakammaṃ karaunti āvainiṃ kammākammāni karaunti| aivaṃ khau, upāli, saṅgharāji caiva hauti saṅghabhaidau cā’’ti|

adhikaraṇavūpasamavaggau niṭṭhitau dasamau|

tassuddānaṃ –

āpattiṃ  adhikaraṇaṃ, chandā appassutaina ca|

vatthuñ‍ca akusalau ca, puggalau āmisaina ca|

bhij‍jati saṅgharāji ca, saṅghabhaidau tathaiva cāti||

11. saṅghabhaidakavaggau

459. cūḷava॰ 354-355; a॰ ni॰ 10.38 ādayau ‘‘katihi nu khau, bhantai, aṅgaihi saman‍nāgatau saṅghabhaidakau āpāyikau nairayikau kappaṭṭhau ataikicchau’’ti? ‘‘pañ‍cahupāli, aṅgaihi saman‍nāgatau saṅghabhaidakau āpāyikau nairayikau kappaṭṭhau ataikicchau| katamaihi pañ‍cahi? idhupāli, bhikkhu adhammaṃ dhammauti dīpaiti, dhammaṃ adhammauti dīpaiti, avinayaṃ vinayauti dīpaiti, vinayaṃ avinayauti  dīpaiti, vinidhāya diṭṭhiṃ kammaina – imaihi khau, upāli, pañ‍cahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgatau saṅghabhaidakau āpāyikau nairayikau kappaṭṭhau ataikicchau|

‘‘aparaihipi, upāli, pañ‍cahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgatau saṅghabhaidakau āpāyikau nairayikau kappaṭṭhau ataikicchau| katamaihi pañ‍cahi? idhupāli, bhikkhu adhammaṃ dhammauti dīpaiti, dhammaṃ adhammauti dīpaiti, avinayaṃ vinayauti dīpaiti, vinayaṃ avinayauti dīpaiti, vinidhāya diṭṭhiṃ uddaisaina – imaihi khau, upāli, pañ‍cahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgatau saṅghabhaidakau āpāyikau nairayikau kappaṭṭhau ataikicchau|

‘‘aparaihipi, upāli, pañ‍cahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgatau saṅghabhaidakau āpāyikau nairayikau kappaṭṭhau ataikicchau| katamaihi pañ‍cahi? idhupāli, bhikkhu adhammaṃ dhammauti dīpaiti, dhammaṃ adhammauti dīpaiti, avinayaṃ  vinayauti dīpaiti, vinayaṃ avinayauti dīpaiti, vinidhāya diṭṭhi vauharantau – imaihi khau, upāli, pañ‍cahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgatau saṅghabhaidakau āpāyikau nairayikau kappaṭṭhau ataikicchau|

‘‘aparaihipi, upāli, pañ‍cahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgatau saṅghabhaidakau āpāyikau nairayikau kappaṭṭhau ataikicchau| katamaihi pañ‍cahi? idhupāli, bhikkhu adhammaṃ dhammauti dīpaiti, dhammaṃ adhammauti dīpaiti, avinayaṃ vinayauti dīpaiti, vinayaṃ avinayauti dīpaiti, vinidhāya diṭṭhiṃ  anussāvanaina – imaihi khau, upāli, pañ‍cahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgatau saṅghabhaidakau āpāyikau nairayikau kappaṭṭhau ataikicchau|

‘‘aparaihipi, upāli, pañ‍cahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgatau saṅghabhaidakau āpāyikau nairayikau kappaṭṭhau ataikicchau| katamaihi pañ‍cahi? idhupāli, bhikkhu adhammaṃ dhammauti dīpaiti, dhammaṃ adhammauti dīpaiti, avinayaṃ vinayauti dīpaiti, vinayaṃ avinayauti dīpaiti, vinidhāya diṭṭhiṃ salākaggāhaina – imaihi khau, upāli, pañ‍cahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgatau saṅghabhaidakau āpāyikau nairayikau kappaṭṭhau ataikicchau|

‘‘aparaihipi, upāli, pañ‍cahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgatau saṅghabhaidakau āpāyikau nairayikau kappaṭṭhau ataikicchau| katamaihi pañ‍cahi? idhupāli, bhikkhu adhammaṃ dhammauti dīpaiti, dhammaṃ adhammauti dīpaiti, avinayaṃ vinayauti dīpaiti, vinayaṃ avinayauti dīpaiti, vinidhāya khantiṃ kammaina…pai॰… vinidhāya khantiṃ uddaisaina…pai॰… vinidhāya khantiṃ vauharantau…pai॰… vinidhāya khantiṃ anussāvanaina…pai॰… vinidhāya khantiṃ salākaggāhaina – imaihi khau, upāli, pañ‍cahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgatau saṅghabhaidakau āpāyikau nairayikau kappaṭṭhau ataikicchau|

‘‘aparaihipi, upāli, pañ‍cahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgatau saṅghabhaidakau āpāyikau nairayikau kappaṭṭhau ataikicchau| katamaihi pañ‍cahi? idhupāli, bhikkhu adhammaṃ dhammauti dīpaiti, dhammaṃ adhammauti dīpaiti avinayaṃ vinayauti dīpaiti, vinayaṃ avinayauti dīpaiti, vinidhāya ruciṃ kammaina…pai॰… vinidhāya ruciṃ uddaisaina…pai॰… vinidhāya ruciṃ vauharantau…pai॰… vinidhāya ruciṃ anussāvanaina…pai॰… vinidhāya ruciṃ salākaggāhaina – imaihi khau, upāli, pañ‍cahaṅgaihi  saman‍nāgatau saṅghabhaidakau āpāyikau nairayikau kappaṭṭhau ataikicchau|

‘‘aparaihipi, upāli, pañ‍cahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgatau saṅghabhaidakau āpāyikau nairayikau kappaṭṭhau ataikicchau| katamaihi pañ‍cahi? idhupāli, bhikkhu adhammaṃ dhammauti dīpaiti, dhammaṃ adhammauti dīpaiti, avinayaṃ vinayauti dīpaiti, vinayaṃ avinayauti dīpaiti, vinidhāya sañ‍ñaṃ kammaina…pai॰… vinidhāya sañ‍ñaṃ uddaisaina…pai॰… vinidhāya sañ‍ñaṃ vauharantau…pai॰… vinidhāya sañ‍ñaṃ anussāvanaina…pai॰… vinidhāya sañ‍ñaṃ salākaggāhaina – imaihi khau, upāli, pañ‍cahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgatau saṅghabhaidakau āpāyikau nairayikau kappaṭṭhau ataikicchau’’ti|

saṅghabhaidakavaggau niṭṭhitau aikādasamau|

tassuddānaṃ –

vinidhāya  diṭṭhiṃ kammaina, uddaisai vauharaina ca|

anussāvanai salākaina, pañ‍caitai diṭṭhinissitā|

khantiṃ ruciñ‍ca sañ‍ñañ‍ca, tayau tai pañ‍cadhā nayāti||

12. dutiyasaṅghabhaidakavaggau

460. ‘‘katihi nu khau, bhantai, aṅgaihi saman‍nāgatau saṅghabhaidakau na āpāyikau na nairayikau na kappaṭṭhau na ataikicchau’’ti? ‘‘pañ‍cahupāli, aṅgaihi saman‍nāgatau saṅghabhaidakau na āpāyikau na nairayikau na kappaṭṭhau na ataikicchau| katamaihi pañ‍cahi? idhupāli, bhikkhu adhammaṃ dhammauti dīpaiti, dhammaṃ adhammauti dīpaiti, avinayaṃ vinayauti dīpaiti, vinayaṃ avinayauti dīpaiti, avinidhāya diṭṭhiṃ kammaina – imaihi khau, upāli, pañ‍cahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgatau saṅghabhaidakau na āpāyikau na nairayikau na kappaṭṭhau na ataikicchau|

‘‘aparaihipi, upāli, pañ‍cahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgatau saṅghabhaidakau na āpāyikau na nairayikau na kappaṭṭhau na ataikicchau| katamaihi pañ‍cahi? idhupāli, bhikkhu adhammaṃ dhammauti dīpaiti, dhammaṃ adhammauti dīpaiti, avinayaṃ  vinayauti dīpaiti, vinayaṃ avinayauti dīpaiti, avinidhāya diṭṭhiṃ uddaisaina – imaihi khau, upāli, pañ‍cahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgatau saṅghabhaidakau na āpāyikau na nairayikau na kappaṭṭhau na ataikicchau|

‘‘aparaihipi, upāli, pañ‍cahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgatau saṅghabhaidakau na āpāyikau na nairayikau na kappaṭṭhau na ataikicchau| katamaihi pañ‍cahi? idhupāli, bhikkhu adhammaṃ dhammauti dīpaiti, dhammaṃ adhammauti dīpaiti, avinayaṃ vinayauti dīpaiti, vinayaṃ avinayauti dīpaiti, avinidhāya diṭṭhiṃ vauharantau| imaihi khau, upāli, pañ‍cahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgatau saṅghabhaidakau na āpāyikau na nairayikau na kappaṭṭhau na ataikicchau|

‘‘aparaihipi, upāli, pañ‍cahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgatau saṅghabhaidakau na āpāyikau na nairayikau na kappaṭṭhau na ataikicchau| katamaihi pañ‍cahi? idhupāli, bhikkhu adhammaṃ dhammauti dīpaiti, dhammaṃ adhammauti dīpaiti, avinayaṃ vinayauti dīpaiti, vinayaṃ avinayauti dīpaiti, avinidhāya  diṭṭhiṃ anussāvanaina – imaihi khau, upāli, pañ‍cahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgatau saṅghabhaidakau na āpāyikau na nairayikau na kappaṭṭhau na ataikicchau|

‘‘aparaihipi, upāli, pañ‍cahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgatau saṅghabhaidakau na āpāyikau na nairayikau na kappaṭṭhau na ataikicchau| katamaihi pañ‍cahi? idhupāli, bhikkhu adhammaṃ dhammauti dīpaiti, dhammaṃ adhammauti dīpaiti, avinayaṃ vinayauti dīpaiti, vinayaṃ avinayauti dīpaiti, avinidhāya diṭṭhiṃ salākaggāhaina – imaihi khau, upāli, pañ‍cahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgatau saṅghabhaidakau na āpāyikau na nairayikau na kappaṭṭhau na ataikicchau|

‘‘aparaihipi, upāli, pañ‍cahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgatau saṅghabhaidakau na āpāyikau na nairayikau na kappaṭṭhau na ataikicchau| katamaihi pañ‍cahi? idhupāli, bhikkhu adhammaṃ dhammauti dīpaiti, dhammaṃ adhammauti dīpaiti, avinayaṃ vinayauti dīpaiti, vinayaṃ avinayauti dīpaiti, avinidhāya khantiṃ kammaina…pai॰… avinidhāya khantiṃ uddaisaina…pai॰… avinidhāya khantiṃ vauharantau…pai॰… avinidhāya khantiṃ anussāvanaina…pai॰… avinidhāya khantiṃ salākaggāhaina – imaihi khau, upāli, pañ‍cahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgatau saṅghabhaidakau na āpāyikau na nairayikau na kappaṭṭhau na ataikicchau|

‘‘aparaihipi, upāli, pañ‍cahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgatau saṅghabhaidakau na āpāyikau na nairayikau na kappaṭṭhau na ataikicchau| katamaihi pañ‍cahi? idhupāli, bhikkhu adhammaṃ dhammauti dīpaiti, dhammaṃ adhammauti dīpaiti, avinayaṃ  vinayauti dīpaiti, vinayaṃ avinayauti dīpaiti, avinidhāya ruciṃ kammaina…pai॰… avinidhāya ruciṃ uddaisaina…pai॰… avinidhāya ruciṃ vauharantau…pai॰… avinidhāya ruciṃ anussāvanaina…pai॰… avinidhāya ruciṃ salākaggāhaina – imaihi khau, upāli, pañ‍cahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgatau saṅghabhaidakau na āpāyikau na nairayikau na kappaṭṭhau na ataikicchau|

‘‘aparaihipi, upāli, pañ‍cahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgatau saṅghabhaidakau na āpāyikau na nairayikau na kappaṭṭhau na ataikicchau| katamaihi pañ‍cahi? idhupāli, bhikkhu adhammaṃ dhammauti dīpaiti, dhammaṃ adhammauti dīpaiti, avinayaṃ vinayauti dīpaiti, vinayaṃ avinayauti dīpaiti, avinidhāya sañ‍ñaṃ kammaina…pai॰… avinidhāya sañ‍ñaṃ uddaisaina…pai॰… avinidhāya sañ‍ñaṃ vauharantau…pai॰… avinidhāya sañ‍ñaṃ anussāvanaina…pai॰… avinidhāya sañ‍ñaṃ salākaggāhaina  – imaihi khau, upāli, pañ‍cahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgatau saṅghabhaidakau na āpāyikau na nairayikau na kappaṭṭhau na ataikicchau’’ti|

dutiyasaṅghabhaidakavaggau niṭṭhitau dvādasamau|

tassuddānaṃ –

avinidhāya diṭṭhiṃ kammaina, uddaisai vauharaina ca|

anussāvanai salākaina, pañ‍caitai diṭṭhinissitā||

khantiṃ ruciñ‍ca sañ‍ñañ‍ca, tayau tai pañ‍cadhā nayā||

haiṭṭhimai kaṇhapakkhamhi, samavīsati vidhī yathā|

tathaiva suk‍kapakkhamhi, samavīsati jānathāti||

13. āvāsikavaggau

461. ‘‘katihi  nu khau, bhantai, aṅgaihi saman‍nāgatau āvāsikau bhikkhu yathābhataṃ nikkhittau aivaṃ nirayai’’ti? ‘‘pañ‍cahupāli, aṅgaihi saman‍nāgatau āvāsikau bhikkhu yathābhataṃ nikkhittau aivaṃ nirayai| katamaihi pañ‍cahi? chandāgatiṃ gacchati, dausāgatiṃ  gacchati, mauhāgatiṃ gacchati, bhayāgatiṃ gacchati, saṅghikaṃ puggalikaparibhaugaina paribhuñ‍jati – imaihi khau, upāli, pañ‍cahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgatau āvāsikau bhikkhu yathābhataṃ nikkhittau aivaṃ nirayai|

‘‘pañ‍cahupāli, aṅgaihi saman‍nāgatau āvāsikau bhikkhu yathābhataṃ nikkhittau aivaṃ saggai| katamaihi pañ‍cahi? na chandāgatiṃ gacchati, na dausāgatiṃ gacchati, na mauhāgatiṃ gacchati, na bhayāgatiṃ gacchati, saṅghikaṃ na puggalikaparibhaugaina paribhuñ‍jati – imaihi khau, upāli, pañ‍cahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgatau āvāsikau bhikkhu yathābhataṃ nikkhittau aivaṃ saggai’’ti|

462. ‘‘kati nu khau, bhantai, adhammikā vinayabyākaraṇā’’ti? ‘‘pañ‍cimai, upāli, adhammikā vinayabyākaraṇā| katamai pañ‍ca? idhupāli, bhikkhu adhammaṃ dhammauti pariṇāmaiti, dhammaṃ adhammauti pariṇāmaiti, avinayaṃ vinayauti pariṇāmaiti, vinayaṃ avinayauti pariṇāmaiti, apañ‍ñattaṃ pañ‍ñāpaiti, pañ‍ñattaṃ samucchindati – imai khau, upāli, pañ‍ca adhammikā vinayabyākaraṇā| pañ‍cimai, upāli, dhammikā vinayabyākaraṇā| katamai pañ‍ca? idhupāli, bhikkhu adhammaṃ adhammauti pariṇāmaiti, dhammaṃ dhammauti pariṇāmaiti, avinayaṃ  avinayauti pariṇāmaiti, vinayaṃ vinayauti pariṇāmaiti, apañ‍ñattaṃ na pañ‍ñapaiti, pañ‍ñattaṃ na samucchindati – imai khau, upāli, pañ‍ca dhammikā vinayabyākaraṇā’’ti|

463. a॰ ni॰ 5.272-285 ‘‘katihi nu khau, bhantai, aṅgaihi saman‍nāgatau bhattuddaisakau yathābhataṃ nikkhittau aivaṃ nirayai’’ti? ‘‘pañ‍cahupāli, aṅgaihi saman‍nāgatau bhattuddaisakau yathābhataṃ nikkhittau aivaṃ nirayai| katamaihi pañ‍cahi? chandāgatiṃ gacchati, dausāgatiṃ gacchati, mauhāgatiṃ gacchati, bhayāgatiṃ gacchati, uddiṭṭhānuddiṭṭhaṃ na jānāti – imaihi khau, upāli, pañ‍cahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgatau bhattuddaisakau yathābhataṃ nikkhittau aivaṃ nirayai|

a॰ ni॰ 5.272-285 ‘‘pañ‍cahupāli, aṅgaihi saman‍nāgatau bhattuddaisakau yathābhataṃ nikkhittau aivaṃ saggai| katamaihi pañ‍cahi? na chandāgatiṃ gacchati, na dausāgatiṃ gacchati, na mauhāgatiṃ gacchati, na bhayāgatiṃ gacchati, uddiṭṭhānuddiṭṭhaṃ  jānāti – imaihi khau, upāli, pañ‍cahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgatau bhattuddaisakau yathābhataṃ nikkhittau aivaṃ saggai’’ti|

464. ‘‘katihi nu khau, bhantai, aṅgaihi saman‍nāgatau saināsanapañ‍ñāpakau…pai॰… bhaṇḍāgārikau…pai॰… cīvarapaṭiggāhakau…pai॰… cīvarabhājakau…pai॰… yāgubhājakau…pai॰… phalabhājakau…pai॰… khaj‍jabhājakau…pai॰… appamattakavissaj‍jakau…pai॰… sāṭiyaggāhāpakau…pai॰… pattaggāhāpakau…pai॰… ārāmikapaisakau…pai॰… sāmaṇairapaisakau yathābhataṃ nikkhittau aivaṃ nirayai’’ti? ‘‘pañ‍cahupāli, aṅgaihi saman‍nāgatau sāmaṇairapaisakau yathābhataṃ nikkhittau aivaṃ nirayai| katamaihi pañ‍cahi? chandāgatiṃ  gacchati, dausāgatiṃ gacchati, mauhāgatiṃ gacchati, bhayāgatiṃ gacchati, paisitāpaisitaṃ na jānāti – imaihi khau, upāli, pañ‍cahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgatau sāmaṇairapaisakau yathābhataṃ nikkhittau aivaṃ nirayai| pañ‍cahupāli, aṅgaihi saman‍nāgatau sāmaṇairapaisakau yathābhataṃ nikkhittau aivaṃ saggai| katamaihi pañ‍cahi? na chandāgatiṃ gacchati, na dausāgatiṃ gacchati, na mauhāgatiṃ gacchati, na bhayāgatiṃ gacchati, paisitāpaisitaṃ jānāti – imaihi khau, upāli, pañ‍cahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgatau sāmaṇairapaisakau yathābhataṃ nikkhittau aivaṃ saggai’’ti|

āvāsikavaggau niṭṭhitau tairasamau|

tassuddānaṃ –

āvāsikabyākaraṇā, bhattusaināsanāni ca|

bhaṇḍacīvaraggāhau ca, cīvarassa ca bhājakau||

yāgu phalaṃ khaj‍jakañ‍ca, appasāṭiyagāhakau|

pattau ārāmikau caiva, sāmaṇairaina paisakauti||

14. kathinatthāravaggau

465. ‘‘kati nu khau, bhantai, ānisaṃsā kathinatthārai’’ti? ‘‘pañ‍cimai, upāli, ānisaṃsā kathinatthārai| katamai pañ‍ca? anāmantacārau, asamādānacārau, gaṇabhaujanaṃ, yāvadatthacīvaraṃ, yau ca tattha cīvaruppādau  sau naisaṃ bhavissati – imai khau, upāli, pañ‍ca ānisaṃsā kathinatthārai’’ti|

466. ‘‘kati nu khau, bhantai, ādīnavā muṭṭhassatissa asampajānassa niddaṃ  auk‍kamatau’’ti? ‘‘pañ‍cimai, upāli, ādīnavā muṭṭhassatissa asampajānassa niddaṃ auk‍kamatau| katamai pañ‍ca? dukkhaṃ supati, dukkhaṃ paṭibujjhati, pāpakaṃ supinaṃ passati, daivatā na rakkhanti, asuci muc‍cati – imai khau, upāli, pañ‍ca ādīnavā muṭṭhassatissa asampajānassa niddaṃ auk‍kamatau| pañ‍cimai, upāli, ānisaṃsā upaṭṭhitassatissa sampajānassa niddaṃ auk‍kamatau| katamai pañ‍ca? sukhaṃ supati, sukhaṃ paṭibujjhati, na pāpakaṃ supinaṃ passati, daivatā rakkhanti, asuci na muc‍cati – imai khau, upāli, pañ‍ca ānisaṃsā upaṭṭhitassatissa sampajānassa niddaṃ auk‍kamatau’’ti|

467. ‘‘kati nu khau, bhantai, avandiyā’’ti? ‘‘pañ‍cimai, upāli, avandiyā| katamai pañ‍ca? antaragharaṃ paviṭṭhau avandiyau, racchagatau avandiyau, autamasikau avandiyau, asaman‍nāharantau avandiyau, suttau avandiyau – imai khau, upāli, pañ‍ca avandiyā|

‘‘aparaipi, upāli, pañ‍ca avandiyā| katamai pañ‍ca? yāgupānai avandiyau, bhattaggai avandiyau, aikāvattau avandiyau, añ‍ñavihitau avandiyau, naggau avandiyau – imai khau, upāli, pañ‍ca avandiyā|

‘‘aparaipi, upāli, pañ‍ca avandiyā| katamai pañ‍ca? khādantau avandiyau, bhuñ‍jantau avandiyau, uc‍cāraṃ karauntau avandiyau, passāvaṃ karauntau avandiyau, ukkhittakau avandiyau – imai khau, upāli, pañ‍ca avandiyā|

‘‘aparaipi, upāli, pañ‍ca avandiyā| katamai pañ‍ca? purai upasampan‍naina pacchā upasampan‍nau avandiyau, anupasampan‍nau avandiyau, nānāsaṃvāsakau vuḍḍhatarau adhammavādī avandiyau, mātugāmau avandiyau, paṇḍakau avandiyau – imai khau, upāli, pañ‍ca avandiyā|

‘‘aparaipi, upāli, pañ‍ca avandiyā| katamai pañ‍ca? pārivāsikau avandiyau, mūlāyapaṭikassanārahau avandiyau, mānattārahau avandiyau, mānattacārikau avandiyau, abbhānārahau avandiyau – imai khau, upāli, pañ‍ca avandiyā’’ti|

468. ‘‘kati  nu khau, bhantai, vandiyā’’ti? ‘‘pañ‍cimai, upāli, vandiyā| katamai pañ‍ca? pacchā upasampan‍naina purai upasampan‍nau vandiyau, nānāsaṃvāsakau vuḍḍhatarau dhammavādī vandiyau, ācariyau vandiyau, upajjhāyau vandiyau, sadaivakai laukai samārakai sabrahmakai sassamaṇabrāhmaṇiyā pajāya sadaivamanussāya tathāgatau arahaṃ sammāsambuddhau vandiyau – imai khau, upāli, pañ‍ca vandiyā’’ti|

469. ‘‘navakataraina, bhantai, bhikkhunā vuḍḍhatarassa bhikkhunau pādai vandantaina kati dhammai ajjhattaṃ upaṭṭhāpaitvā pādā vanditabbā’’ti? ‘‘navakatarainupāli, bhikkhunā vuḍḍhatarassa bhikkhunau pādai vandantaina pañ‍ca dhammai ajjhattaṃ upaṭṭhāpaitvā pādā vanditabbā| katamai pañ‍ca? navakatarainupāli, bhikkhunā vuḍḍhatarassa bhikkhunau pādai vandantaina aikaṃsaṃ uttarāsaṅgaṃ karitvā, añ‍jaliṃ paggahaitvā, ubhauhi pāṇitalaihi pādāni parisambāhantaina, paimañ‍ca gāravañ‍ca upaṭṭhāpaitvā pādā vanditabbā – navakatarainupāli bhikkhunā vuḍḍhatarassa bhikkhunau pādai vandantaina imai pañ‍ca dhammai ajjhattaṃ upaṭṭhāpaitvā pādā vanditabbā’’ti|

kathinatthāravaggau niṭṭhitau cuddasamau|

tassuddānaṃ –

kathinatthāraniddā  ca, antarā yāgukhādanai|

purai ca pārivāsi ca, vandiyau vanditabbakanti||

upālipañ‍cakaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|


taisaṃ vaggānaṃ uddānaṃ

anissitaina kammañ‍ca, vauhārāvikammaina ca|

caudanā ca dhutaṅgā ca, musā bhikkhunimaiva ca||

ubbāhikādhikaraṇaṃ, bhaidakā pañ‍camā purai|

āvāsikā kathinañ‍ca, cuddasā suppakāsitāti||


atthāpattisamuṭṭhānaṃ

1. pārājikaṃ

470. atthāpatti  acittakau āpaj‍jati, sacittakau vuṭṭhāti| atthāpatti sacittakau āpaj‍jati, acittakau vuṭṭhāti| atthāpatti acittakau āpaj‍jati, acittakau vuṭṭhāti| atthāpatti sacittakau āpaj‍jati, sacittakau vuṭṭhāti| atthāpatti kusalacittau āpaj‍jati, kusalacittau vuṭṭhāti| atthāpatti kusalacittau āpaj‍jati, akusalacittau vuṭṭhāti| atthāpatti kusalacittau āpaj‍jati, abyākatacittau vuṭṭhāti| atthāpatti akusalacittau āpaj‍jati, kusalacittau vuṭṭhāti| atthāpatti akusalacittau āpaj‍jati, akusalacittau vuṭṭhāti| atthāpatti akusalacittau āpaj‍jati, abyākatacittau vuṭṭhāti| atthāpatti abyākatacittau āpaj‍jati, kusalacittau vuṭṭhāti| atthāpatti abyākatacittau āpaj‍jati, akusalacittau vuṭṭhāti| atthāpatti abyākatacittau āpaj‍jati, abyākatacittau vuṭṭhāti|

paṭhamaṃ pārājikaṃ katihi samuṭṭhānaihi samuṭṭhāti? paṭhamaṃ pārājikaṃ aikaina samuṭṭhānaina samuṭṭhāti| kāyatau ca cittatau ca samuṭṭhāti, na vācatau|

dutiyaṃ pārājikaṃ katihi samuṭṭhānaihi samuṭṭhāti? dutiyaṃ pārājikaṃ tīhi samuṭṭhānaihi samuṭṭhāti – siyā kāyatau ca cittatau ca samuṭṭhāti, na vācatau; siyā vācatau ca cittatau ca samuṭṭhāti, na kāyatau; siyā kāyatau ca  vācatau ca cittatau ca samuṭṭhāti|

tatiyaṃ pārājikaṃ katihi samuṭṭhānaihi samuṭṭhāti? tatiyaṃ pārājikaṃ tīhi samuṭṭhānaihi samuṭṭhāti – siyā kāyatau ca cittatau ca samuṭṭhāti, na vācatau; siyā vācatau ca cittatau ca samuṭṭhāti, na kāyatau; siyā kāyatau ca vācatau ca cittatau ca samuṭṭhāti|

catutthaṃ  pārājikaṃ katihi samuṭṭhānaihi samuṭṭhāti? catutthaṃ pārājikaṃ tīhi samuṭṭhānaihi samuṭṭhāti – siyā kāyatau ca cittatau ca samuṭṭhāti, na vācatau; siyā vācatau ca cittatau ca samuṭṭhāti, na kāyatau; siyā kāyatau ca vācatau ca cittatau ca samuṭṭhāti|

cattārau pārājikā niṭṭhitā|

2. saṅghādisaisaṃ

471. upak‍kamitvā asuciṃ maucaintassa saṅghādisaisau katihi samuṭṭhānaihi samuṭṭhāti? upak‍kamitvā asuciṃ maucaintassa saṅghādisaisau aikaina samuṭṭhānaina samuṭṭhāti – kāyatau ca cittatau ca samuṭṭhāti, na vācatau|

mātugāmaina saddhiṃ kāyasaṃsaggaṃ samāpaj‍jantassa saṅghādisaisau katihi samuṭṭhānaihi samuṭṭhāti? mātugāmaina saddhiṃ kāyasaṃsaggaṃ samāpaj‍jantassa saṅghādisaisau aikaina samuṭṭhānaina samuṭṭhāti – kāyatau ca cittatau ca samuṭṭhāti, na vācatau|

mātugāmaṃ duṭṭhul‍lāhi vācāhi aubhāsaintassa saṅghādisaisau katihi samuṭṭhānaihi samuṭṭhāti? mātugāmaṃ duṭṭhul‍lāhi vācāhi aubhāsaintassa saṅghādisaisau tīhi samuṭṭhānaihi samuṭṭhāti – siyā kāyatau ca  cittatau ca samuṭṭhāti na vācatau; siyā vācatau  ca cittatau ca samuṭṭhāti, na kāyatau; siyā kāyatau ca vācatau ca cittatau ca samuṭṭhāti|

mātugāmassa santikai attakāmapāricariyāya vaṇṇaṃ bhāsantassa saṅghādisaisau katihi samuṭṭhānaihi samuṭṭhāti? mātugāmassa santikai attakāmapāricariyāya vaṇṇaṃ bhāsantassa saṅghādisaisau tīhi samuṭṭhānaihi samuṭṭhāti…pai॰…|

sañ‍carittaṃ samāpaj‍jantassa saṅghādisaisau katihi samuṭṭhānaihi samuṭṭhāti? sañ‍carittaṃ samāpaj‍jantassa saṅghādisaisau chahi samuṭṭhānaihi samuṭṭhāti – siyā kāyatau samuṭṭhāti, na vācatau na cittatau; siyā vācatau samuṭṭhāti, na kāyatau na cittatau; siyā kāyatau ca vācatau ca samuṭṭhāti, na cittatau; siyā kāyatau ca cittatau ca samuṭṭhāti, na vācatau; siyā  vācatau ca cittatau ca samuṭṭhāti, na kāyatau; siyā kāyatau ca vācatau ca cittatau ca samuṭṭhāti|

sañ‍ñācikāya kuṭiṃ kārāpaintassa saṅghādisaisau katihi samuṭṭhānaihi samuṭṭhāti? sañ‍ñācikāya kuṭiṃ kārāpaintassa saṅghādisaisau chahi samuṭṭhānaihi samuṭṭhāti…pai॰…|

mahal‍lakaṃ vihāraṃ kārāpaintassa saṅghādisaisau katihi samuṭṭhānaihi samuṭṭhāti? mahal‍lakaṃ vihāraṃ kārāpaintassa saṅghādisaisau chahi samuṭṭhānaihi samuṭṭhāti…pai॰…|

bhikkhuṃ amūlakaina pārājikaina dhammaina anuddhaṃsaintassa saṅghādisaisau katihi samuṭṭhānaihi samuṭṭhāti? bhikkhuṃ amūlakaina pārājikaina dhammaina anuddhaṃsaintassa saṅghādisaisau tīhi samuṭṭhānaihi  samuṭṭhāti…pai॰…|

bhikkhuṃ añ‍ñabhāgiyassa adhikaraṇassa kiñ‍ci daisaṃ laisamattaṃ upādāya pārājikaina dhammaina anuddhaṃsaintassa saṅghādisaisau katihi samuṭṭhānaihi samuṭṭhāti? bhikkhuṃ añ‍ñabhāgiyassa adhikaraṇassa kiñ‍ci daisaṃ laisamattaṃ upādāya pārājikaina dhammaina anuddhaṃsaintassa saṅghādisaisau tīhi samuṭṭhānaihi samuṭṭhāti…pai॰…|

saṅghabhaidakassa bhikkhunau yāvatatiyaṃ samanubhāsanāya na paṭinissaj‍jantassa saṅghādisaisau katihi samuṭṭhānaihi samuṭṭhāti? saṅghabhaidakassa bhikkhunau yāvatatiyaṃ samanubhāsanāya na paṭinissaj‍jantassa saṅghādisaisau aikaina samuṭṭhānaina samuṭṭhāti – kāyatau ca vācatau ca cittatau ca samuṭṭhāti|

bhaidakānuvattakānaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ yāvatatiyaṃ samanubhāsanāya na paṭinissaj‍jantānaṃ saṅghādisaisau katihi samuṭṭhānaihi samuṭṭhāti? bhaidakānuvattakānaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ yāvatatiyaṃ samanubhāsanāya na paṭinissaj‍jantānaṃ saṅghādisaisau aikaina samuṭṭhānaina samuṭṭhāti – kāyatau ca vācatau ca cittatau ca samuṭṭhāti|

dubbacassa bhikkhunau yāvatatiyaṃ samanubhāsanāya na paṭinissaj‍jantassa saṅghādisaisau katihi samuṭṭhānaihi samuṭṭhāti? dubbacassa bhikkhunau yāvatatiyaṃ samanubhāsanāya na paṭinissaj‍jantassa  saṅghādisaisau aikaina samuṭṭhānaina samuṭṭhāti – kāyatau ca vācatau ca cittatau ca samuṭṭhāti|

kuladūsakassa  bhikkhunau yāvatatiyaṃ samanubhāsanāya na paṭinissaj‍jantassa saṅghādisaisau katihi samuṭṭhānaihi samuṭṭhāti? kuladūsakassa bhikkhunau yāvatatiyaṃ samanubhāsanāya  na paṭinissaj‍jantassa saṅghādisaisau aikaina samuṭṭhānaina samuṭṭhāti – kāyatau ca vācatau ca cittatau ca samuṭṭhāti|

tairasa saṅghādisaisā niṭṭhitā|

472. …pai॰… anādariyaṃ paṭic‍ca udakai uc‍cāraṃ vā passāvaṃ vā khaiḷaṃ vā karauntassa duk‍kaṭaṃ katihi samuṭṭhānaihi samuṭṭhāti? anādariyaṃ paṭic‍ca udakai uc‍cāraṃ vā passāvaṃ vā khaiḷaṃ vā karauntassa duk‍kaṭaṃ aikaina samuṭṭhānaina samuṭṭhāti – kāyatau ca cittatau ca samuṭṭhāti, na vācatau|

saikhiyā niṭṭhitā|

3. pārājikādi

473. cattārau pārājikā katihi samuṭṭhānaihi samuṭṭhanti? cattārau pārājikā tīhi samuṭṭhānaihi samuṭṭhanti – siyā kāyatau ca cittatau ca samuṭṭhanti, na vācatau; siyā vācatau ca cittatau ca samuṭṭhanti, na kāyatau; siyā kāyatau ca vācatau ca cittatau ca samuṭṭhanti|

tairasa  saṅghādisaisā katihi samuṭṭhānaihi samuṭṭhanti? tairasa saṅghādisaisā chahi samuṭṭhānaihi samuṭṭhanti – siyā kāyatau samuṭṭhanti, na vācatau na cittatau; siyā vācatau samuṭṭhanti, na kāyatau na cittatau; siyā kāyatau ca vācatau na samuṭṭhanti, na cittatau; siyā kāyatau ca cittatau ca samuṭṭhanti, na vācatau; siyā vācatau ca cittatau ca samuṭṭhanti, na kāyatau; siyā  kāyatau ca vācatau ca cittatau ca samuṭṭhanti|

dvai aniyatā katihi samuṭṭhānaihi samuṭṭhanti? dvai aniyatā tīhi samuṭṭhānaihi samuṭṭhanti  – siyā kāyatau ca cittatau ca samuṭṭhanti, na vācatau;  siyā vācatau ca cittatau ca samuṭṭhanti, na kāyatau; siyā kāyatau ca vācatau ca cittatau ca samuṭṭhanti|

tiṃsa nissaggiyā pācittiyā katihi samuṭṭhānaihi samuṭṭhanti? tiṃsa nissaggiyā pācittiyā chahi samuṭṭhānaihi samuṭṭhanti – siyā kāyatau samuṭṭhanti, na vācatau na cittatau; siyā vācatau samuṭṭhanti, na kāyatau na cittatau; siyā kāyatau ca vācatau ca samuṭṭhanti, na cittatau; siyā kāyatau ca cittatau ca samuṭṭhanti, na vācatau; siyā vācatau ca cittatau ca samuṭṭhanti, na kāyatau; siyā kāyatau ca vācatau ca cittatau ca samuṭṭhanti|

dvainavuti pācittiyā katihi samuṭṭhānaihi samuṭṭhanti? dvainavuti pācittiyā chahi samuṭṭhānaihi samuṭṭhanti – siyā kāyatau samuṭṭhanti, na vācatau na cittatau; siyā vācatau samuṭṭhanti, na kāyatau na cittatau; siyā kāyatau ca vācatau ca samuṭṭhanti, na cittatau; siyā kāyatau ca cittatau ca samuṭṭhanti na vācatau; siyā vācatau ca cittatau ca samuṭṭhanti, na kāyatau; siyā  kāyatau ca vācatau ca cittatau ca samuṭṭhanti|

cattārau pāṭidaisanīyā katihi samuṭṭhānaihi samuṭṭhanti? cattārau pāṭidaisanīyā catūhi samuṭṭhānaihi samuṭṭhanti – siyā kāyatau samuṭṭhanti, na vācatau na cittatau; siyā kāyatau ca vācatau ca samuṭṭhanti, na cittatau; siyā kāyatau ca cittatau ca samuṭṭhanti, na vācatau; siyā kāyatau ca vācatau ca cittatau ca samuṭṭhanti|

pañ‍casattati saikhiyā katihi samuṭṭhānaihi samuṭṭhanti? pañ‍casattati saikhiyā tīhi samuṭṭhānaihi samuṭṭhanti – siyā kāyatau ca cittatau ca samuṭṭhanti, na vācatau; siyā vācatau ca cittatau ca samuṭṭhanti, na kāyatau; siyā kāyatau ca vācatau ca cittatau ca samuṭṭhanti|

samuṭṭhānaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|


tassuddānaṃ –

acittakusalā caiva, samuṭṭhānañ‍ca sabbathā|

yathādhammaina ñāyaina, samuṭṭhānaṃ vijānathāti||


dutiyagāthāsaṅgaṇikaṃ

1. kāyikādiāpatti

474. kati  āpattiyau kāyikā, kati vācasikā katā|

chādaintassa kati āpattiyau, kati saṃsaggapac‍cayā||

chāpattiyau kāyikā, cha vācasikā katā|

chādaintassa tissau āpattiyau, pañ‍ca saṃsaggapac‍cayā||

aruṇuggai kati āpattiyau, kati yāvatatiyakā|

kataittha aṭṭha vatthukā, katihi sabbasaṅgahau||

aruṇuggai tissau āpattiyau, dvai yāvatatiyakā|

aikaittha aṭṭha vatthukā, aikaina sabbasaṅgahau||

vinayassa kati mūlāni, yāni buddhaina pañ‍ñattā|

vinayagarukā kati vuttā, duṭṭhul‍lacchādanā kati||

vinayassa dvai mūlāni, yāni buddhaina pañ‍ñattā|

vinayagarukā dvai vuttā, dvai duṭṭhul‍lacchādanā||

gāmantarai kati āpattiyau, kati nadipārapac‍cayā|

katimaṃsaisu thul‍lac‍cayaṃ, katimaṃsaisu duk‍kaṭaṃ||

gāmantarai  catassau āpattiyau, catassau nadipārapac‍cayā|

aikamaṃsai thul‍lac‍cayaṃ, navamaṃsaisu duk‍kaṭaṃ||

kati vācasikā rattiṃ, kati vācasikā divā|

dadamānassa kati āpattiyau, paṭiggaṇhantassa kittakā||

dvai  vācasikā rattiṃ, dvai vācasikā divā|

dadamānassa tissau āpattiyau, cattārau ca paṭiggahai||

2. daisanāgāminiyādiāpatti

475.

 kati daisanāgāminiyau, kati sappaṭikammā katā|

kataittha appaṭikammā vuttā, buddhainādic‍cabandhunā||

pañ‍ca  daisanāgāminiyau, cha sappaṭikammā katā|

aikaittha appaṭikammā vuttā, buddhainādic‍cabandhunā||

vinayagarukā kati vuttā, kāyavācasikāni ca|

kati vikālai dhañ‍ñarasau, kati ñatticatutthaina sammuti||

vinayagarukā dvai vuttā, kāyavācasikāni ca|

aikau vikālai dhañ‍ñarasau, aikā ñatticatutthaina sammuti||

pārājikā kāyikā kati, kati saṃvāsakabhūmiyau|

katinaṃ  ratticchaidau, pañ‍ñattā dvaṅgulā kati||

pārājikā kāyikā dvai, dvai saṃvāsakabhūmiyau|

dvin‍naṃ ratticchaidau, pañ‍ñattā dvaṅgulā duvai||

katattānaṃ  vadhitvāna, katihi saṅghau bhij‍jati|

kataittha paṭhamāpattikā, ñattiyā karaṇā kati||

dvai attānaṃ vadhitvāna, dvīhi saṅghau bhij‍jati|

dvaittha paṭhamāpattikā, ñattiyā karaṇā duvai||

pāṇātipātai kati āpattiyau, vācā pārājikā kati|

aubhāsanā  kati vuttā, sañ‍carittaina vā kati||

pāṇātipātai tissau āpattiyau|

vācā pārājikā tayau|

aubhāsanā tayau vuttā|

sañ‍carittaina vā tayau||

kati puggalā na upasampādaitabbā, kati kammānaṃ saṅgahā|

nāsitakā kati vuttā, katinaṃ aikavācikā||

tayau puggalā na upasampādaitabbā, tayau kammānaṃ saṅgahā|

nāsitakā tayau vuttā, tiṇṇan‍naṃ aikavācikā||

adin‍nādānai kati āpattiyau, kati maithunapac‍cayā|

chindantassa kati āpattiyau, kati chaḍḍitapac‍cayā||

adin‍nādānai tissau āpattiyau, catassau maithunapac‍cayā|

chindantassa tissau āpattiyau, pañ‍ca chaḍḍitapac‍cayā||

bhikkhunauvādakavaggasmiṃ, pācittiyaina duk‍kaṭā|

kataittha navakā vuttā, katinaṃ cīvaraina ca||

bhikkhunauvādakavaggasmiṃ, pācittiyaina duk‍kaṭā katā|

caturaittha navakā vuttā, dvin‍naṃ cīvaraina ca||

bhikkhunīnañ‍ca akkhātā, pāṭidaisaniyā kati|

bhuñ‍jantāmakadhañ‍ñaina, pācittiyaina duk‍kaṭā kati||

bhikkhunīnañ‍ca akkhātā, aṭṭha pāṭidaisanīyā katā|

bhuñ‍jantāmakadhañ‍ñaina, pācittiyaina duk‍kaṭā katā||

gacchantassa kati āpattiyau, ṭhitassa cāpi kittakā|

nisin‍nassa  kati āpattiyau, nipan‍nassāpi kittakā||

gacchantassa catassau āpattiyau, ṭhitassa cāpi tattakā|

nisin‍nassa catassau āpattiyau, nipan‍nassāpi tattakā||

3. pācittiyaṃ

476.

 kati pācittiyāni, sabbāni nānāvatthukāni|

apubbaṃ acarimaṃ, āpaj‍jaiyya aikatau||

pañ‍ca pācittiyāni, sabbāni nānāvatthukāni|

apubbaṃ acarimaṃ, āpaj‍jaiyya aikatau||

kati pācittiyāni, sabbāni nānāvatthukāni|

apubbaṃ acarimaṃ, āpaj‍jaiyya aikatau||

na  pācittiyāni, sabbāni nānāvatthukāni|

apubbaṃ acarimaṃ, āpaj‍jaiyya aikatau||

kati  pācittiyāni, sabbāni nānāvatthukāni|

kati vācāya daisaiyya, vuttā ādic‍cabandhunā||

pañ‍ca pācittiyāni, sabbāni nānāvatthukāni|

aikavācāya daisaiyya, vuttā ādic‍cabandhunā||

kati pācittiyāni, sabbāni nānāvatthukāni|

kati vācāya daisaiyya, vuttā ādic‍cabandhunā||

nava  pācittiyāni, sabbāni nānāvatthukāni|

aikavācāya daisaiyya, vuttā ādic‍cabandhunā||

kati pācittiyāni, sabbāni nānāvatthukāni|

kiñ‍ca  kittaitvā daisaiyya, vuttā ādic‍cabandhunā||

pañ‍ca pācittiyāni, sabbāni nānāvatthukāni|

vatthuṃ kittaitvā daisaiyya, vuttā ādic‍cabandhunā||

kati pācittiyāni, sabbāni nānāvatthukāni|

kiñ‍ca kittaitvā daisaiyya, vuttā ādic‍cabandhunā||

nava pācittiyāni, sabbāni nānāvatthukāni|

vatthuṃ kittaitvā daisaiyya, vuttā ādic‍cabandhunā||

yāvatatiyakai kati āpattiyau, kati vauhārapac‍cayā|

khādantassa kati āpattiyau, kati bhaujanapac‍cayā||

yāvatatiyakai tissau āpattiyau, cha vauhārapac‍cayā|

khādantassa tissau āpattiyau, pañ‍ca bhaujanapac‍cayā||

sabbā  yāvatatiyakā, kati ṭhānāni gacchanti|

katinañ‍caiva āpatti, katinaṃ adhikaraṇaina ca||

sabbā yāvatatiyakā, pañ‍ca ṭhānāni gacchanti|

pañ‍can‍nañ‍caiva āpatti, pañ‍can‍naṃ adhikaraṇaina ca||

katinaṃ vinicchayau hauti, katinaṃ vūpasamaina ca|

katinañ‍caiva anāpatti, katihi ṭhānaihi saubhati||

pañ‍can‍naṃ vinicchayau hauti, pañ‍can‍naṃ vūpasamaina ca|

pañ‍can‍nañ‍caiva anāpatti, tīhi ṭhānaihi saubhati||

kati  kāyikā rattiṃ, kati kāyikā divā|

nijjhāyantassa kati āpatti, kati piṇḍapātapac‍cayā||

dvai kāyikā rattiṃ, dvai kāyikā divā|

nijjhāyantassa aikā āpatti, aikā piṇḍapātapac‍cayā||

katānisaṃsai  sampassaṃ, paraisaṃ saddhāya daisayai|

ukkhittakā kati vuttā, kati sammāvattanā||

aṭṭhānisaṃsai sampassaṃ, paraisaṃ saddhāya daisayai|

ukkhittakā tayau vuttā, taicattālīsa sammāvattanā||

kati ṭhānai musāvādau, kati paramanti vuc‍cati|

kati  pāṭidaisanīyā, katinaṃ daisanāya ca||

pañ‍ca ṭhānai musāvādau, cuddasa paramanti vuc‍cati|

dvādasa pāṭidaisanīyā, catun‍naṃ daisanāya ca||

kataṅgikau  musāvādau, kati upausathaṅgāni|

kati dūtaiyyaṅgāni, kati titthiyavattanā||

aṭṭhaṅgikau musāvādau, aṭṭha upausathaṅgāni|

aṭṭha dūtaiyyaṅgāni, aṭṭha titthiyavattanā||

kativācikā upasampadā, katinaṃ pac‍cuṭṭhātabbaṃ|

katinaṃ āsanaṃ dātabbaṃ, bhikkhunauvādakau katihi||

aṭṭhavācikā upasampadā, aṭṭhan‍naṃ pac‍cuṭṭhātabbaṃ|

aṭṭhan‍naṃ  āsanaṃ dātabbaṃ, bhikkhunauvādakau aṭṭhahi||

katinaṃ chaij‍jaṃ hauti, katinaṃ thul‍lac‍cayaṃ|

katinañ‍caiva anāpatti, sabbaisaṃ aikavatthukā||

aikassa chaij‍jaṃ hauti, catun‍naṃ thul‍lac‍cayaṃ|

catun‍nañ‍caiva anāpatti, sabbaisaṃ aikavatthukā||

kati āghātavatthūni, katihi saṅghau bhij‍jati|

kataittha paṭhamāpattikā, ñattiyā karaṇā kati||

nava āghātavatthūni, navahi saṅghau bhij‍jati|

navaittha paṭhamāpattikā, ñattiyā karaṇā nava||

4. avandanīyapuggalādi

477.

 kati puggalā nābhivādaitabbā, añ‍jalisāmicaina ca|

katinaṃ duk‍kaṭaṃ hauti, kati cīvaradhāraṇā||

dasa  puggalā nābhivādaitabbā, añ‍jalisāmicaina ca|

dasan‍naṃ duk‍kaṭaṃ hauti, dasa cīvaradhāraṇā||

katinaṃ vassaṃvuṭṭhānaṃ, dātabbaṃ idha cīvaraṃ|

katinaṃ bhantai dātabbaṃ, katinañ‍caiva na dātabbaṃ||

pañ‍can‍naṃ vassaṃvuṭṭhānaṃ, dātabbaṃ idha cīvaraṃ|

sattan‍naṃ santai dātabbaṃ, sauḷasan‍naṃ na dātabbaṃ||

katisataṃ rattisataṃ, āpattiyau chādayitvāna|

kati rattiyau vasitvāna, muc‍caiyya pārivāsikau||

dasasataṃ rattisataṃ, āpattiyau chādayitvāna|

dasa  rattiyau vasitvāna, muc‍caiyya pārivāsikau||

kati kammadausā vuttā, buddhainādic‍cabandhunā|

campāyaṃ vinayavatthusmiṃ, sabbaiva adhammikā sabbai adhammikā (sī॰ syā॰) kati||

dvādasa kammadausā vuttā, buddhainādic‍cabandhunā|

campāyaṃ vinayavatthusmiṃ, sabbaiva adhammikā sabbaivādhammikā (sī॰), sabbai adhammikā (syā॰) katā||

kati kammasampattiyau vuttā, buddhainādic‍cabandhunā|

campāyaṃ vinayavatthusmiṃ, sabbaiva dhammikā kati||

catassau kammasampattiyau vuttā, buddhainādic‍cabandhunā|

campāyaṃ  vinayavatthusmiṃ, sabbaiva dhammikā katā||

kati kammāni vuttāni, buddhainādic‍cabandhunā|

campāyaṃ vinayavatthusmiṃ, dhammikā adhammikā kati||

cha  kammāni vuttāni, buddhainādic‍cabandhunā|

campāyaṃ vinayavatthusmiṃ, aikaittha dhammikā katā|

pañ‍ca adhammikā vuttā, buddhainādic‍cabandhunā||

kati kammāni vuttāni, buddhainādic‍cabandhunā|

campāyaṃ vinayavatthusmiṃ, dhammikā adhammikā kati||

cattāri  kammāni vuttāni, buddhainādic‍cabandhunā|

campāyaṃ vinayavatthusmiṃ, aikaittha dhammikā katā|

tayau adhammikā vuttā, buddhainādic‍cabandhunā||

yaṃ daisitaṃnantajinaina tādinā|

āpattikkhandhāni  vivaikadassinā|

kataittha sammanti vinā samathaihi|

pucchāmi taṃ brūhi vibhaṅgakauvida||

yaṃ daisitaṃnantajinaina tādinā|

āpattikkhandhāni vivaikadassinā|

aikaittha sammati vinā samathaihi|

aitaṃ tai akkhāmi vibhaṅgakauvida||

kati āpāyikā vuttā, buddhainādic‍cabandhunā|

vinayaṃ paṭijānantassa, vinayāni visayāni (sī॰ syā॰ aivamuparipi) suṇauma tai||

chaūnadiyaḍḍhasatā vuttā, buddhainādic‍cabandhunā|

āpāyikā nairayikā, kappaṭṭhā saṅghabhaidakā|

vinayaṃ paṭijānantassa, vinayāni suṇauhi mai||

kati nāpāyikā vuttā, buddhainādic‍cabandhunā|

vinayaṃ paṭijānantassa, vinayāni suṇauma tai||

aṭṭhārasa  nāpāyikā vuttā, buddhainādic‍cabandhunā|

vinayaṃ paṭijānantassa, vinayāni suṇauhi mai||

kati aṭṭhakā vuttā, buddhainādic‍cabandhunā|

vinayaṃ paṭijānantassa, vinayāni suṇauma tai||

aṭṭhārasa aṭṭhakā vuttā, buddhainādic‍cabandhunā|

vinayaṃ paṭijānantassa, vinayāni suṇauhi mai||

5. sauḷasakammādi

478.

 kati  kammāni vuttāni, buddhainādic‍cabandhunā|

vinayaṃ paṭijānantassa, vinayāni suṇauma tai||

sauḷasa  kammāni vuttāni, buddhainādic‍cabandhunā|

vinayaṃ paṭijānantassa, vinayāni suṇauhi mai||

kati kammadausā vuttā, buddhainādic‍cabandhunā|

vinayaṃ  paṭijānantassa, vinayāni suṇauma tai||

dvādasa kammadausā vuttā, buddhainādic‍cabandhunā|

vinayaṃ paṭijānantassa, vinayāni suṇauhi mai||

kati kammasampattiyau vuttā, buddhainādic‍cabandhunā|

vinayaṃ paṭijānantassa, vinayāni suṇauma tai||

catassau kammasampattiyau vuttā, buddhainādic‍cabandhunā|

vinayaṃ paṭijānantassa, vinayāni suṇauhi mai||

kati  kammāni vuttāni, buddhainādic‍cabandhunā|

vinayaṃ paṭijānantassa, vinayāni suṇauma tai||

cha kammāni vuttāni, buddhainādic‍cabandhunā|

vinayaṃ paṭijānantassa, vinayāni suṇauhi mai||

kati kammāni vuttāni, buddhainādic‍cabandhunā|

vinayaṃ paṭijānantassa, vinayāni suṇauma tai||

cattāri kammāni vuttāni, buddhainādic‍cabandhunā|

vinayaṃ paṭijānantassa, vinayāni suṇauhi mai||

kati  pārājikā vuttā, buddhainādic‍cabandhunā|

vinayaṃ paṭijānantassa, vinayāni suṇauma tai||

aṭṭha pārājikā vuttā, buddhainādic‍cabandhunā|

vinayaṃ paṭijānantassa, vinayāni suṇauhi mai||

kati saṅghādisaisā vuttā, buddhainādic‍cabandhunā|

vinayaṃ paṭijānantassa, vinayāni suṇauma tai||

taivīsa saṅghādisaisā vuttā, buddhainādic‍cabandhunā|

vinayaṃ paṭijānantassa, vinayāni suṇauhi mai||

kati  aniyatā vuttā, buddhainādic‍cabandhunā|

vinayaṃ paṭijānantassa, vinayāni suṇauma tai||

dvai aniyatā vuttā, buddhainādic‍cabandhunā|

vinayaṃ paṭijānantassa, vinayāni suṇauhi mai||

kati  nissaggiyā vuttā, buddhainādic‍cabandhunā|

vinayaṃ paṭijānantassa, vinayāni suṇauma tai||

dvaicattālīsa nissaggiyā vuttā, buddhainādic‍cabandhunā|

vinayaṃ paṭijānantassa, vinayāni suṇauhi mai||

kati pācittiyā vuttā, buddhainādic‍cabandhunā|

vinayaṃ paṭijānantassa, vinayāni suṇauma tai||

aṭṭhāsītisataṃ pācittiyā vuttā, buddhainādic‍cabandhunā|

vinayaṃ paṭijānantassa, vinayāni suṇauhi mai||

kati  pāṭidaisanīyā vuttā, buddhainādic‍cabandhunā|

vinayaṃ paṭijānantassa, vinayāni suṇauma tai||

dvādasa pāṭidaisanīyā vuttā, buddhainādic‍cabandhunā|

vinayaṃ paṭijānantassa, vinayāni suṇauhi mai||

kati saikhiyā vuttā, buddhainādic‍cabandhunā|

vinayaṃ paṭijānantassa, vinayāni suṇauma tai||

pañ‍casattati saikhiyā vuttā, buddhainādic‍cabandhunā|

vinayaṃ paṭijānantassa, vinayāni suṇauhi mai||

yāva supucchitaṃ tayā, yāva suvissaj‍jitaṃ mayā|

pucchāvissaj‍janāya vā, natthi kiñ‍ci asuttakanti||

dutiyagāthāsaṅgaṇikaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|



saidamaucanagāthā

1. avippavāsapañhā

479.

 asaṃvāsau  bhikkhūhi ca bhikkhunīhi ca|

sambhaugau aikac‍cau tahiṃ na labbhati|

avippavāsaina anāpatti|

pañhā maisā kusalaihi cintitā||

avissaj‍jiyaṃ avaibhaṅgiyaṃ|

pañ‍ca vuttā mahaisinā|

vissaj‍jantassa paribhuñ‍jantassa anāpatti|

pañhā maisā kusalaihi cintitā||

dasa puggalai na vadāmi, aikādasa vivaj‍jiya|

vuḍḍhaṃ vandantassa āpatti, pañhā maisā kusalaihi cintitā||

na ukkhittakau na ca pana pārivāsikau|

na saṅghabhin‍nau na ca pana pakkhasaṅkantau|

samānasaṃvāsakabhūmiyā ṭhitau|

kathaṃ nu sikkhāya asādhāraṇau siyā|

pañhā maisā kusalaihi cintitā||

upaiti  dhammaṃ paripucchamānau, kusalaṃ atthūpasañhitaṃ|

na  jīvati na matau na nibbutau, taṃ puggalaṃ katamaṃ vadanti buddhā|

pañhā maisā kusalaihi cintitā||

ubbhakkhakai na vadāmi, adhau nābhiṃ vivaj‍jiya|

maithunadhammapac‍cayā, kathaṃ pārājikau siyā|

pañhā maisā kusalaihi cintitā||

bhikkhu sañ‍ñācikāya kuṭiṃ karauti|

adaisitavatthukaṃ pamāṇātik‍kantaṃ|

sārambhaṃ aparik‍kamanaṃ anāpatti|

pañhā maisā kusalaihi cintitā||

bhikkhu  sañ‍ñācikāya kuṭiṃ karauti|

daisitavatthukaṃ pamāṇikaṃ|

anārambhaṃ saparik‍kamanaṃ āpatti|

pañhā maisā kusalaihi cintitā||

na kāyikaṃ kiñ‍ci payaugamācarai|

na cāpi vācāya parai bhaṇaiyya|

āpaj‍jaiyya garukaṃ chaij‍javatthuṃ|

pañhā maisā kusalaihi cintitā||

na kāyikaṃ vācasikañ‍ca kiñ‍ci|

manasāpi santau na karaiyya pāpaṃ|

sau  nāsitau kinti sunāsitau bhavai|

pañhā maisā kusalaihi cintitā||

anālapantau manujaina kainaci|

vācāgiraṃ nau ca parai bhaṇaiyya|

āpaj‍jaiyya  vācasikaṃ na kāyikaṃ|

pañhā maisā kusalaihi cintitā||

sikkhāpadā buddhavaraina vaṇṇitā|

saṅghādisaisā caturau bhavaiyyuṃ|

āpaj‍jaiyya aikapayaugaina sabbai|

pañhā maisā kusalaihi cintitā||

ubhau aikatau upasampan‍nā|

ubhin‍naṃ hatthatau cīvaraṃ paṭiggaṇhaiyya|

siyā āpattiyau nānā|

pañhā maisā kusalaihi cintitā||

caturau janā saṃvidhāya|

garubhaṇḍaṃ avāharuṃ|

tayau pārājikā aikau na pārājikau|

pañhā  maisā kusalaihi cintitā||

2. pārājikādipañhā

480.

 itthī  ca abbhantarai siyā,

bhikkhu ca bahiddhā siyā|

chiddaṃ tasmiṃ gharai natthi|

maithunadhammapac‍cayā|

kathaṃ pārājikau siyā|

pañhā maisā kusalaihi cintitā||

tailaṃ madhuṃ phāṇitañ‍cāpi sappiṃ|

sāmaṃ gahaitvāna nikkhipaiyya|

avītivattai  sattāhai|

sati pac‍cayai paribhuñ‍jantassa āpatti|

pañhā maisā kusalaihi cintitā||

nissaggiyaina āpatti|

suddhakaina pācittiyaṃ|

āpaj‍jantassa aikatau|

pañhā maisā kusalaihi cintitā||

bhikkhū siyā vīsatiyā samāgatā|

kammaṃ karaiyyuṃ samaggasañ‍ñinau|

bhikkhu siyā dvādasayaujanai ṭhitau|

kammañ‍ca taṃ kuppaiyya vaggapac‍cayā|

pañhā  maisā kusalaihi cintitā||

padavītihāramattaina vācāya bhaṇitaina ca|

sabbāni garukāni sappaṭikammāni|

catusaṭṭhi āpattiyau āpaj‍jaiyya aikatau|

pañhā maisā kusalaihi cintitā||

nivatthau antaravāsakaina|

diguṇaṃ saṅghāṭiṃ pārutau|

sabbāni tāni nissaggiyāni haunti|

pañhā maisā kusalaihi cintitā||

na  cāpi ñatti na ca pana kammavācā|

na caihi bhikkhūti jinau avauca|

saraṇagamanampi na tassa atthi|

upasampadā cassa akuppā|

pañhā maisā kusalaihi cintitā||

itthiṃ  hanai na mātaraṃ, purisañ‍ca na pitaraṃ hanai|

hanaiyya anariyaṃ mandau, taina cānantaraṃ phusai|

pañhā maisā kusalaihi cintitā||

itthiṃ hanai ca mātaraṃ, purisañ‍ca pitaraṃ hanai|

mātaraṃ pitaraṃ hantvā, na tainānantaraṃ phusai|

pañhā maisā kusalaihi cintitā||

acaudayitvā  assārayitvā|

asammukhībhūtassa karaiyya kammaṃ|

katañ‍ca kammaṃ sukataṃ bhavaiyya|

kārakau  ca saṅghau anāpattikau siyā|

pañhā maisā kusalaihi cintitā||

caudayitvā sārayitvā|

sammukhībhūtassa karaiyya kammaṃ|

katañ‍ca kammaṃ akataṃ bhavaiyya|

kārakau ca saṅghau sāpattikau siyā|

pañhā maisā kusalaihi cintitā||

chindantassa āpatti, chindantassa anāpatti|

chādaintassa āpatti, chādaintassa anāpatti|

pañhā maisā kusalaihi cintitā||

sac‍caṃ bhaṇantau garukaṃ, musā ca lahu bhāsatau|

musā bhaṇantau garukaṃ, sac‍cañ‍ca lahu bhāsatau|

pañhā maisā kusalaihi cintitā||

3. pācittiyādipañhā

481.

 adhiṭṭhitaṃ  rajanāya rattaṃ|

kappakatampi santaṃ|

paribhuñ‍jantassa āpatti|

pañhā maisā kusalaihi cintitā||

atthaṅgatai sūriyai bhikkhu maṃsāni khādati|

na ummattakau na ca pana khittacittau|

na cāpi sau vaidanāṭṭau bhavaiyya|

na cassa hauti āpatti|

sau ca dhammau sugataina daisitau|

pañhā maisā kusalaihi cintitā||

na rattacittau na ca pana thaiyyacittau|

na cāpi sau paraṃ maraṇāya caitayi|

salākaṃ  daintassa hauti chaij‍jaṃ|

paṭiggaṇhantassa thul‍lac‍cayaṃ|

pañhā maisā kusalaihi cintitā||

na cāpi ārañ‍ñakaṃ sāsaṅkasammataṃ|

na cāpi saṅghaina sammuti din‍nā|

na cassa kathinaṃ atthataṃ tatthaiva|

cīvaraṃ nikkhipitvā gacchaiyya aḍḍhayaujanaṃ|

tatthaiva aruṇaṃ uggacchantassa anāpatti|

pañhā maisā kusalaihi cintitā||

kāyikāni na vācasikāni|

sabbāni nānāvatthukāni|

apubbaṃ  acarimaṃ āpaj‍jaiyya aikatau|

pañhā maisā kusalaihi cintitā||

vācasikāni na kāyikāni|

sabbāni nānāvatthukāni|

apubbaṃ acarimaṃ āpaj‍jaiyya aikatau|

pañhā maisā kusalaihi cintitā||

tissitthiyau  maithunaṃ taṃ na saivai|

tayau purisai tayau anariyapaṇḍakai|

na cācarai maithunaṃ byañ‍janasmiṃ|

chaij‍jaṃ  siyā maithunadhammapac‍cayā|

pañhā maisā kusalaihi cintitā||

mātaraṃ cīvaraṃ yācai, nau ca saṅghai nau saṃghassa (ka॰), nau ca saṃghassa (syā॰), nau cai saṃghassa (sī॰) pariṇataṃ|

kainassa hauti āpatti, anāpatti ca ñātakai|

pañhā maisā kusalaihi cintitā||

kuddhau  ārādhakau hauti, kuddhau hauti garahiyau|

atha kau nāma sau dhammau, yaina kuddhau pasaṃsiyau|

pañhā maisā kusalaihi cintitā||

tuṭṭhau ārādhakau hauti, tuṭṭhau hauti garahiyau|

atha kau nāma sau dhammau, yaina tuṭṭhau garahiyau|

pañhā maisā kusalaihi cintitā||

saṅghādisaisaṃ thul‍lac‍cayaṃ|

pācittiyaṃ pāṭidaisanīyaṃ|

duk‍kaṭaṃ āpaj‍jaiyya aikatau|

pañhā maisā kusalaihi cintitā||

ubhau  paripuṇṇavīsativassā|

ubhin‍naṃ aikupajjhāyau|

aikācariyau aikā kammavācā|

aikau upasampan‍nau aikau anupasampan‍nau|

pañhā maisā kusalaihi cintitā||

akappakataṃ nāpi rajanāya rattaṃ|

taina  nivatthau yaina kāmaṃ vajaiyya|

na cassa hauti āpatti|

sau ca dhammau sugataina daisitau|

pañhā maisā kusalaihi cintitā||

na  daiti na paṭiggaṇhāti, paṭiggahau taina na vij‍jati|

āpaj‍jati garukaṃ na lahukaṃ, tañ‍ca paribhaugapac‍cayā|

pañhā maisā kusalaihi cintitā||

na daiti na paṭiggaṇhāti, paṭiggahau taina na vij‍jati|

āpaj‍jati lahukaṃ na garukaṃ, tañ‍ca paribhaugapac‍cayā|

pañhā maisā kusalaihi cintitā||

āpaj‍jati garukaṃ sāvasaisaṃ|

chādaiti anādariyaṃ paṭic‍ca|

na bhikkhunī nau ca phusaiyya vaj‍jaṃ|

pañhā maisā kusalaihi cintitā||

saidamaucanagāthā niṭṭhitā|


tassuddānaṃ –

asaṃvāsau  avissaj‍ji, dasa ca anukkhittakau|

upaiti dhammaṃ ubbhakkhakaṃ, tatau sañ‍ñācikā ca dvai||

na  kāyikañ‍ca garukaṃ, na kāyikaṃ na vācasikaṃ na kāyikaṃ sunāsitaṃ (syā॰)|

anālapantau sikkhā ca, ubhau ca caturau janā||

itthī tailañ‍ca nissaggi, bhikkhu ca padavītiyau|

nivatthau ca na ca ñatti, na mātaraṃ pitaraṃ hanai||

acaudayitvā caudayitvā, chindantaṃ sac‍camaiva ca|

adhiṭṭhitañ‍catthaṅgatai, na rattaṃ na cārañ‍ñakaṃ||

kāyikā vācasikā ca, tissitthī cāpi mātaraṃ|

kuddhau ārādhakau tuṭṭhau, saṅghādisaisā ca ubhau||

akappakataṃ na daiti, na daitāpaj‍jatī garuṃ|

saidamaucanikā gāthā, pañhā viñ‍ñūhi vibhāvitāti viñ‍ñūvibhāvitā (sī॰ syā॰)||


pañ‍cavaggau

1. kammavaggau

482. cattāri  kammāni| apalaukanakammaṃ, ñattikammaṃ, ñattidutiyakammaṃ, ñatticatutthakammaṃ – imāni cattāri kammāni| katihākāraihi vipaj‍janti? imāni cattāri kammāni pañ‍cahākāraihi vipaj‍janti – vatthutau vā ñattitau vā anussāvanatau vā sīmatau vā parisatau vā|

483. kathaṃ vatthutau kammāni vipaj‍janti? sammukhākaraṇīyaṃ kammaṃ asammukhā karauti, vatthuvipan‍naṃ adhammakammaṃ; paṭipucchākaraṇīyaṃ kammaṃ apaṭipucchā karauti, vatthuvipan‍naṃ adhammakammaṃ; paṭiñ‍ñāya karaṇīyaṃ kammaṃ apaṭiñ‍ñāya karauti, vatthuvipan‍naṃ adhammakammaṃ; sativinayārahassa amūḷhavinayaṃ daiti, vatthuvipan‍naṃ adhammakammaṃ; amūḷhavinayārahassa tassapāpiyasikākammaṃ karauti, vatthuvipan‍naṃ adhammakammaṃ; tassapāpiyasikākammārahassa taj‍janīyakammaṃ karauti, vatthuvipan‍naṃ adhammakammaṃ; taj‍janīyakammārahassa niyassakammaṃ karauti, vatthuvipan‍naṃ adhammakammaṃ; niyassakammārahassa pabbājanīyakammaṃ karauti, vatthuvipan‍naṃ adhammakammaṃ; pabbājanīyakammārahassa paṭisāraṇīyakammaṃ karauti, vatthuvipan‍naṃ adhammakammaṃ; paṭisāraṇīyakammārahassa ukkhaipanīyakammaṃ karauti, vatthuvipan‍naṃ adhammakammaṃ; ukkhaipanīyakammārahassa parivāsaṃ daiti, vatthuvipan‍naṃ adhammakammaṃ; parivāsārahaṃ mūlāya paṭikassati, vatthuvipan‍naṃ adhammakammaṃ; mūlāyapaṭikassanārahassa mānattaṃ daiti, vatthuvipan‍naṃ adhammakammaṃ; mānattārahaṃ abbhaiti, vatthuvipan‍naṃ adhammakammaṃ; abbhānārahaṃ upasampādaiti, vatthuvipan‍naṃ adhammakammaṃ; anupausathai upausathaṃ karauti, vatthuvipan‍naṃ adhammakammaṃ; apavāraṇāya pavāraiti, vatthuvipan‍naṃ adhammakammaṃ| aivaṃ vatthutau kammāni vipaj‍janti|

484. kathaṃ  ñattitau kammāni vipaj‍janti? pañ‍cahākāraihi ñattitau kammāni vipaj‍janti – vatthuṃ na parāmasati, saṅghaṃ na parāmasati, puggalaṃ na parāmasati, ñattiṃ na parāmasati, pacchā vā ñattiṃ ṭhapaiti – imaihi pañ‍cahākāraihi ñattitau kammāni vipaj‍janti|

485. kathaṃ  anussāvanatau kammāni vipaj‍janti? pañ‍cahākāraihi  anussāvanatau kammāni vipaj‍janti – vatthuṃ na parāmasati, saṅghaṃ na parāmasati, puggalaṃ na parāmasati, sāvanaṃ hāpaiti, akālai vā sāvaiti – imaihi pañ‍cahākāraihi anussāvanatau kammāni vipaj‍janti|

486. kathaṃ sīmatau kammāni vipaj‍janti? aikādasahi ākāraihi sīmatau kammāni vipaj‍janti – atikhuddakaṃ sīmaṃ samman‍nati, atimahatiṃ sīmaṃ samman‍nati, khaṇḍanimittaṃ sīmaṃ samman‍nati, chāyānimittaṃ sīmaṃ  samman‍nati, animittaṃ sīmaṃ samman‍nati, bahisīmai ṭhitau sīmaṃ samman‍nati, nadiyā sīmaṃ samman‍nati, samuddai sīmaṃ samman‍nati, jātassarai sīmaṃ samman‍nati, sīmāya sīmaṃ sambhindati, sīmāya sīmaṃ ajjhauttharati – imaihi aikādasahi ākāraihi sīmatau kammāni vipaj‍janti|
