487. kathaṃ parisatau kammāni vipaj‍janti? dvādasahi ākāraihi parisatau kammāni vipaj‍janti – catuvaggakaraṇai kammai yāvatikā bhikkhū kammapattā tai anāgatā haunti, chandārahānaṃ chandau anāhaṭau hauti, sammukhībhūtā paṭik‍kausanti, catuvaggakaraṇai kammai yāvatikā bhikkhū kammapattā tai āgatā haunti, chandārahānaṃ chandau anāhaṭau hauti, sammukhībhūtā paṭik‍kausanti, catuvaggakaraṇai kammai yāvatikā bhikkhū kammapattā tai āgatā haunti, chandārahānaṃ chandau āhaṭau hauti, sammukhībhūtā paṭik‍kausanti| pañ‍cavaggakaraṇai kammai…pai॰… dasavaggakaraṇai kammai…pai॰… vīsativaggakaraṇai kammai yāvatikā bhikkhū kammapattā tai anāgatā haunti, chandārahānaṃ chandau anāhaṭau hauti, sammukhībhūtā paṭik‍kausanti, vīsativaggakaraṇai kammai yāvatikā bhikkhū kammapattā, tai āgatā haunti, chandārahānaṃ chandau anāhaṭau hauti, sammukhībhūtā paṭik‍kausanti, vīsativaggakaraṇai kammai yāvatikā bhikkhū kammapattā, tai āgatā haunti, chandārahānaṃ  chandau āhaṭau hauti sammukhībhūtā paṭik‍kausanti – imaihi dvādasahi ākāraihi parisatau kammāni vipaj‍janti|

488. catuvaggakaraṇai kammai cattārau bhikkhū pakatattā kammapattā, avasaisā pakatattā chandārahā| yassa saṅghau kammaṃ karauti sau naiva kammapattau nāpi chandārahau, api ca kammārahau| pañ‍cavaggakaraṇai  kammai pañ‍ca bhikkhū pakatattā kammapattā, avasaisā pakatattā chandārahā| yassa saṅghau kammaṃ karauti sau naiva kammapattau nāpi chandārahau, api ca kammārahau| dasavaggakaraṇai kammai dasa bhikkhū pakatattā kammapattā, avasaisā pakatattā chandārahā| yassa saṅghau kammaṃ karauti sau naiva kammapattau nāpi chandārahau, api ca kammārahau| vīsativaggakaraṇai kammai vīsati bhikkhū pakatattā kammapattā, avasaisā pakatattā chandārahā| yassa saṅghau kammaṃ karauti sau naiva kammapattau nāpi chandārahau, api ca kammārahau|

489. cattāri  kammāni – apalaukanakammaṃ, ñattikammaṃ, ñattidutiyakammaṃ, ñatticatutthakammaṃ| imāni cattāri kammāni katihākāraihi vipaj‍janti? imāni cattāri kammāni pañ‍cahākāraihi vipaj‍janti – vatthutau vā ñattitau vā anussāvanatau vā sīmatau vā parisatau vā|

490. kathaṃ vatthutau kammāni vipaj‍janti? paṇḍakaṃ upasampādaiti, vatthuvipan‍naṃ adhammakammaṃ| thaiyyasaṃvāsakaṃ upasampādaiti, vatthuvipan‍naṃ adhammakammaṃ| titthiyapak‍kantakaṃ upasampādaiti, vatthuvipan‍naṃ adhammakammaṃ| tiracchānagataṃ upasampādaiti, vatthuvipan‍naṃ adhammakammaṃ| mātughātakaṃ upasampādaiti, vatthuvipan‍naṃ adhammakammaṃ| pitughātakaṃ upasampādaiti, vatthuvipan‍naṃ adhammakammaṃ| arahantaghātakaṃ upasampādaiti, vatthuvipan‍naṃ adhammakammaṃ| bhikkhunidūsakaṃ upasampādaiti, vatthuvipan‍naṃ adhammakammaṃ| saṅghabhaidakaṃ upasampādaiti, vatthuvipan‍naṃ adhammakammaṃ| lauhituppādakaṃ upasampādaiti, vatthuvipan‍naṃ adhammakammaṃ| ubhataubyañ‍janaṃ upasampādaiti, vatthuvipan‍naṃ adhammakammaṃ| ūnavīsativassaṃ puggalaṃ upasampādaiti, vatthuvipan‍naṃ adhammakammaṃ| aivaṃ vatthutau kammāni vipaj‍janti|

491. kathaṃ ñattitau kammāni vipaj‍janti? pañ‍cahākāraihi ñattitau kammāni vipaj‍janti| vatthuṃ na parāmasati, saṅghaṃ na parāmasati, puggalaṃ na parāmasati, ñattiṃ na parāmasati, pacchā vā ñattiṃ ṭhapaiti – imaihi pañ‍cahākāraihi ñattitau kammāni vipaj‍janti|

492. kathaṃ anussāvanatau kammāni vipaj‍janti? pañ‍cahākāraihi anussāvanatau kammāni vipaj‍janti – vatthuṃ na parāmasati, saṅghaṃ na parāmasati, puggalaṃ  na parāmasati, sāvanaṃ  hāpaiti, akālai vā sāvaiti – imaihi pañ‍cahākāraihi anussāvanatau kammāni  vipaj‍janti|

493. kathaṃ sīmatau kammāni vipaj‍janti? aikādasahi ākāraihi sīmatau kammāni vipaj‍janti| atikhuddakaṃ sīmaṃ samman‍nati, atimahatiṃ sīmaṃ samman‍nati, khaṇḍanimittaṃ sīmaṃ samman‍nati, chāyānimittaṃ sīmaṃ samman‍nati, animittaṃ sīmaṃ samman‍nati, bahisīmai ṭhitau sīmaṃ samman‍nati, nadiyā sīmaṃ samman‍nati, samuddai sīmaṃ samman‍nati, jātassarai sīmaṃ samman‍nati, sīmāya sīmaṃ sambhindati, sīmāya sīmaṃ ajjhauttharati – imaihi aikādasahi ākāraihi sīmatau kammāni vipaj‍janti|

494. kathaṃ parisatau kammāni vipaj‍janti? dvādasahi ākāraihi parisatau kammāni vipaj‍janti – catuvaggakaraṇai kammai yāvatikā bhikkhū kammapattā tai anāgatā haunti, chandārahānaṃ chandau anāhaṭau hauti, sammukhībhūtā paṭik‍kausanti, catuvaggakaraṇai kammai yāvatikā bhikkhū kammapattā tai āgatā haunti, chandārahānaṃ chandau anāhaṭau hauti, sammukhībhūtā paṭik‍kausanti| catuvaggakaraṇai kammai yāvatikā bhikkhū kammapattā tai āgatā haunti, chandārahānaṃ chandau āhaṭau hauti sammukhībhūtā paṭik‍kausanti| pañ‍cavaggakaraṇai kammai…pai॰… dasavaggakaraṇai kammai…pai॰… vīsativaggakaraṇai kammai yāvatikā bhikkhū kammapattā tai  anāgatā haunti, chandārahānaṃ chandau anāhaṭau hauti, sammukhībhūtā paṭik‍kausanti| vīsativaggakaraṇai kammai yāvatikā bhikkhū kammapattā tai āgatā haunti, chandārahānaṃ chandau anāhaṭau hauti, sammukhībhūtā paṭik‍kausanti| vīsativaggakaraṇai kammai yāvatikā bhikkhū kammapattā tai āgatā haunti, chandārahānaṃ chandau āhaṭau hauti, sammukhībhūtā paṭik‍kausanti – imaihi dvādasahi ākāraihi parisatau kammāni vipaj‍janti|

495. apalaukanakammaṃ kati ṭhānāni gacchati? ñattikammaṃ kati ṭhānāni gacchati? ñattidutiyakammaṃ kati ṭhānāni gacchati? ñatticatutthakammaṃ kati ṭhānāni gacchati? apalaukanakammaṃ pañ‍ca ṭhānāni gacchati| ñattikammaṃ nava ṭhānāni gacchati| ñattidutiyakammaṃ satta ṭhānāni gacchati| ñatticatutthakammaṃ satta ṭhānāni gacchati|

496. apalaukanakammaṃ  katamāni pañ‍ca ṭhānāni gacchati? ausāraṇaṃ, nissāraṇaṃ, bhaṇḍukammaṃ, brahmadaṇḍaṃ, kammalakkhaṇañ‍ñaiva pañ‍camaṃ – apalaukanakammaṃ imāni pañ‍ca ṭhānāni gacchati| ñattikammaṃ katamāni nava ṭhānāni gacchati? ausāraṇaṃ, nissāraṇaṃ, upausathaṃ, pavāraṇaṃ, sammutiṃ, dānaṃ, paṭiggahaṃ, pac‍cuk‍kaḍḍhanaṃ, kammalakkhaṇañ‍ñaiva navamaṃ – ñattikammaṃ imāni nava ṭhānāni gacchati| ñattidutiyakammaṃ katamāni satta ṭhānāni gacchati? ausāraṇaṃ, nissāraṇaṃ, sammutiṃ, dānaṃ, uddharaṇaṃ, daisanaṃ, kammalakkhaṇañ‍ñaiva sattamaṃ – ñattidutiyakammaṃ imāni satta ṭhānāni gacchati| ñatticatutthakammaṃ  katamāni satta ṭhānāni gacchati? ausāraṇaṃ, nissāraṇaṃ, sammutiṃ, dānaṃ, niggahaṃ, samanubhāsanaṃ, kammalakkhaṇañ‍ñaiva sattamaṃ – ñatticatutthakammaṃ imāni satta ṭhānāni gacchati|

497. catuvaggakaraṇai kammai cattārau bhikkhū pakatattā kammapattā, avasaisā pakatattā chandārahā| yassa saṅghau kammaṃ karauti sau naiva kammapattau nāpi chandārahau, api ca kammārahau| pañ‍cavaggakaraṇai  kammai pañ‍ca bhikkhū pakatattā kammapattā, avasaisā pakatattā chandārahā| yassa saṅghau kammaṃ karauti sau naiva kammapattau nāpi chandārahau, api ca kammārahau| dasavaggakaraṇai kammai dasa bhikkhū pakatattā kammapattā, avasaisā pakatattā chandārahā| yassa saṅghau kammaṃ karauti sau naiva kammapattau nāpi chandārahau, api ca kammārahau| vīsativaggakaraṇai kammai vīsati bhikkhū pakatattā kammapattā, avasaisā pakatattā chandārahā| yassa saṅghau kammaṃ karauti sau naiva kammapattau nāpi chandārahau, api ca kammārahau|

kammavaggau niṭṭhitau paṭhamau|

2. atthavasavaggau

498. a॰ ni॰ 2.201-230 dvai atthavasai paṭic‍ca tathāgataina sāvakānaṃ sikkhāpadaṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ| saṅghasuṭṭhutāya, saṅghaphāsutāya – imai dvai atthavasai paṭic‍ca tathāgataina sāvakānaṃ sikkhāpadaṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ| dvai atthavasai paṭic‍ca tathāgataina sāvakānaṃ sikkhāpadaṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ| dummaṅkūnaṃ puggalānaṃ niggahāya, paisalānaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ phāsuvihārāya – imai dvai atthavasai paṭic‍ca tathāgatai  sāvakānaṃ sikkhāpadaṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ| dvai atthavasai paṭic‍ca tathāgataina sāvakānaṃ sikkhāpadaṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ| diṭṭhadhammikānaṃ āsavānaṃ saṃvarāya, samparāyikānaṃ āsavānaṃ paṭighātāya – imai  dvai  atthavasai paṭic‍ca tathāgataina sāvakānaṃ sikkhāpadaṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ| dvai atthavasai paṭic‍ca tathāgataina sāvakānaṃ sikkhāpadaṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ| diṭṭhadhammikānaṃ vairānaṃ saṃvarāya, samparāyikānaṃ vairānaṃ paṭighātāya – imai dvai atthavasai paṭic‍ca tathāgataina sāvakānaṃ sikkhāpadaṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ| dvai atthavasai paṭic‍ca tathāgataina sāvakānaṃ sikkhāpadaṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ| diṭṭhadhammikānaṃ vaj‍jānaṃ saṃvarāya, samparāyikānaṃ vaj‍jānaṃ paṭighātāya – imai dvai atthavasai paṭic‍ca tathāgataina sāvakānaṃ sikkhāpadaṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ| dvai atthavasai paṭic‍ca tathāgataina sāvakānaṃ sikkhāpadaṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ| diṭṭhadhammikānaṃ bhayānaṃ saṃvarāya, samparāyikānaṃ bhayānaṃ paṭighātāya – imai dvai atthavasai paṭic‍ca tathāgataina sāvakānaṃ sikkhāpadaṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ| dvai atthavasai paṭic‍ca tathāgataina sāvakānaṃ sikkhāpadaṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ| diṭṭhadhammikānaṃ akusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ saṃvarāya, samparāyikānaṃ akusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ paṭighātāya – imai dvai atthavasai paṭic‍ca tathāgataina sāvakānaṃ sikkhāpadaṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ| dvai atthavasai paṭic‍ca tathāgataina sāvakānaṃ sikkhāpadaṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ| gihīnaṃ anukampāya, pāpicchānaṃ pakkhupacchaidāya – imai dvai atthavasai paṭic‍ca tathāgataina sāvakānaṃ sikkhāpadaṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ| dvai atthavasai paṭic‍ca tathāgataina sāvakānaṃ sikkhāpadaṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ| appasan‍nānaṃ pasādāya, pasan‍nānaṃ bhiyyaubhāvāya – imai dvai atthavasai paṭic‍ca tathāgataina sāvakānaṃ sikkhāpadaṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ| dvai atthavasai paṭic‍ca tathāgataina  sāvakānaṃ sikkhāpadaṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ| saddhammaṭṭhitiyā, vinayānuggahāya – imai dvai atthavasai paṭic‍ca tathāgataina sāvakānaṃ sikkhāpadaṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

atthavasavaggau niṭṭhitau dutiyau|

3. pañ‍ñattavaggau

499. dvai atthavasai paṭic‍ca tathāgataina sāvakānaṃ pātimaukkhaṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ…pai॰… pātimaukkhuddaisau pañ‍ñattau… pātimaukkhaṭṭhapanaṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ… pavāraṇā pañ‍ñattā… pavāraṇāṭhapanaṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ… taj‍janīyakammaṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ… niyassakammaṃ  pañ‍ñattaṃ… pabbājanīyakammaṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ… paṭisāraṇīyakammaṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ… ukkhaipanīyakammaṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ… parivāsadānaṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ… mūlāyapaṭikassanā pañ‍ñattā… mānattadānaṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ… abbhānaṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ… ausāraṇīyaṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ… nissāraṇīyaṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ… upasampadaṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ… apalaukanakammaṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ… ñattikammaṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ… ñattidutiyakammaṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ… ñatticatutthakammaṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ…pai॰…|

pañ‍ñattavaggau niṭṭhitau tatiyau|

4. apañ‍ñattai pañ‍ñattavaggau

500. …pai॰…  apañ‍ñattai pañ‍ñattaṃ, pañ‍ñattai anupañ‍ñattaṃ…pai॰… sammukhāvinayau pañ‍ñattau…pai॰… sativinayau pañ‍ñattau…pai॰… amūḷhavinayau pañ‍ñattau…pai॰… paṭiñ‍ñātakaraṇaṃ  pañ‍ñattaṃ…pai॰… yaibhuyyasikā pañ‍ñattā…pai॰… tassapāpiyasikā pañ‍ñattā…pai॰… tiṇavatthārakau pañ‍ñattau saṅghasuṭṭhutāya, saṅghaphāsutāya – imai dvai atthavasai paṭic‍ca tathāgataina sāvakānaṃ  tiṇavatthārakau pañ‍ñattau| dvai atthavasai paṭic‍ca tathāgataina sāvakānaṃ tiṇavatthārakau pañ‍ñattau| dummaṅkūnaṃ puggalānaṃ niggahāya, paisalānaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ phāsuvihārāya – imai dvai atthavasai paṭic‍ca tathāgataina sāvakānaṃ tiṇavatthārakau pañ‍ñattau| dvai atthavasai paṭic‍ca tathāgataina sāvakānaṃ tiṇavatthārakau pañ‍ñattau| diṭṭhadhammikānaṃ āsavānaṃ saṃvarāya, samparāyikānaṃ āsavānaṃ paṭighātāya – imai dvai atthavasai paṭic‍ca tathāgataina sāvakānaṃ tiṇavatthārakau pañ‍ñattau| dvai atthavasai paṭic‍ca tathāgataina sāvakānaṃ tiṇavatthārakau pañ‍ñattau| diṭṭhadhammikānaṃ vairānaṃ saṃvarāya, samparāyikānaṃ vairānaṃ paṭighātāya – imai dvai atthavasai paṭic‍ca tathāgataina sāvakānaṃ tiṇavatthārakau pañ‍ñattau| dvai atthavasai paṭic‍ca tathāgataina sāvakānaṃ tiṇavatthārakau pañ‍ñattau| diṭṭhadhammikānaṃ vaj‍jānaṃ saṃvarāya, samparāyikānaṃ vaj‍jānaṃ paṭighātāya – imai dvai atthavasai paṭic‍ca tathāgataina sāvakānaṃ tiṇavatthārakau pañ‍ñattau| dvai atthavasai paṭic‍ca tathāgataina sāvakānaṃ tiṇavatthārakau pañ‍ñattau| diṭṭhadhammikānaṃ bhayānaṃ saṃvarāya, samparāyikānaṃ bhayānaṃ paṭighātāya – imai dvai atthavasai paṭic‍ca tathāgataina sāvakānaṃ tiṇavatthārakau pañ‍ñattau| dvai  atthavasai paṭic‍ca tathāgataina sāvakānaṃ tiṇavatthārakau pañ‍ñattau| diṭṭhadhammikānaṃ akusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ saṃvarāya, samparāyikānaṃ akusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ paṭighātāya  – imai dvai atthavasai paṭic‍ca tathāgataina sāvakānaṃ tiṇavatthārakau pañ‍ñattau| dvai atthavasai paṭic‍ca tathāgataina sāvakānaṃ tiṇavatthārakau pañ‍ñattau| gihīnaṃ anukampāya, pāpicchānaṃ pakkhupacchaidāya – imai dvai atthavasai paṭic‍ca tathāgataina sāvakānaṃ tiṇavatthārakau pañ‍ñattau| dvai atthavasai paṭic‍ca tathāgataina sāvakānaṃ tiṇavatthārakau pañ‍ñattau| appasan‍nānaṃ pasādāya, pasan‍nānaṃ bhiyyaubhāvāya – imai dvai atthavasai paṭic‍ca tathāgataina sāvakānaṃ tiṇavatthārakau pañ‍ñattau| dvai atthavasai paṭic‍ca tathāgataina sāvakānaṃ tiṇavatthārakau pañ‍ñattau| saddhammaṭṭhitiyā, vinayānuggahāya – imai dvai atthavasai paṭic‍ca tathāgataina sāvakānaṃ tiṇavatthārakau pañ‍ñattau|

apañ‍ñattai pañ‍ñattavaggau niṭṭhitau catutthau|

5. navasaṅgahavaggau

501. navasaṅgahā  – vatthusaṅgahau, vipattisaṅgahau āpattisaṅgahau, nidānasaṅgahau, puggalasaṅgahau, khandhasaṅgahau, samuṭṭhānasaṅgahau, adhikaraṇasaṅgahau, samathasaṅgahauti|

adhikaraṇai samuppan‍nai sacai ubhau atthapac‍catthikā āgacchanti ubhin‍nampi vatthu āraucāpaitabbaṃ| ubhin‍nampi vatthu āraucāpaitvā ubhin‍nampi paṭiñ‍ñā sautabbā| ubhin‍nampi paṭiñ‍ñaṃ sutvā ubhaupi vattabbā – ‘‘amhākaṃ imasmiṃ adhikaraṇai vūpasamitai  vūpasamaipi (ka॰) ubhaupi tuṭṭhā bhavissathā’’ti| sacai āhaṃsu – ‘‘ubhaupi tuṭṭhā bhavissāmā’’ti, saṅghaina taṃ adhikaraṇaṃ sampaṭicchitabbaṃ| sacai alaj‍jussan‍nā hauti, parisā ubbāhikāya vūpasamaitabbaṃ| sacai bālussan‍nā hauti, parisā vinayadharau pariyaisitabbau yaina dhammaina yaina vinayaina yaina satthusāsanaina taṃ adhikaraṇaṃ vūpasammati| tathā taṃ adhikaraṇaṃ vūpasamaitabbaṃ|

vatthu jānitabbaṃ, gauttaṃ jānitabbaṃ, nāmaṃ jānitabbaṃ, āpatti jānitabbā|

maithunadhammauti  vatthu caiva gauttañ‍ca – pārājikanti nāmañ‍caiva āpatti ca|

adin‍nādānanti vatthu caiva gauttañ‍ca – pārājikanti nāmañ‍caiva āpatti ca|

manussaviggahauti vatthu caiva gauttañ‍ca – pārājikanti nāmañ‍caiva āpatti ca|

uttarimanussadhammauti vatthu caiva gauttañ‍ca – pārājikanti nāmañ‍caiva āpatti ca|

suk‍kavissaṭṭhīti vatthu caiva gauttañ‍ca – saṅghādisaisauti nāmañ‍caiva āpatti ca|

kāyasaṃsaggauti vatthu caiva gauttañ‍ca – saṅghādisaisauti nāmañ‍caiva āpatti ca|

duṭṭhul‍lavācāti vatthu caiva gauttañ‍ca – saṅghādisaisauti nāmañ‍caiva āpatti ca|

attakāmanti vatthu caiva gauttañ‍ca – saṅghādisaisauti nāmañ‍caiva āpatti ca|

sañ‍carittanti  vatthu caiva gauttañ‍ca – saṅghādisaisauti nāmañ‍caiva āpatti ca|

sañ‍ñācikāya kuṭiṃ kārāpananti vatthu caiva gauttañ‍ca – saṅghādisaisauti nāmañ‍caiva āpatti  ca|

mahal‍lakaṃ vihāraṃ kārāpananti vatthu caiva gauttañ‍ca – saṅghādisaisauti nāmañ‍caiva āpatti ca|

bhikkhuṃ amūlakaina pārājikaina dhammaina anuddhaṃsananti vatthu caiva gauttañ‍ca – saṅghādisaisauti nāmañ‍caiva āpatti ca|

bhikkhuṃ añ‍ñabhāgiyassa adhikaraṇassa kiñ‍ci daisaṃ laisamattaṃ upādāya pārājikaina dhammaina anuddhaṃsananti  vatthu caiva gauttañ‍ca – saṅghādisaisauti nāmañ‍caiva āpatti ca|

saṅghabhaidakassa bhikkhunau yāvatatiyaṃ samanubhāsanāya na paṭinissaj‍jananti vatthu caiva gauttañ‍ca – saṅghādisaisauti nāmañ‍caiva āpatti ca|

bhaidakānuvattakānaṃ  bhikkhūnaṃ yāvatatiyaṃ samanubhāsanāya na paṭinissaj‍jananti vatthu caiva gauttañ‍ca – saṅghādisaisauti nāmañ‍caiva āpatti ca|

dubbacassa bhikkhunau yāvatatiyaṃ samanubhāsanāya na paṭinissaj‍jananti vatthu caiva gauttañ‍ca – saṅghādisaisauti nāmañ‍caiva āpatti ca|

kuladūsakassa bhikkhunau yāvatatiyaṃ samanubhāsanāya na paṭinissaj‍jananti vatthu caiva gauttañ‍ca – saṅghādisaisauti nāmañ‍caiva āpatti ca…pai॰…|

anādariyaṃ paṭic‍ca udakai uc‍cāraṃ vā passāvaṃ vā khaiḷaṃ vā karaṇanti vatthu caiva gauttañ‍ca – duk‍kaṭanti nāmañ‍caiva āpatti cāti|

navasaṅgahavaggau niṭṭhitau pañ‍camau|


tassuddānaṃ –

apalaukanaṃ  ñatti ca, dutiyaṃ catutthaina ca|

vatthu ñatti anussāvanaṃ, sīmā parisamaiva ca||

sammukhā paṭipucchā ca, paṭiñ‍ñā vinayārahau|

vatthu saṅghapuggalañ‍ca, ñattiṃ na pacchā ñatti ca||

vatthuṃ saṅghapuggalañ‍ca, sāvanaṃ akālaina ca|

atikhuddakā mahantā ca, khaṇḍacchāyā nimittakā||

bahinadī samuddai ca, jātassarai ca bhindati|

ajjhauttharati sīmāya, catu pañ‍ca ca vaggikā||

dasa vīsativaggā ca, anāhaṭā ca āhaṭā|

kammapattā chandārahā, kammārahā ca puggalā||

apalaukanaṃ pañ‍caṭṭhānaṃ, ñatti ca navaṭhānikā|

ñatti dutiyaṃ sattaṭṭhānaṃ, catutthā sattaṭhānikā||

suṭṭhu phāsu ca dummaṅku, paisalā cāpi āsavā|

vairavaj‍jabhayañ‍caiva, akusalaṃ gihīnañ‍ca||

pāpicchā  appasan‍nānaṃ, pasan‍nā dhammaṭṭhapanā|

vinayānuggahā caiva, pātimaukkhuddaisaina ca||

pātimaukkhañ‍ca  ṭhapanā, pavāraṇañ‍ca ṭhapanaṃ|

taj‍janīyā niyassañ‍ca, pabbājanīya paṭisāraṇī|

ukkhaipana parivāsaṃ, mūlamānattaabbhānaṃ|

ausāraṇaṃ nissāraṇaṃ, tathaiva upasampadā||

apalaukanañatti  ca, dutiyañ‍ca catutthakaṃ|

apañ‍ñattainupañ‍ñattaṃ, sammukhāvinayau sati||

amūḷhapaṭiyaibhuyya, pāpiya tiṇavatthārakaṃ|

vatthu vipatti āpatti, nidānaṃ puggalaina ca||

khandhā  caiva samuṭṭhānā, adhikaraṇamaiva ca|

samathā saṅgahā caiva, nāmaāpattikā tathāti||

parivārau niṭṭhitau|

parivārapāḷi niṭṭhitā|
